śrīlakṣmīkeśavau jayataḥ
oṃ namo vighnarājāya |
saṃsārādhvagatāgataklamatṛṣāpīyuṣam īśaṃ śanair
dhyātvānargalasaṃpradāya gurutaḥ prāpteḥ manoḥ śāsane |
dṛṣṭvā granthakṛdāśayānanusṛtiṃ vyākhyāntarāṇām imāṃ
ṭīkāṃ śāstrakṛdāśayānusaraṇīṃ govindrarājo vyadhāt || 1 ||
manum ekāgram āsīnam abhigamya maharṣayaḥ |
pratipūjya yathānyāyam idaṃ vacanam abruvan || 1.1 ||
iha bhṛguśiṣyaḥ kaścid vicchinnapāramparyataḥ smṛtyarthaprabandham idam āha – svecchāgṛhītaśarīrāya prajāpataye ‘mitatejase praṇamya manvabhihitān nānāprakārān dharmān adṛṣṭārthakartavyatārūpān iṣṭāniṣṭaprāptiparihāraprayojanasya vedamūlatvasyaiṣām iṣyamāṇatvāt nityān kathyiṣyāmi | adharmāṇāṃ vakṣyamāṇānām apīha pṛthagapratijñānaṃ ninditakarmābhyāsena pātitye sati dharmānuṣṭhānānadhikārāpatteḥ dharmapratijñāyām evāntarbhāvāt | manur nāma maharṣiḥ aśeṣavedārthādijñānena prāptamanusaṃjñaḥ āgamaparamparayā sakalavidvajjanakarṇacarībhūtasargasthitipralayakaraṇādhikṛtaḥ | svayaṃbhuva iti kriyāgrahaṇam api kartavyam iti saṃpradāne caturthī | vedārthopanibandhakatvena chandastulyatvāt smṛtīnāṃ tu duṣṭā kvacid atra chāndasī rūpasiddhiḥ ||
manum iti | taṃ manum ekāgram āsīnam avikṣiptacittaṃ sthitaṃ ābhimukhyena ādarato gatvā | maharṣayaḥ aṇimādiguṇayogān mahāntaḥ ṛgyajuḥsāmadarśanān maharṣayaḥ | yathānyāyaṃ śāstroktamaryādānatikrameṇa prakarṣeṇa pūjayitvā idaṃ samantaravakṣyamāṇaṃ vākyam uktavantaḥ || 1.1 ||
bhagavan sarvavarṇānāṃ yathāvad anupūrvaśaḥ |
antaraprabhavānāṃ ca dharmān no vaktum arhasi || 1.2 ||
bhagavan iti | sa hi sarvaiśvaryopetaḥ sarveṣāṃ varṇānāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ śūdraparyantānāṃ naḥ asmākam eva dvijātīnām, tathā brāhmaṇakṣatriyādisaṃkarajātānām anulomapratilomānāṃ yāvat kṛtsnaṃ kṛtvā ānupūrvyāt niṣekādikrameṇa dharmān asmākaṃ vaktuṃ yogyo bhavati || 1.2 ||
tvam eko hy asya sarvasya vidhānasya svayaṃbhuvaḥ |
acintyasyāprameyasya kāryatattvārthavit prabho || 1.3 ||
tvem eka iti | yasmāt tvam evaiko ‘sya sarvasya śāstrasya akṛtakasya idaṃ kartavyam idaṃ nety evaṃvidhasya | yat tv idaṃ śāstraṃ tat kṛtvā asāv iti tatra granthaviṣaye atigahanatvād evaṃrūpam etad iti | cintayitum aśakyasya atimahattvāt iyad etad iti pramātum aśakyasya kāryarūpo ‘nuṣṭheyarūpo yaḥ pāramārthiko ‘rthaḥ taṃ vetsi sarvaśakte || 1.3 ||
sa taiḥ pṛṣṭas tathā samyag amitaujā mahātmabhiḥ |
pratyuvācārcya tān sarvān maharṣīñ chrūyatām iti || 1.4 ||
sa iti | sa manuḥ aparimitatejāḥ maharṣibhiḥ mahātmabhiḥ parārthakāribhiḥ | ata eva teṣāṃ maharṣīṇām api satāṃ dharmapraśnān tena prakāreṇa abhigamanapūrvakaṃ samyak pūjāpūrvakaṃ ca pṛṣṭaḥ abhyarcayitvā tān prati evam uktavān śrūyatām iti || 1.4 ||
āsīd idaṃ tamobhūtam aprajñātam alakṣaṇam |
apratarkyam avijñeyaṃ prasuptam iva sarvataḥ || 1.5 ||
āsīd iti | jagadutpattisthitipralayānāṃ dharmādharmanimittatvāt tatpratipādanārthatvenedaṃ śāstraṃ mahāprayojanatayā yatnato ‘dhigantavyam ity etadarthaṃ jagadutpadakaṃ prakaraṇam ārabhate | tatra prāgbhāvinīṃ tāvadavasthām anena ślokenāha | idaṃ jagat sṛṣṭeḥ prāk tama ivābhūt | yataḥ kāryātmanā sarvasya tadānīṃ vinaṣṭatvāt pratyakṣeṇa na jñātam | alakṣaṇam avidyamānaliṅgatayānanumeyaṃ pratyakṣapūrvakatvād anumānādīnāṃ tadasaṃbhave teṣām apy abhāvāt | apratarkyam anaupamyatayāpi “gaur iva gavayaḥ” iti ca tarkayitum aśakyam | avijñeyam arthāpattyāpi jīvato devadattasya gṛhādarśanena bahirbhāvavat | evaṃ tarhi abhāva eva prāpta ity ata āha – prasuptam iveti | āgamād evaṃrūpatāsya jñāyata iti | yathā suṣuptāvasthāyām uparatendriyamanovyāpāraḥ ātmā bhavati evaṃ sakalakāryapratyastamaye tadānīṃ samantāt jagad āsīd iti | svapnadarśanarahitā hi nidrāvasthā suṣuptiḥ || 1.5 ||
tataḥ svayaṃbhūr bhagavān avyakto vyañjayann idam |
mahābhūtādivṛttaujāḥ prādur āsīt tamonudaḥ || 1.6 ||
tata iti pūrvoktāvasthānantaram | svecchācikīrṣitaśarīraparigrahaḥ ātmā, avyaktaḥ pratyakṣādyagocaraḥ dhyānaikagamyatvāt utpādayan jagat | mahābhūtāni pṛthivyādīni ādigrahaṇāt anyāny api mahadādīni tattvāni | vṛttaṃ prāptaṃ ojaḥ sṛṣṭisāmarthyaṃ yenāsau prakāśo babhūva śarīragrahaṇaṃ kṛtavān tamonudaḥ pralayasya dhvaṃsakaḥ || 1.6 ||
yo ‘sāv atīndriyagrāhyaḥ sūkṣmo ‘vyaktaḥ sanātanaḥ |
sarvabhūtamayo ‘cintyaḥ sa eva svayam udbadhau || 1.7 ||
yo ‘sāv iti | yo ‘sāv ātmā atīndriyeṇa manasā grahītum aśkyaḥ sūkṣmaḥ sūkṣmabuddhigrāhyatvāt | sanātanaḥ nityaḥ | sarvabhūtamayaḥ sarvasya tatprabhavatvāt | acintyaḥ vilakṣaṇaśaktitvāt sa eva svayaṃ śarīraṃ jagrāha || 1.7 ||
so ‘bhidhyāya śarīrāt svāt sisṛkṣur vividhāḥ prajāḥ |
apa eva sasarjādau tāsu vīryam avāsṛjat || 1.8 ||
sa iti | sa ātmā nānāprakārāḥ prajāḥ sraṣṭum icchan ātmagṛhītaśarīrāt dhyānamātreṇaiva apa eva sṛṣṭeḥ prāk sṛṣṭavān | tāsu vīryaṃ svaśaktiṃ niṣiktavān || 1.8 ||
tad aṇḍam abhavad dhaimaṃ sahasrāṃśusamaprabham |
tasmiñ jajñe svayaṃ brahmā sarvalokapitāmahaḥ || 1.9 ||
tad iti | tad vīryaṃ kāñcanam aṇḍaṃ saṃpannam ata eva ādityasamadīpti | tasmiṃś ca jātavān ātmecchayā hiraṇyagarbhaḥ | sarvalokotpādayitā pitāmaha iti vā saṃjñā tasya || 1.9 ||
āpo nārā iti proktā āpo vai narasūnavaḥ |
tā yad asyāyanaṃ pūrvaṃ tena nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ || 1.10 ||
āpo nārāśabdena kathitā yasmāt tās tasya puruṣasyātmākhyasyoktena prakāreṇāpatyāni | tā āpo yasmād ātmanaḥ pūrvam ayanam ādya āśrayaḥ | ety asminn ity ayanam iti kṛtvā tenāsau nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ | ity evaṃ pūrvoktadārḍhyāya etat svanāmanirvacanam, narāyaṇa eva nārāyaṇaḥ iti anyeṣām api dṛśyata iti dīrgheṇa || 1.10 ||
yat tat kāraṇam avyaktaṃ nityaṃ sadasadātmakam |
tadvisṛṣṭaḥ sa puruṣo loke brahmeti kīrtyate || 1.11 ||
yat tad ātmākhyam uktaṃ vakṣyamāṇanītyā sarvasya kāraṇaṃ vedāntoktayuktyāgamagamyatvāt satsv abhāvaṃ pratyakṣādyagocaratvād asatsvabhāvam iva tenotpāditaḥ sa puruṣas tatra tatra brahmaśabdena saṃkīrtyate || 1.11 ||
tasminn aṇḍe sa bhagavān uṣitvā parivatsaram |
svayam evātmano dhyānāt tad aṇḍam akarod dvidhā || 1.12 ||
tasminn iti | pūrvokte aṇḍe sa brahmā yadṛcchayā saṃvatsaram āsitvā, ātmanaḥ ātmagatena dhyāyenaiva tad aṇḍaṃ dvikhaṇḍaṃ kṛtavān || 1.12 ||
tābhyāṃ sa śakalābhyāṃ ca divaṃ bhūmiṃ ca nirmame |
madhye vyoma diśaś cāṣṭāv apāṃ sthānaṃ ca śāśvatam || 1.13 ||
tābhyām iti | tābhyām aṇḍakapālābhyāṃ sa brahmā ekena divaṃ apareṇa ca bhūmiṃ nirmitavān | tayoś ca madhye ākāśaṃ diśaś cāntarāladigbhiḥ sahāṣṭau apāṃ sthānaṃ ca sthiraṃ samudrākhyaṃ nirmame | etac ca tattvair mahadādibhir nirmitavān || 1.13 ||
udbabarhātmanaś caiva manaḥ sadasadātmakam |
manasaś cāpy ahaṃkāram abhimantāram īśvaram || 1.14 ||
tat tv evam evety etad dharśayitum āha udbabarheti | prajāpatiśarīrād eva tattvasṛṣṭir iti vedāntadarśanam | ato nehāprastutā | śabdārthībhūtaṃ pradhānam ātmaśabdenocyate | tathā ca,
etasmāj jāyate prāṇo manaḥ sarvendriyāṇi ca |
khaṃ vāyur jyotir āpaḥ pṛthivī viśvasya dhāriṇī ||
ityevamādinā upaniṣatsu parikalpitabhedāvasthānād ātmanaḥ idaṃ sarvaṃ kalpitarūpaṃ jāyata ity uktam | sa brahmā uddhṛtavān ātmana eva śarīrāt manaḥ nityānumeyatvād asadātmakam iva yugapaj jñānānutpattiliṅgena tatsattāvagateḥ sadasadātamakaṃ manaḥ pūrvam ahaṃkārākhyaṃ tattvaṃ abhimantāram aham iti abhimānākhyaṃ tatkāryayuktaṃ svakāryakaraṇasamartham uddhatavān || 1.14 ||
mahāntam eva cātmānaṃ sarvāṇi triguṇāni ca |
viṣayāṇāṃ grahītṝṇi śanaiḥ pañcendriyāṇi ca || 1.15 ||
manāhtam iti | ahaṃkārāt pūrvaṃ mahadākhyaṃ tattvaṃ ātmana utpannatvād uddhṛtavān | yāny uktāni tāni ca sattvarajastamaākhyaguṇatrayayuktāni | tathā śabdasparśarasarūpagandhānāṃ vijñānajanakāni śanaiḥ vedāntoktakrameṇa śrotratvakcakṣurjihvānāsikākhyāni buddhīndiyāṇi | caśabdāt pāyūpasthahastapādavāgākhyāni ca karmendriyāṇi indriyatvād viṣayāṇāṃ grahītṝṇi ity ukteḥ viṣayasṛṣṭir apy arthād uktaiva || 1.15 ||
teṣāṃ tv avayavān sūkṣmān ṣaṇṇām apy amitaujasām |
sanniveśyātmamātrāsu sarvabhūtāni nirmame || 1.16 ||
teṣām iti | teṣāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ pūrvaprayuktānāṃ mahataḥ śabdāditanmātrāṇāṃ ca amitaujasāṃ anantakāryanirvartanena ativīryatāṃ ye sūkṣmā avayavāḥ tān ātmamātrāsu adhikārabhāgeṣu yojayitvā sarvāṇi bhūtāni manuṣyādīni nirmitavān || 1.16 ||
yan mūrtyavayavāḥ sūkṣmās tānīmāny āśrayanti ṣaṭ |
tasmāc charīram ity āhus tasya mūrtiṃ manīṣiṇaḥ || 1.17 ||
yat mūrtyavayavā iti | yasmān mūrtisaṃpādakāḥ ākāranirvartakāḥ sūkṣmāḥ tanmātrāhaṃkārākhyāḥ avayavāḥ tasyātmano vikalpante | yasya saṃbandhena imāni vakṣyamāṇāni bhūtāny āśrayanti karyatvena | tasmāt tasyātmano yā mūrtiḥ svabhāvaḥ tām eva pariṇatāṃ śarīram iti laukikā bruvantīty evaṃ śarīranirvacanam uktakramotpattidārḍhyārtham || 1.17 ||
tad āviśanti bhūtāni mahānti saha karmabhiḥ |
manaś cāvayavaiḥ sūkṣmaiḥ sarvabhūtakṛt avyayam || 1.18 ||
tad iti | tad brahma tanmātrāhaṃkārāvastham anantaraṃ mahābhūtāny ākāśādīni āviśanti tanmātrebhyaḥ utpadyante | saha karmabhiḥ yathākramaṃ dhāraṇasaṃgrahapākavyūhanāvakāśadānākhyaiḥ tatkāryaiḥ manaś cāvayavaiḥ śubhāśubhasaṃkalpaiḥ sūkṣmaiḥ bahirdṛśyatvābhāvāt | sarvabhūtakṛt śubhāśubhasaṃkalpaprabhavakarmanimittatvāt jagataḥ avyayaṃ avināśi āviśanti ahaṃkārāt tasyotpatteḥ || 1.18 ||
teṣām idaṃ tu saptānāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ mahaujasām |
sūkṣmābhyo mūrtimātrābhyaḥ saṃbhavaty avyayād avyayam || 1.19 ||
teṣām iti | pūrvaprakṛtatvāt tanmātrāhaṃkārāṇāṃ pūrvataraprastutatvāc ca mahataḥ evaṃ teṣāṃ saptānāṃ puruṣotpannatvāt puruṣāṇāṃ sūkṣmābhyo mūrimātrābhyaḥ śarīrasaṃpādakebhyo bhāgebhyaḥ idaṃ jagat naśvaraṃ saṃbhavati avyayāt anaśvarāt yad yat kāryaṃ tat tad asthiraṃ yad yad utpattikāraṇaṃ tat tat kāryāpakṣayā sthiram ity etat pratipādanārthaḥ śeṣo ‘nuvādaḥ || 1.19 ||
ādyād yasya guṇaṃ tv eṣām avāpnoti paraḥ paraḥ |
yo yo yāvatithaś caiṣāṃ sa sa tāvadguṇaḥ smṛtaḥ || 1.20 ||
ādyād yasyeti | eṣāṃ pūrvataraślokaprastutānāṃ bhūtānāṃ madhyāt ādyasyādyasya ākāśādeḥ saṃbandhinaṃ śabdādikaṃ paraḥ paro vāyvādiḥ gṛhṇāti | ākāśādiguṇatvaṃ śabdādīnāṃ vakṣyate | tathā ākāśādiś ca kramaśaḥ | evam ākāśādivarjaṃ anyeṣāṃ dvaiguṇyaprāptāv āha  yo ya iti | eṣāṃ yo yo yāvatithaḥ yo yāvatāṃ pūraṇo dvitīyādiḥ sa tāvadguṇaḥ dviguṇas triguṇa ityevamādiḥ manvādibhiḥ smṛtaḥ || 1.20 ||
sarveṣāṃ tu sa nāmāni karmāṇi ca pṛthak pṛthak |
vedaśabdebhya evādau pṛthak saṃsthāś ca nimame || 1.21 ||
sarveṣām iti | sa brahmā sarveṣāṃ nāmāni brāhmaṇa ityevamādīni karmāṇi cādhyayanādīni pṛthak pṛthak yasya yāny eva pūrvakalpe ‘bhūvan tāni suptaprabuddhavat prākkalpādhītapratibhātavedaśabdebhya eva sṛṣṭyādau nirmitavān laukikīś ca vyavasthāḥ pṛthag iti || 1.21 ||
karmātmanāṃ ca devānāṃ so ‘sṛjat prāṇināṃ prabhuḥ |
sādhyānāṃ ca gaṇaṃ sūkṣmaṃ yajñaṃ caiva sanātanam || 1.22 ||
karmātmanām iti | karmāṅgabhūtānāṃ devānāṃ prāṇavatām indrādīnāṃ yataḥ aprāṇā api yakṣādayo devāḥ santi, sādhyānāṃ devagaṇānāṃ devaviśeṣāṇāṃ samūhaṃ jyotiṣṭomādikaṃ ca kalpāntare ‘py anuṣṭhānān nityaṃ sṛṣṭavān | sādhyagaṇasya sūkṣamatvāt pṛthaṅnirdeśaḥ || 1.22 ||
agnivāyuravibhyas tu trayaṃ brahma sanātanam |
dudoha yajñasiddhyartham ṛgyajuḥsāmalakṣaṇam || 1.23 ||
agnivāyuravibhya iti | ṛgyajuḥsāmākhyaṃ brahmatrayaṃ sanātanaṃ nityam api sūkṣmaṃ ādau daivyā śaktyā agnivāyuravibhyaḥ ākṛṣṭavān | yajñasiddhyartham, āgamikatvāc ca nātiśaṅkyam etat | tathā ca “ṛgveda evāgner ajāyata” ityādi brāhmaṇam || 1.23 ||
kālaṃ kālavibhaktīṃś ca nakṣatrāṇi grahāṃs tathā |
saritaḥ sāgarāñ chailān samāni viṣamāṇi ca || 1.24 ||
kālam iti | ādityādigatipratibandhapravṛttimān kālaḥ | kālavibhaktayo māsādyāḥ | nakṣatrāṇi aśvayukprabhṛtīni | grahāḥ arkādayaḥ | nadīsamudraparvatasamaviṣamasthānāni ca | atra sasarjety vakṣyamāṇakriyā || 1.24 ||
tapo vācaṃ ratiṃ caiva kāmaṃ ca krodham eva ca |
sṛṣṭiṃ sasarja caivemāṃ sraṣṭum icchann imāḥ prajāḥ || 1.25 ||
tapa iti | ratiḥ cetonirvṛttiḥ | imā vakṣyamānā daivādikāḥ prajāḥ etacchlokaoktāṃ pūrvapaṭhitāṃ ca sṛṣṭiṃ kṛtavān || 1.25 ||
karmaṇāṃ ca vivekāya dharmādharmau vyavecayat |
dvandvair ayojayac cemāḥ sukhaduḥkhādibhiḥ prajāḥ || 1.26 ||
karmaṇām iti | ayaṃ dharmaḥ atas tatsādhakaṃ dānādikarma kartavyam, ayaṃ punar adharmaḥ atas tatsaṃpādakaṃ cauryādi na kāryam ity evaṃ dānacauryādikarmaṇāṃ vibhāgāya dharmādharmau pṛthak pṛthak kṛtavān | dharmādharmaphalabhūtaiś ca dvandvaiḥ parasparaviruddhaiḥ putrotpattivināśādibhiḥ sukhaduḥkhaiḥ, ādiśabdāt kṣutsauhityādibhiś cemāḥ prajā yojitavān || 1.26 ||
aṇvyo mātrā vināśinyo daśārdhānāṃ tu yāḥ smṛtāḥ |
tābhiḥ sārdham idaṃ sarvaṃ saṃbhavaty anupūrvaśaḥ || 1.27 ||
anvya iti | pañcānāṃ tanmātrāṇāṃ yāḥ sūkṣmamātrāḥ bhāgāḥ vināśinyaḥ vipariṇāmitvāt | tābhiḥ sahoktavakṣyamāṇam idam utpadyate | krameṇa sarvatra tāsām anusṛtatvāt | tattvair vinaiva samanantaroktavakṣyamāṇā sṛṣṭir ity etad bhrāntinirākaraṇāya punas tattvodbhavatvaṃ sṛṣṭimadhye smārayati || 1.27 ||
yam tu karmaṇi yasmin sa nyayuṅkta prathamaṃ prabhuḥ |
sa tad eva svayaṃ bheje sṛjyamānaḥ punaḥ punaḥ || 1.28 ||
yam iti | yaṃ jātiviśeṣaṃ siṃhādikaṃ yasmin karmaṇi dviradamāraṇādau prajāpatiḥ sṛṣṭyādau niyuktavān | sa jātiviśeṣaḥ prathamasṛṣṭyuttarakālam api svārjitakarmavaśena yadā yadā sṛjyamāno bhavati tadā tadā tad eva dviradamāraṇādi svayam eva bhajate na bhūyaḥ prajāpatiniyogam apekṣate jātidharmatvena prajāpatinā tasya kṛtatvāt || 1.28 ||
hiṃsrāhiṃsre mṛdukrūre dharmādharmāv ṛtānṛte |
yad yasya so ‘dadāt sarge tat tasya svayam āviśat || 1.29 ||
etat prapañcārtham āha hiṃsrāhiṃsre iti | hiṃsaṃ karma vyāghrādeḥ, ahiṃsraṃ ruruprabhṛteḥ, mṛdu dayopetaṃ brāhmaṇādeḥ, krūraṃ kṣatriyādeḥ | dharmo yathā kākādeḥ parāpatyapoṣaṇādiḥ | yady api na tatas tasya dharmo bhavati paropakāratvāt dharmatvam upacaryate | adharmo yathā bakādeḥ | etad api pūrvavad upacārato vañcanāparatvād ucyate | ṛtaṃ devānām anṛtaṃ manuṣyāṇām, tad uktam – satyavāco devāḥ anṛtaṃ vai manuṣyā iti | yady api dharmādharmagrahaṇena etat sarvaṃ gataṃ tathāpi svarūpabhedāvalambanaṃ pṛthak pṛthak dvandvato ‘bhidhānam | teṣām anyat yad eva karma yasya prajāpatir adadāt dattavān sṛṣṭyādau tat tad eva sṛṣṭyuttarakāle ‘py asau jāyamānaḥ svayam eva bhajate | yathākarma sṛṣṭaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamam iti vakṣyati | ataḥ prākkalpārjitakarmavaśena yad yasya so ‘dadāt iti draṣṭavyam || 1.29 ||
yathartuliṅgāny ṛtavaḥ svayam eva tu paryaye |
svāni svāny abhipadyante tathā karmāṇi dehinaḥ || 1.30 ||
tatraiva dṛṣṭāntam āha yatheti | yathā vasantādayaḥ svāvasare svayam eva svāni karmāṇi cihnabhūtāni kurvanti tadvat karmāṇi hiṃsrādīni prāṇinaḥ || 1.30 ||
lokānāṃ tu vivṛddhyarthaṃ mukhabāhūrupādataḥ |
brāhmaṇaṃ kṣatriyaṃ vaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ ca niravartayat || 1.31 ||
lokānām iti | agnau prāsteti vakṣyamāṇanyāyena pṛthivyādilokabāhulyārthaṃ sukhādibhyo yathākramaṃ brāhmaṇādyān daivyā śaktyā nirmitavān | tathā ca brāhmaṇo ‘sya mukham āsīd ityādi liṅgam || 1.31 ||
dvidhā kṛtvātmano deham ardhena puruṣo ‘bhavat |
ardhena nārī tasyāṃ sa virājam asṛjat prabhuḥ || 1.32 ||
dvidheti | sa brahmā śarīraṃ dvibhāgaṃ kṛtvā ekenārdhena pumān saṃpannaḥ | apareṇa strī sa tasyāś ca sakāśāt virājākhyaṃ puruṣaṃ sṛṣṭavān || 1.32 ||
tapas taptvāsṛjad yaṃ tu sa svayaṃ puruṣo virāṭ |
taṃ māṃ vittāsya sarvasya sraṣṭāraṃ dvijasattamāḥ || 1.33 ||
tapa iti | sa anantarokto virāṭ tapaḥ kṛtvā yaṃ svayaṃ sṛṣṭvān taṃ māṃ jānīta | etasya sarvasyotpādayitāram | he dvijaśreṣṭhatamāḥ || 1.33 ||
ahaṃ prajāḥ sisṛkṣus tu tapas taptvā suduścaram |
patīn prajānām asṛjaṃ maharṣīn ādito daśa || 1.34 ||
ahaṃ prajāḥ sraṣṭum icchan suduścaraṃ tapaḥ kṭtvā prajāpatīn maharṣīn daśa prathamataḥ sṛṣṭavān || 1.34 ||
marīcim atryaṅgirasau pulastyaṃ pulahaṃ kratum |
pracetasaṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ ca bhṛguṃ nāradam eva ca || 1.35 ||
āha tāṃś ca – marīcim iti || 1.35 ||
ete manūṃs tu saptānyān asṛjan bhūritejasaḥ |
devān devanikāyāṃś ca maharṣīṃś cāmitaujasaḥ || 1.36 ||
ete iti | ete daśa bahutejaskāḥ saptānyān manūn sṛṣṭavantaḥ | tathā devān devanikāyān maharṣīṃś ca aparimitatejaskān | teṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ manūnāṃ manvantare ekaikasya sargādyadhikāraḥ || 1.36 ||
yakṣarakṣaḥpiśācāṃś ca gandharvāpsaraso ‘surān |
nāgān sarpān suparṇāṃś ca pitṝṇāṃ ca pṛthag gaṇān || 1.37 ||
yakṣarakṣaḥpiśācāṃś ceti | yakṣāḥ vaiśravaṇāmātyāḥ | rakṣāṃsi rāvaṇādīni | piśācāḥ marubhūmyādivāsinaḥ prāṇiviśeṣāḥ | gandarvāḥ citrasenādayaḥ | apsarasaḥ menakādyāḥ, asurā virocanādayaḥ | nāgāḥ takṣakādyāḥ sarpā ahayaḥ suparṇā garutmantaḥ pitṝṇāṃ somapādīnāṃ svasthānasamūhān asṛjan iti vartate | evam uttaratrāpi || 1.37 ||
vidyuto ‘śanimeghāṃś ca rohitendradhanūṃṣi ca |
ulkānirghātaketūṃś ca jyotīṃṣy uccāvacāni ca || 1.38 ||
vidyuta iti | rohitaṃ śastrotpāta it prasiddham | nānāvarṇa dhanūrūpaṃ indradhanuḥ | ulkā rekhākāram antarikṣāt patat jyotiḥ kadācid rātrau dṛśyate | nirghātaḥ kṣityartargataḥ utpātadhvaniḥ | ketavo daṇḍādyākārāṇi jyotīṃṣi | anyāny api jyotīṃṣi utkṛṣṭāpakṛṣṭāni || 1.38 ||
kinnarān vānarān matsyān vividhāṃś ca vihaṅgamān |
paśūn mṛgān manuṣyāṃś ca vyālāṃś cobhayatodataḥ || 1.39 ||
kinnarān iti | kinnarāḥ kinnaraśarīrāḥ aśvamukhāḥ anye tu viparītāḥ | vihaṅgamāḥ  pakṣiṇaḥ paśavo gavādyāḥ | manuṣyāṇāṃ paśutve ‘pi prādhānyāt pṛthak grahaṇam | vyālāḥ siṃhādyāḥ uttarādharadantayuktāḥ || 1.39 ||
kṛmikīṭapaṅgāṃś ca yūkāmakṣikamatkuṇam |
sarvaṃ ca daṃśamaśakaṃ sthāvaraṃ ca pṛthagvidham || 1.40 ||
kṛmikīṭapataṅgān iti | kṛmayo bhūmiśaraṇāḥ | kīṭāḥ kṛmibhyaḥ kiṃcitsthūlāḥ | pataṅgāḥ śalabhāḥ | sthāvaraṃ vṛkṣavanaspatyādibhedena nānāprakāram || 1.40 ||
evam etair idaṃ sarvaṃ manniyogān mahātmabhiḥ |
yathākarma tapoyogāt sṛṣṭaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamam || 1.41 ||
evam iti | anena prakāreṇa taiḥ munibhiḥ madājñayā sṛjyamānaprāṇikarmagatakarmānurūpeṇa idaṃ tapobalāt sṛṣṭam || 1.41 ||
yeṣāṃ tu yādṛśaṃ karma bhūtānām iha kīrtanam |
tat tathā vo ‘bhidhāsyāmi kramayogaṃ ca janmani || 1.42 ||
yeṣām iti | yeṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ yathārūpaṃ karma tat tahaiveha śāstre kīrtitam eva | yuṣmākaṃ yato nāmanirdeśād eva, devatādyotanāt dyutisthānād vā devatāḥ, yakṣaṇād yakṣāḥ ity evamādikarmapratipattiḥ | idānīṃ yena karmasaṃbandhena jāyante taṃ vakṣyāmīti kecit | atha vā yeṣāṃ yādṛśaṃ karma iha saṃsāre pūrvair api kathitaṃ yathā oṣadhyaḥ phalapākāntā ity evamādi tat tathā abhidhāsyāmi yathā kramayogaṃ ceti || 1.42 ||
paśavaś ca mṛgāś caiva vyālāś cobhayatodataḥ |
rakṣāṃsi ca piśācāś ca mānuṣāś ca jarāyujāḥ || 1.43 ||
paśava iti | jarāyugarbhasaṃpātaḥ tatrādau saṃbhavanti | tatas tanmuktā jāyante || 1.43 ||
aṇḍajāḥ pakṣiṇaḥ sarpā nakrā matyāś ca kacchapāḥ |
yāni caivaṃprakārāṇi sthalajāny audakāni ca || 1.44 ||
aṇḍajā iti | aṇḍe ādau saṃbhavanti | tato jāyante nakraḥ śiśumārākhyaḥ prāṇī | kacchapaḥ kūrmaḥ | sthalajāni kṛkalāsādīni | audakāni ca śaṅkhādīni || 1.44 ||
svedajaṃ daṃśamaśakaṃ yūkāmakṣikamatkuṇam |
ūṣmanaś copajāyante yac cānyat kiṃcid īdṛśam || 1.45 ||
svedajam iti | svedaḥ pārtivadravyāṇāṃ antaḥkledaḥ | anyad api ca yadṛśamaśakādisadṛśaṃ pipīlikādi tat | yūkā matkuṇāḥ svedād eva jāyante || 1.45 ||
udbhijjāḥ sthāvarāḥ sarve bījakāṇḍaprarohiṇaḥ |
oṣadhyaḥ phalapākāntā bahupuṣpaphalopagāḥ || 1.46 ||
udbhijjāstarava iti | ūrdhvaṃ bhuvaṃ bhittvā vṛkṣā jāyante | te tu kecid bījād eva jāyante | anye tu bījāt kāṇḍāc ca | śākhāḥ (śākhaiḥ) bahirbhūmau ropitā vṛkṣībhavanti | adhunā yeṣāṃ tu yādṛśaṃ karmeti yad uktaṃ tad darśayati – oṣadhya iti | oṣadhīnāṃ vrīhyādikānāṃ phalapākenaivāntaḥ tena tāḥ śuṣyanti | bahupuṣpaphalam upagacchanti bahunā saṃyuktā bhavanti || 1.46 ||
apuṣpāḥ phalavanto ye te vanaspatayaḥ smṛtāḥ |
puṣpiṇaḥ phalinaś caiva vṛkṣās tūbhayataḥ smṛtāḥ || 1.47 ||
apuṣpā iti | vanaspatīnāṃ puṣpam antareṇaiva phalāni bhavanti | vṛkṣāḥ punaḥ puṣpaphalenobhayenāpi yuktā bhavanti || 1.47 ||
gucchagulmaṃ tu vividhaṃ tathaiva tṛṇajātayaḥ |
bījakāṇḍaruhāṇy eva pratānā vallya eva ca || 1.48 ||
gucchagulmam iti | mūlata eva yeṣāṃ latāvṛndaṃ bhavati | na tu prakāṇḍāni te gucchāḥ mallikādyāḥ | gulmās tu aspaṣṭahrasvaprāyalatātmakāḥ aprakāṇḍāḥ puṣpakādyāḥ | tṛṇajātayo dūrvādyāḥ pratānā tanturūpāḥ trapuṣālābuprabhṛtayaḥ vallyaḥ vratatyo gūḍucyādayaḥ || 1.48 ||
tamasā bahurūpeṇa veṣṭitāḥ karmahetunā |
antaḥsaṃjñā bhavanty ete sukhaduḥkhasamanvitāḥ || 1.49 ||
tamaseti | sattvarajastama ākhyā guṇāḥ | tatraite vṛkṣādayaḥ padarthā vividhaduḥkhotpādanarūpeṇa prāgjanmārjitaduṣṭakarmahetunā tamasā vyāptā bhavanti | yady api sarva eva kvacid antar eva cetayante tathāpy ete saṃjñākāryasya vyavahārādeḥ bahirabhāvād antaḥsaṃjñā ucyate | sattvarajoleśasyāpi sadbhāvān sukhaduḥkhānvitā api bhavanti || 1.49 ||
etadantās tu gatayo brahmādyāḥ samudāhṛtāḥ |
ghore ‘smin bhūtasaṃsāre nityaṃ satatayāyini || 1.50 ||
etadantā iti | sthāvarāvasānāḥ brahmapradhānāḥ saṃsārotpattayo nityaṃ ghore bhīṣaṇe ‘smin bhūtnānāṃ kṣetrajñānāṃ saṃsāre bahuśaḥ śarīrādānatyāgalakṣaṇāḥ sarvā naśvarā uktāḥ || 1.50 ||
evaṃ sarvaṃ sa sṛṣṭvedaṃ māṃ cācintyaparākramaḥ |
ātmany antardadhe bhūyaḥ kālaṃ kālena pīḍayan || 1.51 ||
evaṃ sṛṣṭiṃ pradarśya idānīṃ pralayaṃ darśayitum āha – sarvam evam iti | anena prakāreṇa idaṃ māṃ ca sṛṣṭvā sa prajāpatiḥ acintyaśaktiḥ punaḥ ātmany evāntardhānaṃ kṛtavān | śarīratyāgena sṛṣṭikālaṃ pralayakālena vināśayan prāṇināṃ karmavaśataḥ svecchayā ca punaḥ punaḥ sargasaṃhārau karotīty arthaḥ || 1.51 ||
yadā sa devo jāgarti tad evaṃ ceṣṭate jagat |
yadā svapiti śāntātmā tadā sarvaṃ nimīlati || 1.52 ||
yadā sa deva iti | yadā sa prajāpatir icchati tad evaṃ jagat utpattyavasthitī karoti | yadā tu necchati śāntātmā upasaṃhṛtavyāpāramanāḥ tad evaṃ pralīyate | antarālapralayas tadā bhavati | yathā śarīre yadā kṣetrajño jāgarti tadā paśupakṣisarīsṛpātmakaṃ jagat ceṣṭate ceṣṭāyuktaṃ bhavati | yadā svapiti tadā niśceṣṭam iti jāgratsvapnasuṣuptyākhyā ātmavṛttayo ‘tra prajāpatikṛtānāṃ jāgratsvapasuṣuptavṛttīnāṃ sthityantarālapralayam āha, pralayakāraṇabhūtānāṃ dṛṣṭāntatvenopapatteḥ || 1.52 ||
tasmin svapati tu svasthe karmātmānaḥ śarīriṇaḥ |
svakarmabhyo nivartante manaś ca glānim ṛcchati || 1.53 ||
pūrvoktam eva spaṣṭayitum āha – tasmin svapati tu svasthe iti | tasmin nivṛttisthe svasthe upasaṃhṛtavyāpāramanaske karmākṣiptaśarīrāḥ śarīriṇaḥ kṣetrajñāḥ svakarmabhyaḥ śarīragrahaṇādibhyo nivartante | manaś ca sarvendriyarahitaṃ nirvyāpāraṃ bhavati || 1.53 ||
yugapat tu pralīyante yadā tasmin mahātmani |
tadāyaṃ sarvabhūtātmā sukhaṃ svapiti nirvṛtaḥ || 1.54 ||
idānīṃ mahāpralayam āha – yugapad iti | tulyakālam eva punaḥ yasmin kāle tasmin parameśvare sarvabhūtāni pralīyante tasminn eva kāle ayaṃ sarvabhūtātmabhūto nirvṛtaḥ nivṛttajāgratsvapna(suṣupti)vyāpāraḥ sukhaṃ svapiti suṣuptim anubhavati | tatra suṣuptāvasthā mahāpralayaś ca yugapad eva bhavata ity arthaḥ || 1.54 ||
tamo ‘yaṃ samāśritya ciraṃ tiṣṭhati sendriyaḥ |
na ca svaṃ kurute karma tadotkrāmati mūrtitaḥ || 1.55 ||
pralayaprasaṅgena ca kṣetrajñotkramaṇam āha – tamo yad iti | sa ātmā atyantavyāmohavyāptaḥ san yadā sendriyo ‘py analpam kālam āste indriyāṇāṃ śarīravināśe ‘py avināśena ca śvāsapraśvāsādikaṃ karma kurute tadā śarīraṃ jahāti || 1.55 ||
yadāṇumātriko bhūtvā bījaṃ sthāsnu cariṣṇu ca |
samāviśati saṃsṛṣṭas tadā mūrtiṃ vimuñcati || 1.56 ||
kiṃ śarīrāntaranirapekṣa eva mūrtiṃ parityjatīty āha – yadeti | yadā sūkṣmapramāṇaḥ san sthāvarabījaṃ jaṅgamabījaṃ vā anyad adhitiṣṭhati tadā idaṃ śarīraṃ tyajati | saṃsṛṣṭaḥ prāṇādisaṃyuktaḥ, yathoktaṃ bṛhadāraṇyake: “tam utkrāmantaṃ prāṇo ‘nūtkrāmati prāṇam anūtkrāmantaṃ sarve prāṇā anūtkrāmanti” iti || 1.56 ||
evaṃ sa jāgratsvapnābhyām idaṃ sarvaṃ carācaram |
saṃjīvayati cājasraṃ pramāpayati cāvyayaḥ || 1.57 ||
evam iti | sa brahmā anena prakāreṇa ātmīyābhyāṃ jāgratsvapnābhyāṃ jaṅgamasthāvaram ajasraṃ anavarataṃ jīvayati mārayati cānaśvaraḥ || 1.57 ||
idaṃ śāstraṃ tu kṛtvāsau mām eva svayam āditaḥ |
vidhivad grāhayāmāsa marīcyādīṃs tv ahaṃ munīn || 1.58 ||
idam iti | sa brahmā idaṃ śāstraṃ imaṃ granthaṃ kṛtvā śāstrasya “idaṃ kartavyam idaṃ na” ity evaṃ svarūpanityatvān mām eva ātmanādau yatnato ‘dhyāpitavān | tato marīcyādīn munīn aham adhyāpitavān || 1.58 ||
etad vo ‘yaṃ bhṛguḥ śāstraṃ śrāvatiṣyaty aśeṣataḥ |
etad dhi matto ‘dhijage sarvam eṣo ‘khilaṃ muniḥ || 1.59 ||
etad iti | ayaṃ bhṛguḥ etac chāstraṃ yuṣmākam aśeṣaṃ śrāvayiṣyati yasmād etat sarvaṃ matsakāśād eṣo ‘khilaṃ kṛtvā adhigatavān || 1.59 ||
tatas tathā sa tenokto maharṣir manunā bhṛguḥ |
tān abravīd ṛṣīn sarvān prītātmā śrūyatām iti || 1.60 ||
tata iti | sa bhṛguḥ manunā tathoktaḥ ayaṃ śrāvayiṣyatīty anena prakāreṇārthād ukto na tu tvaṃ śrāvayeti | tato ‘nantaraṃ sa (guruḥ) bhṛguḥ saṃbhāvanayā prītātmā tān ṛṣīn śrūyatām ity uvāca || 1.60 ||
svāyaṃbhuvasyāsya manoḥ ṣaḍ vaṃśyā manavo ‘pare |
sṛṣṭavantaḥ prajāḥ svāḥ svā mahātmāno mahaujasaḥ || 1.61 ||
svāyaṃbhuvasyeti | asyoktaprakāreṇa svayaṃbhūnaptur manoḥ ṣaḍ anye manavaḥ kāle kāle sṛṣṭyādau adhikṛtā vaṃśyāḥ tatkulodbhūtāḥ tadekakāryāḥ svāh svāḥ prajā utpāditavantaḥ || 1.61 ||
svārociṣaś cottamaś ca tāmaso raivatas tathā |
cākṣuṣaś ca mahātejā vivasvatsuta eva ca || 1.62 ||
te ca – svārociṣa iti || 1.62 ||
svāyaṃbhuvād yāḥ saptaite manavo bhūritejasaḥ |
sve sve ‘ntare sarvam idam utpādyāpuś carācaram || 1.63 ||
svāyaṃbhuvād yā iti | anantaroktāḥ ṣaṭ svāyaṃbhuvaś ca evaṃ sapta | ete svāyaṃbhuvapramukhāḥ manavo bahutejasaḥ | ātmīyātmīyādhikārakāle idaṃ jaṅgamasthāvaram utpādya āpuḥ prajāḥ pālitavantaḥ || 1.63 ||
nimeṣā daśa cāṣṭau ca kāṣṭhā triṃśat tu tāḥ kalā |
triṃśatkalā muhūrtaḥ syād ahorātraṃ tu tāvataḥ || 1.64 ||
kiyān punaḥ kālaḥ pratyekaṃ manūnām adhikāraḥ ity at āha – nimeṣā iti | nimeṣaḥ akṣipakṣmaṇoḥ svābhāvikaḥ kalpaḥ unmeṣasahacārī | te aṣṭādaśa kāṣṭhā | tiṃsatyaḥ kāṣṭhāḥ kalānāma kālaḥ triṃśatkalāḥ muhūrtasaṃjñaḥ | ahorātraṃ tu tāvataḥ triṃṣat | muhūrtās tāvantaḥ iti | dvitīyānirdeśāt vidyādity adhyāhāryam || 1.64 ||
ahorātre vibhajate sūryo mānuṣadaivike |
rātriḥ svapnāya bhūtānāṃ ceṣṭāyai karmaṇām ahaḥ || 1.65 ||
ahorātre iti | devamānuṣasaṃbandhinī ahorātre sūryo vibhajate ādityo vibhāgīkaroti | tataś ca bhūtānāṃ rātriḥ svapnārthaṃ bhavati | karmodyamārthaṃ tv ahaḥ || 1.65 ||
pitrye rātryahnī māsaḥ pravibhāgas tu pakṣayoḥ |
karmaceṣṭāsv ahaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ śuklaḥ svapnāya śarvarī || 1.66 ||
pitrye iti | mānuṣyo māsaḥ pitṝṇām ahorātram | tatra ca pakṣābhyāṃ vibhāgaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ pakṣo ‘haḥ karmodyamārthaṃ śuklaḥ pakṣaḥ punaḥ svāpārthaṃ rātriḥ || 1.66 ||
daive rātryahanī varṣaṃ pravibhāgas tayoḥ punaḥ |
ahas tatrodagayanaṃ rātriḥ syād dakṣiṇāyanam || 1.67 ||
daive iti | mānuṣyaṃ varṣaṃ devānām ahorātram | tatra cottarāyaṇam aho dakṣiṇāyanaṃ rātrir iti vibhāgaḥ || 1.67 ||
brāhmasya tu kṣapāhasya yat pramāṇaṃ samāsataḥ |
ekaikaśo yugānāṃ tu kramaśas tan nibodhata || 1.68 ||
brāhmasyeti | prājāpatyasyāhorātrasya yat pramāṇaṃ yugānāṃ ca pratyekaṃ tat krameṇa saṃkṣepataḥ śrṛṇuta || 1.68 ||
catvāry āhuḥ sahasrāṇi varṣāṇāṃ tatkṛtaṃ yugam |
tasya tāvacchatī saṃdhyā saṃdhyāṃśaś ca tathāvidhaḥ || 1.69 ||
catvārīti | daivikānām anantaraprastutatvāt, purāṇe ca divyenaiva pramāṇena yugasaṃkhyā prakīrtiteti darśanāt, daivikāni catvāri varṣasahasrāṇi kṛtayugaṃ nāma kālam āhur manvādayḥ | tasya ca tāvanty eva varṣaśatāni saṃdhyā atītāgāmiyugdharmānuvartanaṃ saṃdhyāṃśe ‘tītayugadharmāṇāṃ īṣadanuvṛttiḥ āgāmiyugadharmabāhulyaṃ catvāry eva śatāni ca || 1.69 ||
itareṣu sasaṃdhyeṣu sasaṃdhyāṃśeṣu ca triṣu |
ekāpāyena vartante sahasrāṇi śatāni ca || 1.70 ||
itareṣv iti | tretādiṣu punas triṣu yugeṣu saṃdhyāsaṃdhyāṃśasahiteṣu ekaikahānyā sahasrāṇi śatāni ca bhavanti | tretā trīṇi sahasrāṇi, tat saṃdhyāsaṃdhyāṃśaiś ca trīṇi trīṇi śatāni | evaṃ dvāparakaliyugayoḥ samdhyāsaṃdhyāṃśasahitayoḥ pūrvasmād pūrvasmād ekaikasahasrśatāpāyo draṣṭavyaḥ || 1.70 ||
yad etat parisaṃkhyātam ādāv eva caturyugam |
etad dvādaśasāhasraṃ devānāṃ yugam ucyate || 1.71 ||
yad iti | yad etat parigaṇitm ādāv asmāt ślokāt prāk caturyugam etad dvādaśsahasraguṇaṃ devayugaṃ vedayugaṃ nāma kālaḥ || 1.71 ||
daivikānāṃ yugānāṃ tu sahasraṃ parisaṃkhyayā |
brāhmam ekam ahar jñeyaṃ tāvatī rātrir eva ca || 1.72 ||
daivikānām iti | eṣāṃ devayugānāṃ sahasraṃ brāhmam ahar vijñeyam | sahasram eva rātrisaṃkhyayety anuvādaḥ || 1.72 ||
tad vai yugasahasrāntaṃ brāhmaṃ puṇyam ahar viduḥ |
rātriṃ ca tāvatīm eva te ‘horātravido janāḥ || 1.73 ||
tad iti | yugasahasreṇānto yasya tat prājāpatyam ahaḥ tadvedanasya puṇyahetutvāt puṇyaṃ rātriṃ ca tat parimāṇato ye vijānanti te ahorātrajñaninaḥ, na tu manuṣyādyahorātravedaneneti | brahmāharvijñānapraśaṃsā tadvedanāprarocanārtham || 1.73 ||
tasya so ‘harniśasyānte prasuptaḥ pratibudhyate |
pratibuddhaś ca sṛjati manaḥ sadasadātmakam || 1.74 ||
tasyeti | sa brahmā tasyāhorātrasyānte suptaḥ san pratibudhyate | tato mana ātmīyaṃ sṛjati | lokapiṇḍasṛṣṭyarthaṃ niyuṅkte | na tūtpadayati | asmin antarālapralayāpagame, tasya mahāpralayāntaram utpannatvāt | tathā ca “manaḥ sisṛkṣayā yuktaḥ sargāya nidadhe punaḥ” iti purāṇe uktam || 1.74 ||
manaḥ sṛṣṭiṃ vikurute codyamānaṃ sisṛkṣyā |
ākāśaṃ jāyate tasmāt tasya śabdaṃ guṇaṃ viduḥ || 1.75 ||
mana iti | tan manaḥ prajāpatisaṃbandhi tadaiva sraṣṭum icchayā preryamāṇaṃ sṛṣṭiṃ kurute || 1.75 ||
ākāśāt tu vikurvāṇāt sarvagandhavahaḥ śuciḥ |
balavāñ jāyate vāyuḥ sa vai sparśaguṇo mataḥ || 1.76 ||
ākāśāt tv iti | ākāśāt sva(sa)vikārajananonmukhībhūtāt vāyuḥ surabhyasurabhigandhavahaḥ śuciḥ pavitro balavāṃś ca jāyate | sa ca sparśaguṇayuktaḥ ṛṣīṇāṃ mataḥ || 1.76 ||
vāyor api vikurvāṇād virociṣṇu tamonudam |
jyotir utpadyate bhāsvat tad rūpaguṇam ucyate || 1.77 ||
vāyor iti | vāyoḥ pūrvavat tejaḥ virociṣṇu paraprakāśakaṃ tamonāśanaṃ bhāsvat prakāśakam utpadyate | tac ca svarūpākhyaguṇayuktaṃ kathyate || 1.77 ||
jyotiṣaś ca vikurvāṇād āpo rasaguṇāḥ smṛtāḥ |
adbhyo gandhaguṇā bhūmir ity eṣā sṛṣṭir āditaḥ || 1.78 ||
jyotiṣa iti | tejasaḥ pūrvavat | āpaḥ rasākhyaguṇayuktā jāyanta iti smṛtāḥ manvādibhiḥ | adbhyaś ca gandhākhyaguṇayuktā bhūmir jāyate | ity eṣā antarālapralayānantaraṃ prathamataḥ sṛṣṭiḥ || 1.78 ||
yat prāg dvādaśasāhasram uditaṃ daivikaṃ yugam |
tad ekasaptatiguṇaṃ manvantaram ihocyate || 1.79 ||
yad iti | yad etatc caturyugaṃ dvāparaparimāṇaṃ daivayugam uktaṃ tad ekasaptatiguṇaṃ manvantarākhyaḥ kālaḥ iha śāstre ucyate | etāvān kālaḥ ekasya satoḥ sargādyadhikāraḥ || 1.79 ||
manvantrāṇy asaṃkhyāni sargaḥ saṃhāra eva ca |
krīḍann ivaitat kurute parameṣṭhī punaḥ punaḥ || 1.80 ||
yady api purāṇe caturdaśa manvantarāṇi saṃkhyāyante tathāpi āvṛttyā asaṃkhyāni yadā bhavanti tadā sṛṣṭisaṃhārau bhavataḥ | etac ca sṛṣṭisaṃhārākhyaṃ vastu parame anāvṛttilakṣaṇe sthāne sthitatvāt parameṣṭhī prajāpatiḥ krīḍann ivaitat punaḥ punaḥ kurute | na kevalaṃ tadicchaivātra kāraṇaṃ yāvat prāṇikarmāṇy apīty ataḥ evaśabdaḥ || 1.80 ||
catuṣpāt sakalo dharmaḥ satyaṃ caiva kṛte yuge |
nādharmeṇāgamaḥ kaścin manuṣyān upavartate || 1.81 ||
catuṣpād iti | yo ‘yaṃ catuṣpādāśrayo dhamaḥ sakala eva | tathā satyaṃ ca kṛtayuge āsīt | satyasya prādhānyāt pṛthak grahaṇam | evaṃ ca śastrātikrameṇa vidyāyāḥ dhanasya vā āgamaḥ kaścin manuṣyān prati na vartate || 1.81 ||
itareṣv āgamād dharmaḥ pādaśas tv avaropitaḥ |
caurikānṛtamāyābhir dharmaś cāpaiti pādaśaḥ || 1.82 ||
itareṣv iti | tretādiṣu punar āgamāt śāstrād dharmo yāgādyanuṣṭhānaṃ yathākramaṃ pratiyugaṃ pādaṃ pādam avaropitaṃ hīnaṃ kṛtaṃ cauryāsatyachadmabhiḥ nātra yugaiḥ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ cauryādīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ sarvayugeṣu darśanāt | evaṃ ca pādaśo ‘nuṣṭhāṇāpagamāt tatsādhyo ‘py abhyudayahetuḥ mukhyo dharmaḥ pādaśo vyapagacchati || 1.82 ||
arogāḥ sarvasiddhārthāś caturvarṣaśatāyuṣaḥ |
kṛte tretādiṣu hy eṣāṃ vayo hriyati pādaśaḥ || 1.83 ||
arogā iti | arogāḥ roganimittasyādharmasyābhāvāt, sarvasiddhārthāḥ siddhasakalapāmyaphalāḥ | pratibandhakasyādharmasyābhāvāt caturvarṣaśatāyuṣaḥ purāṇadṛṣṭyā | tad āha svābhāvikam etad āyuḥ ata āyuḥ prāpakakarmakaraṇena adhikāyuṣo ‘pi bhavanti | tena daśavarṣasahasrāṇi rāmo rājyam akārayat, ity avirodhaḥ | “śatāyur vāi puruṣaḥ” ityādiśrutau ca śataśabdo bahvarthaḥ | śatasahasraśabdayoḥ bahunāmasv āmnātatvāt | evaṃvidhā manuṣyāḥ krte bhavanti | tretādiṣu  punar eṣāṃ pādaṃ pādaṃ vayaḥ alpībhavati || 1.83 ||
vedoktam āyur martyānām āśiṣaś caiva karmaṇām |
phalanty anuyugaṃ loke prabhāvaś ca śarīriṇām || 1.84 ||
vedoktam iti | “śatāyur vai puruṣaḥ” iti | evaṃ yadi vedoktam āyuḥ karmaṇāṃ kāmyānāṃ pradhānānāṃ prabhāvaś ca samṛddhyati | śatayogo manuṣyāṇāṃ yugānurūpeṇa phalati || 1.84 ||
anye kṛtayuge dharmās tretāyāṃ dvāpare pare |
anye kaliyuge nṝṇāṃ yugahrāsānurūpataḥ || 1.85 ||
anya iti | kṛtādiṣu yugeṣu yugānurūpeṇa dharmavailakṣaṇyaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ bhavati || 1.85 ||
tapaḥ paraṃ kṛtayuge tretāyāṃ jñānam ucyate |
dvāpare yajñam evāhur dānam ekaṃ kalau yuge || 1.86 ||
tathā ca tapa iti | tapaḥ kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādi kṛtayuge paraṃ pradhānato ‘nuṣṭhīyate manuṣyaiḥ, yugasvābhāvyāt | ātmajñānayajñadānānām anuṣaṅgataḥ | evaṃ tretāyāṃ jñānaṃ paraṃ tapaḥprabhṛtīni tu ānuṣaṅgikāni | evaṃ dvāpare yajñam āhuḥ dānam kalāv iti || 1.86 ||
sarvasyāsya tu sargasya guptyarthaṃ sa mahādyutiḥ |
mukhabāhūrupajjānāṃ pṛthak karmāṇy akalpayat || 1.87 ||
sarvasyeti | sa mahādīptir brahmā asya sarvasya jagataḥ “agnau prāstāhutiḥ” ityādinyāyena rakṣārthaṃ mukhādijātānāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ pṛthak pṛthak karmāṇi adṛṣṭārthāni vṛttyarthāni ca kalpitavān | etāni sṛṣṭiśeṣatvenehābhidhīyante | tathā ca sarvasyāsya tu sargasya guptyartham ity uktam | vidhis tv eṣām asyārthavādaprakaraṇatvād uttaratra bhaviṣyati || 1.87 ||
adhyāpanam adhyayanaṃ yajanaṃ yājanaṃ tathā |
dānaṃ pratigrahaṃ caiva brāhmaṇānām akalpayat || 1.88 ||
tatra adhyāpanam iti | adhyayanādhyāpane sāṅgavedasmṛtīnāṃ paṭhanapravacane | yajanayājane yāgārtvijye | annodakāni [datvā] yathāśaktyā prāṇinām artyapaharaṇaṃ dānam | sādhutaḥ pratigrahaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ kalpitavān || 1.88 ||
prajānāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ dānam ijyādhyayanam eva ca |
viṣayeṣv aprasaktiś ca kṣatriyasya samāsataḥ || 1.89 ||
prajānām iti | ijyā yāgaḥ | viṣayeṣu stryādigatarūpādiṣu | atyantam eva parihāraḥ | samāsataḥ saṃkṣepeṇa || 1.89 ||
paśūnāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ dānam ijyādhyayanam eva ca |
vaṇikpathaṃ kusīdaṃ ca vaiśyasya kṛṣim eva ca || 1.90 ||
vaṇikpathaṃ sthapathavāripathādinā vāṇijyāyai gamanam | kusīdaṃ vṛddhyarthaṃ dhanaprayogaḥ || 1.90 ||
ekam eva tu śūdrasya prabhuḥ karma samādiśat |
eteṣām eva varṇānāṃ śuśrūṣām anasūyayā || 1.91 ||
ekam iti | sa prabhuḥ anindayā brāhmaṇādiparicaraṇaṃ karmādiṣṭavān || 1.91 ||
ūrdhvaṃ nābher medhyataraḥ puruṣaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
tasmān medhyatamaṃ tv asya mukham uktaṃ svayambhuvā || 1.92 ||
idānīṃ brāhmaṇādikarmavivekārthatvamukhena śāstrasya stutim adhyayanaśravaṇānuṣṭhānaphalāṃ kartuṃ brāhmaṇastutim āha – ūrdhvam iti | puṃsaḥ sarvato ‘pi [medhyatvasya] siddhatve sati nābher ūrdhvaṃ medhyataratvaṃ tato ‘pi mukhasya medhyatamatvaṃ svayaṃbhuvoktam || 1.92 ||
uttamāṅgodbhāvāj jyaiṣṭhyād brahmaṇaś caiva dharaṇāt |
sarvasyaivāsya sargasya dharmato brāhmaṇaḥ prabhuḥ || 1.93 ||
tataḥ kim ata āha – uttamāṅgodbhavād iti | mūrdhodbhavatvāt pūrvotpannatvāt pravacanādiyuktatvāt sātiśayavedadhāraṇāt sarvasya jagato dharmānuśāsane brāhmaṇaḥ prabhuḥ || 1.93 |||
taṃ hi svayambhūḥ svād āsyāt tapas taptvādito ‘sṛjat |
havyakavyābhivāhyāya sarvasyāsya ca guptaye || 1.94 ||
kasya punar ayam uttamāṅgād udbhūta ity ata āha – tam iti | taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ svayaṃbhūḥ ātmamukhāt tapaḥ kṛtvā devapitrannavahanārthaṃ jagadrakṣārthaṃ ca prathamasṛṣṭau sṛṣṭavān || 1.94 ||
yasyāsyena sadāśnanti havyāni tridivaukasaḥ |
kavyāni caiva pitaraḥ kiṃ bhūtam adhikaṃ tataḥ || 1.95 ||
evaṃ ca yasyeti | yasya brāhmaṇākhyasya mahātmano mukhena mahāprabhāvāḥ tridivaukasaḥ svargavāsino devāḥ havyāni anavaratam aśnanti pitaraś ca kavyāni tato ‘nyat prakṛṣṭaṃ kiṃ bhūtaṃ syāt || 1.95 ||
bhūtānāṃ prāṇinaḥ śreṣṭhāḥ prāṇināṃ buddhijīvinaḥ |
buddhimatsu narāḥ śeṣṭhā nareṣu brāhmaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ || 1.96 ||
adhunā vidvattādiguṇayogena brāhmaṇaṃ stotum āha – bhūtānām iti | bhūtānāṃ sthāvarajaṅgamānāṃ madhyāt prāṇināṃ sarveṣāṃ guṇavattve ‘pi ye tūdriktacaitanyā vṛścikādayaḥ | te sthāvarebhyaḥ taduttarasukhānubhavāt śreṣṭhāḥ | tebhyo ‘pi śvādayo buddhijīvinaḥ nirannasānnadeśagamāgamakāraṇāt | tebhyo ‘pi manuṣyāḥ viśiṣṭhavijñānāt | tebhyo ‘pi brāhmaṇāḥ apavargādhikārayogyatvāt || 1.96 ||
brāhmaṇeṣu ca vidvāṃso vidvatsu kṛtabuddhayaḥ |
kṛtabuddhiṣu kartāraḥ kartṛṣu brahmavedinaḥ || 1.97 ||
brāhmaṇeṣv iti | tebhyo ‘pi vidvāṃsaḥ mahāphalakarmādhikārāt tebhyo ‘pi kṛtabuddhayaḥ śāstrārthānuṣṭhāneṣūtpannasaṃkalpāḥ | tebhyo ‘y anuṣṭhātāraḥ iṣṭāniṣṭaprāptiparihārayuktatvāt | tebhyo ‘pi jñātātmānaḥ apavargaphalatvāt || 1.97 ||
utpattir eva viprasya mūrtir dharmasya śāśvatī |
sa hi dharmārtham utpanno brahmabhūyāya kalpate || 1.98 ||
kiṃ ca utpattir iti | janmamātram eva brāhmaṇasya avinaśvaraṃ dharmasya śarīram, yasmād asau dharmārtham utpannaḥ upanītaḥ san ātmajñānena brahmatvāya saṃpadyate || 1.98 ||
brāhmaṇo jāyamāno hi pṛthivyām adhijāyate |
īśvaraḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ dharmakoṣasya guptaye || 1.99 ||
brāhmaṇ iti | yasmād brāhmaṇa utpadyamānaḥ dharmasaṃcayarakṣaṇārthaṃ sarvabhūtānām īśvaraḥ prabhuḥ san pṛthivyām adhikaḥ ekaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ śuklaiḥ guṇaiḥ jāyate tasmād asau mūrtiḥ dharmasya śāsvatī pūrvoktahetur ayam | kośo dhanasaṃcayaḥ (na) ihopacārāt kośaśbdaḥ | tadgoptṛtvaṃ ca brāhmaṇopadeśakatvāt sarvadharmāṇām || 1.99 ||
sarvaṃ svaṃ brāhmaṇasyedaṃ yat kiṃcij jagatīgatam |
śraiṣṭyenābhijanenedaṃ sarvaṃ vai brāhmaṇo ‘rhati || 1.100 ||
sarvaṃ svam iti | vakṣyamāṇasteyādiśāstravirodhāt asya ca śrutipradhānatvāt sarvaṃ jagadgataṃ dravyajātaṃ brāhmaṇasvam iva stutyocyate | tatra svayam eva yasmāt śraiṣṭhenābhijanena mukhodbhavatvena sarvaṃ brāhmaṇo yogyo bhavati svīkartuṃ tasmāt tasyeyaṃ sarvaṃ svam eva || 1.100 ||
svam eva brāhmaṇo bhuṅkte svaṃ vaste svaṃ dadāti ca |
ānṛśaṃsyād brāhmaṇasya bhuñjate hītare janāḥ || 1.101 ||
evaṃ ca svam iti | yat parakīyasyāpi bhojanācchādanādi asau karoti tat tasya svam eveti pūrvavat stutiḥ | evaṃ cānṛśaṃsyāt tadgatakāruṇyād anye janā bhojanācchādanādi kurvate || 1.101 ||
tasya karmavivekārthaṃ śeṣāṇāṃ cānupūrvaśaḥ |
svāyambhuvo manur dhīmān idaṃ śāstram akalpayat || 1.102 ||
adhunoktabrāhmaṇastuteḥ phalam āha – tasyeti | īdṛg etan mahacchāstraṃ yad uktalakṣaṇasya brāhmaṇasya pradhānataḥ kāryākāryavivekārthaṃ śeṣāṇāṃ kṣatriyādisaṃkaraparyantānām anuṣaṅgataḥ tadvivedārtham etat prajāpatinaptā praśastabuddhimān manuḥ kalpitavān || 1.102 ||
viduṣā brāhmaṇenedam adhyetavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
śiṣyebhyaś ca pravaktavyaṃ samyaṅ nānyena kenacit || 1.103 ||
viduṣeti | hitāhitānīcchatā tajjñena brāhmaṇenedaṃ śāstraṃ prayatnenādhyetavyam iti brāhmaṇasya prayatnādhyayanārtho ‘yam ārambhaḥ | prayatnagrahaṇāt kṣatriyavaiśyayor apy atrādhyayanādhikāraḥ | tathā ca “niṣekādiśmaśānānta” ityādi vakṣyati | na tu brāhmaṇavad yatnātiśayaḥ | na ca tenaitat brāhmaṇasyāpi viddhyati, tatra brāhmaṇaviśeṣaṇasy prayatnaśabdasyānupādānāt, na ca kṣatriyavaiśyayor apy anenaiva siddhyati | niṣekādīty etadanārambhe prayatnaśabdasyaivaṃvidhaviśeṣapratipādanāsāmarthāt | na hīha kṣatriyavaiśyagrahaṇama asti | sati niṣekādiśāstre ‘smiṃś ca etal labhyate dvijātimātrasyādhyayanādhikāro brāhmaṇasya tu prayatnavān | iti | śiṣyebhyaś ca brāhmaṇenaitat pravaktavyaṃ na kṣatriyādineti | brāhmaṇasyaitacchāsrādhyāpanādhikārartho ‘yam ārambhaḥ anyasyādhyāpananiṣedharthaś ca | yathā [yat tu] arthavādatvam asya ślokasya kaiścid uktam | tad asiddham arthapratīteḥ | arthasya cārthavādatve sati prayatnakṛto viśeṣo na bhavati | tan mā nāmātra bhūt brāhmaṇasyaitacchāstrādhyāpanādhikāro ‘nyasya niṣedha ity ayaṃ punar viśeṣo ‘syārthavādatve sati durlabhaḥ | yat tu adhīyīraṃs trayo varṇā ityādi sa vedaviṣayo ‘nuvāda iti vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || 1.103 ||
idaṃ śāstram adhīyāno brāhmaṇaḥ śaṃsitavrataḥ |
manovāgdehajair nityaṃ karmadoṣair na lipyate || 1.104 ||
idam iti | idaṃ śāstraṃ paṭhan brāhmaṇaḥ etadarthāvabodhena śaṃsitavrataḥ avikalayamaniyamaḥ san manovākkāyajair vihitākaraṇapratiṣedhasevanajanitaiḥ pāpaiḥ na saṃbadhyate || 1.104 ||
punāti paṅktiṃ vaṃśyāṃś ca sapta sapta parāvarān |
pṛthivīm api caivemāṃ kṛtsnām eko ‘pi so ‘rhati || 1.105 ||
kiṃ ca punātīti | bhojanādānapaṅktisaṃbandhād apavitrībhūtāṃ paṅktiṃ śāstrasthatvāt paṅktipāvanatve sati pavitrīkaroti | tathaivānvayāt sapta parān atītān pitrādīn saptāvarān āgāminaḥ punāti | samagrāṃ cemāṃ prthivīṃ pratigrahītum arhati | viduṣaḥ pratigrahadoṣāpākaraṇasāmarthāt || 1.105 ||
idaṃ svastyayanaṃ śreṣṭham idaṃ buddhivivardhanam |
idaṃ yaśasyaṃ satatam idaṃ niḥśreyasaṃ param || 1.106 ||
idānīṃ śāstraṃ sākṣāt stauti – idam iti | idam anyasmāt svastyayanakarmaṇaḥ śreṣṭaṃ atiśayena praśasyam, svatyayanaṃ avināśaprāpakam | vināśopaśamakarmāvabodhena, buddhivivardhanaṃ ca śāstrārthaparigaṇanāt, buddhivikāsena yaśonimittaṃ ca satataṃ sarvakālaṃ khyātikaratvāt, paraṃ prakṛṣṭaṃ niḥśreyasaṃ niḥśreyasasya mokṣasya sādhakatvāt || 1.106 ||
asmin dharmo ‘khilenokto guṇadoṣau ca karmaṇām |
caturṇām api varṇānām ācāraś caiva śāśvataḥ || 1.107 ||
asminn iti | asmin smārto dharmaḥ kārtsnyenoktaḥ karmaṇāṃ ca vihitapratiṣiddhānām, guṇadoṣau iṣṭāniṣṭaphale varṇānām ācāraḥ puruṣagharmākhyaḥ sādhāraṇaprādhānyakhyāpanārthaṃ cāsya pṛthag grahaṇam | evaṃ caiva tatsaṃnidhau dharmaśabdo ‘tra sādhāraṇaḥ saṃdhyopāsanādiprativacanaḥ || 1.107 ||
ācāraḥ paramo dharmaḥ śrutyuktaḥ smārta eva ca |
tasmād asmin sadā yukto nityaṃ syād ātmavān dvijaḥ || 1.108 ||
ācāra iti | yaḥ śrutyuktaḥ ācāraḥ puruṣasādhāraṇaprādhānyakhyāpanārthaṃ sa dharmākhyaḥ, yaś ca smārtaḥ sa paramaḥ parkēṣṭto dharmaḥ paramo jyotiṣṭomāṣṭakādibhyaḥ śrautasmārtadharmebhyaḥ yataḥ tasmāt yo nityaṃ praśastātmā ācāre sarvakāle yukto yatnavān syāt || 1.108 ||
ācārād vicyuto vipro na vedaphalam aśnute |
ācāreṇa tu saṃyuktaḥ saṃpūrṇaphalabhāk smṛtaḥ || 1.109 ||
ācārad iti | ācārād vicyutaḥ śrautasmārtādhikārāt bhraṣṭācāro brāhmaṇo na vaidikaṃ karmaphalaṃ labhate | ācāravān punaḥ samagraphalabhāg bhavati || 1.109 ||
evam ācārato dṛṣṭvā dharmasya munayo gatim |
sarvasya tapaso mūlam ācāraṃ jagṛhuḥ param || 1.110 ||
evam iti | evam ācārad dharmasya prāptim ṛṣayo buddhyā kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādes tapasaḥ saphalāt paraṃ prakṛṣṭaṃ mūlakāraṇaṃ tasmin sati tapasaḥ sāphalyād ācāram anuṣṭheyatvena gṛhītavanta iti | evam ihācārāstutiḥ śāstrastutyarthā | uttaratra tu ācārānuṣṭhānārtham || 1.110 ||
jagataś ca tam utpattiṃ saṃskāravidhim eva ca |
vratacaryopacāraṃ ca snānasya ca paraṃ vidhim || 1.111 ||
idānīṃ śāstrasya mahāvṛttāntopetatvapradarśanārtham anukramaṇīm āha – jagata iti || 1.111 ||
dārādhigamanaṃ caiva vivāhānāṃ ca lakṣaṇam |
mahāyajñavidhānaṃ ca śrāddhakalpaṃ ca śāśvatam || 1.112 ||
dārādhigamanam iti || 1.112 ||
vṛttīnāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ caiva snātakasya vratāni ca |
bhakṣyābhakṣyaṃ ca śaucaṃ ca dravyāṇāṃ śuddhim eva ca || 1.113 ||
vṛttīnām iti || 1.113 ||
strīdharmayogaṃ tāpasyaṃ mokṣaṃ saṃnyāsam eva ca |
rājñaś ca dharmam akhilaṃ kāryāṇāṃ ca vinirṇayam || 1.114 ||
strīdharmayogam iti || 1.114 ||
sākṣipraśnavidhānaṃ ca dharmaṃ strīpuṃsayor api |
vibhāgadharmaṃ dyūtaṃ ca kaṇṭakānāṃ ca śodhanam || 1.115 ||
sākṣipraśnavidhānam iti || 1.115 ||
vaiśyaśūdropacāraṃ ca saṃkīrṇānāṃ ca saṃbhavam |
āpaddharmaṃ ca varṇānāṃ prāyaścittavidhiṃ tathā || 1.116 ||
vaiśyaśūdropacāram iti || 1.116 ||
saṃsāragamanaṃ caiva trividhaṃ karmasaṃbhavam |
niḥśreyasaṃ karmaṇāṃ ca guṇadoṣaparīkṣaṇam || 1.117 ||
saṃsāragamanam iti || 1.117 ||
deśadharmāñ jātidharmān kuladharmāṃś ca śāśvatān |
pāṣaṇḍagaṇadharmāṃś ca śāstre ‘sminn uktavān manuḥ || 1.118 ||
deśadharmān iti | jagatutpattir yathoktā | brahmaṇaḥ stutyādeś ca śāstradharmastutyarthatvenaikārthatvād atraivāntarbhāvaḥ |  [1.111f] saṃkārāṇāṃ jātakarmādīnāṃ vidhir anuṣṭhānam | vratacaryā vratacaraṇaṃ vrataṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ | upacāro gurvādīnām abhivādanādyanuvṛttiḥ | vratacaryopacāram iti dvandvaikavadbhāvaḥ | snānaṃ gurukulān nivartamānasya saṃskāraviśeṣaḥ tasya paro vidhiḥ prakṛṣṭaṃ vidhānam | dārādhigamanaṃ vivāhaḥ | vivāhādīnāṃ brāhmādīnāṃ ca lakṣaṇam | mahāyajñāś ca vaiśvadevādayaḥ | śrāddhakalpaḥ śrāddhavidhiḥ | śāśvataḥ anādipravāhapravṛttyā nityaḥ vṛttīnāṃ jīvanopāyānām ṛtādīnāṃ lakṣaṇam | snātakasya gṛhasthasya vratāni | bhakṣyaṃ dadhyādi abhakṣyaṃ laśunādi | śaucaṃ jananādau daśāhādinā | dravyāṇāṃ taijasādīnāṃ kṣārādinā śuddhiḥ | striyā dharmayogo dharmānuṣṭhānam | tāpasyaṃ tāpasasya vānaprasthasya karma | bhikṣoḥ saṃsārahetutvān mokṣaḥ saṃnyāsaś ca bhikṣudharmaprakāra eva | rājño ‘bhiṣiktasya dharmaḥ kāryāṇāṃ vyavahārāṇām arthipratyarthisamarpitānāṃ niścayaḥ | sākṣipraśnavidhānaṃ ca | strīpuṃsayoḥ dampatyoḥ dharmaḥ parasparavartanam | paitṛkaṛkthavibhāgadharmaḥ | dyūtam akṣādiḥ tadvyavasthā kaṇṭakānāṃ caurādīnāṃ śodhanam uddharaṇaṃ vaiśyaśūdrayor upacāro vārtā śuśrūṣādyanuṣṭhānam | saṃkīrṇānām anulomapratilomānām utpattiḥ | āpadi jīvikopadeśaḥ āpaddharmaḥ | vihitākaraṇādinā prāyaścittānuṣṭhānam | uttamamadhyamādhamabhedena trividhakarmahetunā saṃsārotpattiḥ | yasmāt param anyac chreyo nāsti tan niḥśreyasam ātmajñānaṃ naiśreyasahetutvāt | karmaṇāṃ ca vihitapratiṣiddhānāṃ guṇadoṣaparīkṣaṇam | pratiniyatadeśajātikulāśritā dharmāḥ bāhyadarśanasamāśrayeṇa liṅgadhāraṇaṃ pākhaṇḍam | vaṇikkārvādīnāṃ gaṇaḥ saṃghaḥ || 1.111–118 ||
yathedam uktavāñ chāstraṃ purā pṛṣṭo manur mayā |
tathedaṃ yūyam apy adya matsakāśān nibodhata || 1.119 ||
yatheti | pūrvaṃ mayā manuḥ pṛṣṭo yathaivedam uktavān tathaivāvibhraṣṭāgamaṃ matsakāśāt yūyaṃ śṛṇutety uttaraśāstrārthāpatter naitat siddham (?) || 1.119 ||
iti śrībhaṭṭamādhavātmajagovindarājaviracitāyāṃ manvāśayānasāriṇyāṃ
prathamo ‘dhyāyaḥ samāptaḥ
atha dvitīyo ‘dhyāyaḥ
vidvadbhiḥ sevitaḥ sadbhir nityam adveṣarāgibhiḥ |
hṛdayenābhyanujñāto yo dharmas taṃ nibodhata || 2.1 ||
oṃ namaḥ sarasvatyai | evam adhyayanaśravaṇārthaṃ mahāprayojanatāṃ śāśtrasya pratipādyādhunā prakṛtābhidhānārtham upakramate | tatra tāvad abhidhāsyamānaṃ dharmasāmānyalakṣaṇaṃ sakalaśāstravyāpakatvena paribhāṣārtham āha vidvadbhir iti | dharmasyānanyapramāṇatvād vidvadbhir vedārthajñaiḥ sadbhiḥ dhārmikaiḥ sarvadā rāgadveṣaśūnyaiḥ ācarito hṛdayenābhyanujñāto ‘ntaḥkaraṇa[vi]cikitsāśūnyo yo dharmaḥ adṛṣṭārthakartavyarūpas taṃ śṛṇuta || 2.1 ||
kāmātmatā na praśastā na caivehāsty akāmatā |
kāmyo hi vedādhigamaḥ karmayogaś ca vaidikaḥ || 2.2 ||
paribhāṣāntaram āha – kāmātmateti | kāmātmatā na praśastā, phalābhilāṣaśīlatvam ayuktam | yataḥ śāstracoditakarmāṇi phalādbhilāṣatyāgenācaryamāṇāny ātmajñānasācivyena mokṣāya kalpante | phalecchayā tv anuṣṭhāne teṣāṃ saṃsārahetutvāt | astu (paran tu) kāmātmatā na praśastety anenecchā niṣiddhā, etan matvāha – na caiveti | icchām antareṇa neha śāstre pravṛttir asti yasmād vedasvīkaraṇaṃ vaidikakarmasaṃbandhaś cābhilaṣaṇīya eva || 2.2 ||
saṃkalpamūlaḥ kāmo vai yajñāḥ saṃkalpasaṃbhavāḥ |
vratāni yamadharmāś ca sarve saṃkalpajāḥ smṛtāḥ || 2.3 ||
atraivopapattim āha – saṃkalpamūla iti | anena vastunā iyam arthakriyā sādhyate ity evaṃvidho manovyāpāraḥ saṃkalpaḥ | sa ca mūlaṃ kāmasya | tadanantaraṃ hi tadvastusaṃpādanecchopajāyate | tad anu cādhyavasāyo bhautikavyāpāreṇa karaṇodyogo bhavati | evaṃ ca yajñādayaḥ saṃkalpaprabhavāḥ | teṣāṃ ca saṃkalpodbhavatvae sati avaśyam anatricchayā bhavitavyam | saṃkalpābhimānavyavasāyebhyaḥ kriyāpravṛttiḥ | yamadharmāḥ pratiṣedhātmakāḥ || 2.3 ||
akāmasya kriyā kācid dṛśyate neha karhicit |
yad yad dhi kurute kiṃcit tat tat kāmasya ceṣṭitam || 2.4 ||
atraiva laukikaṃ dṛṣṭāntam āha – akāmasyeti | buddhipūrvakāribhāvyā yā kācit kriyā dṛṣṭārthā sā anicchato na kadācid dṛśyate | tasmād yat kiṃcit kurute karma tat sarvaṃ kāmasya ceṣṭitam icchākāryam ity arthaḥ || 2.4 ||
teṣu samyag vartamāno gacchaty amaralokatām |
yathā saṃkalpitāṃś ceha sarvān kāmān samaśnute || 2.5 ||
atra parihāram āha – teṣv iti | nātrecchā niṣidhyate | api tu yathāśrutaṃ tat tathaivānuṣṭheyam itīyaṃ samyagvṛttiḥ tatra teṣu karmasu phalābhilāṣatyāgena pravartamāno bandhātmakatvābhāvāt amaralokatāṃ amaradharmatvaṃ mokṣaṃ prāpnoti | iha saṃsāre ātmajñānābhyāsenāṣṭaguṇaiśvaryayogāt yathābhilaṣitān sarvān arthān samaśnute || 2.5 ||
vedo ‘khilo dharmamūlaṃ smṛtiśīle ca tadvidām |
ācāraś caiva sādhūnām ātmanas tuṣṭir eva ca || 2.6 ||
idānīṃ dharmapramāṇaparigaṇanārtham āha – veda iti | vedaḥ ṛgyajuḥsāmalakṣaṇaḥ akhilaḥ samagraḥ | arthavādādīnām api vidhyekavākyatvena śravaṇāt dharmasya mūlaṃ pramāṇaṃ mithājñānasaṃśayābhāvāt nisargaprāmāṇyāc ca | etan nyāyasiddham | etad asyānūdyate smṛtyādīnāṃ tanmūlatvena prāmāṇyapratipādanāya | tathā ca mānavādiśāstropanibandhanaṃ vedavidāṃ kartavyākartavyasmaraṇam śīlaṃ ca rāgadveṣaparityāgātmakam, ācāraś ca vivāhādau kaṅkaṇabandhanādyācaraṇarūpaḥ, sādhūnāṃ dharmikāṇāṃ ātmanas tuṣṭiś ca adṛṣṭārthācaryamāṇapadārthacetaḥ paritoṣarūpā vaikalpikapadārthaviṣayā ca dharmaṃ prati pramāṇam | eṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ vedamūlakatvāt || 2.6 ||
yaḥ kaścit kasyacid dharmo manunā parikīrtitaḥ |
sa sarvo ‘bhihito vede sarvajñānamayo hi saḥ || 2.7 ||
tathā ca vedamūlatvam eṣāṃ darśayati – ya iti | kasyacit śrutiśīlācārātmatuṣṭipramāṇako dharmaḥ kasyacit brāhmaṇādeḥ manunā kathitaḥ sa sarvair vedair uktaḥ ucchinnaviprakīrṇamantrārthavādaliṅgamūlatvāt tatsmaraṇasya, atīndriyārthapratipādane hi vedavidām agṛhyamāṇakāraṇānāṃ vedasyaiva mūlatvasaṃbhavāt | yasmāt sarvadṛṣṭārthajñānahetutvāt sarvajñānamayaḥ sarvajñānārabdha iva vedaḥ | atra ca aṣṭakādīnāṃ mahāpratyavāyādīnāṃ granthenopanibandhaḥ | śīlācārātmatuṣṭīnāṃ punar alpapratyavāyatvāt [na] upanibandhaḥ || 2.7 ||
sarvaṃ tu samavekṣyedaṃ nikhilaṃ jñānacakṣuṣā |
śrutiprāmānyato vidvān svadharme niviśeta vai || 2.8 ||
sarvam iti | yāvat kiṃcic chāstrajātaṃ tat sarvaṃ vijñānākhyena cakṣuṣā nikhilaṃ niḥśeṣaṃ kṛtvā paryālocya vedaprāmāṇyenaiva śāstrajñaḥ svadharme ‘vatiṣṭheta | vedasyaiva dharmaṃ prati prāmāṇyāt na vedabrāhmaṇaśāstraprāmāṇyena, jñānaṃ cakṣur iva prameyaparicchedāt || 2.8 ||
śrutismṛtyuditaṃ dharmam anutiṣṭhan hi mānavaḥ |
iha kīrtitam avāpnoti pretya cānuttamaṃ sukham || 2.9 ||
śrutismṛtyuditam iti | śrutismṛtyuktaṃ dharmam ācaran puruṣaḥ iha loke khyātiṃ paraloke cotkṛṣṭaṃ sukhaṃ prāpnotīti sadbhūtaguṇākhānenānuṣṭhānastutiḥ prarocanārthā | na hy akasmān niṣprayojanā stutiḥ pravarteta ity evaṃ sarvatreha stutiṣu vijñeyam || 2.9 ||
śrutis tu vedo vijñeyo dharmaśāstraṃ tu vai smṛtiḥ |
te sarvārtheṣv amīmāṃsye tābhyāṃ dharmo hi nibabhau || 2.10 ||
śrutir iti | mandabuddhivyutpādanārthaṃ mīmāṃsāpratiṣedhādivyavahāraphalaṃ lokasiddhapadārthakathanam | śrutir vedo dharmaśāstraṃ smṛtir iti | smṛtigrahaṇaṃ śīlāder api samaraṇaprabhedatvāt pradarśanārtham | te śrutismṛtī sarvārtheṣu āśunarakaphalasurāpānādyātmakeṣu kutarkavikalpaiḥ na vicārayitavye | katham etat syād iti yataḥ pramāṇāntarāgocaro dharmaḥ tābhyāṃ nirbabhau prakāśatāṃ gataḥ || 2.10 ||
yo ‘vamanyet te mūle hetuśāstrāśrayād dvijaḥ |
sa sādhubhir bahiṣkāryo nāstiko vedanindakaḥ || 2.11 ||
evaṃ kartuḥ bahiḥkaraṇam āha – yo ‘vamanyeta te ubhe iti | yaḥ śrutismṛtibhyām evopāttadvijabhāvaḥ san tata evāsattarkaśāstrāvalambanena nindet | nāsti paralokādir ity evaṃ sthitapajñaḥ vedanindako dvijasampādyāt karmaṇo ‘dhyayanādeḥ śiṣṭaiḥ parihāryaḥ || 2.11 ||
vedaḥ smṛtiḥ sadācāraḥ svasya ca priyam ātmanaḥ |
etac caturvidhaṃ prāhuḥ sākṣād dharmasya lakṣaṇam || 2.12 ||
idānīṃ smṛtiśīlācārātmatuṣṭyātmakaprakārabhedasyātantratāṃ darśayituṃ śīlāpātena caturdhā dharmalakṣaṇam āha – vedaḥ smṛtīti | śrutyādīnāṃ prakāramātrabhedo na tu vastutaḥ, sarveṣāṃ śiṣṭānusmaraṇarūpatvāc ca | tathā ca “śrutis tu vedo vijñeyo dharmaśāstraṃ tu vai smṛtiḥ” svayam evāvocat | sadācāraḥ sādhvācāraḥ | svātmanaś ca priyaṃ ātmatuṣṭiḥ || 2.12 ||
arthakāmeṣv asaktānāṃ dharmajñānaṃ vidhīyate |
dharmaṃ jijñāsamānānāṃ pramāṇaṃ paramaṃ śrutiḥ || 2.13 ||
arthakāmeṣv iti | arthakāmākhyadṛṣṭapuruṣārtheṣu asaktānāṃ tallipsāśūnyānāṃ tattvāvabodhanaṃ śāstreṇa vidhīyate | tallipsayā lokavyaktyarthaṃ dharmānuṣṭhāne sati adṛṣṭaphalābhāvāt kiṃ dharmajñānenety abhiprāyaḥ | tathā ca śrutismṛtyādivirodhe sati tattvaṃ jñātum icchatāṃ śrutyādīnāṃ madhyāt śrutiḥ prakṛṣṭaṃ pramāṇam | ataś ca śrutivirodhe smṛtyavabādhaḥ, pratyakṣavirodhe ‘numānasya durbalatvāt || 2.13 ||
śrutidvaidhaṃ tu yatra syāt tatra dharmāv ubhau smṛtau |
ubhāv api hi tau dharmau samyag uktau manīṣibhiḥ || 2.14 ||
śrutidvaidham iti | yatra punaḥ śrutyor eva dvividhyaṃ parasparaṃ viddhābhidhānaṃ tatra tatpratipādyau dvāv api vikalpānuṣṭheyatvena manvādibhiḥ pūrvatarair api vidvadbhiḥ dvāv api tau dharmau samyak samīcīnāv ity uktam | nātra kasyacid api hānaṃ yujyate tulyapramāṇaśiṣṭatvāt | evaṃ ca samānanyāyatvāt smṛtyor api virodhe etad eva vijñeyam iti prakṛtopayogaḥ || 2.14 ||
udite ‘nudite caiva samayādhyuṣite tathā |
sarvathā vartate yajña itīyaṃ vaidikī śrutiḥ || 2.15 ||
atrodāharaṇam āha – udita iti | udite hotavyam anudite hotavyaṃ samayādhyuṣite rātrivivāsakāle hotavyam iti itaretaraviruddhahomakālaśravaṇe ‘pi sarvathā sarvaṃ prāpnoti | anyasminn api kāle home kṛte saṃpadyate homaḥ | na hi kālābhyāṅgānurodhena homākhyaṃ pradhānam āvartayituṃ yuktam ity evam udite hotavyam ity evamādikaṃ vaidikaṃ vākyam iti || 2.15 ||
niṣekādiḥ śmaśānanto mantrair yasyodito vidhiḥ |
tasya śāstre ‘dhikāro ‘smin jñeyo nānyasya kasyacit || 2.16 ||
niṣekādīti | garbhādhānādyantyeṣṭiparyanto yasya mantrair yukto ‘nuṣṭhānakalpaḥ uktaḥ sa ca dvijāter uktaḥ | atas tasya asmin smṛtiśāstre adhyayanaśravaṇādhikāro boddhavyaḥ na tu śūdrādeḥ arthānuṣṭhānaṃ punaḥ pratilomaparyantān praty api coditatvān nānena niṣedhyate | pravacanaṃ tv atra brāhmaṇasyety uktam | vidiṣety atra dharmapramāṇāni paribhāṣāś coktāḥ || 2.16 ||
sarasvatīdṛṣadvatyor devanadyor yad antaram |
taṃ devanirmitaṃ deśaṃ brahmāvartaṃ pracakṣate || 2.17 ||
adhunā sadācāranirūpaṇārthaṃ dharmānuṣṭhānayogyasthānakathanārthaṃ ca deśān āha – sarasvatīdṛṣadvatyor iti | sarasvatīdṛṣadvatyākhyanadīmadhyaṃ brahmāvrtākhyaṃ deśam āhuḥ | devanadīdevanirmitaśabdau deśastutyarthau || 2.17 ||
tasmin deśe ya ācāraḥ pāramparyakramāgataḥ |
varṇānāṃ sāntarālānāṃca sa sadācāraḥ ucyate || 2.18 ||
tasminn iti | tasmin deśe prāyeṇa sadācārasya saṃbhavāt yo brāhmaṇādīnām antaraprabhāvānāṃ pāramparyakramāgataḥ prabandhato 'vicchedenāgataḥ na sāmpratīko ya ācāraḥ sa sadācāra ucyate || 2.18 ||
kurukṣetraṃ ca matsyāś ca pañcālāḥ śūrasenakāḥ |
eṣa brahmarṣideśo vai brahmāvartād anantaraḥ || 2.19 ||
kurukṣetram iti | matsyādiḥ virāṭadeśāḥ pāñcālāḥ kānyakubjā āhicchatraḥ śūrasenakāḥ mādhurāḥ eṣa brahmarṣideśākhyo deśaḥ brahmāvartād īṣannyūnaḥ || 2.19 ||
etaddeśaprasūtasya sakāśād agrajanmanaḥ |
svaṃ svaṃ caritraṃ śikṣeran pṛthivyāṃ sarvamānavāḥ || 2.20 ||
etaddeśaprasūtasyeti | kurukṣetrādideśajātasya brāhmaṇasya sakāśāt svaṃ svam ācāraṃ pṛthivyāṃ sarve manuṣyāḥ śikṣeran || 2.20 ||
himavadvindhyayor madhyaṃ yat prāg vinaśanād api |
pratyag eva prayāgāc ca madhyadeśaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 2.21 ||
himavadvindhyayor iti | himavadvindhyākhyottaradakṣiṇadigavasthitaparvatayor madhyaṃ vinaśanāc ca sarasvatyantardhānadeśāt pratyagdigvartinaḥ prāk pūrvadiṅmārge | prayāgāc ca gaṅgāyamunāsaṃgamāt pratyak paścimadiṅmārge sa madhyadeśākhyo deśaḥ || 2.21 ||
ā samudrāt tu vai pūrvād ā samudrāc ca paścimāt |
tayor evāntaraṃ giryor āryāvartaṃ vidur budhāḥ || 2.22 ||
ā samudrād iti | pūrvapaścimasamudrayoḥ punar arvāgvarti yan madhyaṃ tayoś ca himavadvindhyayoś cottaradakṣiṇādigavalambinoḥ parvatayoḥ yan madhyaṃ āryāvartākhyaṃ deśaṃ vidvāṃso manyante || 2.22 ||
kṛṣṇasāras tu carati mṛgo yatra svabhāvataḥ |
sa jñeyo yajñiyo deśo mlecchadeśas tv ataḥ paraḥ || 2.23 ||
kṛṣṇasāra iti | kṛṣṇaḥ śavalo mṛgo yasmin deśe svabhāvato nivasati svacchandaṃ viharati, na haṭhād ānītaḥ, sa yāgārho deśo vijñeyaḥ ato ‘nyo anadhikṛtāndhrādimlecchadeśo na yāgārharḥ | adhyayana etad eva || 2.3 ||
etān dvijātayo deśān saṃśrayeran prayatnataḥ |
śūdras tu yasmiṃs tasmin vā nivased vṛttikarśitaḥ || 2.24 ||
ataḥ etān iti | sādhvācārayāgārhatvasaṃbhavāt adṛṣṭārthatvāc caitān eva deśān deśāntarodbhūtā api dvijātayo yatnataḥ āśrayeran | śudraḥ punaḥ yadā jīvikābhāvapīḍito bhavati tadaitadvyatiriktadeśe ‘pi nivaset || 2.24 ||
eṣā dharmasya vo yoniḥ samāsena prakīrtitā |
saṃbhavaś cāsya sarvasya varṇadharmān nibodhata || 2.25 ||
eṣā dharmasya yoniḥ kāraṇam | yo vidvadbhiḥsevitaḥ tasya āphaladānāvasthāyinaḥ, kāraṇam iti saṃkṣepato yuṣmākam uktā | iha dharmaśabdaḥ apūrvākhyātmasaṃskāre vartate | “vidvadbhiḥ sevitaḥ” ity atra tu tatkāraṇeṣu kadācit ity ubhayatrāpi cāyaṃ dṛṣṭaproyogaḥ | saṃbhavaś cottpattir jagataḥ uktā |sāṃprataṃ varṇaiḥ brāhmaṇādibhir ye anuṣṭheyā dharmāḥ varṇadharmāḥ āśramadharmāḥ varṇāśramadharmāḥ naimittikākhyāḥ pañcaprakārāḥ tān śṛṇuta | varṇadharmā yathā brāhmaṇasya madyapānaniṣedhaḥ, āśramadharmo yathā brahmacāriṇo bhaikṣacaraṇaṃ brāhmaṇo (brāhmaṇasya) bailvadaṇḍadhāraṇam | varṇāśramadharmo yathā pālāśadaṇḍo brāhmaṇasyeti | guṇadharmo yathā abhiṣekādiguṇayuktasya rājñaḥ prajāpālanam | naimittikā yathā prāyaścittam iti || 2.25 ||
vaidikaiḥ karmabhiḥ puṇyair niṣekādir dvijanmanām |
kāryaḥ śarīrasaṃskāraḥ pāvanaḥ pretya ceha ca || 2.26 ||
vaidikair iti | vaidikamantrasādhyatvāt vedamūlasādhyatvāc ca vaidikaiḥ smārtaiḥ karmabhiḥ “viṣṇur yoniṃ kalpayatu” ityevamādimantraprayogādibiḥ puṇyaiḥ pāramparyeṇa dharmahetubhiḥ garbhādhānādiḥ dvijātīnāṃ śarīrasaṃskāraḥ kartavyaḥ | pāvanaḥ pavitrīkārakaḥ pretya paraloke saṃskṛtasya yāgādikarmaphalasaṃbandhāt | iha loke ca saṃskṛtasyādhyayanādyadhikārāt | niṣekaguṇāt (-grahaṇāt) pitur adhikāraḥ | niṣekavarjite tu kadācit tatsthānāpannasya tatprayuktasya vā anyasyāpi (na) syāt || 2.26 ||
gārbhair homair jātakarmacauḍamauñjīnibandhanaiḥ |
baijikaṃ gārbhikaṃ caino dvijānām apamṛjyate || 2.27 ||
kutaḥ punar eṣām aśucitvam, yena pāvanaṃ kriyate ity at āha – gārbhair iti | garbhanimittaṃ ye kriyante te gārbhāḥ, homagrahaṇaṃ yathāsaṃbhavaṃ gārbhe viśeṣaṇam, garbhādhānāder ahomātmakatvāt, sīmantonnayanādeś ca sahomakatvāt | jātasya yat kriyate tat jātakarma | ete sarve gṛhyābhidheyasvarūpāḥ ihaiva teṣāṃ sāmānyamātrābhidhānāt sāmānyasmṛtitvād asya | caulaṃ cūḍākarma mauñjīnibandhanam upanayanam | etaiḥ baijikaṃ pratiṣiddamaithunasaṃkalpāt pitṛretaso dṣṭatvaṃ, gārbhikaṃ garbhavāsanivāsitvād duṣṭatvaṃ dvijānām apanudyate || 2.27 ||
yeṣu karmasu māṇavakasya saṃskārā upakārakās tān idānīm udāharaṇamātreṇa darśayati |
svādhyāyena vratair homais traividyenejyayā sutaiḥ |
mahāyajñaiś ca yajñaiś ca brāhmīyaṃ kriyate tanuḥ || 2.28 ||
svādhyāyeneti | vedādhyayanena vrataiḥ sāvitryādibhiḥ momaiḥ sāyaṃprātaragnikāryaiḥ traividyākhyena vratena, prādhānyāt pṛthagupadiṣṭena | ijyayā brahmacaryāvasthāyāṃ devarṣipitṛtarpaṇarūpayā, putraiḥ, mahāyajñaiḥ vaiśvadevādibhiḥ yajñaiḥ agniṣṭomādibhiḥ brahmaprāptiyogyā tanuḥ tatstha ātmā kriyate jānakarmasamuccayān mokṣāvāpteḥ || 2.28 ||
prāṅnābhivardhanāt puṃso jātakarma vidhīyate |
mantravat prāśanaṃ cāsya hiraṇyamadhusarpiṣām || 2.29 ||
prāṅnābhīti | nābhichedanāt pūrvaṃ puṃso jātakarmākhyaḥ saṃskāro vidhīyate | asya ca śiśoḥ tadānīm “pratidadāmi (pra te dadāmi) madhuno ghṛtasya” ity evamādimantrayuktaṃ hiraṇyamākṣikaghṛtānāṃ prāśanaṃ kāryam | puṃso grahaṇaṃ napuṃsakavyāvṛttyartham | yady arthitā tu dāraiḥ syāt klībādīnām iti napuṃsakasya dāradarśanāt | ye stenāḥ patitāḥ klībā iti ca śrotriyatve sati śrāddhabhojanaprāpteḥ pratiṣedhadarśanāt | stryarthatve tasya pramāṇaṃ nāsti | tathā ca “amantarkāsu” iti strīṇām aprāptiṃ vakṣyati || 2.29 ||
nāmadheyaṃ daśamyāṃ tu dvādaśyāṃ vāsya kārayet |
puṇye tithau muhūrte vā nakṣatre vā guṇānvite || 2.30 ||
nāmadheyam iti | jātakarmeti pratutatvāt janmaprabhṛti daśame ‘hani dvādaśe vā praśaste vānyasmin navamyādiriktāhovarjite muhūrte ‘bhijidādau lagne puṇyanakṣatre ca jyotiḥśāstraguṇayukte asya śiśor nāma kārayet asāmarthye sati | śaktau tu ātmanaiva kuryāt, “pitā nāma karoti” iti gṛhyaśravaṇāt | pūrvakālāsaṃbhave uttarottarakālāśrayaṇaṃ kartavyam iti, kramaprayojanābhāvāt, evam uttaratrāpi kālavikalpeṣu vijñeyam || 2.30 ||
idānīṃ yādṛśaṃ nāma kartavyaṃ tan niyamayati svarūpato ‘rthataś ca |
maṅgalyaṃ brāhmaṇasya syāt kṣatriyasya balānvitam |
vaiśyasya dhanasaṃyuktaṃ śūdrasya tu jugupsitam || 2.31 ||
tac ca māṅgalyaṃ brāhmaṇasyeti | maṅgalabaladhananindāpratipādakāni eṣāṃ yathākramaṃ bhadraśaktidhanadīnakādīni nāmāni syuḥ || 2.31 ||
śarmavad brāhmaṇasya syād rājño rakṣāsamanvitam |
vaiśyasya puṣṭisaṃyuktaṃ śūdrasya preṣyasaṃyutam || 2.32 ||
upapadaniyamārtham āha – śarmavad brāhmaṇasyeti | śarmarakṣāpuṣṭipreṣyavācakāny eṣāṃ yathākramaṃ śarmapālapuṣṭidāsāny upapadāni syuḥ | udāharaṇāni bhadraśarmā śaktipālo dhanapuṣṭo dīnadāsa iti || 2.32 ||
strīṇāṃ sukhodyamakrūraṃ vispaṣṭārthaṃ manoharam |
maṅgalyaṃ dīrghavarṇāntam āśīrvādābhidhānavat || 2.33 ||
strīṇām iti | susukhoccāraṇam akrūrārthavāci vispaṣṭārthaṃ manaḥprītikaraṃ maṅgalavāci dīrghākṣarāntam āśīrvācakenābhidhānena śabdena saṃsaktaṃ nām strīṇāṃ kāryam | yathā amaradevī iti || 2.33 ||
caturthe māsi kartavyaṃ śiśor niṣkramaṇaṃ gṛhāt |
ṣaṣṭhe ‘nnaprāśanaṃ māsi yad veṣṭaṃ maṅgalaṃ kule || 2.34 ||
caturtha iti | caturthe māsi bālasya gṛhān niṣkramaṇaṃ kartavyam | ṣaṣṭhe cānnaprāśanam | yathākuladharmatvena maṅgalam iṣṭam, yayā nāmyā svāmyādyupapadaparigrahaḥ tathā kartavyaṃ pūrvoktaṃ ca || 2.34 ||
cūḍākarma dvijātīnāṃ sarveṣām eva dharmataḥ |
prathame ‘bde tṛtīye vā kartavyaṃ śrutinodanāt || 2.35 ||
cūḍākarmeti | dvijātīnāṃ prathame varṣe tṛtīye vā cūḍākaraṇam | dharmato dharmeṇādṛṣṭārthaṃ kāryam, “oṃ yatra bāṇāḥ saṃpatanti kumārā viśikhā iva” iti śrutiliṅgāt | anenaitad darśayati – yad evaṃ prāyāḥ vedaliṅgamūlāḥ smṛtaya iti || 2.35 ||
garbhāṣṭame ‘bde kurvīta brāhmaṇasyopanāyanam |
garbhād ekādaśe rājño garbhāt tu dvādaśe viśaḥ || 2.36 ||
garbhāṣṭama iti | garbhagrahaṇasaṃvatsarad aṣṭame saṃvatsare brāhmaṇasya, upanayanam evopanāyanaṃ mauñjībandhanaṃ kartavyam | evaṃ garbhaikādaśe kṣatriyasya garbhadvādaśe vaiśyasya || 2.36 ||
brahmavarcasakāmasya kāryaṃ viprasya pañcame |
rājño balārthinaḥ ṣaṣṭhe vaiśyasyehārthino ‘ṣṭame || 2.37 ||
brahmavarcasakāmasyeti | adhyayanavijñānādyatiśayakṛtaṃ tejo brahmavarcasaṃ bālasya viśeṣavijñānābhāvāt tad yuktaḥ putro me bhaved ity evaṃvidhapitṛkāmanā phalāspadatvāt putro ‘py upadiśyate brahmavarcasakāma iti | tasya brāhmaṇasya garbhapañcame ‘bde upanayanaṃ kāryam | evaṃ kṣatriyasya hastyaśvabāhuvīryādi brāhmābhyantarabalārthino garbhaṣaṣṭhe, vaiśyasya bahukṛṣyādiceṣṭārthino garbhāṣṭame, garbhasaṃvatsarāṇām eva prakṛtatvāt || 2.37 ||
ā ṣoḍaśād brāhmaṇasya sāvitrī nātivartate |
ā dvāviṃśāt kṣatriyabandhor ā caturviṃśater viśaḥ || 2.38 ||
anukalpam āha – ā ṣoḍaśād iti | “chandaḥ pādākṣaravad abdasamavāye upanayanam” iti hārītasmaraṇāt | āṅo ‘bhividhyarthatvena ṣaḍaśavarṣaṃ yāvat brāhmaṇasya sāvitryanuvacanasādhanam upanayanaṃ nātikrāntakālaṃ bhavati | dvāviṃśaṃ yāvat kṣatriyajātīyasya | caturviṃśaṃ yāvat vaiśyasya | upanayanaṃ ca puruṣasya saṃskāraḥ kāryaḥ śarīrasaṃskāra iti āmnānāt na tu tadanantaraṃ vedādhyayanadarśanāt brahmasaṃskārasya (tva) bhrāntiḥ kāryāḥ | tatrānumānikatvāt || 2.38 ||
ata ūrdhvaṃ trayo ‘py ete yathākālam asaṃskṛtāḥ |
sāvitrīpatitā vrātyā bhavanty āryavigarhitāḥ || 2.39 ||
ata ūrdhvam iti | ete yathākālaṃ yo yasyopanayanakālaḥ tatropakalpite ‘pi asaṃskrtāḥ santaḥ asmāt kālāt ūrvaṃ sāvitrīpatitāḥ upanayanabhraṣṭāḥ śiṣṭavigarhitāḥ santo vrātyasaṃjñā bhavanti | saṃjñāvidhānaṃ “vrātyānāṃ yājanaṃ kṛtvā” ity evamādivyavahārārtham || 2.39 ||
naitair apūtair vidhivad āpady api hi karhicit |
brāhmān yaunāṃś ca saṃbandhān nācared brāhmaṇaiḥ saha || 2.40 ||
yāvad vidhivat yathāśāstraṃ ete apūtāḥ kṛtaprāyaścittā na bhavanti tāvad etaiḥ sahāpady api kadācit adhyayanakanyādānādīn saṃbandhān brahmaṇo nācaret || 2.40 ||
kārṣṇarauravabāstāni carmāṇi brahmacāriṇaḥ |
vasīrann ānupūrvyeṇa śāṇakṣaumāvikāni ca || 2.41 ||
kārṣṇarauravabāstānīti | kārṣṇa iti sāmānyābhidhāne ‘pi rurvākhyamṛgasaṃbandhāt kṛṣṇamṛgaruruchāgacarmāṇi brahmacāriṇaḥ uttarīyāṇi vasīran | “carmāṇi uttarīyāni” iti gṛhyavacanāc ca | śāṇakṣumorṇāmayāni arthāt paridhānīyāni vāsāṃśi vasīran | ānupūrvyagrahaṇaṃ kramārtham | “śāṇaṃ kṣaumaṃ cābrāhmaṇasya” iti gṛhyadarśanāt etayor brāhmaṇagatatvābhrāntyā viṣamasaṃkhyatve sati yathākramasaṃbandhāt || 2.41 ||
mauñjī trivṛt samā ślakṣṇā kāryā viprasya mekhalā |
kṣatriyasya tu maurvī jyā vaiśyasya śaṇatāntavī || 2.42 ||
mauñjīti | muñjākhyā kakṣamyī triguṇā samaguṇā aparuṣā brāhmaṇasya mekhalā kāryā | kṣatriyasya tu mūrvāmayī jyā dhanurguṇaḥ mekhalā kāryā | atra ca jyātvavināśāpatteḥ trivṛtvaṃ nāsti pūrvāsāṃ (pūrveṣāṃ) gāndhiṣu (grantheṣu) tathā prasiddheḥ | vaiśyasya śāṇasūtramayī | atra ca traiguṇyam anuvartate guṇānām apekṣaṇāt, gṛhyasmṛtau ca guṇāmnānāt || 2.42 ||
muñjālābhe tu kartavyāḥ kuśāśmantakabalvajaiḥ |
trivṛttā granthinaikena tribhiḥ pañcabhir eva vā || 2.43 ||
muñjālābha iti | trayāṇāṃ brahmacāriṇāṃ prakṛtatvād abhāvasya tulyatvāt kauśādīnāṃ bahvīnām upādānaṃ muñjālābhe muñjādyalābha iti vyācakṣate | muñjādyabhāve kuśādibhiḥ yathākramaṃ brāhmaṇādibhiḥ kartavyāḥ kuśādayas tṛṇaviśeṣāḥ | etāś ca triguṇena granthinaikena tribhir vā pañcabhir vā kāryāḥ | vāśabdāt granthīnāṃ viprādibhiḥ kramasaṃbandhābhāvaḥ, granthibhedaś cāyaṃ mukhyāsv api bhavati, “vittasya samo ‘si daivyo granthir asi mā viśraṃsa” iti granthiṃ kṛtvā iti laugākṣiṇā mukhyāsu granthyāmnānāt | granthisaṃkhyābhedaṃ ca traividyavidaḥ smaranti || 2.43 ||
kārpāsam upavītaṃ syād viprasyordhvavṛtaṃ trivṛt |
śaṇasūtramayaṃ rājño vaiśasyāvikasautrikam || 2.44 ||
kārpāsam iti | bhāviny upavītasaṃjñā yasya viśiṣṭanyāsasya tad viprādīnāṃ kārpāsaśaṇorṇāmayaṃ yathākramaṃ kāryam | trivṛt triguṇaṃ sat ūrdhvavṛtaṃ dakṣiṇāvartacalitam || 2.44 ||
brāhmaṇo bailvapālāśau kṣatriyo vāṭakhādirau |
pailavaudumbarau vaiśyo daṇḍān arhanti dharmataḥ || 2.45 ||
brāhmaṇa iti | brāhmaṇasya bailvaḥ “pālāśo vā daṇḍaḥ” ityādi vasiṣṭhasmaraṇāt, “pratigṛhyepsitaṃ daṇḍam” iti cehaiva darśanāt, vikalpena dvau dvau daṇḍau bailvapālāśavaṭakhadirapīlūdumbarākhyavṛkṣāṇāṃ brāhmaṇādayo ‘rhanti || 2.45 ||
keśāntiko brāhmaṇasya daṇḍaḥ kāryaḥ pramāṇataḥ |
lalāṭasaṃmito rājñaḥ syāt tu nāsāntiko viśaḥ || 2.46 ||
keśāntaka iti | keśalalāṭanāsikāsamīpapramāṇā yathākramaṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ daṇḍāḥ kāryāḥ || 2.46 ||
ṛjavas te tu sarve syur avraṇāḥ saumyadarśanāḥ |
anudvegakarā nṝṇāṃ satvaco ‘nagnidūṣitā || 2.47 ||
ṛjava iti | te daṇḍāḥ mṛṣṭanicchridrāḥ bhīṣaṇīyākārarahitāḥ satvacaḥ agnidoṣaśūnyāḥ syuḥ | na ca taiḥ kiṃcid bhūtajātam udvejanīyam || 2.47 ||
pratigṛhyepsitaṃ daṇḍam upasthāya ca bhāskaram |
pradakṣiṇaṃ parītyāgniṃ cared bhaikṣaṃ yathāvidhi || 2.48 ||
pratigṛhyeti | āptum iṣṭaṃ saṃskṛtaṃ daṇḍaṃ gṛhītvā ādityaṃ cābhidhyāya agniṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya bhakṣaṃ yathāśastraṃ caret || 2.48 ||
bhavatpūrvaṃ cared bhaikṣam upanīto dvijottamaḥ |
bhavanmadhyaṃ tu rājanyo vaiśyas tu bhavaduttaram || 2.49 ||
katham ity āha – bhavatpūrvam iti | “bhavati dehi” ity evaṃ bhavacchabdapūrvaṃ bhikṣāprārthanāvākyaṃ brāhmaṇa uccārayet | evaṃ “bhikṣāṃ bhavati dehi” iti bhavanmadhyaṃ kṣatriyaḥ, “bhikṣāṃ dehi bhavati” iti bhavaduttaraṃ vaiśyaḥ || 2.49 ||
mātaraṃ vā svasāraṃ vā mātur vā bhaginīṃ nijām |
bhikṣeta bhikṣāṃ prathamaṃ yā cainaṃ nāvamānayet || 2.50 ||
mātaram iti | mātur bhaginī mātṛṣvasā prathamam upanayanāṅgabhūtaṃ bhaikṣam etāṃ yācet nānyāṃ yā ca pratyākhyānena brahmacāriṇaṃ nāvamānayet | nijā sodarā || 2.50 ||
samāhṛtya tu tad bhaikṣaṃ yāvadartham amāyayā |
nivedya gurave ‘śnīyād ācamya prāṅmukhaḥ śuciḥ || 2.51 ||
samāhṛtyeti | tad bhaikṣaṃ yāvadarthaṃ tṛptiparimāṇam, amāyayā kadannaparihāreṇa bahubhya āhṛtya gurave nivedya tenānugṛhītaṃ sadācamya śuciḥ san prāṅmukho ‘śnīyād iti diṅniyamaḥ | ācamya upaspṛśya dvijo nityam iti vakṣyamāṇavidhyanuvādaḥ | tatra “khāni ca” ityādinā asya saviśeṣasya codiṣyamāṇatvāt || 2.51 ||
āyuṣyaṃ prāṅmukho bhuṅkte yaśasyaṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ |
śriyaṃ pratyaṅmukho bhuṅkte ṛtaṃ bhuṅkte hy udaṅmukhaḥ || 2.52 ||
kāmyam idānīm āha – āyuṣyam iti | āyuṣe hitam annaṃ prāṅmukho bhyṅkte iti liṅartho ‘yaṃ draṣṭavyaḥ, anyathā ānarthyakyāt | evaṃ cāyuṣyakāmaḥ prāṅmukho bhuṅkte [iti] uktaṃ bhavati | evaṃ yaśase hitaṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ, śriyam icchan pratyaṅmukhaḥ | ṛtaṃ satyaṃ tatphalahetutvāt annam amṛtaṃ udaṅmukho bhuṅkte || 2.52 ||
upaspṛśya dvijo nityam annam adyāt samāhitaḥ |
bhuktvā copaspṛśet samyag adbhiḥ khāni ca saṃspṛśet || 2.53 ||
upaspṛśyeti | sarvadā brahmacaryād ūrdhvam api dvijaḥ ācamya annaṃ samāhito ananyamanā bhuñjīta | bhuktvā ca yathāśāstram upaspṛśet | adbhiś ca khāni indriyachidrāṇi “khāni copaspṛśec chīrṣṇyāni” iti gautamasmaraṇāt śirasthāni spṛśet | upaspṛśed ity uktvā khāni ca saṃspṛśed iti yat pṛthag āha ato ‘vasīyate yad upasparśanācamanaśabdābhyāṃ trir abhyukṣaṇamātraṃ pradhānam ucyate khasparśanādikā tu itikartavyatayeti | tathā ca ācamanam iti bhakṣaṇārthenaiva dhātusamākhyānāt || 2.53 ||
pūjayed aśanaṃ nityaṃ adyāc caitad akutsyan |
dṛṣṭvā hṛṣyet prasīdec ca pratinandec ca sarvaśaḥ || 2.54 ||
pūjayed iti | sarvadā annaṃ pūjayed devatātvena prāṇārthatvena ca dhyāyet anindayaṃś caitad annam adyāt | dṛṣṭvā ca tuṣyet prasīdec ca | hetvantarajam api khedaṃ jahyāt | pratinandet śraddadhyāt nityam etan me syād ity evaṃ sarvaśaḥ sarvaśaḥ sarvaparkāram || 2.54 ||
pūjitaṃ hy aśanaṃ nityaṃ balam ūrjaṃ ca yacchati |
apūjitaṃ tu tad bhuktam ubhayaṃ nāśayed idam || 2.55 ||
pūjitam iti | yāmāt pūjitam annaṃ sāmarthyaṃ vīryaṃ ca dadāti, apūjitaṃ ca tad bhuktaṃ ubhayaṃ nāśayati tasmāt pūjitam adyād iti prakṛtastutiḥ phalārthatve (na ni) nityatāpatteḥ
|| 2.55 ||
nocchiṣṭaṃ kasyacid dadyān nādyād etat tathāntarā |
na caivātyaśanaṃ kuryān na cocchiṣṭaḥ kvacid vrajet || 2.56 ||
nocchiṣṭam iti | ucchiṣṭaṃ kasyacid api na dadyāt | tathā antarā sāyaṃprātaḥ kalayor madhye nādyāt sāyaṃ prātar bhojanasyaucityaprāptatvāt antarāśabdasya vā madhyavācitvād evaṃ labhyate | atimātrabhojanaṃ na kuryāt | ucchiṣṭaś ca na kvacid gacchaet || 2.56 ||
anārogyam anāyuṣyam asvargyaṃ cātibhojanam |
apuṇyaṃ lokavidviṣṭaṃ tasmāt tat parivarjayet || 2.57 ||
anārogyam iti | yasmād rogahetutvāt anārogyakṛd iti ca prāṇaharaṃ viṣṭhādikādyutpādād apy nāyuṣyaṃ ca, asvargyaṃ ca [a]dṛṣṭārthajanakatvenāpi śāstranodanāsāmarthyāt atikrameṇa karmāntarārjitasvargopabhogapratibandhatvāt | apuṇyaṃ ca taddhetutvāt, lokavidviṣṭaṃ ca bahubhojinam avalokya garhaṇaṃ tat parivarjayed iti saṃbhūtadoṣakathanena prakṛti[ta]nindārthavādo vidveṣaṇarthaḥ || 2.57 ||
brāhmeṇa vipras tīrthena nityakālam upaspṛśet |
kāyatraidaśikābhyāṃ vā na pitryeṇa kadācana || 2.58 ||
brāhmaṇeti | brāhmeṇa tīrthena prājāpatyadaivābhyāṃ vā vipro yāvajjītam upaspṛśet | pitryeṇa punaḥ kadācid iti nityānuvādaḥ itarastutyarthaḥ | avidhānād eva pitryasyāprapteḥ vipragrahaṇaṃ kṣatriyādipradarśanārtham, “upaspṛśya dvijo nityam” iti etayor api prakṛtatvāt
|| 2.58 ||
aṅguṣṭhamūlasya jate brāhmaṃ tīrthaṃ pracakṣate |
kāyam aṅgulimūle ‘gre daivaṃ pitryaṃ tayor adhaḥ || 2.59 ||
arthasiddhatvāl lakṣaṇam āha – aṅguṣṭhamūlasyeti | “brāhmaṇasya dakṣiṇahaste pañcatīrthāni bhavanti” iti pracetasaḥ smaraṇāt | dakṣiṇahastāṅguṣṭhamūlasyādhobhāge brāhmaṃ tīrthaṃ pracakṣate | kāyam aṅgulīnāṃ mūle | agre daivam, aṅgulīnām evāgraśabdasya sākāṅkṣatvāt | pitryaṃ tayor adhaḥ | adhastayor ity asyāpi sākāṅkṣatvāt prakṛtenāṅguṣṭhāṅgulinā saṃbandhaḥ
|| 2.59 ||
trir ācāmed apaḥ pūrvaṃ dviḥ pramṛjyāt tato mukham |
khāni caiva spṛśed adbhir ātmānaṃ śira eva ca || 2.60 ||
ācamanasyālaukikatvāt svarūpam āha trir iti | prathamaṃ trīn vārān apaḥ tīrthenācāmet | tataḥ āsyaṃ dvau vārau api śodhayet | tad anu ca khāni indriyacchidrāṇi adbhiḥ spṛśet | tataḥ ātmasthānaṃ hṛdayam, atho śira iti || 2.60 ||
anuṣṇābhir aphenābhir adbhis tīrthena dharmavit |
śaucepsuḥ sarvadācāmed ekānte prāg udaṅmukhaḥ || 2.61 ||
anuṣṇābhir iti | “aśṛtābhiḥ” iti śaṅkhasmaraṇāt agnisaṃparkarahitābhiḥ, phenaśūnyābhiḥ adbhiḥ uktena tīrthena vidvān śuddhikāmaḥ ekānte śucau deśe prāṅmukhaḥ udaṅmukho vā ācāmet || 2.61 ||
hṛdgābhiḥ pūyate vipraḥ kaṇṭhagābhis tu bhūmipaḥ |
vaiśyo ‘dbhiḥ prāśitābhis tu śūdraḥ spṛṣṭābhir antataḥ || 2.62 ||
kiṃpramāṇā āpaḥ ācāmed ity āha hṛdgābhir iti | hṛdayaprāptābhir adbhir vipraḥ śudhyati | kaṇṭhaprāptābhiḥ kṣatriyaḥ, vaiśyaḥ prāśitābhiḥ tālugatābhiḥ, “hṛtkaṇṭhatālugābhiḥ” iti yājñavalkyasmaraṇāt | śudro ‘ntataḥ, ācamanasya prakṛtatvāt jihvauṣṭhāsyānte spṛṣṭābhiḥ
|| 2.62 ||
uddhṛte dakṣiṇe pāṇāv upavīty ucyate dvijaḥ |
savye prācīna āvītī nivītī kaṇṭhasajjane || 2.63 ||
upavītyādīnām aprasiddhatvāt lakṣaṇam āha ācamanāvasare ca upavītasyācamanāṅgatvārtham uddhṛta iti | kārpāsam upavītaṃ syād uktalakṣaṇam | upavīte kaṇṭhasajjane kaṇṭhaprakṣipte sati nivītī bhavati | evaṃ sthite sati uddhṛte dakṣiṇe bāhau upavītī bhavati | vāma uddhṛte prācīnāvītīty evaṃ lakṣaṇdvayaprasiddhyartham iha | evaṃ nivītalakṣaṇaṃ manuṣyakāryārthaṃ ca “nivītaṃ manuṣyāṇām” iti śruteḥ | prācīnāvītīti saṃjñā | vṛttānurodhāt tu prācīna āvītīty uktam | etac ca pitrye karmaṇi || 2.63 ||
mekhalām ajinaṃ daṇḍam upavītaṃ kamaṇḍalum |
apsu prāsya vinaṣṭāni gṛhṇītānyāni mantravat || 2.64 ||
mekhalām iti | vinaṣṭāni mekhalādīni apsu prakṣipyānyāni samantrakāṇi gṛhṇīyāt tāni | tathā ca baudhāyanena sarveṣāṃ mantra āmnātaḥ mekhalādibhiś ca śāstravadgrahaṇaiḥ saha pratipattiniyamāt “gṛhṇītānyāni mantravat” iti ca kamaṇḍalor apy uktatvāt kamaṇḍalor apy udakārthaṃ śāstravadgrahaṇam ity avasīyate | punargrahaṇāc copanayanottarakālam apy eṣām anuvṛttir anumīyate nopanayanagataiva || 2.64 ||
keśāntaḥ ṣoḍaśe varṣe brāhmaṇasya vidhīyate |
rājanyabandhor dvāviṃśe vaiśyasya dvyadhike tataḥ || 2.65 ||
keśānta iti | keśāntākhyaḥ saṃskāraḥ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ yathākramaṃ ṣoḍaśadvāviṃśacaturviṃśeṣu varṣeṣu vidhīyate || 2.65 ||
amantrikā tu kāryeyaṃ strīṇām āvṛd aśeṣataḥ |
saṃskārārthaṃ śarīrasya yathākālaṃ yathākramam || 2.66 ||
“prāṅnābhivardhanāt puṃsaḥ” iti puṃgrahaṇasya stryarthatve pramāṇābhāvāt strīṇām aprāpta ity ata āha amantriketi | eṣā sakalasaṃskārapaddhatiḥ kālakramānatikrameṇa strīṇāṃ saṃskārārthaṃ mantravarjitā kāryā || 2.66 ||
vaivāhiko vidhiḥ strīṇāṃ saṃskāro vaidikaḥ smṛtaḥ |
patisevā gurau vāso gṛhārtho ‘gniparikriyā || 2.67 ||
evam upanayane ‘py amantrake prāpte āha vaivāhika iti | yad vivāhavidhānaṃ tad evāsāṃ vaidikasaṃskāraḥ upanayanasthāne | patisevā ca guruśuśrūṣāsthāne, gṛhakṛtyaṃ cāgniparicaraṇasthāne || 2.68 |||
eṣa prokto dvijātīnām aupanāyaniko vidhiḥ |
utpattivyañjakaḥ puṇyaḥ karmayogaṃ nibodhata || 2.68 ||
eṣa iti | eṣa dvijātīnām upanayanabhavo vidhir uktaḥ utpatter vyañjakaḥ prakāśakaḥ jātasyāpy anupanītasyājā(jñā)tasamatvāt puṇyahetutvāt puṇyaḥ | idānīm upanītasyopanayanakarmaṇi saṃbandhaḥ tac chṛṇuta || 2.68 ||
upanīya guruḥ śiṣyaṃ śikṣayec chaucam āditaḥ |
ācāram agnikāryaṃ ca saṃdhyopāsanam eva ca || 2.69 ||
upanīyeti | guruḥ śiṣyopanayanaṃ krtvā ādau prathamata eva śaucaṃ śikṣayet | aśuddhasya sarvatrānadhikārāt | tat ācāraṃ pratyutthānādi, agnikāryaṃ ca sāyaṃprātaragniprakṣepādi sāndhyakarmānuṣṭhānaṃ ca śikṣayet || 2.69 ||
adhyeṣyamāṇas tv ācānto yathāśāstram udaṅmukhaḥ |
brahmāñjalikṛto ‘dhyāpyo laghuvāsā jitendriyaḥ || 2.70 ||
adhyeṣyamāṇa iti | pratyāsannādhyayanaḥ śiṣyaḥ kṛtaśāstracoditetikartavyatāyuktācamanaḥ kṛtavakṣyamāṇabrahmāñjaliḥ sthūlavasanaḥ san jitendriyaḥ udaṅmukho ‘dhyāpyaḥ ||2.70 ||
brahmārambhe ‘vasāne ca pādau grāhyau guroḥ sadā |
saṃhatya hastāv adhyeyaṃ sa hi brahmāñjaliḥ smṛtaḥ || 2.71 ||
brahmārambha iti | anvāhikavedapāṭhārambhāntayoḥ sadā ahar ahaḥ guroḥ pādopasaṃgraṇakaṃ kartavyam | saṃhatau ca kacchapake iti prasiddhasanniveśena hastau kṛtvādhyetavyam | yasmāt sa brahmāñjaliśabdenocyata ity ṛṣibhiḥ smṛtaḥ ity alaukikatvāt brahmāñjalisvarūpanirūpaṇam || 2.71 ||
vyatyastapāṇinā kāryam upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ guroḥ |
savyena savyaḥ spraṣṭavyo dakṣiṇena tu dakṣiṇaḥ || 2.72 ||
kathaṃ punaḥ padopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kartavyam ity ata āha vyatyastapāṇineti | itaretaradigarpitahastena guroḥ pādopasarpaṇaṃ kartavyam | vāmena ca hastena vāmapādaḥ sparśanīyaḥ dakṣiṇena tu dakṣiṇaḥ || 2.72 ||
adhyeṣyamāṇaḥ tu gurur nityakālam atandritaḥ |
adhīṣva bho iti brūyād virāmo ‘stv iti cāramet || 2.73 ||
adhyeṣyamāṇam iti | pratyāsannādhyayanaṃ śiṣyam analaso guruḥ sarvakālam adhīṣva bho iti brūyat | virāmo ‘stv iti coktvā anantaram adhyāpanān nivarteta || 2.73 ||
brahmaṇaḥ praṇavaṃ kuryād ādāv ante ca sarvadā |
sravaty anoṃkṛtaṃ pūrvaṃ parastāc ca viśīryati || 2.74 ||
brahmaṇa iti | vedapāṭhasyārambhāntayoḥ oṃkāraṃ sarvadā grahaṇārthādhyāpanakālāt kālāntare ‘pi kuryāt | yasmād yasya brahmaṇaḥ pūrvam anoṃkṛtaḥ tat sravati gṛhītam api vismṛtya gacchati | parastāc ca viśīryate | yasya na kṛtaḥ tad viśīryate naiva gṛhyate || 2.74 ||
prākkūlān paryupāsīnaḥ pavitraiś caiva pāvitaḥ |
prāṇāyāmais tribhiḥ pūtas tata oṃkāram arhati || 2.75 ||
prākkūlān iti | “prāṇopasparśanaṃ darbhaiḥ prākkūleṣv āsanaṃ ca” iti gautamasmaraṇāt prāgagreṣu ca darbheṣv āsīnaḥ, ata eva ca pavitrair darbhair eva prāṇasthānād hṛdayaspṛṣṭaiḥ pavitrīkṛtaḥ prāṇāyāmaiś ca śvāsanirodharūpaiḥ “tribhiḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ tripañcadaśamātrā” iti gautamoktaprāṇāyāmaiḥ pavitrīkṛtaḥ tataḥ oṃkāraṃ pūrvoktaṃ kartuṃ yogyo bhavati || 2.75 ||
akāraṃ cāpy ukāraṃ ca makāraṃ ca prajāpatiḥ |
vedatrayān niraduhad bhūr bhuvaḥ svar itīti ca || 2.76 ||
tribhya eva tu vedebhyaḥ pādaṃ pādam adūduhat |
tad ity ṛco ‘syāḥ sāvitryāḥ parameṣṭhī prajāpatiḥ || 2.77 ||
“brahmaṇaḥ praṇavaṃ kuryāt” ity asyārthavādam āha akāram iti | tribhya iti akārokāramakārātmakaḥ kiloṃkāraḥ | etac cākṣaratrayaṃ vedatrayāt sārabhūtaṃ prajāpatir uddhṛtavān | tathā bhūr bhuvaḥ svar ity etac ca mahāvyāhṛtitrayam uddhṛtavān idaṃ (itthaṃ) ceyaṃ vyāhṛtistutiḥ | adhyayanakāle tadvidhānārthā atrākasmāt stutir vartata iti prayojanāt “tat savitur vareṇyam” ity asyāḥ savitṛdevatākāyā ṛcaḥ pādaṃ pādaṃ yathākramaṃ tribhya eva vedebhyaḥ parameṣṭhī hiraṇyagarbhaḥ prajāpatiḥ uddhṛtavān ity ayam api adhyayanakāla(ḥ) vidhyarthaḥ sāvitryarthavādaḥ || 2.76–77 ||
etad akṣaram etāṃ ca japan vyāhṛtipūrvakām |
saṃdhyayor vedavid vipro vedapuṇyena yujyate || 2.78 ||
etat oṃkārākhyam akṣaraṃ etāṃ ca sāvitrīṃ bhūrbhuvaḥsvarityetad vyāhṛtipūrvikāṃ vedārthajño vipraḥ saṃdhyayor japan samastavedapuṇyena saṃbadhyate iti vidvān brāhmaṇaḥ saṃdhyājapasaṃbhūtaphalamāhātmyakathanena prakṛtavidhitrayastutiḥ || 2.78 ||
sahastrakṛtvas tv abhyasya bahir etat trikaṃ dvijaḥ |
mahato ‘py enaso māsāt tvacevāhir vimucyate || 2.79 ||
sahasrakṛtv iti | etat trayaṃ sahasravārān ahar māsaṃ yāvat bahir grāmād dvijo ‘bhyasya mahat brahmahatyādy api pāpam apanudati | jīrṇacarmeva sarpa iti rahasyaprāyaścittagatamāhātmyakathanena prakṛtavidhitrayastutiḥ || 2.79 ||
etayarcā visaṃyuktaḥ kāle ca kriyayā svayā |
brahmakṣatriyaviḍyonir garhaṇāṃ yāti sādhuṣu || 2.80 ||
etayeti | etayā ṛcā sāvitryā viyuktaḥ vedādhyayanābhāvāt upanayanakāle cātmīyayopanayanākhyakriyayā viyuktaḥ brāhmanakṣatriyavaiśyajātīyaḥ nindyatāṃ śiṣṭaviṣaye prāpnotīty anuṣṭhānanindādvāreṇa vidhitrayopanayanyoḥ stutiḥ || 2.80 ||
oṃkārapūrvikās tisro mahāvyāhṛtayo ‘vyayāḥ |
tripadā caiva sāvitrī vijñeyaṃ brahmaṇo mukham || 2.81 ||
oṃkārapūrvikā iti | oṃkārapūrvikās tisra etāḥ bhūrādyā mahāvyāhṛtayaḥ avyayāḥ avināśinyaḥ tatphalsya dīrghakālatvāt pādatrayam uktā caiṣā “tat savitur varaṇyam” iti sāvitrī brahmaṇo mukhaṃ vedaprāptidvāraṃ vijñeyam | ataḥ svādhyāyādāv etad adhyeyam iti prakṛtārthastutiḥ || 2.81 ||
yo ‘dhīte ‘hany ahany etāṃ trīṇi varṣāṇy atandritaḥ |
sa brahma param abhyeti vāyubhūtaḥ khamūrtimān || 2.82 ||
ya iti | ya tāṃ sāvitrīṃ pratyahaṃ trīṇi varṣāṇi analaso ‘dhīte sa vāyur iva sarvatrāpratihatagatiḥ khamūrtimān ākāśarūpaḥ san sarvavyāptitvāt paraṃ brahma paramātmalakṣaṇam ābhimukhyena prāpnotīti japamāhātmyakathanena prakṛtavidhistutiḥ
|| 2.82 ||
ekākṣaraṃ paraṃ brahma prāṇāyāmāḥ paraṃ tapaḥ |
sāvitryās tu paraṃ nāsti maunāt satyaṃ viśiṣyate || 2.83 ||
ekākṣaram iti | oṃkārākhyam etad ekākṣaraṃ paraṃ brahma śabdaparabrahmaṇor vedāntadṛṣṭatoḥ, prāṇāyāmaḥ kṛcchrāditapobhyaḥ prakṛṣṭaṃ tapaḥ sāvitryāḥ (pra)sakāśād anyat prakṛṣṭaṃ japanīyaṃ nāstīty evaṃ prakṛtoṃkāraprāṇāyāmasāvitrīvidhīnāṃ stutiḥ | maunāt satyaṃ viśiṣyate | maunaṃ anṛtadoṣaparihārātirekeṇa satyavacanenābhyudayotpādād iti stutiprasaṅgena satyābhidhānastutiḥ || 2.83 ||
kṣaranti sarvā vaidikyo juhotiyajatikriyāḥ |
akṣaraṃ tv akṣayaṃ jñeyaṃ brahma caiva prajāpatiḥ || 2.84 ||
kṣarantīti | sarvāṇi vedacoditāni homayāgānuṣṭhānāni naśvarāṇi tatphalakṣayatvāt | akṣaraṃ punaḥ etad oṃkārākhyaṃ akṣayaṃ jñeyaṃ tadarthabhāvanādvāreṇa brahmāvāptyākhyasya phalasyākṣayatvāt | brahma ca prajāpatyākhyaṃ etad eva || 2.84 ||
śabdārthayor vedāntadṛṣṭyotpādita (śabdārtho yo vedāntadṛṣṭyotpāditaḥ) vaidikakarmanindayā prakṛtārthastutiḥ |
vidhiyajñāj japayajño viśiṣṭo daśabhir guṇaiḥ |
upāṃśuḥ syāc chataguṇaḥ sāhasrao mānasaḥ smṛtaḥ || 2.85 ||
vidhiyajñād iti | bāhyavyāpārasaṃpādyād agniṣṭomādeḥ japayajñaḥ viśiṣṭo daśabhir guṇaiḥ daśaguṇaphalatvāt | sa eva copāṃśuḥ yaṃ paro na śṛṇoti sa śataguṇaḥ | manovyāpārasaṃpādyaś ca sahasraguṇa iti abhyāsātiśayāpekṣayā dravyatyāgād api cāntaḥsaṃyamasya duḥsahatvāt vaidikakarmaphalādhikyena japamāhātmyakathanaṃ prakṛtavidhistutyartham || 2.85 ||
ye pākayajñāś catvāro vidhiyajñasamanvitāḥ |
sarve te japayajñasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm || 2.86 ||
ye pākayajñādayaś catvāro jyotiṣṭomādividhiyajñasahitāḥ te sarve japayajñasya ṣaḍaśabhāgaṃ mūlyārthaṃ na labhante iti śrautasmārtakarmanindayā idaṃ ātmajñānavān (-vatāṃ) prakṛṣṭaphaladarśanena japamāhātmyakathanaṃ prakṛtavidhistutyartham || 2.86 ||
japyenaiva tu saṃsidhyed brāhmaṇo nātra saṃśayaḥ |
kuryād anyan na vā kuryān maitro brāhmaṇa ucyate || 2.87 ||
japyenaiva tv iti | japyenaiva brāhmaṇo mokṣaṃ prāpnuyāt atra saṃśaya eva nāsti katham alpaprayāsena mahatphalaṃ prāpyata iti | dravyatyāgād apy antaḥsaṃyamasya duṣkaratvāt | evaṃ ca kuryād anyat tādarthyena jyotiṣṭomādikam asau karma yadi vā na kuryāt yataḥ sarvabhūtopakārarato brāhmaṇaḥ syād iti śāstreṇocyate | agniṣṭomīyādi (agnīṣomīyādi) vadhena kuto maitraḥ iti | evaṃ śrautasmārtakarmānuṣṭhānāśakto japyaparaḥ puruṣaḥ saṃsiddhyanuvādo (saṃsiddhytīty anuvādo) ‘yaṃ prakṛtavidhistutyarthaḥ || 2.88 ||
indriyāṇāṃ vicaratāṃ viṣayeṣv apahāriṣu |
saṃyame yatnam ātiṣṭhed vidvān yanteva vājinām || 2.88 ||
