indriyāṇām iti | indriyāṇāṃ cakṣurādīnāṃ viṣayeṣu rūpādiṣu apahāriṣu ca upabhogārtham ākarṣanaśīleṣu yathecchaṃ pravartamānānāṃ niyamane prayatnaṃ kuryāt, yathā śikṣitaḥ sārathiḥ rathyārabdhānām aśvānāṃ niyamane yatnaṃ karoti || 2.88 ||
ekādaśendriyāṇy āhur yāni pūrve manīṣiṇaḥ |
tāni samyak pravakṣyāmi yathāvad anupūrvaśaḥ || 2.89 ||
ekādaśeti | yāni pūrve manvādayo vidvāṃsaḥ indriyāṇy uktavantaḥ tāni yathāvat kārtsnyenānupūrvaśaḥ karmeṇa samyaṅ yathārūpaṃ buddhyatha tathā vakṣyāmi || 2.89 ||
śrotraṃ tvak cakṣuṣī jihvā nāsikā caiva pañcamī |
pāyūpasthaṃ hastapādaṃ vāk caiva daśamo smṛtā || 2.90 ||
tāni ca śrotram iti | tvak carma tātsthyāt | atra sparśanendriyaṃ tvak ucyate | cakṣuṣī iti adhiṣṭhānabhedāt dvivacanam | śrotrādau ca tadādāraśaktyaikyālambanam ekavacanam | pāyuḥ apānam upasthaḥ liṅgam | vāk śabdābhivyañjakaśarīraikadeśaḥ || 2.90 ||
buddhīndryāṇi pañcaiṣāṃ śrotrādīny anupūrvaśaḥ |
karmendriyāṇi pañcaiṣāṃ pāyvādīni pracakṣate || 2.91 ||
buddhīndryāṇīti | eṣāṃ madhyāt śrotrādīni kramena pañca buddheḥ śabdādiviṣayavijñānasya janakāni, paṇca pāyvādīni visargādikarmārthāni manvādayaḥ kathayantīti kāryabhedāt vargadvayavibhāgaḥ || 2.91 ||
ekādaśaṃ mano jñeyaṃ svaguṇenobhayātmakam |
yasmin jite jitāv etau bhavataḥ pañcakī gaṇau || 2.92 ||
ekādaśam iti | manaḥ svaguṇena saṃkalpākhyena śubhāśubhasvabhāvenobharūpam atraikādaśaṃ vijñeyam | yasmin vaśīkṛte sti etau pañcaparimāṇau vargau vaśīkṛtau havataḥ manovyāpāramūlatvād indriyapravṛtteḥ || 2.92 ||
indriyāṇāṃ prasaṅgena doṣam ṛcchaty asaṃśayam |
saṃniyamya tu tāny eva tataḥ siddhiṃ niyacchati || 2.93 ||
adhunā indriyasaṃyamaprayojanam āha indryāṇām iti | indriyāṇāṃ viṣayāsaktyā dṛṣṭādṛṣṭadoṣaṃ niścitaṃ puruṣaḥ prāpnoti | tāny eva saṃyamya punaḥ dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthasiddhiṃ niścayena prāpnoti || 2.93 ||
na jātu kāmaḥ kāmānām upabhogena śāmyati |
haviṣā kṛṣṇavartmeva bhūya evābhivardhate || 2.94 ||
athopabhogatṛṣṇayā kim indriyāṇāṃ viṣayanivṛttir nocyate, kiṃ saktiniṣedhenocyate ity ata āha na jātv iti | na kadācid abhilāṣaḥ kāmyamānānām arthānām upasevanena nivartate | praty uta ājyādināgnir ivādhikataraṃ vṛddhim eti || 2.94 ||
yaś caitān prāpnuyāt sarvān yaś caitān kevalāṃs tyajet |
prāpaṇāt sarvakāmānāṃ parityāgo viśiṣyate || 2.95 ||
evaṃ ca ya iti | yaḥ kāmān sakalān seveta yo vā kevalān anupabhuktvaiva tyajet tayoḥ sarvakāmopabhogād api parityāgo viśiṣyate | yasmād upabhuktapūrveṣv api asati cittasaṃyame punar upabhogecchā jāyate | tadasaṃpattau cāvaśyaṃbhāvi duḥkham, tasmān mūlata eva tṛṣṇātyāgo varaḥ || 2.95 ||
na tathaitāni śakyante saṃniyantum asevayā |
viṣayeṣu prajuṣṭāni yathā jñānena nityaśaḥ || 2.96 ||
evaṃ tarhi viṣayaśūnyadehāvasthānamātra upāya ity ata āha neti | etānīndiryāṇi viṣayāsaktyā prakṣṭadoṣajanakāni na tathā rūpādiviṣayānupabhogena samyak niyantuṃ śakyante yathā viṣayagatadoṣabhāvanāvijñānena nityakālaṃ niyantuṃ pāryate || 2.96 ||
vedās tyāgaś ca yajñāś ca niyamāś ca tapāṃsi ca |
na vipraduṣṭabhāvasya siddhiṃ gacchanti karhicit || 2.97 ||
vedā iti | japadānasnānayāgādikṛcchrādīni duṣṭacittasya na kadācit saṃpannāni bhavantīti
|| 2.97 ||
śrutvā spṛṣṭvā ca dṛṣṭvā ca bhuktvā ghrātvā ca yo naraḥ |
na hṛṣyati glāyati vā sa vijñeyo jitendriyaḥ || 2.98 ||
śrutveti | yaś śrotratvakcakṣurnāsikeneṣṭān śabdasparśarasarūpagandhān upalabhya na harṣam upaiti, kutsitāṃś copalabhya na glānim eti, sa jitendriyo boddhavyaḥ || 2.98 ||
indriyāṇāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ yady ekaṃ kṣaratīndriyam |
tenāsya kṣarati prajñā dṛteḥ pādād ivodakam || 2.99 ||
sakalendriyaviṣayasya saṃyame yatnaḥ kāryaḥ yasmāt indriyāṇām iti | indriyāṇāṃ madhyāt yadi punaḥ ekam apīndriyaṃ yathecchaṃ svaviṣaye pravartate tadā svendriyāntarasaṃyamavijñānam api naśyati yathā udakaparipūrṇāc carmaṇaḥ ekapādānāvaraṇāt sarvam ambho nisḥsarati || 2.99 ||
vaśe kṛtvendriyagrāmaṃ saṃyamya ca manas tathā |
sarvān saṃsādhayed arthān akṣiṇvan yogatas tanum || 2.100 ||
evam asañjitendriyasya manaskasya na vedādisiddhiḥ tasmāt vaśa iti | śaktyā śarīram apīḍayan indriyasamūhaṃ vijitya manaś ca niyamya sarvān vedādhigamādīn arthān saṃpādayet
|| 2.110 ||
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japaṃs tiṣṭhet sāvitrīm ārkadarśanāt |
paścimāṃ tu sadāsīta samyag ṛkṣavibhāvanāt || 2.101 ||
“sajyotiṣyājyotiṣo darśanād vāgyataḥ” iti gautamasamaraṇāt [GDh 2.11], sanakṣatrakālād ārabhya pūrvām iti | yady apy atra tiṣṭhatyāḥ paro vidhipratyayaḥ śrūyate tathāpi upalabhyamānaśrutimūlatvāt yajjapo ‘tra pradhānam | sthānāsanayos tv aṅgatā | tathā ca (yac ca) yajurvedabrāhmaṇe japater eva paro vidhipratyayaḥ śrūyate | yad āha “athātaḥ saṃdhyopāsanavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ” ity upakramya “pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ sthitaḥ san sāvitrīm etad akṣarām etāṃ ca japan vyāhṛtipūrvikāṃ saṃdhyayoḥ” iti darśanāt | praṇatavyāhṛtipūrvikāṃ japed ādityadarśanaṃ yāvat iti vākyārthaḥ | paścimāṃ punar āsīṇaḥ sāvitrīṃ japet uktagautamīyāt sadarkakālād ārabhya samyak nakṣatradarśanāt yāvat || 2.101 ||
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japaṃs tiṣṭhan naiśam eno vyapohati |
paścimāṃ tu samāsīno malaṃ hanti divākṛtam || 2.102 ||
pūrvām iti | pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ tiṣṭhan sāvitrīṃ japan saṃcitaṃ pratanu rātryutpannaṃ pāpam apanudati sarvāpanodane prāyaścittakāṇḍānarthakyāt | evaṃ paścimasaṃdhyāyām upaviṣṭo japan divākṛtapāpam apahanti || 2.102 ||
na tiṣṭhati tu yaḥ pūrvāṃ nopāste yaś ca paścimām |
sa śūdravad bahiṣkāryaḥ sarvasmād dvijakarmaṇaḥ || 2.103 ||
evaṃ kathaṃcid akṛtainaso ‘nanuṣṭhānaprāptau nityatām āha neti | yaḥ punaḥ pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ nānutiṣṭhati yo vā paścimāṃ nopāste so ‘dhyayanadānādidvijakarmaṇaḥ sarvasmāc chūdravat pariharaṇīyaḥ | ity evaṃ nindeyam avaśyānuṣṭhānārthā || 2.103 ||
apāṃ samīpe niyato naityakaṃ vidhim āsthitaḥ |
sāvitrīm apy adhīyīta gatvāraṇyaṃ samāhitaḥ || 2.104 ||
brahmayajñavidhyarthaṃ brahmacāriṇām anudinam āha apām iti | grāmād bahir viviktaṃ pradeśaṃ gatvā niyataḥ śuciḥ samāhitaḥ saṃyatamanāḥ nityo ‘yaṃ brahmayajñavidhir ity evam āsthitaḥ utpannāsthaḥ apāṃ samīpe mantrāntarapāṭhāt sāvitrīm api japet || 2.104 ||
vedopakaraṇe caiva svādhyāye caiva naityake |
nānurodho ‘sty anadhyāye homamantreṣu caiva hi || 2.105 ||
svādhyāyavidhivat prakaraṇāntareṇānadhyāyāmnānāt vedavedāṅgoccāraṇadharmatvāt (śaṃkāyāṃ) brahmayajñādāv apy anadhyāyaprāptāv āha vedopakaraṇa iti | vedopakārake niruktādāv aṅge, nityabhave ca svādhyāye brahmayajñākhye homārthaṃ mantroccāraṇe vānadhyāyānurodho nāsti | vedopakaraṇādīnāṃ prasaṅgāc ca lāghavārtham(an)abhidhānam | hiśabdaḥ āgamaprasūcakaḥ || 2.105 ||
naityake nāsty anadhyāyo brahmasatraṃ hi tat smṛtam |
brahmāhuihutaṃ puṇyam anadhyāyavaṣaṭkṛtam || 2.106 ||
atrārthavādam āha naityaka iti | naityake ekasmin brahmayajñe anadhyāyo nāsti | yasmāt brahmasatraṃ tat manvādibhiḥ smṛtam | ataḥ satatapravṛttagavāmayanādisatratulyatvāt sarvadaiva tad anuṣṭheyam | kim asya sattrasādṛśyam ity ata āha brahmāhutituam ityādi | sattraṃ somāhutyāhutaṃ bhavati | idam api vedoccāraṇāhutyāhutaṃ brahma, āhutir iva āhutyarthatvāt puṇyahetutvāc ca sattratulyam | sattraṃ ca pradānārthena vaṣaṭkāreṇa yuktaṃ bhavati | idam api anadhyāyādhyayanāt anadhyāyavaṣaṭkṛtam || 2.106 ||
yaḥ svādhyāyam adhīte ‘bdaṃ vidhinā niyataḥ śuciḥ |
tasya nityaṃ kṣaraty eṣa payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ madhu || 2.107 ||
ya iti | evaṃ brahmayajñākhyaṃ svādhyāyam araṇye gamanādyuktavidhānena saṃyatamanāḥ śuciḥ san yaḥ saṃvatsaram adhīte tasyaiṣa payodadhighṛtamadhūni nityaṃ dadātīti | evaṃ ca nityatve ‘pi phalakāmanayā phalam apy ato bhavati darśapaurṇamāsvat || 2.107 ||
agnīndhanaṃ bhaikṣacaryām adhaḥśayyāṃ guror hitam |
ā samāvartanāt kuryāt kṛtopanayano dvijaḥ || 2.108 ||
agnīndhanam iti | agnisamidādhānabhaikṣacaraṇakhaṭvādyaśanagurugṛhopalepanādyātmaka-guruhitāni samāvartanākhyavratakālaṃ yāvat upanayanakālottarakālaṃ dvijaḥ kuryāt | pradhānānupayāyitvenāṅgānāṃ yāvad brahmacaryam eṣām anuvṛttisiddhyarthaṃ punarvacanaṃ niyamāya bhavantīty etāvanty eva samāvartane nivartante | anyāni punaḥ saṃdhyopāsanādīni āśramāntarāviruddhāni yāvajjīvaṃ bhavantīti | yathā ca gautamaḥ: “uttareṣāṃ caitad avirodhi” [GDh 3.10] iti | ata eva caitāni ā samāvartanāt kuryāt | yāni punar arvāg api nivartante ity evaṃ vidhiviparītaniyamo na bhavati pradhānānuvartinyāyāvādhaprasaṅgāc ca || 2.108 ||
ācāryaputraḥ śuśrūṣur jñānado dhārmikaḥ śuciḥ |
āptaḥ śakto ‘rthadaḥ sādhuḥ svo ‘dhyāpyā daśa dharmataḥ || 2.109 ||
ācāryaputra iti | ācāryaputraḥ paricaryāparaḥ vidyāpradaḥ dharmānuṣṭhānaśīlaḥ mṛdvāryādiśaucayuktaḥ bandhuḥ grahaṇadhāraṇasamarthaḥ paṇapūrvam apy arthadātā ārjavopetaḥ ātmanā upanītaḥ ete daśa dharmārtham adhyāpyāḥ | arthadaḥ śuśrūṣuḥ “jñānād adhyāpanād api dharmo ‘sti niyamādṛṣṭarūpeṇa” iti vacanāt || 2.109 ||
nāpṛṣṭaḥ kasyacid brūyān na cānyāyena pṛcchataḥ |
jānann api hi medhāvī jaḍaval loka ācaret || 2.110 ||
nāpṛṣṭa iti | pūrvoktavyatirekeṇānyasya kasyacid adhīyānasya visvarādikṛtavataḥ apṛṣṭo na kathayet | nāpi vitaṇḍayā pṛcchataḥ | kiṃ tarhi | tatra jānann api prājñaḥ badhira iva loke vyavaharet | atrārthavādo niṣedhārthaḥ || 2.110 ||
adharmeṇa ca yaḥ prāha yaś cādharmeṇa pṛcchati |
tayor anyataraḥ praiti vidveṣaṃ vādhigacchati || 2.111 ||
adharmeṇeti | uktamaryādātikrameṇa yaḥ prāha yo vā pṛcchati tayor anyataraḥ yo ‘tikramakṛt sa mriyate dveṣaṃ vā loke bhajate || 2.111 ||
dharmārthau yatra na syātāṃ śuśrūṣā vāpi tadvidhā |
tatra vidyā na vaptavyā śubhaṃ bījam ivoṣare || 2.112 ||
pūrvoktanimittānāṃ madhyād dharmādinimittatrayasya upāyatvaṃ darśayitum āha dharmārthāv iti | yad adhyāpane dharmaḥ paṇapūrvo vā artho na bhavet paṇapūrvakasya bhṛtakādhyāpakatvena niṣiddhatvāt | paricaryā adhyāpanānurūpā paripuṣṭaṃ bījam iva aṅkurajananāsamarthe bhūpradeśe || 2.112 ||
vidyayaiva samaṃ kāmaṃ martavyaṃ brahmavādinā |
āpady api hi ghorāyāṃ na tv enām iriṇe vapet || 2.113 ||
evaṃ ca vidyayeti | varam adattavidyenaiva vidyādhyāpanśīlena martavyaṃ na punaḥ kaṣṭāyām apy āpadi uktādhyāpyābhāve ‘pi enāṃ vidyāṃ iriṇe ūṣara iva dharmādiphalotpādanśūnye vapet | anena caitad darśayati yad avaśyaṃ dātavyā vidyeti | anyathā kāmaṃ vidyayaiva saha martavyam ity etad abhāvasaṃsucanam ācāryasaṃbandhenopapadyate | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “yo ha vai vidyām adhītyārthine na brūyāt sa kāryahā syāt” iti | asya pratiṣedhasya purākalparūpo ‘yam arthavādaḥ || 2.113 ||
vidyā brāhmaṇam etyāha śevadhiṣṭe ‘smi rakṣa mām |
asūyakāya māṃ mādās tathā syāṃ vīryavattamā || 2.114 ||
yam eva tu śuciṃ vidyā niyataṃ brahmacāriṇam |
tasmai māṃ brūhi viprāya nidhipāyāpramādine || 2.115 ||
vidyā brāhmaṇam etyāheti | yam eva tv iti | vidyā kila vigrahavatī kiṃcid brāhmaṇam āgatya nidhir iva tavāham, ato rakṣa māṃ ity āha | rakṣā ca asūyakam apātraṃ māṃ mādhyāpaya | tathā saty aham atiśayena tava dṛṣṭādṛṣṭakāryakaraṇasamarthā bhavāmi | yaṃ punaḥ mṛdvāryādiśaucayuktaṃ niyataṃ saṃyatendriyaṃ brahmacāriṇaṃ astrīsaṃprayogaṃ jānīyāt prādhānyād asya pṛthag upadeśaḥ | taṃ vipraṃ vidyānidhipālanasamartham avismaraṇaśīlaṃ aparmādinam anabhyāsaśūnyam adhyāpayeti || 2.114–15 ||
brahma yas tv ananujñātam adhīyānād avāpnuyāt |
sa brahmasteyasaṃyukto narakaṃ pratipadyate || 2.116 ||
brahmeti | anyān avyāpayataḥ avismaraṇārthaṃ cābhyasyataḥ ananujñātaṃ brahma (yaḥ) śikṣet sa brahmacauryayuktāḥ narakaṃ vrajati || 2.116 ||
laukikaṃ vaidikaṃ vāpi tathādhyātmikam eva vā |
ādadīta yato jñānaṃ taṃ pūrvam abhivādayet || 2.117 ||
laukikam iti | laukikaṃ dṛṣṭahitārtham āyurvedādikaṃ vaidikaṃ vedavedāṅgaviṣayam | ādhyātmikaṃ sāṃkhyādyadhyātmavidyāviṣayaṃ jñānam | yataḥ śikṣet taṃ pūrvaṃ tadabhibhāṣaṇanirapekṣavakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇābhivādayet || 2.117 ||
sāvitrīmātrasāro ‘pi varaṃ vipraḥ suyantritaḥ |
nāyantritas trivedo ‘pi sarvāśī sarvavikrayī || 2.118 ||
atrārthavādaḥ sāvitrīmātrasāra iti | yadi vipraḥ suyantritaḥ abhivādācārānuṣṭhānayantritātmā bhavati tadā sāvitrīmātrapradhāno ‘pi tāvanmātrādhyetāpi varaṃ praśastaḥ | na tv anācāras trivedo ‘pi praśasyaḥ, yataḥ asāv ayantritatvāt sarvāśī sarvavikrayī ca bhavati || 2.118 ||
śayyāsane ‘dhyācarite śreyasā na samāviśet |
śayyāsanasthaś caivainaṃ pratyutthāyābhivādayet || 2.119 ||
śayyāsane iti | śayyāsanaṃ yat gurvādinā sevitaṃ sevyamānaṃ vā tatra nopaviśet | śayyāsanasthaś cainam āyāntaṃ dṛṣṭvā tataḥ utthāyābhivādayet || 2.119 ||
ūrdhvaṃ prāṇā hy utkrāmanti yūnaḥ sthavira āyati |
pratyuttānābhivādābhyāṃ punas tān pratipadyate || 2.120 ||
atrārthavādo bhayadarśanena | ūrdhvam iti | yasmāt jyeṣṭha āgacchati sati kanīyasaḥ prāṇāḥ ūrdhvaṃ nirgantum icchanti anācāreṇāyuḥkṣayāt | pratyutthānābhivādābhyāṃ hetubhūtābhyāṃ punas tān svīkaroti tasmāt tau kartavyau || 2.120 ||
abhivādanaśīlasya nityaṃ vṛddhopasevinaḥ |
catvāri saṃpravardhante āyur dharmo yaśo balam || 2.121 ||
ayaṃ tv aparo ‘traivopakāradarśanena abhivādanaśīlasyeti | abhivādanakaraṇasvabhāvasya sarvadā sarvadā vṛddheṣu paricaryāratasya āyuḥprajñākhyātibalāni catvāri vṛddhim upayānti
|| 2.121 ||
abhivādāt paraṃ vipro jyāyāṃsam abhivādayan |
asau nāmāham asmīti svaṃ nāma parikīrtayet || 2.122 ||
kathaṃ punar abhivādanaṃ kāryam ity ata āha abhivādāt param iti | jyeṣṭhābhivādanaṃ kurvan “abhivādaye” ity etacchabdāt paraṃ “bhadranāmāsmi” ity evaṃ svaṃ nāmoccārayet | vipragrahaṇam iha pratyabhivādavākye ca dvijopadarśanārthaṃ trayāṇāṃ prakṛtatvāt || 2.122 ||
nāmadheyasya ye kecid abhivādaṃ na janate |
tān prājño ‘ham iti brūyāt striyaḥ sarvās tathaiva ca || 2.123 ||
evaṃ sarvatra svanāmoccāraṇaprāptāv āha nāmadheyasyeti | nāmnaḥ uccāritasya sato yad abhivādanaṃ abhivādyā na budhyante asaṃskṛtatvād anabhijñā ye tān prājñaḥ “abhivādaye ‘ham” ity evaṃ brūyāt | striyaś cābhijñā apy evam eva brūyāt || 2.123 ||
bhoḥśabdaṃ kīrtayed ante svasya nāmno ‘bhivādane |
nāmnāṃ svarūpabhāvo hi bhobhāva ṛṣibhiḥ smṛtaḥ || 2.124 ||
bhośabdam iti | abhivādane yat svaṃ nāma tadante bhoḥśabdaṃ kīrtayet | sa cābhivādyasaṃbodhanārtham, yata āha nāmnām iti | yasmān nāmnāṃ yā svarūpasattā saiva bhobhāgaḥ bho ity asya sattā iti manvādibhiḥ smṛtaḥ | ataś cābhivādyanāmasthānāpannaṃ tatsaṃbodhanārthaṃ bhoḥśabdaṃ kīrtayet | tenābhivādayet “bhadranāmāham asmi bho” ity abhivādanarūpam || 2.124 ||
āyuṣmān bhava saumyeti vācyo vipro ‘bhivādane |
akāraś cāsya nāmno ‘nte vācyaḥ pūrvākṣaraḥ plutaḥ || 2.125 ||
idānīṃ pratyabhivādanavidhim āha āyuṣmān iti | abhivādane kṛte āyuṣmān bhava saumety evam abhivādayitā vācyaḥ abhivādayituś ca nāmno ‘nte yaḥ akārādivarṇaḥ nāmnām akārāntatvaniyamābhāvāt sa plutaḥ kāryaḥ | pūrvāṇy akṣarāṇi yasmāt saḥ pūrvākṣaraḥ nāgantukaḥ tato ‘pakṛṣya vā | tenāyuṣmān bhava saumya (myeti) bhadrā 3 iti pratyabhivādarūpam || 2.125 ||
yo na vetty abhivādasya vipraḥ pratyabhivādanam |
nābhivādyaḥ sa viduṣā yathā śūdras tathaiva saḥ || 2.126 ||
atrārthavādaḥ – yo na vetty abhivādasyeti | yathāśāstram abhivādanasya kṛtasya yaḥ pratyabhivādanaṃ kartuṃ na jānāti vipro ‘pi kim utānyaḥ sa viduṣā śūdravan nādbhivādyaḥ | tasmāt pratyabhivādye ‘yaṃ yathāśāstraṃ vijñāya samyak kartavya iti sthitiḥ pratyabhivādastutiḥ | vidhyarthatve prakaraṇoparodhaḥ | “nāmadheyasya ye kecid” ity etadbādhaś ca || 2.126 ||
brāhmaṇaṃ kuśalaṃ pṛcchet kṣatrabandhum anāmayam |
vaiśyaṃ kṣemaṃ samāgamya śūdram ārogyam eva ca || 2.127 ||
pratyabhivādanaprasaṅgena anyam api pratyabhivādayitur dharmam āha brāhmaṇam iti | pratyabhivādayitā samāgatyābhivādayitāraṃ brāhmaṇaṃ kuśalam asti ity evaṃ pṛcchet, kṣatrajātīyaṃ anāmayam iti, vaiśyaṃ kṣemam iti, śūdram punar ārogyam iti || 2.127 ||
avācyo dīkṣito nāmnā yavīvān api yo bhavet |
bhobhavatpūrvakaṃ tv enam abhibhāṣeta dharmavit || 2.128 ||
avāca iti | yāgārthaṃ kṛtadīkṣaḥ pratyabhivādanārthaṃ kāryāntarārthaṃ vā nāmnā kanīyān api na vācyaḥ | kiṃ tarhi | bho dīkṣita bhavatā yajamānena ity evaṃ bhobhavacchabdapūrvaṃ yaugikaṃ śabdaiḥ enaṃ dharmajño ‘bhibhāṣeta || 2.128 ||
parapatnī tu yā strī syād asaṃbaddhā ca yonitaḥ |
tāṃ brūyād bhavatīty evaṃ subhage bhaginīti ca || 2.129 ||
parasya patnī yā bhavati sā cātmano yadi mātṛpitṛbhyo yonisaṃbaddhā na bhavet, tadā tāṃ kāryārthaṃ bhavati ity anena śabdena brūyāt subhage bhagini ityevamādyantena “bhavati subhage” “bhavati mātaḥ” ityevaṃ brūyāt | ānurūpyeṇa bhaginīti ceti itiśabdasya prakārārthatvāt
|| 2.129 ||
mātulāṃś ca pitṛvyāṃś ca śvaśurān ṛtvijo gurūn |
asāv aham iti brūyāt pratyutthāya yavīyasaḥ || 2.130 ||
mātulāṃś ceti | mātulādīn hīnavayasaḥ pratyutthāya bhadro ‘ham ity evaṃ brūyāt | ṛtvik vakṣyate | guruś cātra mātṛśvasrapatyādiḥ, na vidyāguruḥ śaiśavākhyānasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt ||
2.130 ||
mātṛṣvasā mātulānī śvaśrūr atha pitṛṣvasā |
saṃpūjyā gurupatnīvat samās tā gurubhāryayā || 2.131 ||
mātṛsvaseti | mātṛṣvasrādyāḥ gurupatnīvat vakṣyamāṇarūpeṇa pādopasaṃgrahaṇādinā saṃpūjyāḥ, yataḥ tā gurubhāryātulyāḥ || 2.131 ||
bhrātur bhāryopasaṃgrāhyā savarṇāhany ahany api |
viproṣya tūpasaṃgrāhyā jñātisaṃbandhiyoṣitaḥ || 2.132 ||
bhrātur bhāryeti | pūjāprakaraṇāt jyeṣṭhabhrātṛbhāryā upasaṃgrāhyā pādayoḥ, “nābhivādyeha pādayoḥ” iti darśanāt | savarṇā samānajātīyā pratyaham | jñatayo mātṛpitṛpakṣāḥ saṃbandhino vaivāhyāḥ tadbhāryāḥ punaḥ pravāsapratyāgatenopasaṃgrāhyāḥ, na tva ahar ahaḥ || 2.132 ||
pitur bhaginyāṃ mātuś ca jyāyasyāṃ ca svasary api |
mātṛvad vṛttim ātiṣṭhen mātā tābhyo garīyasī || 2.133 ||
pitur bhaginyām iti | pitṛṣvasṛmātṛṣvasṛjyeṣṭhabhaginīnāṃ mātṛvad varteta | mātṛṣvasṛpitṛṣvasroḥ “saṃpūmyā gurupatnīvat” ity anenaiva pādopasaṃgrahaṇādipūjāsiddhāv idaṃ vacanam priyakaraṇādimātṛvṛttyatideśārtham | yathā vakṣyati – “tayor nityaṃ priyaṃ kuryāt” iti aupadeśikātideśikavṛttividhānenaiva ca mātuḥ prakarṣasiddhau “mātā tv ābhyo garīyasī” “pitṛṣvasṛmātṛṣvasṛjyeṣṭheti” vacanaṃ mātṛniṣiddhasya tatpriyakaraṇapratiniṣedhārtham || 2.133 ||
daśābdākhyaṃ paurasakhyaṃ pañcābdākhyaṃ kalābhṛtām |
tryabdapūrvaṃ śrotriyāṇāṃ svalpenāpi svayoniṣu || 2.134 ||
daśābdākhyam iti | ekapuranivāsinā nirguṇānām ekasya daśabhir varṣaiḥ jyeṣṭhe saty api sakhyam ākhyāyate | tataś cāsau “bho bhavat iti vayasyaḥ” [GDh 6.14] ity evaṃ gautamasmaraṇād vācyaḥ | evaṃ kalābhṛtāṃ gītajñānāṃ pañcabhir abdaiḥ sakhyam ākhyāyate vedādhyāyināṃ tryabdapūrvaṃ tryabdādhikaṃ trivarṣajyeṣṭhena sakhyam | jñātiṣu punar apy alpenaiva kālena | yatra varṣavyavahāro nāsti tena sakhyam anyathā sarvatrātra jyeṣthatvavyavahāraḥ || 2.134 ||
brāhmaṇaṃ daśavarṣaṃ tu śatavarṣaṃ tu bhūmipam |
pitāputrau vijānīyād brāhmaṇas tu tayoḥ pitā || 1.135 ||
brāhmaṇam iti | brāhmaṇakṣatriyayoḥ daśavarṣaśatavarṣavayasayoḥ abhivādanādau pitāputravyavahāraḥ syāt | tayor madhyāt punaḥ brāhmaṇaḥ pitṛsthānīyaḥ || 1.135 ||
vittaṃ bandhur vayaḥ karma vidyā bhavati pañcamī |
etāni mānasthānāni garīyo yad yad uttaram || 1.136 ||
vittam iti | dhanajñātiyavaḥśrautasmārtakarmācaraṇavidyāḥ samānajātīyatve sati mānyasthānāni pūjyatve karaṇīyāni | tataś cāvittasya vittavān mānyaḥ | yathaiṣāṃ vittādīnāṃ madhyāt yad yat paścān nirdiṣṭaṃ tat tat pūrvasmāt pūrvasmāt gurutaram | ataś ca vittavatāṃ bandhumān mānya ity avatiṣṭhate || 1.136 ||
pañcānāṃ triṣu varṇeṣu bhūyāṃsi guṇavanti ca |
yatra syuḥ so ‘tra mānārhaḥ śūdro ‘pi daśamīṃ gataḥ || 1.137 ||
pañcānāṃ iti | triṣu varṇeṣu brāhmaṇādiṣu yasya hīnajātīyasyāpi pañcānāṃ vittādīnāṃ madhyāt bhūyāṃsi bahūni trīṇi catvāri pañca vā bhavanti na tv ekaṃ dve vā tāni yadi guṇavanti prakṛṣṭāni bhavanti tadā sa utkṛṣṭajāter api mānyaḥ | evaṃ ca “brāhmaṇaṃ daśavarṣaṃ tu” ity etat nirguṇaviṣaye samaguṇaviṣaye vāvatiṣṭhate | tathā śūdro ‘pi navatihāyanātīto dvijātīnāṃ mānyaḥ | evaṃ ca vittabandhukarmāṇi dvijātīn prati śūdrasya mānahetavaḥ | vidyā tu tasya naivāsti
|| 1.137 ||
cakriṇo daśamīsthasya rogiṇo bhāriṇaḥ striyāḥ |
snātakasya ca rājñaś ca panthā deyo varasya ca || 1.138 ||
cakriṇa iti | cakrayuktagantryādiyānārūḍhasya ativṛddhāturabhārikastrīṇāṃ ca rājaśabdasaṃnidhānāc ca brāhmaṇasyaiva kṛtasamāvartanasnānasya bhūpativivāhārthaprasthitayoś ca gamanoparodhipatho ‘pakrāmed iti pūjāprakaraṇāt eṣām etat pūjaikadeśavidhānam || 1.138 ||
teṣāṃ tu samavetānāṃ mānyau snātakapārthivau |
rājasnātakayoś caiva snātako nṛpamānabhāk || 1.139 ||
teṣām iti | teṣāṃ tu punaḥ cakrādīnām ekavartmani vartināṃ cakrādibhiḥ prakṛtena patho dānena rājasnātakau mānyau | rājasnātakayoḥ ekavartmani vartinoḥ snātako mānaṃ bhajate
|| 1.139 ||
upanīya tu yaḥ śiṣyaṃ vedam adhyāpayed dvijaḥ |
sakalpaṃ sarahasyaṃ ca tam ācāryaṃ pracakṣate || 1.140 ||
sopacāratvād ācāryādilakṣaṇaṃ pūrvavaicitryārtham āha upanīyeti | yaḥ śiṣyaṃ upanayanapūrvakaṃ kalpasūtropaniṣatsahitaṃ vedam adhyāpayati tam ācāryaṃ manvādaya āhuḥ | rahasyasya vedaśabdena grahaṇe sati prādhānyāt pṛthag upadeśaḥ || 1.140 ||
ekadeśaṃ tu vedasya vedāṅgāny api vā punaḥ |
yo ‘dhyāpayati vṛttyartham upādhyāyaḥ sa ucyate || 1.141 ||
ekadeśam iti | vedabhāgaṃ vedāṅgāni vā śikṣākalpaniruktavyākaraṇajyotiṣachandāṃsi yo jīvikārtham arpaṇapūrvam adhyāpayati sa upādhyāya ucyate || 1.141 ||
niṣekādīni karmāṇi yaḥ karoti yathāvidhi |
saṃbhāvayati cānnena sa vipro gurur ucyate || 1.142 ||
niṣekādīnīti | garbhādhānādikarmāṇi yathāśāstraṃ yaḥ karoti annena ca saṃbhāvayati vardhayati sa vipro gurur ucyate | evaṃ ca pitāyaṃ guruḥ niṣekagrahaṇāt || 1.142 ||
agnyādheyaṃ pākayajñān agniṣṭomādikān makhān |
yaḥ karoti vṛto yasya sa tasyartvig ihocyate || 1.143 ||
agnyādheyam iti | ādhānadarśapūrṇamāsāgniṣṭomādisomayāgān yasya kṛtaśāstrīyācaraṇaḥ san yaḥ karoti sa tasyeha śāstre ṛtvig ucyate  | tasyāpi mānyatvād ihopadeśaḥ, tad uktaṃ śvaśurādi ṛtvijo gurūn iti || 1.143 ||
ya āvṛṇoty avitathaṃ brahmaṇā śravaṇāv ubhau |
sa mātā sa pitā jñeyas taṃ na druhyet kadācana || 2.144 ||
ya āvṛṇotīti | āpūrayaty avitathaṃ visvarādirahitaṃ kṛtvā vedenobhau karṇau sa mātāpitṛvat jñeyaḥ | tasmai cādhyayanottarakāle ‘pi drohaṃ na kuryāt || 2.144 ||
upādhyāyān daśācarya ācāryāṇāṃ śataṃ pitā |
sahasraṃ tu pitṝn mātā gauraveṇātiricyate || 2.145 ||
upādhyāyān iti | daśabhya upādhyāyebhya ācāryo gauraveṇa hetubhūtenādhikī bhavati | upādhyāyapūjātaḥ daśaguṇā ācāryasya pūjā kāryety arthaḥ | evam ācāryāṇāṃ śataṃ pitā gauraveṇādhiko bhavati yaḥ sakalasaṃskārādikartā | janakamātrapitṛsahasrāt sakalasaṃskārādikārayitrī mātā gauraveṇādhikī bhavati || 2.145 ||
utpādakabrahmadātror garīyān brahmadaḥ pitā |
brahmajanma hi viprasya pretya ceha ca śāśvatam || 2.146 ||
utpādaketi | saṃskārādikaraṇarahitotpādakācāryayoḥ pitroḥ ācāryākhyaḥ pitā praśastataraḥ | yasmād brahmagrahaṇārthaṃ yaj janma upanayanākhyaṃ viprasya tad iha loke śāśvataṃ nityaṃ pāraṃparyeṇa muktinimittatvāt | ataś ca janakamātṛpitrācāryasaṃnidhāv ācārya evādau pūjanīyaḥ | vipragrahaṇaṃ dvijapradarśanāc ca trayāṇāṃ prakṛtatvāc ca || 2.146 ||
kāmān mātā pitā cainaṃ yad utpādayato mithaḥ |
saṃbhūtiṃ tasya tāṃ vidyād yad yonāv abhijāyate || 2.147 ||
ācāryas tv asya yāṃ jātiṃ vidhivad vedapāragaḥ |
utpādayati sāvitryā sā satyā sājarāmarā || 2.148 ||
atrārthavādaḥ kāmād iti | ācāryas tv iti | pitarāv enaṃ mithaḥ parasparaṃ kāmād utpādayato na tadupakāracikīrṣayā | punaḥ saṃbhavamātraṃ tasya jānīyād yad yonāv asāv utpadyate | ācāryaḥ punaḥ samastavedādhyāpayitā yathāśāstram upanayanāṅgabūtayā sāvitryā yāṃ jātiṃ yaj janma asya karoti sā satyā syāt satyātmakā śrautādikarmanimittatvāt | sā ajarā abādhakā punas tūdbhave tasyānapacayāt, amarā amarātmakajñānādhikārotpādanadvāreṇāmarahetutvāt
|| 2.147–48 ||
alpaṃ vā bahu vā yasya śrutasyopakaroti yaḥ |
tam apīha guruṃ vidyāc chrutopakriyayā tayā || 2.149 ||
aparaṃ gurulakṣaṇam āha pūjārtham alpam iti | yasya kasyacit śrutasyālpaṃ bahu vā yo bodhayati tam apīha pūjāprakaraṇe tena śrutopakāreṇa jānīyāt || 2.149 ||
brāhmasya janmanaḥ kartā svadharmasya ca śāsitā |
bālo ‘pi vipro vṛddhasya pitā bhavati dharmataḥ || 2.150 ||
na ca vayojyaiṣṭhyaṃ vidyāguroḥ pūjārtham apekṣyam, yasmāt brāhmasyeti | upanayanasya kanīyaḥ kartṛtvāsaṃbhavāt tadupalakṣitādhyāpanādi kartā svadharmasya ca ayaṃ dharmam ity evaṃ bodhayitā bālo ‘pi vṛddhasyāpi dharmārthakarmanimittatvena pitṛtulyo bhavati || 2.150 ||
adhyāpayāmāsa pitṝn śiśur āṅgirasaḥ kaviḥ |
putrakā iti hovāca jñānena parigṛhya tān || 2.151 ||
te tam artham apṛcchanta devān āgatamanyavaḥ |
devāś caitān sametyocur nyāyyaṃ vaḥ śiśur uktavān || 2.152 ||
ajño bhavati vai bālaḥ pitā bhavati mantradaḥ |
ajñaṃ hi bāla ity āhuḥ pitety eva tu mantadam || 2.153 ||
atrārthavādaḥ paraḥ prakṛtirūpaḥ adhyāpayāmāseti | ta iti | ajña iti | aṅgirasaḥ putraḥ kavir nāma bālaḥ san pitṛpitṛvyādīn adhyāpitavān | tāṃś ca vedādijñānena hetubhūtena śiṣyatvena svīkṛtya kāryārthena āhvānādikāleṣu putrakā ity uktavān | tatas te pitaraḥ tathāhvānotpannakrodhāḥ tam arthaṃ samāgatya putrakaśabdāhvānākhyaṃ kim etat sādhu ity evaṃ devān pṛṣṭavantaḥ | devāś caitadvicārārthaṃ samāgatya “yuktaṃ śiśuḥ yuṣmān uktavān” ity evam etāṃs tān pitṝn uktavantaḥ | yato ‘jño mūrkhaḥ sa bālo bhavati vṛddho ‘pi san | yaś cādhyāpayitā sa bālo ‘pi san pitṛtulyo bhavati | yato ‘samadanye ajñaṃ bālam ity āhuḥ adhyāpayitāraṃ ca pitety evaṃ bruvate || 2.151–53 ||
na hāyanair na palitair na vittena na bandhubhiḥ |
ṛṣayaś cakrire dharmaṃ yo ‘nūcānaḥ sa no mahān || 2.154 ||
ataś ca na hāyanair iti | na vayo bhūyaḥ śiraḥ pāṇduryadhanabandhubhiḥ jyāyastvam | api tu yo ‘dhyāpanasamarthaḥ sa śreṣṭha ity asmākaṃ matam ity evam ṛṣayo dharmaṃ vyavasthāpitavantaḥ || 2.154 ||
viprāṇāṃ jñānato jyaiṣṭhyaṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ tu vīryataḥ |
vaiśyānāṃ dhānyadhanataḥ śūdrāṇām eva janmataḥ || 2.155 ||
evaṃ ca sati viprāṇām iti | viprāṇāṃ jñānena śraiṣṭhyam, kṣatriyāṇāṃ punaḥ pauruṣeṇa, vaiśyānāṃ dhānyahiraṇyādeḥ, śūdrāṇāṃ vayastaḥ || 2.155 ||
na tena vṛddho bhavati yenāsya palitaṃ śiraḥ |
yo vai yuvāpy adhīyānas taṃ devāḥ sthāvaraṃ viduḥ || 2.156 ||
yena kāraṇena vayobhūyiṣṭhyākhyena asya palitayuktaṃ śiraḥ na tena vṛddho bhavati | yas taruṇo ‘pi san adhītavedo bhavati taṃ devāḥ vṛddhaṃ manyante ity abhidhānaṃ prakṛtadārḍhyārtham || 2.156 ||
yathā kāṣṭhamayo hastī yathā carmamayo mṛgaḥ |
yaś ca vipro ‘nadhīyānas trayas te nāma bibhrati || 2.157 ||
yatheti | dārumayo yathā hastī na yuddhādisamarthaḥ yathā carmamayo mṛgaḥ ākheṭakādāv akiṃcitkaraḥ, evam anadhīyāno brāhmaṇaḥ brāhmaṇasaṃpādyāsu pratigrahādikriyāsu | evaṃ ca trayo ‘py ete nāmamātraṃ dhārayati | na tu hastyādisaṃpādyāḥ kriyāḥ kurvanti || 2.157 ||
yathā ṣaṇḍho ‘phalaḥ strīṣu yathā gaur gavi cāphalā |
yathā cājñe ‘phalaṃ dānaṃ tathā vipro ‘nṛco ‘phalaḥ || 2.158 ||
yathā ṣaṇḍha iti | yathā napuṃsakaṃ strīṣu gacchann api (gacchad api) apatyaśūnyaṃ yathā ca strīgauḥ strīgavi gamanābhāvād apatyaphalaśūnyā yathā cājñe niṣphalaṃ dānaṃ tathā vedarahito brāhmaṇaḥ aphalaḥ paramātmano dṛṣṭādṛṣṭānupakāritvāt || 2.158 ||
ahiṃsayaiva bhūtānāṃ kāryaṃ śreyo ‘nuśāsanam |
vāk caiva madhurā ślakṣṇā prayojyā dharmam icchatā || 2.159 ||
śiyācāryaprakrame adhunācaryasyāpi kiṃcin niyamam āha ahiṃsayaiveti | “rajjvā veṇudalena vā” iti śarīrapṛṣṭe īṣattāḍanasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt atitāḍanaparihāreṇaiva bhūtānāṃ bhāryādīnām api bhūtagrahaṇān na śiṣyāṇām eva prakṛtānām ācāryeṇa vedādhyayanādi kartavyam | tadarthaṃ ca vāg evānākrośarūpānuccair vaktavyā anuśāsanād dharmād dhamam icchatā || 2.159 ||
yasya vāṅmanasī śuddhe samyag gupte ca sarvadā |
sa vai sarvam avāpnoti vedāntopagataṃ phalam || 2.160 ||
yasmāt yasyeti | yasya vāṅmanasī śuddhe vikārarahitau saty api na kāluṣyaṃ gacchataḥ | samyag gupte cotpanne na kathaṃcit kāluṣyeṇa tat kāryaṃ kurutaḥ sarvadā yāvajjīvam upaniṣaduktaṃ mokṣākhyaṃ phalam akhilaṃ prāpnoti || 2.160 ||
nāruntudaḥ syād ārto ‘pi na paradrohakarmadhīḥ |
yayāsyodvijate vācā nālokyāṃ tām udīrayet || 2.161 ||
ataś ca nāruntuda iti | marmaspṛk atyārto ‘pi na syāt na parahiṃsārthavyāpārabuddhiḥ syāt | yayā ca vācā lokaḥ enaṃ dveṣṭi tāṃ svargādilokapratibandhikāṃ noccārayet || 2.161 ||
saṃmānād brāhmaṇo nityam udvijeta viṣād iva |
amṛtasyeva cākāṅkṣed avamānasya sarvadā || 2.162 ||
sammānād iti | pūjāto brāhmaṇaḥ yāvajjīvaṃ viṣād iva khidyeta | amṛtam iva cāvajñāṃ sarvadā kāṅkṣet || 2.162 ||
sukhaṃ hy avamataḥ śete sukhaṃ ca pratibudhyate |
sukhaṃ carati loke ‘sminn avamantā vinaśyati || 2.163 ||
sukham iti | yo vāvajñātaḥ sa sukhaṃ svapiti na yayāvamantuḥ “ayuktaṃ mayā kṛtam” iti cittasamkṣobho bhavati | evaṃ sa ca sukhaṃ nidrāṃ jahāti | sukhaṃ loke vyavaharati | na punar avamānakṛt so ‘sādhu mayā kṛtam itīha loke paraloke cāpuṇyād vinaśyati || 2.163 ||
anena kramayogena saṃskṛtātmā dvijaḥ śanaiḥ |
gurau vasan saṃcinuyād brahmādhigamikaṃ tapaḥ || 2.164 ||
evaṃ prasaktānuprasaktikayā ruṣamātradharmam uktvā adhunā prakṛtam āha aneneti | uktaparipāṭīsaṃbandhena kṛtopanayano dvijaḥ gurukule vasan vedaprāptiprayojanaṃ taponiyamākhyaṃ tapo’rjanam ātmasaṃskāraṃ ca śanaiḥ anvaham upārjayet || 2.164 ||
tapoviśeṣair vividhair vrataiś ca vidhicoditaiḥ |
vedaḥ kṛtsno ‘dhigantavyaḥ sarahasyo dvijanmanā || 2.165 ||
tapoviśeṣair iti | tapoviśeṣaiḥ uktavakṣyamāṇaiḥ agnīndhanādibhiḥ nānāprakāraiḥ vrataiś ca prativedaṃ gṛhyaśāstracoditaiḥ sāvitryādibhiḥ vedaḥ kṛtsnaḥ samastāḥ vedaśākhāmantrabrāhmaṇātmikā dvijātīnām adhyetavyāḥ, ekadeśe ‘pi vedaśabdaprayogadarśanāt “udāharaṇe jihvācchedaḥ” iti ca ekadeśe ‘pi smārtavyavahārāt | kṛtsnagrahaṇād aṅgādhyayanaprāptiḥ arthāvabodhārthatvād adhyayanavidheḥ sāmarthyāl labhyate | na hi aṅgair vinārthāvabodho ghaṭate | rahasyam upaniṣat, prādhānyāt pṛthaggrahaṇam | pitrādiprabandhāya naiva ca śākhādhyetavyā, anyathā svaśākhātyāgāt yaḥ taṃ smarati | tathā ca,
svasūtraṃ yaḥ parityajya parasūtreṇa vartate |
apramāṇam ṛṣiṃ kṛtvā so ‘py adharmeṇa yujyate ||
iti pariśiṣṭeṣu smaryate | na punaḥ śākhayā puruṣasya gotraniyataḥ saṃbandhaḥ | ato yaiva yena śākhādhītā saiva tasya svā śākhā tadarthānuṣṭhānaviṣayam eva ca (smaraṇam yat tu) svasūtraṃ yaḥ parityajati sa eva vedaśākhātyāgīty āha – tad asat | yathaiva idam asmākaṃ gotram iti pratiniyatagotratvasmaraṇaṃ evam iyam asmākaṃ śākheti pratiniyataśākhātvam api smaranti tattyāgenaiva ca śākhātyāgadoṣaṃ smaranti | tadarthānuṣṭhānaṃ parityāgaviṣayam eva ca svasūtraṃ “yaḥ parityajya” iti | pitrādiprabandhā yā saiva śākhādhyetavyā |
nanu pravacananimittā idānīṃtanāḥ kāṭhaka ity evamādyāḥ samākhyāḥ kathaṃ vayaṃ kathā ity evamādiniyato vyapadeśa upapadyate |
satyam | ka evam āha | arthāntaranimittakā etāḥ samākhyāḥ | ko vā vayaṃ kaṭhā ity evamāder vyapadeśasyānityatvam āha | katham | proktādhyayananimittakaḥ idānīṃtano ‘yaṃ vyapadeśaḥ | samākhyāś ca pravacananimittāḥ tathāpi samākhyānām āvirbhāvatirobhāveṣu satsv api śākhāyā nityatvāt nityasaṃbandhatvasmaraṇasya na kadācid dhāniḥ | rahasyam upaniṣadaḥ, ātmajñānaparatvenāsāṃ prādhānyāt pṛthag upadeśaḥ || 2.165 ||
vedam eva sadābhyasyet tapas tapsyan dvijottamaḥ |
vedābhyāso hi viprasya tapaḥ param ihocyate || 2.166 ||
prasaṅgāt yāvajjīvam adhyayanadharmam āha vedam iti | tapaḥ yamaniyamādi kartum icchan brāhmaṇaḥ vedam eva yāvajjīvam abhyasyet | yasmād vedābhyāsaḥ tasya tapo ‘ntarebhyaḥ prakṛṣṭaṃ tapaḥ śāstre ucyate | dvijottamasya grahaṇaṃ (dvijapardarśanārtham) trayāṇāṃ pradarśanārtham | prakṛtatvād evārthavādaḥ || 2.166 ||
ā haiva sa nakhāgrebhyaḥ paramaṃ tapyate tapaḥ |
yaḥ sragvy api dvijo ‘dhīte svādhyāyaṃ śaktito ‘nvaham || 2.167 ||
ā haiveti | yat kṛtamālyādiko ‘pi brahmaniyamaparityāgenāpi dvijaḥ pratyahaṃ yathāśakti svādhyāyam adhīte sa ā nakhāgrebhyaḥ nirjīvanakhāgravyāpakam eva tapaḥ karoti | paraṃ tapontarebhyaḥ prakṛṣṭaṃ teṣāṃ nakhānāṃ vyāpakatvāt | kiṃ punaḥ yo ‘yaṃ niyamayukto ‘dhīta iti prakṛtastutiḥ | na tu yamaniyamatyāgena svādhyāyo ‘sti | haśabda āgamasūcakaḥ |
anasūyaś ca yo vipraḥ paramaṃ tasya tat tapaḥ |
yad ṛco ‘pi dvijo ‘dhīte svādhyāyaṃ śaktito ‘nvaham ||
vedasvīkaraṇaṃ pūrvaṃ vicāro ‘bhyasanaṃ tapaḥ |
taddānaṃ caiva śiṣyebhyo vedābhyāso hi pañcadhā || 2.167 ||
yo ‘nadhītya dvijo vedam anyatra kurute śramam |
sa jīvann eva śūdratvam āśu gacchati sānvayaḥ || 2.168 ||
ya iti | yo dvija upanītaḥ san vedam agṛhītvaivāṅgādau yatnaṃ kurute sa vedānabhijñatvāt ātmaputrādisaṃskārākaraṇena jīvann eva sānvayaḥ kṣipraṃ śūdratulyatāṃ prāpnoti | dvijagrahaṇāc ca vedamiśravedāṅgādhyayane anupanītasya niṣedhaḥ | tathā ca “nābhivyāhārayed brahma” iti veda eva niṣedho bhaviṣyati || 2.168 ||
mātur agre ‘dhijananaṃ dvitīyaṃ mauñjibandhane |
tṛtīyaṃ yajñadīkṣāyāṃ dvijasya śruticodanāt || 2.169 ||
idānīṃ vedādhyayanam eva taskaraṇastutidvāreṇa srotam idam āha mātur agre ‘dhijananam iti | mātuḥ sakāśāt prathamaṃ puruṣasya janma | dvitīyaṃ mekhalānibandhanam | tṛtīyaṃ yāgārthaṃ dīkṣākāle dvijasya sataḥ, “punar vā etam ṛtvijo garbhaṃ kurvanti yad dīkṣayanti” ity evaṃ śruticodanāt || 2.169 ||
tatra yad brahmajanmāsya mauñjībandhanacihnitam |
tatrāsya mātā sāvitrī pitā tv ācārya ucyate || 2.170 ||
tatreti | yasmin janmatrayamadhye yat brahmagrahaṇārthaṃ puṃso janma mekhalābandhanopalakṣaṇārthaṃ tasya mātā sāvitrī pitā punaḥ ācāryaḥ vakṣyate iti rūpakabhaṅgyā janma samarthayate || 2.170 ||
vedapradānād ācāyaṃ pitaraṃ paricakṣate |
na hy asmin yujyate karma kiṃcid ā maujñibandhanāt || 2.171 ||
vedapradānād iti | vedādhyāpanād ācāryaṃ pitaram asyāhuḥ yasmāt tasmin upanayanāt prāk kiṃcid utkarṣahetubhūtaṃ karma adhyayanādi na yujyate | evaṃ ca prāg upanayanād ajātasamatvāt yuktaṃ vedapradānenāryajanmatvam || 2.171 ||
nābhivyāhārayed brahma svadhāninayanād ṛte |
śūdreṇa hi samas tāvad yāvad vede na jāyate || 2.172 ||
evaṃ ca prāg upanayanād adhyayanapratiṣedhe sati pratiprasavam āha nābhivyāhārayed iti | svadhāśabdākhyamantrasādhanaṃ karma ninīyate pitṝn prati prāpyate yena tat svadhāninayanam, pitryaṃ karma tad varjayitvā na vedam udāhārayet | yasmāt vedanimittaṃ yāvan na jāyate yāvad upanītas tāvat śūdratulyo ‘sau | etad eva jñāpakam upanītapitṛprasave (anupanītapitṛmaraṇe) saty ekoddiṣṭaṃ śrāddhādikaraṇādhikārasya || 2.172 ||
kṛtopanayasyāsya vratādeśanam iṣyate |
brahmaṇo grahaṇaṃ caiva krameṇa vidhipūrvakam || 2.173 ||
kṛtopanayanaseti | upanītasya sato ‘sya ādau svaśāstrācoditasāvitrādivratopadeśa iṣyate | tata uktarūpavedādhyayanaṃ svaśākhāgatamantrabrāhmaṇādhyayanaṃ krameṇoktaṃ prāṇāyāmādipūrvakam || 2.173 ||
yad yasya vihitaṃ carma yat sūtraṃ yā ca mekhalā |
yo daṇḍo yac ca vasanaṃ tat tad asya vrateṣv api || 2.174 ||
yad iti | yasya brahmacāriṇo yajjātīyāni carmovītamekhalādaṇḍavastrāṇy upanayane uktāni tajjātīyāny eva nānyāni prativratam upādeyāni | pūrvopāttānāṃ dhāraṇopadeśāt punas tadvidhānam anarthakam iti tajjātīyāny anyāni pratīyate | pūrveṣāṃ ca kāryānupayogāt vinaṣṭatulyatve sati apsu prāsyeti pratipattiḥ || 2.174 ||
sevetemāṃs tu niyamān brahmacārī gurau vasan |
saṃniyamyendriyagrāmaṃ tapovṛddhyartham ātmanaḥ || 2.175 ||
seveteti | brahmacārī gurukule vasan indriyagrāmam indriyasamūhaṃ niyamya ātamas tapovṛddhyarthaṃ imān yamaniyamān anutiṣṭhet || 2.175 ||
nityaṃ snātvā śuciḥ kuryād devarṣipitṛtarpaṇam |
devatābhyarcanaṃ caiva samidādhānam eva ca || 2.176 ||
nityam iti | pratyahaṃ snātvā śuciḥ devānāṃ maheśvarādīnām aṅgiraḥprabhṛtīnām ṛṣīṇāṃ ca pramītānāṃ ca pitrādīnām udakadānaṃ kuryāt | tathā devatānāṃ harādīnāṃ puṣpādinārcanaṃ sāyaṃprātar agnisamidādhānaṃ ca kuryāt || 2.176 ||
varjayen madhu māṃsaṃ ca gandhaṃ mālyaṃ rasān striyaḥ |
śuktāni yāni sarvāṇi prāṇināṃ caiva hiṃsanam || 2.177 ||
varjayed iti | mākṣikamāṃsamālyastrīprāṇihiṃsanāni varjayet tathā gandhavad dravyaṃ karpūrādi udriktarasaṃ ca guḍādi | madhuno rasaśabdagrahaṇe ‘pi prāyaścittaviśeṣārthaṃ pṛthag upadeśaḥ | śuktāni yāni prāptasvarasāni kālaparivāsenodakādidravyāntarakālaparivāsābhyāṃ cāmlībhavanti | sarvagrahaṇaṃ pādapūraṇam | na tv asyārthāntaragrahaṇe sāmarthyam asti | yat tu gautamīye “śuktā vāca” iti tat vākśabdasaṃnidhānāt śuktaśabdasyārthāntaravṛttitvam | iha caivamabhiprete buddhipūrvakārī śuklāś caiva giraḥ sarvā ity evam avekṣate | yady api “sarvaśuktāni caiveha” ity anena brahmacāriṇo ‘pi pratiṣedhasiddhiḥ tathāpīhābhidhānaṃ svādhyāyāṅgatvārtham | etena pratiṣedhena bhakṣaṇe puruṣārtho ‘tikramamātraṃ syāt na vidhivaiguṇyaṃ kā vā gatir anṛtahiṃsādipratiṣedhānām | punar abhidhānasyātra copabhogārthaṃ māṃsādipratiṣedho na auṣadhārthaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhena sā “cānyādhīyīta kāmād guror ucchiṣṭaṃ bheṣajārthaṃ prāśnīyāt” iti māṃsādyabhyanujñānam || 2.177 ||
abhyaṅgam añjanaṃ cākṣṇor upānacchatradhāraṇam |
kāmaṃ krodhaṃ ca lobhaṃ ca nartanaṃ gītavādanam || 2.178 ||
abhyaṅgam iti | abhyaṅgam añjanaghṛtādyabhyañjanopānacchatradhāraṇakāmakrodha-paradravyādigītavādanāni varjayet | kāmaḥ bhojanādyabhilāṣātiśayaḥ | manmathasya strīgrahaṇena niṣiddhatvāt || 2.178 ||
dyūtaṃ ca janavādaṃ ca parivādaṃ tathānṛtam |
strīṇāṃ ca prekṣaṇālambhāv upaghātaṃ parasya ca || 2.179 ||
dyūtam iti | akṣādikrīḍāṃ janaiḥ saha kalahaṃ paradoṣādikīrtanam asatyaṃ ca strīṇāṃ ca maithunasevāśaṅkayāliṅganaṃ parasya ca arthanāśādyupaghātaṃ ca varjayet || 2.179 ||
ekaḥ śayīta sarvatra na retaḥ skandayet kvacit |
kāmād dhi skandayan reto hinasti vratam ātmanaḥ || 2.180 ||
eka iti | nānyena saha śayīta | sarvatra gurugṛhe pitṛgṛhādau | na ca strīprayogāt prakārāntareṇāpi retaḥ prakṣārayet kvacid ayonāv api | yasmād buddhipūrvaṃ retaḥ skandayan ātmano brahmacaryaṃ khaṇḍayati || 2.180 ||
svapne siktvā brahmacārī dvijaḥ śukram akāmataḥ |
snātvārkam arcayitvā triḥ punar mām ity ṛcaṃ japet || 2.181 ||
svapna iti | (yat) dvijo brahmacārī abuddhipūrvaṃ svpne reta utsṛjya snātvārkaṃ puṣpādinā arjayitvā trīn vārān “punar mām etv indriyaṃ” ity etām ṛcaṃ japet | asya prāyaścittasya prakaraṇotkarṣo retaḥsaṃrakṣaṇaniyamāntarebhyo brahmacāriṇo gurutvaprakhyāpanārthaḥ || 2.181 ||
udakumbhaṃ sumanaso gośakṛnmṛttikākuśān |
āhared yāvad arthāni bhaikṣaṃ cāhar ahaś caret || 2.182 ||
udakumbham iti | udakumbhapuṣpagomayamṛddarbhān gurvātmanoḥ yāvatprayojanam ānayet bhaikṣaṃ pratyahaṃ caret | naikadināhṛtena paryuṣitena snehāniyama [snehayukta] śuktādinā paredyur varteta || 2.182 ||
vedayajñair ahīnānāṃ praśastānāṃ svakarmasu |
brahmacāry āhared bhaikṣaṃ gṛhebhyaḥ prayato ‘nvaham || 2.183 ||
vedayajñair iti | vedavedārthānuṣṭhānayuktānāṃ svakarmasu ca vṛttyartheṣu adhyāpanādiṣu praśastānāṃ svavṛttyā jīvatāṃ gṛhebhyo brahmacārī śuciḥ san pratyahaṃ bhaikṣam āharet || 2.183 ||
guroḥ kule na bhikṣeta na jñātikulabandhuṣu |
alābhe tv anyagehānāṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ vivarjayet || 2.184 ||
guror iti | gurugṛhe na bhikṣeta | tathā ātmanaḥ pitṛmātṛgṛheṣu | yadi punar etadvyatiriktagṛhebhyo na labhyate pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ pariharet | prathamaṃ bandhugṛhe bhikṣeta | tadalābhe gurugṛhe || 2.184 ||
sarvaṃ vāpi cared grāmaṃ pūrvoktānām asaṃbhave |
niyamya prayato vācam abhiśastāṃs tu varjayet || 2.185 ||
sarvam iti | bandhugṛhādibhyo ‘py alābhe cāturvarṇyādiṣv āharet | sa jitendriyaḥ san bhikṣāvākyavarjaṃ maunaṃ abhiśastān punaḥ pātakitvena prasiddhān niścitapātakān daṇḍāpūpakayā patitāṃś ca varjayet || 2.185 ||
dūrād āhṛtya samidhaḥ saṃnidadhyād vihāyasi |
sāyaṃprātaś ca juhuyāt tābhir agnim atandritaḥ || 2.186 ||
dūrād āhṛtyeti | araṇyāt samidha āhṛtyeti gṛhyadarśanāt | dūrasya vā aparyavasitatvāt aparigṛhītāḥ samidha ānīya vihāyasi ākāśe nidhānāsaṃbhavāt gṛhapaṭakādau sthāpayet | tābhiś cānalasaḥ san sāyaṃprātar agnikāryaṃ kuryāt || 2.186 ||
akṛtvā bhaikṣacaraṇam asamidhya ca pāvakam |
anāturaḥ saptarātram avakīrṇivrataṃ caret || 2.187 ||
akṛtveti | prāyaścittagurutvāt bhaikṣāgnikārye samuccite | kāmakāreṇa saptāhāni prabandhena anāturaḥ san akṛtvā avakīrṇī prāyaścittaṃ ekādaśe vakṣyamāṇaṃ caret | kathaṃcit punaḥ pratyekavyaktikrame gautamīyam agnīndhanabhaikṣacaraṇe saptarātram akṛtvā ājyahomaṃ samidho vā retasy ābhyām iti | anātura ity āturasya prāyaścittāntaram | na tu prāyaścittābhāvaḥ | etāvad yataḥ pratīyate anāturasyedaṃ prāyaścittaṃ na tv āturasya vyatikramo nāsti iti | vāhyaṃ (na hi) tat anāturaḥ svāni khāni na spṛśet | itivan niyamavākyagataṃ viśeṣaṇam, yenāturasyānadhikāra iti gamyeta | api tu prāyaścittaviśeṣaṇaṃ tasmād āturasya prāyaścittāntaraṃ bhavaty eva | asyāpi prāyaścittasya prakarṣeṇotkarṣo lāghavārthaḥ | tatra hi kriyamāṇe brahmacārigrahaṇaṃ kartavyaṃ syāt || 2.187 ||
bhaikṣeṇa vartayan nityaṃ naikānnādī bhaved vratī |
bhaikṣeṇa vratino vṛttir upavāsasamā smṛtā || 2.188 ||
bhakṣeṇeti | brahmacārī bhakṣeṇa yāvad brahmacaryaṃ prāṇayātrāṃ kuryāt | na tv ekasaṃbandhi annam aśnīyāt | yasmād asya bhakṣeṇa vṛttiḥ upavāsatulyaphaleti | etan niyamaphalābhidhānārthaṃ ca bhaikṣeṇa vartayed ity uktasaṃkīrtanam | na cānityatvāśaṅka, prāyaścittasyoktatvāt |
na bhakṣaṃ paraparakaḥ syān na ca bhakṣaṃ pratigrahaḥ |
somapānasamaṃ bhaikṣaṃ tasmād bhaikṣeṇa vartayet ||
bhaikṣasyāgamaśuddhasya prokṣitasya hutasya ca |
yas tasya grasate grāsān tat tasya kratubhiḥ samāḥ || 2.118 ||
vratavad devadaivatye pitrye karmaṇy atharṣivat |
kāmam abhyarthito ‘śnīyād vratam asya na lupyate || 2.189 ||
vratavad iti | daive karmaṇi vratārthaṃ madhvādivarjitam | śrāddhe ca ṛṣivat araṇyanīvārādi na grāmyam, prārthitaḥ san (a) kāmaṃ icchate ekānnaṃ samaśnīyāt | nāsyaivaṃ kurvato vratalopo bhavati | ṛṣiśabdaś ca vasiṣṭhādau prasiddhaprayogo na vaikhānase yena tadarhānnābhyanujñāne caikam apy upabhuñjīteti māṃsāśanāśaṅkā syāt | śrāddhabhojanasyācārādhikaduṣṭatvād ṛṣivad iti niyamātiśayam āha || 2.189 ||
brāhmaṇasyaiva karmaitad upadiṣṭaṃ manīṣibhiḥ |
rājanyavaiśayos tv evaṃ naitat karma vidhīyate || 2.190 ||
brāhmanasyeti | etad daivapitryaikānnabhojanākhyaṃ karma brāhmaṇasyaiva manvādibhiḥ smṛtam | kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ punaḥ naitat karma bruvate bhaikṣasya pratigrahaprakāratve ‘pi | brahmacāritvaṃ ca prakaraṇāt kṣatriyavaiśyayor api | brahmacāriṇo bhaikṣyabhojanābhyanujñāne sati tadapavādaikānnabhojane 'pi tayor api prastutatvāt pratigrahasya ca brahmacārivyatiriktakṣatriyaviṣaye caritārthatvāt prāptau satyām evaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ || 2.190 ||
nodito guruṇā nityam apranodita eva vā |
kuryād adhyayane yogam ācāryasya hiteṣu ca || 2.191 ||
nodita iti | guruṇā niyuktaḥ aniyukto vā gṛhītādhyayanabhāgābhyāsayatnam ācāryaparicāryāyatnaṃ yāvad brahmacaryaṃ kuryāt || 2.191 ||
śarīraṃ caiva vācaṃ ca buddhīndriyamanāṃsi ca |
niyamya prāñjalis tiṣṭhed vīkṣamāṇo guror mukham || 2.192 ||
śarīram iti | dehavākcakṣurādibuddhīndriyamanāṃsi niyamya baddhāñjaliḥ san guror mukhaṃ vīkṣyamānas tiṣṭhet || 2.192 ||
nityam uddhṛtapāṇiḥ syāt sādhvācāraḥ susaṃvṛtaḥ |
āsyatām iti coktaḥ san nāsītābhimukhaṃ guroḥ || 2.193 ||
nityam iti | yāvajjīvaṃ gurusaṃnidhau vased uddhṛtabāhuḥ sadācārayukto ‘nalasaḥ syāt | guruṇā upaviśyatām iti coktaḥ san guroḥ saṃmukham upaviśet || 2.193 ||
hīnānnavastraveṣaḥ syāt sarvadā gurusaṃnidhau |
uttiṣṭhet prathamaṃ cāsya caramaṃ caiva saṃviśet || 2.194 ||
hīnānnavastraveṣa iti | gurvapekṣayā saṃskāraparimāṇādinā nyūnānnavastrālaṃkāro yāvajjīvaṃ guroḥ saṃnidhāne syāt | śayanāc ca gurūtthānāt prāg uttiṣṭhet | tatsvapnānantaraṃ śayīta || 2.194 ||
pratiśraṇasaṃbhāṣe śayāno na samācaret |
nāsīno na ca bhuñjāno na tiṣṭhan na parāṅmukhaḥ || 2.195 ||
pratiśravaṇasaṃbhāṣe iti | ājñāṅgīkaraṇakāryārthasaṃbhāṣaṇe śayitaḥ upaviṣṭaḥ aśnann utthitaḥ anabhimukho na kuryāt || 2.196 ||
āsīnasya sthitaḥ kuryād abhigacchaṃs tu tiṣṭhataḥ |
pratyudgamya tv āvrajataḥ paścād dhāvaṃs tu dhāvataḥ || 2.196 ||
parāṅmukhasyābhimukho dūrasthasyaitya cāntikam |
praṇamya tu śayānasya nideśe caiva tiṣṭhataḥ || 2.197 ||
kathaṃ tarhi | āsīnasyeti | parāṅmukhasyeti | upaviṣṭasyājñāṃ dadataḥ utthitaḥ pratiśravaṇasaṃbhāṣe kuryāt | utthitasya ca tadabhimukhaṃ gatvā, vegena gacchataḥ tasya paścād vegena gacchan | parāvṛttasya avasthitasya ca abhimukho bhūtvā dūrāvasthitasya samīpam āgatya, śayānasya prahvo bhūtvā nikaṭe ca deśe tiṣṭhataḥ || 2.196–97 ||
nīcaṃ śayyāsanaṃ cāsya nityaṃ syād gurusaṃnidhau |
guros tu cakṣurviṣaye na yatheṣṭāsano bhavet || 2.198 ||
tathaiva nīcaṃ śayyāsanam iti | guruśayyādyapekṣayā hrasvaśayyāsanaṃ yāvajjīvaṃ gurusaṃnidhau syāt | tathā guroś cakṣurviṣaye na yatheṣṭāsanaḥ syāt || 2.198 ||
nodāhared asya nāma parokṣam api kevalam |
na caivāsyānukurvīta gatibhāṣitaceṣṭitam || 2.199 ||
nodāhared iti | guror asaṃnidhāne ‘pi pūjāvākyapadarahitaṃ nāma noccārayet | guror gamanabhāṣaṇabhojanādiceṣṭāṃ nānukuryāt evam asmadgurur gacchatīti || 2.199 ||
guror yatra parīvādo nindā vāpi pravartate |
karṇau tatra pidhātavyau gantavyaṃ vā tato ‘nyataḥ || 2.200 ||
guror iti | yasmin deśe guroḥ sadoṣābhidhānam asaddoṣakathanaṃ vā kecana kurvanti karṇau tatrācchādanīyau tasmāt pradeśād anyatra gantavyam | na cātmanā parīvādādi kartavyam || 2.200 ||
parīvādāt kharo bhavati śvā vai bhavati nindakaḥ |
paribhoktā kṛmir bhavati kīṭo bhavati matsarī || 2.201 ||
parīvādād iti | parīvādakartā janmāntare khara utpadyate | śvā ca nindākartā | ananujñātagurudhanopajīvakaḥ kṛmir bhavati | guruguṇāsahanaḥ kīṭaḥ, kṛmer īṣadupacitaḥ prāṇi bhavatīti pṛthakkathanam, ata eva na kartavyam iti | na cādyārthasya lokasya pūrvārdhavāditvaṃ yuktam, śrutaśabdānupādānāt | uttarābhyāṃ ca vidhyarthābhyāṃ sāhacaryāt | śravaṇaniṣedhe daṇḍāpūpikāsiddhaḥ karaṇaniṣedha iti cet, phalābhidhānaṃ tarhi kutaḥ siddham
|| 2.201 ||
dūrastho nārcayed enaṃ na kruddho nāntike striyāḥ |
yānāsanashaś caivainam avaruhyābhivādayet || 2.202 ||
dūrastha iti | dūrasthaḥ san parahastapuṣpapreṣaṇenācāryaṃ nārcayet, nāpy enaṃ pratyupajātakrodham, na ca bhāryāṇāṃ samīpastham arcayet | yānāsanasthaś ca yadā bhavati tadā avatīryainam abhivādayet | yānāsanasthās caivainaṃ pratyutthāyābhivādayet | anena cāvaruhyābhivādayet | tatra nīcayānāsanāt anutthāyāpy avarohaṇaṃ saṃbhavati etad uktaṃ pratyutthāyābhivādayet | gantrīgavādiyānāsanasthasyāpi cotthānaṃ saṃbhavati ata uktam avaruhyābhivādayed iti || 2.202 ||
prativātānuvāte ca nāsīta guruṇā saha |
asaṃśrave caiva guror na kiṃcid api kīrtayet || 2.203 ||
prativāta iti | prativāto mukhībhūtaḥ purovartī gurudeśāc chiṣyadeśaṃ prati vāyuḥ yasmin pradeśe, yatra cānugataśiṣyaḥ gurudeśaṃ prativātaḥ | tatra paścād guruṇā saha nāsīta | tathā gurusaṃnidhāne yasmin deśe guroḥ saṃśravaḥ ākarṇanaṃ nāsti tatrānyasya tatrānyasya kasyacit na kiṃcid api kathayet || 2.203 ||
go’śvoṣṭrayānaprāsādaprastareṣu kaṭeṣu ca |
āsīta guruṇā sārdhaṃ śilāphalakanauṣu ca || 2.204 ||
gośvoṣṭrayānaprāsādaprastareṣv iti | gośvoṣṭrayāne yukte sati prāsāde dhavalagṛhe tṛṇaprastare kaṭe ca naṭādikṛte kulāle śilāphalakanauṣu ca guruṇā sahāsīta |
śayyāsane ‘dhyācarite śreyasā ca samācaret |
iti pratiṣedhasyāyaṃ pratiprasavaḥ || 2.204 ||
guror gurau saṃnihite guruvad vṛttim ācaret |
na cānisṛṣṭo guruṇā svān gurūn abhivādayet || 2.205 ||
guror iti | ācāryagurur yadā śiṣyasya saṃnihito bhavati tadā tasyāpy ācāryavat varteta | tathā ācāryagṛhe ‘vasthitaḥ tenānanujñātaḥ svayonigurūn abhivādayet || 2.205 ||
vidyāguruṣv evam eva nityā vṛttiḥ svayoniṣu |
pratiṣedhatsu cādharmād dhitaṃ copadiśatsv api || 2.206 ||
vidyāguruṣv evem eveti | ācāryād anyeṣu upādhyāyādiṣu vidyāguruṣv evam eva varteta | tathā svayoniṣu guruṣu adharmanivartakauṣadhādyupadeṣṭṛṣv eva nityā vṛttiḥ || 2.206 ||
śreyaḥsu guruvad vṛttiḥ nityam eva samācaret |
guruputre tathācārye guroś caiva svabandhuṣu || 2.207 ||
śreyaḥsu svāpekṣayā vidyādibhir adhikeṣu ācāryeṣv iti (āryeṣv iti) guruputraviśeṣaṇam | anena viśeṣaṇena svāpekṣāyādhikavayaskeṣu svasamānajātiṣu utkṛṣṭajātiṣu paramāvastheṣu ca guruputreṣu guruvad vṛttim ācared ity abhiprāyo manor bodhyaḥ | guroś caiva svabandhuṣu guroḥ pitṛvyādiṣu || 2.207 ||
bālaḥ samānajanmā vā śiṣyo vā yajñakarmaṇi |
adhyāpayan gurusuto guruvan mānam arhati || 2.208 ||
bāla iti | brahmacāriṇaḥ kanīyān samaḥ yajñavidyādau vā śiṣyaḥ tathā adhyāpayan gurusuto guruvat pūjām arhati | na tv adhyāpayitṛtvād evāsya guruvṛttiḥ saiddhaiva vidyāguruṣv evam evam iti | na ca kanīyastvāpekṣayā śaiśavākhyā(pa)nasya darśitatvāt | satyam | kiṃ tu “śiṣyo vā yajñakarmaṇi” ity ukto ‘dhyāpayato (vya) ‘rthaśiṣyatvāt aprāptāśaṅkāyām idaṃ vacanam | idam eva jñāpakaṃ granthaguror arthagurur garīyān || 2.208 ||
utsādanaṃ ca gātrāṇāṃ snāpanocchiṣṭabhojane |
na kuryād guruputrasya pādayoś cāvanejanam || 2.209 ||
utsādanam iti | udvartanasnāpanocchiṣṭabhojanapādadhāvanāni guruputrasya na kāryāṇi | asmād eva pratiṣedhāt guror etat anuktam api kartavyam ity avasīyate || 2.209 ||
guruvat pratipūjyāḥ syuḥ savarṇā guruyoṣitaḥ |
asavarṇās tu saṃpūjyāḥ pratyutthānābhivādanaiḥ || 2.210 ||
guruvad iti | guroḥ sajātīyā bhāryāḥ guruvat pūjanīyāḥ | vijātīyāḥ punaḥ pratyutthānābhivādanamātreṇa || 2.210 ||
abhyañjanaṃ snāpanaṃ ca gātrotsādanam eva ca |
gurupatnyā na kāryāṇi keśānāṃ ca prasādhanam || 2.211 ||
abhyañjanam iti | abhyaṅgasnāpanodvartanakeśaracā gurubhāryāyā na kāryāḥ || 2.211 ||
gurupatnī tu yuvatir nābhivādyeha pādayoḥ |
pūrṇaviṃśativarṣeṇa guṇadoṣau vijānatā || 2.212 ||
gurupatīti | taruṇī gurubhāryā yūnā kāmaṃ sukhaduḥkhe jānatā pādopasaṃgrahapūrvakaṃ nābhivādyā || 2.212 ||
svabhāva eṣa nārīṇāṃ narāṇām iha dūṣaṇam |
ato ‘rthān na pramādyanti pramadāsu vipaścitaḥ || 2.213 ||
svabhāva iti | svarūpam etat strīṇāṃ yan manuṣyāṇāṃ smarodrekajananam | ato ‘smād dhetoḥ prājñaḥ na sparśanādipramādaṃ bhajate || 2.213 ||
avidvāṃsam alaṃ loke vidvāṃsam api vā punaḥ |
pramadā hy utpathaṃ netuṃ kāmakrodhavaśānugam || 2.214 ||
yataḥ kāmakrodhavidheyaṃ puruṣaṃ samyak śāstrajñaṃ vā loke adharmamārgaṃ netuṃ striyo ‘lam samarthāḥ || 2.214 ||
mātrā svasrā duhitrā vā na viviktāsano bhavet |
balavān indriyagrāmo vidvāṃsam api karṣati || 2.215 ||
matreti | mātṛbhaginīduhitṛbhir api saha vijane na āsīta | yato ‘pradhṛṣya indriyasamūhaḥ śāstrajñam api prasaṅgāt cyāvayati || 2.215 ||
kāmaṃ tu gurupatnīnāṃ yuvatīnāṃ yavā bhuvi |
vidhivad vandanaṃ kuryād asāv aham iti bruvan || 2.216 ||
kāmam iti | gurupatnīnāṃ yuvā vandanaṃ bhuvi vyatyastapāṇinā iti anena vidhinā asāv aham iti bruvan ity uktarūpenābhivādanaṃ kuryāt || 2.216 ||
viproṣya pādagrahaṇam anvahaṃ cābhivādanam |
gurudāreṣu kurvīta satāṃ dharmam anusmaran || 2.217 ||
viproṣyeti | pravāsapratyāgataḥ pādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ pratyahaṃ punar abhivādanamātraṃ gurubhāryāsu kuryāt | eṣa eva sādhvācāra iti saṃcintayan || 2.217 ||
yathā khanan khanitreṇa naro vāry adhigacchati |
tathā gurugatāṃ vidyāṃ śuśrūṣur adhigacchati || 2.218 ||
yuktam etat putradārādiparyantaṃ guruparicaraṇaṃ yasmāt yatheti | yathā kuddālādinā khanitreṇa mahatā yatnena khanan naraḥ pātālagatam udakaṃ prāpnoti evam ācāryasthāṃ vidyāṃ paricaraṇaśīlaḥ prāpnoti na tv ālasyāt || 2.218 ||
muṇḍo vā jaṭilo vā syād atha vā syāc chikhājaṭaḥ |
nainaṃ grāme ‘bhinimlocet sūryo nābhyudiyāt kvacit || 2.219 ||
muṇḍa iti | lūnākhilakeśo vā syāt, itaretarasaṃlagnadhṛtasamastakeśo vā syāt śikhaiva vā jaṭā asya yathābhūtaḥ syāt | tathā ca kuryād yenāsya grāmanagarāvasthitasyottaraśloke śayānagrahaṇāc chayānasya sūryo nāstaṃ yāyāt nābhyudiyāt kvacit kasmiṃścit kāle || 2.219 ||
taṃ ced abhyudiyāt sūryaḥ śayānaṃ kāmacārataḥ |
nimloced vāpy avijñānāj japann upavased dinam || 2.220 ||
tam iti | taṃ brahmacāriṇam icchātaḥ suptaṃ yadi sūryaḥ udayenābhivyāptaṃ kuryāt, tasya vā śāstrārthāparijñānāt śayānasya yady astaṃ yāyāt tadā “japan sāvitrīm” iti gautamasmaraṇāt sāvitrīṃ japan dinaṃ nāśnīyāt | asyāpi prāyaścittasya prakaraṇotkarṣo lāghvārtham | tatra hi kriyamāṇe brahmacārigrahaṇaṃ kartavyaṃ syāt || 2.220 ||
sūryeṇa hy abhinimluktaḥ śayāno ‘bhyuditaś ca yaḥ |
prāyaścittam akurvāṇo yuktaḥ syān mahatainasā || 2.221 ||
atrārthavādaḥ | sūryeṇa hy abhinimlukta iti | sūryenābhinimluktaḥ nimlocanenāstaṃ gamanena śayānaḥ san abhivyāptaḥ anantaroktaṃ prāyaścittam akurvāṇo mahat pāpena yuktaḥ syāt || 2.221 ||
ācamya prayato nityam ubhe saṃdhye samāhitaḥ |
śucau deśe japañ japyam upāsīta yathāvidhi || 2.222 ||
yata evam ataḥ ācamyeti | prayataḥ śuciḥ san samāhitaḥ saṃyatendriyaḥ upaspṛśya śucau deśe japaṃ kurvan saṃdhyopāsanoktavidhinā “yāvajjīvam ubhe saṃdhye seveta” iti saṃdhyālopaprāyaścittaprasaṅgenāyaṃ saṃdhyopāsanānuvādaḥ | prayatatvaṃ samāhitatvaṃ śucideśe vidhyartham || 2.222 ||
yadi strī yady avarajaḥ śreyaḥ kiṃcit samācaret |
tat sarvam ācared yukto yatra cāsya ramen manaḥ || 2.223 ||
yadīti | strīśūdrāv api yac chreyo vakṣyamāṇaṃ śāstraviruddham anutiṣṭhataḥ tad api tayoḥ sakāśāt dṛṣṭvā yukto yatnavān anutiṣṭhet | tatyā yatra ca śāstrīyapadārthe japādau mano ‘sya parituṣyet tat samācaret || 2.223 ||
dharmārthāv ucyate śreyaḥ kāmārthau dharma eva ca |
artha evaha vā śreyas trivarga iti tu sthitiḥ || 2.224 ||
“śreya ācared” ity uktam, kiṃ tac chreya ity ata āha dharmārthāv iti | dharmārthau śreya iti kaiścid ucyate | paralokehalokayoḥ yathākramaṃ tayoḥ karmanimittatvāt | karmārthāv ity paraiḥ kathyate, pratyakṣataḥ tayor eva puruṣārthasādhakatvopalabhyāt dharma evety anyaiḥ bhāṣyate arthakāmayos tanmūlatvaśravaṇāt | artha evehaloke śreya ity anyaiḥ abhidhīyate | tata eva dharmakāmasiddheḥ | evaṃ matāntarāṇy uktvā sthitapakṣam āha trivarga iti tu sthitir iti | dharmārthakāmarūpatryātmako vargaḥ śreya ity ucyate | ity eṣa niścayaḥ | parasparāvirodhena trayāṇām api puruṣārthasādhakatvāt | na ca brahmacāriṇaḥ kāmasevanam ato nopadiśyate śreyaḥpadārthanirūpaṇaparatvāt || 2.224 ||
ācāryaś ca pitā caiva mātā bhrātā ca pūrvajaḥ |
nārtenāpy avamantavyā brāhmaṇena viśeṣataḥ || 2.225 ||
ācāryaś ca pitā caiveti | ācāryapitṛmātṛjyeṣṭhabhrātaraḥ pīḍitenāpi kṣatriyādinā nāvamānanīyāḥ viśeṣato brāhmaṇena nānyeṣāṃ tanmūlatvād dharmānuṣṭhānasyātiprasaṅgaḥ syād yasmād iti || 2.225 ||
ācāryo ‘brahmaṇo mūrtiḥ pitā mūrtiḥ prajāpateḥ |
mātā pṛthivyā mūrtis tu bhrātā svo mūrtir ātmanaḥ || 2.226 ||
ācāryo brahmaṇo mūrtir iti | brahmopadeśotpādanasādhāraṇaikodaranivāsālambanam etad eṣāṃ brahmādiśarīratvābhidhānaṃ prakṛtavidhyarthavādārthaṃ brahmāprajāpatī paramātmahiraṇyagarbho svaḥ sodaryaḥ || 2.226 ||
yaṃ mātāpitarau kleśaṃ sahete saṃbhave nṛṇām |
na tasya niṣkṛtiḥ śakyā kartuṃ varṣaśatair api || 2.227 ||
saṃbhave garbhagrahaṇe sati tadanantaram evānavarataṃ yad duḥkham apatyānāṃ saṃbandhi mātāpitarāv anubhavataḥ | tasyānekavarṣaśataṃ pratyupakārakaraṇenāpi ānṛṇyaṃ prāptuṃ na śakyate kim utaikajanmaneti || 2.227 ||
taponityaṃ priyaṃ kuryād ācāryasya ca sarvadā |
teṣv eva triṣu tuṣṭeṣu tapaḥ sarvaṃ samāpyate || 2.228 ||
tasmāt taponityam iti | mātāpitrācāryāṇāṃ yāvajjīvaṃ priyaṃ kuryāt | na ca tapontarāsaktyā tatpriyopekṣayā kāryā| tasmāt teṣv eva ca tuṣṭeṣu sarvaṃ tapaḥphalaṃ samyak prāpyate
|| 2.228 ||
teṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ śuśrūṣā paramaṃ tapa ucyate |
na tair anabhyanujñāto dharmam anyaṃ samācaret || 2.229 ||
yata evam ataḥ teṣām iti | teṣāṃ mātrādīnāṃ rayāṇāṃ paricaryā tapo’ntarebhyaḥ prakṛṣṭaṃ tapaḥ manvādibhiḥ kathyate | evaṃ ca tair anujñātaḥ nityanaimittakavarjam anyaṃ dharmaṃ nācaret || 2.229 ||
ta eva hi trayo lokās ta eva traya āśramāḥ |
ta eva hi trayo vedās ta evoktās trayo ‘gnayaḥ || 2.230 ||
yasmāt ta iti | pṛthivyantarikṣabrahmalokāvāptihetutvāt tacchuśrūṣāyāḥ ta eva trayo lokā ity ucyante | evaṃ ca brahmacaryavyatiriktāśramatrayatulyaphalatvāt tatsevāyāḥ ta eva traya āśramāḥ | vedatrayajapatulyaphalatvāt tatparicaryāyāḥ ta eva trayo vedāḥ | agnisaṃpādyakarmatulyaphalatvāt tadārādhanāyāḥ ta eva trayo ‘gnayaḥ | iti manvādibhir uktā iti pūrvavidhyarthavādaḥ || 2.230 ||
pitā vai gārhapatyo ‘gnir mātāgnir dakṣiṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
gurur āhavanīyas tu sāgnitretā garīyasī || 2.231 ||
pitā vai gārhapato ‘gnir iti | gārhapatyād haviṣāṃ saṃbhavaḥ, asya ca pituḥ sakāśāt | dakṣināgneś ca guṇatvam, mātuś ca pitṛparatantratvam | āhavanīyasya yāganirvartaktvaṃ prādhānyam | ācāryasyāpy akhilapuruṣārthakāritvāt prādhānyam ity eva | kiṃcit sāmānyam āśrityāyam eṣāṃ agnivyapadeśaḥ | agnibhyaścaiṣām upakaroti sāgnitretā trāṇārtham iva gurutareti | ayam apy arthavādaḥ || 2.231 ||
triṣv apramādyann eteṣu trīn lokān vijayed gṛhī |
dīpyamānaḥ svavapuṣā devavad divi modate || 2.232 ||
triṣv iti | eteṣu triṣu śuśrūṣāyām askhalan trīn lokān vakṣyamāṇān jayet gṛhī ādhipatyena svīkuryāt | tataḥ svadehena kāntiyuktena jvalanādityādidevavat sukhaṃ dyuloke bhavati | gṛhasthasyāpy ayaṃ dharmaḥ iti darśayituṃ gṛhīty uktam || 2.232 ||
imaṃ lokaṃ mātṛbhaktyā pitṛbhaktyā tu madhyamam |
guruśuśrūṣayā tv evaṃ brahmalokaṃ samaśnute || 2.233 ||
imam iti | mātṛpitrācāryaśuśrūṣābhiḥ pṛthivyantarikṣabrahmalokān prāpnoti || 2.233 ||
sarve tasyādṛtā dharmā yasyaite traya ādṛtāḥ |
anādṛtās tu yasaite sarvās tasyāphalāḥ kriyāḥ || 2.234 ||
sarva iti | sarve yāgādayo dharmāḥ tasya phaladānonmukhā bhavanti yena mātṛpitrācāryā ādṛtāḥ ārādhitāḥ | yena punas ete na paritoṣitāḥ tasya sarvā yāgādikriyā niṣphalā iti śuśrūṣāvidhyarthavādaḥ || 2.234 ||
yāvat trayas te jīveyus tāvan nānyaṃ samācaret |
teṣv eva nityaṃ śuśrūṣāṃ kuryāt priyahite rataḥ || 2.235 ||
mātṛpitrācāryeṣu jīvatsu anyaṃ dharmaṃ nācaret | kiṃ tarhi | teṣv eva sarvadā vartamānabhaviṣyadupakārataḥ kuryād iti prakṛtavidhyarthvādaḥ | “teṣāṃ trayāṇām” ity etatsiddhasyaivasya punar abhidhānaṃ prakṛtavidhidārḍhyārtham || 2.235 ||
teṣām anuparodhena pāratryaṃ yad yad ācaret |
tat tan nivedayet tebhyo manovacanakarmabhiḥ || 2.236 ||
teṣām iti | tacchuśrūṣāvirodhena paralokārthaṃ yāgārthaṃ yad yad ācaret tat tad eṣāṃ tatphalaṃ syād ity evaṃ manovākkāyavyāpāreṇa tebhyo dadyāt || 2.236 ||
triṣv eteṣv iti kṛtyaṃ hi puruṣasya samāpyate |
eṣa dharmaḥ paraḥ sākṣād upadharmo ‘nya ucyate || 2.237 ||
triṣv iti | yasmān mātrādiṣv ārādhiteṣu yāvat kiṃcid yāgādikartavyaṃ tat sarvaṃ puruṣasya paripūrṇaṃ bhavati | yasmād eṣa sākṣāt prakṛṣṭo dhamaḥ agnihotrādiḥ gauṇa iti manvādibhir ucyate iti prakṛtavidhyarthavādaḥ || 2.237 ||
śraddadhānaḥ śubhāṃ vidyām ādadītāvarād api |
antyād api paraṃ dharmaṃ strīratnaṃ duṣkulād api || 2.238 ||
śraddadhāna iti | śraddhopetaḥ prakṛṣṭāṃ vidyām uktakramātmajñānādyupadeśaṃ kṣatriyāder nikṛṣṭajātīyād api gṛhṇīyāt, śūdrād api prakṛṣṭaṃ dharmaṃ śāstrāviruddhaṃ tena kuto ‘pi jñātaṃ śiṣyet | yat “yadi strī yady avarajaḥ” ity uktam, tan na, tataḥ śikxayanty api (tu) tadanuṣṭhānadarśanenānuṣṭhānam | utkṛṣṭāṃ ca avaidhavyādilakṣaṇopetāṃ kṣatriyakumbhakārādikulād apy ādadīta || 2.238 ||
viṣād apy amṛtaṃ grāhyaṃ bālād api subhāṣitam |
amitrād api sadvṛttam amedhyād api kāñcanam || 2.239 ||
viṣād iti | viṣād api tatsādhanakarasāyanādikaraṇena amṛtaṃ grāhyam | grāmādiprasthitena praśastavākyaṃ bālodīritam api abhīṣṭārthasaṃsiddheḥ sūcakatvena grāhyam | amedhyapatitam api akṛtasaṃskāram api kāñcanaṃ grāhyam || 2.239 ||
striyo ratnāny atho vidyā dharmaḥ śaucaṃ subhāṣitam |
vividhāni ca śilpāni samādeyāni sarvataḥ || 2.240 ||
striyo ratnānīti | śilpāni cāpy aduṣṭānīti | atra strīratnaśaucacitrakarmādyaduṣṭa-śilpābhidhānārthaḥ, tathaitāni sadāśrayāṇy upādeyāny evam || 2.240 ||
abrāhmaṇād adhyayanam āpatkāle vidhīyate |
anuvrajyā ca śuśrūṣā yāvad adhyayanaṃ guroḥ || 2.241 ||
abrāhmaṇād iti | brāhmaṇādhyāpakābhāve āpatkāle upātkalpa iti vā pāṭhaḥ | tasmin abrāhmaṇād adhyayanaṃ brahmacāriṇa upadiśyate, adhyayanadharmatvena ca śuśrūṣāyāḥ prāptatve saty api punarvacanasya niyamārthatvād anugamanam eva guroḥ śuśrūṣā nānyā, sā ca yāvadvedagrahaṇam, na pāṭhakāla eva, “prāṅmukha upaviśya” iti gṛhyasmaraṇāt | na tatra gamanābhāvāt | tatra saty api varṇatvena sādṛśyatvena dvijatvena ca sadṛśataratvāt “śūdraśiṣyo guruś caiva” iti ca niṣedhadarśanāt ihābrāhmaṇaśabdena kṣatriyavaiśyāv evocyete na tu śūdraḥ tathāpi ca “jīvet kṣatriyadharmeṇa sa hy asya pratyanantaraḥ” ity āpadi evaṃ darśanāt ihāpi prathamaṃ kṣatriyāt tadabhāvāt vaiśyād iti veditavyam | atra guruśabdenābhidhānāt śuśrūṣaikadeśasya cābhyanujñānāt na kṣatriyavaiśyayor apy evaṃvidhādhyetradhyāpanam ācāryasyābhimatam ity avasīyate | kathaṃ tarhi niṣiddhādhyāpano ‘dhyāpanaguruḥ syāt śuśrūṣyo vā adṛṣṭārthaṃ cātraitad | anayor vṛttyarthatve ca,
yo lobhād adhamo jātyā jīved utkṛṣṭakarmabhiḥ |
iti pratyavāyasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt || 2.241 ||
nābrāhmaṇe gurau śiṣyo vāsam ātyantikaṃ vaset |
brāhmaṇe vānanūcāne kāṅkṣan gatim anuttamām || 2.242 ||
evaṃ vakṣyamāṇasya naiṣṭhikasyāpi abrāhmaṇād adhyayanād yāvajjīvaṃ tad agṛhavāsaprāptau idaṃ tanniṣedhārtham āha neti | naiṣṭhikaḥ śiṣyo ‘brāhmaṇagurugṛhe adhyapanācārābhijanaśūnyabrāhmaṇagṛhe vā sarvotkṛṣṭāṃ gatim icchan yāvajjīvikaṃ vāsaṃ na kuryāt || 2.242 ||
yadi tv ātyantikaṃ vāsaṃ rocayet guroḥ kule |
yuktaḥ paricared enam ā śarīravimokṣaṇāt || 2.243 ||
idānīṃ naiṣṭhikabrahmacaryavidhyartham āha yadīti | yadi punar brahmacārī yāvajjīvikavāsaṃ gurugṛhe icchet tadā yatnavā bhūtvā ā śarīrapatanāt gurum ārādhayet ity eṣa eva naiṣṭhika ucyate | tathā ca hārītaḥ: “niṣṭhāṃ ced gacchati” iti | itaras tūpakurvāṇaka ucyate | adhyāpanārthaṃ śuśrūṣākaraṇaṃ pravṛttatvāt | na cātra “yāvajjīvam agnihotraṃ juhuyād” ity evamādiśrutyā bādha āśaṅkanīyaḥ “brahmacāry ācāryakulāvāsī tṛtīyo ‘tyantam ātmānam ācāryakule ‘vasādayan” ity asyāpy etac chrutimūlatvāt || 2.243 ||
ā samāpteḥ śarīrasya yas tu śuśrūṣate gurum |
sa gacchaty añjasā vipro brāhmaṇaḥ sadma śāśvatam || 2.244 ||
asya phalam āha ā samāpter iti | yāvac charīraṃ yo guruṃ paricarati sa brahmalokaṃ ā mahāpralayād anaśvaraṃ añjasā spaṣṭena mārgeṇa gatyantarāvyavahitena gacchati | vipragrahaṇaṃ pradarśanārtham, trayāṇāṃ prakṛtatvāt || 2.244 ||
na pūrvaṃ gurave kiṃcid upakurvīta dharmavit |
snāsyāṃs tu guruṇājñaptaḥ śaktyā gurvartham āharet || 2.245 ||
na pūrvam iti | snānapūrvam agṛhītavedaḥ śiṣyo dhanam āharan yathā (yadā) na (dhanaṃ vinā) adhyāpayāmīty evaṃ guruṇādiṣṭaḥ na kiṃcid api gurave dadyāt, bhṛtakādhyāpakatvaprasaṃgāt | ata evāha dharmavit | apaṇapūrvatve ‘niṣedhaḥ “dharmārthau yatra na syātām” ity uktatvāt | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “yadācāryaṃ bhojayitvā śeṣam annam aśnāti havir utkṛṣṭam eva tat yad anyāni dravyāṇi yathālābham upaharati dakṣiṇā eva vā sa eṣa brahmacāriṇo yajño nityaprayataḥ” iti | snānaṃ punaḥ kartum icchan samāvartanakāle imam artham āharety evaṃ guruṇādiṣṭaḥ yathāśakti avaśyaṃ gurvartham āharet tadānīṃ bhṛtakādhyāpitatvāsaṃbhavāt || 2.245 ||
kṣetraṃ hiraṇyaṃ gām aśvaṃ chatropānaham antataḥ |
dhānyaṃ vāsāṃsi śākaṃ vā gurave prītim āharan || 2.246 ||
kṣetram iti | ataś ca prītyutpādanaparatvāt pradarśanārthaṃ bhūmyādigrahaṇam | antato yady utpannaṃ kiṃcit syāt tadā chatropānaham || 2.246 ||
ācārye tu khalu prete guruputre guṇānvite |
gurudāre sapiṇḍe vā guruvad vṛttim ācaret || 2.247 ||
naiṣṭhikadharmavidhyartham āha ācārya iti | ācārye pramīte guruvat guruputradārasapiṇḍānāṃ pūrvasya pūrvasyābhāve uttarottarasya śuśrūṣā kuryāt || 2.248 ||
eteṣv avidyamāneṣu sthānāsanavihāravān |
prayuñjāno ‘gniśuśrūṣāṃ sādhayed deham ātmanaḥ || 2.248 ||
eteṣv avidyamāneṣv iti | guruputrādiṣv asatsu kālaniyamāvalambanena utthānopaveśanāṭanavān bhaktyā agnikāryam anitiṣṭhan ātmasaṃbandhi śarīraṃ kṣapayet || 2.248 ||
evaṃ carati yo vipro brahmacaryam aviplutaḥ |
sa gacchaty uttamasthānaṃ na ceha jayate punaḥ || 2.249 ||
evam iti | “ā samāpteḥ śarīrasya” ity uktam apy etan naiṣṭhikaphalāviplutividhānārtham upasaṃhārārtham anūdyate | uktena rūpeṇa yo ‘skhalan naiṣṭhikabrahmacaryaṃ carati sa brahmalokaṃ gacchati | na ca saṃsāre jāyate | mahāpralaye brahmaṇā saha yujyate | vipragrahaṇaṃ pradarśanārtham, trayāṇāṃ prakrtatvāt || 2.249 ||
iti śrībhaṭṭamādhavātmajagovindarājaviracitāyāṃ manuṭīkāyāṃ
śrīmanvāśayānusāriṇyāṃ brahmacaryāśramavidhānaṃ nāma
dvitīyo ‘dhyāyaḥ
tṛtīyo ‘dhyāyaḥ
ṣaṭtriṃśadābdikaṃ caryaṃ gurau traivedikaṃ vratam |
tadardhikaṃ pādikaṃ vā grahaṇāntikam eva vā || 3.1 ||
ṣaṭtriṃśadābdikam upakurvāṇakabrahmacāriṇo gārhasthyādhikārāt kiyadavadhini brahmacarye kṛte gārhasthyam āśrayaṇīyam ity ata āha ṣaṭtriṃśadābdikam iti ṣaṭtriṃśadvarṣasaṃbhavaṃ gurugṛhe ṛgyajuḥsāmākhyavedatraviṣayaṃ pratiśākhaṃ dvādaśavarṣāṇi vrataṃ niyamasamūhaḥ cartitārthaṃ tadardhikaṃ cāṣṭādaśa pratiśākhaṃ ṣaḍ iti, pādikaṃ vā nava pratiśākhaṃ trīṇīti yāvatā vā kalinoktavadher ūrdhvam adho vā vedān gṛhṇīyāt tāvatkālaṃ caritavyam | niyamaphalāpekṣo vikalpaḥ “niyamenādhītaṃ vīryavattaraṃ bhavati” iti śruteḥ |
atra cātharvavedasya “netā ete atharvāṅgirasī” ityādiśruteḥ, “śrutīr atharvāṅgirasīḥ” ityādismṛteś ca dṛṣṭavyavahāre ca itaravedavat nirupapadavedaśabdaproyogāt “aṅgāni vedāś catvāra” iti dharmaḥ (dharmasthānatvasya) teṣv api caturṣu vyavahārāt saty api vedatve śāntyādiprayojanatvena prāyeṇa dṛṣṭārthatvāt “ṛgvedenaiva hautram akurvata yajurvedenādhvaryavaṃ sāmavedenodgīthaṃ yad eva trayyai vidyāyai saṃśleṣaṃ tena brhmatvam” iti śruteḥ trayyaiva kriyāparisamāpteḥ grahaṇam | “vedaḥ kṛtsno ‘dhigantavyaḥ” iti ekaśākhādhyayanenaiva svādhyāyanivṛttau puṇyātiśayāpekṣayā || 3.1 ||
vedān adhītya vedau vā vedaṃ vāpi yathākramam |
aviplutabrahmacaryo gṛhasthāśramam āvaset || 3.2 ||
vedān iti | śākhātrayaṃ dvayaṃ caikāṃ vā svaśākhāpūrvakrameṇa mantrabrāhmaṇapaurvāparyasthitikrameṇa cādhītya akhaṇḍitabrahmacaryo ‘saṃjātastrīsaṃprayogaḥ gārhasthyaṃ pratipadyate || 3.2 ||
taṃ pratītaṃ svadharmeṇa brahmadāyaharaṃ pituḥ |
sragviṇaṃ talpa āsīnam arhayet prathamaṃ gavā || 3.3 ||
tam iti | taṃ kṛtabrahmacaryaṃ brahmacaryāvasthānoditadharmānuṣṭhānānuśayena pratikhyātaṃ “tasmāt putram anuśiṣṭaṃ lokyam āhuḥ” iti śrutyā pituḥ prathamakalpikatvāt pituḥ sakāśāt tadabhāva ācārya adhītavedaṃ kṛtamālyādyalaṃkāraṃ parārghyaśayanopaviṣṭaṃ pitā tadabhāve ācāryādiḥ gṛhyoktasvarūpagosādhanakamadhuparkākhyakarmaṇā gārhapathyapratipatteḥ prāk pūjayet | diyate iti dāyaḥ, brahmaiva dāyo brahmadāyaḥ, taṃ haratīti brahmadāyaharati || 3.3 ||
guruṇānumataḥ snātvā samāvṛtto yathāvidhi |
udvaheta dvijo bhāryāṃ savarṇāṃ lakṣaṇānvitām || 3.4 ||
guruṇeti | gurvanujñātaḥ gṛhyaśāstravidhyanatikrameṇa kṛtagṛhyoktasvarūpasnānasamāvartanākhyakarma dvijaḥ samānajātīyāṃ praśastalakṣaṇāṃ bhāryām upayachet || 3.4 ||
asapiṇḍā ca yā mātur asagotrā ca yā pituḥ |
sā praśastā dvijātīnāṃ dārakarmaṇy maithunī || 3.5 ||
asapiṇḍeti | “sapiṇḍatā tu puruṣe saptame vinivartate” (MDh 5.60) ity api vakṣyati | ato mātur asapiṇḍā mātāmahapramātāmahānvayajātā yā na bhavati yā ca mātāmahīpramātāmahyādyanvayajātā ca bhavati, pituś ca yā samānagotrā na bhavati, caśabdāt sapiṇḍāc ca pitṛṣvsrādisantatijātā yā na bhavati sā dvijātīnāṃ maithunādau dārakarmaṇi yojyā || 3.5 ||
manānty api samṛddhāni go’jāvidhanadhānyataḥ |
strīsaṃbandhe daśaitāni kulāni parivarjayet || 3.6 ||
mahāntīti | imāni daśa vakṣyamāṇāni kulāni utkṛṣṭāny api varjayet | tathā gochāgāvimahiṣīdhānyaiḥ samṛddhāny api tacchīlaprajotpattivyādhisaṃkrāntiparihārārthaṃ śāstranodanāsāmarthād adṛṣṭadoṣaparihārārthaṃ ca dārasaṃbandhīti varjayet || 3.6 ||
hīnakriyaṃ niṣpuruṣaṃ niśchando romaśārśasam |
kṣayyāmayāvyapasmāriśvitrikuṣṭhikulāni ca || 3.7 ||
hīnakriyam iti | yāgādirahitaṃ kulaṃ strījanakaṃ vedaśūnyaṃ pracurataradīrghatomāñcitam ārśādyākhyavyādhyupetaṃ rājayakṣmimandāgnyapasmāriśvitrikuṣṭhīnāṃ ca saṃbandhīni kulāni varjayet || 3.7 ||
nodvahet kapilāṃ kanyāṃ nādhikāṅgīṃ na rogiṇīm |
nālomikāṃ nātilomāṃ na vācālāṃ na piṅgalām || 3.8 ||
nodvahed iti | kapilāṃ ṣaḍaṅgulyā adhikāṃ vyādhitādhiṣṭhitāṃ avidyamānalomāṃ bahubhāṣiṇīṃ piṅgalāṃ varṇāṃ kanyāṃ nopayacchet || 3.8 ||
narkṣavṛkṣanadīnāmnīṃ nāntyaparvatanāmikām |
na pakṣyahipreṣyanāmnīṃ na ca bhīṣaṇanāmikām || 3.9 ||
neti | nakṣatravṛkṣanadīmlecchaparvatapakṣisarpadāsabhyānakanāmikāṃ kanyāṃ nodvahet
|| 3.9 ||
avyaṅgāṅgīṃ saumyanāmnīṃ haṃsavāraṇagāminīm |
tanulomakeśadaśanāṃ mṛdvaṅgīm udvahet striyam || 3.10 ||
avyaṅgāṅgīm iti | avikalāṅgīm aprasannanāmnīṃ rājahaṃsagajaruciragamanām anatisthūlaromakeśadantāṃ komalāṅgīṃ kanyāṃ pariṇayet | vidhiniṣedhabhayābhidhānaṃ nirdoṣasaguṇakanyopayamane ‘bhyudayārtham || 3.10 ||
yasyās tu na bhaved bhrātā na vijñāyet vai (vā) pitā |
nopayaccheta tāṃ prājñaḥ putrikādharmaśaṅkayā || 3.11 ||
yasyā iti | yasyā bhrātā nāsti tāṃ “yad apatyaṃ bhaved asyāḥ tan mama syāt svadhākaram” “ityabhisandhimātrāt putrikety eke” ity evaṃ manu-gautamoktaputrikāvidhibhrāntyā novahet | bhrātrabhāve hi pitari vijñāyamāne ‘pi putrābhāvāt kadācid asau pitrā pitrikā kṛtā syāt | satyām api vā sabhrātṛkaprasiddhau “anenaivam utpanneyaṃ na” iti yasyā janako viśeṣeṇa na jñāyate tām api putrikātvabhrāntyā nodvahet | janake hi samyak jñāyamāne bhinnapitṛkayor apy ekamātṛkāyā bhātṛtvaprasiddheḥ asti janakakṛte putrikātvāśaṅkā etad uktam ubhayor viśeṣaṇam | putrikāyāś ca na paripūrṇaṃ bhāryātvam, ata eva niṣedhāt | “abhrāteva puṃsa sa eti pratīci” iti vyākhyātaṃ caitan niruktakṛtā – “abhrātṛkeva puṃsaḥ pitṝn eti abhimukhī santānakarmaṇe piṇḍadānāya bhavati” iti |
atra ca prakaraṇe yeṣāṃ vidhiniṣedhānāṃ dṛṣṭaguṇadoṣānuṣaṅgo nāsti yathā “asapiṇḍā ca yā mātuḥ” iti tadatikrame bhāryā na bhavati, bhāryāśabdasyāhavanīyādivat saṃskāraśabdatvāt | yeṣāṃ punaḥ dṛṣṭaguṇadoṣadarśanena vidhiniṣedhābhidhānaṃ yathā hīnakriyam iti na bhavati, tadatikrame bhāryātvābhāvaḥ kevalaniyamātikramāt prāyaścittamātraṃ bhavati |
yac caitat “narkṣavṛkṣanadīnāmnīm” (MDh 3.9) ityādi tad api dṛṣṭaguṇadośānuvidhāyi vidhiniṣedhaprakaraṇamadhyapāṭhāt tadekayogakṣemam eva | tathā ca manunā sapiṇḍādipratiṣedham abhidhāyānantaraṃ “mahānty api samṛddhāni” (MDh 3.6) ityādipṛthakkaraṇam upakramya hīnakriyam ityādi narkṣavṛkṣetyādi caitprakaraṇe ‘bhihitam
|| 3.11 ||
savarṇāgre dvijātīnāṃ praśastā dārakarmaṇi |
kāmatas tu pravṛttānām imāḥ syuḥ kramaśo ‘varāḥ || 3.12 ||
savarṇeti | dvijātīnām ādau pāṇigrahaṇe samānajātīyā yuktety uktam | kāmāt punar dārakarmaṇi pravṛttānāṃ imāḥ vakṣyamāṇavarṇakrameṇa śreṣṭhāḥ syuḥ || 3.12 ||
śūdraiva bhāryā śūdrasya sā ca svā ca viśaḥ smṛte |
te ca svā caiva rājñaś ca tāś ca svā cāgrajanmanaḥ || 3.13 ||
śūdreti | śūdrays śūdraiva bhāryā bhavati, notkṛṣṭāpakṛṣṭe śūdrā vaiśyā ca vaiśyasya manvādibhiḥ smṛtā | vaiśyā śūdrā kṣatriyā ca kṣatriyasya, kṣatriyā vaiśyā brāhmaṇī ca brāhmaṇasya | “śūdrām py eke mantravarjam” iti vasiṣṭhasmaraṇāt mantravarjitā eṣāṃ dvijātīnāṃ bhavati || 3.13 ||
na brāhmaṇakṣatriyayor āpady api hi tiṣṭhatoḥ |
kasmiṃścid api vṛttānte śūdrā bhāryopadiśyate || 3.14 ||
neti | brāhmaṇakṣatriyayoḥ gārhasthyapratipattaye savarṇām alabhamānayoḥ kasmiṃścid apītihāsākhyāne śūdrā bhāryā nopavarṇyate | evaṃ ca śūdrāniṣedhāt brāhmaṇakṣatriyayoḥ savarṇālābhe asti kṣatriyavaiśyodvāha iti gamyate | tatrāpi ca dhanapadyādau kṣatrapravṛttidarśnāt prathamaṃ brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyodvāhaḥ | tadalābhe vaiśyodvāhaḥ || 3.14 ||
hīnajātistriyaṃ mohād udvahanto dvijātayaḥ |
kulāny eva nayanty āśu sasantānati śūdratām || 3.15 ||
atrārthavādaḥ hīnajatistriyam iti | śūdratām ity upādānāt hīnajātiṃ śūdrāṃ śāstrārthāparijñānāt upayacchamānā dvijātayaḥ kulāni putrapautrādiprabandhāt cirantanakālabhāvisantānāni śūdratāṃ gamayanti tathāvidhānām eva tatrotpādāt | atra dvijātaya iti vacanāt vaiśyasyāpy akṛtasavarṇāvivāhasya śūdrāniṣedha iti gamyate | brāhmaṇakṣatriyayoḥ punaḥ pratyavāyātiśayaḥ pūrvaṃ pratiṣedhāraṃbhasāmarthyāt || 3.15 ||
śūdrāvedī pataty atrer utathyatanayasya ca |
śaunakasya sutotpattyā tadapatyatyā bhṛgoḥ || 3.16 ||
matantarāṇy āhuḥ śūdrāvedīti | atrer utathyaputrasya ca gautamasyaitan mataṃ yat śūdrodvoḍhā patito bhavatīti | nindayā pratiṣedhānumānam | na hy akasmāt nindā pravartata iti | tanmatenāyaṃ śūdrāyā atyantapratiṣedhaḥ | śaunakasya punar mataṃ yat śūdrāsutotpattyā patatīti | ataś ca tanmate ‘nartau nām upeyāt | bhṛgoḥ punar mataṃ yat kevalaśūdrāpatyatayā patatīti | evaṃ ca tatpakṣe itarāsu jātāpatyaḥ śūdrām ṛtau vrajet || 3.16 ||
śūdrāṃ śayanam āropya brāhmaṇo yāty adhogatim |
janayitvā sutaṃ tasyāṃ brāhmaṇyād eva hīyate || 3.17 ||
śūdrām iti | śūdrāṃ gatvā brāhmaṇo narakaṃ vrajati, ity atyantapratiṣedhaḥ | śeṣo ‘rthavādaḥ | sutaṃ śūdrāyām utpādya brāhmaṇyād eva bhraśyatīty ayaṃ sutotpattiniṣedhārthavādaḥ | evaṃ cehārthavādo yo brāhmaṇagrahaṇād atyantapratiṣedhaḥ sutotpattiniṣedho brahmaṇanaviṣayād eva || 3.17 ||
daivapitryātitheyāni tatpradhānāni yasya tu |
nāśnanti pitṛdevās tan na ca svargaṃ sa gacchati || 3.18 ||
daivapitryatitheyāni iti | “yas tu tatkārayen mohāt sajātyā sthitayānyayā” (MDh 9.87) iti niṣedhaṃ vakṣyati | ato ‘saṃnihitāyām api sajātyāyāṃ yāgaśrāddhātithidānāni yasya śūdrayā dattāni tad dhavyakavyaṃ pitṛdevā nopajīvanti | na ca tenātitheyena sa gṛhasthaḥ svaraṃ gacchati || 3.18 ||
vṛṣalīphenapītasya niḥśvāsopahatasya ca |
tasyāṃ caiva prasūtasya niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate || 3.19 ||
vṛṣalīphenapītasyeti | śūdrayā kṛtaparicuṃbanasya tayaiva ca ekaparyaṅkādau mukhaśvāsāpavitrīkṛtasya tasyām eva cotpannāpatyasya saṃśuddhiḥ nopdiśyata iti pūrvottarāv ardhaślokau atyantaniṣedhāt bhṛgumatārthavādau || 3.19 ||
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ pretya ceha hitāhitān |
aṣṭāv imān samāsena strīvivāhān nibodhata || 3.20 ||
caturṇām iti | caturṇām api brāhmaṇādivarṇānāṃ paralokehalokayoḥ kāṃścid dhitāhitān imān vakṣyamāṇān bhāryāprāptihetūn vivāhān saṃkṣepeṇa śṛṇuta || 3.20 ||
brāhmo daivas tathaivārṣaḥ prājāpatyas tathāsuraḥ |
gāndharvo rākṣasaś caiva paiśācaś cāṣṭamo ‘dhamaḥ || 3.21 ||
brāhma iti | stutinindāpratipādano ‘yam eṣāṃ brāhmādivyapadeśaḥ | na tv asādhāraṇyena brahmādidevatāsaṃbandha eṣāṃ vidyate || 3.21 ||
ye yasya dharmyā varṇasya guṇadoṣau ca yasya yau |
tad vaḥ sarvaṃ pravakṣyāmi prasave ca guṇāguṇān || 3.22 ||
ye yasya dharmā iti | ye vivāhā yasya varṇasya dharmānapetā yasya ca vivāhasya yau guṇadoṣau voḍhur iṣṭāniṣṭaphaladātārau pratiniyatavivāhotpannāyatteṣu ca ye guṇadoṣau tat sarvaṃ yuṣmākaṃ pravakṣyāmi || 3.22 ||
ṣaḍ ānupūrvyā viprasya kṣatrasya caturo ‘varān |
viṭśūdrayos tu tān eva vidyād dharmyān arākṣasān || 3.23 ||
ṣaḍ iti | ṣaḍ āditaḥ kramāvasthitān viprasya kṣatriyasya coparitanān caturaḥ (catvāro), viṭśūdrayoḥ punaḥ rākṣasavarjitān tān eva dharmād anapetān jānīyāt || 3.23 ||
caturo brāhmaṇasyādyān praśastān kavayo viduḥ |
rākṣasaṃ kṣatriyasyaikam āsuraṃ vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ || 3.24 ||
catura iti | brāhmaṇasyādyāṃś caturaḥ | kṣatriyasya rākṣasam ekaṃ vaiśyaśūdrayoś cāsuraṃ smṛtyarthopanibandhanakārāḥ praśastān manyante | atra cārurādīnāṃ brāhmaṇādiṣu pūrvavākyavihitānām iha nirākaraṇāt vikalpato nityāsaṃbhave vaikalpikeṣu pravṛttiḥ | evam uttaratrāpi veditavyam || 3.24 ||
pañcānāṃ tu trayo dharmyā dvāv adharmyau smṛtāv iha |
paiśācaś cāsuraś caiva na kartavyo kadācana || 3.25 ||
pañcānām iti | iha paiśācaniṣedhāt prājāpatyādayaḥ pañca gṛhyante | pañcānāṃ prājāpatyādīnāṃ madyāt trayo dharmād anapetāḥ, dvau tv adharmyāv iha śāstre smṛtau paiśācāsurau | tāv āpady api na kartavyau || 3.25 ||
pṛthak pṛthag vā miśrau vā vivāhau pūrvacoditau |
gāndharvo rākṣasaś caiva dharmyau kṣatriyasya tau smṛtau || 3.26 ||
pṛthag iti | gāndharvarākṣasākhyau vivāhau pūrvoktau tau pṛthak pṛthak miśrau vā kṣatrasyādharmāv iti pūrvaṃ siddham | miśrau dharmyau yasya tau smṛtau ity anenocyate | tatretaretarānurāgapūrvaṃ yadā kanyā vareṇātmānaṃ saṃvidā yuddhādināpahārayati tadā gāndharvarākṣasau miśrau bhavataḥ || 3.26 ||
ācchādya cārhayitvā ca śrutaśīlavate svayam |
āhūya dānaṃ kanyāyā brāhmo dharmaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 3.27 ||
ācchādya cārcayaitveti | vāsoviśeṣaiḥ kanyām ācchādyālaṃkārādibhiś cārhayitvā śrutācāravantaṃ varaṃ yācñārahitaṃ svayam ānīya yat tasmai kanyādānaṃ sa brāhmo vivāho manvādibhiḥ smṛtaḥ || 3.27 ||
yajñe tu vitate samyag ṛtvije karma kurvate |
alaṃkṛtya sutādānaṃ daivaṃ dharmaṃ pracakṣate || 3.28 ||
yajña iti | jyotiṣṭomādiyāge prārabdhe arthavahartvija pracarati yad alaṃkṛtya kanyādānaṃ kriyate taṃ daivaṃ vivāhaṃ manvādaya ācakṣate || 3.28 ||
ekaṃ gomithunaṃ dve vā varād ādāya dharmataḥ |
kanyāpradānaṃ vidhivad ārṣo dharmaḥ sa ucyate || 3.29 ||
ekam iti | gobalīvardam ekaṃ dve vā varāt dharmataḥ yāgādyāvaśakakartavyadharmanimittaṃ gṛhītvā yat kanyāyā yathāśāstraṃ pradānaṃ vidhīyate sa ārṣo vivāha ucyate || 3.29 ||
sahobhau caratāṃ dharmam iti vācānubhāṣya ca |
kanyāpradānam abhyarcya prājāpatyo vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ || 3.30 ||
saheti | saha yuvāṃ dharmaṃ kuruta iti pradānakāle vācoktvā yad arcāpūrvaṃ kanyādānaṃ sa prājāpatyo vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ || 3.30 ||
jñātibhyo draviṇaṃ datvā kanyāyai caiva śaktitaḥ |
kanyāpradānaṃ svācchandyād āsuro dharma ucyate || 3.31 ||
jñātibhya iti | kanyāyai kanyājñātibhyaḥ yathāśakti dhanaṃ datvā yat kanyāyāś cāpradānaṃ ādānaṃ svīkāraṃ svecchāto nārṣavad dhanajātiparimāṇāniyamena āsuro vivāha ucyate || 3.31 ||
icchayānyonyasaṃyogaḥ kanyāyāś ca varasya ca |
gāndharvaḥ sa tu vijñeyo maithunyaḥ kāmasaṃbhavaḥ || 3.32 ||
icchayeti | kanyāvarayoḥ parasparānurāgāt yaḥ saṃyogaḥ maithunācaraṇaṃ sa maithunāya hitaḥ kāmanimittako gāndharvo vivāho boddhavyaḥ | iha gāndharvādau mukhyam evopagamanam | tuśabdas tathā pratīte | na ca “pāṇigrahaṇakā mantrāḥ kanyātve ca pratiṣṭhitāḥ” iti vacanāt saṃskaraparilopaḥ | “yā garbhiṇī saṃskriyate,” “voḍhuḥ kanyāsamudbhavaḥ,” “akṣatā ca kṣatā caiva punarbhū saṃskṛtā punaḥ” ityādimanu-yājñavalkyoktasahoḍhakānīna-paunarbhavakakṣaṇeṣu saṃskāradarśanāt | gāndharvādivivāheṣu punar vaivāhiko vidhiḥ, “kartavyaś ca tribhir varṇaiḥ samayenāgnisākṣikaḥ” iti ca devalasmaraṇāt | tathā,
gāndharvāsurapaiśācā vivāhā rākṣasāś ca yāḥ (ye) |
pūrvaṃ pariṇayas teṣāṃ paścād dhomo vidhīyate ||
iti bahvṛcagṛhyapariśiṣṭadarśanāt | bhāratādau ca mukhyopagamanavat | evaṃ ca “pāṇigrahaṇakā mantrāḥ” ityādi narāntaropabhuktakanyāviṣaye ‘vatiṣṭhate, “punar vaivāhiko vidhiḥ kartavyaḥ” ityādivacanāt gamanottarakālam akṛtasaṃskārā dvijātīnāṃ bhāryā na bhavati, śūdrasya bhavaty eva, “tribhir varṇaiḥ” ity upādānāt tathāvidhasya putrasya rikthabhāg (-bhāgktva-) darśanāt || 3.32 ||
hatvā chittvā ca bhittvā ca krośantīṃ rudatīṃ gṛhāt |
prasahya kanyāharaṇaṃ rākṣaso vidhir ucyate || 3.33 ||
hatveti | hatvā kanyāparkṣīyān pramāthya chittvā apramāthya aṅgacchedādīn kṛtvā bhittvā prākārādīn “hā mātaḥ hā pitaḥ anāthā apahriye” ity evam āhvayantīm aśrujalāvilanayanāṃ yaḥ kanyāṃ gṛhād āharati tad api bhūyaḥ kanyāharaṇaṃ rākṣaso vivāhaḥ || 3.33 ||
suptāṃ mattāṃ pramattāṃ vā raho yatropagacchati |
sa pāpiṣṭho vivāhānām paiśācaḥ prathito ‘dhamaḥ || 3.34 ||
suptām iti | suptāṃ nidropetāṃ kṣībāṃ śīlarakṣaṇānibarhaṇāṃ nirjane pradeśe yasmin vivāhe upagacchati sa pāpahetuḥ vivāhānāṃ madhye paiśāco ‘ṣṭamaḥ khyātaḥ || 3.34 ||
adbhir eva dvijāgryāṇāṃ kanyādānaṃ viśiṣyate |
itareṣāṃ tu varṇānām itaretarakāmyayā || 3.35 ||
adbhir eva dvijātīnāṃ kanyādānaṃ praśasyate iti brāhmaṇānāṃ udakadānapūrvam eva kanyādānaṃ praśastam ity ucyate | kṣatriyādīnāṃ punaḥ parasparecchayā vināpy udakaṃ vāṅmātreṇāpi dānam iṣyate || 3.35 ||
yo yasyaiṣāṃ vivāhānāṃ manunā kīrtito guṇaḥ |
sarvaṃ śṛṇuta taṃ viprāḥ samyak kīrtayato mama || 3.36 ||
ya iti | eṣāṃ vivāhādīnāṃ madhyāt yasya vivāhasya yat phalaṃ manunā kathitaṃ tat sarvaṃ mama yathāvat kathayato viprāḥ śṛṇuta || 3.36 ||
daśa pūrvān parān vaṃśyān ātmānaṃ caikaviṃśakam |
brāhmīputraḥ sukṛtakṛn mocayaty enasaḥ pitṝn || 3.37 ||
daśa pūrvān parān iti brāhmavivāhoḍhaputraḥ yadi sukṛtakṛd bhavati tadā daś pūrvān pitrādīn anvayino mṛtān daś cāparān putrādīn ātmānaṃ caikaviṃśakaṃ pāpān mocayati | na cākṛtābhyām akṛtavipraṇāśāv atra nodanīyau | śrāddhādivat vacanagamyatvād amuṣyārthasya putrādīnāṃ cāpāpācārāṇām utpattimātreṇaivaiṣāṃ vimokṣas tadānīm anutpannatvāt || 3.37 ||
daivoḍājaḥ sutaś caiva sapta sapta parāvarān |
ārṣoḍhājaḥ sutas trīn ṣaṭ ṣaṭ kāyoḍhajaḥ sutaḥ || 3.38 ||
daivoḍhāja iti | daivavivāhotpannaḥ putraḥ sapta parān pitrādīn sapta avarān putrādīn pāpān mocayati | ārṣavivāhotpannaś ca trīn putrādīn trīṃś ca putrādīn | tathā prājāpatyavivāhoḍhājotpannaś ca ṣaṭ putrādīn ṣaṭ pitrādīṃś ca || 3.38 ||
brāhmādiṣu vivāheṣu caturṣv evānupūrvaśaḥ |
brahmavarcasinaḥ putrā jāyante śiṣṭasaṃmatāḥ || 3.39 ||
rūpasattvaguṇopetā dhanavanto yaśasvinaḥ |
paryāptabhogā dharmiṣṭhā jīvanti ca śataṃ samāḥ || 3.40 ||
brāhmādiṣv iti | rūpasattvaguṇopetā iti | brāhmādiṣu vivāheṣu caturṣu kramāvasthiteṣu adhyayanādisaṃpattejoyuktāḥ sādhupriyālāvaṇyena dvādaśādhyāyavakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇena sattvena dayādibhiḥ śrīguṇair yuktāḥ dhaninaḥ khyātimantaḥ saṃpannasraganulepanādi-bhogānuṣṭhātāraḥ putrā jāyante śataṃ varṣāṇāṃ jīvanti || 3.39–40 ||
itareṣu tu śiṣṭeṣu nṛśaṃsānṛtavādinaḥ |
jāyante durvivāheṣu brahmadharmadviṣaḥ sutāḥ || 3.41 ||
itareṣv iti | brāhmādibhyaś caturbhyo ‘nyeṣu āsurādiṣu duṣṭavivāheṣu krūrakarmāṇo ‘nṛtavādino vedayāgādidveṣiṇaś ca sutā jāyante || 3.41 ||
aninditaiḥ strīvivāhair anindyā bhavati prajā |
ninditair ninditā nṝṇāṃ tasmān nindyān vivarjayet || 3.42 ||
aninditair iti | agarhitaiḥ bhāryāprāptihetubhiḥ vivāhaiḥ agarhitāni manuṣyāṇām apatyāni bhavanti | garhitaiś ca garhitāni bhavanti | tasmāt garhyān vivarjayet na kuryāt || 3.42 ||
pāṇigrahaṇasaṃskāraḥ savarṇāsūpadiśyate |
asavarṇāsv ayaṃ jñeyo vidhir udvāhakarmaṇi || 3.43 ||
pāṇigrahaṇasaṃskāra iti | hastagrahaṇātmakasaṃskāraḥ gṛhyoktaḥ samānajātiṣu gṛhyamāṇāsu śāstreṇocyate asajātiṣu punaḥ uhyamānāsu vivāhakarmaṇi ayaṃ vakṣyamāṇo vidhiḥ pāṇigrahaṇasthāne vijñeyaḥ || 3.43 ||
śaraḥ kṣatriyayā grāhyaḥ pratodo vaiśyakanyayā |
vasanasya daśā grāhyā śūdrayotkṛṣṭavedane || 3.44 ||
śara iti | kṣariyayā pāṇigrahaṇasthāne brāhmaṇapāṇigṛhītaśaraprānto brāhmaṇodvāhe grāhyaḥ | vaiśyayā ca brāhmaṇa[kṣatriya]pāṇiparigṛhītadātrāditodanamayaḥprāntaṃ brāhmaṇakṣatriyodvāhe grāhyam | śūdrayā punar dvijātyudvāhe tattatprāvṛtavastradaśā grāhyāḥ || 3.44 ||
ṛtukālābhigāmī syāt svadāranirataḥ sadā |
parvavarjaṃ vrajec caināṃ tadvrato ratikāmyayā || 3.45 ||
ṛtukālābhigāmīti | ṛtuḥ garbhādhānasamarthā striyo ‘vasthā | tatkāle bhāryām ajātaputro niyamata upagacchet, anupagamane doṣaśravaṇāt | tathā ca parāśaraḥ:
ṛtusnātāṃ tu yo bhāryāṃ saṃnidhau nopagacchati |
ghorāyāṃ bhrūṇahatyāyāṃ yujyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
kiṃ ca “tribhiḥ ṛṇaiḥ ṛṇavā jāyate yajñena devebhyaḥ prajayā pitṛbhyaḥ svādhyāyena ṛṣibhyaḥ” ity etacchrutiḥ mūlaṃ tv asya saṃbhavati mūlāntarakalpanāyā anyāyyatvam | tathā ca “putrārthī saṃviśed” ity anenaitacchrutimūlatvam asyācārya āviṣkariṣyati | evaṃ caitanmūlatvād apy asya niyamatvam avasīyate | jātaputrasya tv aniyamaḥ, ekaputrotpādane vidheś caritārthatvāt | tathā ca “kāmajāṃs tu” iti darśanāt | “naikaputraṃ dadyāt pratigṛhṇīyād vā sa hi santānāya pūrveṣām” iti ca | yat tu “daśāsyāṃ putrān ādhehi” iti bahvapatyapraśaṃsāparam, jātaputrasyāpi ṛtukāle niyamagamane kathaṃ daśatve vāvatiṣṭhet | evaṃ ca niyamo ‘yaṃ na tv anṛtukāle gamananiṣedhārthā parisaṃkhyeti |
