kaḥ punar anayor bhedaviśeṣo niyamaḥ | parisaṃkhyeti, tatrāprāptaprāpako vidhir niyamaḥ | pakṣe prāptiṃ cāhuḥ | same yajetetivat | atra hi darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ yajetety evamādiyāgacodanāvākyata eva pakṣe yāgādhikaraṇabhūtasamadeśaprāptir asty eva, adhikaraṇaṃ vinā yāgānupapatteḥ same yiyakṣamāṇasyaitad vacanam udāste svata eva pravṛtteḥ | viṣame tu yiyakṣamāṇasya samāprāptāv etad vacanaṃ samārthavidhāyakatvenārthavat | evaṃ ca vidhiphalam eva śrutir niyamaḥ |
apare tu prāptasya punarvacanaṃ niyamam āhuḥ yathā “sarvata evātmānaṃ gopāyet” iti | tatra cānyatra ca prāptau parisaṃkhyā bhavati | yathā pañca pañcanakhā bhakṣyā iti | asty atra śaśādīnāṃ (itareṣāṃ) ca jighāṃsāprayuktatvāt yugapat bhakṣaṇaprāptiḥ | ataḥ pare na bhakṣyā iti parisaṃkhyāyante | yady api svārthahāniparārthakalpanāprāptabādhadoṣākrāntatvaṃ parisaṃkhyāyāḥ tathāpi vākyārthānarthakyāpattir ubhayataḥ tathāśrayaṇaṃ varam ity asāv apy āśrīyate | svārthahāniḥ vidhipratipādakaśravaṇe ‘pi vidhyarthaparityāgaḥ | tatra ca ādyāpretavākyaprameyabhūtasya pratiṣedhakalpanā | pañca vyatiriktapañcanakhānāṃ jighāṃsāprayuktasya bhakṣaṇabādhanam iti prāptabādhaḥ |
evaṃ ceha ṛtāv anṛtau cārhitvāt prāptau satyāṃ anṛtau na gacched ity evaṃ yady api parisaṃkhyā tu (na) māpadyate (na) niyamarūpatā pākṣiktvābhāvāt tathāpy agamane doṣaśravaṇāt niyamārthataiva nyāyyā | svadārasantuṣṭaś ca nityaṃ syāt na stryantaram upāgacched iti ṛtau upagacched iti bhāryāgamanaprāptā ca bhāryāgamanaparisaṃkhyaiṣā amāvāsyādiparvavarjam ṛtāv api bhāyāṃ tatsaṃrakṣaṇavataḥ santatikāmyayā vināpy apatyotpattiṃ tatsuratasaṃbhogecchayā niyamata upeyāt | yat tu “amāvāsyām aṣṭamīṃ ca” (4.128) ityādi vakṣyati tat “ṛtukālābhigāmī syād” ity anenārtau parvasv api gamanaprāptau niṣedhārtham | “tathā cānṛtau” iti tatra ca vakṣyati || 3.45 ||
ṛtuḥ svābhāvikaḥ strīṇāṃ rātrayaḥ ṣoḍaśa smṛtāḥ |
caturbhir itaraiḥ sārdham ahobhiḥ sadvigarhitaiḥ || 3.46 ||
ṛtur iti | caturbhir divasaiḥ uttamajananinditaiḥ saha ṣoḍaśāhorātrāṇi strīṇāṃ rogyādyajanitaṛtukramaḥ || 3.46 ||
tāsām ādāś catasras tu ninditaikādaśī ca yā |
trayodaśī ca śeṣās tu praśastā daśa rātrayaḥ || 3.47 ||
tāsām iti | tāsāṃ punaḥ ṣoḍaśānāṃ rātrīṇāṃ ādyāś catasraḥ ṛtudarśanāt prabhṛti caikādaśītrayodaśyau ca ninditāḥ | atas tāsu na gantavyam | śiṣṭā daś rātrayo gamane praśastā syuḥ || 3.47 ||
yugmāsu putrā jāyante striyo ‘yugmāsu rātriṣu |
tasmād yugmāsu putrārthī saṃviśed ārtave striyam || 3.48 ||
yugmāsv iti | yugmāsu ṣaṣṭyaṣṭamyādyāsu gacchataḥ putrā jāyante | ayugmāsu saptamīnavamyādyāsu striyaḥ | ataḥ putrārthī yugmāsu ṛtukāle bhāryāṃ vrajet, yāsv alpībhāvād ārtavasya | tathā cāyurvedaḥ “yugmāsv alpī bhavaty ārtavam ayugmāsu tasmāt putrasya duhitur janmotpadyate” || 3.48 ||
pumān puṃso ‘dhike śukre strī bhavaty adhike striyāḥ |
same ‘pumān puṃstriyau vā kṣīṇe ‘lpe ca viparyayaḥ || 3.49 ||
pumān iti | puṃso ‘dhikabīje ayugmāsv api rātriṣu pumān stryākṛtiḥ durbalo hīnāṅgo jāyate, āyurvede evābhidhānāt | strībīje tv adhike yugmāsv api strī puruṣākrtiḥ durbalā hīnāṅgī vā jāyate āyurvedadarśanāt | same strīpuruṣabīje yugmāsv ayugmāsv api napuṃsakaṃ jāyate || 3.49 ||
nindyāsv aṣṭāsu cānyāsu striyo rātriṣu varjayan |
brahmacāry eva bhavati yatra tatrāśrame vasan || 3.50 ||
nindyāsu ṣaṭsu anyāsu cāṣṭāsu rātriṣu yāsu kāsucin na krameṇaiva striyaṃ pariharan rātridvaye ‘vaśiṣṭe gacchan gārhapathyād āśramāntare ‘pi vasan brahmacāry eva bhavati | na brahmacaryakhaṇḍanadoṣo 'sya bhavatīty utpannavinaṣṭaputrasya āśramāntarasthasyāpi icchayā putrārthaṃ rātridvayagamanaṃ yatra tatrāśrame vasann api putrārthī saṃviśed iti prastutatvāt putrasya ca mahopakārakatvena śravaṇād yat āśramāntare ‘tyantabrahmacaryopadeśād ayuktam etad ity āhuḥ | tad asat, asyāpi śāstragocaratvāt || 3.50 ||
na kanyāyāḥ pitā vidvān gṛhṇīyāc chulkam aṇv api |
gṛhṇañ chulkaṃ hi lobhena syān naro ‘patyavikrayī || 3.51 ||
neti | kanyāyāḥ pitā dhanagrahaṇadoṣajñaḥ svalpam api kanyādānavinimayarūpadhanaṃ na gṛhṇīyāt | yasmāl lobhena tad gṛhṇan apatyavikrayī bhavet || 3.51 ||
strīdhanāni tu ye mohād upajīvanti bāndhavāḥ |
nārīyānāni vastraṃ vā te pāpā yānty adhogatim || 3.52 ||
strīdhanānīti | duhitṛbhagin¥āsisaṃbandhīni hiraṇyāni dāsīvāhanavastrāṇi vā pitrādaya upabhuñjate | atas te pāpāḥ pākakarmāṇo narakaṃ vrajanti | prasaṅgāt strīdhanopajīvanapratiṣedhaḥ || 3.52 ||
ārṣe gomithunaṃ śulkaṃ kecid āhur mṛṣaiva tat |
alpo ‘py evaṃ mahān vāpi tāvān eva sa vikrayaḥ || 3.53 ||
ārṣe iti | ārṣe vivāhe gomithunaṃ śulkaṃ varād grāhyam iti kecid ācāryāḥ prāhuḥ tat punar asatyam, yasmād alpārthaṃ sādhyatvād alpo vā bhavatu bahumūlyasādhyatvān mahān vā bhavatu sarvathā vikrayas tāvad asau bhavati | “evam ekaṃ gomithunam” ity etat paramatam ity avasīyate || 3.53 ||
yāsāṃ nādadate śulkaṃ jñātayo na sa vikrayaḥ |
arhaṇaṃ tat kumārīṇām ānṛśaṃsyaṃ ca kevalam || 3.54 ||
yāsām iti | yāsāṃ kanyānāṃ punaḥ pitrādayaḥ ātmārthaṃ dānaṃ dattaṃ varān na gṛhṇanti api tu prasannaiḥ bhartrādibhir arpaṇapūrvakaṃ kanyārthaṃ dattaṃ gṛhyate, nāsau vikrayaḥ | kiṃ tarhi | pūjanaṃ tat, kanyānāṃ kevalaṃ vikrayasparśaśūnyam anukampārūpaṃ anukampārūpaṃ tat
|| 3.54 ||
pitṛbhir bhrātṛbhiś caitāḥ patibhir devarais tathā |
pūjyā bhūṣayitavyāś ca bahukalyāṇam īpsubhiḥ || 3.55 ||
pitṛbhir iti | pitṛbhrātṛbhartṛbhiḥ bahvīṃ saṃpadam āptukāmaiḥ striyaḥ pūjyāḥ alaṃkartavyāś ca || 3.55 ||
yatra nāryas tu pūjyante ramante tatra devatāḥ |
yatraitās tu na pūjyante sarvās tatrāphalāḥ kriyāḥ || 3.56 ||
yatreti | yasmin kule pitrādibhiḥ striyaḥ pūjyante tatra devatāḥ prasīdanti | yatra punar etā na pūjyante tatra devatāprasādābhāvāt yāgādikriyāḥ sarvā niṣphalā bhavantīti || 3.56 ||
śocanti jāmayo yatra vinaśyaty āśu tatkulam |
na śocanti tu yatraitā vardhate tad dhi sarvadā || 3.57 ||
śocantīti | yasmin kule navoḍhā duhitṛstuṣādyāḥ saśokā bhavanti tat kulaṃ kṣipram eva vinaśyati | yatra punar etā śokārtā na bhavanti tat sarvakālaṃ vṛddhim eti || 3.57 ||
jāmayo yāni gehāni śapanty apratipūjitāḥ |
tāni kṛtyāhatānīva vinaśyanti samantataḥ || 3.58 ||
jāmaya iti | yāni gṛhāṇi na navoḍhāduhitrādyāś ca apūjitāḥ sadyo ‘bhiśapanti tāni abhicāraviśeṣahā=atānīva sarvanāśaṃ naśyanti || 3.58 ||
tasmād etāḥ sadā pūjyā bhūṣaṇācchādanāśanaiḥ |
bhūtikāmair narair nityaṃ satkāreṣūtsaveṣu ca || 3.59 ||
yasmād evaṃ tasmād etāḥ sadābyarcyāḥ iti | bhūtikāmair narair nityaṃ saṃkareṣūtsaveṣu ca | tasmāt kāraṇāt etāḥ saṃkareṣu kaumidīmahānavamyādiṣu upanayanādiṣu ca sarvakāmaiḥ manuṣyaiḥ ābharaṇavastrabhojanaiḥ nityam abhipūjanīyāḥ || 3.59 ||
saṃtuṣṭo bhāryayā bhartā bhartrā bhāryā tathaiva ca |
yasminn eva kule nityaṃ kalyāṇaṃ tatra vai dhruvam || 3.60 ||
saṃtuṣṭa iti | yasmin kule svabhāryayā hetubhūtayā nityaṃ bhartā nivṛttābhilāṣaḥ bhāryāpi ca yatra nityaṃ svabhartrā hetubhūtena anupajātanarāntarābhilāṣā tatrāvicalaṃ maṅgalaṃ bhavati || 3.60 ||
yadi hi strī na roceta pumāṃsaṃ na pramodayet |
apramodāt punaḥ puṃsaḥ prajanaṃ na pravartate || 3.61 ||
yadīti | yadi strī sugandhalepanādi kāntimatī na bhavet tadā bhartāraṃ na praharṣayet | bhartuḥ apraharṣāc ca garbhagrahaṇaṃ na bhavati || 3.61 ||
striyāṃ tu rocamānāyāṃ sarvaṃ tad rocate kulam |
tasyāṃ tv arocamānāyāṃ sarvam eva na rocate || 3.62 ||
kiṃ ca striyāṃ tu rocamānāyām iti | striyāṃ veṣādinā kāntimatyāṃ bhartṛmanoharatayā parapuruṣasaṃparkābhāvāt tatkulam ujjvalaṃ bhavati | tasyāṃ punar arocamānāyāṃ bhartṛdviṣṭatayā narāntarasaṃkalpataḥ sakalam eva kulaṃ maninī bhavati || 3.62 ||
kuvivāhaiḥ kriyālopair vedānadhyayanena ca |
kulāny akulatāṃ yānti brāhmaṇātikrameṇa ca || 3.63 ||
kuvivāhair iti | āsurādivivāhasaṃskārābhāvavedānadhyayanabrāhmaṇāpūjanaiḥ prakhyātakulāny apakarṣaṃ yānti | tasmād etat pariharaṇīyam || 3.63 ||
śilpena vyavahāreṇa śūdrāpatyaiś ca kevalaiḥ |
gobhir aśvaiś ca yānaiś ca kṛṣyā rājopasevayā || 3.64 ||
ayājyayājanaiś caiva nāstikyena ca karmaṇām |
kulāny āśu vinaśyanti yāni hīnāni mantrataḥ || 3.65 ||
śilpena vyavahāreṇeti | ayājyayājanaiś ceti | citrakarmādiśilpadhanaprayogakevalaśūdāpatya-gavāśvarathakrayavikrayādikṛṣirājasevāvrātyādiyājanayāgādikarmābhāvaśuddhi-manānadhyayanaiḥ kṣipram eva kulāny apakarṣaṃ yānti || 3.64–65 ||
mantratas tu samṛddhāni kulāny alpadhanāny api |
kulasaṃkhyā ca gacchanti karṣati ca mahad yaśaḥ || 3.66 ||
mantrata iti | yady api dhanena kulam iti pravādaḥ | tathāpi vedādhyayanāvabodha-tadarthānuṣṭhānayuktāni punaḥ kulāni alpadhanāny api utkṛṣṭakulamadhye guṇyatāṃ prāpnuvanti mahatīṃ khyātim arjayanti | vivāhaprasaṅgena prasaktānuprasaktikayaitat sarvam uktam || 3.66 ||
vaivāhike ‘gnau kurvīta gṛhyaṃ karma yathāvidhi |
pañcayajñavidhānaṃ ca paktiṃ cānvāhikīṃ gṛhī || 3.67 ||
vaivāhika iti | aṣṭakādi gṛhyoktaṃ karma yathāśāstraṃ vakṣyamāṇapañcayajñānuṣṭhānaṃ cāgnisaṃpādyaṃ prātyahikaṃ pākaṃ vivāhabhave ‘gnau gṛhasthaḥ kurvīta || 3.67 ||
pañca sūnā gṛhasthasya cullī peṣaṇy upaskaraḥ |
kaṇḍanī codakumbhaś ca badhyate yās tu vāhayan || 3.68 ||
pañca sūneti | pacanadṛṣatśūrpolūkhalādyāḥ pañca gṛhasthasya sūnā iva ghātanasthānāni yāni svakārye yojayan pāpena saṃbadhyate | evaṃ cāsmāt sūnāvacanāt tāsāṃ niṣkṛtyartham iti ca niṣkṛtivacanāt sūnādoṣair na lipyata iti taddoṣābhidhānāt adharmahetutvam āsāṃ tanniṣkṛtyarthatvaṃ mahāyajñānām ity avasīyate, śāstraikagocaratvāt dharmādharmayoḥ | na caivaṃ sati mahāyajñānām anityatāpattiḥ devatātithibhṛtyānāṃ ṛṣayaḥ pitaro devatā ity evamādibhir eṣāṃ nityatvapratipādanāt saṃdhyopāsanavad ubhayārthatvam eṣām || 3.68 ||
tāsāṃ krameṇa sarvāsāṃ niṣkṛtyarthaṃ maharṣibhiḥ |
pañca kḷptā mahāyajñāḥ pratyahaṃ gṛhamedhinām || 3.69 ||
tāsām iti | tāsāṃ cullyādīnāṃ sūnānāṃ yathākramaṃ niṣkṛtyarthaṃ tadutpannapāpanirharaṇārthaṃ pratyahaṃ pañca mahāyajñāḥ gṛhasthānāṃ manvādibhiḥ smṛtāḥ || 3.69 ||
adhyāpanaṃ brahmayajñaḥ pitṛyajñas tu tarpaṇam |
homo daivo balir bhauto nṛyajño ‘tithipūjanam || 3.70 ||
te ca adhyāpanam iti | ‘japo ‘huta’ iti vakṣyamāṇaḥ | adhyāpanaśabdenādhyayanam api gṛhyate | sa brahmayajñaḥ | pitṛtarpaṇaṃ pitṛyajñaḥ | agnau homo daivayajñaḥ | bhūtabalir bhūtayajñaḥ | atithipūjanaṃ manuṣyayajñaḥ | ete vakṣyamāṇāḥ kartavyatāviśeṣā iha svarūpamātreṇa nirdiṣṭāḥ || 3.70 ||
pañcaitān yo mahāyajñān na hāpayati śaktitaḥ |
sa gṛhe ‘pi vasan nityaṃ sūnādoṣair na lipyate || 3.71 ||
pañceti | etān pañca mahāyajñān yo yathāśakti na jahāti sa sarvakālam api gṛhe vasan cullyādisūnājanitaiḥ pāpair na saṃbadhyate || 3.71 ||
devatātithibhṛtyānāṃ pitṝṇām ātmanaś ca yaḥ |
na nirvapati pañcānām ucchvasan na sa jīvati || 3.72 ||
devatātithibhṛtyānām iti | devatāśabdena bhūtānām api grahaṇaṃ teṣām api devatātvāt | tathā ṛṣīṇām api devatānām api grahaṇaṃ teṣām api devatātvasmaraṇāt | bhṛtyāḥ | “
vṛddhau ca mātāpitarau sādhvī bhāryā sutaḥ śiśuḥ |
apy akāryaśataṃ kṛtvā bhartavyā manur abravīt ||
ity uktvā eṣāṃ devatādīnāṃ pañcānāṃ yo na dadāti saḥ prāṇān dhārayann api na jīvati jīvitakāryābhāvāt || 3.72 ||
ahutaṃ ca hutaṃ caiva tathā prahutam eva ca |
brāhmyaṃ hutaṃ prāśitaṃ ca pañcayajñān pracakṣate || 3.73 ||
evaṃ cākaraṇanindayā eṣāṃ nityatām āha ahutaṃ ca hutaṃ ceti | uttaraśloke eṣāṃ vyākhyārtho bhaviṣyati | etān eva pañcamahāyajñān ebhir nāmāntaraiḥ munaya ācakṣate | nāmamātrabhedo na vastubheda ity etat pratipādayituṃ saṃjñāntarapradarśanam etat || 3.73 ||
japo ‘huto huto homaḥ prahuto bhautiko baliḥ |
brāhmyaṃ hutaṃ dvijāgryārcā prāśitaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam || 3.74 ||
japa iti | ahutaśabdena japo brahmayajñākhyaḥ smṛtaḥ | hutaś ca homo devayajñaḥ prahutaś ca bhautiko baliḥ bhūtayajñaḥ brāhmaṃ hutaṃ dvijāgryasya brāhmaṇasyārcā atithiyajñaḥ prāśitaṃ ca pitṛtarpaṇam pitṛyajñaḥ | yadi kathaṃcit sakalamahāyajñamahānuṣṭhānāsaṃpattis tadā || 3.74 ||
svādhyāye nityayuktaḥ syād daive caiveha karmaṇi |
daivakarmaṇi yukto hi bibhartīdaṃ carācaram || 3.75 ||
“svādhyāye nityayuktaḥ syāt daive pitrye ca karmaṇi | ubhayatrāpi yukto hi” (3.75) iti | brahmayajñadevayajñānuṣṭhāne nityaṃ yatnavān syāt | tasmād evaṃ yajñānuṣṭhānapara idaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamarūpaṃ jagat dhārayati || 3.75 ||
agnau prāstāhutiḥ samyag ādityam upatiṣṭhate |
ādityāj jāyate vṛṣṭir vṛṣṭer annaṃ tataḥ prajāḥ || 3.76 ||
tathā ca agnau prāstāhutir iti | agnāv āhutiḥ samyag yathāvidhi prakṣiptā hutā rasāharaṇarūpatvād adityasya ādityaṃ prāpnoti sa āhutirasaḥ kālena paripakvaḥ ādityād vṛṣṭirūpeṇa jāyate | tato 'nnam, tadupabhogena ca prajā jāyante || 3.76 ||
api ca –
yathā vāyuṃ samāśritya sarve jīvanti jantavaḥ |
tathā gṛhastham āśritya vartante itarāśramāḥ || 3.77 ||
yathā prāṇavāyvavalambanena sarvaprāṇino jīvanti, evaṃ gṛhasthāśramam āśritya avalambyāśramāntarāṇi dhriyante || 3.77 ||
yasmāt trayo ‘py āśramiṇo jñānenānnena cānvaham |
gṛhasthenaiva dhāryante tasmāj jyeṣṭhāśramo gṛhī || 3.78 ||
evaṃ ca yasmād iti | yasmāt gṛhasthavyatiriktāḥ trayo ‘py āśramiṇo vedārthopadeśānnadānābhyāṃ gṛhasthair evopakriyante tasmāt śreṣṭāśramo gṛhasthaḥ | gṛhā patnī medho yajñaḥ | gṛhādhīno medho yasya gṛhamedhī | gṛhāyāṃ tiṣṭhantīti gṛhasthāḥ | gṛhā asyāstīti gṛhī || 3.78 ||
sa saṃdhāryaḥ prayatnena svargam akṣayam icchatā |
sukhaṃ cehecchatātyantaṃ yo ‘dhāryo durbalendriyaiḥ || 3.79 ||
yat evam ataḥ sa saṃdhāryo gṛhastheneti | sa gṛhasthāśramaḥ svargasaṃbandham anantam ihaloke suratasaṃbhogasvādvannātisukham antam icchatā prayatnenānuṣṭheyo ‘saṃyatendriyaiḥ dhārayituṃ na śakya iti gṛhasthāśramaphalakathanam || 3.79 ||
ṛṣayaḥ pitaro devā bhūtāny atithayas tathā |
āśāsate kuṭumbibhyas tebhyaḥ kāryaṃ vijānatā || 3.80 ||
na cānityatāpattiḥ, yata āha ṛṣaya iti | ṛṣiprabhṛtayo gṛhasthebhyaḥ sakāśāt yato ‘rthayante yataḥ śāstrajñena ebhyaḥ kartavyam || 3.80 ||
svādhyāyenārcayetarṣīn homair devān yathāvidhi |
pitṝñ chrāddhaiś ca nṝn annair bhūtāni balikarmaṇā || 3.81 ||
kiṃ tad ity āha svādhyāyeneti | nānāprakāratvād arcanasya svādhyāyāder arcanārthatvam aviruddhaṃ pūrvoktamahāyajñārthaiḥ svādhyāyādibhiḥ ṛṣidevapitratithibhūtāni yathāśāstram arcayet || 3.81 ||
dadyād ahar ahaḥ śrāddham annādyenodakena vā |
payomūlaphalair vāpi pitṛbhyaḥ prītim āvahan || 3.82 ||
teṣu pitṛyajñanirūpaṇārthaṃ tāvad āha dadyād ahar ahar iti | pitṛbhyaḥ prītim utpādayan annodakakṣīramūlaphalaiḥ yathāsaṃbhavaṃ pratyahaṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryāt || 3.82 ||
ekam apy āśayed vipraṃ pitrarthaṃ pāñcayajñike |
na caivātrāśayet kiṃcid vaiśvadevaṃ prati dvijam || 3.83 ||
ekam iti | pañcayajñāntargate pitṛprayojane ekam api śaktyā sati saṃbhave bahūn api viprān bhojayet | śrāddhaśabdenodanāt vaiśvadevabrāhmaṇabhojane prāpte āha na caiveti | viśvān devān uddiśya anyaṃ brāḥmaṇaṃ na kiṃcid bhojayet || 3.83 ||
vaiśvadevasya siddhasya gṛhye ‘gnau vidhipūrvakam |
ābhyaḥ kuryād devatābhyo brāhmaṇo homam anvaham || 3.84 ||
vaiśvadevasyeti | vaiśvadevārthasyānnasya sato vaivāhike ‘gnau kālāntarāhite vā parisamūhanādi gṛhyoktetikartavyatāpūrvakam ābhyo vakṣyamāṇābhyo devatābhyaḥ pratyahaṃ brāhmaṇo homaṃ kuryāt, brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ dvijapradarśanārtham, trayāṇāṃ prakṛtatvāt || 3.84 ||
agneḥ somasya caivādau tayoś caiva samastayoḥ |
viśvebhyaś caiva devebhyo dhanvantaraya eva ca || 3.85 ||
kuhvai caivānumatyai ca prajāpataya eva ca |
saha dvyāvāpṛthivyoś ca tathā sviṣṭakṛte ‘ntataḥ || 3.86 ||
kuhvai caiveti | “svāhākārapradā(dhā)nā homā” iti smaraṇāt agnaye svāhā somāya svāhā ityevamādau kṛtvā tato ‘gnīṣomābhyāṃ svāhety agnīṣomayoḥ samastahor homaṃ kuryāt | tad anu viśvebhyo devebhyo hutvā dhanvantaraye kuhvai anumatyai prajāpataye dyāvāpṛthivībhyāṃ ante agnaye sviṣṭakṛta ity evaṃ gṛhye ‘gniviśeṣaṇatvāt sviṣṭakṛta ete svāhākārāntahomāḥ kāryāḥ || 3.85–86 ||
evaṃ samyag ghavir hutvā sarvadikṣu pratikṣaṇam |
indrāntakāppatīndubhyaḥ sānugebhyo baliṃ haret || 3.87 ||
evam iti | evam uktaprakriyayā samyak pratidaivataṃ havir hutvā prācyādyāsu sarvāsu dikṣu prācyām indrāya namaḥ indrapuruṣebhyo dakṣiṇasyā yamāya yamapuruṣebhyaḥ paścimāyāṃ varyṇāya varuṇapuruṣebhyaḥ uttarasyāṃ somāya somapuruṣebhya ity eva pradakṣiṇāvartaṃ indrādibhyaḥ sāgucarebhyo baliṃ dadyāt, gṛhya evaṃ darśanāt indrāntakādiśabdābhidhānaṃ chandobhaṅgabhayāt || 3.87 ||
marudbhya iti tu dvāri kṣiped apsv adbhya ity api |
vanaspatibhya ity evaṃ musalolūkhale haret || 3.88 ||
marudbhya iti dvāri hared iti | marudbhya ity evaṃ dvārapradeśe adbhya ity etad apāṃ madhye vanaspatibhya ity evaṃ musalolūkhale dvandvaikavadbhāvāt vikalpenānyatra baliṃ dadyāt || 3.88 ||
ucchīrṣake śriyai kuryād bhadrakālyai ca pādataḥ |
brahmavāstoṣpatibhyāṃ tu vāstumadhye baliṃ haret || 3.89 ||
ucchīrṣaka iti | vāstupuruṣasya śiraḥpradeśe uttarapūrvasyāṃ diśi śriyaṃ tasyaiva pādapradeśe dakṣiṇapaścimāyāṃ bhadrakālyai brahmaṇe vāstoṣpataye ceti gṛhamadhye dvandvanirdeśe ‘pi āṅnirdeśābhāvāt devatādvayam || 3.89 ||
viśvebhyaś caiva devebhyo balim ākāśa utkṣipet |
divācarebhyo bhūtebhyo naktaṃcāribhya eva ca || 3.90 ||
viśvebhya iti | viśvebhyaḥ vaiśvadevebhyaḥ iti caśabdād ekeyam āhutiḥ | viśvebhyo devebhya iti nāmnākāraḥ (nāmākāśe) prakṣipet | divācarebhyo bhūtebhya iti divānaktaṃcarebhyo bhūtebhjya iti naktam iti gṛhyadarśanāt etādādhārāntarasyāvacanāt ākāśasya ca prastutatvāt ākāśa eva || 3.90 ||
pṛṣṭhe vāstuni kurvīta baliṃ sarvānnabhūtaye |
pitṛbhyo baliśeṣaṃ tu sarvaṃ dakṣiṇato haret || 3.91 ||
pṛṣṭhe vāstuni kurvīteti | veśmana upari veśmanaḥ pṛṣṭhe vāsupradeśe vā tatra sarvānnabhūtaye nama ity evaṃ baliṃ kuryāt | balyarthaṃ ghṛtānnaśeṣaṃ sarvaṃ dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi “svadhā pitṛbhya iti prācīnāvītī” ity evaṃ gṛhyadarśanāt pitṛbhyo haret || 3.91 ||
śunāṃ ca patitānāṃ ca śvapacāṃ pāparogiṇām |
vayasāṃ ca kṛmīṇāṃ ca śanakair nirvaped bhuvi || 3.92 ||
śunāṃ ceti | tad anu anyad annam uddhṛtya śvapatitaśvapākakuṣṭhyādikākakṛmīṇāṃ śanakai rajovagūṇṭhanaparihāreṇa bhuvi nikṣipet || 3.92 ||
evaṃ yaḥ sarvabhūtāni brāhmaṇo nityam arcati |
sa gacchati paraṃ sthānaṃ tejomūrtiḥ patharjunā || 3.93 ||
evam iti | evam uktanītyā abhihitasarvabhūtāni nityam annādinā yaḥ pūjayati saḥ tejaḥśarīro bhūtvā spaṣṭena mārgeṇa yenāśuprāpyena śarīrapātasamanantaram eva tena brahmalokaṃ gacchati iti pañcayajñaphalakathanam, na cānityatāpattir ity uktam || 3.93 ||
kṛtvaitad balikarmaivam atithiṃ pūrvam āśayet |
bhikṣāṃ ca bhikṣave dadyād vidhivad brahmacāriṇe || 3.94 ||
kṛtveti | evam uktasvarūpeṇa etad balikarma kṛtvā sarvagṛhabhoktṛbhyaḥ pūrvaṃ atithiṃ pūjayet | bhikṣaṇaśīlāya brahmacāriṇe samakālam eva “svastivācya bhikṣādānaṃ appūrvam” iti gautamoktavidhānena bhikṣāṃ dadyāt || 3.94 ||
yat puṇyaphalam āpnoti gāṃ datvā vidhivad guroḥ |
tat puṇyaphalam āpnoti bhikṣāṃ datvā dvijo gṛhī || 3.95 ||
yat puṇyaphalam iti | “alpagur hemaśṭṅgī” ity ādiyājñavalkyoktavidhinā gāṃ datvā yat puṇyaphalaṃ “godo bradhnasya viṣṭapam” iti prāpnoti tad eva dvijagṛhasthaḥ bhikṣāṃ datvā prāpnoti || 3.95 ||
bhikṣām apy udapātraṃ vā satkṛtya vidhipūrvakam |
vedatattvārthaviduṣe brāhmaṇāyopapādayet || 3.96 ||
bhikṣām iti | phalapuṣpādinā satkṛtya pāramārthikavedārthajñāya brāhmaṇāya svastivācyetyādividhipūrvakaṃ pratipādayet iti brahmacāribhikṣādānād vidhyantaram idam | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “śrotriyāya annaṃ datvā brahmacāriṇe” ity āha || 3.96 ||
naśyanti havyakavyāni narāṇām avijānatām |
bhasmabhūteṣu vipreṣu mohād dattāni dātṛbhiḥ || 3.97 ||
naśyantīti | yāni pitṛdaivoddeśena annāni mohāt dānaśāstrānabhijñatayā dātṛbhiḥ adhyayanavidhiyajñānuṣṭhānatejaḥśūnyatvāt bhasmarūpeṣu vipreṣu dattāni tāni teṣāṃ dānaśāstrānabhijñānāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ niṣphalībhavanti || 3.97 ||
vidyātapaḥsamṛddheṣu hutaṃ vipramukhāgniṣu |
nistārayati durgāc ca mahataś caiva kilbiṣāt || 3.98 ||
vidyātapaḥsamṛddeṣv iti | jñānānuṣṭhānatejaḥsaṃpanneṣu vipramukheṣu agnitulyeṣu havyakavyādi yat prakṣiptaṃ tad iha dustarāt śatruvyādhyādyabhibhavāt amutra ca mahataḥ pāpāt trāyate || 3.98 ||
saṃprāptāya tv atithaye pradadyād āsanodake |
annaṃ caiva yathāśakti saṃskṛtya vidhipūrvakam || 3.99 ||
saṃprāptāyeti | svayam upasthitāyātithaye āsanācamanādi udakānnaṃ ca vyajanaviśeṣaiḥ yathāsaṃbhavaṃ satkṛtya “āsanāvasathau” ityādivakṣyamāṇavidhipūrvakaṃ dadyāt || 3.99 ||
śilān apy uñchato nityaṃ pañcāgnīn api juhvataḥ |
sarvaṃ sukṛtam ādatte brāhmaṇo ‘narcito vasan || 3.100 ||
śilān apy uñchato nityam iti | atidaurgatyā vṛttisaṃyamātiśayārthitayā śilān kṣetraphaladoṣān apy uccinvataḥ sabhyāvasathyābhyāṃ saha tretayā pañcasv apy agniṣu homaṃ vā kurvāṇasya sarvavṛttisaṃkocāgnihomārjitaṃ puṇyaṃ anarcito ‘tithir vasan svīkaroti | adānadoṣakathanena niyamān āha sabhyo nāmāgniḥ mahāsādhana iti | sītādyapanuttyarthaṃ yas tatra tatra vihīyate || 3.100 ||
tṛṇāni bhūmir udakaṃ vāk caturthī ca sūnṛtā |
etāny api satāṃ gehe nocchidyante kadācana || 3.101 ||
annāsaṃbhave punaḥ tṛṇānīti | tṛṇāni viśrāmabhūmyācamanādyudakapriyasatyavacanāni sādhugṛheṣu atithyarthaṃ na kadācid apy ucchidyante | ato ‘nnābhāve etāny avaśyaṃ deyāni
|| 3.101 ||
ekarātraṃ tu nivasann atithir brāhmaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
anityaṃ hi sthito yasmāt tasmād atithir ucyate || 3.102 ||
aprasiddhatvād atithilakṣaṇam āha ekarātram iti | brāhmaṇa ekarātraṃ paragṛhe vasan atitiḥ yatiḥ smṛtaḥ yad asyāhanityāvasthānena na vidyate dvitīyā tithir asyety evam atithir ucyate || 3.102 ||
naikagrāmīṇam atithiṃ vipraṃ sāṃgatikaṃ tathā |
upasthitaṃ gṛhe vidyād bhāryā yatrāgnayo ‘pi vā || 3.103 ||
naikagrāmīṇam iti | samānagrāmanivāsinaṃ yaṃ ca saṃgatyā lokayātrayā caritaṃ vicitraparihāsakathājīvanaṃ yasya sa bhāryāgnayaḥ tasminn eva grāme saṃprāptaḥ taṃ vaiśvadevakāle ‘pi saṃprāptam atithiṃ na vidyāt || 3.103 ||
upāsate ye gṛhasthāḥ parapākam abuddhayaḥ |
tena te pretya paśutāṃ vrajanty annādidāyinaḥ || 3.104 ||
upāsata iti | ātitheyoddeśata eva grāmāntarāṇi gatvā ye gṛhasthāḥ durmedhasaḥ audarikatayā parānnaṃ sevante tena parānnabhojanena te janmāntare annādyadāyināṃ gṛhe paśutāṃ vrajanti iti phalapratipādanaparam etat | ataḥ parapākopasevanaṃ na kāryam || 3.104 ||
apraṇodyo ‘tithiḥ sāyaṃ sūryoḍho gṛhamedhinā |
kāle prāptas tv akāle vā nāsyānaśnan gṛhe vaset || 3.105 ||
apraṇodya iti | yasmād ayam ādityenāstaṃ gacchatā prāpito ‘tithiḥ, tadānīṃ svagṛhagamanāśakteḥ, sa gṛhamedhinā gṛhānuṣṭheyapañcayajñavatā gṛhasthena na pratyākhyeyaḥ | sa ca caiśvadevakāle prāptaḥ taduttarakāle vā nāsya gṛhasthasya annam anaśnan gṛhe vaset | avaśyam asyāśanaṃ deyam, ayaṃ ca pratiṣiddhātithipratiprasavārtham āraṃbhaḥ, anyathā “vaiśvadevaṃ hi nāmaitat sāyaṃ prātar vidhīyate” (MDh 3.121) iti vakṣamāṇenaiva sāyam atithidānasiddhau anārambhaḥ syāt || 3.105 ||
na vai svayaṃ tad aśnīyād atithiṃ yan na bhojayet |
dhanyaṃ yaśasyam āyuṣyaṃ svargyaṃ vātithipūjanam || 3.106 ||
neti | yad atithaye rocate tat tasmā adatvā ātmanā nāśnīyāt | atithibhojanaṃ ca dhanakhyātyā duḥsvarganimittam ity ātitheyaphalakathanaṃ na tv anityatāpattiḥ “sarvaṃ sukṛtam ādatte” ityādyuktatvāt || 3.106 ||
āsanāvasathau śayyām anuvrajyām upāsanam |
uttameṣūttamaṃ kuryād dhīne hīnaṃ same samam || 3.107 ||
āsanāvasathāv iti | yugapadanekātithyavasthāne sati hy āsanasthānaviśrāmasthānaśraddhādi-śayanānugamanaparicaraṇaṃ itaretarāpekṣayotkṛṣṭamadhyameṣu utkṛṣṭāpakṛṣṭam amadhyamaṃ kuryāt | annaṃ punas tulyam eva, tatra viśeṣāśravaṇāt || 3.107 ||
vaiśvadeve tu nirvṛtte yady anyo ‘tithir āvrajet |
tasyāpy annaṃ yathāśakti pradadyān na baliṃ haret || 3.108 ||
vaiśvadeva iti | kṛte ‘tithibhojanaparyante vaiśvadeve yady aparo ‘tithir āvrajet tasmai tadā cānnaṃ yathāśakti punaḥ paktvā pradadyāt | baliharaṇaṃ ca tato ‘nnān na kuryāt | baliharaṇaniṣedhād ātmasaṃskārārthatā vaiśvadevasyābhipretety avasīyate | annasaṃskārapakṣe katham asaṃskṛtānnabhojanam anujānīyāt || 3.108 ||
na bhojanārthaṃ sve vipraḥ kulagotre nivedayet |
bhojanārthaṃ hi te śaṃsan vāntāśīty ucyate budhaiḥ || 3.109 ||
prasaṅgāt bhoktṛdharmam āha na bhojanārtham iti | bhojanalābhārthaṃ viraḥ sve kulagotre na kathayet yasmāt bhojanārthaṃ te kathayan udgīrṇāśīti vidvadbhiḥ kathyate || 3.110 ||
na brāhmaṇasya tv atithir gṛhe rājanya ucyate |
vaiśyaśūdrau sakhā caiva jñātayo gurur eva ca || 3.110 ||
na brāhmaṇasya tv atithir iti | brāhmaṇagṛhe gurumitrabandhukṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrā nātithayo bhavanti | guroḥ prabhutvāt mitrabandhūnām ātmasamatvāt kṣatriyādeś ca hīnajātitvāt kṣatriyasyotkṛṣṭaḥ brāhmaṇaḥ syāt | brāhmaṇakṣatriyāv utkṛṣṭajātīyau syātām evaṃ vaiśyasyāpi || 3.110 ||
yadi tv atithidharmeṇa kṣatriyo gṛham āvrajet |
bhuktavatsu ca vipreṣu kāmaṃ tam api bhojayet || 3.111 ||
yadīti | kṣīṇapātheyo grāmāntaravāsitvāt atithidharmeṇa yadi kṣatriyo vipragṛhe āgacchet tadā gṛhāvasthitavipreṣu kṛtabhojaneṣu tam api bhojayet || 3.11 ||
vaiśyaśūdrāv api prāptau kuṭumbe ‘tithidharmiṇau |
bhojayet saha bhṛtyais tāv ānṛśaṃsyaṃ prayojayan || 3.112 ||
vaiśyaśūdrāv iti | vaiśyaśūdrāv apātheyāv atithirūpau gṛhe prāptau tadā kāruṇyāt sahabhojanīyau || 3.112 ||
itarān api sakhyādīn saṃprītyā gṛham āgatān |
prakṛtyānnaṃ yathāśakti bhojayet saha bhāryayā || 3.112 ||
itarān iti | uktakṣatriyādivyatiriktān sakhibandhusahādhyāyiprabhṛtīn prītyā hetubhūtayā gṛham āgatān annaṃ yathāśakti saṃskṛtya bhāryā bhojanakāle bhojayet | ātmano ‘pi kālaḥ “avaśiṣṭaṃ tu dampatī” iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt | guros tu kālānvabhidhānaprabhutvena svāyattakālatvāt || 3.113 ||
suvāsiṇīḥ kumārīś ca rogiṇo garbhiṇīḥ striyaḥ |
atithibhyo ‘nvag evaitān bhājayed avicārayan || 3.114 ||
suvāsinīr iti | jīvacchvaśurapitṛkāḥ striyaḥ kumāryāturagarbhiṇīś ca atithyanugatān evāvilambamāno bhojayet agrata eva (iti) pāṭhe atithibhyaḥ pūrvam eva || 3.114 ||
adatvā tu ya etebhyaḥ pūrvaṃ bhuṅkte ‘vicakṣaṇaḥ |
sa bhuñjāno na jānāti śvagṛdhrair jagdhim ātmanaḥ || 3.115 ||
adatveti | etebhyo bhṛtyaparyantebhya atithibhyo ‘datvā yo vyatikramadoṣānabhijñaḥ pūrvaṃ bhuṅkte sa bhuñjānaḥ etat phalabhūtaṃ maraṇasamantaraprāptam ātmanaḥ śvagṛdhrakhādanaṃ na jānāti || 3.115 ||
bhuktavatsv atha vipreṣu sveṣu bhṛtyeṣu caiva hi |
bhuñjīyātāṃ tataḥ paścād avaśiṣṭaṃ tu dampatī || 3.116 ||
bhuktavatsv iti | atithyādijñātisaṃbandheṣu dāseṣu kṛtabhojaneṣu tato ‘nnāvaśiṣṭaṃ jāyāpatī paścād aśnīyātām || 3.116 ||
devān ṛṣīn manuṣyāṃś ca pitṝn gṛhyāś ca devatāḥ |
pūjayitvā tataḥ paścād gṛhasthaḥ śeṣabhug bhavet || 3.117 ||
devān iti | gṛhe bhavā gṛhyā ityantena bhūtayajña ukta iti | vakṣyamāṇapratyavāyakathanārtho ‘yam anuvādaḥ || 3.117 ||
aghaṃ sa kevalaṃ bhuṅkte yaḥ pacaty ātmakāraṇāt |
yajñaśiṣṭāśanaṃ hy etat satām annaṃ vidhīyate || 3.118 ||
agham iti | ya ātmārtham eva pacati na devādibhyo dadāti sa pāpahetutāt pāpam eva kevalaṃ bhuṅkte nānnaleśam api yasmāt yad etat pākayajñaśiṣṭāśanaṃ sādhūnām annam upadiśyate
|| 3.118 ||
rājartviksnātakagurūn priyaśvaśuramātulān |
arhayen madhuparkeṇa parisaṃvatsarān punaḥ || 3.119 ||
atithipūjāprasaṅgenāyaṃ rājādīnāṃ pūjāviśeṣa ucyate – rājartviksnātakagurupriyaśvaśura-mātulān iti | janapadābhiṣaktaṃ kṣatriyaṃ gṛhāgataṃ yacakaṃ ṛtvijaṃ yaḥ kaścid gṛhastho dvijātiḥ vidyāvratasnātakaṃ śiṣyo guruṃ śvaśuro jāmātaraṃ jāmātā śvaśuraṃ bhāgineyo mātulaṃ madhuparkākhyena gṛhyoktarūpeṇa karmaṇā pūjayet | parimitāt saṃvatsarāt saṃvatsare ‘tīte punar gṛhāgatān etān pūjayet | ye tu snātakam ācāryaḥ pūjayet taṃ pratītam ity uktatvād ity āhuḥ, teṣāṃ parivarsarān iti snātakaviśeṣaṇaṃ kathaṃ syāt || 3.119 ||
rājā ca śrotiyaś caiva yajñakarmaṇy upasthite |
madhuparkeṇa saṃpūjyau na tva ayajña iti sthitiḥ || 3.120 ||
rājasnātayakoḥ saṃkocāyedam āha rājeti | rājasnātakau yajñakarmaṇy eva saṃprāptau madhuparkeṇa pūjanīyau | na tv ayajñe saṃvatsarād ūrdhvam apīti śāstramaryādā | saṃvatsarāt punaḥ arvāk yajñe ‘pi na pūjyau pūrvavākyopari saṃvatsarād ity upādāne | atra ca na tu yajña ity upādānāt rājasnātakapūjāsaṅkocārthatvam asya pratīyate || 3.120 ||
sāyaṃ tv annasya siddhasya patny amantraṃ baliṃ haret |
vaiśvadevaṃ hi nāmaitat sāyaṃ prātar vidhīyate || 3.121 ||
sāyam iti | dinānte ‘nnasya siddhasya “indrāya namaḥ” ity evamādimantravarjaṃ manasoddeśena patnī baliharaṇaṃ bhūtayajñākhyaṃ kuryāt | yasmād evaṃ nāma yajñādyātmabhojanaparyantaṃ etat sāyaṃ prātaḥ gṛhasthasyopadiśyate || 3.121 ||
pitṛyajñaṃ tu nirvartya vipraś candrakṣaye ‘gnimān |
piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryān māsānumāsikam || 3.122 ||
pitṛyajñam iti | āhitāgnir amāvāsyāyāṃ śrautaṃ piṇḍayajñākhyaṃ karma kṛtvā, “aupasadāgnir api evam anāhitāgnir nitye śrapayitvā” iti sūtravacanāt sūtroktaṃ tad eva kṛtvā māsi bhavaṃ piṇḍapitṛyajñāṅgabhūtānāṃ piṇḍānām anu paścāt āhriyata iti piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryāt | vipragrahaṇaṃ dvijapradarśanārtham, trayāṇāṃ prakṛtatvāt || 3.122 ||
piṇḍānāṃ māsikaṃ śrāddham anvāhāryaṃ vidur budhāḥ |
tac cāmiṣeṇa kartavyaṃ praśastena prayatnataḥ || 3.123 ||
adhunā nāmavacanena piṇḍapitṛyajñānantaram asya samarthayate piṇḍānām iti | yat eva tan māsikaṃ śrāddhaṃ piṇḍānām anu paścād āhriyate tataḥ piṇḍānvāhāryakam etad vidvāṃso manyante | evaṃ ca yuktaṃ yat piṇḍapitṛyajñānantaraṃ kriyat iti | tan māṃsena viśeṣeṇa vihitena khaḍgamāṃsādinā prayatnataḥ karyam, tadabhāve punar anyair haviṣyaiḥ || 3.123 ||
tatra ye bhojanīyāḥ syur ye ca varjyā dvijottamāḥ |
yāvantaś caiva yaiś cānnais tān pravakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ || 3.124 ||
tatreti | tasmin śrāddhe bhojanīyā ye ca varjanīyāḥ yathāsaṃkhyākāś ca yaiś cānnais tat kartavyaṃ tat sarvaṃ pravakṣyāmi || 3.124 ||
dvau daive pitṛkṛtye trīn ekaikam ubhayatra vā |
bhojayet susamṛddho ‘pi na pravarteta vistare || 3.125 ||
yady apy upadeśataḥ tasya ye bhojanīyāḥ syuḥ iti prāptaṃ tathāpi alpavaktavyatvāt saṃkhyāṃ tāvad āha dvāv iti | dvau pitryāṅgabhūte daive pitṛpitāmahaprapitāmahoddeśena trīn ekaikaṃ vā daivapitryayoḥ brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayet | uktātiriktabhojanasamartho ‘rgham adhikabhojane na vai pravarteta | yat tu “na tv ekaikaṃ sarveṣāṃ piṇḍair vyākhyātam” iti gṛhyadarśanāt, “nimantrayeta tryavarān” iti cehābhidhānāt “ekaikam ubhayatra vā” ity asya vistarapratiṣedhārthavādatvam āha tad asat | “dvau daive pitṛkṛtye trīn” ity etad vikalpena asya śravaṇāt | yājñavalkye ca “ekaikam eva” iti vidhidarśanāt, vasiṣṭhe ca,
yady ekaṃ bhojayec chrāddhe daivaṃ tatra kathaṃ bhavet |
annaṃ pātre samuddhṛtya sarvasya prakṛtasya ca ||
devatāyatane kṛtvā tataḥ śrāddhaṃ pravartayet ||
ity ekabrāhmaṇabhojanāt | yat punaḥ “na tv ekaikam” ityādigṛhyavacanaṃ tat dravyabrāhmaṇādisamyaksaṃpadviṣayam na tv anyapratiṣedhārtham; tathā ca etadanantaram eva gṛhyakṛt “kāmam anādya” ity āha, adanīyādyabhāvāt kāmam ekam api sarveṣāṃ bhojayet iti | evaṃ ca “nimantrayet tryavarān” ity etad api dravyabrāhmaṇādisamyaksampattiviṣayaṃ na tv ekaikavidhyatyantābhāvajñāpakam | vistara iti vṛttavaśāt “prathame vā caśabde” iti smaraṇāt
|| 3.125 ||
satkriyāṃ deśakālau ca śaucaṃ brāhmaṇasaṃpadaḥ |
pañcaitān vistaro hanti tasmān neheta vistaram || 3.126 ||
satkriyām iti | brāhmaṇapūjāṃ cokṣādideśam aparāhṇakālaṃ śaucaṃ guṇavadbrāhmaṇānāṃ vistāro nāśayati | tasmād uktātiriktabrāhmabhojanaṃ na kuryāt | hetuto vistaraniṣedhāt satkriyādisaṃbhave ‘dhikabrāhmaṇabhojanam apy asti | tathā ca gautamenoktaḥ: “avarān na ca brāhmaṇān bhojayet | ayujo vā yathotsāham” iti || 3.126 ||
prathitā pretakṛtyaiṣā pitryaṃ nāma vidhukṣaye |
tasmin yuktasyaiti nityaṃ pretakṛtyaiva laukikī || 3.127 ||
prathiteti | yo ‘yaṃ pitryo nāma vidhiḥ pitryaṃ karma eṣā pretakṛtyā pretopakārārthā kriyā kṣaye candrakṣaye gṛhe vā nāśramantare, sarvagṛhiṇāṃ khyātā | tasmin kṛtye pitrye karmaṇi yuktasya tanniṣṭhasya laukikī smārtā pretakriyā iti preta upakriyate guṇavatputrapautraprabandhopapattyā tasmād etad yatnataḥ kāryam || 3.127 ||
śrotriyāyaiva deyāni havyakavyāni dātṛbhiḥ |
arhattamāya viprāya tasmai dattaṃ mahāphalam || 3.128 ||
śrotriyāyeti | daivapitryādīny annāni chandomātrādhyāyini deyāni | chandomātrādhyāyini śrotriyaśabdaprayogān nātyantāpāyena niyamaḥ | saṃbhave punaḥ samastaśākhādhyāyine dānam | “yatnena bhojayec chrāddhaṃ bahvṛcam” ityādinimaye sati punaḥ śrotriyatve sati śrutavṛttābhijanādiyoge ca pūjyatamāya viprāya deyāni yatas tasmai datvā pitṛtṛptidvāreṇātiśayenopakārakaṃ bhavati || 3.128 ||
ekaikam api vidvāṃsaṃ daive pitrye ca bhojayet |
puṣkalaṃ phalam āpnoti nāmantrajñān bahūn api || 3.129 ||
yasmāt ekaikam iti | daivapitryoḥ ekaikam api vedārthavidaṃ bhojayet | paripūrṇaṃ phalaṃ pitṛtṛptyākhyaṃ prāpnoti | na tv aviduṣo bahūn api bhojayan puṣkalaṃ phalam āpnoti ity abhidhānāt brāhmaṇasya bhojanam atra pradhānaṃ piṇḍadānādi tu aṅgam ity avasīyate || 3.129 ||
dūrād eva parīkṣeta brāhmaṇaṃ vedapāragam |
tīrthaṃ tad dhavyakavyānāṃ pradāne so ‘tithiḥ smṛtaḥ || 3.130 ||
dūrād iti | “ye mātṛto daśapuruṣaṃ pitṛto daśapuruṣam” ityādisūtrakāroktartvikparīkṣānyāyena samastaśākhādhyāyinaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ parīkṣeta yasmāt tasmāt tathāvidho brāhmaṇo havyakavyānāṃ tīrtham iva (yathā) tīrthenodakāvatāraṇamārgeṇodakārthino gacchanti evaṃ tathāvidhena brāhmaṇena havyakavyāni devān pitṝn gacchanti | tathāvidho brāhmaṇaḥ atithitulyaḥ smṛtaḥ mahāphalatvāt || 3.130 ||
sahasraṃ hi sahasrāṇām anṛcāṃ yatra bhuñjate |
ekas tān mantravit prītaḥ sarvān arhati dharmataḥ || 3.131 ||
sahasram iti | yatra daivapitryādau anṛgvidāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ daśalakṣāṇi bhuñjate tatraiko vedavit bhuktvā prītas tuṣṭaḥ dharmato dharmotpādanena tān sarvān arhati svīkartuṃ yogyo bhavati, tadbhojanajanyadharmān utpādayatīty arthaḥ | iti vidvadbrāhmaṇabhojanastutiḥ
|| 3.131 ||
jñānotkṛṣṭāya deyāni kavyāni ca havīṃṣi ca |
na hi hastāv asṛgdagdhau rudireṇaiva śudhyataḥ || 3.132 ||
jñānotkṛṣṭāyeti | vidyotkṛṣṭebhyo havyakavyāni deyāni nānyebhyo yasmāt yathā rudhiropaliptau hastau rudhireṇaivāpagatalepau kartuṃ na pāryate, api tūdakenaiva śakyate, evaṃ cājñadānamukhena nātmā uttārayituṃ śakyate api tu vidvaddānadvāreṇaivety avidvannindayā vidvaddānastutiḥ || 3.132 ||
yāvato grasate grāsān havyakavyeṣv amantravit |
tāvato grasate preto dīptaśūlaṣṭarṣṭyayoguḍān || 3.133 ||
yāvata iti | yatsaṃkhyakān grāsān havyakavyeṣv avedavid grasate tatsaṃkhyākān eva prastutaśrāddhakartā jvalitān sthūlān śūlarṣṭyākhyāyudhān ayaḥpiṇḍān grasate iti avidvaddānaphalakathanam | tathā ca vyāso dātur eva phalam āha,
grasate yāvataḥ piṇḍān yasya vai haviṣo ‘nṛcaḥ |
grasate tāvataḥ śūlān gatvā vaivasvatakṣayam || 3.133 ||
jñānaniṣṭhā dvijāḥ kecit taponiṣṭhās tathāpare |
tapaḥsvādhyāyaniṣṭhāś ca karmaniṣṭhās tathāpare || 3.134 ||
jñānaniṣṭhā iti | kecid ātmajñānaparā viprā bhavanti | apare kṛcchrāditapaḥpradhānāḥ | anye tu tapo’dhyayananiratāḥ | apare yāgāditatparāḥ || 3.134 ||
jñānaniṣṭheṣu kavyāni pratiṣṭhāpyāni yatnataḥ |
havyāni tu yathānyāyaṃ sarveṣv eva caturṣv api || 3.135 ||
iti kim āha jñānaniṣṭheṣv iti | jñānapradhānebhyo yatnena pitrarthāni deyāni | devārthāni punaḥ śāstramaryādānatikrameṇa caturbhyo jñānaniṣṭhādibhyo ‘pīti || 3.135 \\
aśrotriyaḥ pitā yasya putraḥ syād vedapāragaḥ |
aśrotriyo vā putraḥ syāt pitā syād vedapāragaḥ || 3.136 ||
aśrotiya iti | yaḥ aśrotriyaputraḥ ātmanā śrotriyo, yaḥ śrotriyaputraḥ ātmanā cāśrotriyaḥ || 3.136 ||
jyāyāṃsam anayor vidyād yasya syāc chrotriyaḥ pitā |
mantrasaṃpūjanārthaṃ tu satkāram itaro ‘rhati || 3.137 ||
tayoḥ kaḥ śreyān ity upanyasyāha jyāyāṃsam iti | anayoḥ pūrvanirdiṣṭayor madhyāt śrotriyaputram ātmanāśrotriyam api praśasyataraṃ jānīyāt | paścāt aśrotriyaputraḥ ātmanā ca śrotriyaḥ sa tadadhītavedapūjārthaṃ pūjām arhati | vedas taddvāreṇa pūjyate | na tv asau pūjārha iti pitrādividyāparīkṣārtham | etac cāśrotriyadānārtham, “śrotriyaiva deyāni” iti niyamāt | evaṃ ca “dūrād eva parīkṣeta” ity etat vidyāvyatiriktajātyatiśayaparīkṣārthatvenāvatiṣṭhate || 3.137 ||
na śrāddhe bhojayen mitraṃ dhanaiḥ kāryo ‘sya saṃgrahaḥ |
nāriṃ na mitraṃ yaṃ vidyāt taṃ śrāddhe bhojayed dvijam || 3.138 ||
na śrāddhe bhojayen mitraṃ dhanaiḥ kāryo ‘sya saṃgrahaḥ | nāriṃ na mitraṃ yaṃ vidyāt taṃ śrāddhe bhojayed dvijam || mitraṃ śrāddhe na bhojayet | kiṃ tarhi | dhanāntarair asya maitryādisnehaḥ kāryaḥ | yaṃ punaḥ śatruṃ na mitraṃ nābhibhartuṃ vijñātaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ śrāddhe bhojayet || 3.138 ||
yasya mitrapradhānāni śrāddhāni ca havīṃṣi ca |
tasya pretya phalaṃ nāsti śrāddheṣu ca haviḥṣu ca || 3.139 ||
yasyeti | yasya mitropakāraparāṇi havyakavyāni tasya pāralaukikaṃ phalaṃ na bhavatīti phalābhāvakathanaparam etat, na kevalaṃ pūrvamitrasya śrāddhādinā deyaṃ yāvat || 3.139 ||
yaḥ saṃgatāni kurute mohāc chrāddhena mānavaḥ |
sa svargāc cyavate lokāc chrāddhamitro dvijādhamaḥ || 3.140 ||
ya iti | “śrāddhadānenānena saha maitrī me bhaviṣyati” iti evaṃ yo manuṣyaḥ śāstrānabhijñatayā śrāddhena mitrabhāvaṃ kurute sa mitrahetutvāt śrāddhaṃ mitram asyeti śrāddhamitro dvijāpasadaḥ karmāntarārjitenāpi svarloke na saṃbadhyata iti || 3.140 ||
saṃbhojinī sābhihitā paiśācī dakṣiṇā dvijaiḥ |
ihaivāste tu sā loke gaur andhevaikaveśmani || 3.141 ||
saṃbhojinīti | sā dakṣiṇā taddānaṃ saha bhujyate yayeti saṃbhojinī yā goṣṭhī piśācadharmatvāt paiśācī manvādibhir uktā | sā ca mitrārthatvād iha loke evāste andhā gaur yathaikasmin gṛhe | na paraloke pratyupakārāya bhavati || 3.141 ||
yatheriṇe bījam uptvā na vaptā labhate phalam |
yathānṛce havir datvā na dātā labhate phalam || 3.142 ||
yatheti | yathā ūṣare bhūpradeśe dhānyāny uptvā karṣakaḥ phalaṃ na prāpnoti evam aśrotriyāya śrāddhādi datvā dātā na phalaṃ prāpnoti | śrotriyadāne vaiphalyakathanārtham etat || 3.142 ||
dātṝn pratigrahītṝṃś ca kurute phalabhāginaḥ |
viduṣe dakṣiṇāṃ datvā vidhivat pretya ceha ca || 3.143 ||
viduṣe dakṣiṇā bhavati pretya ceha ca | vedārthavide ca yathāśāstraṃ śrāddhādi dattaṃ pitṛtṛptyākhyāmmutrikaphalena kīrtyanuṣaṅgaihikaphalena ca dātṝn saphalā kurute | pratigrahītṝṃś ca tatpratigrahalabdhadhanānuṣṭhitayāgādiphalena amutra saphalān kurute | adharmārjitadravyānuṣṭhitayāgādinaiṣphalyāt | iha loke ca dharmārjitadhanakṛtakṛṣyādiphala-viṣeyān saphalān kurute iti vidvaddānasya dātṛpratigrahītṛdṛṣṭādṛṣṭāphalakathanārtham etat
|| 3.143 ||
kāmaṃ śrāddhe ‘rcayen mitraṃ nābhirūpam api tv arim |
dviṣatā hi havir bhuktaṃ bhavati pretya niṣphalam || 3.144 ||
kāmam iti | varaṃ guṇavadbrāhmaṇāntarābhāve saguṇaṃ mitraṃ śrāddhe bhojayet na yogyam api śatrum, yato ‘riṇā śrāddhādi bhuktaṃ paraloke niṣphalaṃ bhavatīti | anyābhāve guṇavān mitrapratiprasavārtham etat | chandomātrādhyāyini śrotriyaśabdaprayogāt tadāśrayaṇaṃ naiyamikam uktam || 3.144 ||
yatnena bhojayec chrāddhe bahvṛcaṃ vedapāragam |
śākhāntaragam athādhvaryuṃ chandogaṃ tu samāptikam || 3.145 ||
adhunā tu sati saṃbhave phalaviśeṣārthaṃ samastaśākhādhyāyini dānam āha yatneneti | samastaśākhādhyayinaṃ bahvṛcaṃ tathāvidham evādhvaryuṃ chandogaṃ caivaṃvidham eva yatnena śrāddhe bhojayet | vṛttavaśān nānāprakāraiḥ śabdaiḥ sakalaśākhādhyāyina evaite yuktāḥ | ye tu chandomātrādhyāyipratiṣedhārthaṃ śrotriyāyaiva deyānīty etadviśeṣaṇārthatvam asyāhuḥ | teṣāṃ anenaiva siddhe śrotriyāyaiva deyānīty etad anarthakaṃ syāt || 3.145 ||
eṣām anyatamo yasya bhuñjīta śrāddham arcitaḥ |
pitṝṇāṃ tasya tṛptiḥ syāc chāśvatī sāptapauruṣī || 3.146 ||
eṣām iti | eṣāṃ bahvṛcādīnāṃ madhyād anyatamo yasya tv arcitamanāḥ śrāddhaṃ bhuñjīta tasya pitrādisaptapuruṣavyāpinī anavacchinnā pitṝṇāṃ tṛptiḥ syād iti samastaśākhādhyāyibhojanaphalakathanam | ata eva vijñāyate – yatra kvacanāvasthitāḥ pitaraḥ putrādyupahṛtakavyaiḥ sukham āsate | itaś ca pitṛtṛptiphalam etat, śrāddhakarmaṇi ca akṛtābhyāgamakṛtavipraṇāśāv atra codanīyau, śāstraikagocaratve vaiśvānaravat tathaivāgamāt
|| 3.146 ||
eṣa vai prathamaḥ kalpaḥ pradāne havyakavyayoḥ |
anukalpas tv ayaṃ jñeyaḥ sadā sadbhir anuṣṭhitaḥ || 3.147 ||
eṣa iti | havyakavyapradānena eṣa mukho vidhiḥ yad asaṃbandhibhyaḥ śrotriyādibhyo dīyata iti | ayaṃ vakṣyamāṇaḥ punar mukhābhāve vidhir boddhavyaḥ, mukhyābhāve ca sarvadā sādhubhir anuṣṭhitaḥ || 3.147 ||
mātāmahaṃ mātulaṃ ca svstrīyaṃ śvaśuraṃ gurum |
dauhitraṃ viṭpatiṃ bandhum ṛtvigyājyau ca bhojayet || 3.148 ||
mātāmaham iti | mātāmahamātulabhāgineyaśvaśuravidyāgurudauhitrartvigyājyān bhojayet | viṭ prajā tasyāḥ patiṃ viṭpatiṃ jāmātaraṃ bandhuṃ ca sagotrādikam || 3.148 ||
na brāhmaṇaṃ parīkṣeta daiva karmaṇi dharmavit |
pitrye karmaṇi tu prāpte parīkṣeta prayatnataḥ || 3.149 ||
neti | dharmajñaḥ daive karmaṇi bhojanārthaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ na parīkṣeta lokaprasiddhimātreṇaivāsau tathā pratipadya tatra bhojanīyaḥ | pitrye karmaṇy upasthite “dūrād eva parīkṣeta" ityuktanītyā yatnataḥ parīkṣeta || 3.149 ||
ye stenāḥ patitā klībā ye ca nāstikavṛttayaḥ |
tān havyakavyayor viprān anarhān manur abravīt || 3.150 ||
ye stenāḥ patitāḥ klībā iti | stenādīn viprān havyakavyayor ubhayor apy ayogyān manur āha | corān brāhmaṇasuvarṇād anyatra, tasya patitatvenopasaṃgrahāt | patitam ekādaśe vakṣayati suvarṇād anyatra, tasya patitatvenopasaṃgrahāt | patitam ekādaśe vakṣyati klībo napuṃsakam | nāsti paralokakarmety evaṃ vartanaṃ yeṣāṃ te nāstikavṛttayaḥ || 3.150 ||
jaṭilaṃ cānadhīyānaṃ durbalaṃ kitavaṃ tathā |
yācayanti ca ye pūgāṃs tāṃś ca śrāddhe na bhojayet || 3.151 ||
jaṭilam iti | brahmacāriṇam asvīkṛtavedam atyantānadhyayanasya śrotiryāyaiva deyānīti niyame sati prāptyabhāvāt | tathā duścarmadyūtavṛttyayājyayājakān śrāddhe na bhojayet | daive tv aniṣedhaḥ, tathā yatrobhayaniṣedham icchati tatra havyakavyagrahaṇam ubhayatra ceti karoti
|| 3.151 ||
cikitsakā devalakā māṃsavikrayiṇas tathā |
vipaṇena ca jīvanto varjyāḥ syur havyakavyayoḥ || 3.152 ||
cikitsakā devalakā māṃsavikrayiṇas tatheti | cikitsakādayo havyakavyayor ubhayor api varjanīyā bhaveyuḥ | cikitsako bhiṣak, devalakaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ na dharmārtham, tatra vihitatvāt | māṃsavikrayiṇaḥ sakṛd api | tathā ca “sadyaḥ patati māṃsena” iti liṅgam | anyathā vipaṇena gatārthaṃ syāt, iha ca jīvanta ity upādānāt māṃsena sakṛd api pratīyate | vipaṇo vāṇijyam |
|| 3.152 ||
pratiroddhā guroś caiva tyaktāgnir vārdhuṣis tathā |
preṣyo grāmasya rājñaś ca kunakhī śyāvadantakaḥ || 3.153 ||
no commentary
yakṣmī ca paśupālaś ca parivettā nirākṛtiḥ |
brahmadviṭ parivittiś ca gaṇābhyantara eva ca || 3.154 ||
yakṣmīti | kṣayī vṛttyarthatvena paśupālaḥ prasiddhaḥ, parivettṛparivittī ca vakṣyamāṇau | nirākṛtiḥ vedādīnāṃ nirākartā anupajīvyaḥ, brāhmaṇānāṃ dveṣṭā gaṇmadhyagato brāhmaṇādibhiḥ ity ete havyakavyayoḥ varjyāḥ syuḥ || 3.154 ||
kuśīlavo ‘vakīrṇo ca vṛṣalīpatir eva ca |
paurnabhavaś ca kāṇaś ca yasya copapatir gṛhe || 3.155 ||
kuśīlava iti | naṭagāyakādiḥ | kṛtastrīsaṃsargayogo brahmacārī śūdrāyā bhartā | punarbhvāḥ putro vakṣyamāṇaḥ, kāṇaḥ, yasya ca jāyājāro gṛhe ity ete havyakavyayoḥ pariharaṇīyāḥ
|| 3.155 ||
bhṛtakādhyāpako yaś ca bhṛtakādhyāpitas tathā |
śūdraśiṣyo guruś caiva vāgduṣṭaḥ kuṇḍagolakau || 3.156 ||
bhṛtakādhyāpaka iti | paṇapūrvādhyāpakaḥ, paṇapūrvādhyāpitaḥ, śūdraśiṣyaḥ tasyaiva ca guruḥ, na svaguruḥ, tasyānukalpe bhojyatvenoktatvāt | paruṣabhāṣī, kuṇḍagolakau vakṣyamāṇau, ete havyakavyayoḥ varjyāḥ || 3.156 ||
akāraṇaparityaktā mātāpitror guros tathā |
brāhmair yonaiś ca saṃbandhaiḥ saṃyogaṃ patitair gataḥ || 3.157 ||
akāraṇaparityakteti | mātṛpitṛvidyāguṛūṇāṃ niṣkāraṇaṃ parityaktā patitaiś cādhyayanakanyādānādisaṃbandhaḥ saṃparkaṃ gataḥ arvāg api saṃvatsarāt | saṃvatsareṇa tu patitatvād eva gatarthatā “saṃvatsareṇa patati” iti || 3.157 ||
agāradāhī garadaḥ kuṇḍāśī somavikrayī |
samudrayāyī bandī ca tailikaḥ kūṭakārakaḥ || 3.158 ||
agāradāhīti | gṛhasya dāhako, māraṇadravyadātā kuṇḍasya vakṣyamāṇasya yo ‘nnam aśnāti, somasya latārūpasya vikretā, samudraṃ yo gacchati, stutīnāṃ pāṭhakaḥ, tilādeḥ peṣakaḥ, lekhyasākṣyādau kūṭasya kartā ity eta ubhayatra pariharaṇīyāḥ || 3.158 ||
pitrā vivadamānaś ca kitavo madyapas tathā |
pāparogy abhiśastaś ca jāliko rasavikrayī || 3.159 ||
pitreti | pāparogy abhiśastaś ca jāliko rasavikrayī | śāstrālokavastuṣu yaḥ pitrā saha vivadate, kitavaḥ aparaḥ devayati, itarasyoktatvāt | kekara iti pāṭhakācāraḥ | surāvyatiriktamadyasya pātā kuṣṭhī, pātakopapātakakartṛtvenotpannābhiśāpo ‘saty api tatkāritvaniścaye, savyājo dharmānuṣṭhāne, rasaśabdasyekṣurasādāv api prasiddhatvāt tadvikrayī viṣasyeva tatrāpy arthaśāstre rasaśabdaprayogāt ity ete ubhayatra parihartavyāḥ || 3.159 ||
dhanuḥśarāṇāṃ kartā ca yaś cāgredidhiṣūpatiḥ |
mitradhrugdyūtavṛttiś ca putrācāryas tathaiva ca || 3.160 ||
dhanuḥśarāṇām iti | dhanuḥśarasya kārakaḥ | agredidhiṣūpatiḥ ‘bhrātur mṛtasya” iti vakṣyamāṇaḥ | vṛttavaśāt agra ity adhyalopena satyabhāmā bhāmetivat | yat tu gautamīyaparyālocanayā agredidhiṣūpatiśabdena dvayaṃ niṣpadyate – agredidhiṣūpatiḥ didhiṣūpatiś ca | tatrāgredidhiṣūpatiḥ anūḍhākanyāgāmī, didhiṣūpatis tadvoḍhety āhuḥ – tad asat, aśabdārthatvāt | na ca didhiṣūpatiś ca tadvoḍheti vyākhyānaṃ yuktam, “bhrātur mṛtasya” iti mānavalakṣaṇavirodhāt | mitrasya yo ‘pakāre vartate, dyūtavṛttiḥ sabhikaḥ, putreṇādhyāpitaḥ mukhyasya putrācāryatvasyāsaṃbhavāt || 3.160 ||
bhrāmarī gaṇḍamālī ca śvitry atho piśunas tathā |
unmatto ‘ndhaś ca varjyāḥ syur vedanindaka eva ca || 3.161 ||
bhrāmarīti | apasmārī, gaṇḍamālākhyavyādhyupetaḥ, śvitrī, sūcakaḥ, dhātusaṃkṣobhādiyuktaḥ, andho vedasya kutsakaḥ, ity ete havyakavyayor varjyāḥ syuḥ || 3.161 ||
hastigo’śvoṣṭradamako nakṣatrair yaś ca jīvati |
pakṣiṇāṃ poṣako yaś ca yuddhācāryas tathaiva ca || 3.162 ||
hastigo’śvoṣṭradamaka iti | hastigavāśvoṣṭrāṇāṃ vinetā, jyitiḥśāstreṇa jīvanaḥ pakṣiṇāṃ vārtanapūrvakaṃ krīḍārthaṃ poṣayati yuddhaśikṣayitā ete ubhayatra parihartavyāḥ || 3.162 ||
srotasāṃ bhedako yaś ca teṣāṃ cāvaraṇe rataḥ |
gṛhasaṃveśako dūto vṛkṣāropaka eva ca || 3.163 ||
srotasām iti | udakāgamanasetubandhabhedakaḥ bhedādinā deśāntaranetā ca | teṣām avacchādanakṛt, vāstuvidyājīvanaḥ dūto rājapreṣyavyatirikto ‘pi | vṛkṣāṇāṃ ropayitā mūlyena, na dharmārthaṃ “daśāmravāpī narakaṃ na yāti” ityādinodanāt || 3.163 ||
śvakrīḍī śyenajīvī ca kanyādūṣaka eva ca |
hiṃsro vṛṣalavṛttiś ca gaṇānāṃ caiva yājakaḥ || 3.164 ||
śvakrīḍīti | śvabhiḥ yaḥ krīḍati, yājakakriyādijīvakaḥ kanyāyā nindakaḥ hiṃsārataḥ, śūdrajīvnaḥ | vṛṣalaputra iti vā pāṭhaḥ | caturdaśyādau vināyakādigaṇayāgakṛt ity eva ubhayatra varjanīyāḥ
|| 3.164 ||
ācārahīnaḥ klībaś ca nityaṃ yācanakas tathā |
kṛṣijīvī ślīpadī ca sadbhir nindita eva ca || 3.165 ||
ācārahīna iti | pratyutthānādyācāravarjitaḥ, klībaḥ nirutsāhaḥ, napuṃsakasyoktatvāt, sarvadā yācñāsvabhāvaḥ | asvayaṃkṛtaṃ vāpi yaḥ kṛṣyā vṛttyantarasaṃbhave ‘pi jīvati, ślīpadī vyādhiviśeṣād ucchūnnaikacaraṇaḥ sādhubhir ninditaḥ kenāpi nimittenety etat | ubhayatra bahiḥkāryāḥ || 3.165 ||
aurabhriko māhiṣikaḥ parapūrvāpatis tathā |
pretaniryāpakaś caiva varjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ || 3.166 ||
aurabhrikaḥ meṣamahiṣajīvī punarbhuvo bhartā mūlyena pretānāṃ nirhārakaḥ na dharmārtham, “etad vai paramaṃ tapo yaṃ pretam araṇyaṃ haranti” iti śrutyā noditatvāt | ete yatnata ubhayatra varjanīyāḥ || 3.166 ||
etān vigarhitācārān apāṅkteyān dvijādhamān |
dvijātipravaro vidvān ubhayatra vivarjayet || 3.167 ||
etān iti | ete ninditācārāḥ stenādayaḥ ihajanmani | kāṇādayaś ca “evaṃ karmāvśeṣeṇa jāyante” iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt prāgjanmani | ata etān yogyabrāhmaṇaiḥ saha ekapaṅktibhojanānarhān brāhmaṇāpasadān dvijaśreṣṭhaḥ śāstrajñaḥ daive pitrye ca varjayet || 3.167 ||
brāhmaṇo hy anadhīyānas tṛṇāgnir iva śāmyati |
tasmai havyaṃ na dātavyaṃ na hi bhasmani hūyate || 3.168 ||
brāhmaṇa iti | tṛṇāgnir yathā haviṣopaśāmyati na havirdahanasamarthaḥ, tataś ca niṣphalaḥ tatra homaḥ, evaṃ niṣphalatvāt vedādhyayanena śūnyo brāhmaṇaḥ tṛṇāgnitulyaḥ tasmā uddeśena na dātavyam | yato na bhasmani homaḥ kriyate | “śrotriyāyaiva deyāni” ity anenaiva anadhīyānaniṣedhasiddhau idaṃ vacanaṃ daive kāṇādīnāṃ chando’dhyāyināṃ svācārāṇām abhyuanujñānārtham | garhitācāraṃ varjayet anadhīyānaṃ ca | yas tu svācāro ‘dhīyānaś ca tasya daive kāṇatvaliṅgasaṃsūcitaṃ prāgjanmārjitagarhitakarmaśeṣavatvam anapekṣam | tathā vasiṣṭhaḥ
atha cen mantrasaṃyuktaḥ śārīraiḥ paṅktidūṣaṇaiḥ |
adūṣyaṃ taṃ yamaḥ prāha paṅktipāvana eva saḥ || iti || 3.168 ||
apaṅktyadāne yo dātur bhavaty ūrdhvaṃ phalodayaḥ |
daive karmaṇi pitrye vā taṃ pravakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ || 3.169 ||
apaṅktyadāna iti | paṅktibhojanānarhadāne dātur yad eno devapitrarthahaviṣi phalotpattir bhavati tat sākalyena pravakṣyāmi || 3.169 ||
avratair yad dvijair bhuktaṃ parivettrādibhis tathā |
apāṅkteyair yad anyaiś ca tad vai rakṣāṃsi bhuñjate || 3.170 ||
vedagrahaṇārthaṃ sāvitrādivratarahitaiḥ tathā parivettrādibhir anyaiś ca stenādibhir apāṅkteyaiḥ yat devapitrarthaṃ bhuktaṃ tat sukṛtaleśavaśena rakṣāṃsi bhuñjate na dātrabhimataphalāya bhavati | avratānāṃ ca niṣiddhamadhyāpaṭhitānāṃ apy asmād eva niṣedho bodhyaḥ || 3.170 ||
dārāgnihotrasaṃyogaṃ kurute yo ‘graje sthite |
parivettā sa vijñeyaḥ parivittis tu pūrvajaḥ || 3.171 ||
parivettrādīn aprasiddhatvāl lakṣayati dānāgnihotrasaṃyogam iti | yo jyeṣṭhe bhrātari akṛtadārake sati dāraśrautasmārtāgnyānayanaṃ kurute sa parivettā, jyeṣṭhaś ca parivittir bodhyaḥ || 3.171 ||
parivittiḥ parīvettā yayā ca parividyate |
sarve te narakaṃ yānti dātṛyājakapañcamāḥ || 3.172 ||
prasaṅgād etadvyatikramaphalam āha parivittir iti | parivittiparivettārau yayā ca kanyayā hetubhūtayā parivedanaṃ kriyate dātā ca kanyāyāḥ yājakaś ca tadudvāhahomasyopadeṣṭā yeṣāṃ pañcamaḥ te narakaṃ gacchanti || 3.172 ||
bhrātur mṛtasya bhāryāyāṃ yo ‘nurajyeta kāmataḥ |
darmeṇāpi niyuktāyāṃ sa jñeyo didhiṣūpatiḥ || 3.173 ||
bhrātur iti | mṛtasya bhrātuḥ vakṣyamāṇaniyoge dharmeṇāpi niyuktāyāṃ bhāryāyāṃ yo niyogadharmaṃ hitvā kāmena hetubhūtenānurāgaṃ bhāvayet so ‘gredidhiṣūpatiḥ niṣiddhatvāt | atra vṛttavaśāt satyabhāmāvac cāgraśabdasyāprayogaḥ ||3.173 ||
paradāreṣu jāyete dvau sutau kuṇḍagolakau |
patyau jīvati kuṇḍaḥ syān mṛte bhartari golakaḥ || 3.174 ||
paradāreṣv iti | parabhāyāyāṃ kuṇḍagolakākhyau sutāv utpadyete | tatra sapatikāyāṃ ya utpadyate sa kuṇḍaḥ, vidhavāyāṃ ca golakaḥ || 3.174 ||
te tu jātāḥ parakṣetre prāṇinaḥ pretya ceha ca |
dattāni havyakavyāni nāśayanti pradāyinām || 3.175 ||
te tu jātāḥ parakṣetre iti | te parabhāryājātāḥ kuṇḍagolakākhāḥ adṛṣṭārthānupayogāt prāṇimātravyapadeśārhā havakavyānīha dattāni ihaloke ānuṣaṅgikakīrtyākhyaphalābhāvāt amutra devapitṛtṛptyabhāvāt dātṝṇāṃ niṣphalāni bhavanti kurvanti vā || 3.175 ||
apāṅktyā yāvataḥ paṅktyān bhuñjānān anupaśyati |
tāvatāṃ na phalaṃ tatra dātā prāpnoti bāliśaḥ || 3.176 ||
apāṅktyā iti | paṅktibhuñjānānarhān tān api | yāvataḥ yāvatsaṃkhyākān anarhā bhojanānarhā anupaśyanti tāvatsaṅkhyākānāṃ saṃbandhi tatra śrāddhādau dātā ajñaḥ nātra phalam āpnuyāt iti apāṅktyadarśanaphalam uktam || 3.176 ||
vīkṣyāndho navateḥ kāṇaḥ ṣaṣṭeḥ śvitrī śatasya ca |
pāparogī sahasrasya dātur nāśayate phalam || 3.177 ||
andhādīnāṃ viśeṣam āha vīkṣyeti | andhasya darśanābhāvāt tatra saṃnihito ‘sau pāṅktyānāṃ navateḥ saṃbandhibhojanaphalaṃ nāśayati | evaṃ kāṇaḥ ṣaṣṭeḥ śvitrī śatasya kuṣṭhī sahasrasya ity andhādisaṃnidhiphalakathanam | saṃkhyāvaicitryād evedam apy avasīyate yat atha cen mantraviyukta ity uktaṃ ca śaṣṭidarśanāvaṣṭaṃbhena kāṇādīn bhojayiṣyataḥ pūrvapūrvam apacitasaṃkhyā ninditapravṛttiḥ | tadakāme ‘dhikaninidite iti || 3.177 ||
yāvataḥ saṃspṛśed aṅgair brāhmaṇāñ chūdrayājakaḥ |
tāvatāṃ na bhaved dātuḥ phalaṃ dānasya paurtikam || 3.178 ||
yāvataḥ saṃspṛśatīti | śūdrasya pākayajñāder upadeṣṭā kartā ca yatsaṃkhyākān brāhmaṇān aṅgaiḥ spṛśati āsaneṣūpakḷpteṣu barhiṣmatsu pṛthag iti sparśaniṣedhāt yāvatāṃ paṅktāv upaviśati tatsaṃkhyākānāṃ saṃbandhi bhojanaphalaṃ na bhavatīti | śūdrayājakabhojanaphalam | ata eva cāgraniṣiddhagaṇapaṭhitasyāpi niṣedho boddhavyaḥ
|| 3.178 ||
vedavic cāpi vipro ‘sya lobhāt kṛtvā pratigraham |
vināśaṃ vrajati kṣipram āmapātram ivāmbhasi || 3.179 ||
vedajño ‘pi vipraḥ śūdrayājakasakāśāl lobhena dānādi pratigṛhya mṛnmayāpakvaśarāvādyudaka ivāśu vināśm upaiti | prasaṅgāl lāghavārthaṃ aprakṛtam apy etad uktam | anyatra hi kriyamāṇe śūdrayājakagrahaṇaṃ kartavyaṃ syāt || 3.179 ||
somavikrayiṇe viṣṭhā bhiṣaje pūyaśoṇitam |
naṣṭaṃ devalake dattam apratiṣṭhaṃ tu vārdhuṣau || 3.180 ||
somavikrayiṇa iti | somavikrayiṇe yad dattaṃ tad dātṛbhojanārthaṃ śakṛt saṃpadyate | tac ca śakṛdbhojane tiryagjātyutpattiphalatā syāt [iti] anena prakāreṇoktaṃ bhavati | evaṃ pūyarudhire api vyākhyeye | naṣṭaṃ vināśam upagatam, ataś ca na tatphalaṃ bhavati | apratiṣṭhitam alabdhāspadam, ataś cānāśrayatvāt na phaladānasamartham || 3.180 ||
yat tu vāṇijake dattaṃ neha nāmutra tad bhavet |
bhasmanīva hutaṃ dravyaṃ tathā paunarbhave dvije || 3.181 ||
yad iti | vāṇijakāya yad dattaṃ tat neha kīrtaye, nāpi paralokaphalāya bhavati | punarbhūputrāya ca dattaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhasmanīva hutaṃ havyaṃ tatpreṣyam [tadvaj jñeyam]
|| 3.181 ||
itareṣu tv apaṅktyeṣu yathoddiṣṭeṣv asādhuṣu |
medo’sṛṅmāṃsamajjāsthi vadanty annaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ || 3.182 ||
itareṣv iti | itarebhyaḥ anuktaphalebhyo ‘pāṅktrebhyaḥ yad dattam annaṃ tat medaśoṇitamāṃsamajjāsthi bhavatīti vidvāṃsaḥ | atha “somavikrayiṇe viṣṭhā” itivad vyākhyeyam || 3.182 ||
apaṅktyopahatā paṅktiḥ pāvyate yair dvijottamaiḥ |
tān nibodhata kārtsneya dvijāgryān paṅktipāvanān || 3.183 ||
apāṅktyopahateti | stenādibhiḥ paṅktyupaviṣṭaiḥ dūṣitā paṅktiḥ yair brāhmaṇaiḥ pavitrīkriyate tān paṅktipavitrīkaraṇāt brāhmaṇān sākalyena śṛṇuta | yady apy asya paṅktiniṣedhāt stenādīnāṃ stenādīnāṃ śrāddhadānāsaṃbhave ‘pi sati paṅktipāvanavacanam anarthakam, tathāpi parīkṣāyāṃ kṛtāyām api rahasyavijñānadoṣasaṃbhavāt idaṃ tannivṛttyartham arthavad bhavati || 3.183 ||
agryāḥ sarveṣu vedeṣu sarvapravacaneṣu ca |
śrotriyānvayajāś caiva vijñeyāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ || 3.184 ||
agryā iti | sarveṣu vedeṣu ṛgvedādiṣu ye agre bhavāḥ (vyāḥ) vedapāragatve saty api vedaṃ karāmalakavat paśyantīti agryā ucyante, te paṅktipāvanā boddhavyāḥ | tathā procyate ebhir vedārtham iti pravacāny aṅgāni teṣu pūrvavad agryāḥ vedajñatvābhāve ‘pi paṅktipāvanāḥ tathā vaṃśam avicchinnavedāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ | pūrvaṃ “aśrotriyaḥ pitā yasya” (1.136) iti pitṛgataśrotiyatvena bhojyatvam uktam | anena tu sakalavaṃśāvicchinnavedatvena paṅktipāvana ucyate || 3.184 ||
triṇāciketaḥ pañcāgnis trisuparṇaḥ ṣaḍaṅgavit |
brahmadeyānusantāno jyeṣṭhasāmaga eva ca || 3.185 ||
triṇāciketa iti | triṇāciketākhyādhvaryava vedavratācaraṇāt guṇāt brāhmaṇo ‘pi triṇāciketaḥ, pañcāgnir vyākhyātaḥ, trisuparṇākhyaṛgvedavratayogāt pūrvavat trisuparṇaḥ | vyākaraṇādīni ṣaḍaṅgāni yo vetti | “sarvapravacaneṣu” ity anena aṅgādhyetā uktaḥ | anena tv aṅgajña ucyate | brāhmavivāhoḍhāpatyaṃ jyeṣṭhasāmākhyasāmavedavratayogāt jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ || 3.185 ||
vedārthavit pravaktā ca brahmacārī sahasradaḥ |
śatāyuś caiva vijñeyā brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ || 3.186 ||
vedārthavid iti | antareṇāpy aṅgāni yo vedārthaṃ prājñatayā vetti pravaktā adhyāpayitā vyākhyātā dharmārthaṃ vedavedārthayoḥ brahmacārī | “gāvo vai yajñasya mātaraḥ” iti vedadarśanāt ihāpi vedamūlatvāt gosahasrasya dātā bahupradaś ca, evaṃ śatavarṣaś ca ity ete brāhmaṇāḥ “śrotriyāyaiva deyāni” iti niyamāt | sati śrotriyatve etadguṇayoge paṅktipāvanā boddhavyāḥ || 3.186 ||
pūrvedyur aparedyur vā śrāddhakarmaṇy upasthite |
nimantrayīta tryavarān samyag viprān yathoditān || 3.187 ||
pūrvedyur iti | śrāddhe karmaṇi prāpte strīprayogāśaṅkāyāṃ śrāddhadivasāt pūrvasminn ahani parasminn eva asati saṃbhave yady atyantanyūnān tatas trīn na tu trīn eva, ekaikavidher apy uktatvāt | praśrayapūrvaṃ vā yathoktān viprān nimantrayet || 3.187 ||
nimantrito dvijaḥ pitrye niyatātmā bhavet sadā |
na ca chandāṃsy adhīyīta yasya śrāddhaṃ ca tad bhavet || 3.188 ||
nimantrita iti | śrāddhe brāhmaṇo nimantritaḥ strīnivṛttiyamaniyamānuṣṭhānavān sadā nimantraṇāt prabhṛti śrāddhāhorātraṃ yāvat syāt | vedāṃś ca japādyavaśyakartavyavyatirekeṇa nādhīyīta | śrāddhakṛc ca tadvad eva syāt || 3.188 ||
nimantritān hi pitara upatiṣthanti tān dvijān |
vāyuvac cānugacchanti tathāsīnān upāsate || 3.189 ||
nimantritān iti | yastān tān dvijān nimantritān pitara upatiṣṭhanti adṛśyarūpeṇa samīpam āyānti | prāṇavac ca gacchato ‘nugacchanti | upaviṣṭeṣu vipreṣu samīpe upaviśanti tasmāt niyatātmanā nimantritena bhāvyam iti pūrvavidhyarthavādaḥ || 3.189 ||
ketitas tu yathānyāyaṃ havye kavye dvijottamaḥ |
kathaṃcid apy atikrāman pāpaḥ sūkaratāṃ vrajet || 3.190 ||
ketita iti | havyakavyayoḥ śāstramaryādānatikrameṇa nimantrito brāhmaṇaḥ aṅgīkṛtya kenāpi prakāreṇābhuñjānaḥ tadatikramapāpavān janmāntare sūkaratvaṃ yāti | pāpaśūkara iti pāṭhe pāpasūkaro grāmasūkaraḥ || 3.190 ||
āmantritas tu yaḥ śrāddhe vṛśalyā saha modate |
dātur yad duṣkṛtaṃ kiṃcit tat sarvaṃ pratipadyate || 3.191 ||
āmantrita iti | “tad ahar brahmacārī syāt” iti gautamasmaraṇāt | vṛṣaly api kāmayamānā cālayati bhartāram iti vṛṣaly atra brāhmaṇy api | śrāddhe nimantritaḥ san tayā saha strīpuṃdharmeṇa yo ramate sa dātur yat pāpaṃ tat sarvaṃ prāpnoti iti pāpotpattipratipādanamātram etat | anyathā apāpe dātari duṣkṛtasaṃbandho na syāt | tatra (na cātra) dātur etat prāyaścittatayā coditaṃ yenāsau duṣkṛtān mucyate || 3.191 ||
akrodhanāḥ śaucaparāḥ satataṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ |
nyastaśastrā mahābhāgāḥ pitaraḥ pūrvadevatāḥ || 3.192 ||
akrodhanā iti | krodhaśūnyāḥ mṛdvāryādiśuddhiniratāḥ sarvadāvarjitastrīsaṃprayogāḥ tyaktāyudhāḥ mahātmānaḥ anādidevatārūpāḥ pitaraḥ ataḥ krodhādirahitaiḥ śrāddhabrāhmaṇair bhāvyam || 3.192 ||
yasmād utpattir eteṣāṃ sarveṣām apy aśeṣataḥ |
ye ca yair upacaryāḥ syur niyamais tān nibodhata || 3.193 ||
yasmād iti | eṣāṃ pitṝṇāṃ yasya sakāśād upattiḥ ye ca pitaro yaiḥ brāhmaṇādibhiḥ yāgādibhir niyamaiḥ śāstroktakarmabhiḥ yathā syuḥ tat śṛṇuta || 3.193 ||
manor hairaṇyagarbhasya ye marīcydayaḥ sutāḥ |
teṣām ṛṣīṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ putrāḥ pitṛgaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ || 3.194 ||
manor iti | hiraṇyagarbhāpatyasya manoḥ smṛtāḥ ye marīcyādayaḥ putrāḥ yathoktam “marīcim atry āṅgirasam” ityādi | teṣāṃ marīcyādīnāṃ sarveṣām ṛṣīṇām ete ye somapādayaḥ pitṛmukhyāḥ manvādibhiḥ smṛtāḥ || 3.194 ||
virāṭsutāḥ somasadaḥ sādhyānāṃ pitaraḥ smṛtāḥ |
agniṣvāttāś ca devānāṃ mārīcā lokaviśrutāḥ || 3.195 ||
virāṭsutā iti | virāṭsutāḥ somasado nāma sādhyānāṃ pitaraḥ devānāṃ ca marīciputrāḥ agniṣvāttākhyāḥ loke khyātāḥ || 3.195 ||
daityadānavayakṣāṇāṃ gandharvoragarakṣasām |
suparṇakinnarāṇāṃ ca smṛtā barhiṣado ‘trijāḥ || 3.196 ||
daityadānavayakṣāṇām iti | daityādīnāṃ prathamādhyāyoktabhedānāṃ atriputrāḥ barhiṣado nāma pitaraḥ smṛtāḥ || 3.196 ||
somapā nāma viprāṇāṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ havirbhujaḥ |
vaiśyānām ājyāpā nāma śūdrāṇāṃ tu sukālinaḥ || 3.197 ||
somapā nāmeti | brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrāṇāṃ yathākramaṃ somapahavirbhujājyapa-sukālināmānaḥ pitaraḥ || 3.197 ||
somapās tu kaveḥ putrā haviṣmanto ‘ṅgiraḥsutāḥ |
pulastyasyājyapāḥ putrā vasiṣṭhasya sukālinaḥ || 3.198 ||
somapā iti | brāhmaṇasya pitaraḥ somapāḥ kaveḥ bhṛgoḥ putrāḥ | kṣatriyapitaro havirbhujo haviṣmanto ‘ṅgiraḥsutāḥ | vaiśyapitaras tv ājyapāḥ pulastyasya putrāḥ | śūdrapitaraḥ sukālino vasiṣṭhasya putrāḥ || 3.198 ||
anagnidagdhāgnidagdhān kāvyān barhiṣadas tathā |
agniṣvāttāṃś ca saumyāṃś ca viprāṇām eva nirdiśet || 3.199 ||
anagnidagdhān iti | anagnidagdhāgnidagdhakāvyabarhiṣadaḥ agniṣvāttasaumākhyān aparān pitṝn viprāṇām eva jānīyat | vede purāṇeṣu ca evaṃnāma(kaṃ) pitṝṇāṃ prasiddham
|| 3.199 ||
ya ete tu gaṇā mukhyāḥ pitṝṇāṃ parikīrtitāḥ |
teṣām apīha vijñeyaṃ putrapautram anantakam || 3.200 ||
ya iti | ya ete somapādayaḥ pradhānabhūtāḥ pitṛgaṇā uktāḥ teṣām apīha saṃsāre pitara eva putrapautrā vijñeyāḥ || 3.200 ||
ṛṣibhyaḥ pitaro jātāḥ pitṛbhyo devamānavāḥ |
devebhyas tu jagat sarvaṃ caraṃ sthāṇv anupūrvaśaḥ || 3.201 ||
ṛṣibhyaḥ pitara iti | marīcyādibhya uktaṃ nāmabhyo ye somapādayo jātāḥ, tebhyaś ca devadānavaṃ jātam | devebhyaś ca sthāvarajaṅgamarūpaṃ sarvaṃ jagat krameṇa jātam | evaṃ coktapitṛbhavatvāt pitṛpitāmahaprapitāmahādīnāṃ tatpūjāyāṃ te pūjitā bhavantīty etatpratipādanārthaṃ somapādipitṛgaṇopanyāsaḥ prakṛtapitṛpūjāstutyarthaṃ vijñeyaḥ || 3.201 ||
rājatair bhājanair eṣām atho vā rajatānvitaiḥ |
vāry api śraddhayā dattam akṣayāyopakalpate || 3.202 ||
rājatair iti | teṣāṃ pitṝṇāṃ raupyapātraiḥ raupyayuktair vā pānīyam api bhaktyā dattam akṣayyasukhahetu saṃpadyate | kiṃ punaḥ praśastāmiṣādīni ghṛtādīni dānapātraviśeṣaphalam etat || 3.202 ||
daivakāryād dvijātīnāṃ pitṛkāryaṃ viśiṣyate |
daivaṃ hi pitṛkāryasya pūrvam āpyāyanaṃ smṛtam || 3.203 ||
devakāryād iti | daivasya devakarmaṇaḥ sakāśāt pitryaṃ karma dvijātīnāṃ viśeṣeṇa kartavyatayopadiśyate | pradhānaṃ hi tat yasmāt daivakarma pitṛkarmaṇaḥ pūrva sat paripūrakaṃ smṛtam | evaṃ ca tasya pitṛkāryaparipūrakavad aṅgatvam iti || 3.203 ||
teṣām ārakṣabhūtaṃ tu pūrvaṃ daivaṃ niyojayet |
rakṣāṃsi vipralumpanti śrāddham ārakṣavarjitam || 3.204 ||
teṣām iti | pitṝṇāṃ rakṣābhūtam, gṛhyadarśanāt viśvān devān uddiśya pūrvaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ nimantrayet | yasmād rakṣodarśitaṃ śrāddhaṃ rakṣāṃsy ācchindanti yasmād rakṣābhūtaṃ pitryasya daivaṃ tasmāt || 3.204 ||
daivādyantaṃ tad īheta pitrādyantaṃ na tad bhavet |
pitryādyantaṃ tv īhamānaḥ kṣipraṃ naśyati sānvayaḥ || 3.205 ||
daivādyantam iti | pitrādyantaṃ tv īhamānaḥ kṣipraṃ naśyati sānvaya iti | tataḥ śrāddhaṃ daivādi daivāntaṃ cānutiṣṭheta | na ca pitryopakramāpavargaṃ pitrādyantaṃ tad anutiṣṭhan sasantānam āśu vinaśyatīti || 3.205 ||
śuciṃ deśaṃ viviktaṃ ca gomayenopalepayet |
dakṣināpravaṇaṃ caiva prayatnenopapādayet || 3.206 ||
śucim iti | anupahataṃ deśaṃ janair anākīrṇaṃ gośakṛtā upalepayet | atha vā dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi avanato yadi svabhāvato na syāt tadā prayatnataḥ taṃ saṃpādayet || 3.206 ||
avakāśeṣu cokṣeṣu jalatīreṣu caiva hi |
vivikteṣu ca tuṣyanti dattena pitaraḥ sadā || 3.207 ||
avakāśeṣv iti | svabhāvaśuciṣu sthāneṣu nadyādipulineṣu ca nirjaneṣu pradeśeṣu dattena śrāddhādinā pitaraḥ sarvadā tuṣyanti tasmiṃś ca deśe || 3.207 ||
āsaneṣūpakḷpteṣu barhiṣmatsu pṛthak pṛthak |
upaspṛṣṭodakān samyag viprāṃs tān upaveśayet || 3.208 ||
āsaneṣv iti | āsaneṣu saṃsarbheṣu pṛthak pṛthak vinyasteṣu samyak kṛtasnānācamanān tān pūrvaṃ nimantritān upaveśayet || 3.208 ||
upaveśya tu tān viprān āsaneṣv ajugupsitān |
gandhamālyaiḥ surabhibhir arcayed daivapūrvakam || 3.209 ||
upaveśyeti | tān viprān aninditān āsaneṣūpaveśya anantaraṃ kuṅkumamālyadhūpādibhiḥ svāmodaiḥ devabrāhmaṇapūrvakam arcayet || 3.209 ||
teṣām udakam ānīya sapavitrāṃs tilān api |
agnau kuryād anujñāto brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇaiḥ saha || 3.210 ||
teṣām iti | teṣāṃ brāhmaṇānām udakaṃ darbhatilamiśraṃ datvā “agnau karavāṇi kariṣye cety anujñāto yadā kurvity evaṃ brūyuḥ” iti gṛhyoktrūpeṇa saha brāhmaṇair anujñātaḥ agnau vakṣyamāṇaṃ homaṃ kuryāt | brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ dvijapradarśanārtham, trayāṇāṃ prakṛtatvā
|| 3.210 ||
agneḥ somayamābhyāṃ ca kṛtvāpy āyanam āditaḥ |
havirdānena vidhivat paścāt saṃtarpayet pitṝn || 3.211 ||
agner iti | somayamayoḥ dvandvanirdeśe ‘pi agner nirdeśābhāvāt devatādvayaṃ tena agnaye svāhā, somāya svāhā, yamāya svāhety evaṃ agnyādīnāṃ paryukṣaṇādi vidhipūrvakaṃ havirdānena tarpaṇaṃ ādau kṛtvā paścād brāhmaṇabhojanādi || 3.211 ||
agnyabhāve tu viprasya pāṇāv evopapādayet |
yo hy agniḥ sa dvijo viprair mantradarśibhir ucyate || 3.212 ||
agnyabhāva iti | anagnikasya anupanītopanītāsamāvṛttākṛtadāramṛtabhāryāder mṛtāhādiśrāddhe vidhānād aupāsanāgnyabhāve brāhmaṇahasta etadāhutitrayaṃ dadyāt, yasmāt ya evāgniḥ sa eva brāhmaṇa iti vedavidbhiḥ brāhmaṇair ucyate || 3.212 ||
akrodhanān suprasādān vadanty etān purātanāḥ |
lokasyāpyāyane yuktān śrāddhe devān dvijottamān || 3.213 ||
akrodhanān  iti | akrodhanān krodhaśūnyān svārādhanādīn “agnau prāstāhutir” iti nyāyena lokavṛddhyupādānāt śrāddhasaṃpradānabhūtān etān brāhmaṇān manvādayo vadanti | tasmāt devatulyatvāt taddhaste pūrvavidhyarthavādaḥ || 3.213 ||
apasavyam agnau kṛtvā sarvam āvṛt parikramam |
apasavyena hastena nirvaped udakaṃ bhuvi || 3.214 ||
apasavyam iti | agnau yaḥ paryukṣaṇaparistaraṇāhutidānādīni kartavyatākramaḥ taṃ sarvam apasavyapitṛkarmavat dakṣiṇāsaṃsthaṃ kṛtvā tad etad udaksaṃsthaṃ apasavyena dakṣiṇena hastena pitṛtīrthena piṇḍādhārabhūtāyāṃ bhuvi udakaṃ prakṣipet || 3.214 ||
trīṃs tu tasmād dhaviḥśeṣāt piṇḍān kṛtvā samāhitaḥ |
audakenaiva vidhinā nirvaped dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ || 3.215 ||
trīn iti | tasmād agnyādihomārthāhutiṃ datvā hutaśiṣṭān trīn piṇḍān kṛtvā audakena vidhinā dakṣiṇahastena pitṛtīrthenānanyacitto dakṣiṇāmukhas teṣu darbheṣu “taṃ hastaṃ nimṛjyāt” iti darśanāt darbheṣu dadyāt | ata eva codakam api pūrvoktapiṇḍādhārabhuvi darbheṣv eva dadyāt
|| 3.125 ||
nyupya piṇḍāṃs tatas tāṃs tu prayato vidhipūrvakam |
teṣu darbheṣu taṃ hastaṃ nirmṛjyāl lepabhāginām || 3.216 ||
nypyeti | tān piṇḍān piṇḍeṣu añjanābhyañjanavāso dadyāt ity evamādisūtrakāroktavidhipūrvakaṃ datvā tataḥ teṣu piṇḍādhārabhūteṣu darbheṣu karāvagharṣaṇaṃ iti vaiṣṇavasmaraṇāt | darbhamūleṣu taṃ hastaṃ prapitāmahāt pūrveṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ lepabhāgināṃ hastasaṃśliṣṭānnāvayavabhāgitṛptaye samarpitalepaṃ kuryāt
|| 3.216 ||
ācamyodakparāvṛtya trir āyamya śanair asūn |
ṣaḍṛtūṃś ca namas kuryāt pitṝn eva ca mantravat || 3.217 ||
ācamyodakparāvṛtyeti | tad anūpaspṛśya ācamyodaṅmukhaḥ yathāśakti trīn prāṇāyāmān kṛtvā “vasantāya namaḥ” iti ṣaḍṛtūn namaskuryāt | pitṝn ca pitṝṃś ca “namo vaḥ pitaraḥ” ity evamādi mantrayuktaṃ kṛtvā “abhiparyāvṛttyā” iti (MDh 4.1) gṛhyadarśanāt dakṣiṇābhimukho bhūtvā namaskuryāt || 3.217 ||
udakaṃ ninayec cheṣaṃ śanaiḥ piṇḍāntike punaḥ |
avajighrec ca tān piṇḍān yathānyuptān samāhitaḥ || 3.218 ||
udakam iti | piṇḍādhāradarbhadattodakaśeṣam udakaṃ piṇḍasamīpe punaḥ uttarayotsṛjet | tāṃś ca piṇḍān yenaiva krameṇa dattān tenaiva krameṇa ananyamanā jighret || 3.218 ||
piṇḍebhyaḥ svalpikāṃ mātrāṃ samādāyānupūrvaśaḥ |
tān eva viprān āsīnān vidhivat pūrvam āśayet || 3.219 ||
piṇḍebhya iti | piṇḍebhyaḥ atyantālpabhāgaṃ pitṛpiṇḍakrameṇa gṛhītvā tān eva pitrādyarthopaveśitān viprān vidhivat nayec ca | ānupūrvyāṃ bhojanakāle anyabhājanāt pūrvaṃ bhojayet || 3.219 ||
dhriyamāṇe tu pitari pūrveṣām eva nirvapet |
vipravad vāpi taṃ śrāddhaṃ svakaṃ pitaram āśayet || 3.220 ||
dhriyamāṇa iti | jīvati pitari pitāmahādīnām eva trayāṇāṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryāt na pituḥ | atha vā pitṛsthāne tam eva svakaṃ pitaraṃ śrāddhe bhojayet pitāmahaprapitāmahayoś ca brāhmaṇau bhojayet piṇḍau ca dadyāt || 3.220 ||
pitā yasya tu vṛttaḥ syāj jīvec cāpi pitāmahaḥ |
pituḥ sa nāma saṃkīrtya kīrtayet prapitāmaham || 3.221 ||
pitā yasya tu vṛttaḥ iti | pitṛjīvanapakṣāpekṣo vāśabdaḥ | yasya  punaḥ pitā mṛtaḥ syāt pitāmahaś ca jīvac ca sa pitur brāḥmaṇabhojanapiṇḍadānādau nāmoccāryaṃ prapitāmahaṃ kīrtayet tābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryāt prapitāmahasya ca pituḥ | yathāha viṣṇuḥ: “yasya pitā pretaḥ syāt sa pitre piṇḍaṃ nidhāya pitāmahāt parābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃ dadyāt” iti || 3.221 ||
pitāmaho vā tacchrāddhaṃ bhuñjītety abravīn manuḥ |
kāmaṃ vā samanujñātaḥ svayam eva samācaret || 3.222 ||
pitāmaha iti | pitāmahaviprasthāne pitāmahaṃ vā bhojayet | pitṛprapitāmahayoḥ piṇḍadānaṃ brāhmaṇabhojanaṃ ca kuryāt | atha vā pitrā pitāmahena vā jīvatā ahaṃ na bhuñje yatheccham eva (kurviti) anujñātaḥ svayam eva yathāruci samācaret | uktapakṣebhyaḥ pakṣāntaraṃ yathā gṛhye uktam: “jīvatpitṛkasyānārambhaḥ karmāṇīti homāntaḥ” || 3.222 ||
teṣāṃ datvā tu hasteṣu sapavitraṃ tilodakam |
tat piṇḍāgraṃ prayacchet tu svadhaiṣām astv iti bruvan || 3.223 ||
teṣām iti | teṣāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ hasteṣu sadarbhaṃ tilodakaṃ datvā piṇḍebhyaḥ svalpikāṃ mātrām ity anena yan nirdiṣṭaṃ tat piṇḍāgraṃ pitre svadhāstu ity evamāi bruvan krameṇa dadyāt || 3.223 ||
pāṇibhyāṃ tūpasaṃgṛhya svayam annasya vardhitam |
viprāntike pitṝn dyāyan śanakair upanikṣipet || 3.224 ||
pāṇibhyām iti | annapūrṇapātraṃ karābhyāṃ gṛhītvā pitṛn smaran viprasamīpe pariveṣaṇārthaṃ śanaiḥ sthāpayet || 3.224 ||
ubhayor hastayor muktaṃ yad annam upanīyate |
tad vipralumpanty asurāḥ sahasā duṣṭacetasaḥ || 3.225 ||
ubhayor iti | ubhābhyāṃ hastābhyām agṛhītaṃ yad annaṃ brāhmaṇāntikam ānīyate dīyate vā tat asurāḥ devadviṣaḥ duṣṭabuddhayaḥ ācchindati | tasmān naikena (smaran) hastenānīya pariveṣṭavyam || 3.225 ||
guṇāṃś ca sūpaśākādyān payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ madhu |
vinyaset prayataḥ pūrvaṃ bhūmāv eva samāhitaḥ || 3.226 ||
guṇān iti | sūpādīni vyañjanāni kṣīrādīni copasecanāni prādhānyāt pṛthak pṛthak yathopadiṣṭāni prayataḥ śuciḥ samāhitaḥ ananyamanāḥ san yathā na viśīryante tathā bhūmāv eva nānyatra sthāpayet || 3.226 ||
bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ ca vividhaṃ mūlāni ca phalāni ca |
hṛdyāni caiva māṃsāni pānāni surabhīṇi ca || 3.227 ||
bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ ca vividhaṃ surasāni phalāni ceti | tathā bhakṣyaṃ kharaviśadaṃ modakādi bhojyaṃ pāyasādinā yathprakāraṃ mūlaṃ phalaṃ ca hṛdayasya priyāṇi māṃsāni pānakādīna ca surabhīṇi bhavanti bhūmāv eva vinyaset || 3.227 ||
upanīya tu tat sarvaṃ śanakaiḥ susamāhitaḥ |
pariveṣayeta prayato guṇān sarvān pracodayan || 3.228 ||
upanīya sarvam etad iti | sarvam etad annādi brāhmaṇasamīpe ānīya “idaṃ madhuraṃ” “idaṃ ślakṣṇam” ity evaṃ sarvān mādhuryādīn kathayan suciram ananyamanāḥ śanaiḥ yathopayogaṃ na tu yugapad eva sarvaṃ pariveṣayet || 3.228 ||
nāsram āpātayej jātu na kupyen nānṛtaṃ vadet |
na pādena spṛśed annaṃ na caitad avadhūnayet || 3.229 ||
nāsram iti | rodanakrodhāsatyabhāṣaṇāni na kuryāt pādena cānnaṃ na spṛśet | nāpy utkṣipyotkṣipya saṃtyajet | krodhād anṛtaṃ na vadet krodhānṛtayor apuruṣārthatvāt niṣiddhayoḥ śrāddhāṅgatvārthaṃ śrāddhāṅgatvārtham ihopadeśaḥ || 3.229 ||
asraṃ gamayati pretān kopo ‘rīn anṛtaṃ śunaḥ |
pādasparśas tu rakṣāṃsi duṣkṛtīn avadhūnanam || 3.230 ||
asraṃ gamayati pretān iti | aśru kriyamāṇaṃ pretākhān bhūtaviśeṣān śrāddhānnādi prāpayati krodhaḥ anṛtaṃ śunaḥ pādasparśo rakṣāṃsi avadhūnanaṃ pāpakāriṇaṃ tasmād etan na kuryāt || 3.230 ||
yad yad roceta viprebhyas tat tad dadyād amatsaraḥ |
brahmodyāś ca kathāḥ kuryāt pitṝṇām etad īpsitam || 3.231 ||
yad iti | brahmodyāś ceti | yad yad annaṃ vyañjanādi bhavati tat tad aduṣṭaḥ san dadyāt | tathā paramātmanirūpaṇaṃ puṇyakathāstotraṃ kuryāt yad etat pitṝṇām abhipretam || 3.231 ||
svādhyāyaṃ śrāvayet pitrye dharmaśāstrāṇi caiva hi |
ākhyānānītihāsāṃś ca purāṇāni khilāni ca || 3.232 ||
svādhyāyaṃ śrāvayet pitryam iti | vedamānavādiśāstrāṇi ākhyānāni sauparṇamaitrāvaruṇādīni pṛthak vedoditāni bhāratādīni vāyvādiproktāni śivasaṃkalpādīni śrāddhe brāhmaṇān śrāvayet || 3.232 ||
harṣayed brāhmaṇāṃs tuṣṭo bhojayec ca śanaiḥ śanaiḥ |
annādyenāsakṛc caitān guṇaiś ca paricodayet || 3.233 ||
harṣayed brāhmaṇān hṛṣṭo bhojayec cāśanaṃ śanaiḥ | anndyenāsakṛc cainān guṇaiś ca pariśodhayet || hṛṣṭo bhūtvā tais taiḥ priyavacanādibhiḥ brāhmaṇān toṣayet annaṃ cātvarayā bhojayet pāyasādinā ghṛtapūrādibhiḥ etān brāhmaṇān idaṃ gṛhyatām ity evaṃ punaḥ punaḥ prabodhayet || 3.233 ||
vratastham api dauhitraṃ śrāddhe yatnena bhojayet |
kutapaṃ cāsanaṃ dadyāt tilaiś ca vikiren mahīm || 3.234 ||
vratastham api dauhitram iti | brahmacāriṇam api sadā dauhitraṃ śrāddhe yatnena bhojayet | dauhitrasya cānukalpakamadhyād āmnāte sati punaḥ idaṃ yatnavacanam ānukalpikamadhye ‘tyājyatvaṃ (jāḍyatvaṃ) khyāpayati | satkṛtaṃ cāru āsanaṃ dadyāt | na dauhitra eva dadyāt api tu sarvabrāhmaṇeṣu, “trīṇi śrāddhe pavitrāṇi” (3.235) ityādivacanāt | tilāṃś ca bhūmau prakṣipet || 3.234 ||
trīṇi śrāddhe pavitrāṇi dauhitraḥ kutapas tilāḥ |
trīṇi cātra praśaṃsanti śaucam akrodham atvarām || 3.235 ||
yasmāt trīṇīti | dauhitrādīni trīṇi śrāddhe pavitrāṇi trīṇi caiva vastūni śaucākrodhaviśraṃbhākhyāni śrāddhe atiśayenecchanti || 3.235 ||
atyuṣṇaṃ sarvam annaṃ syād bhuñjīraṃs te ca vāgyatāḥ |
na ca dvijātayo brūyur dātrā pṛṣṭā havirguṇān || 3.236 ||
atyuṣṇam iti | uṣṇam annaṃ dvijāgryebhyaḥ śraddhayā vinivedayet |
anyatra phalamūlebhyaḥ pānakād dadhna eva ca |
iti śaṅkhasmaraṇāt phalādivyatirekeṇa sarvam uṣṇaṃ dadyāt | saṃyatavācaś ca brāhmaṇā aśnīyuḥ | dātrā ca svādvasvādu vety enam annaguṇān pṛṣṭāḥ na kathayeyuḥ abhinayenāpi | vāṅniyamasyoktatvāt (cāgnipraśasyoktatvāt) || 3.236 ||
yāvad ūṣmā bhavaty anne yāvad aśnanti vāgyatāḥ |
pitaras tāvad aśnanti yāvan noktā havirguṇāḥ || 3.237 ||
yāvad ūṣmā bhavaty anna iti | yāvad anne auṣṇyaṃ havati viprā maunenāśnanti havirguṇāṃś ca nābhidadhati tāvat pitaras tad annaṃ brāhmaṇamukhena bhuñjate || 3.237 ||
yad veṣṭitaśirā bhuṅkte yad bhuṅkte dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ |
sopanatkaś ca yad bhuṅkte tad vai rakṣāṃsi bhuñjate || 3.238 ||
yad iti | uṣṇīṣādinā veṣṭitaśirāḥ yad annaṃ brāhmaṇo buṅkte tathā dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ sopānatkapādaś ca tad annaṃ rakṣāṃsi aśnanti, na pitaraḥ | tasmād etat pariharaṇīyam
|| 3.238 ||
cāṇḍālaś ca varāhaś ca kukkuṭaḥ śvā tathaiva ca |
rajasvalā ca ṣaṇḍhaś ca nekṣerann aśnato dvijān || 3.239 ||
tathā ca kāryaṃ yathā caṇḍālaś ca varāhaś ca kukkuṭa iti | caṇḍālavarāhagrāmasūkarakukkuṭaś codakyānapuṃsakāni dvijabhojanakāle tato ‘pasārayet || 3.239 ||
home pradāne bhojye ca yad ebhir abhivīkṣyate |
daive haviṣi pitrye vā tad gacchaty ayathātatham || 3.240 ||
yasmād homa iti | agnihotrādihome dāne dharmārthaṃ brāhmaṇabhojane havyakavyayoś ca yat karma caṇḍālādibhir avekṣyate yat yad arthaṃ kriyate tat na sādhayati || 3.240 ||
ghrāṇena śūkaro hanti pakṣavātena kukkuṭaḥ |
śvā tu dṛṣṭinipātena sparśeṇāvaravarṇajaḥ || 3.241 ||
ghrāṇeneti | sūkaras tadannādi ghrātvā tatkarma niṣphalayati | kukkuṭaḥ pakṣavātena vāyunā, śvāpi akṣinirīkṣaṇena, avaravarṇena śūdreṇa brāhmaṇyāṃ jātaḥ avaravarṇajaḥ cāṇḍālaḥ | nadītīrādiśrāddhakṛt sparśanena tatra vāyujaśucitvāśaṅkāyām idam | na dātuḥ na cānnādisparśaviṣayaṃ yena caṇḍālasya sāmānyataḥ sparśaniṣedhenaiva siddham annavacanam anarthakaṃ syāt | ity evam ānarthakyāśaṅkāyāṃ yac chūdrasparśaniṣedhārthatvam asyāhuḥ – tad asat, uktanītyārthavattvāt | avarajaśabdena ca śūdrasyānabhidhānāt | sūkarādīnāṃ ca svabhāvānuvādo ‘yaṃ prakṛtaviśeṣatayocyate na tv etat kriyāvyatirekeṇa duṣṭam iti || 3.241 ||
khañjo vā yadi vā kāṇo dātuḥ preṣyo ‘pi vā bhavet |
hīnātiriktagātro vā tam apy apanayet punaḥ || 3.242 ||
khañja iti | khañjaḥ pādavikalaḥ, kāṇaḥ ekekṣaṇavikalaḥ, dātur vā sāsaḥ śūdrasyaiva preṣyatvāt | apiśabdād anyo ‘pi śūdro, yo vā kunakhiṣaṇḍakādiḥ tam api tataḥ pradeśād aposārayet || 3.242 ||
brāhmaṇaṃ bhikṣukaṃ vāpi bhojanārtham upasthitam |
brāhmaṇair abhyanujñātaḥ śaktitaḥ pratipūjayet || 3.243 ||
brāhmaṇaṃ bhikṣukaṃ vāpīti | brāhmaṇam atithirūpaṃ anyaṃ vā bhikṣukaṃ bhojanārthaṃ tatkālam āgataṃ śrāddhabrāhmaṇair anujñāto dātā yathāśaktyā annadānena pūjayet || 3.243 ||
sārvavarṇikam annādyaṃ saṃnīyāplāvya vāriṇā |
samutsṛjed bhuktavatām agrato vikiran bhuvi || 3.244 ||
sārvavarṇikam iti | sarvaprakāram annādyam ekīkṛtya udakena plāvayitvā bhuktavatāṃ brāhmaṇānām agre vikṣipan bhūmau “darbheṣu vikiraś ca yaḥ” iti vakṣyamāṇaliṅgāt darbheṣu samutsṛjet || 3.244 ||
asaṃskṛtapramītānāṃ tyāgināṃ kulayoṣitām |
ucchiṣṭaṃ bhāgadheyaṃ syād dharbheṣu vikiraś ca yaḥ || 3.245 ||
asaṃskṛtapramītānām iti | “nāsya kāryo ‘gnisaṃskāraḥ” [5.69] ityādinā niṣiddhāgnikāryāṇāṃ mṛtānāṃ kulastrīṇāṃ ca kulaṃ tyaktvā gatānāṃ pātrastham ucchiṣṭaṃ dharbheṣu yo vikiraḥ sa bhāgaḥ syāt | kulayoṣitām iti viśeṣābhidhānaṃ saṃskṛtaṃ bhaikṣam ityvat || 3.245 ||
uccheṣaṇaṃ bhūmigatam ajihmasyāśaṭhasya ca |
dāsavargasya tat pitrye bhāgadheyaṃ pracakṣate || 3.246 ||
uccheṣaṇam iti | bhūmipatitaṃ yad ucchiṣṭaṃ tat akuṭilasya analasasya dāsagaṇasya bhāgadheyaṃ bhāgaṃ manvādayaḥ prāhuḥ || 3.246 ||
ā sapiṇḍakriyākarma dvijāteḥ saṃsthitasya tu |
adaivaṃ bhojayec chrāddhaṃ piṇḍam ekaṃ ca nirvapet || 3.247 ||
āsapiṇḍakriyākarmeti | aciramṛtasya dvijter ekādaśāhādāv āsapiṇḍakriyādhiṣṭhānaṃ kartavyam | nāmāvāsyāvat pūrvābhyāṃ sahapiṇḍadānaṃ kāryam | kiṃ tarhi | vaiśvadevabrāhmaṇabhojanarahitaṃ śrāddhārtham annaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ ca bhojayet | ekaṃ piṇḍaṃ tasmā eva dadyāt | tasmāt yāvad brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayet | sādhitaṃ caitat savistaraṃ smṛtimañjaryāṃ (?) ṛjupañjikāyāṃ ca | iha tu granthabhārabhayān na pratāryate || 3.247 ||
sahapiṇḍakriyāyāṃ tu kṛtāyām asya dharmataḥ |
anayaivāvṛtā kāryaṃ piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ sutaiḥ || 3.248 ||
sahapiṇḍakriyāyām iti | asyetyupādānāt anantaraprakṛtasya ekoddiṣṭakriyāsampradānabhūtasyāciramṛtasya sapiṇḍīkaraṇe śāstravyavasthayā kṛte sati yathā yājñavalkye (1.253) uktam |
gandhodakatilair yuktaṃ kuryāt pātracatuṣṭayam |
arghārthaṃ pitṛpātreṣu pretapātraṃ prasiñcayet ||
ye samānā iti dvābhyāṃ śeṣaṃ pūrvavad ācaret |
yathā ca uśanasā uktam |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryāt pārvaṇavat sadā |
pṛthak ca kalpayed anyat ekoddiṣṭavidhānataḥ || iti |
tathā ca śātātapenoktam |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ śrāddhaṃ daivapūrvaṃ niyojayet |
pitṝn āvāhayet tatra punaḥ pretaṃ ca nirdiśet || iti |
tathā ca gṛhyapariśiṣṭe uktam:
datvā piṇḍān pitṛbhyas tu paścāt pretāya pārśvataḥ |
taṃ tu piṇḍaṃ tridhā kṛtvā ānupūrvyarthasantatam |
nidadhyāt triṣu piṇḍeṣu eṣa saṃmārjano vidhiḥ || iti |
pretapātraṃ ca (na) sarvaṃ pitṛpātreṣu prasektavyam | api tu pṛthak pretabrāhmaṇopadeśasāmarthāt tadarghārthaṃ tatra kiṃcida(nu)vaśeṣaṇīyam | sādhāranatvāt avibhaktabhrātṛdhanopayogavat tasmād argho deyaḥ | evaṃ ca yat kaiścit sapiṇḍīkaraṇe pañcabrāhmaṇabhojanaṃ pratitāmahapitṛsaṃbandhyarghyapātraṃ piṇḍatridhādhāraṇaṃ coktam, tad asat, uktavākyavirodhād eva śāstrataḥ kṛte saty anayaivāvṛtā abhāvāsya prakriyayā putraiḥ pratisaṃvatsaraṃ mṛtāhe kartavyam |
nanu saṃnihitaparāmarśakatvāt sarvanāmnaḥ anayaivety ekoddiṣṭātideśo nyāyyaḥ | naivam, evaṃ bhedanirdeśena śāstranirdeśaṃ na kuryāt | asapiṇḍakriyāyām eṣa vidhiḥ | sahapiṇḍakriyāyāṃ punaḥ kṛtāyām ayam iti | tasmād amāvāsyetikartavyatānirdeśa evaṃ nyāyyaḥ | iti sapiṇḍakaraṇasāṃvatsarike ‘pi smṛtimañjaryām | ṛjupañjikāyāṃ vistarato nirūpyate || 3.248 ||
śrāddhaṃ bhuktvā ya ucchiṣṭaṃ vṛṣalāya prayacchati |
sa mūḍho narakaṃ yāti kālasūtram avākśirāḥ || 3.249 ||
śrāddham iti | śrāddhabhojanocchiṣṭaṃ yaḥ śūdrāya dadāti sa mūrkhaḥ kālasūtrākhyanarakamadhye adhomukho vrajati | āśritasacchūdratvāt ucchiṣṭam annaṃ dātavyam iti prāpte ‘yaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ || 3.249 ||
śrāddhabhug vṛṣalītalpaṃ tadahar yo ‘dhigacchati |
tasyāḥ purīṣe taṃ māsaṃ pitaras tasya śerate || 3.250 ||
śrāddhabhug iti | āmantritas tu yaḥ śrāddhe (iti) bhojanāt prāk brahmacaryam uktam | anena tu taduttarakālaṃ tad ahorātram ucyate | vṛṣalī ca vyākhyātā | śrāddhaṃ bhuktvā yaḥ tad ahorātraṃ strīsaṃyogaṃ bhajate tasya tadviṣṭhāyāṃ māsaṃ pitaraḥ avatiṣṭhante ity ataś ca iyān asyā dharmayogo bhavati || 3.250 ||
pṛṣṭvā svaditam ity evaṃ tṛptān ācāmayet tataḥ |
ācāntāṃś cānujānīyād abhito ramyatām iti || 3.251 ||
pṛṣṭvā svaditam ity evam iti | tān brāhmaṇān tṛptān vṛddhyā svaditam ity evaṃ pṛṣṭvā tata upasparśayet | kṛtācamanāṃś ca iha vā svagṛhe vāsyatām ity evam anujñātavyḥ || 3.251 ||
svadhāstv ity evaṃ taṃ brūyur brāhmaṇās tadanantaram |
svadhākāraḥ parā hy āśīḥ sarveṣu pitṛkarmasu || 3.252 ||
svadhety evaṃ vacanaṃ brūyur iti | anujñānantaraṃ dātāraṃ svadhey evaṃ brāhmaṇā brūyuḥ yasmāt sarveṣu kṛtānnākṛtānnaśrāddhodakatarpaṇādiṣu pitṛkarmasu svadhāśabdoccāraṇaṃ prakṛṣṭā āśīḥ || 3.252 ||
tato bhuktavatāṃ teṣām annaśeṣaṃ nivedayet |
yathā brūyus tathā kuryād anujñātas tato dvijaiḥ || 3.253 ||
tata iti | sahakṛtabhojanānāṃ viprāṇāṃ svadhākārād anantaraṃ śeṣam annam ity evaṃ gṛhyoktavidhinā annaśeṣaṃ nivedayet | taiś ca dvijaiḥ idam anenānnena kriyatām iti dattānujñaḥ yathā tair uktaṃ tathā viniyuñjīta || 3.253 ||
pitrye svaditam ity eva vācyaṃ goṣṭhe tu suśṛtam |
saṃpannam ity abhyudaye daive rucitam ity api || 3.254 ||
pitrye svaditam ity evaṃ goṣṭhe vācyam iti śṛtam iti | anyenāpi śrāddhakālopasthitena svaditam ity evaṃ gogaṇamadhye ca śṛtam iti, saṃpannam iti, vivāhādau devatārthaṃ rocitam iti | 3.254 ||
aparāhṇas tathā darbhā vāstusaṃpādanaṃ tilāḥ |
sṛṣṭir mṛṣṭir dvijāś cāgryāḥ śrāddhakarmasu saṃpadaḥ || 3.255 ||
aparāhṇas tathā darbhā iti | aparāhṇaḥ viṣṭarārthaṃ darbhāḥ vāstusaṃpādanaṃ sudhāgomayādinā gṛhasaṃśodhanam, tilāś cāntarbhūvikaraṇādyarthāḥ, sṛṣṭiḥ akārpaṇyenānnavisargaḥ | mṛṣṭiḥ annasamskāraḥ, agryā brāhmaṇāḥ ity etāḥ śrāddhe sampadaḥ ity aṅgāntarebhyaḥ eṣāṃ praśasyatvajñāpanārtham etat || 3.255 ||
darbhāḥ pavitraṃ pūrvāhṇo haviṣyāṇi ca sarvaśaḥ |
pavitraṃ yac ca pūrvoktaṃ vijñeyā havyasampadaḥ || 3.256 ||
darbhā iti | viṣṭarādyarthaṃ dharbhāḥ pavitraṃ darbhaṃ prādhānyāt pṛthag upadiṣṭam | pūrvāhṇakālaḥ haviṣyāṇi munyannādīni sarvāṇi | yac ca vāstusaṃpādanādi pāvanaṃ pūrvam uktam etāvad evārthakarmaṇaḥ siddhayaḥ || 3.256 ||
munyannāni payaḥ somo māṃsaṃ yac cānupaskṛtam |
akṣāralavaṇaṃ caiva prakṛtyā havir ucyate || 3.257 ||
munyannānīti | āraṇyānnaṃ kṣīraṃ somo māṃsaṃ cānatitīkṣṇaṃ biḍalavaṇādivarjitaṃ lavaṇaṃ saindhavaṃ svabhāvata evaitad dhavir iti manvādibhir ucyate || 3.257 ||
visṛjya brāhmaṇāṃs tāṃs tu prayato vidhipūrvakam |
dakṣiṇāṃ diśam ākāṃkṣan yācetemān varān pitṝn || 3.258 ||
visṛjyeti | tān brāhmaṇān visṛjya ananyamanāḥ śuciḥ san dakṣiṇāṃ diśam īkṣamāṇaḥ imān vakṣyamāṇān abhilaṣitārthān pitṝn prārthayet || 3.258 ||
dātāro no ‘bhivardhantāṃ vedāḥ santatir eva ca |
śraddhā ca no mā vyagamad bahu deyaṃ ca no ‘stv iti || 3.259 ||
dātāra iti | śraddhā ca no mā vyagamat bahu deyaṃ ca no ‘stv iti | asmatkule bahavo dānaśīlā bhavantu | vedāś ca adhyayanādhyāpanāvabhodhejyārūpeṇa vardhantām | santatiḥ putrādiḥ vardhatām | havyakavyaviṣayā śraddhāsmākaṃ mā vyagamat | bahu deyaṃ ca no ‘stv iti | dānārthaṃ ca dhanaṃ bahvasmākaṃ bhavatāt || 3.259 ||
evaṃ nirvapaṇaṃ kṛtvā piṇḍāṃs tāṃs tadanantaram |
gāṃ vipram ajam agniṃ vā prāśayed apsu vā kṣipet || 3.260 ||
evam iti | evaṃ “trīṃs tu tasmād dhaviḥśeṣāt” (3.215) ityādiprakriyayā piṇḍānirvapaṇaṃ kṛtvā tān piṇḍān anantaraṃ prakrāntavarayācanānantaraṃ goviprājānām anyatamam āśayet | agnāv udake vā kṣipet  || 3.260 ||
piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ kecit parastād eva kurvate |
voyobhiḥ khādayanty anye prakṣipanty anale ‘su vā || 3.261 ||
piṇḍanirvapaṇam iti | piṇḍadānaṃ kecid ācāryāḥ brāhmaṇabhojanād arvāk kurvate | anye pakṣibhiḥ khādayanti | idaṃ pūrvapratipattivaikalpikam | apy anye agnyudakaprakṣepaṃ pakṣibhakṣaṇaṃ vaikalpikam icchanti || 3.261 ||
pativratā dharmapatnī pitṛpūjanatatparā |
madhyamaṃ tu tataḥ piṇḍam adyāt samyak sutārthinī || 3.262 ||
pativratā dharmapatīti | manovākkāyakarmabhiḥ dharmārthakāmeṣu patir eva mayā (pari)caraṇīya iti vrataṃ yasyāḥ sā pativratā dharmapatnī prathamoḍhā savarṇā śraddhāvatī putrārthinī teṣāṃ piṇḍānāṃ madhyamapiṇḍaṃ samyak “ādhatta pitaro garbhaṃ” ityādisūtrakāroktamantreṇādyāt || 3.262 ||
āyuṣmantaṃ sutaṃ sūte yaśomedhāsamanvitam |
dhanavantaṃ prajāvantaṃ sāttvikaṃ dhārmikaṃ tathā || 3.263 ||
