āyuṣmantam iti | dharmānuṣṭhānarataṃ dvādaśādhyāyavakṣyamāṇasattvākhyaguṇayuktaṃ cirāyuṣaṃ khyātimantam atītārthasmaraṇayuktaṃ hiraṇyādidhanayuktaṃ santatimantaṃ evaṃvidhaguṇayuktaṃ putraṃ janayati || 3.263 ||
prakṣālya hastāv ācamya jñātiprāyaṃ prakalpayet |
jñātibhyaḥ satkṛtaṃ datvā brāhmaṇān api pūjayet || 3.264 ||
prakṣālya hastāv ācamyeti | jñātibhyaḥ satkṛtaṃ datvā brāhmaṇān api pūjayet | tadanu hastau prakṣālyācamya jñātīn pitṛpakṣāt pratiyacchatīti jñātiprāyam annaṃ kuryāt | tebhyaḥ pūjāpūrvakaṃ datvā mātṛpakṣādibhyo ‘pi saṃpūjāṃ dadyāt || 3.264 ||
uccheṣaṇaṃ tu tat tiṣṭhed yāvad viprā visarjitāḥ |
tato gṛhabaliṃ kuryād iti dharmo vyavasthitaḥ || 3.265 ||
uccheṣaṇam iti | tat brāhmaṇocchiṣṭaṃ tāvan na mārṣṭavyaṃ yāvad brāhmaṇā na nirgatāḥ | sampanne ca śrāddhe vaiśvadevādyanvāhikaṃ samagraṃ kuryāt | yathā vyāsenoktam |
yadā śrāddhaṃ pitṛbhyas tu dātum icchati mānavaḥ |
vaiśvadevaṃ tadā kuryāt nirvṛtte śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
iti dharmo vyavasthitaḥ || 3.265 ||
havir yac cirarātrāya yac cānantyāya kalpate |
pitṛbhyo vidhivad dattaṃ tat pravakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ || 3.266 ||
havir yac cirarātrāyeti | yat pitṛbhyo yathā ca dattaṃ havyakavyam annaṃ dīrghakālatṛptaye samyak pratipadyate | yac cānnaṃ tat tṛptaye tanniḥśeṣeṇa vakṣyāmi || 3.266 ||
tilair vrīhiyavair māṣair adbhir mūlaphalena vā |
dattena māsaṃ tṛpyanti vidhivat pitaro tṛṇām || 3.267 ||
dvau māsau matsyamāṃsena trīn māsān hāriṇena tu |
aurabhreṇātha caturaḥ śākulenātha pañca vai || 3.268 ||
ṣaṇmāsāṃś chāgamāṃsena pārṣatena ca sapta vai |
aṣṭāv aiṇeyamāṃsena rauraveṇa navaiva tu || 3.269 ||
tilar iti | dvai māsau matsyamāṃseneti | ṣaṇmāsāṃś chāgamāṃseneti | tiladhānyayavamāṣodakamūlaphalānāṃ śrāddhena dattena manuṣyāṇāṃ māsaṃ pitaras tṛpyanti dvau pāṭhīnādimatyamāṃsena trīn mārgeṇa māṃsena caturo meṣasaṃbandhinā, pañca bhakṣyapakṣyudbhavena ṣaḍ ājena sapta citramṛgasaṃbandhinā aṣṭau sāraṅgamāṃsena, nava śambarākhyamṛgajena || 3.267–269 ||
daśamāsāṃs tu tṛpyanti varāhamahiṣāmiṣaiḥ |
śaśakūrmayos tu māṃsena māsān ekādaśaiva tu || 3.270 ||
daśamāsāṃs tu tṛpyantīti | daśamāsān āraṇyasūkaramahiṣamāṃsais tṛpyanti | ekādaśa śaśakacchapamāṃsena || 3.270 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ tu gavyena payasā pāyasena ca |
vārdhrīṇasasya māṃsena tṛptir dvādaśavārṣiṅī || 3.271 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ tu gavyeneti | saṃvatsaraṃ tu punaḥ gavyena kṣīreṇa vārdhrīṇasasya ca |
triḥpibaṃ tv indriyakṣīṇaṃ śvetaṃ vṛddham ajāpitam |
vārdhrīṇasaṃ tu taṃ prāhuḥ yājñikāḥ pitṛkarmaṇi ||
ity evaṃ nigame paṭhitasy māṃsena dvādaśavārṣikī tṛptir bhavati  | pibataḥ karṇajihvaṃ yasya jalaṃ spṛśati saḥ tripiba iva tripibaḥ || 3.271 ||
kālaśākaṃ mahāśalkāḥ khaḍgalohāmiṣaṃ madhu |
ānantyāyaiva kalpyante munyannāni ca sarvaśaḥ || 3.272 ||
kālaśākaṃ mahāśalkam iti | kālaśākākhyaṃ śākaṃ mahāśalkākhyo matsyabhedaḥ śakalīcitacima ityādyāyurvedaprasiddhaḥ | khaḍgo gaṇḍakaḥ loho raktachāgaḥ | tayor āmiṣaṃ māṃsaṃ madhumākṣikaṃ munyannāni āraṇyāni sarvāṇy etāni anantatṛptyutpādanāya saṃpadyante || 3.272 ||
yat kiṃcin madhusaṃyuktam pradadyāt tu trayodaśīm |
tad apy akṣayam eva syād varṣāsu ca maghāsu ca || 3.273 ||
yat kiṃcin madhusaṃyuktam iti | yat kiṃcid apratiṣiddhaṃ madhusaṃyuktaṃ varṣākāle maghātrayodaśyāṃ hastisaṃbandhinī chāyā yasmin pradeśe prācīṃ diśaṃ gatā tatra tasyāṃ chāyāyāṃ madhusaṃyuktaṃ pāyasaṃ dadyāt || 3.273 ||
api naḥ sa kule bhūyād yo no dadyāt trayodaśīm |
pāyasaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ prākchāye kuñjarasya ca || 3.274 ||
apīti |
yad yad dadāti vidhivat samyak śraddhāsamanvitaḥ |
tat tat pitṝṇāṃ bhavati paratrānantam akṣayam || 3.275 ||
yad yad dadāti vidhivat samyak śraddhāsamanvitam | tat tat pitṝṇāṃ bhavati paratrānantyam avyayam | yad yad apratiṣiddhaṃ samyak rūpaśraddhāyukto yathāśāstraṃ dadyāt iti tatra sarvakālam anapacayaṃ paraloke pitṝṇāṃ bhavati annaṃ ca saty api dvimāsādyanantāvadhi tṛptivad evaṃ kurvan māsānumāsikam iti nodanāt śāstraikagocaratvena cāmuṣyārthasya tṛptānām āpyāyanakaputro ‘pahṛtakavyatṛptisāmarthyāt pratimāsam etad avaśyaṃ kartavyam || 3.275 ||
kṛṣṇapakṣe daśamyādau varjayitvā caturdaśīm |
śrāddhe praśastās tithayo yathaitā na tathetarāḥ || 3.276 ||
kṣṇapakṣa iti | kṛṣṇapakṣe daśamīprapbhṛti caturdaśīṃ varjayitvā tathayaḥ śrāddhe praśastāḥ yuktāḥ mahāphalāḥ pavitrāḥ praśastā na tathetarā iti || 3.276 ||
yukṣu kurvan dinarkṣeṣu sarvān kāmān samaśnute |
ayukṣu tu pitṝn sarvān prajāṃ prāpnoti puṣkalām || 3.277 ||
yukṣv iti | yugmāsu tithiṣu dvitīyācaturthyādiṣu yugmeṣu nakṣatreṣu aśvinīkṛttikādiṣu śrāddheṣu pitṝn pūjayan śrutadhanādiparipuṣṭāṃ santatiṃ prāpnoti || 3.277 ||
yathā caivāparaḥ pakṣaḥ pūrvapakṣād viśiṣyate |
tathā śrāddhasya pūrvāhṇād aparāhṇo viśiṣyate || 3.278 ||
yatheti | “caitrasitādyā māsā” iti jyotiḥśāstradarśanena aparapakṣaḥ śuklapakṣāt śrāddhe viśiṣītaphalo bhavati | evaṃ śrāddhasaṃbandhipūrvāhṇabhāgād aparāhṇabhāgo viśiṣṭaphalo bhavati | viśiṣyata iti vacanāt kadācit pūrvāhṇe ‘pi śrāddhaṃ kartavyam iti pratīyat | dṛṣṭāntasya ca dṛṣṭāntadānāny athānupapattyaiva siddhir boddhavyā | na hy asiddhasya dṛṣṭāntatopapadyate || 3.278 ||
prācīnāvītinā samyag apasavyam atandriṇā |
pitryam ā nidhanāt kāryaṃ vidhivad darbhapāṇinā || 3.279 ||
prācīnāvītineti | sarveṇa prācīnāvītītyuktaprācīnāvītayuktenānalasena darbhapavitrahastena apasavyapitṛtīrthena yathāśāstraṃ samyak pitryaṃ karma yāvajjīvaṃ kāryam || 3.279 ||
rātrau śrāddhaṃ na kurvīta rākṣasī kīrtitā hi sā |
saṃdhyayor ubhayoś caiva sūrye caivācirodite || 3.280 ||
rātrāv iti | rātrau śrāddhaṃ na kurvīta śrāddhasya dhvaṃsanasāmānyāt rākṣasī cāsau manvādibhiḥ kathitā | tathā saṃdhyahoḥ “āditye ‘cirodite” iti rātryādau niṣedhaṃ kurvan aparāhṇād anyatrāpi tithicchedagrahaṇādyapekṣayā śrāddham astīti jñāpayati māsānumāsikaśrāddhādyasaṃbhavena || 3.280 ||
anena vidhinā śrāddhaṃ trir abdasyeha nirvapet |
hemantagrīṣmavarṣāsu pāñcayajñikam anvaham || 3.281 ||
aneneti | anena uktavidhānena saṃvatsarasya madhye trīn vārān hemantagrīṣmavarṣāsu śrāddhaṃ kurvīta | pañcayajñamahāyajñamadhyabhavaṃ punaḥ pratyahaṃ kurvīta || 3.281 ||
na paitṛyajñiyo homo laukike ‘gnau vidhīyate |
na darśena vinā śrāddham āhitāgner dvijanmanaḥ || 3.282 ||
na paitryayajñiko homa iti | agneḥ somayamābhyām ity evaṃ pitṛyajñāṅgabhūto homaḥ laukike śrautasmārtavyatirikte ‘gnau śāstreṇa na codyate | āhitāgneś ca dvijāgneḥ amāvāsyakarmaṇā vinā kṛṣṇapakṣadaśamyādau śrāddhaṃ na vidhīyate | mṛtāhaśrāddhādes tu aniṣedhaḥ, tasya naiyamikatvena coditatvāt || 3.282 ||
yad eva tarpayaty adbhiḥ pitṝn snātvā dvijottmaḥ |
tenaiva kṛtsnam āpnoti pitṛyajñakriyāphalam || 3.283 ||
pūrvoktapāñcayajñikaśrāddhāsaṃbhave yad iti | snātvā udakatarpaṇaṃ yat pitṝṇāṃ brāhmaṇaḥ karoti tenaiva sarvaṃ pitṛyajñānuṣṭhānaphalaṃ prāpnoti | dvijottamagrahaṇaṃ pradarśanārtham, trayāṇāṃ prakṛtatvāt || 3.283 ||
vasūn vadanti tu pitṝn rudrāṃś caiva pitāmahān |
prapitāmahāṃs tathādityān śrutir eṣā sanātanī || 3.284 ||
vasūn iti | ya ete pitṛpitāmahaprapitāmahāḥ tān vasurudrādityākhyān devān iti manvādayo vadanti | yasmāt “pitrādayo vasvādaya” ity eṣā anādibhūtā śrutiḥ | pitṛdveṣān nāstikyādyaḥ pitṛkarmaṇi na pravarteta taṃ prati pravartanārthaṃ devatātvādhyāropeṇa pitṝṇāṃ stutivacanam || 3.284 ||
vighasāśī bhaven nityaṃ nityaṃ vāmṛtabhojanaḥ |
vighaso bhuktaśeṣam tu yajñaśeṣaṃ tathāmṛtam || 3.285 ||
vighasāśīti | sarvadā vidhasabhojanaḥ syāt ity etat yajñaśeṣabhuk tulyatvāpādanena stutyartham asya punarvacanam | sarvadā ca amṛtabhojanaḥ syāt | aprasiddhatvāt vighasāmṛte nirūpayati atithyādibhuktaśeṣaṃ vighasam ucyate | yajñāvaśiṣṭaṃ puroḍāśādi amṛtam iti || 3.285 ||
etad vo ‘bhihitaṃ sarvaṃ vidhānaṃ pāñcayajñikam |
dvijātimukhyavṛttīnāṃ vidhānaṃ śrūyatām iti || 3.286 ||
etad iti | etat pañcayajñabhavam anuṣṭhānaṃ sarvaṃ yuṣmākam uktam iti | pitṛkarmaṇo ‘nantaraṃ vyavahitakaraṇopasaṃhāro maṅgalārthatvāt | idānīṃ brāḥmaṇasaṃbhandhiṇināṃ amṛtādīnāṃ jīvikānām anuṣṭhānaṃ śrūyatām iti || 3.287 ||
iti śrībhaṭṭamādhavātmajagovindarājaviracitāyāṃ manuṭīkāyāṃ
manvanusāriṇyāṃ śrāddhavidhir nāma tṛtīyo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha caturho ‘dhyāyaḥ
caturyam āyuṣo bhāgam uṣitvādyaṃ gurau dvijaḥ |
dvitīyam āyuṣo bhāgaṃ kṛtadāro gṛhe vaset || 4.1 ||
brahmacaryapūrvakaṃ gṛhāśramam anutiṣṭhataḥ vakṣyamāṇā vṛttayo bhavantīty etat pratipādayitum uktaṃ brahmacaryakālaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ ca gārhasthyakālam anuvaditum āha caturtham iti | vakṣyamāṇam āśramasamuccayapakṣam āśrito brāhmaṇaḥ ādyam āyuścaturbhāge prāpte “ṣaḍviṃśadābdikam” ityādiviśeṣanodanāt ūnātiriktam api gurukula āsitvā tato dvitīyam āyuścaturbhāgaṃ “gṛhasthas tu yadā paśyed valīpatam” (6.2) ityādivakṣyamāṇaviśeṣāt ūnādhyadhikam api kṛtabhāryo gārhasthyam anutiṣṭhet | na cāsya kālasamarpaṇārthatvaṃ yuktam, aniyatatvenāyuṣo ‘sya sākāṅkṣatvāt || 4.1 ||
adroheṇaiva bhūtānām alpadroheṇa vā punaḥ |
yā vṛttis tāṃ samāsthāya vipro jīved anāpadi || 4.2 ||
adroheṇeti | vakṣyamāṇavṛttīnāṃ madhyāt bhūtānpapīḍanena yā jīvikā śiloñchādinā tadalābhe ‘lpopapīḍanena yācanādinā yā tām āśritya bhāryādibharaṇanityānuṣṭhānasaṃpattau satyāṃ vipro jīvet | na cātrālpajighāṃsābhyanujñānaṃ, hiṃsāyā niṣiddhatvāt | vipragrahaṇaṃ kṣatriyādivyudāsārtham | tathāpi jīvet || 4.2 ||
yātrāmātraprasiddhyarthaṃ svaiḥ karmabhir agarhitaiḥ |
akleśena śarīrasya kurvīta dhanasaṃcayam || 4.3 ||
yātrāmātraprasiddhyartham iti | śāstroktaiḥ karmabhiḥ vakṣyamāṇaiḥ ṛtādibhir aninditaiḥ śarīrakleśaparihāreṇa bhāryābharaṇanityānuṣṭhānapūrvakaṃ prāṇavṛttisamāptiparimāṇam arthasaṃgrahaṃ kurvīta, nādhikam iti niyamaḥ || 4.3 ||
ṛtāmṛtābhyāṃ jīvet tu mṛtena pramṛtena vā |
satyānṛtābhyām api vā na śvavṛttyā kadācana || 4.4 ||
kais taiḥ karmabhir ity ata āha ṛtāmṛtābhyām iti | ṛtādibhir anāpadi jīvet, na tv anāpadi kadācid api sevayet || 4.4 ||
ṛtam uñchaśilaṃ jñeyam amṛtaṃ syād ayācitam |
mṛtaṃ tu yācitaṃ bhaikṣaṃ pramṛtaṃ karṣaṇaṃ smṛtam || 4.5 ||
satyānṛtaṃ tu vāṇijyaṃ tena caivāp jīvyate |
sevā śvavṛttir ākhyātā tasmāt tāṃ parivarjayet || 4.6 ||
ṛtam iti | satyānṛtam iti | dhānyādikaṇoccayaṃ śiloccayanaṃ ca satyatulyaphalatvāt ṛtaṃ vaktavyam | aprārthitopanataṃ amṛtavat sukhahetutvād amṛtam | prārthitaṃ punaḥ bhaikṣaṃ maraṇakalpapīḍānubhavāt mṛtam | karṣaṇaṃ ca “bhūmiṃ bhūmiśayāṃś caiva hanti kāṣṭham ayomukham” iti bahūnāṃ maraṇanimittatvāt parkarṣeṇa mṛtam | vāṇijyaṃ ca satyānṛtavyavaharaṇasādhyatvāt satyānṛtaṃ tenāpi “kṛṣivāṇijye vāsvayaṃkṛte kusīdaṃ ca” iti gautamasmaraṇāt asvayaṃkṛtena jīvyante | sevā ca dīnadṛṣṭisaṃdarśanādirūpatayā śvavṛttir ity uktā, tasmāt tāṃ prakṛto brāhmaṇaḥ parityajet || 4.5–6 ||
kusūladhānyako vā syāt kumbhīdhānyaka eva vā |
tryahaihiko vāpi bhaved aśvastanika eva vā || 4.7 ||
sa ca brāhmaṇaḥ śaucasaṃkocatāratamyāpekṣayā kusūladhānyaka iti | koṣṭhakapramāṇadhānyādisaṃcayo vā syāt dvādaśāhamātraparyāptadhanaḥ | uṣṭrikāpramāṇadhānyādisaṃgraho vā ṣaḍahamātraparyāptadhanaḥ | tryahamātraparyāptaṃ īhāyāṃ ceṣṭāyāṃ bhavaṃ dhānyādi yasya tathā vā syāt | śvo bhavaṃ dhānyādi yasya nādyāhnikaśvastano vā syāt | evaṃ yātrāmātraprasiddhyarthaṃ nityasya saṃcayasaṃkocadharmatāratamyāpekṣayāyam apavādaḥ || 4.7 ||
caturṇām api caiteṣāṃ dvijānāṃ gṛhamedhinām |
jyāyān paraḥ paro jñeyo dharmato lokajittamaḥ || 4.8 ||
caturṇām iti | eṣāṃ kusūladhānyakādīnāṃ caturṇām api brāḥmaṇānāṃ gṛhasthānāṃ madhyāt yaḥ paścāt paṭhitaḥ sa sa praśastataro boddhavyaḥ | yato ‘sau vṛttisaṃkocasaṃyamadharmātiśayena svargādilokajid bhavati || 4.8 ||
ṣaṭkarmaiko bhavaty eṣāṃ tribhir anyaḥ pravartate |
dvābhyāṃ ekaś caturthas tu brahmasattreṇa jīvati || 4.9 ||
ṣaṭkarmeti | eṣāṃ gṛhasthānāṃ madhyāt kaścid gṛhasthaḥ parigrahabhūyastvādyapekṣayā prākṛtaṛtādikarmā bhavati | anyaḥ punaḥ yājanādhyāpanapratigrahāṇāṃ brāhmaṇasya noditatvāt etais tribhir jīvituṃ pravartate, aparaḥ punaḥ pratigrahaḥ pratyavara iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt | tattyāgena dvābhyāṃ yājanādhyāpanābhyāṃ jīvituṃ pravartate | uktatrayāpekṣayā caturthaḥ punar adhyāpanena jīvati || 4.9 ||
vartayaṃś ca śiloñchābhyām agnihotraparāyaṇaḥ |
iṣṭīḥ pārvāyaṇāntīyāḥ kevalā nirvapet sadā || 4.10 ||
vartayann iti | śiloñchābhyāṃ jīvan karmāntarāsaṃbhave sāyaṃprātarhomaniṣṭhaḥ san darśapūrṇamāsān pārvaṇākhyān yāgān kevalān sarvakālaṃ kuryāt || 4.10 ||
na lokavṛttaṃ varteta vṛttihetoḥ karthaṃcana |
ajihmām aśaṭhāṃ jīved brāhmaṇajīvikām || 4.11 ||
na lokavṛttaṃ varteteti | jīvikārthaṃ ca vicitraparihāsādi na kadācid api kuryāt | api tu svanoditatvena apāpāṃ śraddhāvatīṃ dambhādivyājaśūnyāṃ brāhmaṇavṛttim anutiṣṭhet || 4.11 ||
saṃtoṣaṃ param āsthāya sukhārthī saṃyato bhavet |
saṃtoṣamūlaṃ hi sukhaṃ duḥkhamūlaṃ viparyayaḥ || 4.12 ||
saṃtoṣam iti | yaḥ samaḥ svargādidṣṛṣṭādṛṣṭasukhārthī svopagataviśeṣaspṛhaḥ san ātmakuṭumbanityānuṣṭhānamātraparyāptasaṃcaya eva dhane prakṛṣṭā nivṛttim āśritya dhanārjanaṃ saṃcayaṃ kuryāt yasmāt saṃtoṣaprabhavaṃ samastasvargādidṛṣṭādṛṣṭasukham | asaṃtoṣaḥ punar dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaduḥkhasya mūlam || 4.12 ||
ato ‘nyatamayā vṛttyā jīvaṃs tu snātako dvijaḥ |
svargyāyuṣyayaśasyāni vratānīmāni dhārayet || 4.13 ||
ata iti | āsāṃ mṛtādīnāṃ madhyāt anyatamayā vṛttyā kevalayā “ṣaṭkarmaiko bhavaty eṣām” ityādyuktatvāt, vṛttyantarasahitayā jīvan snātako brāhmaṇaḥ svargāyuḥkhyātinimittānīmāni vakṣyamāṇāni vratāni “idaṃ mayā kartavyam, idam na” ity evaṃ manaḥsaṃkalpaviśeṣātmakāni sadā dhārayet | na ca phalaśravaṇe ‘py eṣām anityatāpattiḥ, “etāvatā hainasā viyukto bhavati” iti śrutau nityatvenāmnānāt || 4.13 ||
vedoditaṃ svakaṃ karma nityaṃ kuryād atandritaḥ |
tad dhi kurvan yathāśakti prāpnoti paramāṃ gatim || 4.14 ||
vedoditam iti | śrautaṃ vedoditaṃ smārtam api ca vedamūlatvād vedoktam, svakaṃ yad yasya coditaṃ karma nityaṃ na kāmyaṃ tad analasaḥ san kuryāt yasmād yathāśakti kurvan nityakaraṇāt pāpakṣayānutpattibhyāṃ kāmyākaraṇāc ca phalahetudharmābhāve sati śubhāśubhakāryābhāvān mokṣaṃ prāpnoti || 4.14 ||
nehetārthān prasaṅgena na viruddhena karmaṇā |
na kalpamāneṣv artheṣu nārtyām api yatas tataḥ || 4.15 ||
na kalpamāneṣv artheṣv iti | gītādinā arthān nārjayet | nāpy ayājyayājanādinā | na cāvaśyakartavyasamartheṣu dhaneṣu satsu | nāpy ātmakuṭumbadharmāvasāde ‘pi upāyāntarasaṃbhave sati jātikarmaduṣṭān upāyāntarābhāve ca āpady asatpratigrahaṃ vakṣyati || 4.15 ||
indriyārtheṣu sarveṣu na prasajyeta kāmataḥ |
atiprasaktiṃ caiteṣāṃ manasā saṃnivartayet || 4.16 ||
indriyārtheṣv iti | cakṣurādigrāhyeṣu rūpādiṣu sarveṣūpabhogahetoḥ saṅgaṃ na kuryāt | apratiṣiddhānām api caiteṣāṃ svadāragamanādīnāṃ atiśayaprasaṅgam asthiratvādidoṣasaṃbhāvanayā manasā samyaṅ nivartayet || 4.16 ||
sarvān parityajed arthān svādhyāyasya virodhinaḥ |
yathā tathā yāpayaṃs tu sā hy asya kṛtakṛtyatā || 4.17 ||
sarvān iti | yathā tathā yāpayaṃs tv iti | yena kenacid upāyenātmādīn bibhrad vedādhyayanaviruddhān apratiṣiddhān api viṣayopabhogādīn arthān parityajet yasmāt sā brāhmaṇasya kṛtakṛtyatā yat svādhyāyaparatvam || 4.17 ||
vayasaḥ karmaṇo ‘rthasya śrutasyābhijanasya ca |
veṣavāgbuddhisārūpyam ācaran vicared iha || 4.18 ||
vayasa iti | vayaso ‘nuṣṭhānasya dhanasya śrutasya kulasya ānurūpyeṇa veṣakathābuddhīr ācaran loke vyavaharet | tad yathā snānasraganulepanādikaraṇaṃ trivarṇānusāriṇī ca vāṅmanasīty evaṃ karmādiṣv ap yojyam || 4.18 ||
buddhivṛddhikarāṇy āśu dhanyāni ca hitāni ca |
nityaṃ śāstrāṇy avekṣeta nigamāṃś caiva vaidikān || 4.19 ||
buddhivṛddhikarāṇīti | vedāviruddhanyāyaśāstrādīni kṣipraṃ buddhipāṭavajanakāni dhanārthāni ca auśanasyādīni hitāni ca dṛṣṭopakārakādīni āyurvedānīni anavagataśabdārthaparyāyakathanena vedārthabodhakān vedārthabodhakān nigamākhyān granthān nityaṃ paryālocayet || 4.19 ||
yathā yathā hi puruṣaḥ śāstraṃ samadhigacchati |
tathā tathā vijānāti vijñānaṃ cāsya rocate || 4.20 ||
yatheti | yasmāt yāvat puruṣaḥ śāstraṃ samyak vivinakti tāvat tat tad viśeṣeṇa jānāti śāstrāntaraviṣayam api cāsya vijñānam ujjvalaṃ bhavati || 4.20 ||
ṛṣiyajñaṃ devayajñaṃ bhūtayajñaṃ ca sarvadā |
nṛyajñaṃ pitṛyajñaṃ ca yathāśakti na hāpayet || 4.21 ||
ṛṣiyajñam iti | svādhyāyādīn yathāśakti na parityajed iti uktapañcayajñābhidhānaṃ vakṣyamāṇendiryasaṃpādyatvakathanārtham || 4.21 ||
etān eke mahāyajñān yajñaśāstravido janāḥ |
anīhamānāḥ satatam indriyeṣv eva juhvati || 4.22 ||
etān iti | bāhyābhyantarayajñopadeśakaśāstrajñāḥ kecin manuṣyāḥ jñānabalān nityānuṣṭhānārtham uparatadhanaceṣṭā etān mahāyajñān indriyeṣv eva sarvakālaṃ saṃpādayanti | indriyāṇi agnyātithyādirūpatayā tatsaṃyamaṃ vāgbāhulyādirūpatvena bhāvayanti | etān saṃpādayanti || 4.22 ||
vācy eke huhvati prāṇaṃ prāṇe vācaṃ ca sarvadā |
vāci prāṇe ca paśyanto yajñanirvṛttim akṣayām || 4.23 ||
vācīti | anye punaḥ vāci prāṇākhye ca vāyau akṣayaphalāṃ yajñasaṃpattiṃ manyamānā abhidhānakāme vāgagnau tūṣṇīṃbhāvakād eva ca prāṇāgnau vāgāhutiṃ juhvati prāṇāhutiṃ juhvati | evam anavarataṃ bhāvayatna āsate | tathā ca kauṣītakirahasyabrāhmaṇam: “yāvad vai puruṣo bhāṣate na tāvat prāṇituṃ śaknoti prāṇaṃ tadā vāci juhoti | yāvad vai puruṣaḥ pāṇiti na tāvad bhāṣituṃ śaknoti vācaṃ tadā prāṇe juhoti | ete anante amṛte āhutī jāgat svapaṃś ca satataṃ juhoti atha yā anyā āhutayo ‘nnavatyas tāḥ karmamayyo hi bhavanti, evaṃ tadvasmai tatpūrve vidvāṃso ‘gnihotraṃ juhavāṃ cakruḥ” iti || 4.23 ||
jñānenaivāpare viprā yajante tair makhaiḥ sadā |
jñānamūlāṃ kriyām eṣāṃ paśyanto jñānacakṣuṣā || 4.24 ||
jñāneneti | apare punar viprāḥ eṣāṃ yajñānāṃ vidvān yajate vidvān yājayatīty evaṃ avabodhaṃulam anuṣṭhānaṃ jñānacakṣuṣā  śāstreṇa paśyantaḥ vijñānabalād etān yajñān etadviṣayasamyagavabodhenaiva yāvajjīvaṃ saṃpādayanti | tathā ca “yo hy evaṃ dvijān etat karma kurute yo vai tad evaṃ veda” iti brāhmaṇam || 4.24 ||
agnihotraṃ ca juhuyād ādyante dyuniśoḥ sadā |
darśena cārdhamāsānte paurṇamāsena caiva hi || 4.25 ||
agnihotraṃ ceti | uditahomapakṣe dinādau dinānte homaṃ kuryāt | tathā anuditahomapakṣe rātryādau rātryante ca homaṃ kuryāt | tathā kṛṣṇapakṣānte darśākhyena karmaṇā, śuklapakṣānte paurṇamāsākhyena yajeta || 4.25 ||
sasyānte navasasyeṣṭyā tathartvante dvijo ‘dhvaraiḥ |
paśunā tv ayanānte tu samānte saumikair makhaiḥ || 4.26 ||
sasyānta iti | prāksaṃcitadhānyādikṣaye yadā varṣasya tṛptaḥ syāt “athāgrayaṇena yajeta śaradi navānnam” iti sūtrakāravacanāt | vidyamānadhānyo ‘pi cāgrayaṇena yajeta | ṛtuḥ saṃvatsaram ity asmin darśanena caturṣu caturṣu māseṣv ṛtvante ‘dhvaraiḥ cāturmāsyākhyaiḥ karmabhir yajeta | paśubandhākhyena karmaṇā uttarāyaṇadakṣiṇāyanādau yajeta | varṣānte ca jyotiṣṭomādinā somayāgeneti || 4.26 ||
nāniṣṭvā navasasyeṣṭyā paśunā cāgimān dvijaḥ |
navānnam adyān māṃsaṃ vā dīrgham āyur jijīviṣuḥ || 4.27 ||
neti | āhitāgnir dvijo dīrghāyur jīvitum icchuḥ āgrayaṇam akṛtvā navānnaṃ paśuyāgaṃ vākṛtvā māṃsaṃ nāśnīyat || 4.27 ||
navenānarcitā hy asy paśuhavyena cāgnayaḥ |
prāṇān evāttum icchanti navānnāmiṣagardhinaḥ || 4.28 ||
naveneti | yasmān navasasyena paśuyāgena (dānena) cāniṣṭvā agnayo navānnamāṃsāni kāṅkṣiṇo ‘syāgnyāhitasya prāṇān eva bhakṣayitum icchanti | tasmād āgrayaṇapaśuyāgau kartavyau || 4.28 ||
āsanāśanaśayyābhir adbhir mūlaphalena vā |
nāsya kaścid vased gehe śaktito ‘narcito ‘tithiḥ || 4.29 ||
āsanāśanaśayyābhir iti | āsanādanādibhir yathāśakti atithiṃ pūjayet ity uktvā atithyanuvādo mahāyajñaprasaṅgena vakṣyamāṇaḥ pākhaṇḍyādiniṣedhārthaḥ || 4.29 ||
pākhaṇḍino vikarmathān baiḍālavratikāñ chalān |
haitukān bakavṛttīṃś ca vāṅmātreṇāpi nārcayet || 4.30 ||
tathā cāha pākhaṇḍina iti | raktāṃbarādiliṅgadhāriṇaḥ paravṛttijīvanaratān biḍālavṛttikān bakavṛttikān vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇān śaṭhān aśrddadhānān vedabāhyatarkavyavahāriṇo vāṅmātreṇāpi na pūjayet iti pūjāpūrvakānnadānādiniṣedho dānamātraṃ tu śaktito ‘pacamānebhyo dātavyam iti vakṣyati || 4.30 ||
vedavidyāvratasnātāñ chrotriyān gṛhamedhinaḥ |
pūjayed dhavyakavyena viparītāṃś tu varjayet || 4.31 ||
vedavidyāvratasnātān iti | vedavidyāvratasnātāṇ ity anena vedasnātakavrasnātaka-vidyāvratasnātakatvena triprakārā api snātakā gṛhyante | yathā hārītaḥ: “samāpya vedaṃ asamāpya vratāni yaḥ snāti sa vedasnātakaḥ, asamāpya vedaṃ samāpya vratāni yaḥ snāti sa vratasnātaḥ | ubhayaṃ samāpya yaḥ snāti sa vidyāvratasnātakaḥ” iti | tāṃs tathā snatakagrahanena gṛhītān apy atiśayārthitvena pṛthaṅnirdiṣṭān śrotriyān gṛhasthān havyakavyena pūjayet | etadviparītāṃs tu varjayet itīdaṃ pākhaṇḍyādiniṣedhadārḍhyam, śrotriyādīnāṃ punar abhidhānam, pūjādipūrvakam antaṃ pākhaṇḍyādhibhyo na deyam || 4.31 ||
śaktito ‘pacamānebhyo dātavyaṃ gṛhamedhinā |
saṃvibhāgaś ca bhūtebhyaḥ kartavyo ‘nuparodhataḥ || 4.32 ||
annamātraṃ punaḥ śaktata iti | gṛhasthena pākarahitebhyaḥ pākhaṇḍyādibhyo yathāśaktyānnaṃ deyam, svakuṭuṃbādnuparodhena vṛkṣaparyantebhyo dohyādidānena saṃvibhāgaḥ kartavyaḥ || 4.32 ||
rājato dhanam anvicchet saṃsīdan snatakaḥ kṣudhā |
yājyāntevāsinor vāpi na tv anyata iti sthitiḥ || 4.33 ||
rājata iti | snātakaḥ kṣudhāvasādaṃ gacchan anyajātipratigrahasaṃbhave ‘pi rājñaḥ śāstrānuvartino yājyaśiṣyābhyāṃ vā prathamaṃ lipseta | na tv anyasmād dvijāter api | tadasaṃbhave punaḥ anyasmāt dvijāter gṛhṇīyāt anāpadvihitopāyāsaṃbhave punaḥ sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyād ity āpadharmaṃ vakṣyati | rājño mahādhanatvāt yājyānevāsinoḥ svasukṛtopakāratvāt idaṃ prathamadhanagrahaṇaṃ niyamyate | evaṃ cānāpadi prathamarājayājyaśiṣyavyatiriktadhanagrahaṇaniṣedhārtham idaṃ vacanam | ata evoktam na tv anyata iti | yat tu saṃsīdann iti liṅgāt āpadviṣayatām asyāhuḥ – tad asat, aprakaraṇāt | na ca dhanābhāvamātram āpad ucyate | api tu dhanābhāve iti vihitopāyaparikṣayaḥ | anyathā sadyaḥprakṣālakasya āpadharmā āvir bhaveyuḥ | apadviṣaye (-viṣayatve) cāsya “na tv anyata” ity etat “sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyāt” ity anena viruddhyeta | yat tu sīdadbhiḥ kupyam icchadbhiḥ dhanaṃ vā pṛthivīpatir yācyaḥ syād iti tadā yat prakaraṇāt kṣatriyād rājñaḥ pratigrahārtham | ata eva tatra pṛthivīpatigrahaṇam, iha tu rājagrahaṇam || 4.33 ||
na sīdet snātako vipraḥ kṣudhā śaktaḥ kathaṃcana |
na jīrṇamalavadvāsā bhavec ca vibhave sati || 4.34 ||
neti | snātako brāhmaṇo vidvattvāt pratigrahasamartho rājādidhanasaṃbhave sati kṣudhāvasādaṃ na kadācid gacchet | na ca saṃbhave jīrṇamalinavasanaḥ syāt || 4.34 ||
kṛtakeśanakhaśmaśrur dāntaḥ śuklāmbaraḥ śuciḥ |
svādhyāye caiva yuktaḥ syān nityam ātmahiteṣu ca || 4.35 ||
kṛtakeśanakhaśmaśrur iti | lūnakeśanakhaśmaśruḥ śamavān śuklavāsāḥ bāhyābhyantaraśaucopetaḥ vedābhyāsaśīlaḥ auṣadhopayogādirataḥ syāt || 4.35 ||
vaiṇavīṃ dhārayed yaṣṭiṃ sodakaṃ ca kamaṇḍalum |
yajñopavītaṃ vedaṃ ca śubhe raukme ca kuṇḍale || 4.36 ||
vaiṇavīm iti | veṇudaṇḍam udaksahitaṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ yajñopavītaṃ darbhamuṣṭiṃ rucirasauvarṇakuṇḍale dhārayet | yajñopavītakamaṇḍalvoḥ “agnīndhanaṃ bhaikṣacaryāṃ” (2.108) ity anena ca gṛhasthādīnām anuvṛttisiddhau snātakavratārthaṃ punargrahaṇam | evam anyeṣām api “na cocchiṣṭaḥ kvacid vrajet” ityevamādīnāṃ punargrahaṇaṃ vijñeyam || 4.36 ||
nekṣetodyantam ādityaṃ nāstaṃ yantaṃ kadācana |
nopasṛṣṭaṃ na vāristhaṃ na madhyaṃ nabhaso gatam || 4.37 ||
neti | udayāstamanarāhugrastodakapratibimbamadhyāhnavartinam ādityaṃ kasmiṃścid api nekṣeta || 4.37 ||
na laṅghayed vatsatantrīṃ na pradhāvec ca varṣati |
na codake nirīkṣeta svarūpam iti dhāraṇā || 4.38 ||
na laṅghayed iti | vatsavandhanarajjuṃ na laṅghayet varṣati na dhāvet udake ca svapratibimbaṃ nekṣetety evaṃ niścayaḥ || 4.38 ||
mṛdaṃ gāṃ daivataṃ vipraṃ ghṛtaṃ madhu catuṣpatham |
pradakṣiṇāni kurvīta prajñātāṃś ca vanaspatīn || 4.39 ||
mṛdam iti | prasthitaḥ san sammukhoparisthān uddhṛtamṛdgopāṣāṇādikṛtadevatābrāhmaṇaghṛta-mākṣikacatuṣpathāśvatthādidhyātavṛkṣān dakṣiṇahastamārgeṇa kuryāt || 4.39 ||
nopagacchet pramatto ‘pi striyam ārtavadarśane |
samānaśayane caiva na śayīta tayā saha || 4.40 ||
nopagacched iti | kāmārto ‘pi udakyāṃ na yāyāt nāpy ekaśayyāyāṃ tayā saha śayīta || 4.40 ||
rajasābhiplutāṃ nārīṃ narasya hy upagacchataḥ |
prajñā tejo balaṃ cakṣur āyuś caiva prahīyate || 4.41 ||
rajaseti | yasmād rajasvalāstriyaṃ puṃsa upagacchataḥ prajñāvīryādīni naśyanti, tasmāt tāṃ nopeyāt || 4.41 ||
tāṃ vivarjayatas tasya rajasā samabhiplutām |
prajñā tejo balaṃ cakṣur āyuś caiva pravardhate || 4.42 ||
tām iti | tāṃ punaḥ rajasvalāṃ na gacchataḥ prajñādīni vardhante tasmāt tāṃ na vrajed iti
|| 4.42 ||
nāśnīyād bhāryayā sārdhaṃ nainām īkṣeta nāśnatīm |
kṣuvatī jṛmbhamāṇāṃ vā na cāsīnāṃ yathāsukham || 4.43 ||
nāśnīyād iti | bhāryayā saha nādyāt yat punaḥ “nityam āsyaṃ śuci strīṇām” (5.130) iti tat “striyaś ca ratisaṃsarge” iti gautamasmaraṇāt ratikālaviṣayam eva | na cāpy enāṃ bhuñjānāṃ kṣuvantīṃ vāyunā cāsyavikāsaṃ kurvāṇāṃ prasṛtacaraṇatvādinā vā aniyantraṇāvasthāṃ paśyet || 4.43 ||
nāñjayantīṃ svake netre na cābhyaktām anāvṛtām |
na paśyet prasavantīṃ ca tejaḥkāmo dvijottamaḥ || 4.44 ||
nāñjayantīm iti | tathā svanetrayoḥ añjanaṃ dadatīṃ ghṛābhyaktāṃ “nagnāṃ nānyatra maithunāt” ity āpastambasmaraṇāt nekṣeta | apatyaṃ ca prasavantīṃ vīryakāmo brāhmaṇo na paśyet || 4.44 ||
nānnam adyād ekavāsā na nagnaḥ snānam ācaret |
na mūtraṃ pathi kurvīta na bhasmani na govraje || 4.45 ||
nānnam iti | ekavastro nāśnīyāt | na ca kaupīnācchādanaṃ vinā snāyāt | mūtrapurīṣe ca mārgabhasmagoṣṭheṣu na kuryāt || 4.45 ||
na phālakṛṣṭe na jale na cityāṃ na ca parvate |
na jīrṇadevāyatane na valmīke kadācana || 4.46 ||
na phālakṛṣṭa iti | tathā phālakṛṣṭe udake iṣṭakācite cāgnyadhikaraṇe parvate devāyatane pipīlikākṛṣṭe mṛtsaṃcaye ca na kadācit kuryāt || 4.46 ||
na sasattveṣu garteṣu na gacchan nāpi ca sthitaḥ |
na nadītīram āsādya na ca parvatamastake || 4.47 ||
na sasattveṣv iti | tathā saprāṇiṣu bileṣu na kuryāt, na vrajan na sthito vā nāpi nadītīram āśritya kuryāt parvataśṛṅge vā | parvatagrahaṇās eva parvatamastakaniṣedhasiddhau punarvacanaṃ na śṛṅgād anyatra parvate vikalpārtham | na ca tulyavikalpaḥ | tathāvidhau svecchāsiddhatvāt sāmānyapratiṣedhānarthakyam | tasmād atyantārtasya parvate na doṣaḥ, anyadā tu doṣa iti vyavasthito ‘yaṃ vikalpaḥ || 4.47 ||
vāyvagnibrāhmaṇādityam apaḥ paśyaṃs tathaiva gāḥ |
na kadācana kurvīta viṇmūtrasya visarjanam || 4.48 ||
vāyvagnibrāhmaṇādityam iti | vāyvagnibrāhmaṇādityodakagā avalokayan na kadācin mūtrapurīṣotsargaṃ kuryāt vāyoś ca saṃmukhībhāvo ‘valokanam || 4.48 ||
tiraskṛtyoccaret kāṣṭhaloṣṭapatratṛṇādi ca |
niyamya prayato vācaṃ saṃvītāṅgo ‘vaguṇṭhitaḥ || 4.49 ||
tiraskṛtyeti | kāṣṭhapatraloṣṭatṛṇaiḥ bhūmim antardhāya maunī ucchiṣṭacāṇḍālādisparśa-prāyaścittarahitaḥ ācchāditagātraḥ prāvṛtaśirā mūtrapurīṣe kuryāt || 4.49 ||
mūtroccārasamutsargaṃ divā kuryād udaṅmukhaḥ |
dakṣiṇābhimukho rātrau saṃdhyayoś ca yathā divā || 4.50 ||
mūtroccārasamutsargam iti | mūtrapurīṣotsargaṃ divā saṃdhyayoś cottarābhimukho rātrau dakṣiṇābhimukhaḥ kuryāt || 4.50 ||
chāyāyām andhakāre vā rātrāv ahani vā dvijaḥ |
yathāsukhamukhaḥ kuryāt prāṇabādhabhayeṣu ca || 4.51 ||
chāyāyām iti | rātrāv ahani vā chāyāyāṃ rātrāv andhakāre ahani vā nīhārādyandhakāre eteṣu, diṅmohe sati tathā ca vāyvādikṛtaprāṇavināśasaṃśayeṣu yatheṣṭamukho mūtrapurīṣe kuryāt || 4.51 ||
pratyagniṃ pratisūyaṃ ca pratisomodakadvijān |
pratigāṃ prativātaṃ ca prajñā naśyati mehataḥ || 4.52 ||
pratyagnim iti | agnyādityasomāmbhobrāhmaṇagovāyvabhimukham mūtrapurīṣe kurvataḥ prajñā naśyati, ata eva tan na kartavyam iti vāyuagnivipram ity asya phalakathanaṃ sīmābhimukhaṃ ca, ata eva niṣedhasiddhiḥ pūrvatrāpaṭhitatvāt || 4.52 ||
nāgniṃ mukhenopadhamen nagnāṃ nekṣeta ca striyam |
nāmedhyaṃ prakṣiped agnau na ca pādau pratāpayet || 4.53 ||
nāgnim iti | mukhavāyunāgniṃ na dhamet | na nagnāṃ parayoṣitam īkṣeta gautamasmaraṇāt | mūtrādi agnau na nikṣipet pādau na tatra pratāpayet || 4.53 ||
adhastān nopadadhyāc ca na cainam abhilaṅghayet |
na cainaṃ pādataḥ kuryān na prāṇābādham ācaret || 4.54 ||
adhastād iti | na cainaṃ pādataḥ kuryān na prāṇivadham ācaret | tathā ca khaṭvādiṣu agniṃ adhastān na sthāpayet | nāpy enam ullaṅghayet | na ca pādadeśataḥ kuryāt | prāṇipīḍākaraṃ ca nācaret || 4.54 ||
nāśnīyāt saṃdhivelāyāṃ na gacchen nāpi saṃviśet |
na caiva pralikhed bhūmiṃ nātmano ‘paharet srajam || 4.55 ||
nāśnīyād iti | saṃdhyākāle aśanagamanasvāpān na kuryāt, bhūmiṃ sarvadā nakhādinā na likhet | mālāṃ ca śirasaḥ svayaṃ nāpanayet || 4.55 ||
nāpsu mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ vā ṣṭhīvanaṃ vā samutsṛjet |
amedhyaliptam anyad vā lohitaṃ vā viṣāṇi vā || 4.56 ||
nāpsv iti | mūtrapurīṣaśleṣmāmedhyaliptarudhiraviṣāṇi udake na kṣipet || 4.56 ||
naikaḥ śūnyagṛhe svapyān na śreyāṃsaṃ prabodhayet |
nodakyayābhibhāṣeta yajñaṃ gacchen na cāvṛtaḥ || 4.57 ||
naikaḥ svapyād iti | udvasitagṛhe eko na śayīta vittādyabhyadhikaṃ suptaṃ ca na probodhayet | rajasvalayā saha saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ na kuryāt | yajñaṃ cākṛtavaraṇaṃ ṛtvik na gacchet | “darśanārthaṃ tu kāmam” iti gautamaḥ || 4.57 ||
agnyagāre gavāṃ goṣṭhe brāhmaṇānāṃ ca saṃnidhau |
svādhyāye bhojane caiva dakṣiṇaṃ pāṇim uddharet || 4.58 ||
agnyāgāra iti | agniśālāyāṃ gavāṃ goṣṭhe nivāse brāhmaṇasamīpe svādhyāyabhojanakālayoḥ dakṣiṇaṃ pāṇim ā bāhor vāsasa uddharet || 4.58 ||
na vārayed gāṃ dhayantīṃ na cācakṣīta kasyacit |
na divīndrāyudhaṃ dṛṣṭvā kasyacid darśayed budhaḥ || 4.59 ||
na vārayed iti | gāṃ pibantīṃ na nivartayet | nāpi parakīyāṃ kṣīrādi pibantīṃ tasya kathayet | na ca dyuloke indradhanur dṛṣṭvā śāstrajño ‘nyasya darśayet || 4.59 ||
nādhārmike vased grāme na vyādhibahule bhṛśam |
naikaḥ prapadyetādhvānaṃ na ciraṃ parvate vaset || 4.60 ||
nādhārmika iti | adharmabahulajanagrāmādau atyarthaṃ vyādhibahule na vaset | ekaś cādhvānaṃ na yāyāt | parvate ca dīrghakālaṃ na tiṣṭhet || 4.60 ||
na śūdrarājye nivasen nādhārmikajanāvṛte |
na pāṣaṇḍigaṇākrānte nopasṛṣṭe ‘ntyajair nṛbhiḥ || 4.61 ||
na śūdrarājya iti | śūdrarājake deśe adhārmikajaniaś ca bāhyataḥ parivṛte śrutyādibāhyliṅgadhāribhiś ca vaśīkṛte caṇḍālādibhiś copadrute na nivaset || 4.61 ||
na bhuñjītoddhṛtasnehaṃ nātisauhityam ācaret |
nātiprage nātisāyaṃ na sāyaṃ prārarāśitaḥ || 4.62 ||
na bhuñjteti | uddhṛtasnehaṃ piṇyākādi na bhuñjīta | tṛptiṃ ca nācaret | atiprātaḥ dinānte na bhuñjīta | sāyaṃ prātaś ca na tṛptaḥ syāt | api tu ekasmin kāle tṛpto ‘parasmin sākāṅkṣam aśnīyāt
|| 4.62 ||
na kurvīta vṛthāceṣṭāṃ na vāry aṅjalinā pibet |
notsaṅge bhakṣayed bhakṣyān na jātu syāt kutūhalī || 4.63 ||
na kurvīteti | dṛṣṭādṛṣṭānupakāraphalaṃ vyāpāraṃ na kuryāt | udakaṃ cāñjalinā na pibet | ūrvoś copari vinyasya modakāni na bhakṣayet | niṣprayojanaṃ ca kim etat syād iti kutūhalaparo na kadācit syāt || 4.63 ||
na nṛtyen naiva gāyec ca na vāditrāṇi vādayet |
nāsphoṭayen na ca kṣveḍen na ca rakto virāvayet || 4.64 ||
na nṛtyed iti | aśāstrīyāṇi nṛtyagītavādyāni na kuryāt | pāṇinā bāhuṃ nābhihanyāt | avyaktadantadhvaniṃ na kuryāt ca harṣitaḥ san kharavirāvaṃ na kuryāt || 4.64 ||
na pādau dhāvayet kāṃsye kadācid api bhājane |
na bhinnabhāṇḍe bhuñjīta na bhāvapratidūṣite || 4.65 ||
na pādāv iti | kāṃsyapātre na kadācid api pādau prakṣālayet | “tāmrarajatasuvarṇānāṃ bhinnam abhinnaṃ ca” iti paiṭhīnasismaraṇāt tato ‘nyatra bhinnabhājane nābhibhuñjīta nāpi hṛllekhotpādake || 4.65 ||
upānahau ca vāsaś ca dhṛtam anyair na dhārayet |
upavītam alaṃkāraṃ srajaṃ karakam eva ca || 4.66 ||
upānahāv iti | upānadvastrayajñopavītābharaṇamālyakamaṇḍlūn anyopayuktān na dhārayet || 4.66 ||
nāvinītair vrajed dhuryair na ca kṣudvyādhipīḍitaiḥ |
na bhinnaśṛṅgākṣikhurair na vāladhivirūpitaiḥ || 4.67 ||
nāvinītair iti | aśvavṛṣabhādibhiḥ gantryādidhurvahanaviniyuktair adāntaiḥ bubhukṣāvyādhyārtiyuktaiḥ bhinnaśṛṅgākṣikhuraiḥ chinnapucchaiḥ na yāyāt || 4.67 ||
vinītais tu vrajen nityam āśugair lakṣaṇānvitaiḥ |
varṇarūpopasaṃpannaiḥ pratodenākṣipan bhṛśam || 4.68 ||
vinītair iti | suśikṣitaiḥ punaḥ kṣipragāmibhiḥ devamaṇyādyāvartādiyuktaiḥ kakudādiśūnyaiḥ śoṇādivarṇasaṃpannaiḥ svākṛtibhiḥ pratodenātyartham avyathayan vrajet || 4.68 ||
bālātapaḥ pretadhūmo varjyaṃ bhinnaṃ tathāsanam |
na chindyān nakhalomāṇi dantair notpāṭayen nakhān || 4.69 ||
bālātapa iti | prathamoditādityātapaḥ | śavadhūmabhagnapīṭhādyupaveśanāni varjyāni | nakhalomāni cātmana na chindyāt | dantaiś ca nakhān notpāṭayet || 4.69 ||
na mṛlloṣṭaṃ ca mṛdnīyān na chindyāt karajais tṛṇam |
na karma niṣphalaṃ kuryān nāyatyām asukhodayam || 4.70 ||
na mṛlloṣṭam iti | “nākāraṇaṃ mṛlloṣṭaṃ gṛhṇīyāt tṛṇāni vā chindyāt” ity āpastambasmaraṇāt | nirnimittaṃ mṛlloṣṭaṃ na gṛhṇīyāt nakhais tṛṇaṃ na chindyāt | yataḥ svakarmaṇo dṛṣṭam adṛṣṭaṃ phalaṃ notpadyate yac cāgāmini asukhāvahaṃ tan na kuryāt || 4.70 ||
loṣṭamardī tṛṇachedī nakhakhādī ca yo naraḥ |
sa vināśaṃ vrajaty āśu sūcako ‘śucir eva ca || 4.71 ||
loṣṭamardīti | loṣṭatṛṇanakhānāṃ mardanachedanakhādanakṛt sūcako ‘śuciś ca manuṣyo vināśaṃ kṣipraṃ yāti | tasmād etāni na kartavyāni | bhakṣaṇāśucitvayor ata eva niṣedhaḥ
|| 4.71 ||
na vigṛhya kathāṃ kuryād bahirmālyaṃ na dhārayet |
gavāṃ ca yānaṃ pṛṣṭhena sarvathaiva vigarhitam || 4.72 ||
ni vigṛhyeti | sābhiniveśaṃ kathāṃ na kuryāt, vāsaś ca bahirmālyaṃ na dhārayet | gopṛṣṭhārohaṇena gamanaṃ paryaṭanavyavadhānādiprakāreṇādidharmāpaham || 4.72 ||
advāreṇa ca nātīyād grāmaṃ vā veśma vā vṛtam |
rātrau ca vṛkṣamūlāni dūrataḥ parivarjayet || 4.73 ||
advāreṇeti | prākārādyāvṛtaṃ grāmaṃ gṛhaṃ vā dvāravyatiriktapradeśena nātikramya praviśet | rātrau ca vṛkṣamūlāvasthānaṃ dūrāt parivarjayet || 4.73 ||
nākṣair dīvyet kadācit tu svayaṃ nopānahau haret |
śayanastho na bhuñjīta na pāṇisthaṃ na cāsane || 4.74 ||
nākṣair iti | akṣādibhiḥ kelyartham api na krīḍeta | ātmanā copānahau pādavyatirekeṇa na gṛhṇīyāt | khaṭvāvyavasthitaś ca na bhuñjīta | nāpi pātrāvyavahitahastam annam āsane ca pātram avasthāya bhuñjīta || 4.74 ||
sarvaṃ ca tilasaṃbaddhaṃ nādyād astam ite ravau |
na ca nagnaḥ śayīteha na cocchiṣṭaḥ kvacid vrajet || 4.75 ||
sarvam iti | yat kiṃcit tilasaṃyuktaṃ kṛsarādi tad astam ite ‘rke nādyāt | kaupīnācchādanarahitaś cāsmin loke na supyāt | ucchiṣṭaś ca kvacid yāyāt || 4.75 ||
ārdrapādas tu bhuñjīta nārdrapādas tu saṃviśet |
ārdrapādas tu bhuñjāno dīrgham āyur avāpnuyāt || 4.76 ||
ārdrapāda iti | jalārdrapādo bhojanam upakramet | ārdrapādaḥ punaḥ na svapyāt | ārdrapādo hy aśnan dīrgham āyuḥ prāpnuyād iti phalakathanam etat | na cānityatāpattiḥ |
pañcārdro bhojanaṃ kuryāt prāṅmukho maunam āsthitaḥ |
na ninded dattabhakṣyāṃś ca syād svādu na kutsayet ||
iti vyāsena nityasmaraṇāt || 4.76 ||
acakṣurviṣayaṃ durgaṃ na prapadyeta karhicit |
na viṇmūtram udīkṣeta na bāhubhyāṃ nadīṃ taret || 4.77 ||
acakṣurviṣayam iti | cakṣurgocaro yo na bhavati durgamo deśaḥ taṃ kadācid api na gacchet | mūtrapurīṣe ca nodīkṣeta | bāhubhyāṃ nadyādi na taret || 4.77 ||
adhitiṣṭhen na keśāṃs tu bhasmāsthikapālikāḥ |
na kārpāsāsthi na tuṣān dīrgham āyur jijīviṣuḥ || 4.78 ||
adhitiṣṭhed iti | dīrgham āyur jīvitum icchuḥ keśabhasmāsthikapālikāḥ kārpāsabījatuṣān nādhirohet || 4.78 ||
na saṃvasec ca paitair na cāṇḍālair na pulkasaiḥ |
na mūkhair nāvaliptaiś ca nāntyair nāntyavasāyibhiḥ || 4.79 ||
na saṃvaśed iti | patitacāṇḍālapulkasālpabuddhigarhitāntyāvasāyibhiḥ grāmantaranivāsibhir api kārtyārtham ekapradeśe muhūrtam api nāsīteti | “nādharmike vased grāme” ityādibhyo bhedaḥ | patitādayaś ca daśamaikādaśādhyāye vakṣyante || 4.79 ||
na śūdrāya matiṃ dadyān nocchiṣṭaṃ na haviṣkṛtam |
na cāsyopadiśed dharmaṃ na cāsya vratam ādiśet || 4.80 ||
na śūdrāyeti | matir atra dharmasya pṛthag upadeśāt dṛṣṭopakārakocchiṣṭahaviṣkṛtasāhacaryāt vā dṛṣṭārthahitopadeśaṃ śūdrasya na kuryāt | ucchiṣṭaṃ vādāsasūdrāya na deyaṃ yato dāsaviṣaye ucchiṣṭam annaṃ dātavyam iti vakṣyati | yat tu “dvijocchiṣṭaṃ tu bhojanam” iti sabhoktur upadeśo na dātus tathā havirarthaṃ yac ca puroḍāśādi tasya “devānnāni havīṃṣi cea” iti pratiṣedham uktvā taccheṣasya “haviḥśeṣaṃ ca yad bhavet” | dvijātīnāṃ bhakṣyatāṃ vakṣyaty ataś ca taddhaviḥśeṣībhūtaṃ śūdrāya na deyam | tathedam idam adṛṣṭārthaṃ tvayā kartavyam ity śūdrasya dharmaṃ nopadiśet | vrataṃ cāsya prāyaścittaṃ sākṣān nopadiśet tathā cātyantadhārmikaśūdraviṣayeṣv aṅgirāḥ |
tathā śūdraṃ samāsādya sadā dharmaṃ puraḥsaram |
antarā brāhmaṇaṃ krtvā prāyaścittaṃ samādiśet || iti |
brāhmaṇopadeśapraṇālikayā prāyaścittadānam āha na sākṣād iti || 4.80 ||
yo hy asya dharmam ācaṣṭe yaś caivādiśati vratam |
so ‘saṃvṛtaṃ nāma tamaḥ saha tenaiva majjati || 4.81 ||
ya iti | yasmāt yaḥ śūdrasya dharmavrate upadiśati sa brāhmaṇaḥ pāpotpattinimittabhūtena tenaiva śūdreṇa saha atimahattvād asaṃvṛtākhyaṃ tamaḥ andhakāraṃ praviśati | pañcake prakṛte dvayoḥ phalakathanaṃ prāyaścittagauravārtham || 4.81 ||
na saṃhatābhyāṃ pāṇibhyāṃ kaṇḍūyed ātmanaḥ śiraḥ |
na spṛśec caitad ucchiṣṭo na ca snāyād vinā tataḥ || 4.82 ||
na saṃhatābhyām iti | saṃśliṣṭābhyāṃ pāṇibhyāṃ svaśiro na kaṇḍayet | saṃhatavacanād asaṃhatābhyāṃ na niṣedhaḥ | evaṃ sarvatra vyāvartya jñeyam | ucchiṣṭahastādinā yena kenacid api śiro na spṛśet | śirasā vinā ca nityanaimittakasnāne na kuryāt | dṛṣṭārthe tv aniṣedhaḥ, snānavidhināsyaikavākyatvāt || 4.82 ||
keśagrahān prahārāṃś ca śirasy etān vivarjayet |
śiraḥsnātaś ca tailena nāṅgaṃ kiṃcid api spṛśet || 4.83 ||
keśagrahān iti | keśagrahaṇaprahārau kopena mūrdhani na kuryāt | saśiraskaḥ snātaḥ tailena kiṃcid apy aṅgaṃ spṛśet || 4.83 ||
na rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇīyād arājanyaprasūtitaḥ |
sūnācakradhvajavatāṃ veśenaiva ca jīvatām || 4.84 ||
na rājña iti | “rājño ‘patye jātigrahaṇam” iti kṣatriya eva rājanya ucyate | tasya santatir apatyaṃ viśeṣavacanāt kṣatriya eva rājanyaprasūtiḥ na rājanyaprasūtiḥ arājanyaprasūtiḥ | akṣatriyaḥ tasmāt akṣatriyād rājñaḥ prakaraṇād anāpadi na pratigṛhṇīyāt tathā saunikatailikamadyavaṇijāṃ paṇyastrīvyavahāreṇa ca jīvatāṃ na pratigṛhṇīyāt || 4.84 ||
daśasūnāsamaṃ cakraṃ daśacakrasamo dhvajaḥ |
daśadhvajasamo veśyā daśaveśyāsamo nṛpaḥ || 4.85 ||
daśasūnāsamam iti | daśadhvajasamā veśyā daśaveśyāsamo nṛpaḥ | sūnā āghātanasthānaṃ daśasu sūnāsu yāvān saunikasya doṣaḥ tāvān cakravartini tailike | daśasu ca tilikeṣu yāvāṃs tāvān dhvajini madyavaṇiji | daśasu dhvajiṣu yāvāṃs tāvān veśavati | daśasu veśavatsu yāvāṃs tāvān nṛpe | evaṃ cottarottaranindātiśayadarśanena āpadi pūrvadātṛsaṃhve uttaraḥ pariharaṇīyaḥ
|| 4.85 ||
daśasūnāsahasrāṇi yo vāhayati saunikaḥ |
tena tulyaḥ smṛto rājā ghoras tasya pratigrahaḥ || 4.86 ||
evaṃ coktakalanayā – daśasūnāsahasrāṇīti | daśasūnāsahasrāṇiyaḥ saunikaḥ svārthe vyāpārayati tena tulyo rājā manvādibhiḥ smṛtaḥ | atas tatsaṃbandhī pratigraho narakapātahetutvāt bhīṣanīyaḥ || 4.86 ||
yo rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇāti lubdhasyocchrāstravartinaḥ |
sa paryāyeṇa yātīmān narakān ekaviṃśatim || 4.87 ||
ya iti | kṣatriyasyāpi ca kṛpaṇasya śāstrātikramavartinaḥ rājño yaḥ pratigrahaṃ karoti sa imān vakṣyamāṇān narakān yātanāsthānāni krameṇa prāpnoti || 4.87 ||
tāmisram andhatāmisraṃ mahārauravarauravau |
narakaṃ kālasūtraṃ ca mahānarakam eva ca || 4.88 ||
saṃjīvanaṃ mahāvīciṃ tapanaṃ saṃpratāpanam |
saṃghātaṃ ca sakākolaṃ kuḍmalaṃ pūtimṛttikam || 4.89 ||
lohaśaṅkum ṛjīṣaṃ ca panthānaṃ śālmalīṃ nadīm |
asipatravanaṃ caiva lohadārakam eva ca || 4.90 ||
tāmisram iti | saṃjīvanam iti | lohaśaṃkum ṛjīṣam iti | atra yātīty anuvartate | tatrāndhkārarūpas tāmisraḥ nibiḍāndhakārātmakāndhatāmisro mahārauravarauravau mahākalakala-kalakalātmakau kālasūtrākhyo narako yatra mahatī sarvaprakārapīḍā || saṃjīvanaṃ yatra bahuśaḥ saṃjīvya vyāpādyante | mahāvīcir yatra mahākallolair nīyate | tapano ‘gnyādisantāpātmakaḥ | saṃpratāpanaḥ kumbhīpākaḥ | saṃghāto ‘tyantasaṃvṛte pradeśe bahūnām avasthānam | kākolaṃ yatra kākair bhakṣyante | kuḍmalaṃ yatra rajjubhiḥ pīḍanam | pūtimṛttikaḥ purīṣagandhamṛttikaḥ | lohaśaṅkuḥ sūcībhedanaṃ yatra | ṛjīṣaṃ piṣṭapacanaprakṣepo yatra | panthā yatrāṭyate | śālmalo yatra śālmalipaṇṭakais tudyate | nadī yatra nadyāpahriyate | asipatravanaṃ yatra karapatrair vidāryate | lohadārako yatra nigaḍabandhaḥ || 4.88–90 ||
etad vidanto vidvāṃso brāhmaṇā brahmavādinaḥ |
na rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇanti pretya śreyo ‘bhikāṃkṣiṇaḥ || 4.91 ||
etad rājapratigrahaphalaṃ śāstrajñā vedādhyāyinaḥ brāhmaṇā jānantaḥ paralokaduḥkhābhāvārthinaḥ rājño dhanaṃ na pratigṛhṇanti || 4.91 ||
brāhme muhūrte budhyeta dharmārthau cānucintayet |
kāyakleśāṃś ca tanmūlān vedatattvārtham eva ca || 4.92 ||
brāhma iti | rātreḥ paścime muhūrte pratibudhyeta | tato dharmārtau itaretarāvirodhenārjanārthaṃ vicārayet | tathā dharmāṛthatanmūlabhūtaṃ kāyakleśaṃ iyatā kālakleśena iyān iyān dharmo ('rtho) vārjyat ity evaṃ cintayet | vedasya ca kaḥ pāramārthiko 'rthaḥ syād ity evaṃ tam api cintayet || 4.92 ||
utthāyāvaśyakaṃ kṛtvā kṛtaśaucaḥ samāhitaḥ |
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japaṃs tiṣṭhet svakāle cāparāṃ ciram || 4.93 ||
utthāyeti | tataḥ uṣaḥkāle śayanād utthāya sati saṃbhave mehanādi kṛtvā kṛtavakṣyamāṇaśaucaḥ saṃyatamanāḥ ukte kāle pūrvāpare saṃdhye arkadarśanād ūrdhvam api japaṃ kurvan uditahomo varteta | anuditahomy api homaṃ kṛtvā cāsīta | āyurādidīrghasaṃdhyāphalavidhānārtham idaṃ punaḥ saṃdhyābhidhānam || 4.93 ||
ṛṣayo dīrghasaṃdhyatvād dīrgham āyur avāpnuyuḥ |
prajñāṃ yaśaś ca kīrtiṃ ca brahmavarcasam eva ca || 4.94||
tathā cāha ṛṣaya iti | prajñām iti | yadmād ṛṣayo dīrghasaṃdhyānuṣṭhānāt dīrgham āyuḥ prajñāvantaś ca yaśaḥ svaryātāś ca kīrtiim adhyayanādisaṃpattiś ca prāptavantaḥ tasmād etat kāmena dīrghasaṃdhyānuṣṭhānaṃ kartavyam || 4.94 ||
śrāvaṇyāṃ prauṣṭhapadyāṃ vāpy upākṛtya yathāvidhi |
yuktāś chandāṃsy adhīyīta māsān vipro ‘rdhapañcamān || 4.95 ||
śrāvaṇyām iti | śrāvaṇasya bhādrapadasya vā paurṇamāsyām upākarmākhyaṃ (karma) gṛhyoktavidhānena kṛtvā tataḥ sārdhāś caturo māsān brāhmaṇo yatnavān vedān adhīyīta || 4.95 ||
puṣye tu chandasāṃ kuryād bahir utsarjanaṃ dvijaḥ |
māghaśuklasya vā prāpte pūrvāhṇe prathame ‘hani || 4.96 ||
yena śrāvaṇyām upākarma kṛtaṃ sa dvijo ‘rdhapañcameṣu māseṣu pauṣamāse tu puṣyanakṣatre grāmād bahir niṣkramya chandasām utsargākhyaṃ gṛhyoktaṃ karma kuryāt | yena pauṣṭhapadyāṃ kṛtaṃ sa māghaśuklapakṣasya prathame ‘hani prāpte pūrvāhṇe vā kuryāt
|| 4.96 ||
yathāśāstraṃ tu kṛtvaivam utsargaṃ chandasāṃ bahiḥ |
viramet pakṣiṇīṃ rātriṃ tad evaikam aharniśam || 4.97 ||
yathāśāstram iti | evam uktaśāstrānatikrameṇa chandasām utsargākhyaṃ karma grāmād bahiḥ kṛtvā tataḥ ubhayatohanī pakṣāv iva yasyā madhyavartunyā rātyāḥ tāṃ rātriṃ tam evaikam ahorātram anadhyāyaṃ kuryāt || 4.97 ||
ata ūrdhvaṃ tu chandāṃsi śukleṣu niyataḥ paṭhet |
vedāṅgāni ca sarvāṇi kṛṣṇapakṣeṣu saṃpaṭhet || 4.98 ||
ata iti | utsargānadhyāyād ūrdhvaṃ śuklapakṣeṣu saṃyamavān vedān adhīyīta | kṛṣṇapakṣe tu vedāṅgāni vyākaraṇādīni niyamitaḥ paṭhet || 4.98 ||
nāvispaṣṭam adhīyīta na śūdrajanasaṃnidhau |
na niśānte pariśrānto brahmādhītya punaḥ svapet || 4.98 ||
nāvispaṣṭam iti | sarvadā avispaṣṭam avyaktaṃ na paṭhet | tathā śūdrajanasaṃnidhau nādhīyīta | tathā suptotthitaḥ na rātreḥ paścime bhāge vedān adhītya śrāntaḥ san punaḥ supyāt
|| 4.98 ||
yathoditena vidhinā nityaṃ chandaskṛtaṃ paṭhet |
brahma chandaskṛtaṃ caiva dvijo yukto hy anāpadi || 4.100 ||
yathoditeneti | adhyāpakasya karmāntaravyagratvaṃ ātmanaś ca bahuvedane hy āpat tadabhāve pūrvoktavidhinā dvijo yatnavān sarvadā gāyatryādichandoyuktaṃ ṛksāmākhyaṃ brahma paṭhet | tathā brahma brāhmaṇaṃ gāyatryādichandoyuktamantramiśraṃ yajurvedākhyam | mantrabrāhmaṇayor miśrapāṭhāt vailakṣaṇye saty ayaṃ pṛthaṅnirdeśaḥ || 4.100 ||
imān nityam anadhyāyān adhīyāno vivarjayet |
adhyāpanaṃ ca kurvāṇaḥ śiṣyāṇāṃ vidhipūrvakam || 4.101 ||
imān iti | pūrvoktena vidhānenādhīyānaḥ śiṣyāṃś  cādhyāpayan vakṣyamāṇān anadhyāyān sarvadā varjayet || 4.101 ||
karṇaśrave ‘nile rātrau divā pāṃsusamūhane |
etau varṣāsv anadhyāyāv adhyāyajñāḥ pracakṣate || 4.102 ||
karṇaśrava iti | karṇābhyām eva śravaṇopapatteḥ atiśayavivakṣayā karṇaśrava ity uktam | tena rātrāv atiśabdavati vāyau, divā ca rajaḥsamāharaṇasamarthe vāti, etau vaeṣākāle tāvatkālikāv anadhyāyau adhyayanavidhijñāḥ kathayanti || 4.102 ||
vidyutstanitavarṣeṣu maholkānāṃ ca saṃplave |
ākālikam anadhyāyam eteṣu manur abravīt || 4.103 ||
vidyutstanitavarṣeṣv iti | vidyudgarjitavarṣeṣu yugapadupasthiteṣu mahatāṃ ca divyajyotirviśeṣeṇa punaḥ sthānataś ca pāte sati eteṣu nimittakālād ārabyāparedyuyāvat sa eva kālaḥ tāvat prakṛtāsu varṣāsu anadhyāyaṃ manur āha || 4.103 ||
etāṃs tv abhyuditān vidyād yadā prāduṣkṛtāgniṣu |
tadā vidyād anadhyāyam anṛtau cābhradarśane || 4.104 ||
etān iti | etān vidyudādīn juhūṣayā prakaṭīkṛtāgniṣu saṃdhyālakṣaṇeṣu kāleṣu yatopajātān jānīyāt tadānadhyāyaṃ kuryāt | na sarvadā varṣāsu etadutpāte tathā varṣābhyo ‘nyatrābhradarśane saty anadhyāyaḥ || 4.104 ||
nirghāte bhūmicalane jyotiṣāṃ copasarjane |
etān ākālikān vidyād anadhyāyān ṛtāv api || 4.105 ||
nirghāta iti | antarikṣe utpātadhvanau bhūkampe āditādīnāṃ pariveṣaṇe sati anṛtāv api varṣāsv api | apiśabdāt anyadāpi ākālikān anadhyāyān jānīyāt || 4.105 ||
prāduṣkṛteṣv agniṣu tu vidyutstanitaniḥsvane |
sajyotiḥ syād anadhyāyaḥ śeṣe rātrau yathā divā || 4.106 ||
prāduṣkṛteṣv iti | agnīnāṃ punar apāsitabhasmatayā prakaṭīkaraṇavelāyāṃ saṃdhyākāle vidyudgarjitaśabdāv eva yadā bhavato na varṣaṃ tadā yāvaj jyotiḥ tāvad evānadhyāyaḥ nākālikam, divādityajyotiḥ rātrau tārakāḥ | ataḥ pūrvaṃ saṃdhyāyām etad utpāte divaivānadhyāyaḥ paścimāyāṃ cotpāte rātrāv eva | śeṣeṣu ca punar varṣākhye tṛtīya upajāte yathā divā tathā rātrāv apy anadhyāyaḥ ity uktānuvādaḥ || 4.106 ||
nityānadhyāya eva syād grāmeṣu nagareṣu ca |
dharmanaipuṇyakāmānāṃ pūtigandhe ca sarvaśaḥ || 4.107 ||
nityānadhyāya iti | dharmātiśayārthināṃ grāmanagarayoḥ sarvadānadhyāyaḥ syāt | kutsitagandhena ca sarvasminn upalabhyamāne ‘nadhyāyaḥ asminn anadhyāyaprakaraṇe nityaśabdādīnāṃ kvacit kvacid abhāvāt tatra tatra nityatvaṃ anyatra vikalpaḥ | sa ca dharmanaippuṇavidyānaipuṇakāmabhedena vyavasthito jñeyaḥ | dvividhā hy adhyetāraḥ, dharmanaipuṇakāmā vidyānaipuṇakāmāś ca | tatra ye svīkṛtaprāyavedāḥ kevalaṃ niyamānuṣthānajanyam adṛṣṭātiśayam arthayante te dharmanaipuṇakāmāḥ teṣām aśeṣānadhyāyādaraṇam | punaḥ prathamādhyetāraḥ te vidyātiśayārthinaḥ eṣāṃ nityatvaliṅgavyatiriktānadhyāyādaraṇaṃ nāsti || 4.107 ||
antargataśave grāme vṛṣalasya ca saṃnidhau |
anadhyāyo rudyamāne samavāye janasya ca || 4.108 ||
antargataśava iti | vidyamānaśave vṛṣalasya ca adhārmikasya ca saṃnidhau “na śūdrajanasaṃnidhau” it śūdrasyoktatvāt rodane ca, janānāṃ kāryārtham ekatra saṃnidhāne saty anadhyāyaḥ || 4.108 ||
udake madhyarātre ca viṇmūtrasya visarjane |
ucchiṣṭaḥ śrāddhabhuk caiva manasāpi na cintayet || 4.109 ||
udaka iti | jalamadhye madhame ca praharadvaye mahāniśākhye “mahāniśā ca vijñeyā madhyasthaṃ praharadvayam” iti devalasmaraṇāt | mūtrapurīṣakāle prāg ācamanād ucchiṣṭaḥ śrāddhabhuk nimantraṇāt prabhṛti śrāddhabhojanād ahorātraṃ yāvat manasāpi vedasya na smaret || 4.109 ||
pratigṛhya dvijo vidvān ekoddiṣṭaniketanam |
tryahaṃ na kīrtayed brahma rājño rāhoś ca sūtake || 4.110 ||
pratigṛhyeti | śāstravid brāhmaṇaḥ navaśrāddhe ca nimantraṇam urarīkṛtya nṛpasya ca putrajanmādau rāhuṇā vā arkendugrāme sati tryahaṃ vedaṃ nādhīyīta || 4.110 ||
yāvad ekānudiṣṭasya gandho lepaś ca tiṣṭhati |
viprasya viduṣo dehe tāvad brahma na kīrtayet || 4.111 ||
tryahād ūrdhvam api vā | yāvad iti | yāvad ekoddeśena nimantritasya śāstravido brāhmaṇasya śrāddhagatasrakcandanādigandhalepo gātre bhavataḥ tāvav vedam nādhīyīta || 4.111 ||
śayānaḥ prauḍhapādaś ca kṛtvā caivāvasakthikām |
nādhīyītāmiṣaṃ jagdhvā sūtakānnādyam eva ca || 4.112 ||
śayāna iti | śayyotsṛṣṭāṅgaprasāritapādaḥ pādāropitapādo vā paryastikāṃ ca kṛtvā māṃsam āśaucānnaṃ ca bhuktvā nādhīyīta || 4.112 ||
nīhāre bāṇaśabde ca saṃdhyayor eva cobhayoḥ |
amāvāsyācaturdaśyayoḥ paurṇamāsyaṣṭamīṣu ca || 4.113 ||
dhūmikāyāṃ vāṇaśabde saṃdhyayoḥ amāvāsyācaturdaśīpaurṇamāsyaṣṭamīṣu ca na adhīyīta | aṣṭakānām uttaratrābhidhānād ihāmāvāsyādisāhacaryād aṣṭakāgrahaṇam aṣṭamyartham
|| 4.113 ||
amāvāsyā guruṃ hanti śiṣyaṃ hanti caturdaśī |
brahmāṣṭakāpaurṇamāsyau tasmāt tāḥ parivarjayet || 4.114 ||
yasmāt amāvāsyeti | amāvāsyā guruṃ hanti śiṣyaṃ ca caturdaśī hanti vedaṃ cāṣṭamīpaurṇamāsyau vismāryataḥ | tasmād etāsu nityam adhyayanaṃ pariharet || 4.114 ||
pāṃsuvarṣe diśāṃ dāhe gomāyuvirute tathā |
śvakharoṣṭre ca ruvati paṅktau ca na paṭhed dvijaḥ || 4.115 ||
pāṃsuvarṣa iti | rajovarṣe digdāhe ca yatra jvalitā iva diśo lakṣyante | śṛgālaśvakharavirute paṅktyākāreṇa copaviśya dvijo na paṭhet || 4.115 ||
nādhīyīta śmaśānānte grāmānte govraje ‘pi vā |
vasitvā maithunaṃ vāsaḥ śrāddhikaṃ pratigṛhya ca || 4.116 ||
nādhīyīteti | “śmaśne sarvataḥ śamyāprāsān nādhīyīta” ity āpastambasmaraṇāt tathāvidhaśmaśānasamīpagoṣṭhayoś ca strīsaṃprayogaprāvṛtaṃ ca vāsaḥ prāvṛtya śrāddhārthaṃ (cā) kṛtānnādyarthaṃ pratigṛhya nādhīyīta || 4.116 ||
prāṇi vā yadi vāprāṇi yat kiṃcic chrāddhikaṃ bhavet |
tadālabhyāpy anadhyāyaḥ pāṇyāsyo hi dvijaḥ smṛtaḥ || 4.117 ||
gohiraṇyādi yat kiṃcit śrāddhārthaṃ tat spṛṣṭvāpy anadhyāyaḥ | kim uta pratigṛhya | yasmāt pāṇir evāsyam asyeti pāṇyāsyo brāhmaṇaḥ || 4.117 ||
caurair upaplute grāme saṃbhrame cāgnikārite |
ākālikam anadhyāyaṃ vidyāt sarvādbhuteṣu ca || 4.118 ||
caurair iti | caurair upaplute grāme agnidāhakṛte ca bhaye divyāntarikṣabhaumeṣu cāśvaryarūpeṣu utpāteṣu rudhiravarṣaṇādiṣu ākālikam anadhyāyaṃ jānīyāt || 4.118 ||
upākarmaṇi cotsarge trirātraṃ kṣepaṇaṃ smṛtam |
aṣṭakāsu tv ahorātram ṛtvantāsu ca rātriṣu || 4.119 ||
upākarmaṇīti | upākarmotsargākhyayoḥ gṛhyoktakarmaṇoḥ kṛtayoḥ trirātram smṛtam | tatrotsarge pakṣiṇy ahorātrābhyām uktābhyāṃ saha anena vikalpaḥ | ūrdhvam āgrahāyaṇyāḥ kṛṣṇapakṣāṣṭamīṣu tisṛṣu catasṛṣu vā ahorātram anadhyāyaḥ | aṣṭamīṣu punaḥ yāvad aṣṭamīty evam artham | tathā ca tatra “aṣṭakāsu nādhīyīta” ity uktam | iha punaḥ “aṣṭakāsu tv ahorātram” iti yat tu punarvacanaṃ nityārtham ity āhus tad asat | tatrāpi “amāvāsyā guruṃ hanti” ity arthavādena nityatvasya samarthitatvāt ṛtvantareṣu cāhorātram anadhyāyaḥ || 4.119 ||
nādhīyītāśvam ārūḍho na vṛkṣaṃ na ca hastinam |
na nāvaṃ na kharaṃ noṣṭraṃ neriṇastho na yānagaḥ || 4.120 ||
nādhīyīteti | aśvavṛkṣahastinaukharoṣṭrārūḍhaḥ tathoṣarapradeśasthaḥ gantryādibhir yānena ca gacchan nādhīyīta || 4.120 ||
na vivāde na kalahe na senāyāṃ na saṅgare |
na bhuktamātre nājīrṇe na vamitvā na śuktake || 4.121 ||
na vivāda iti | vyākrośāyāṃ pravṛttāyāṃ daṇḍādaṇḍi vā yadotpannaṃ bhavati apravṛttayuddhāyāṃ vā senāyāṃ pravṛttayuddhe vā sthitaḥ bhojanasamanantaraṃ ca “yāvad ārdrapāṇiḥ” iti vasiṣṭhasmaraṇāt | aparedyuś cājīrṇe vamitvā cānnāni śuktake vodgāre nādhīyīta
|| 4.121 ||
atithiṃ cānanujñāpya mārute vāti vā bhṛśam |
rudhire ca srute gātrāc chastreṇa ca parikṣate || 4.122 ||
atithim iti | atithiḥ adhīṣvety evaṃ yāvad ananujñāpitaḥ, vāyau cādhyayanaśrameṇātikupyati rudhire ca śrīrāt srute, śastreṇa cārudhirotpāditāpi kṣte utpanne nādhīyīta || 4.122 ||
sāmadhvanāv ṛgyajuṣī nādhīyīta kadācana |
vedasyādhītya cāpy antam āraṇyakam adhītya ca || 4.123 ||
sāmadhvanāv iti | sāmadhvanau śūyamāṇe ṛgyajuṣor na kadācid apy adhyayanaṃ kuryāt | tathā vedaṃ samāpya āraṇyākhyaṃ ca vedaikadeśaṃ adhītya tasminn ahorātre anyat nādhīyīta || 4.123 ||
ṛgvedo devadaivatyo yajurvedas tu mānuṣaḥ |
sāmavedaḥ smṛtaḥ pitryas tasmāt tasyāśucir dhvaniḥ || 4.124 ||
ṛgveda iti | devā devatā yasyāsau devadaivatya ṛgvedaḥ | manuṣyadevatākatvāt yajurvedo mānuṣaḥ | sāmavedaḥ pitṛdevatākaḥ smṛtaḥ | pitryaṃ karma kṛtvā ācamyate | tasmāt tasyāśucir iti dhvaniḥ | atas tasmin śrūyamāṇe ṛgyajuṣī nādhyeye iti pūrvavidhyarthavādaḥ || 4.124 ||
etad vidanto vidvāṃsas trayīniṣkarṣam anvaham |
kramataḥ pūrvam abhyasya paścād vedam adhīyate || 4.125 ||
etad devapitṛmanuṣyādau devatvaṃ (-tvāt kathaṃ) vedatrayasya (jānantaḥ) nānāśastrajñā ṛgyajuḥsāmātmikāyās trayyāḥ sārabhūtaṃ praṇavavyāhṛtisāvitryākhyaṃ pratyahaṃ praṇavavyāhṛtisāvitryātmakena dvitīyādhyāyoktakrameṇa prathamaṃ abhyasya paścād vedam adhīyīyate | yathānadhyāyeṣu nādhyayanaṃ evaṃ praṇavādyabhyāsaṃ vinā nādhyeyam ity etad asyeha punarvacanam || 4.125 ||
paśumaṇḍūkamārjāraśvasarpanakulākhubhiḥ |
antarāgamane vidyād anadhyāyam aharniśam || 4.126 ||
paśumaṇḍūkamārjāraśvasarpanakulākhubhir iti | śiṣyopādhyāyayor adhīyānayoḥ gavādipaśumaṇḍūkabiḍālaśvasarpanakulamūṣakaiḥ madhyagamane kṛte ahorātram anadhyāyaṃ jānīyat || 4.126 ||
dvāv eva varjayen nityam anadhyāyau prayatnataḥ |
svādhyāyabhūmiṃ cāśuddham ātmānaṃ cāśuciṃ dvijaḥ || 4.127 ||
vidyānaipuṇakāmaṃ prati pūrvoktānadhyāyavikalpārtham āha dvāv iti | adhyayanabhūmim aśuddhāṃ ātmānaṃ cāśucim apekṣya dvau vānadhyāyau | tāv eva prayatnena nityaṃ dvijo varjayet | tābhyāṃ (mantro ‘pi) anyatrāpi yatra nityagrahaṇam arthavādādi vā nityatve liṅgam asti tathāpi nityam eva varjayet | anyatra tu vikalpaḥ || 4.127 ||
amāvāsyām aṣṭamīṃ ca paurṇasāsīṃ caturdaśīm |
brahmacārī bhaven nityam apy ṛtau snātako dvijaḥ || 4.128 ||
amāvāsyām iti | amāvāsyādiṣu snātako dvijaḥ ṛtāv api nityaṃ striyaṃ nopeyāt | “parvavarjaṃ vrajec cainām” ity anena paunarukthyaṃ tatraiva parihṛtam | snātakavrataprāyaścittāṅgatvenāsyehābhidhānam || 4.128 ||
na snānam ācared bhuktvā nāturo na mahāniśi |
na vāsobhiḥ mahājasraṃ nāvijñāte jalāśaye || 4.129 ||
na snānam iti | bhuktvā nityaṃ yādṛcchikaṃ snānaṃ na kuryāt | naimittikaṃ punaḥ “muhūrtam api śaktiviṣaye nāpramattaḥ syāt” ity āpastambasmaraṇān na niṣidhyate | tathā vyādhito naimittikam api snānaṃ na kuryāt, api yathāsaṃbhavaṃ mantravan mārjanahiraṇyodakābhyukṣaṇagosparśanādīn snānapratinidhīn paṭaparivartanaṃ ca kuryāt |
mahāniśā tu vijñeyā madhyasthaṃ praharadvayam |
tasyāṃ snānaṃ na kurvīta kāmyanaimittikād ṛte ||
iti devalasmaraṇāt tatra na snāyāt | bahuvāsaskaś ca satataṃ na snāyāt | naimittike tv aniṣedhaḥ | gādhāgādhagrāhādyākīrṇatayā vā ajñāte jalādhāre na snāyāt || 4.129 ||
devatānāṃ guro rājñaḥ snātakācāryayos tathā |
nākrāmet kāmataś chāyāṃ babhruṇo dīkṣitasya ca || 4.130 ||
devatānām iti | devatānāṃ citrapustakādikṛtānāṃ guroḥ pitrādeḥ nṛpateḥ snātakācāryasya ca prādhānyād gurutaḥ pṛthagupadiṣṭasya kapilasya yajñārthaṃ kṛtadīkṣasyecchātaḥ chāyāṃ nākrāmet || 4.130 ||
madhyaṃdine ‘rdharātre ca śrāddhaṃ bhuktvā ca sāmiṣam |
saṃdhyayor ubhayoś caiva na seveta catuṣpatham || 4.131 ||
madhaṃdina iti | madhyāhnārdharātrasaṃdhyāsu samāṃsaṃ ca śrāddhaṃ bhuktvā catuṣpathe ciraṃ na tiṣṭhet || 4.131 ||
udvartanam apasnānaṃ viṇmūtre raktam eva ca |
śleṣmaniṣthyūtavāntāni nādhitiṣṭhet tu kāmataḥ || 4.132 ||
udvartanam iti | udvartanaṃ snānodakamūtrapurīṣarudhiraśleṣmavāntāni icchāto nākrāmet tiṣṭhyūtaṃ ca tāmbūlādyaṃ śleṣmarūpam api || 4.132 ||
vairiṇaṃ nopaseveta sahāyaṃ caiva cairiṇaḥ |
adhārmikaṃ taskaraṃ ca parasyaiva ca yoṣitam || 4.133 ||
vairiṇam iti | śatrutanmitrādharmaśīlavauraparadānān nopaseveta | caurasyātyantakutsitatvāt adhārmikāt pṛthakgrahaṇam | śatrusevanādau ca yady api dṛṣṭa eva doṣaḥ tathāpi śāstraniṣedhasāmarthyād adṛṣṭo ‘pi bhavatīty avasīyate | evam anyatrāpi vijñeyam || 4.133 ||
na hīdṛśam anāyuṣyaṃ loke kiṃcana vidyate |
yādṛśaṃ puruṣasyeha paradāropasevanam || 4.134 ||
na hīdṛśam iti | yasmāt puṃsa evaṃvidham āyuḥkṣayakaraṃ loke anyan na kiṃcid asti yādṛk loke paradāragamanam, tasmād etan na kāryam || 4.134 ||
kṣatriyaṃ caiva sarpaṃ ca brāhmaṇaṃ ca bahuśrutam |
nāvamanyeta vai bhūṣṇuḥ kṛśān api kadācana || 4.135 ||
kṣatriyaṃ caiveti | yo bhavanaśīlaḥ kṣatriyasarpavidvadbrāhmaṇān tadānīṃ durbalān api kadācin nāvajānīyāt || 4.135 ||
etat trayaṃ hi puruṣaṃ nirdahed avamānitam |
tasmād etat trayaṃ nityaṃ nāvamanyeta buddhimān || 4.136 ||
etat trayam iti | yasmād etat trayam avajñātaṃ puruṣaṃ vināśayati | kṣatriyasarpau dṛṣṭaśaktyā brāhmaṇas tv abhicārādinā | tasmāt praśastabuddhiḥ etat trayaṃ sarvadā na tiraskuryāt || 4.136 ||
nātmānam avamanyeta pūrvābhir asamṛddhibhiḥ |
ā mṛtyoḥ śriyam anvicchen naināṃ manyeta durlabhām || 4.137 ||
nātmānam iti | prathamotpannābhir asaṃpaddhiḥ ātmānaṃ nāvajānīyāt | api tu ā mṛtyoḥ śryartham udyamaṃ kuryāt, na durlabhā śrīr iti budhyeta || 4.137 ||
satyaṃ brūyāt priyaṃ brūyān na brūyāt satyam apriyam |
priyaṃ ca nānṛtaṃ brūyād eṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ || 4.138 ||
satyam iti | kenacit pṛṣṭaḥ san satyam eva brūyāt | satyaṃ priyaṃ cāpy apṛṣṭo ‘pi putrajanmādi kathayet | apriyaṃ punaḥ satyaṃ bandhuviyogādi na vadet, api tu satyāṃ gatau tūṣṇīm āsīta | priyam api cāsatyaṃ nāpi(bhi) brūyāt ity eṣa nityo dharmaḥ || 4.138 ||
bhadraṃ bhadram iti brūyād bhadram ity eva vā vadet |
śuṣkavairaṃ vivādaṃ ca na kuryāt kenacit saha || 4.139 ||
bhadram iti | “abhadraṃ bhadram” iti gautamasmaraṇād iha vṛttavaśāt prayuktam | tatrādhyāhāreṇa vyākhyeyam akalyānarūpaṃ yat tat bhadraṃ kalyāṇaṃ śreya ity evamādibhiḥ śabdaiḥ itikaraṇasya prakārārthatvāt brūyāt | bhadram ity anenaivaṃ vāśabdena tat | vādānimittau vairakalahau cāsamarthenāpi saha naiva kuryāt || 4.139 ||
nātikalyaṃ nātisāyaṃ nātimadhyaṃdine sthite |
nājñātena samaṃ gacchen naiko na vṛṣalaiḥ saha || 4.140 ||
nātikalyam iti | uṣaḥpradoṣayoḥ madhyāhne ca jāte avijñātena puruṣeṇa śūdraiś cādhvānaṃ na gacchet | “naikaḥ prapadyetādhvānam” ity ukte ‘pīha eka it punarvacanaṃ prāyaścittaviśeṣārtham || 4.140 ||
hīnāṅgān atiriktāṅgān vidyāhīnān vayo’tigān |
rūpadraviṇahīnāṃśca jātihīnāṃś ca nākṣipet || 4.141 ||
hīnāṅgān iti | hīnāṅgādhikāṅgamūrkhātivṛddhavirūpadaridravaidhaveyādīn kāṇādiśabdāhvānādinā nopahaset || 4.141 ||
na spṛśet pāṇinocchiṣṭo vipro gobrāhmaṇānalān |
na cāpi paśyed aśuciḥ svastho jyotirgaṇān divi || 4.142 ||
na spṛśed iti | ācamanādyarho brāhmaṇaḥ gobrāhmaṇāgnīn na spṛśet | tathāśuciḥ san anāturo dyulokagatam ādityādijyotiḥsamūham api na paśyet || 4.142 ||
spṛṣṭvaitān aśucir nityam adbhiḥ prāṇān upaspṛśet |
gātrāṇi caiva sarvāṇi nābhiṃ pāṇitalena tu || 4.143 ||
spṛṭveti | aśuciḥ san gavādīn spṛṣṭvā adbhiḥ prāṇāyatanāni cakṣurādīny aṅgāni ca sarvāṇi spṛśet “nābhim adbhir hastamadhyena spṛśet” iti prakaraṇapaṭhitaṃ prāyaścittaṃ lāghavārtham | tatra hi kriyamāṇe gavādisparśe kartavyaṃ syāt || 4.143 ||
anāturaḥ svāni khāni na spṛśed animittataḥ |
romāṇi ca rahaḥsthāni sarvāṇy eva vivarjayet || 4.144 ||
anātura iti | svasthaḥ san svāni chidrāṇi romāṇi ca pāyūpasthagatāni animittaṃ na spṛśet || 4.144 ||
maṅgalācārayuktaḥ syāt prayatātmā jitendriyaḥ |
japec ca juhuyāc caiva nityam agnim atandritaḥ || 4.145 ||
maṅgalācārayukta iti | abhipretasiddhirūpamaṅgalahetutvāt gorocanācandanādau maṅgale pratyutthānādau ācāre yatnavān śuddhātmā vaśīkṛtajihvādīndriyaḥ syāt, analasaś ca san japahomau nityaṃ kuryāt | atra ācārādīnām uktānām api vinipātavinivṛttyarthaṃ vivakṣayā punar abhidhānam || 4.145 ||
maṅgalācārayuktānāṃ nityaṃ ca prayatātmanām |
japatāṃ juhvatāṃ caiva vinipāto na vidyate || 4.146 ||
yathā cāha maṅgalācārayuktānām iti | maṅgalādyanuṣṭhānaśīlānāṃ śuddhānāṃ japahomaparāyaṇānāṃ daivapuruṣakṛta upadravo na vidyate || 4.146 ||
vedam eva japen nityaṃ yathākālam atandritaḥ |
taṃ hy asyāhuḥ paraṃ dharmam upadharmo ‘nya ucyate || 4.147 ||
vedam eva japen nityam iti | yasmin yasmin kāle dṛṣṭārthakarmoparodho bhavati tatra nityaṃ vedam evānalasaḥ san japaet | yasmāt taṃ brahmaṇasya paraṃ dharmaṃ manvādaya āhuḥ | tadanyas tv amukhyo dharmas tair ucyate || 4.147 ||
vedābhyāsena satataṃ śaucena tapasaiva ca |
adroheṇa ca bhūtānāṃ jātiṃ smarati paurvikīm || 4.148 ||
uktasyāpi vedābhyāsasya yad arthaṃ punar abhidhānaṃ tad āha vedābhyāseneti | nityaṃ vedābhyāsaśaucatapo’hiṃsābhiḥ pūrvabhavasya janma smarati || 4.148 ||
paurvikīṃ saṃsmaran jātiṃ brahmaivābhyasyate dvijaḥ |
brahmābhyāsena cājasram anantaṃ sukham aśnute || 4.149 ||
tataḥ kim ata āha paurvikīm iti | pūrvajātiṃ svāṃ saṃsmaran īdṛg ayaṃ vedābhyāsaḥ yena janmāntaraṃ smaryata iti matvā abhīkṣṇaṃ vedābhyāsam eva karoti tena cānavaratam ācaryamāṇe vedārthāvabodhajāte sati brahmaprāptau paramātmarūpam akṣayaṃ sukhaṃ prāpnoti || 4.149 ||
sāvitrān śāntihomāṃś ca kuryāt parvasu nityaśaḥ |
pitṝṃś caivāṣṭakāsv arcen nityam anvaṣṭakāsu ca || 4.150 ||
sāvitrān iti | savitṛdevatākān aniṣṭanivṛttyarthāṃś ca homān pakṣasaṃdhiṣu amāvāsyādiṣu ca “ājyaṃ havir anādeyam” iti sūtrakārasmaraṇāt ājyena nityaṃ kuryāt | tathā aṣṭakākhyena karmaṇā aṣṭakāpaścādbhāvinīṣu ca navamīṣu anvaṣṭakākhyena karmaṇā pramītān pitrādīn tarpayet || 4.150 ||
dūrād āvasathān mūtraṃ dūrāt pādāvasecanam |
ucchiṣṭānnaniṣekaṃ ca dūrād eva samācaret || 4.151 ||
dūrād iti | “kṣipraṃ gatvā kṣepamātraṃ śarasya” iti purāṇasmaraṇad evaṃvidhāt gṛhasya dūrāt mūtrapurīṣapādadhāvanodakocchiṣṭapariṣekodakādy utsṛjet || 4.151 ||
maitraṃ prasādhanaṃ snānaṃ dantadhāvanam añjanam |
pūrvāhṇa eva kurvīta devatānāṃ ca pūjanam || 4.152 ||
maitram iti | maitraṃ pāyur iti paśvaṅgasaṃstave pāyau mitradevatākathanaśravaṇād ihāpy abhedopacāreṇa maitraḥ pāyuḥ | tatra bhavaḥ utsarge sati śaucaṃ tathānulepanādiśarīrasaṃskāranityasnānadantadhāvanāñjanadevārcanāni pūrvāhṇa eva kurvīta || 4.152 ||
daivatāny abhigacchet tu dhārmikāṃś ca dvijottamān |
īśvaraṃ caiva rakṣārthaṃ gurūn eva ca parvasu || 4.153 ||
daivatāny abhigacched iti | daivatāni suvarṇamaṇyādikṛtāni dharmapradhānāṃś ca brāhmaṇān pālanasamarthaṃ ca rājādikaṃ rakṣārthaṃ gurūṃś cāpi pitrādīn amāvāsyādiṣu pakṣasaṃdhiṣu ca draṣṭuṃ gacchet || 4.153 ||
abhivādayed vṛddhāṃś ca dadyāc caivāsanaṃ svakam |
kṛtāñjalir upādīta gacchataḥ pṛṣṭhato ‘nviyāt || 4.154 ||
abhivādayed iti | gṛhāgatān (gatā) jyāyaso (jyāyāṃso) ‘bhivādayet teṣāṃ cātmīyam āsanam upaveśanārthaṃ dadyāt | baddhāñjaliṃś ca tatsamīpam āsīta | gacchataścb ca paścād anuvrajet | uktam apīdam abhivādanādi vakṣyamāṇaphalābhidhitsayā punar ucyate || 4.154 ||
śrutismṛtyuditaṃ samyaṅ nibaddhaṃ sveṣu karmasu |
dharmamūlaṃ niṣeveta sadācāram atandritaḥ || 4.155 ||
śrutismṛtyuditam iti | śrutismṛtibhyāṃ samyag uktaṃ sveṣu cādhyayanayāgādiṣv aṅgatvena saṃbaddhaṃ dharmasya mūlabhūtaṃ sādhvācāram analasaḥ san abhīkṣṇam anutiṣṭhet
|| 4.155 ||
ācārāl labhate hy āyur ācārād īpsitāḥ prajāḥ |
ācārād dhanam akṣayyam ācāro hanty alakṣaṇam || 4.156 ||
yad artham ācārasya punar vacanaṃ tad āha ācārād iti | āyuriṣṭāpatyākṣayadhanāny ācārāt tadanuṣṭhānāl labhate | duṣṭalakṣaṇaṃ ca priyabhāryāvināśasaṃsūcakaṃ tilakādyācāro niṣphalīkarotīty ācāraphalakathanam | na cānityatāāpattiḥ, “sāvitrīmātrasāro ‘pi” (2.118) ityādinā nityatvasya samarpitatvāt || 4.156 ||
durācāro hi puruṣo loke bhavati ninditaḥ |
duḥkhabhāgī ca satataṃ vyādhito ‘lpāyur eva ca || 4.157 ||
durācāra iti | yasmād durācāraḥ puruṣo loke ca garhitaḥ sarvadā ca duḥkhito vyādhito ‘lpāyur bhavati, tasmāt svācāreṇa bhāvyam || 4.157 ||
sarvalakṣaṇahīno ‘pi yaḥ sadācāravān naraḥ |
śraddadhāno ‘nasūyaś ca śataṃ varṣāṇi jīvati || 4.158 ||
sarvalakṣaṇahīna iti | yaḥ samyag ācāravān śraddadhāno ‘kutsakaḥ sa ājānubāhutvādisarva-praśastalakṣaṇaśūnyo ‘pi varṣaśataṃ jīvati | prathame ‘dhyāye śāstrastutyarthā ācārastutiḥ | iha tv ācāraphalasamarpaṇārtham etat || 4.158 ||
yad yat paravaśaṃ karma tat tad yatnena varjayet |
yad yad ātmavaśaṃ tu syāt tat tat seveta yatnataḥ || 4.159 ||
yad yat paravaśam iti | yad yat parāyattam ayācitalābhādinā viśeṣadānādi karma tat tad yatnato varjayet | yad yat punar ātmādhīnaṃ svadhanaleśenāpi kenacit svalābhaparopakārādinā tat tat yatnena abhyaset || 4.159 ||
sarvaṃ paravaśaṃ duḥkhaṃ sarvam ātmavaśaṃ sukham |
etad vidyāt samāsena lakṣaṇaṃ sukhaduḥkhayoḥ || 4.160 ||
yasmāt sarvam iti | yat paraprārthanādisaṃsādhyaṃ tat sarvaṃ duḥkhahetu | yac cātmaikasādhanaṃ tat sarvaṃ sukhanimittam ity etat saṃkṣepataḥ sukhaduḥkhalakṣaṇaṃ jānīyāt || 4.160 ||
yat karma kurvato ‘sya syāt paritoṣo ‘ntarātmanaḥ |
tat prayatnena kurvīta viparītaṃ tu varjayet || 4.161 ||
aśiṣṭāpratiṣiddhaṃ vaikalpikaṃ vā | yat karmeti | yat karma anutiṣṭhataḥ puṃso ‘ntarāmanaḥ paritoṣo bhavati tat yatnataḥ kurvīta | ātmatuṣṭikaraṃ punar varjayet || 4.161 ||
ācāryaṃ ca pravaktāraṃ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ gurum |
na hiṃsyād brāhmaṇān gāś ca sarvāṃś caiva tapasvinaḥ || 4.162 ||
ācāryaṃ ceti | ācāryam adhyāpayitāraṃ copanayanamātrakāriṇaṃ lokavat pitaraṃ mātaraṃ gurum “alpaṃ vā bahu vā yasya” (2.149) ity uktam gobrāhmaṇataponiṣṭhāṃś ca ātatāyino ‘pi na hiṃsyāt | anyathā sāmānyahiṃsāniṣedhe sati idaṃ pṛthagvacanam anarthakaṃ syāt || 4.162 ||
nāstikyaṃ vedanindāṃ ca devatānāṃ ca kutsanam |
dveṣaṃ stambhaṃ ca mānaṃ ca krodhaṃ taikṣṇyaṃ ca varjayet || 4.163 ||
nāstikyam iti | dveṣaṃ stambhaṃ ca mānaṃ ca krodhaṃ taikṣṇyaṃ ca varjayet | nāsty adṛṣṭam ity eva buddhitvam, vedadevatāgarhaṇaṃ mātsaryānamratvādāv abhimānakrodhakrauryāṇi varjayet || 4.163 ||
parasya daṇḍaṃ nodyacchet kruddho nainaṃ nipātayet |
anyatra putrāc chiṣyād vā. śiṣṭyarthaṃ tāḍayet tu tau || 4.164 ||
parasyeti | parasya daṇḍādi nodguryet kruddhaś ca san ātmanaḥ putraśiṣyabhāryākanīyānsodaryabhrātṛdāsān vakṣyamānān varjayitvā na prahāreṇa | tān punar anuśāsanārthaṃ “rajjvā veṇudalena vā” ityādiprakāreṇa tāḍayet || 4.164 ||
brāhmaṇāyāvaguryaiva dvijātir vadhakāmyayā |
śataṃ varṣāṇi tāmisre narake parivartate || 4.165 ||
brāhmaṇāyeti | dvijātir api hananecchayā brāhmaṇasya daṇḍādyudyamyaiva varṣaśataṃ tāmisrākhye narake paribhrāmyati || 4.165 ||
tāḍayitvā tṛṇenāpi saṃrambhān matipūrvakam |
ekaviṃśatim ājātīḥ pāpayoniṣu jāyate || 4.166 ||
tāḍayitveti | krodhena buddhipūrvaṃ tṛṇenāpi prahṛtya ekaviṃśatijanmāni pāpanimittāsu śvādiyoniṣu jāyate | ājātīr ity āṅ pādapūraṇaḥ || 4.166 ||
ayudhyamānasyotpādya brāhmaṇasyāsṛg aṅgataḥ |
duḥkhaṃ sumahad āpnoti pretyāprājñatayā naraḥ || 4.167 ||
ayudhyamānasyeti | yuddhaṃ vinā brāhmaṇaśarīrato maurkhyāt rudhiraṃ nara utpādya paraloke mahad duḥkhaṃ prāpnoti || 4.167 ||
śoṇitaṃ yāvataḥ pāṃśūn saṃgṛhṇāti mahītalāt |
tāvato ‘bdān amutrānyaiḥ śoṇitotpādako ‘dyate || 4.168 ||
śoṇitam iti | prahṛtabrāhmaṇotpannaṃ rudhiraṃ kṣitipṛṣṭhān yatsaṃkhyākān rajaḥkaṇān piṇḍīkaroti tatsaṃkhyākāni varṣāṇi rudhirasyotpādakaḥ paraloke śvādibhiḥ bhakṣyate || 4.168 ||
na kadācid dvije tasmād vidvān avagūred api |
na tāḍayet tṛṇenāpi na gātrāt srāvayed asṛk || 4.169 ||
na kadācid iti | yat evaṃ brāhmaṇāvagoraṇādinā bhavati tasmāt śāstravin na kadācid api brāhmaṇasya daṇḍādi udyacchet | nāpi taṃ tṛṇenāpi hanyāt | na ca śarīrād rudhiram utpādayet || 4.169 ||
adhārmiko naro yo hi yasya cāpy anṛtaṃ dhanam |
hiṃsārataś ca yo nityaṃ nehāsau sukham edhate || 4.170 ||
adhārmika iti | yasmād yo naraḥ adharmācaraṇaśīlo yasya vā asatyam eva dhanaṃ tat prādhānyena vyavahārākhyaṃ sarvadā hiṃsānirataḥ sa iha loke ‘pi na sukhaṃ vardhate | tasmād etan na kāryam || 4.170 ||
na sīdann api dharmeṇa mano ‘dharme niveśayet |
adhārmikāṇāṃ pāpānām āśu paśyan viparyayam || 4.171 ||
na sīdann iti | yathā dharmaśabdo yāgādau tajjanye cātmasaṃskāre dṛṣṭaprayogaḥ, evam adharmaśabdo ‘pi hiṃsādau tajjanye cātmasaṃskāre dṛṣṭapryogaḥ, ataḥ adhārmikāṇāṃ pāpānām iti evaṃ vyākhyāyate | hiṃsādyadharmacaraṇaśīlānāṃ tajjanyapāpākhyasaṃskārayuktānāṃ ihaloke rājopadravaṃ kṣipraṃ paśyan paraloke ca śāstradṛṣṭyā narakaṃ paśyan dharmānuṣṭhānenātmasaṃyamātiśaye sati avasādaṃ gacchann api manaḥ adharme na niyojayet || 4.171 ||
nādharmaś carato loke sadyaḥ phalati gaur ive |
śanair āvartyamānas tu kartur mūlāni kṛntati || 4.172 ||
nādharma iti | yathā gauḥ vāhanadohanādinā sadyo loke phalati tathādharmaś caritaḥ sado na phalati | kiṃ tarhi | śanaiḥ śanaiḥ kālāntareṇa phaladānonmukhī bhavan kartur mūlānīva kṛntati | kṛttamūlāpādapavat kartāraṃ niaranvayaṃ nāśayati || 4.172 ||
yadi nātmani putreṣu na cet putreṣu naptṛṣu |
na tv eva tu kṛto dharmaḥ kartur bhavati niṣphalaḥ || 4.173 ||
yadīti | yadi svaśarīravyādhyutpādanadvāreṇādharmo na phalati tadā putrapautrarogādyudbhavamukhena phalati | na tv asau kṛtaḥ kadācit kartur niṣphalo bhavati yaiś ca duṣkṛtavaśena vyādhyādyupetair bhavitavyaṃ tathāvidhāḥ tasyātmaduṣkṛtabalāt tadvipattyādidarśanapīḍānubhavārthaṃ putrapautrā iha janmani janmāntare vā jāyante || 4.173 ||
adharmeṇaidhate tāvat tato bhadrāṇi paśyati |
tataḥ sapatnān jayati samūlas tu vinaśyati || 4.174 ||
adharmeneti | adharmeṇa parasvaharaṇādinā saṃprati dhanavṛddhyādiyukto bhavati | tadanantaraṃ taddhanādyupabhogadvāreṇa sukhāny anubhavati, tadanubhāvāc ca sukhitvādinā śatrūn abhibhavati, tato ‘sminn eva janmani sarvanāśaṃ naśyati mṛto vā apunarbhavaṃ narakam upaiti || 4.174 ||
satyadharmāryavṛtteṣu śauce caivāramet sadā |
śiṣyāṃś ca śiṣyād dharmeṇa vāgbāhūdarasaṃyataḥ || 4.175 ||
yata evam ataḥ satyadharmāryavṛtteṣv iti | satyadharmasādhvācāraśauceṣu, sarvadā ratiṃ bhāvayet | śāsanīyāṃś ca bhāryādīn “pṛṣṭhatas tu śarīrasya” ityādiśāstramaryādayā vāgbāhūdarasayamayukto niyamayed ity uktam | punarvacana aśiṣṭānām abhiniveśadārḍhyārtham || 4.175 ||
parityajed arthakāmau yau syātāṃ dharmavarjitau |
dharmaṃ cāpy asukhodarkaṃ lokasaṃkruṣṭam eva ca || 4.176 ||
parityajed iti | saty api ca trivargasya puruṣārthatvābhidhāne partityajed iti | dharmam apy asukhodarkaṃ lokasaṃkruṣṭam eva ca | dharmaviruddhāv arthakāmau asukhottarakālaṃ cāśaktasya tīvratapaścaraṇādirūpaṃ dharmaṃ lokābhiśāpajanakaṃ ca madhuparkagovadhādirūpaṃ nācaret || 4.176 ||
na pāṇipādacapalo na netracapalo ‘nṛjuḥ |
na syād vākcapalaś caiva na paradrohakarmadhīḥ || 4.177 ||
na pāṇipādacapala iti | hastapādacakṣurvāgvyāpārān niṣprayojanān na kuryāt anṛjuś ca krūrakṛt paropaghātakam ayuktabuddhiś ca na syād iti | punar vacanam uktaprayojanam || 4.177 ||
yenāsya pitaro yātā yena yātāḥ pitāmahāḥ |
tena yāyāt satāṃ mārgaṃ tena gacchan na riṣyate || 4.178 ||
dvidhāvākyārthapratibhāne vaikalpikapadārtheṣu vā ya eva pitṛpitāmahādibhiḥ tasyānusṛto mārgaḥ yad eva tair anuṣṭhitaṃ tenaiva (tam eva) sādhumārgam anusaret tad evācaret, tad ācaran dharmeṇa na hiṃsyate || 4.178 ||
ṛtvikpurohitācāryair mātulātithisaṃśritaiḥ |
bālavṛddhāturair vaidyair jñātisaṃbandhibāndhavaiḥ || 4.179 ||
mātāpitṛbhyāṃ jāmībhir bhrātrā putreṇa bhāryayā |
duhitrā dāsavargeṇa vivādaṃ na samācaret || 4.180 ||
ṛtvig iti | mātāpitṛbhyām iti | ṛtvigādibhiḥ saha vākkalahaṃ na kuryāt | purohitaḥ śāntyādikartā | saṃśritā upajīvinaḥ | āturo rogī | jñātayaḥ pitṛpakṣasaṃbandhinaḥ vaivāhyāḥ bāndhavāḥ mātṛpakṣā jāmibhaginīsnuṣādyāḥ | dāsavargo dāsagaṇaḥ || 4.179–180 ||
etair vivādān saṃtyajya sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
etair jitaś ca jayati sarvāṃl lokān imān gṛhī || 4.181 ||
etair iti | ebhiḥ saha vivādān parityajyāsau atikṛtapratanubhiḥ (?) sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate | tathaitair vivādaiḥ nyakkṛtaiḥ imān vakṣyamānān sarvān lokān gṛhasthaḥ svīkaroti || 4.181 ||
ācāryo brahmalokeśaḥ prājāpatye pitā prabhuḥ |
atithis tv indralokeśo devalokasya cartvijaḥ || 4.182 ||
ācārya iti | yata ācāryādayo dṛṣṭyā śaktyā brahmaprajāpatīndradevalokānāṃ prabhavaḥ ataḥ parihṛtatadvivādasya gṛhasthasyaitān lokān vadanti | tadvivādaparityāgena brahmalokādi prāpyata ity etad anayā bhaṅgyā uktam eva (evaṃ) uttaratrāpi vijñeyam || 4.182 ||
jāmayo ‘psarasāṃ loke vaiśvadevasya bāndhavāḥ |
saṃbandhino hy apāṃ loke pṛthivyā mātṛmātulau || 4.183 ||
jāmaya iti | jāmayo ‘psarasāṃ loke prabhavanti bāndhavā vaiśvadevaloke, mātṛmāulau bhūloke || 4.183 ||
ākāśeśās tu vijñeyā bālavṛddhakṛśāturāḥ |
bhrātā jyeṣṭhaḥ samaḥ pitrā bhāryā putraḥ svakā tanuḥ || 4.184 ||
ākāśeśā iti | bālavṛddhasaṃśritāturāḥ antarikṣaloke prabhavanti | jyeṣṭho bhrātā pitṛtulyaḥ | ato ‘sāv api prajāpatiloke prabhuḥ | bhāryāputrau “ardho ha vā eṣa ātmanaḥ yaj jāyā ātmā vaiputranāmāsi” (KauśUp 2.51) iti śruteḥ svam eva śarīram ataḥ ātmanā saha kathaṃ vivadet || 4.184 ||
chāyā svo dāsavargaś ca duhitā kṛpaṇaṃ param |
tasmād etair adhikṣiptaḥ sahetāsaṃjvaraḥ sadā || 4.185 ||
chāyeti | svo dāsavargaḥ nīcair gāmitvāt ātmachāyeva | ato nāsau vivādāspadam, duhitā ca kṛpaṇaṃ kṛpāpātram ata etair ākruṣṭo ‘samtaptaḥ sarvadā kṣamet || 4.185 ||
pratigrahasamartho ‘pi prasaṅgaṃ tatra varjayet |
pratigraheṇa hy asyāśu brāhmaṃ tejaḥ praśāmyati || 4.186 ||
pratigrahasamartham iti | śrutādhyayanādinā pratigraheṇa samartho ‘pi tatra punaḥpravṛttiṃ varjayet | yasmāt pratigraheṇāsyādhyayanādinimittaprabhāvaḥ kṣipram eva pralīyate “yātrāmātraprasiddhyartham” ityādinoktam apy etat tejaḥ praśamaphalakathanārthaṃ punar ucyate || 4.186 ||
na dravyāṇām avijñāya vidhiṃ dharmyaṃ pratigrahe |
prājñaḥ pratigrahaṃ kuryād avasīdann api kṣudhā || 4.187 ||
na dravyāṇām iti | pratigrahamantradravyadevatādikaṃ dharmād anapetaṃ pratigrahavidhānam abuddhvā | kṣudhāvasādaṃ gacchann api na prājñaḥ pratigrahaṃ kuryāt kiṃ punar anāpadi
|| 4.187 ||
hiraṇyaṃ bhūmim aśvaṃ vāsas tilān ghṛtam |
pratigṛhṇann avidvāṃs tu bhasmībhavati dāruvat || 4.188 ||
hiraṇyam iti | śrutādhyayanaśūnyaḥ suvarṇādīn pratigṛhṇan agnisaṃyogena dāruvat bhasmībhavati || 4.188 ||
hiraṇyam āyur annaṃ ca bhūr gauś cāpy oṣatas tanum |
aśvaś cakṣus tvacaṃ vāso ghṛtaṃ tejas tilāḥ prajāḥ || 4.189 ||
tathā cāha hiraṇyam āyur annaṃ ca bhūr gauś cāpy oṣatas tanum iti | hiraṇyānne pratigṛhīte aviduṣaś cāyur dahataḥ gobhūmī ca śarīram | aśvavastraghṛtatilāḥ yathākramaṃ cakṣuścarmatejo’patyāni dahanti | na kevalam avidvān pratigraheṇa sarvadā taṃ dahyate yāvat
|| 4.189 ||
atapās tv anadhīyānaḥ pratigraharucir dvijaḥ |
ambhasy aśmaplaveneva saha tenaiva majjati || 4.190 ||
atapās tv anadhīyāna iti | yas tapaḥsvādhyāyaśūnyaḥ pratigrahe ‘bhilāṣuko bhavati tadātraivānarhapratigrahadānapāpayuktena saha pāṣāṇamayena nāvādina taran udaka iva narake ‘ntardhīyate | dātā hy atra pratigrahasaṃbandhād ajñau dātṛpratīcchakāv iti vakṣyamāṇadarśanāt teneti parāmṛśyate || 4.190 ||
tasmād avidvān bibhiyād yasmāt tasmāt pratigrahāt |
svalpakenāpy avidvān hi paṅke gaur iva sīdati || 4.191 ||
tasmād iti | yasmād asāradravyaprtigrahe ‘pi ajñaḥ kardame gaur iva narake majjati, tasmād avidvān yataḥ kutaścit hiraṇyādivyatiriktapratigrahād api trasyet || 4.191 ||
na vāry api prayacchet tu baiḍālavratike dvije |
na bakavratike pāpe nāvedavidi dharmavit || 4.192 ||
na nārīti | udakaṃ kila sarvadeyaṃ tad api baiḍālavratikādibhyo dharmajño na dadyāt iti atiśayoktyā dravyāntaradānaṃ niṣidhyate, na tu dāridānam, pākhaṇḍyādi lokaviddho 'pi baiḍālavratike dānaniṣedhaḥ tallakṣaṇavivakṣayeha punar ucyate | avidvaddānaniṣedhaḥ punaḥ vidvatsaṃbhave sati avidvaddānapratiṣedhārthaḥ | evaṃ ca “śrotriyāyaiva deyāni” ity etat vidvacchrotriyāsaṃbhave sati draṣṭavyam || 4.192 ||
triṣv apy eteṣu dattaṃ hi vidhināpy arjitaṃ dhanam |
dātur bhavaty anarthāya paratrādātur eva ca || 4.193 ||
triṣv iti | nyāypārjitaṃ dhanaṃ baiḍālavratikādiṣu triṣv api dattaṃ dātṛpratigrahītroḥ paraloke narakapātahetutvāt anarthāya saṃpadyate || 4.193 ||
yathā plavenaupalena nimajjaty udake taran |
tathā nimajjato ‘dhastād ajñau dātṛpratīcchakau || 4.194 ||
yatheti | tathā nimajjato viprāv agñau dātṛpratīcchakau | yathāśmamayena nāvādina taran tenaiva saha (narake) udake ‘ntardhīyate evaṃ dātṛpratigrahītārau pratigrahaśāstrānabhijñau anarhadānapratigrahakartārau narakaṃ yātaḥ | dātā kila pratigrahītṛpotāvataraṇamārgeṇa saṃsārārṇavapāram uttitīrṣuḥ yadi bāliśatayā upaplavaprakhyapratigrahītṛsamavalambanam urarīkaroti tadā pratyuta tenaiva samam agādhodadhyantardhānavedanāsaṃnihitam adhikatarām arthāntaratayānarthāntaram adhikam api majjata ity upamārthaḥ | “atapās tv anadhīyānaḥ” iti pratigrahītṛprādhānyenoktam, idaṃ tu dātṛprādhānyeneti apaunaruktyaṃ (aprasiddhāt) pratiṣiddhād yatnataḥ parihārārtham || 4.194 ||
dharmadhvajī sadālubdhaś chādmiko lokadambhakaḥ |
baiḍālavratiko jñeyo hiṃsraḥ sarvābhisandhakaḥ || 4.195 ||
baiḍālavratikādilakṣaṇam āha dharmadhvajīti | dharmaḥ cihnam iti lokakhyāpanārthaṃ prakāśadeśāvaraṇāt yasya sa dharmadhvajī | lubdhaḥ kṛpaṇaḥ chādmikaḥ gūḍhapāpo lokadaṃbhako vañcakaḥ hiṃsraḥ hiṃsāśīlaḥ sarvābhisaṃdhakaḥ paraguṇāsahanatayā sarvākṣepakaḥ sa biḍālavratena caratīti baiḍālavratiko brāhmaṇo jñeyaḥ || 4.195 ||
adhodṛṣṭir naiṣkṛtikaḥ sarvārthasādhanatatparaḥ |
śaṭho mithyāvinītaś ca bakavratacaro dvijaḥ || 4.196 ||
adhodṛṣṭir naiṣkṛtika iti | adhodṛṣṭir vinītatvakhyāpanārtham | adha eva nirīkṣate | naiṣkṛtikaḥ naiṣṭhuryavyavahārī svārthasādhanatatparaḥ svārthasādhananiṣṭhaḥ śaṭhaḥ parānnasyopekṣakaḥ mithyāvinītaḥ kapaṭe praśrayavān yaḥ sa bakavṛttena carati iti bakavratadharo brāhmaṇaḥ || 4.196 ||
ye bakavratino viprā ye ca mārjāraliṅginaḥ |
te patanty andhatāmisre tena pāpena karmaṇā || 4.197 ||
ya iti | ye brāhmaṇāḥ bakavratabiḍālacihnayuktāḥ tena pāpahetunā karmaṇā andhatāmisrākhye narake patanti || 4.197 ||
na dharmasyāpadeśena pāpaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ caret |
vratena pāpaṃ pracchādya kurvan strīśūdradambhanam || 4.198 ||
na dharmasyeti | pāpaṃ kṛtvā dharmārthavratakhyāpanavyājena dhārmiko ‘yam iti śūdrādimūrkhalokamohanaṃ kurvan tena dharmavratavyājena pāpam avacchādya prāyaścittaṃ na kuryāt || 4.198 ||
pretyeha cedṛśā viprā garhyante brahmavādibhiḥ |
chadmanā caritaṃ yac ca vrataṃ rakṣāṃsi gacchati || 4.199 ||
pretyeti | evaṃvidhā brāhmaṇā jīvantaḥ mṛtāś ca ātmasvarūpanirūpaṇaparaiḥ nindyante | tac ca vyājacaritaṃ vrataṃ rakṣāṃsi gacchati || 4.199 ||
aliṅgī liṅgaveṣeṇa yo vṛttim upajīvati |
sa liṅgināṃ haraty enas tiryagyonau ca jāyate || 4.200 ||
aliṅgīti | abrahmacārī brahmacāryādiveṣeṇa yas tadvṛttyā jīvati sa brahmacāryādīnāṃ yat pāpaṃ tad ātmany āharati | śvādiyonau ca jāyate | tasmād etan na kartavyam || 4.200 ||
parakīyanipāneṣu na snāyāc ca kadācana |
nipānakartuḥ snātvā tu duṣkṛtāṃśena lipyate || 4.201 ||
parakīyanipāneṣv iti | parakīyapuṣkariṇyādiṣu na kadācid api snāyāt | tatra snātvā tat puṣkariṇyādiṣu yat pāpaṃ katuḥ taccaturthāṃśena vakṣyamāṇena saṃbadhyate | dhārmikāṇām api saṃcitakṛtaṃ janmāntarakṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ bhavaty eva | ato na dhārmikanipānasnānena pāpābhāvaprasaṅgaḥ || 4.201 ||
yānaśayyāsanāny asya kūpodyānagṛhāṇi ca |
adattāny upayuñjāna enasaḥ syāt turīyabhāk || 4.202 ||
yānaśayyāsanānīti | yānaśayyāsanāni rathakhaṭvāmañcakādīni kūpārāmagṛhāṇi ca parasya saṃbandhīni adattāny upayuñjānaḥ tatpāpasya cuturbhāgaṃ prāpnoti | atra ca parakīyagrahaṇāt adattagrahaṇāc ca sarvārthotsṛṣṭānujñātasnāne nāsti doṣaḥ || 4.202 ||
nadīṣu devakhāteṣu taḍāgeṣu saraḥsu ca |
snānaṃ samācaren nityaṃ gartaprasravaṇeṣu ca || 4.203 ||
nadīṣv iti | nadīṣv akṛtakeṣu taḍāgeṣu saraḥsu ca mahatsu udakādhāreṣu chidrasrotaḥsu ca sarvadā snānaṃ samācaret anenaiva parakīyanipānavyāvṛttisiddhau ca pṛthagvacanaṃ tadātmīyotsṛṣṭānujñāteṣu snānābhyanujñānārtham, tatrāpīha nityagrahaṇāt prathamaṃ nadyādisnāne yatitavyam | tadabhāve ātmīyādiṣu || 4.203 ||
yamān seveta satataṃ na nityaṃ niyamān budhaḥ |
yamān pataty akurvāṇo niyamān kevalān bhajan || 4.204 ||
yamān iti | hiṃsādayo varjanīyā yamāḥ saṃdhyānuṣṭhānādayaḥ kartavyātmakā niyamāḥ tatra gurulāghavajñaḥ niyamakopenāpi sarvadā yamān anutiṣṭhet | ya yamatyāgena niyamān yasmāt kevanān kartavyarūpān anutiṣṭhet varjanīyān brahmahatyādīn avarjayan patati | niyamānuṣṭhāne ‘py anadhikṛto bhavati | yathoktaṃ gautamena “dvijātikarmabhyo hāniḥ patanam” iti | etac ca yamagauravakhyāpanārtham ucyate, na niyamaniṣedhārtham, teṣāṃ śāstreṇa kartavyatayā coditatvāt || 4.204 ||
nāśrotriyatate yajñe grāmayājikṛte tathā |
striyā klībena ca hute bhuñjīta brāhmaṇaḥ kvacit || 4.205 ||
nāśrotriyatata iti | anadhītavedena prārabdhe yajñe agnīṣomīyād ūrdhvam api brāhmaṇo na bhuñjīta | arthāt (punaḥ) [pūrvam] agnīṣomīyāt “dīkṣitasya kadaryasya” [4.210] ity anenaiva pratiṣedhasiddheḥ | sūtrakāreṇa agnīṣomīyāvadhikaṃ dīkṣitasyābhojyānnatvam uktam | ato “dīkṣitasya kadaryasya” ity ayaṃ pratiṣedhas tadavadhika eva | sadā bahūnāṃ yājakena ṛtvijā striyā vā napuṃsakena vā yatra gṛhe vaiśvadevādihomaḥ kṛtaḥ na tatra kadācid api brāḥmaṇo bhuñjīta || 4.205 ||
aślīlam etat sādhūnāṃ tatra juhvaty amī haviḥ |
pratīpam etad devānāṃ tasmāt tat parivarjayet || 4.206 ||
aślīlam iti | yatra pūrvoktā homaṃ kurvanti tat śiṣṭānāṃ tadaślīkam ayuktaṃ devānāṃ ca pratikūlam | tasmād etān homaṃ na kārayet || 4.206 ||
mattakrudhāturāṇāṃ ca na bhuñjīta kadācana |
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca padā spṛṣṭaṃ ca kāmataḥ || 4.207 ||
mattakruddhāturāṇām iti | klībakruddhavyādhitānnaṃ keśādisakaladvādaśakṛmisaṃsargaduṣṭaṃ pādena cecchātaḥ spṛṣāaṃ na bhuñjīta || 4.207 ||
bhrūṇaghnāvekṣitaṃ caiva saṃspṛṣṭaṃ cāpy udakyayā |
patatriṇāvalīḍhaṃ ca śunā saṃspṛṣṭam eva ca || 4.208 ||
bhrūṇaghnāvekṣitam iti | brahmahādipatitāvekṣitaṃ rajasvalādyaspṛśyaspṛṣṭaṃ pakṣiṇā kākādinā āsvāditaṃ śunā spṛṣṭaṃ na bhuñjīta || 4.208 ||
gavā cānnam upāghrātaṃ ghuṣṭānnaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca viduṣā ca jugupsitam || 4.209 ||
gaveti | gavāghrātaṃ “ko bhuṅkte” iti yadāghuṣya dīyate maṭhabrāhmaṇādisaṃghānnaṃ ca tathā veśyānnaṃ śāstravidā caitat kutsitam iti yad uktaṃ tan nāśnīyāt viśeṣata iti prāyaścittaviśeṣārtham || 4.209 ||
stenagāyanayoś cānnaṃ takṣno vārdhuṣikasya ca |
dīkṣitasya kadaryasya baddhasya nigaḍasya ca || 4.210 ||
stenagāyanayor iti | corageyajīvitakṣakavṛddhyupajīvyannaṃ na bhuñjīta | yajñārthe dīkṣitasya prāg agnīṣomīyāt baddhasya, bandanena vināpy ayonigaḍair nigaḍitasya dattāyonigaḍasya abandhane ‘pi ca || 4.210 ||
abhiśastasya ṣaṇḍhasya puṃścalyā dāmbhikasya ca |
śuktaṃ paryuṣitaṃ caiva śūdrasyocchiṣṭam eva ca || 4.211 ||
abhiśastasyeti | mahāpātakitvena utpannābhiśāpasya, aniścitapāpasyāpi napuṃsakasya puṃścalyā vyabhicāriṇyāḥ agaṇikāyā api, dāmbhikasya vyājadharmacāriṇaḥ annaṃ na bhuñjīta | tathā śuktaṃ yat prātaḥ svarasaṃ kālāntareṇa udakādisaṃsargakālāntarābhyāṃ cāmlībhavati, paryuṣitaṃ ca rātryantaritaṃ śūdrasya cocchiṣṭaṃ nāśnīyāt | śuktaparyuṣitayoḥ abhakṣaṇaṃ pañcame vakṣyati | iha tu snātakavratatvārtham abhidhānam || 4.211 ||
cikitsakasya mṛgayoḥ krūrasyocchiṣṭabhojinaḥ |
ugrānnaṃ sūtikānnaṃ ca paryācāntam anirdaśam || 4.212 ||
cikitsakasyeti | vaidyasya cikitsājīvinaḥ | tathā lubhakānārjavaprakṛtyucchiṣṭādīnām ugrasya kṣatriyeṇa śūdrāyām utpannasya sūtikoddeśena anāśaucibhir api yat kṛtaṃ kṛtāntarācamanaṃ ca tathā anirgatadaśāhikaṃ sūtikānnaṃ pretānnasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt na bhuñjīta | anye tu ugraṃ rājānam āhuḥ | tad asat, yājñavalkyenobhayapāṭhāt || 4.212 ||
anarcitaṃ vṛthāmāṃsam avīrāyāś ca yoṣitaḥ |
dviṣadannaṃ nagaryannaṃ patitānnam avakṣutam || 4.213 ||
anarcitam iti | arcārhasya yad akṛtāraṃ dīyate akṛtadevatādyarcanaṃ yan māsam atithyāder dīyate kalpayituṃ “devān pitṝṃś cārcayitvā” ity evamāder vakṣyamāṇatvāt na bhūjñīta || 4.213 ||
