piśunānṛtinoś cānnaṃ kratuvikrayakasya ca |
śailūṣatunnavāyānnaṃ kṛtaghnasyānnam eva ca || 4.214 ||
piśunānṛtinoś ceti | bhaṣakāsatyavādikratupuṇyavikrayinaṭasaucikānāṃ upakṛtāpakāriṇām annaṃ nāśnīyāt || 4.214 ||
karmārasya niṣādasya raṅgāvatarakasya ca |
suvarṇakartur veṇasya śastravikrayiṇas tathā || 4.215 ||
karmārasyeti | karmārasya niṣādaveṇayoś ca daśamādhyāyavakṣyamāṇayoḥ naṭagāyanavyatiriktasyāpi ca raṅgāvataraṇajīvinaḥ suvarṇakāraśastravikrayiṇoś cānnaṃ nāśnīyāt || 4.215 ||
śvavatāṃ śauṇḍikānāṃ ca cailanirṇejakasya ca |
rajakasya nṛśaṃsasya yasya copapatir gṛhe || 4.216 ||
śvakrīḍimadyavikrayivastradhāvanapaṭarāgakṛnniṣṭhurāṇāṃ jāyājāraś ca yasyāvijñāto gṛhe ‘sti teṣām annaṃ na bhakṣayet || 4.216 ||
mṛṣyanti ye copapatiṃ strījitānāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ |
anirdaśaṃ ca pretānnam atuṣṭikaram eva ca || 4.217 ||
mṛṣyantīti | bhāryājāraṃ ye sahante tadannam abhojyam evaṃ (ca) gṛhād gatāyāṃ jāriṇyām upekṣitāyām api pratiṣedhaḥ | sarvakarmasu stryāyattānāṃ anirgatāśaucaṃ ca mṛtakānnam aprītikaraṃ ca na bhūjñīta || 4.217 ||
rājānnaṃ teja ādatte śūdrānnaṃ brahmavarcasam |
āyuḥ suvarṇakārānnaṃ yaśaś carmāvakartinaḥ || 4.218 ||
rājānnam iti | rājānnaṃ tejo nāśayati ata eva ca rājānnapratiṣedho boddhavyaḥ | “nādyāc chūdrasya pakvānnam” (4.223) adhikulamitraṃ veti śūdrānnapratiṣedhaṃ vakṣyati, atas tadannaṃ adhyayanādinimittaṃ tejo ‘pahanti suvarṇakāracarmachedyanne āyuḥkhyātī harata iti vyatikramaphalakathanam || 4.218 ||
kārukānnaṃ prajāṃ hanti balaṃ nirṇejakasy aca |
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca lokebhyaḥ parikṛntati || 4.219 ||
kārukānnam iti | sūpakārādyannam apatyāni hanti | asyāpy ata eva niṣedhaḥ | carmakārādīnāṃ cānnaṃ gobalīvardanyāyena nirdeśo draṣṭavyaḥ | evam iha śāstre sarvatra vijñeyam | nirṇejakānnaṃ ca karmāntarārjitebhyaḥ svargādilokebhyaḥ ācchinatti || 4.219 ||
pūyaṃ cikitsakasyānnaṃ puṃścalyās tv annam indriyam |
viṣṭhā vārdhuṣikasyānnaṃ śastravikrayiṇo malam || 4.220 ||
pūyam iti | cikitsakānnādanena tathāvidhāyāṃ jātāv utpadyate yatra pūyabhuk bhavati | evaṃ puṃścalyannaṃ śukraṃ vārdhuṣikānnaṃ purīṣaṃ śastravikrayiṇaḥ śukraviṣṭhādivyatiriktaṃ mūtrādimalam || 4.220 ||
ya ete ‘nye tv abhojyānnāḥ kramaśaḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
teṣāṃ tvagasthiromāṇi vadanty annaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ || 4.221 ||
ya iti | sākṣāduktaphalebhyo ye anye krameṇa bhojyānnāḥ kathitāḥ tatsaṃbandhyannaṃ carmāsthiromāṇi praśastabuddhayo vadanti || 4.221 ||
bhuktvāto ‘nyatamasyānnam amatyā kṣapaṇaṃ tryaham |
matyā bhuktvācaret kṛcchraṃ retoviṇmūtram eva ca || 4.222 ||
bhuktveti | eṣāṃ madhyād anyatamasaṃbandhyannam ajñānāt bhuktvā tryaham abhojanaṃ kuryāt | icchātas tu bhuktvā caret prājāpatyaṃ kṛcchram | retoviṇmūtraṃ cāmatyā bhuktvā gautamasamaraṇāt taptakṛcchram ācaret | tathā cāha – “surāyā amatyā pāne payo ghṛtam udakaṃ vāyuṃ pratitryahaṃ taptāni sa taptakṛcchraḥ, tato ‘sya saṃskāraḥ | mūtrapurīṣaretasāṃ prāśane caivam” iti | atra cānyatamasyeti yady api śrutaṃ tathāpi,
svabhāvakālasaṃsarganimittapariṇāmataḥ |
bhāvāśrayapraduṣṭaṃ ca nityam annaṃ vivarjayet ||
iti saptavidhasyātrapaṭhitasyāpi duṣṭasyedam eva prāyaścittam ekaprakaraṇopadeśāt | asmiṃś ca prakaraṇe eṣāṃ prāyaścittāntarānāmnānāt | śuktaparyuṣitayoś ca pañcamādhyāyavakṣyamāṇaniṣedhayor api iha prāyaścittaviśeṣārtham evopādānam ity āhuḥ | tad asat, “evam abhipretam uktvā” ato ‘nyatamaduṣṭam ity evam avakṣyat | apauruṣeyeṣu hi grantheṣu liṅgamātram api vyavasthāhetur bhavati | tāvataiva vaktrāśayānumānāt | ekaprakaraṇopadeśaś ca snātakavratatvakhyāpanārthaḥ | iha prāyaścittān āmnāne ‘pi ca eṣām ekādaśādhyāyoktāni prāyaścittāni bhaviṣyanti | śuktaparyuṣitayoś ceha snātakavratatvārtham evābhidhānam | tasmād yathāśrutam eva tyājyam, tatra svabhāvaduṣṭaṃ mūtrādi, kāladuṣṭaṃ paryuṣitādi, saṃsargaduṣṭaṃ duṣṭānnādi, pariṇāmaduṣṭaṃ śuktādi, bhāvaduṣṭaṃ sadyaḥpīḍitadrākṣārasādi, āśrayaduṣṭaṃ mattādisaṃbandhi | aprakaraṇe ca prāyaścittopadeśo lāghavādyartham, tatra his kiryamāṇe mantrādigrahaṇaṃ kartavyaṃ syāt || 4.222 ||
nādyāc chūdrasya pakvānnaṃ vidvān aśrāddhino dvijaḥ |
ādadītāmam evāsmād avṛttāv ekarātrikam || 4.223 ||
nādyād iti | śrāddhādikriyāśūnyasya sacchūdrasya śāstravit dvijaḥ pakvānnaṃ na bhuñjīta | api tv annāntarābhāve āmam eva na pakvam ekāhorātraparyāptaṃ nādhikam asmāt pratigṛhṇīyāt
|| 4.223 ||
śrotiyasya kadaryasya vadānyasya ca vādhuṣeḥ |
mīmāṃsitvobhayaṃ devāḥ samam annam akalpayan || 4.224 ||
śrotriyasyeti | eko ‘dhītavedaḥ kṛpaṇaḥ para udāro vṛddhijīvī vā tayor guṇam ubhayaṃ vicārya devās tulyam annam anayor iti kalpitavantaḥ, ubhayor api guṇayogāt || 4.224 ||
tān prajāpatir āhaitya mā kṛḍhvaṃ viṣamaṃ samam |
śraddāpūtaṃ vadānyasya hatam aśraddayetarat || 4.225 ||
tān iti | tān devān brahmā āgatya viṣamaṃ yat tat mā samam iti vyācaṣṭa | yasmād vadānyānnaṃ śraddhayā pavirtīkṛtaṃ bhavati | kadaryānnaṃ punaḥ aśraddhayā dūṣitam ity ubhayapratiṣedhe ‘pi śraddhāvad vārdhuṣikānnapratiprasavārtham idam || 4.225 ||
śraddhayeṣṭaṃ ca pūtaṃ ca nityaṃ kuryād atandritaḥ |
śraddhākṛte hy akṣaye te bhavataḥ svāgatair dhanaiḥ || 4.226 ||
yata evam ataḥ śraddayeti || 4.226 ||
dānadharmaṃ niṣeveta nityam aiṣṭikapaurtikam |
parituṣṭena bhāvena pātram āsādya śaktitaḥ || 4.227 ||
dānadharmam iti | āntarvediko bahirvedikaś ca yo dānākhyo dharmaḥ taṃ śrutādhyayanādisarvaguṇopetaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ prāpya paritoṣākhyena cittadharmeṇa yathāśakti nityaṃ kuryāt || 4.227 ||
yat kiṃcid api dātavyaṃ yācitenānasūyatā |
utpatsyate hi tat pātraṃ yat tārayati sarvataḥ || 4.228 ||
yat kiṃcid iti | prārthitena akṛtadoṣavicāreṇa svalpam api yathāsaṃbhavaṃ dātavyam | yasmād anavarataṃ dānapravṛttasya kadācid avaśyaṃ tathāvidhaṃ pātram āgamiṣyati yat dṛṣṭādṛṣṭadoṣebhyo mocayati || 4.228 ||
vāridas tṛptim āpnoti sukham akṣayam annadaḥ |
tilapradaḥ prajām iṣṭāṃ dīpadaś cakṣur uttamam || 4.229 ||
vārida iti | udakapradaḥ sauhityam, annadaḥ anantasukham, tilapradaḥ īpsitāny apatyāni, dīpadaḥ timirādyanupahataṃ cakṣuḥ prāpnoti || 4.229 ||
bhūmido bhūmim āpnoti dīrgham āyur hiraṇyadaḥ |
gṛhado ‘gryāṇi veśmāni rūpyado rūpam uttamam || 4.230 ||
bhūmida iti | bhūmido bhūmyādhipatyaṃ ca suvarṇado dīrgham āyuḥ, gṛhadaḥ śreṣṭhāni gṛhāṇi, rūpyadaḥ utkṛṣṭaṃ rūpaṃ labhate || 4.230 ||
vāsodaś candrasālokyam aśvisālokyam aśvadaḥ |
anaḍuhaḥ śriyaṃ puṣṭāṃ godo bradhnasya viṣṭapam || 4.231 ||
vāsoda iti | vastaradaś candreṇa saha samānalokatāṃ chandrlokaṃ aśvadaḥ aśvibhyāṃ samānalokatāṃ vṛṣapradaḥ mahatīṃ śriyaṃ gopradaḥ ādityalokaṃ prāpnoti || 4.231 ||
yānaśayyāprado bhāryām aiśvaryam abhayapradaḥ |
dhānyadaḥ śāśvataṃ saukhyaṃ brahmado brahmasārṣṭitām || 4.232 ||
yānaśayyāprada iti | khaṭvādiprado bhāryām ārtatrāṇakṛt prabhutvam, dhānyapradaḥ sārvakālikaṃ sukhaṃ vedapradaḥ paraṃ brahmasamānagatitvaṃ prāpnoti | sarvatra cātra deyadravyasārālpatvabhūyastvādyapekṣayā dṛṣṭavatyalpa(dṛṣṭaphalālpa)syālpabahutvakālāditātamyaṃ vijñeyam || 4.232 ||
sarveṣām eva dānānāṃ brahmadānaṃ viśiṣyate |
vāryannagomahīvāsastilakāñcanasarpiṣām || 4.233 ||
sarveṣām iti | jalānnagobhūvastratilasuvarṇaghṛtādīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ yāni dānāni teṣāṃ madhyād vedadānaṃ phalādhikyāt viśiṣyate || 4.233 ||
yena yena tu bhāvena yad yad dānaṃ prayacchati |
tat tat tenaiva bhāvena prāpnoti pratipūjitaḥ || 4.234 ||
yeneti | yādṛśena rājasena sātvikena tāmasena vā śuddhena vā madhyamenāśuddhena vā cittadharmeṇa ca yad yad eva dravyaṃ dadāti tat tad dravyaṃ phaladvāreṇa tu tenaiva dānggatabhāvena bhāvānurūpeṇaiva sukhaṃ madhyamaduḥkhavatā cittena (na) ca janmāntare pūjitaḥ san prāpnoti || 4.234 ||
yo ‘rcitaṃ pratigṛhṇāti dadāty arcitam eva vā |
tāv ubhau gacchataḥ svargaṃ narakaṃ tu viparyaye || 4.235 ||
tasmād yatnataḥ [arcita]kareṇa deyam, ya iti | yāvad arcāpūrvakaṃ dānapratigrahau kurutaḥ tau dvāv api dātṛprtigrahītārau svargaṃ gacchataḥ | anarcitena kareṇa punaḥ narakaṃ vrajataḥ | pratigrahītuḥ saty api vṛttyarthatvena dṛṣṭārthatve pratigrhasya “arcitam eva mayā grāhyaṃ nānyathā” ity evaṃ niyamādṛṣṭadvāreṇa vacanān nāyuktaḥ svargalābhaḥ || 4.235 ||
na vismayeta tapasā vaded iṣṭvā ca nānṛtam |
nārto ‘py apavaded viprān na datvā parikīrtayet || 4.236 ||
na vismayeteti | mahatāpi kṛcchracāndrāyaṇāditapasā kṛtena “mayā kiyat tapaḥ kṛtam” ity evam āścaryaṃ na bhajeta | yāgaṃ ca kṛtvā asatyaṃ na vadet | saty api puruṣārthatvenānṛtaniṣedhe yāgāṅgatvārthaḥ punar upadeśaḥ | tathā pīḍito ‘pi brāhmaṇān na nindet | dānaṃ kṛtvā “idaṃ mayaā dattam” iti na kathayet || 4.236 ||
yajño ‘nṛtena kṣarati tapaḥ kṣarati vismayāt |
āyur viprāpavādena dānaṃ ca parikīrtanāt || 4.237 ||
yasmād yajña iti | yasmāt yajñādīny anṛtādibhiḥ kṛtair akṛtasamāni bhavanti tasmād etan na kartavyam || 4.237 ||
dharmaṃ śanaiḥ saṃcinuyād valmīkam iva puttikāḥ |
paralokasahāyārthaṃ sarvabhūtāny apīḍayan || 4.238 ||
dharmaṃ śanair iti | sarvaprāṇipīḍāṃ pariharet paralokopakārārthaṃ mṛdrāśiṃ yathā pipīlikāḥ saṃcinvanti evaṃ dharmaṃ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ arjayet || 4.238 ||
nāmutra hi sahāyārthaṃ pitā mātā ca tiṣṭhataḥ |
na putradāraṃ na jñātir dharmas tiṣṭhati kevalaḥ || 4.239 ||
nāmutreti | yasmāt paraloke sahāyakāryasampattyarthaṃ mātṛpitṛputrabhāryābāndhavā na tiṣṭhanti | api tu dharma eva kevala upakārārtham āste | tasmān na mātrādyapekṣayā dharmam utsṛjet || 4.239 ||
ekaḥ prajāyate jantur eka eva pralīyate |
eko ‘nubhuṅkte sukṛtam eka eva ca duṣkṛtam || 4.240 ||
eka iti | eka eva san prāṇī utpadyate na bāndhavaiḥ sahaiva | eka eva mriyate | sukṛtaduṣkṛtaphale ca eka eva bhūkkte | ato mātrādīnāṃ jananamaraṇaduṣkṛtapīḍāṃśabhoktṛtvābhāvāt na tadapekṣayā dharmaṃ jahyāt || 4.240 ||
mṛtaṃ śarīram utsṛjya kāṣṭhaloṣṭasamaṃ kṣitau |
vimukhā bāndhavā yānti dharmas tam anugacchati || 4.241 ||
mṛtam iti | mṛtasya śarīraṃ kāṣṭhavad avajñayā bhūmau parityajya parāṅmukhā bāndhavā gacchanti na tatsahāyarthaṃ vartante | dharmaḥ punaḥ taṃ paraloke yāntaṃ paścād gacchati
|| 4.241 ||
tasmād dharmaṃ sahāyārthaṃ nityaṃ saṃcinuyāc chanaiḥ |
dharmeṇa hi sahāyena tamas tarati dustaram || 4.242 ||
tasmād iti | yasmād dharmeṇa sahāyena satā dustaranarakādiduḥkhaṃ tarati tasmāt dharmaṃ sahāyārthaṃ sarvadā śanair jayet || 4.242 ||
dharmapradhānaṃ puruṣaṃ tapasā hatakilbiṣam |
paralokaṃ nayaty āśu bhāsvantaṃ khaśarīriṇam || 4.243 ||
dharmapradhānam iti | dhārmikaṃ puruṣaṃ kathaṃcit pramādakṛte ca vyatikrame prāyaścittenāpagatapāpaṃ svatejaḥpuṃjojjvalitaṃ vicaratvāt brahmavad ākāśātmakaṃ santaṃ prakṛto dharmaḥ kṣipraṃ paralokaṃ nayati | tasmād dharmaṃ śanaiḥ saṃcinuyāt || 4.243 ||
uttamair uttamair nityaṃ (sārdham) saṃbandhān ācaret saha |
ninīṣuḥ kulam utkarṣam adhamān adhamāṃs tyajet || 4.244 ||
uttamar uttamair sārdham iti | kulam utkṛṣṭatāṃ netum icchan śrutābhijanādyutkṛṣṭaiḥ saha anavarataṃ kanyādānādisaṃbandhān ācaret | apakṛṣṭān tatsaṃbandheṣu pariharet | uttamavidhānād evādharmavyāvṛttisiddhaye pṛthagvacanam uttamābhāve samābhyanujñānārtham || 4.244 ||
uttamān uttamān eva gacchan hīnāṃs tu varjayan |
brāhmaṇaḥ śreṣṭhatām eti pratyavāyena śūdratām || 4.245 ||
uttamān uttamān eva gacchan hīnāṃś ca varjayann iti | uttamasaṃbandheṣū uttamānanusaran nikṛṣṭāṃś ca varjayan brāhmaṇaḥ śreṣṭhatvaṃ prāpnoti | viparyayan punaḥ jāter anapāyāt śūdratulyatāṃ prāpnoti || 4.245 ||
dṛḍhakārī mṛdur dāntaḥ krūrācārair asaṃvasan |
ahiṃsro damadānābhyāṃ jayet svargaṃ tathāvrataḥ || 4.246 ||
dṛḍhakārīti | ārabdhasampādanaśaktiḥ akrūraḥ śītātapādi, dvandvasahiṣṇuḥ nirdayācāraiḥ sahāvasan ahiṃsako yaḥ sa evaṃ niyamaḥ sadamenopaśamena indriyasaṃyamākhyena dānena ca svargaṃ prāpnuyāt || 4.246 ||
edhodakaṃ mūlaphalam annam abhyudyataṃ ca yat |
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyān madhv athābhayadakṣiṇām || 4.247 ||
ekodakam iti | kāṣṭhodakaphalamūlākṣikam annaṃ ca ayācitopanatam anāpatprakaraṇāt patitādyatyantakutsitavarjaṃ sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyāt | tathā abhayaṃ cātmaparitrāṇam atyantaprītikaraṃ matvā dakṣiṇātulyam ātmabhayarakṣaṇasamartham iti sarvataś caṇḍālāder apy aṅgīkuryāt || 4.247 ||
āhṛtābhyudyatāṃ bhikṣāṃ purastād apracoditām |
mene prajāpatir grāhyām api duṣkṛtakarmaṇaḥ || 4.248 ||
āhṛtābhyudyatām iti | pratigrahītṛdeśamānītāṃ datuḥ codyamitāṃ “idaṃ tubhyaṃ dāsyāmi” ity evaṃ pūrvam akathitāṃ gohiraṇyādibhikṣāṃ duṣṭakarmakāriṇo ‘pi anāpatprakaraṇāt patitādyatyantakutsitavarjaṃ hiraṇyagarbho grāhyaṃ manyate sma || 4.248 ||
nāśnanti pitaras tasya daśavarṣāṇi pañca ca |
na ca havyaṃ vahaty agnir yas tām abhyavamanyate || 4.249 ||
nāśnantīti | tadīyaṃ kavyaṃ pañcadaśavarṣāṇi pitaro nādanti havyaṃ ca puroḍāśādi devān prati agniḥ na prāpayati yas tāṃ bhikṣāṃ pratyācaṣṭe || 4.249 ||
śayyāṃ gṛhān kuśān gandhān apaḥ puṣpaṃ maṇīn dadhi |
dhānā matsyān payo māṃsaṃ śākaṃ caiva na nirṇudet || 4.250 ||
śayyāṃ gṛham iti | śayanagṛhadarbhodakapuṣparatnadadhidhānāmatsyakṣīramāṃsaśākāni gandhavanti ca karpūrādīni ayācitopasthitāni na pratyācakṣīta | edhodakādivākyapaṭhitasyodakasyeha punaḥ pāṭhaḥ ślokadvayasya paṭhitayoḥ pratyākhyāne tulyatvajñāpanārthaḥ | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “edādīni śayyādibhiḥ saha” ekasmin sūtre saha paṭhitavān || 4.250 ||
gurūn bhṛtyāṃś cojjihīrṣann arciṣyan devatātithīn |
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyān na tu tṛpyet svayaṃ tataḥ || 4.251 ||
gurūn bhṛtyān iti | mātāpitrādīn gurūn bhṛtyān bhāryāputrādīn avaśyaṃbharaṇīyān kṣudhāvasannān uddhartum icchan devatātithīṃś ca pūjayiṣyan anāpatprakaraṇāt patitādyatyantakutsitavarjaṃ sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyāt  na rūpāntarasadbhāve sati tena dhanena ātmanas tṛptiṃ kuryāt | mukhyopāyaparikṣaye hi avṛttikarśitaḥ saṃsīdann ity āpaddhamaṃ vakṣyati || 4.251 ||
guruṣu tv abhyatīteṣu vinā vā tair gṛhe vasan |
ātmano vṛttim anvicchan gṛhṇīyāt sādhutaḥ sadā || 4.252 ||
guruṣv iti | mātrādiṣv atīteṣu tair vā vinā gṛhe vasan ātmajīvam anvicchan anāpadi sarvadā sādhubhya eva pratigṛhṇīyāt || 4.252 ||
ārdhikaḥ kulamitraṃ ca gopālo dāsanāpitau |
ete śūdreṣu bhojyānnā yaś cātmānaṃ nivedayet || 4.253 ||
ārdhika iti | śūdreṣu madhyāt karṣakakulamitragopāladāsanāpitā bhojyānnāḥ | yaś ca tvadāśrito ‘ham ity evam ātmānaṃ samarpayet | śūdraś ca nāpitādikarmaṇā jīvet kārukakarmabhiḥ iti vakṣyati || 4.253 ||
yādṛśo ‘sya bhaved ātmā yādṛśaṃ ca cikīrṣitam |
yathā copacared enaṃ tathātmānaṃ nivedayet || 4.254 ||
yathā ātmanivedanaṃ tena kāryaṃ tathāha yādṛśa iti | yādṛśo ‘sya bhaved ātmā kulaśīlādibhir yādṛśaṃ dṛṣṭārtham adṛṣṭātiśayārthaṃ copacaraṇaṃ kartum iṣṭaṃ yena vā prakāreṇa mṛdudakādyāharaṇādinimittaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ seveta anena prakāreṇātmānaṃ nivedayet || 4.254 ||
yo ‘nyathā santam ātmānam anyathā satsu bhāṣate |
sa pākakṛttamo loke stena ātmāpahārakaḥ || 4.255 ||
yasmāt ya iti | yaḥ kaścit prakṛtāc chūdrād anyo ‘pi kulādibhiḥ anyathā bhūtam ātmānaṃ itthaṃbhūto ‘ham ity anyathā sādhu kathayati sa ātamarūpāpalāpata eva coro loke atiśayena pāpakṛt sarvavastupradhānātmāpahāritvāt || 4.255 ||
vācy arthā niyatāḥ sarve vāṅmūlā vāgviniḥsṛtāḥ |
tāṃ tu yaḥ stenayed vācaṃ sa sarvasteyakṛn naraḥ || 4.256 ||
vācy arthā iti | sarve ‘rthāḥ śabdeṣu saṃbaddhāḥ śabdārthasaṃbandhasya niyatatvāt śabdamūlakāś ca yataḥ śabdebhyaḥ pratīyante śabdapratītipuraḥsaraṃ cārthasaṃvyavahārapratīteḥ śabdaviniyatā eva | atas tāṃ vācaṃ yās corayet, anyad uktvā anyad anutiṣṭhet sa sarvavastusteyakṛn naro bhavati || 4.256 ||
maharṣipitṛdevānāṃ gatvānṛṇyaṃ yathāvidhi |
putre sarvaṃ samāsajya vasen mādhyasthyam āsthitaḥ || 4.257 ||
maharṣipitṛdevānām iti | maharṣipitṛdevānāṃ svādhyāyaprajāyajñasaṃpādanena yathāśāstram ṛṇasaṃśuddhiṃ kṛtvā tataḥ putre sarvāṃ gṛhadhuraṃ ca saṃnyasya dhanārjanāt dānadīnāśritaḥ san “vācy eke juhvati prāṇam” (4.23) ityādinītyā jñānabalān mahāyajñādīn sampādayan gṛha eva kvacit kālaṃ vaset || 4.257 ||
ekākī cintayen nityaṃ vivikte hitam ātmani |
ekākī cintayāno hi paraṃ śreyo ‘dhigacchati || 4.258 ||
tathā ca vasan ekākīti | nirjane pradeśe ekākī san upaniṣaduktopāsanādibhiḥ paramātmasvarūpanirūpaṇam ātmahitaṃ cintayan prakṛṣṭaṃ śreyo mokṣākhyaṃ prāpnoti || 4.258 ||
eṣoditā gṛhasthasya vṛttir viprasya śāsvatī |
snātakavratakalpaś ca sattvavṛddhikaraḥ śubhaḥ || 4.259 ||
eṣeti | eṣā ṛtādikā jīvikā nityā anityāyā āpadi vidhānāt gṛhasthasya brāhmaṇasyoktā | tathā snātakavratavidhiś ca sattvākhyaguṇavṛddhikaraḥ praśastasyaivoktaḥ || 4.259 ||
anena vipro vṛttena vartayan vedaśāstravit |
vyapetakalmaṣo nityaṃ brahmaloke mahīyate || 4.260 ||
aneneti | anena uktaśāstrācāreṇa vedārthavit brāhmaṇaḥ nityakarmānuṣṭhānenopakṣīṇapāpaḥ san brahmaloke mahimānama aśnute || 4.260 ||
iti śrībhaṭṭamādhavātmajagovindarājaviracitāyāṃ
manvāśayasāriṇyāṃ ṭīkāyāṃ gārhasthasadācāraś
caturtho ‘dhyāyaḥ
pañcamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
śrutvaitān ṛṣayo dharmān snātakasya yathoditān |
idam ūcur mahābhāgam analaprabhavaṃ bhṛgum || 5.1 ||
śrutvaitān iti | idam ūcur mahābhāgam iti | etān yathoktān snātakadharmān ṛṣayaḥ śrutvā agner jātaṃ paropakāriṇaṃ bhṛguṃ idaṃ vakṣyamāṇam uktavantaḥ | prathame ‘dhyāye manuprabhavatvaṃ bhṛtor uktam | iha tu śrautārthavādadṛṣṭyāgniprabhavatvam ucyate |  tatra hi “tasya yad retasaḥ prathamaṃ dedīyate tad asāv ādityo ‘bhavat | yad dvitiyam āsīt tad bhṛguḥ” iti śrūyate | ata eva bhraṣṭād retasa utpanna it ibhṛgur ity evaṃ bhavati | evaṃ ca ekasmin janmani tasyaikaṃ kāraṇam anyasmiṃś cāparam iti na kadācid anupapattiḥ || 5.1 ||
evaṃ yathoktaṃ viprāṇāṃ svadharmam anutiṣṭhatām |
kathaṃ mṛtyuḥ prabhavati vedaśāstravidāṃ prabho || 5.2 ||
evam iti | evam atikrāntaṃ śāstroktaṃ svadharmam ācaratāṃ vedaśāstrajñānāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ kathaṃ bhāvyam, āyuḥkṣayakāraṇasya dharmalopāder abhāvāt || 5.2 ||
sa tān uvāca dharmātmā maharṣīn mānavo bhṛguḥ |
śrūyatāṃ yena doṣeṇa mṛtyur viprān jighāṃsati || 5.3 ||
anabhyāsena vedānām ācārasya ca varjanāt |
ālasyād annadoṣāc ca mṛtr viprān jighāṃsati || 5.4 ||
sa tān uvāceti | anabhyāseneti | vedānabhyāsa-ācāralopa-ālasya-annadoṣākhyāyuḥkṣayakāraṇāni, evaṃ ca vedābhyāsācārānuṣṭhānānālasyeṣu uktaśāstraprameyabhūteṣu kṛteṣv api yadā vakṣyamāṇānnadoṣo na parihriyate tadā tena hetubhūtena akālaṃ mṛtyur viprān hantum icchati
|| 5.3–4 ||
laśunaṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva palāṇḍuṃ kavakāni ca |
abhakṣyāṇi dvijātīnām amedhyaprabhavāni ca || 5.5 ||
kaḥ punar asāv annadoṣa ity āśaṅkya tam āha laśunam iti | laśunagṛñjanapalāṇḍvākhyāni śākāni | kavakaṃ chatrākaviśeṣaḥ khukaṃ khaṇḍakam ity āyurvedaprasiddham | amedhyaprabhāni ca manuṣyādijagdhaprabījapurīṣanirgatatvena kevalāmedhyotpattikāni purīṣasthānotpannāni cāmedhyasaṃsargaprabhavāni taṇḍulīyakādīni dvijātīnām abhakṣyāṇi | dvijātigrahaṇaṃ śūdraparyudāsārtham, cāturthyaṃ tu abhakṣyaprakaraṇaṃ brāhmaṇasyaiva “nāśrotriyatate yajñe” (4.205) ity upakramya “bhuñjīta brāhmaṇaḥ kvacit” ity abhidhānāt | ata eva prakaraṇabhedaḥ || 5.5 ||
lohitān vṛkṣaniryāsān vraścanaprabhavāṃs tathā |
śeluṃ gavyaṃ ca peyūṣaṃ prayatnena vivarjayet || 5.6 ||
lohitān yatra puṣpādivyatiriktān prasrāvādinā vṛkṣebhyo nirgatāṃs tathā | vṛkṣe gundapradeśodbhavān śeluṃ ca śleṣmātaṃ “śeluḥ śleṣmātakaḥ smrtaḥ” ity abhidhānakośādyāyurvedaprasiddheḥ | na tu kṣīrasṛtkṣīrasantānikā yathānyair vyākhyātam, asiddheḥ | gosaṃbandhi ca pīyūṣam | navaprasūtāyā goḥ kṣīraṃ yad agnisaṃyogāt kaṭhinībhavati tad yatnato varjayet | etac ca daśāhāt pareṇāpi yadi kadācit syāt tadā niṣidhyate | daśāhonavartinas tu “anirdaśāyā goḥ kṣīram” ity anenaiva niṣiddhatvāt || 5.7 ||
vṛthākṛsarasaṃyāvaṃ pāyasāpūpam eva ca |
anupākṛtamāṃśāni devānnāni havīṃṣi ca || 5.7 ||
vṛthākṛsarasaṃyāvam iti | kṛsaraṃ tilamudgasiddham odanam, saṃvāyaṃ ghṛtaguḍakṣīrādikṛtaṃ puṣkariketi prasiddham, pāyasam apūpaṃ ca vāstuyajñādiviśiṣṭadevatāyāgoddeśam antareṇa kṛtam | kṛtānvāhikavaiśvadevakāryam api na bhakṣayet | tathā upākaraṇākhyaśrautasaṃskāraviśeṣārhaṇāṃ go’vyajānāṃ yāny anupākṛtāni māṃsāni tāni kalpayatā na bhakṣayet | atithyādes tu “anarcitaṃ vṛthāmāṃsam” ity uktvā devānnāni ca nevedakādīni havīṃṣi ca prāgghomāt varjayet | haviḥśeṣasya hi bhakṣyatvaṃ vakṣyati || 5.7 ||
anirdaśāyā goḥ kṣīram auṣṭram ekaśakaṃ tathā |
āvikaṃ sandhinīkṣīraṃ vivatsāyāś ca goḥ payaḥ || 5.8 ||
anirdaśāyācb iti | prasūtāyāś cānirdaśāyā yoḥ saṃbandhi kṣīram | tathā uṣṭrasaṃbandhi aśvādyekaśaphaiḍakasaṃbandhi ca tathā “adugdhyā dohakāleṣ sandhinī sadbhir ucyate” iti trikāṇḍābhidhānāt bhakṣa(?)kṣīrāyā api sandhinyāḥ kṣīram, tathā mṛtavatsāyāś ca goḥ payo varjayet | vatsagrahaṇād ev gavilabdhāyāṃ gogrhaṇaṃ gaur eva, na tv ajāmahiṣyor iti jñāpanārtham | atra kṣīragrahaṇe prakṛte sandhiṇīkṣīraṃ vivatsāyāś ca goḥ paya iti punaḥ kṣīragrahaṇapayograhaṇāc ca sandhinīvivatsāyāś ca kṣīram eva pratiṣidhyate | na tu tadvikārā iti kaścid āha – tad asat, svabhāvāśucino vikārābhyanujñānasyānyāyyatvāt | yathā cāśubhāgneṣu tasya niṣedhadarśanam | śaṅkhaś cāha:
kṣīrāṇi yāny abhakṣyāṇi tadvikārāśane budhaḥ |
saptarātravrataṃ kuryāt prayatnena samāhitaḥ || iti |
sandhinyāś ca taddhitanirdeśasya kṛtatvāc ca kṣīragrahaṇaṃ kartum upayujyate sandhnīkṣīram iti ca samāsāntarvartitvāt kṣīraśabdasya goḥ paya iti payograhaṇam upayujyate || 5.8 ||
āraṇyānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ mṛgāṇāṃ māhiṣaṃ vinā |
strīkṣīraṃ caiva varjyāni sarvaśuktni caiva hi || 5.9 ||
āraṇyānām iti | māhiṣavarjam āraṇyānāṃ sarveṣāṃ hastyādīnāṃ mṛgāṇāṃ saṃbandhi kṣīraṃ straiṇaṃ ca varjayet | tathā yāni prāptasvarasāni kālāntareṇodakādisaṃsargakālaparivāsābhyāṃ cāmlībhavanti tāni sarvāṇi śuktāni varjayet || 5.9 ||
dadhi bhakṣyaṃ ca śūkteṣu sarvaṃ ca dadhisaṃbhavam |
yāni caivābhiṣūyante puṣpamūlaphalaiḥ śubhaiḥ || 5.10 ||
atra pratiprasavam āha dadhi bhakṣyaṃ tu śukteṣu sarvaṃ ca dadhisaṃbhavam iti | śukteṣu madhyād dadhi tatsaṃbhavaṃ tu sarvam udaśvidādi | yāni ca śuddheḥ puṣpādibhir udakena saha sandhīyante tāni bhakṣaṇārhāṇi | ācārasya ca smṛtivākyavyākhyānasthānīyatvāt ya ācārāvirodho sa mstyarthaṃ kalpayituṃ nyāyyaḥ | ataḥ śiṣṭācārānatikrameṇa puṣpādigrahaṇaṃ dravyāntarapradarśanam api vijñeyam | atra cābhiiṣutaśukta(śukla)pratiprasavāt sarvaśuktānīty ayaṃ pratiṣedho ‘py abhiṣutaviṣaya eva | tataḥ svabhāvaśuktānām āmalakādīnām apratiṣedhaḥ iti kecit – tad asat, “dadhi bhakṣyaṃ tu” ity anabhiṣutasyāpi pratiprasavāt | na cāmalakādīnām abhakṣyatāpattiḥ, na hy amlaparyāyaśuktaśabdo ‘pi [tu] yathoktārtham eva || 5.10 ||
kravyādaḥ śakunīn sarvān tathā grāmanivāsinaḥ |
anirdiṣṭāṃś caikaśapāṃṣ ṭiṭṭibhaṃ ca vivarjayet || 5.11 ||
kravyādaśakunīn sarvān iti | āmamāṃsam eva ye bhakṣayanti tān śyenādīn sarvān pakṣiṇo varjayet | evaṃ cobhayabhakṣyāṇāṃ mayūrādīnām apratiṣedhaḥ | tathā ca śaṅkhaḥ:
tittiraṃ ca mayūraṃ ca lāvakaṃ ca kapiñjalam |
vārdhrīṇasaṃ vartakaṃ ca bhakṣyān āha yamaḥ satām || iti |
grāmanivāsinaś ca pakṣiṇaḥ pārāvatādīn varjayet | tathā śrutau bhakṣyatvena ca “uṣṭraṃ vaḍavam ālabheta tasya ca māṃsam aśnīyāt” itivat ye na nirdiṣṭā ekaśaphāḥ tān varjayet | ye tu yatra nirdiṣṭāḥ tāṃs tatraiva bhakṣayet yajñāṅgatvena teṣāṃ tatraiva bhakṣyatvanodanāt nānyatra ṭittibhākhyaṃ ca pakṣiṇaṃ varjayet || 5.11 ||
kalaviṅkaṃ plavaṃ haṃsaṃ cakrāhvaṃ grāmakukkuṭam |
sārasaṃ rajjuvālaṃ ca dātyūhaṃ śukasārike || 5.12 ||
kalaviṅkam iti | kalaviṅko grāmacaṭakaḥ nigameṣu pāṭhāt | tasya varṣāsu vanavāsatvenobhayanivāsāt grāmanivāsapratiṣedhaḥ | vṛttyāśaṅkāyām idaṃ vacanam | tathā plavaṃ saṃkaṭavilākhyaṃ pakṣiṇaṃ haṃsacakravākagrāmakukkuṭaśukaśārikāṃ varjayet | grāmakukkuṭagrahaṇam āraṇyābhyanujñānārtham | tasya grāmanivāsitvād eva pratiṣedhe siddhe jātyaikāc cāraṇyasyābhyakṣyatāśaṅkā syāt | tadarthaṃ vacanam | nanu grāmanivāsitvād eva pratiṣedhasiddheḥ (grāmakukkuṭapratiṣedhasiddhyai) grāmakukkuṭagrahaṇam etadvyatiriktagrāmanivāsivikalpārtham kiṃ na vyākhyāyate kim āraṇyābhyanujñānaparatveneti | naivam, svapadārthagataprayojanasadbhāve sati vākyāntaragataviśeṣāvadhāraṇaparatvasyānyāyyatvāt sārasaṃ lakṣmaṇākhyaṃ pakṣiṇaṃ rajjuvālaṃ ca pāyikākhyaṃ dātyūhaṃ cakravākajalakukkuṭaśabdābhyāṃ prasiddhaṃ varjayet
|| 5.12 ||
pratudān jālapādāṃś ca koyaṣṭinakhaviṣkirān |
nimajjataś ca matsyādān saunaṃ vallūram eva ca || 5.13 ||
pratudān iti | prahṛtya cañcvā bhakṣayanti tān dārvāghāṭādīn, jālapādāṃś ca koyaṣṭidhūsarikākhyaṃ pakṣiṇaṃ, nakhaiḥ vikṣipya ye bhakṣayanti tān, śraddhābhyanujñātāraṇyakukkuṭavyatiriktān cakorakakubhāgīṃś ca tathā nimajjya ye matsyān adanti madgvādayaḥ tān “sūnā ghātanasthānam” itītyabhidhānikasmaraṇāt āghātasthāne bhavaṃ māṃsaṃ bhakṣyāṇām api vallūraṃ śuṣkamāṃsaṃ varjayet | jālapādatvād eva plavahaṃsacakravākānāṃ niṣedhasiddhau pṛthagvacanaṃ tadanyeṣāṃ māyādīnāṃ vikalpajñāpanārtham | vikalpaś cāpadi bhakṣyāṇy anāpadi nety evaṃ vyavasthito vijñeyaḥ | yato buddhipūrvakāriviracite granthe ācāryasyaivamabhiprāya unnīyate | nūnaṃ jālapādapratiṣedho nāsvāsati (pratiṣedhenāśvāsaḥ) yena jālapādaviśeṣān haṃsādīn svaśabdena niṣedhati | na cātyantānāśvāsaḥ tathā sati jālapādapratiṣedho ‘narthakaḥ syāt | tasmād viśeṣo ‘tryānumīyate | kasyāṃcid avasthāyāṃ madgvādīnāṃ bhakṣaṇam abhimataṃ kasyāṃcid pratiṣedha eveti | tenāpadi tu bhakṣaṇaṃ anāpadi tu niṣedha eva, pratiṣedhadvyayārthatvāt | yadi punas tulyavikalpaḥ syāt tadā jālapādaniṣedhasyānarthakyam eva syāt, tadvacanam antareṇaiva tathāvidhavikalpasya kadācid bhakṣyāṇi kadācin nety eva tatphalaṃ saṃpadyate | tac ca jñāpanam antareṇaiva laukikatvād bhakṣaṇasya arthitvānarthitvābhyāṃ siddham | tasmāt jālapādapratiṣedhābhidhānād eva vyavasthito ‘yaṃ vikalpa ity avasīyate || 5.13 ||
bakaṃ caiva balākāṃ ca kākolaṃ khañjarīṭakam |
matsyādān viḍvarāhāṃś ca matsyān eva ca sarvaśaḥ || 5.14 ||
bakaṃ caiveti | bakaṃ balākaṃ kākolaṃ ca droṇakākaṃ “kākolo droṇakāka udāhṛtaḥ” iti trikāṇḍapāṭhāt | kañjarīṭākhyaṃ pakṣiṇaṃ matsyādāṃś ca apakṣiṇo ‘pi nakrādīn grāmasūkarāṃś ca matsyāṃś ca sarvān varjayet || 5.14 ||
yo yasya māṃsam aśnāti sa tanmāṃsāda ucyate |
matsyādaḥ sarvamāṃśādas tasmān matsyān vivarjayet || 5.15 ||
asyārthavādaḥ yo yasya māṃsam iti | yo yatsaṃbandhi māṃsam aśnāti tanmāṃsāda evaṃ nindārtham ucyate | nānyamāṃsādapapaleśaṃ labhate matsyādaḥ punaḥ sarvamāṃsabhakṣa iti vyapadeṣṭuṃ yujyate tān matsyān varjayet || 5.15 ||
pāṭhīnarohitāv ādyau niyuktau havyakavyayoḥ |
rājīvāḥ siṃhatuṇḍāś ca saśalkāś caiva sarvaśaḥ || 5.16 ||
idānīṃ jātiviśeṣakriyāviśeṣasamavalambanena matsyapratiprasavam āha pāṭhīnarohitāv iti | pāṭhīnaḥ candrkāraṇyo matsyabheda eva | rohito ‘pi rohitamatsyākhyaḥ | tau daivapitryakarmaviniyuktau ity adanārhau na tv anyadā | rājīvāḥ punaḥ padmavarṇā matsyaviśeṣāḥ | tathā siṃhamukhāḥ saśalkāś ca śakalicilicimaśabdābhyām āyurvedaprasiddhāḥ | sarvadā havyakavyābhyām anyatrāpi bhakṣaṇārhāḥ || 5.16 ||
na bhakṣayed ekacarān ajñātāṃś ca mṛgadvijān |
bhakṣyeṣv api samuddiṣṭān sarvān pañcanakhāṃs tathā || 5.17 ||
na bhakṣayed iti | ye ekakāḥ prāyeṇāṭanti sarpādayaḥ tāṃs tathā abhiyuktair api nāmajātibhiḥ ye mṛgapakṣiṇo na jñāyante tān viśeṣasāmānyapratiṣedhānākramaṇe sati bhakṣyapakṣe patitatvāt bhakṣyatvena samuddhiṣṭān evaṃ pañcanakhāṃś ca sarvān naramārjārādīn na bhakṣayet
|| 5.17 ||
śvāvidhaṃ śalyakaṃ godhāṃ khaḍgakūrmaśaśāṃs tathā |
bhakṣyān pañcanakheṣv āhur anuṣṭrāṃś caikatodataḥ || 5.18 ||
atra pratiprasavam āha śvāvidhaṃ śalyakaṃ godhāṃ khaḍgakūrmaśaśāṃs tatheti | śvāvidhaṃ sedhākhyaṃ prāṇibhedaṃ śalyakaṃ cāṣaṃ godhāṃ ca prāṇiviśeṣaṃ khaḍgaṃ gaṇḍakaṃ kūrmaṃ kacchapaṃ śaśaṃ ca pañcanakheṣu madhyāt bhakṣaṇārhān manvādaya āhuḥ | tathā ekadantapaṅktyupeteṣu madhyāt uṣṭravarjitān ajādīn iti bhāvaḥ || 5.18 ||
chatrākaṃ viḍvarāhaṃ ca laśunaṃ grāmakukkuṭam |
palāṇḍuṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva matyā jagdhvā pated dvijaḥ || 5.19 ||
chatrākam iti | kavakagrāmasūkaralaśunādīnām anyatamaṃ buddhipūrvaṃ prāyaścittagurutvāt abhyāsato bhakṣayitvā dvijātiḥ patet | dvijātikarmabhyo hāniḥ patanam iti | tataś ca prāyaścittaṃ tasya na bhavati | tathā vakṣyati “garhitānndyayor jagdhiḥ surāpānasamāni ṣaṭ” (11.56) iti
|| 5.19 ||
amatyaitāni ṣaḍ jagdhvā kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret |
yaticāndrāyaṇaṃ vāpi śeṣeṣūpavased ahaḥ || 5.20 ||
amatyaitānīti | etāni chatrākādīni ṣaṭ buddhipūrvakaṃ pratyekaṃ bhakṣayitvā ekādaśādhyāyavakṣyamānasvarūpaṃ sāntapanaṃ yaticāndrāyaṇaṃ caret | etadvyatirikteṣu lohitaniryāsādiṣu pratyekabhakṣaṇe ‘horātraṃ nāśnīyāt | prāyaścittasya prakarṣeṇotkarṣo laśunādīnāṃ varjanādarārtham | śeṣeṣūpavased ahar ity etat punar lāghavārtham | tatra hi kriyamāṇe lohitaniryāsādigrahaṇaṃ kartavyaṃ syāt || 5.20 ||
saṃvatasarasyaikam api cared kṛcchraṃ dvijottamaḥ |
ajñātabhuktaśuddhyarthaṃ jñātasya tu viśeṣataḥ || 5.21 ||
saṃvatsarasyaikam apīti | saṃvatsarasya madhye brāhmaṇaḥ ‘śeṣeṣūpavased ahar’ iti samanantaraprastutalohitavṛkṣaniryāsādyasaṃvettibhuktaprāyaścittārtham api kṛcchraṃ kṛcchramadhye prathamāmnānāt prājāpatyaṃ caret | jñātasya punar abhakṣyabhakṣaṇe viśeṣato yad yasya coditaṃ prāyaścittaṃ tad eva kuryāt | ata eva ca jñāyate asaṃveditaḥ kṛte ‘pi vyatikrame pāpaṃ prāyaścittaṃ bhavatīti | evaṃ ca “trīṇi devāḥ pavitrāṇi brāhmaṇānām akalpayan | adṛṣṭam adbhir nirṇiktam” ity etatprāyaścittavyatiriktaviṣaye ‘vatiṣṭhate | tathā ca dravyaśuddhiprakaraṇa etad āryeṇābhihitam | brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ dvijapradarśanārtham, trayāṇāṃ prakṛtatvāt | “etad uktaṃ dvijātīnām” iti copasaṃhārāt || 5.21 ||
yajñārthaṃ brāhmaṇair vadhyāḥ praśastā mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ |
bhṛtyānāṃ caiva vṛttyartham agastyo hy ācarat purā || 5.22 ||
yāgārthaṃ brāhmaṇādibhiḥ kṛtaiḥ praśastā ye coditāḥ śrutau mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ te vadhyā iti śrutyuktānuvādaḥ | bhṛtyānāṃ caivety etad vidhitsitam | svāmyād āpanārthaṃ (?) bhṛtyānām avaśyabharaṇīyānāṃ vṛddhamātrādīnāṃ sthityarthaṃ ca vadhyāḥ | agastyo yasmāt pūrvam evaṃ kṛtavān iti copasaṃhārāt || 5.22 ||
babhūvur hi puroḍāśā bhakṣyāṇāṃ mṛgapakṣiṇām |
purāṇeṣv ṛṣiyajñeṣu brahmakṣatrasaveṣu ca || 5.23 ||
babhūvur iti | yasmāc cirantaneṣv api saṃbandhiṣu yajñeṣu brāhmaṇakṣatriyayajñeṣu ca bhakṣyāṇāṃ mṛgapakṣiṇāṃ saṃbandhināmamāṃsena puroḍāśā abhūvan | tasmād yajñārtham api te vadhyāḥ || 5.23 ||
yat kiṃcit snehasaṃyuktaṃ bhakṣyaṃ bhojyam agarhitam |
tat paryuṣitam apy ādyaṃ haviḥśeṣaṃ ca yad bhavet || 5.24 ||
adhunā paryuṣitapratiprasavārtham āha yat kiṃcid iti | yad bhakṣyaṃ kharaviṣadam abhyavahāryam modakādi | yac ca bhojyaṃ pāyasādi upaghātāntararahitaṃ tad dadhighṛtādisnehayuktaṃ kṛtvā paryuṣitam api rātryantaritam adyam adanārham ity evaṃ tacchabdavyatyayenaitad vyākhyeyam | yathā śrutayojanāyāṃ haviṣo ‘bhighāraṇe sati snehasaṃyogāvaśyaṃbhāvitvād dhaviḥśeṣasya “yat kiṃcid snehasaṃyuktam” ity anenaiva siddhatve sati punar abhidhānam anarthakaṃ syāt | yac ca hutaśiṣṭaṃ puroḍāśādi paryuṣitaṃ syāt tat bhojanakāle snehasaṃyogaśūnyam apy adanārtham || 5.24 ||
cirasthitam api tv ādyam asnehāktaṃ dvijātibhiḥ |
yavagodhūmajaṃ sarvaṃ payasaś caiva vikriyāḥ || 5.25 ||
cirasthitam iti | yavagodhūmakṣīravikārāḥ sarve naikarātryantaritaḥ santaḥ snehaprokṣaṇarahitā api dvijādibhir adanīyāḥ || 5.25 ||
etad uktaṃ dvijātīnāṃ bhakṣyābhakṣyam aśeṣataḥ |
māṃsasyātaḥ pravakṣyāmi vidhiṃ bhakṣaṇavarjane || 5.26 ||
dvijātīnām etat bhakṣyābhakṣyaṃ niḥśeṣeṇoktam, ata ūrdhvaṃ māṃsasya bhakṣaṇe varjane ca vidhiḥ vidhānaṃ vakṣyāmi || 5.26 ||
prokṣitaṃ bhakṣayen māṃsaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca kāmyayā |
yathāvidhi niyuktas tu prāṇānām eva cātyaye || 5.27 ||
prokṣitam iti | prokṣaṇākhyaśrautasaṃskārayuktaḥ go’vyajasaṃcitpriyamāṃsaṃ tad bhakṣayet iti śrutiḥ | karmāṅgabhūtabhakṣaṇaniyamānuvādo ‘yaṃ brāhmaṇakāmanādinimittabhakṣaṇaniyamasāmyāpādanārthaḥ | yat tu prokṣitaparisaṃkhyārthatvam āhuḥ | tad asat, “anupākṛtamāṃsāni” ity asya aprokṣitapratiṣedhārthatvāt | brāhmaṇānāṃ ceti brāhmaṇaprārthanayā vyaktapratiṣiddhamāṃsāśino ‘pi ca brahmacaryādiniyamato ‘śnīyāt | tathā śrāddhe madhuparke vā tadāmiṣeṇa kartavyam, “nāmāṃso madhuparkaḥ syāt” iti smṛtigṛhyaśāstraprayuktena abrāhmaṇenāpi niyukto niyamato bhakṣayet | tathā ca niyuktas tu iti atikramadoṣaṃ vakṣyati | prāṇātyaye ‘pi cakṣurnimitte vyādhinimitte ca tyaktamāṃsāśino ‘pi ca brahmacaryādiniyamato bhuñjīta || 5.27 ||
prāṇasyānnam idaṃ sarve prajāpatir akalpayat |
sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ caiva sarvaṃ prāṇasya bhojanam || 5.28 ||
prāṇasyeti | koṣṭhyasya vāyoḥ śarīrāvasthityartham idaṃ sarvaṃ prajāpatir annaṃ kalpitavān | kiṃ tat | ata āha paśvādi jaṅgamaṃ mūlaphalādi ca sthāvaraṃ sarvaṃ prāṇasya bhojanam | tasmāt prāṇātyaye māṃsam adanīyam iti prāṇātyaye māṃsabhakṣaṇārthavādaḥ || 5.28 ||
carāṇām annam acarā daṃṣṭriṇām apy adaṃṣṭriṇaḥ |
ahastāś ca sahastānāṃ śūrāṇāṃ caiva bhīravaḥ || 5.29 ||
carāṇām iti | jaṅgamānām ajaṅgamāḥ sthāvarāṇy annam | daṃṣṭrāvatāṃ ca vyāghrādīnām adaṃṣṭrā sarpādayaḥ | hastavatāṃ mārjārādīnāṃ ahastā matsyādayaḥ śūrāṇāṃ siṃhādīnāṃ bhīravo hastyādayaḥ | animittam annam iti pūrvaślokasyaivāyaṃ prapañcaḥ || 5.29 ||
nāttā duṣyaty adann ādyān prāṇino ‘hany ahany api |
dhātraiva sṛṣṭā hy ādyāś ca prāṇino ‘ttāra eva ca || 5.30 ||
itthaṃ prajāpatikalpanāyāṃ satyāṃ nāttā duṣyatīti  | adanārhān prāṇino ‘har ahar apy aśnan bhakṣayitā na doṣān prāpnoti | yasmāt prajāpatinaivādanarhā bhakṣayitāraś cotpāditā iti prāṇātyayamāṃsabhakṣaṇaniyamadārḍhyārtho ‘yam arthavādo na hetuḥ prāṇyupaghātaniṣedhopadeśāt || 5.30 ||
yajñāya jagdhir māṃsasyety eṣa daivo vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |
ato ‘nyathāpravṛttis tu rākṣaso vidhir ucyate || 5.31 ||
yajñāyeti | yajñasaṃpattyarthaṃ tadaṅgabhūtaṃ yan māṃsabhakṣaṇam etad devocitam anuṣṭhānam | ata eva yatnataḥ kāryam | etadvyatiriktaprakāreṇa punar māṃsabhakṣaṇapravṛttiḥ rakṣaḥsamucitam ācaraṇaṃ manvādibhir ucyate | tasmād etan na kāryam iti prokṣitabhakṣaṇaniyamād anuvādārthaḥ || 5.31 ||
krītvā svayaṃ vāpy utpādya paropakṛtam eva vā |
devān pitṝṃś cārcayitvā khādan māṃsaṃ na duṣyati || 5.32 ||
krītveti  | krītvā ātmanā cotpādya | yathoktaṃ “bhṛtyānāṃ caiva vṛttyartham” iti | anena cānītaṃ yathoktam “dhānā matsyān payo māṃsam” iti | evaṃvidhaṃ māṃsaṃ devapitṛbhyo datvāśnan pāpaṃ na prāpnoti ity upākaraṇānarhaśaśādimāṃsaviṣayam idam upākaraṇārhago’vyajamāṃsasyānupākṛtasya devādyarcanaśiṣṭasya “anupākṛtamāṃsādi” iti pratiṣedhāt || 5.32 ||
nādyād avidhinā māṃsaṃ vidhijño ‘nāpadi dvijaḥ |
jagdhvā hy avidhinā māṃsaṃ pretas tair adyate ‘vaśaḥ || 5.33 ||
nadyād iti | māṃsabhakṣaṇaprakārajño dvijo ‘nāpadi devādyarcanavidhānaṃ vinā māṃsaṃ nāśnīyāt yasmād avidhānena yo māṃsam aśnāti sa mṛtaḥ san paraloke taiḥ prāṇibhiḥ paravaśo bhujyate, yan māṃsam anena bhakṣitaṃ yuktam iti pūrvārthavādaḥ || 5.33 ||
na tādṛśaṃ bhavaty eno mṛgahantur dhanārthinaḥ |
yādṛśaṃ bhavati pretya vṛthāmāṃsāni khādataḥ || 5.34 ||
na tādṛśam iti | mṛgavadhajīviśabalādivyatiriktasya dhananimittaṃ mṛgāṇāṃ hantuḥ na tathāvidhaṃ pāpaṃ bhavati yādṛgakṛtadevādyarcanāni khādataḥ paraloke bhavatītipūrvārthavāda eva || 5.34 ||
niyuktas tu yathāvad yo māṃsaṃ nāśnāti mānavaḥ |
sa pretya paśutāṃ yāti saṃbhavān ekaviṃśatim || 5.35 ||
niyuktas tu yathāvad yo māṃsaṃ nāśnāti mānavaḥ iti | śrāddhamadhuparkayoḥ śāstramaryādānatikrameṇa niyuktaḥ san yo manuṣyo māṃsaṃ nāśnāti sa mṛtaḥ san ekaviṃśatijanmāni paśutvaṃ prāpnoti | iti yathāvidhiniyuktas tv iti etanniyamavyatikramaphalakathanam || 5.35 ||
asaṃskṛtān paśūn mantrair nādyād vipraḥ kadācana |
mantrais tu saṃskṛtān adyāc chāśvataṃ vidhim āsthitaḥ || 5.36 ||
asaṃskṛtān iti | paśuyāgādicoditaprokṣaṇādimantrasaṃskārarāhitān go’vyajādipaśūn viprādiḥ na kadācid apy adyāt | kiṃ tarhi | naimittikaṃ vaidikapaśuyāgādividhim āśritaḥ san mantrasaṃskṛtān evāśnīyād ity anupākṛtamāṃsārthe nirūpaṇārthavādam etat [māṃsanirākaraṇānuvādārtham etat] || 5.36 ||
kuryād ghṛtapaśuṃ saṅge kuryāt piṣṭapaśuṃ tathā |
na tv eva tu vṛthā hantuṃ paśum icchet kadācana || 5.37 ||
kuryād iti | saṅge bhūtādidoṣeṇa śarīrādyavasāde sati tadvināśanārthaṃ ghṛtamayīṃ vā paśuprakṛtim upahareta na punar devādyuddeśam antareṇaiva paśuṃ hantuṃ kadācid api icchet
|| 5.37 ||
yāvanti paśuromāṇi tāvatkṛtveha māraṇam |
vṛthāpaśudhnaḥ prāpnoti pretya janmani janmani || 5.38 ||
yasmād yāvanti paśuromāṇīti | vṛthā uddeśam antareṇa yaḥ paśuṃ hanti sa mṛtaḥ san tasya hatasya paśoḥ sakāśāt [yāvatsaṃkhyāni paśuromāṇi] tatsaṃkhyākān vārān janmani janmani māraṇaṃ prāpnoti tasmād vṛthā paśuṃ na hanyāt | haśabda āgamasūcakaḥ || 5.38 ||
yajñārthaṃ paśavaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ svayam eva svayambhuvā |
yajñasya bhūtyai sarvasya tasmād yajñe vadho ‘vadhaḥ || 5.39 ||
yajñārtham iti | yajñasaṃpattyarthaṃ prajāpatinā ātmanaivādareṇa paśavaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ | yajñaś ca “agnau prāstā” iti nyāyenāsya jagataḥ bhūtyabhivṛddhyartham, tasmād yajñe yo vadhaḥ so ‘dharmākhyavadhakāryābhāvād avadha eva || 5.39 ||
oṣadhyaḥ paśavo vṛkṣās tiryañcaḥ pakṣiṇas tathā |
yajñārthaṃ nidhanaṃ prāptāḥ prāpnuvanty ucchritīḥ punaḥ || 5.40 ||
oṣadhya iti | oṣadayo darbhādyāḥ | paśavaḥ chāgādayaḥ | vṛkṣāḥ plakṣādyāḥ | tiryañcaḥ kūrmādayah | pakṣiṇaḥ kapiñjalādyāḥ yajñārthaṃ vināśaṃ prāptāḥ te tam adharmārjitaṃ nikarṣaṃ hitvā punaḥ ātmajñānādhikṛtaśarīralābhenotkarṣān prāpnuvanti || 5.40 ||
madhuparke ca yajñe ca pitṛdaivatakarmaṇi |
atraiva paśavo hiṃsyā nānyatrety abravīn manuḥ || 5.41 ||
madhuparke ceti | rājartviksnātaketyevamādinodite gṛhyoktasvarūpe madhuparke yajñe ca jyotiṣṭomādau pitrye daive ca karmaṇi eva paśavo hiṃsanīyāḥ nānyatrety evaṃ manur uktavān || 5.41 ||
eṣv artheṣu paśūn hiṃsan vedatattvārthavid dvijaḥ |
ātmānaṃ ca paśuṃ caiva gamayaty uttamāṃ gatim || 5.42 ||
eṣv iti | pa[pā]ramārthikavedārthajño dvija eteṣu madhuparkādiṣu padārtheṣu paśūn hiṃsan ātmānaṃ tāṃś ca paśūn uttamāṃ gatiṃ svargādau vicitraśarīrasaṃbandhaṃ prāpayati | na cānyatrānyadīyakarmaṇaḥ katham anyasya phalaprāptir iti nodanīyam, śabdapramāṇako ‘rtho yatśabdo bodhyati tat tathaiva pratipādanīyam | anyathā śabdapramāṇakadharmārthaphalalābhe ‘pi ka āśvāsaḥ | paśusaṃjñapanamantramadhye [-sañjavamantra] veda āmnāyate – “na vā u etan mriyase na riṣyasi | devāṃ id eṣi pathibhiḥ sugebhiḥ” iti | paśuṃ kila hotā evam āha | na tvai tvaṃ mriyase nāpi riṣyasi hiṃsyase | kiṃ tarhi | sugaiḥ nikṛṣṭayonivyavadhānaśūnyaiḥ mārge devān eṣi svargaṃ prāpnoṣīty arthaḥ | evaṃ cānyatra svaparopakārabhāvāt || 5.42 ||
gṛhe gurāv araṇye vā nivasann ātmavān dvijaḥ |
nāvedavihitāṃ hiṃsām āpady api samācaret || 5.43 ||
evaṃ cānyatra svaparopakārabhāvāt [-kārābhāvāt] gṛha iti | gṛhasthavānaprasthabrahmacāri-bhikṣvavasthaḥ praśastātmo dvijaḥ āpady api aśāstrīyāṃ hiṃsāṃ nācaret || 5.43 ||
yā vedavihitā hiṃsā niyatāsmiṃś carācare |
ahiṃsām eva tāṃ vidyād vedād dharmo hi nirbabhau || 5.44 ||
kathaṃ punas tulye prāṇivyapādane laukikyā adharmaḥ śāstrīyāyā dharma ity ata āha yeti | yā śāstranoditā hiṃsā sthāvarajaṅgame ‘smin jagati anādikālapravṛttāṃ tāṃ hiṃsākāryābhāvād ahiṃsām eva vidyāt | ato nānyapramāṇako dharmo vedād eva nirbabhau prakāśatām gataḥ | evaṃ ca nātra kutārkikavitarkā ākramante || 5.44 ||
yo ‘hiṃsakāni bhūtāni hinasty ātmasukhecchayā |
sa jīvaṃś ca mṛtaś caiva na kvacit sukham edhate || 5.45 ||
yo ‘hiṃsakānīti | yo ‘nupaghātakān prāṇino hariṇādīn ātmasukhābhilāṣeṇa hinasti sa ihaloke paraloke na kadācit sukhaṃ vardhate || 5.45 ||
yo bandhanavadhakleśān prāṇināṃ na cikīrṣati |
sa sarvasya hitaprepsuḥ sukham atyantam aśnute || 5.46 ||
yo bandhanavadhakleśān iti | yo bandhanavadhatrāsādīn prāṇināṃ kartuṃ necchati sa sarvasya hitaprāpaṇecchāśilo ‘nantaṃ sukhaṃ prāpnoti || 5.46 ||
yad dhyāyati yat kurute ratiṃ badhnāti yatra ca |
tad avāpnoty ayatnena yo hinasti na kiṃcana || 5.47 ||
yad dhyāyatīti | yaś cintayati [yac cintayati] bhagavadīśvarārādhanādi, yad vā kurute hariharacaraṇanamaskārādi, yatra vā bhavabhaktibhāvanādau ratiṃ badhnāti tat sarvam ayatnena prāpnoti ya ātatāyyādīn api prāṇino na hinasti || 5.47 ||
nākṛtvā prāṇināṃ hiṃsāṃ māṃsam utpadyate kvacit |
na ca prāṇivadhaḥ svargyas tasmān māṃsaṃ vivarjayet || 5.48 ||
evaṃ māṃsabhakṣaṇaprasaṅgena guṇadoṣānabhidhāyādhunā prakṛtam anusartum āha nākṛtveti | prāṇihiṃsām antareṇa na kvacin māṃsam utpadyate | prāṇivadhaś ca svarganimittaṃ na bhavati, pratyuta narakādihetuḥ tasmād avidhinā māṃsabhakṣaṇaṃ varjayet || 5.48 ||
samutpattiṃ ca māṃsasya vadhabandhau ca dehinām |
prasamīkṣya nivarteta sarvamāṃsasya bhakṣaṇāt || 5.49 ||
samutpattiṃ ceti | śukraśoṇitābhyāṃ cikitsakotpādanāgresarābhyāṃ samutpattiṃ māraṇakāle ca sakalakrūrakarmāgraṇībhūtau vadhabandhau paryālocya vihitamāṃsabhakṣaṇād api nivarteta || 5.49 ||
na bhakṣayati yo māṃsaṃ vidhiṃ hitvā piśācavat |
sa loke priyatāṃ yāti vyādhibhiś ca na pīḍyate || 5.50 ||
kim utāvihitamāṃsabhakṣaṇād ity avihitamāṃsabhakṣaṇanindārtham āha na bhakṣayatīti | yo devādyarcanādividhiparityāgena piśācavan māṃsaṃ na bhakṣayati sa sarvalokapriyo bhavati | vyādhibhiś ca na pīḍyate || 5.50 ||
anumantā viśasitā nihantā krayavikrayī |
saṃskartā copahartā ca khādakaś ceti ghātakāḥ || 5.51 ||
anumanteti | anyena hanyamānam avalokya sādhv ayaṃ hantā karotīti yo ‘numodate so ‘numantā | viśasitā aṅgavibhāgakaro na hantā (nihantā) vadhakaḥ, krayavikrayī krītvā vikrīṇīte, saṃskartā randhakaḥ upahartā pariveṣṭā khādako bhakṣayitā, ete ghātakā iti | atra ca nihantāraṃ varjayitvā anyeṣām aghātakānāṃ ghātakavacanam aśāstritaprāṇivadhe ‘numodanādīny api na kāryāṇīty evamarthaṃ na tu ghātakatvapratipādanārthaṃ kartavyākartavyapratipādanaparatvād asya śāstrasya ghātakakhādakādīnāṃ pṛthak prāyaścittadarśanāt || 5.51 ||
svamāṃsaṃ paramāṃsena yo vardhayitum icchati |
anabhyarcya pitṝn devāṃs tato ‘nyo nāsty apuṇyakṛt || 5.52 ||
svamāṃsam iti | svaśarīramāṃsaṃ paramāṃsena devārcanarahitena yo vṛddhiṃ netum icchati tasmād adhiko ‘nyo ‘puṇyakartā nāstīty avidhimāṃsabhakṣaṇanindārthavāda eva || 5.52 ||
varṣe varṣe ‘śvamedhena yo yajeta śataṃ samāḥ |
māṃsāni ca na khāded yat tayoḥ puṇyaphalaṃ samam || 5.53 ||
idānīm aśiṣṭāpratiṣiddhamāṃsanivṛttir dharmāyety etat pratipādayitum āha varṣe varṣa iti | prativarṣaṃ aśvamedhena yajeta | tathā yo yāvajjīvaṃ māṃsaṃ nāśnāti tayor aśvamedhamāṃsatyāgajanitasya puṇyasya saṃbandhi svargādiphalaṃ tulyam || 5.53 ||
phalamūlāśnair medhyair munyannānāṃ ca bhojanaiḥ |
na tatphalam avāpnoti yan māṃsaparivarjanāt || 5.54 ||
phalamūlāśanair iti | pavitrāṇāṃ phalamūlānāṃ ca bhakṣaṇaiḥ tathā ṛṣisaṃbandhinām annānāṃ nīvārādīnām āraṇyānāṃ bhojane tathāvidhaṃ phalaṃ nāpnoti || 5.54 ||
māṃ sa bhakṣayitāmutra yasya māṃsam ihādmy aham |
etan māṃsasya māṃsatvaṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ || 5.55 ||
māṃ sa bhakṣayiteti || 5.55 ||
na māṃsabhakṣaṇe doṣo na madye na ca maithune |
pravṛttir eṣā bhūtānāṃ nivṛttis tu mahāphalā || 5.56 ||
na māṃsbhakṣaṇe doṣa iti | śiṣṭāpratiṣiddhamāṃsabhakṣaṇamadyapānamaithunācaraṇe na kaścid doṣo yasmāj jaṅgamātmakabhūtānāṃ eṣā aśanapānamaithunātmikā pravṛttiḥ svābhāviko ‘yaṃ vyāpāraḥ | varjanaṃ punar mahāpalam | iti māṃsavarjanaphalaprasaṅgena aśiṣṭāpratiṣiddhamadyamaithunādinivṛttiphalakathanaparam etat || 5.56 ||
pretaśuddhiṃ pravakṣyāmi dravyaśuddhiṃ tathaiva ca |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ yathāvad anupūrvaśaḥ || 5.57 ||
pretaśuddhim iti | pretanimittāṃ śuddhiṃ dravyāṇāṃ taijasādīnāṃ śuddhiṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ caturṇāṃ kārtsnyena brāhmaṇāḍyānupūrvyā ca pravakṣyāmi || 5.57 ||
dantajāte ‘nujāte ca kṛtacūḍe ca saṃsthite |
aśuddhā bāndhavāḥ sarve sūtake ca tathocyate || 5.58 ||
dantajāte ‘nujāte ceti | jātadante ‘nujāte ca jātadantād bālatare kṛtacūḍākaraṇe ce | cakārāt kṛtopanayane ca mṛte sapiṇḍasamānodakāḥ sarve bāndhavāḥ aśuddhās tathā prasave ca sati tathaivāśuddhā ity ucyate | kṛtacūḍa iti ca bhūyiṣṭhe tṛtīya iti gṛhyoktacūḍākālopalakṣaṇena kṛtacūḍatvaṃ vivakṣitam | “rātribhir māsatulyābhiḥ” iti garbhāt prabhṛty eva | bhūyastvenāśaucavṛddhidarśanād ihāpi ca dantajāte ‘nujāte ceti vayodvāreṇaivopakramād eva daśāhāśauce ‘pi mukhyopanayanakālalakṣaṇāvijñeyās tu pañcavarṣa upanītas tasyāpekṣāyā abhāvāt tadānīm eva paripūrṇam āśaucaṃ bhavati || 5.58 ||
daśāhaṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu vidhīyate |
arvāk saṃcayanād asthnāṃ tryaham ekāham eva vā || 5.59 ||
daśāham iti | sapiṇḍeṣu preteṣu śavanimittam āśaucaṃ daśāhorātrāṇāṃ brāhmaṇasyopadiśyate | kṣatriyāder vakṣyamāṇatvād asthimañcayanaṃ vā yāvac caturahorātrān āhitāgneḥ saṃcayanaṃ caturthyām iti gṛhye caturthe ‘hani saṃcayanavidhānāt | tryahaṃ caikāhaṃ vā sarvatrāśaucaprakaraṇe ‘hargrahaṇaṃ rātrigrahaṇaṃ cāhorātrapradarśanārtham | vṛttasvādhyāyādyapekṣaś cāyaṃ vikalpas tatrādhyayanāgnihotrau pratigrahādyārtitāratamyāpekṣayaite vikalpāḥ | yathāyogam āśrayaṇīyān anvicchāto ‘nyatamapakṣāśrayaṇam | tathā ca parāśaraḥ.
ekāhāc chudyate vipro yo ‘gnivedasamanvitaḥ |
tryahāt kevalavedas tu nirguṇo daśabhir dinaiḥ ||
iti vṛttādyapekṣayaiva śaucavaicitryam āha | vṛttādyapekṣe ‘pi śaucāśaucasaṃkoce brāhmaṇasya ca svādhyāyānivṛttyartham ityevamādigautamādivākyadarśanād adhyayanādimadhyād yata eva yasyārtis tāvanmātrānuṣṭhānaprayojana eva tasyāśaucasaṃkoco na kāryāntare | yasyaiva ca svādhyāyādyārtis tasyaivāyam āśaucasaṃkōcaḥ | na piṇḍāntarasyāgnihotrārthaṃ snānopasparśanāt tatkālaṃ pitā śucir ity evamādiśaṅkhādivākyadarśanād iti | evaṃ cārtiviṣaye śaucasaṃkoce sthite tadvyatiriktāntareṣūbhayatra daśāhāni kulasyānnaṃ na bhujyeta |
dānaṃ pratigraho homaṃ svādhyāyaś ca nivartate |
ity etad vākyaṃ bhavaty eva | uttaratrāpi vṛttādyapekṣayaiva vikalpāvijñeyāḥ || 5.59 ||
sapiṇḍatā tu puruṣe saptame vinivartate |
samānodakabhāvas tu janmanāmnor avedane || 5.60 ||
alaukikatvāt sapiṇḍasya lakṣaṇam āha sapiṇḍateti | ṣaṭ puruṣān pitṛpitāmahādīn atikramya saptame prāpte sapiṇḍatvaṃ nivartate | evaṃ putrapautrādiṣv api vijñeyam | samānodakatvaṃ punar ayam asmatkule jāta evaṃnāmaś cāsmadvaṃśyo ‘syādipuruṣa ityevaṃ janmanāmobhayāparijñāne sati nivartate || 5.60 ||
yathedaṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu vidhīyate |
janane ‘py evam eva syān nipuṇāṃ śuddhim icchatām || 5.61 ||
sarveṣāṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ mātāpitros tu sūtakam |
sūtakaṃ mātur eva syād upaspṛśya pitā śuciḥ || 5.62 ||
janana iti | sarveṣām iti | yathā sapiṇḍeṣu daśāhaṃ śāvam āśaucam ityādipakṣacatuṣṭayaṃ tathaiva sapiṇḍajanane ‘pi syāt sūtakaṃ punar upaspṛśyatvatatkāraṇaṃ mātāpitror eva na sapiṇḍānāṃ tad api ca pituḥ |
jāte putre pituḥ snānaṃ sacailaṃ tu vidhīyate |
mātā suddhyed daśāhena snātvā tu sparśanaṃ pituḥ ||
iti saṃvartadarśanena kṛtasnānopasparśaṇaḥ pitā śuddho bhavati | yat tv atra daśāha etaiḥ ko ‘tidiśyate nānye kalpāḥ sarvātideśe mātur apy ācāravirodhasyekāhādiprāpnotīty āhuḥ | tad asat, ya adhyayanādyarthātvenaikāhāder iṣyamāṇatvāt kuto mātuḥ prāptiḥ || 5.61–62 ||
nirasya tu pumāñ chukram upaspṛśyaiva śudhyati |
baijikād abhisaṃbandhād anurundhyād aghaṃ tryaham || 5.63 ||
nirasya tv iti | “snānaṃ maithuninaḥ smṛtam” iti mithune snānavidhānān maithunād anyatrābuddhipūrvaṃ retaḥ srāve saty upaspṛśyaiva pumān śuddhyati | tathā ca mūtravad retasa utsarge śaucam ity āpastambo ‘bravīd etac ceha prasaṅgenāha | yatra punar janane bījanimittamātra evābhisaṃbandho na śāstrato yathā parapūrvābhāryāprasave tatra tryaham āśaucam anuvartate | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “parapūrvāsu bhāryāsu prasūtāsu trirātram” iti || 5.63 ||
ahnā caikena rātryā ca trirārair eva ca tribhiḥ |
śavaspṛśo viśudhyanti tryahād udakadāyinaḥ || 5.64 ||
ahnā caikeneti | ekenāhorātreṇa navabhiś cāhorātrair evaṃvṛttavaśād daśāhena nirdiṣṭena mūlyena brāhmaṇasya mūlyena brāhmaṇasya snānālaṃkāranirharaṇādikāriṇo brāhmaṇaḥ sapiṇḍasamānodakavyatiriktāḥ śuddhyanti | snehanirharaṇe sati | “asapiṇḍaṃ dvijaṃ pretam” iti vakṣyati | dharmārthaṃ punar nirharaṇamāṇānām āśaucābhāva eva | “etad vai paramaṃ tapo yat pretam araṇyaṃ nayanti” iti śruteḥ |
na teṣām aśucaṃ kiṃcit pāpaṃ vāśubhakarmaṇām |
jalāvagāhanāt teṣāṃ sadyaḥ śaucaṃ vidhīyate ||
iti parāśarasmaraṇāc caivaṃ vedaṃ pāraśiṣyān mūlyaviṣaye ca tiṣṭhate | udakadāyinaḥ kevalodakaṃ samānodakās trirātreṇa śuddhyanti || 5.64 ||
guroḥ pretasya śiṣyas tu pitṛmedhaṃ samācaran |
pretahāraiḥ samaṃ tatra daśarātreṇa śuddhyati || 5.65 ||
guror iti | asapiṇḍasyācāryāder guroḥ pretasyāntyeṣṭiṃ śiṣyaḥ kṛtvā pretanirhārikaiḥ pūrvoktais tulyaṃ daśarātreṇa śuddhyati || 5.65 ||
rātribhir māsatulyābhir garbhasrāve viśuddhyati |
rajasy uparate sādhvī snānena strī rajasvalā || 5.66 ||
nṛṇām akṛtacūḍānāṃ viśuddhir naiśikī smṛtā |
nirvṛttamuṇḍakānāṃ tu trirātrāc chuddhir iṣyate || 5.67 ||
rātribhir iti |
navame daśame vāpi prabalaiḥ sūtimātutaiḥ |
niḥsāryate bāṇa iva yantracchidreṇa sajvaraḥ ||
iti yājñavalkyena prasavakālasmaraṇāt navamāsād arvāk garbhasrāve sati yāvan māso garbhas tatsaṃkhyābhiḥ rātribhir viśudhyati | yatra punar aprāpte kāle jātasya kathaṃcij jīvanaṃ bhavati tatra paripūrṇam evāśaucaṃ vṛddhavyavahārābhivyaṃgyatvāc chabdārthsaṃbandhasya tathāvidher garbhaprasrāvaśabdāprayogāt | rajasvalāstrī rajonivṛttau satyāṃ snāne sādhyadṛṣṭārthakarmayogyā bhavati | sparśayogyā punas trirātraṃ rajasvalāśucir bhavatīti vasiṣṭhasmaraṇāc caturtha evāhni snānaṃ kṛtvā śuddhā bhavati |
ādantajanmanaḥ sadya ācūḍān naiśikī smṛtā |
trirātram ā vratādeśād daśarātram ataḥ param ||
iti yājñavalkyena smaraṇād dantajananād ūrdhvaṃ prāk cūḍākaraṇakālāt mṛtānāṃ narāṇāṃ sapiṇḍeṣv ahorātreṇa śuddhiḥ | cūḍākaraṇakālād ūrdhvaṃ prāg upanayanakālāt mṛtānāṃ trirātreṇa śuddhir iṣyate | 5.66–67 ||
ūnadvivārṣikaṃ pretaṃ nidadhyur bāndhavā bahiḥ |
alaṃkṛtya śucau bhūmāv asthisaṃcayanād ṛte || 5.68 ||
ūnadvivārṣikam iti | māsamātronavarṣadvayapramāṇaṃ pretam alaṃkṛtya grāmād bahiḥ śuddhāyāṃ bhūmau kālāntarasaṃbhāvyasthisaṃcayanavarjaṃ bāndhavāḥ prakṣipeyuḥ || 5.68 ||
nāsya kāryo ‘gnisaṃskāro na ca kāryodakakriyā |
araṇye kāṣṭhavat tyaktvā kṣapeta tryaham eva tu || 5.69 ||
nāsyeti | asyonadvivārṣikasya pretasyāgnisaṃskāro na kartavyo nāpi sakṛt prasiñcaty udakaṃ nāmagotreṇa vāgyatāḥ | ityevamādiyājñavalkyādyuktaprakriyodakakriyādyaurdhvadehikaṃ kiṃcit kartavyam | kiṃ tarhi, agnisaṃskāranirapekṣyam araṇye kāṣṭhavat parityajya tyaham apetāśauce sati śāstratakarmāṇi na kuryād ayaṃ cākṛtacūḍasya tryahavikalpaḥ, kṛtacūḍaviṣaye tryahasyoktatvāt | anarthakam idaṃ syād vikalpaś ca vṛttādyapekṣyety uktam || 5.69 ||
nātrivarṣasya kartavyā bāndhavair udakakriyā |
jātadantasya vā kuryur nāmni vāpi kṛte sati || 5.70 ||
nātrivarṣasyeti | yasya tṛtīyaṃ varṣaṃ nāpakrāntaṃ taṃ tasyodakakriyā agnisaṃskārādyaurdhvadehikaṃ na kartavyam ity uttaravidhitsārthaṃ pūrvānuvādaḥ | jātadantasya codakakryā agnisaṃskārādyaurdhvadehikaṃ kartavyaṃ daśamyām upasthāpya pitā nāma karotīti gṛhyadarśaṇād asmād vākyād ūrdhvaṃ kartavyam | yady akaraṇapakṣasaṃbhave karaṇapakṣādaraṇaṃ kleśāvahaṃ tathā pakṣadvayāmnānāj jātandatānamno [*?] karaṇe pretopakāro bhavaty akaraṇāt punaḥpratyavāyābhāva ity avasīyate || 5.70 ||
sabrahmacāriny ekāham atīte kṣapaṇam smṛtam |
janmany ekodakānāṃ tu trirātrāc chuddhir iṣyate || 5.71 ||
sabrahmacāriṇīrti | sahādhyāyini prete [e]kāham āśaucaṃ samānodakānāṃ punar janane sati trirātrāc chuddir iṣyate | tryahāt tūdakadāyina iti prathamaviṣayam uktam || 5.71 ||
strīṇām asaṃskṛtānāṃ tu tryahāc chudhyanti bāndhavāḥ |
yathoktenaiva kālena śudhyanti tu sanābhayaḥ || 5.72 ||
strīṇām iti | vāgdattānāṃ na vivāhitānāṃ bhartrādayas tryahāc chudhyanti | bhrātrādayaḥ punaḥ strīṇām akṛtacūḍānām ity etad uktena vidhineti || 5.72 ||
akṣāralavaṇānnāḥ syur nimajjeyuś ca te tryaham |
māṃsāśanaṃ ca nāśnīyuḥ śayīraṃś ca pṛthak kṣitau || 5.73 ||
akṣāralavaṇānnā iti | biḍalavaṇādikṣāravarjitam annam aśnīyuḥ | nadādau mṛdvāvarjaṃ snānaṃ kuryuḥ | “māṃsavarjaṃ cānnam ā śuddheḥ” iti gautamasmaraṇāt | yāvad āśaucaṃ nāśnīyur bhūmau caikakāḥ śayīran || 5.73 ||
saṃnidhāv eṣa vai kalpaḥ śāvāśaucasya kīrtitaḥ |
asaṃnidhāv ayaṃ jñeyo vidhiḥ saṃbandhibāndhavaiḥ || 5.74 ||
saṃnidhāv eṣa iti | pretadāhāvasthānāparijñānena saty eṣa śāvāśaucasya kīrtito vidhiḥ kathito deśāṇtarāvasthānād ajñāne saty ayaṃ vakṣyamāṇo vidhiḥ sapiṇḍasamānodakair boddhavyaḥ
|| 5.74 ||
vigataṃ tu videśasthaṃ śṛṇuyād yo hy anirdaśam |
yac cheṣaṃ daśarātrasya tāvad evāśucir bhavet || 5.75 ||
vigataṃ tv iti | tac cheṣaṃ daśarātraṃ syād iti | deśāntarasthaṃ mṛtaṃ jātaṃ vā anigatadaśāhādikaṃ śrutvā daśarātrāder yac chiṣṭaṃ tāvad evāśaucavān bhaven na tu parijñānāt prabhṛti daśāhāśaucam ādriyate || 5.75 ||
atikrānte daśāhe ca trirātram aśucir bhavet |
saṃvatsare vyatīte tu spṛṣṭvaivāpo viśuddhyati || 5.76 ||
atikrānta iti | āśaucātikrame sapiṇḍajananaṃ maraṇaṃ vā jñātvā trirātram āśaucavān bhavati | saṃvatsare punar atīte jñātvā snātvaiva śudhyati || 5.76 ||
nirdaśaṃ jñātimaraṇaṃ śrutvā putrasya janma ca |
savāsā jalam āplutya śuddho bhavati mānavaḥ || 5.77 ||
nirdaśaṃ jñātimaraṇam iti | nirgatadaśāhādikaṃ sapiṇḍamaraṇaṃ śrutvā putrasya ca janma manuṣyaḥ śrutvā sacailaṃ snātvā śuddho bhavaty evaṃ cāntaroktena | trirātreṇāsya sahasye vikalpaḥ || 5.77 ||
bāle deśāntarasthe ca pṛthakpiṇḍe ca saṃsthite |
savāsā jalam āplutya sadya eva viśudhyati || 5.78 ||
bāla iti | ādeśāntarasthe ‘pi dantajātyūne bāle deśāntarasthe vā bāle ‘pi asapiṇḍasamānodake | deśāntarastheti | mṛte sacailaṃ snātvā sada eva viśudhyati | deśāntarasthasapiṇḍajananamaraṇayos taccheṣaśuddhir uktā | samānodakānāṃ ca tryahāc chuddhir uktā tadvaikalpikam anena sadyaḥśaucam ucyate || 5.78 ||
antardaśāhe cet syātāṃ punarmaraṇajanmanī |
tāvat syād aśucir vipro yāvat tat syād anirdaśam || 5.79 ||
antardaśāha iti | tāvat syād aśucir vipro yāvat tat syād anirdaśam | daśāhādimadhye yadi janmapunarmaraṇe maraṇaṃ janane jananaṃ syāt | punaḥśabdāt,
sūtake sūtakaṃ bhūyo yady api syāt kathaṃcana |
taccheṣeṇaiva śuddhiḥ syān mṛtake mṛtakaṃ tathā ||
ity aṅgirasaḥ smaraṇāt | ca tadā tāvatkālam eva brāhmaṇāder āśaucaṃ syād yāvat pūrvotpannam āśaucaṃ nāpagataṃ tat paścād atītam | daśāhādi yāvat tatrāpi ca “rātriśeṣe dvābhyāṃ prabhāte tisṛbhiḥ” iti gautamīyo viśeṣo draṣṭavyaḥ || 5.79 ||
trirātram āhur āśaucam ācārye saṃsthite sati |
tasya putre ca patnyāṃ ca divārātram iti sthitiḥ || 5.80 ||
trirātram iti | ācārye prete trirātram āśaucam āhus tatputrapatnyoś cāhorātram iti śāstramaryādā || 5.80 ||
śrotriye tūpasaṃpanne trirātram aśucir bhavet |
mātule pakṣiṇīṃ rātriṃ śiṣyartvigbāndhaveṣu ca || 5.81 ||
śrotriya iti | vedādhyāyini kenāpi maitryādinā nimittena nikaṭavartiś ca sabrahmacāriṇy api prete trirātram āśaucavān bhavati | mātulaśiṣyarvigbhāgineyādiṣu ubhayato ‘hardvayaṃ pakṣadvayam iva | yasyās tāṃ rātrim āśaucaṃ bāndhavatve ‘pi mātulasya punarvacanaṃ mātuleyādīnāṃ vikalpārtham || 5.81 ||
prete rājani sajyotir yasya syād viṣaye sthitaḥ |
aśrotriye tv ahaḥ kṛtsnam anūcāre tathā gurau || 5.82 ||
prete rājani sajyotir yasya syād viṣayeṣu ceti | yatsaṃbandhini deśe brāhmaṇādisthitaḥ tasmin rājani prete sajyotiṣā dinādityena rātrau tārakābhiḥ saha vartate iti sajyotir divotpanne divaivāśaucaṃ rātrāv upajāyate rātrāv eveti | “śrotriye saṃgate tridivasam” ity uktam aśrotriye punaḥ saṃgate ‘haḥ samagraṃ rātrau na bhavati | rātrāv utpanne rātrim eva nāhau aṅgādi avaktir iva (?) “alpaṃ vā bahu vā yasya” ity evaṃ dharmake gurāv evam eva || 5.82 ||
śuddhyed vipro daśāhena dvādaśāhena bhūmipaḥ |
vaiśyaḥ pañcadaśāhena śūdro māsena śuddhyati || 5.83 ||
śuddhyed iti | anupūrvaśaś caturṇāṃ varṇānāṃ pretaśuddhiṃ vakṣyāmīty uktamatāpūrvo daśāhavidhiḥ | brāhmaṇaviṣaya evaivaṃ cāsau daśāhena brāhmaṇaḥ śuddhyed itītaravidhitsayā vihitam anūdyate | jananamaraṇanimitta āśauce | kṣatriyo dvādaśena vaiśyaḥ pañcadaśāhena śūdro māsena śudhyati || 5.83 ||
na vardhayed aghāhāni pratyūhen nāgniṣu kriyāḥ |
na ca tat karma kurvāṇaḥ sanābhyo ‘py aśucir bhavet || 5.84 ||
na vardhayed iti | yasyāgnihotrādyapekṣam arvāk saṃcayanād asthnām ity evamādyaśaucasaṃkoca uktaḥ sa tu kriyārthaṃ tāvad evāśaucam āśrayen na tu niṣkarmā sukham āsiṣyuḥ iti buddhyā | aśaucasaṃyuktāny ahāni vardhayet tasyāṃ kriyāyāṃ daśāhaṃ nāśrayet | rateṣ cāgniṣv api hotrādikriyā na nirasyan na ca tacchrautaṃ karma kurvan sapiṇḍo ‘py aśuddho bhavati kiṃ punar anyat tasmād anyaṃ kārayet | tad uktaṃ gṛhye “nityāni nivartante | vaitanavarjaṃ śālāgnau caike anya etāni kuryuḥ” iti || 5.84 ||
divākīrtim udakyāṃ ca patitaṃ sūtikāṃ tathā |
śavaṃ tatspṛṣṭinaṃ caiva spṛṣṭvā snānena śūdhyati || 5.85 ||
divākīrtim iti | caṇḍālarajasvalāpatitasūtikāśavatatspṛṣṭinaḥ spṛṣṭvā snānena śūddhyati | anye tu caṇḍālādisparśe pi tatspṛṣṭinyāyam icchanti | kiṃ tu,
udakyāśucibhiḥ snāyāt saṃspṛṣṭas tair upaspṛśet | [YDh 3.30]
ity evaṃ yājñavalkyavacanam asti | itthaṃ smṛtyarthe ‘pi samācāro ‘nveṣyaḥ || 5.85
ācamya prayato nityaṃ japed aśucidarśane |
saurān mantrān yathotsāhaṃ pāvamānīś ca śaktitaḥ || 5.86 ||
ācamyeti | prakṛtacaṇḍālādyaśucidarśane sarvadācamya śuciḥ | sa sūryadaivatyān mantrān yathāsāmarthyaṃ pāvamānīś ca “svādiṣṭhaye”tyādyāḥ śaktyā japet || 5.86 ||
nāraṃ spṛṣṭvāsthi sasnehaṃ snātvā vipro viśudhyati |
ācamyaiva tu niḥsnehaṃ gām ālabhyārkam īkṣya vā || 5.87 ||
nāram iti | snehayuktaṃ mānuṣāsthi spṛṣṭvā brāhmaṇādi snātvā viśudhyati | snehaśūnyaṃ punaḥ spṛṣṭvopaspṛśya gosparśanādityekṣaṇayor anyatarat kṛtvā viśuddhyati || 5.87 ||
ādiṣṭī nodakaṃ kuryād ā vratasya samāpanāt |
samāpte tūdakaṃ kṛtvā trirātreṇaiva śudhyati || 5.88 ||
ādiṣṭī nodakam iti | vratādeśayogād ādiṣṭī brahmacārī brahmacāriṇaḥ “śavakarmaṇāṃ vratān nivṛttir anyatra mātāpitror” iti vasiṣṭhasmaraṇān mātāpitṛvyatiriktānāṃ sapiṇḍādīnām aniṣṭodkasamāvartanān na kuryat | samāpte punaḥ brahmacarye tebhyo ‘niṣṭodakaṃ datvā trirātreṇaiva śudhyati || 5.88 ||
vṛthāsaṃkarajātānāṃ pravrajyāsu ca tiṣṭhatām |
ātmanas tyāgināṃ caiva nivartetodakakriyā || 5.89 ||
vṛthāsaṃkarajātānām iti | vṛthājātānāṃ bāhulyenotsṛṣṭasvadharmāṇāṃ saṃkarajātānāṃ vā niyuktāsutādīnāṃ vyatikramasāhacaryāc ca vedabāhyeṣu ca vrateṣu raktapaṭāditvādiṣu vartamānānāṃ tathā || 5.89 ||
pāṣaṇḍam āśritānāṃ ca carantīnāṃ ca kāmataḥ |
garbhabhartṛdruhāṃ caiva surāpīnāṃ ca yoṣitām || 5.90 ||
pākhaṇḍam iti | vedabrāhmavratavartināṃ kulaṭānāṃ ca yathā garbhabhartṛghnīnāṃ surāpīṇāṃ ca dvijātivan niṣedhātikrameṇa carantīnāṃ ca yathāsaṃkhyaṃ dvijātistriyaṃ pratyāha | surālaśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanakādīny abhakṣyāṇi varjayed evaṃvidhānāṃ striyām udakakriyādyaurdhvadehikam āśaucaṃ nivarteta || 5.90 ||
ācāryaṃ svam upādhyāyaṃ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ gurum |
nirhṛtya tu vratī pretān na vratena viyujyate || 5.91 ||
ācāryam iti | svam ācāryaṃ na gurau saṃnihite guruvṛttim ācared iti nyāyenācāryam api mātṛpitrupādhyāyam “alpaṃ vā bahu vā yasya" ityevaṃvidhaṃ guruṃ pretān nirhṛtya brahmacārī luptavrato na bhavati | evaṃ vā nirhṛtya bhavatīti gamyate || 5.91 ||
dakṣiṇena mṛtaṃ śūdraṃ puradvāreṇa nirharet |
paścimottarapūrvas tu yathāyogaṃ dvijanmanaḥ || 5.92 ||
dakṣiṇeneti | śūdraṃ mṛtaṃ dakṣiṇadigvartinā nagarādidvāreṇa nirharet | paścimottarapūrvadigvartibhiḥ puradvārair yathākramaṃ vaiśyakṣatriyabrāhmaṇān nirharet | amaṅgalatvāc chūdrādiprakrameṇātra dharmābhidhānam || 5.92 ||
na rājñām aghadoṣo ‘sti vratināṃ na ca sattriṇām |
aindraṃ sthānam upāsīnā brahmabhūtā hi te sadā || 5.93 ||
na rājñām iti | yato rājāna aindraṃ sthānam ādhipatyapadam āśritā brahmacāriṇaḥ cāndrāyaṇādivratapravṛttāḥ sattriṇaś ca gavāmayanādisthā brahmatvaprāptyabhimukhā atas teṣāṃ svakarmaṇy āśaucākhyo doṣo na bhavati | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “na rājñāṃ rājakarmaṇi na vratināṃ vrate na satriṇāṃ sattre ca” iti evaṃ ca || 5.93 ||
rājño māhātmike sthāne sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate |
prajānāṃ parirakṣārtham āsanaṃ cātra kāraṇam || 5.94 ||
rājña iti | mahānātmasvarūpaṃ yasya sthānasya tasmin rājākhye pade vartamānasya rājñaḥ sadyahśaucam upadiśyate | atra ca sadyaḥśauce śāntihomadurbhikṣānnadānādi-prajāparipālanārthaṃ rājño yad avasthānaṃ tat kāraṇam || 5.94 ||
ḍimbhāhavahatānāṃ ca vidyutā pārthivena ca |
gobrāhmaṇasya caivārthe yasya cecchati pārthivaḥ || 5.95 ||
ḍimbāhavahatānām iti | ḍimbe janaughasaṃpīḍane aśastrakalahe vā hatānāṃ tathā saṃgrāmāśanihatānāṃ rājñā vā saṃgrāme ‘pi hatānāṃ gobrāhmaṇaparitrāṇārtham asaṃgrāme ‘pi vyāghrādivyāpāditānāṃ yasya ca purohitādeḥ svakāryavighātārthaṃ pṛthivīpatir icchati tasyāpi sadyaḥśaucam || 5.95 ||
somāgnyarkānilendrāṇāṃ vittāppatyor yamasya ca |
aṣṭānāṃ lokapālānāṃ vapur dhārayate nṛpaḥ || 5.96 ||
somāgnyarkānilendrāṇām iti | candrāgnyādityavāyvindrayamānāṃ dhanapateḥ vaiśravaṇasyāpāṃ pateś ca varuṇasyety eṣāṃ lokapālānāṃ saṃbandhi tejaḥ pārthivo dhārayati || 5.96 ||
lokeśādhiṣṭhito rājā nāsyāśaucaṃ vidhīyate |
śaucāśaucaṃ hi martyānāṃ lokeśaprabhavāpy ayam || 5.97 ||
lokeśādhiṣṭhita iti | evamuktanītyā yato lokeśvarākrānto rājāno ‘nyasyāśaucaṃ vidhīyate svakāryam upadiśyate | yasmān manuṣyāṇāṃ yac chaucam āśaucaṃ tal lokeśebhyaḥ prabhavaty apaiti ca | tasmād anyaśaucāśaucotpādanopāsanasamarthalokeśarūpasya rājñaḥ kuta evātmano ‘śaucam iti prakṛtārthastutiḥ || 5.97 ||
udyatair āhave śastaiḥ kṣatradharmahatasya ca |
sadyaḥ saṃtiṣṭhate yajñas tathāśaucam iti sthitiḥ || 5.98 ||
udyatair iti | saṃgrāma udyataiḥ khaḍgādibhiḥ aparāṅmukhatvādikṣatriyadharmayuktasya hatasya tatkṣaṇād eva yajñasamāptis tatkālam eva yajñaphalasaṃbandhāt tathāśaucam api tatkṣaṇād eva samāptim etīty evaṃ śāstramaryādā || 5.98 ||
vipraḥ śudhyaty apaḥ spṛṣṭvā kṣatriyo vāhanāyudham |
vaiśyaḥ pratodaṃ raśmīn vā yaṣṭiṃ śūdraḥ kṛtakriyaḥ || 5.99 ||
vipra iti | āśaucānte kṛtaśrāddhādikriyo brāhmaṇo ‘pa aucityād dhastena spṛṣṭvā kṣatriyaś ca khaḍgādikaṃ vaiśyo balīvardādipratodam ayaḥprāntaṃ pragrahaṃ vāśvādiniyamarajjuṃ śūdro yaṣṭiṃ spṛṣṭvā śudhyati || 5.99 ||
etad vo ‘bhihitaṃ śaucaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu dvijottamāḥ |
asapiṇḍeṣu sarveṣu pretaśuddhiṃ nibodhata || 5.100 ||
etad iti | he brāhmaṇāḥ | sapiṇḍeṣu preteṣu yac chaicaṃ yuṣmākam uktaṃ adhunā tv asapiṇḍeṣu sarveṣu mṛteṣu pretaśuddhiṃ śṛṇuteti bhṛgur maharṣīn āha || 5.100 ||
asapiṇḍaṃ dvijaṃ pretaṃ vipro nirhṛtya bandhuvat |
viśudhyati trirātreṇa mātur āptāṃś ca bāndhavān || 5.101 ||
asapiṇḍam iti | asapiṇḍaṃ mṛtaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ brāhmaṇo bandhuvat snehena nirhṛtya mātuś ca saṃnikṛṣṭān bāndhavān mātulādīn nirhṛtya trirātreṇa śudhyati || 5.101 ||
yady annam atti teṣāṃ tu daśāhenaiva śūdhyati |
anadann annam ahnaiva na cet tasmin hṛhe vaset || 5.102 ||
yadīti | sa nirhārako yadi teṣām āśaucināṃ saṃbandhyannam aśnāti tadā daśāhena śudhyati, na trirātreṇa | annaṃ punas tatsambandhi anaśnan yadi tatsaṃbandhini gṛhe na nivaset tadāhorātreṇaiva, na trirātradaśarātrābhyām | evaṃ ca nirhārakasya nivāsānnabhojanarahitasyāhorātram anaśnatas tatra vasataḥ pūrvoktaṃ trirātram aśnato vasataś ca daśāham || 5.102 ||
anugamyecchayā pretaṃ jñātim ajñātim eva ca |
snātvā sacailaṃ spṛṣṭvāgniṃ ghṛtaṃ prāsya viśudhyati || 5.103 ||
anugamyeti | bandhum anyaṃ pretaṃ vā buddhipūrvakam anugamya snānaṃ kṛtvā tato ‘gniṃ spṛṣṭvā tadanu ca ghṛtaprāśanaṃ kṛtvā viśudhyati || 5.103 ||
na vipraṃ sveṣu tiṣṭhatsu mṛtaṃ śūdreṇa nāyayet |
asvargyā hy āhutiḥ sā syāc chūdrasaṃsparśadūṣitā || 5.104 ||
neti | putrādir vipraṃ mṛtaṃ samānajātiṣu tiṣṭhatsu sthiteṣu satsu śūdreṇa na nirhārayet | yasmāt sā śārīrāhutiḥ śūdrasparśaduṣṭā satī mṛtasya svargalokasādhanī na bhavaty evaṃ ca doṣanirdeśāt sveṣu tiṣṭhatsu iti vivakṣitam || 5.104 ||
jñānaṃ tapo ‘gnir āhāro mṛn mano vāry upāñjanam |
vāyuḥ karmārkakālau ca śuddheḥ kartṝni dehinām || 5.105 ||
idānīṃ kānicid vākyāntarasiddhāni śuddhisādhanāni saṃkṣepāvabodhāya prasaṃgato ‘nuvadituṃ kānicic ca prasaṅgato ‘ṃśapūrvāni vidhātum āha jānam iti | jñānaṃ buddheḥ śuddhirtartṛ, yathā vakṣyati “buddhir jñānena śudhyati” iti | saṃkalpakaṃ mano niścāyikā buddhir iti | buddhir manaso bhedaḥ | evaṃ tapo vedavidāṃ yathābhidhāsyati “tapasā vedavittamāḥ” ity agnir yathā vadiṣyati “punaḥpākena mṛnmayam” ity āhārto yathā kathayiṣyati “haviṣyena yavāgvā vā” iti mṛvāriṇī yathā jalpiṣyati “mṛdvāti cādeyam arthavad” iti | mano yathā khyāsyati “manaḥpūtaṃ sadā bhaved” iti | upāñjanam upalepanaṃ yathā bhaṇiṣyati “saṃmāṛjanenāñjanena” iti | vāyor iha śāstre śodhyaṃ na nirdiṣṭaṃ tasya mṛtparṇatṛṇakāṣṭānāṃ ca cāṇḍālādivāyasaiḥ sparśane śaucam “somasūryāṃsumārutaiḥ” ity evamādiviplāvyādyuktaṃ karma yathā kīrtayiṣyati “yajeta vāśvamedhena” ityadityau yathoktaṃ “gām ālabhyārkaṃ vīkṣya vā” iti kālo yathoktam “śudhyed vipro daśāhena” ity evaṃ jñānādīni manuṣyāṇāṃ sākṣād upayogyadravyadvāreṇa ca śuddheḥ kartṝṇi || 5.105 ||
sarveṣām eva śaucānām arthaśaucaṃ paraṃ smṛtam |
yo ‘rthe śucir hi sa śucir na mṛdvāriśuciḥ śuciḥ || 5.106 ||
sarveṣām iti | sarveṣāṃ mṛdvārīndiryamanaḥśaucādīnāṃ madhyād arthaśaucaṃ paradrādīcchāparihārārthaṃ prakṛṣṭam ṛṣībhiḥ smṛtam | yasmād yo ‘rthe śuddhaḥ sa śuddho boddhavyo yaḥ punar mṛdvāriśucir arthe cāśuddheḥ so ‘śuddha eva || 5.106 ||
kṣāntyā śudhyanti vidvāṃso dānenākāryakāriṇaḥ |
pracchannapāpā japyena tapasā vedavittamāḥ || 5.107 ||
kṣāṇtyeti | ye śāstrajñās te ‘parādhakṣamayā śudhyanti | yathā vakṣyati (11.245) “mahāyajña(kriyā) kṣamā | nāśayanty āsu pāpāni” iti | akāryakāriṇo dānena | yathā vakṣyati “sarvasvaṃ vā vedavide” iti | aprakhyātapāpā japyena | yathā vakṣyati paraṃ taratsamandīyam iti | vedatattvārthajñāś cāndrāyaṇādinā prāyaścittaprakāreṇa vakṣyamāṇena || 5.107 ||
mṛttoyaiḥ śudhyate śodhyaṃ nadī vegena śudhyati |
rajasā strī manoduṣṭā saṃnyāsena dvijottamāḥ || 5.108 ||
mṛttoyair iti | mṛttoyaiḥ śudhyate śodhanīyaṃ tāvan mṛdvāri cādeyam iti vakṣyamāṇena prakāreṇa | nadī ca nidāghādāv atyalpapravāhatayā śudhyaty upahatapulinadeśā satī varṣādijanitenodakavegenāvakṣālitapārāvāratayā śudhyati | sakṛt strī ca kathaṃcin manasā parapuruṣasaṃkalpā ārtavena śudhyati | brāhmaṇāś ca saṃnyāsena ṣaṣṭādhyāyavakṣyamāṇena śudhyanti || 5.108 ||
adbhir gātrāṇi śudhyanti manaḥ satyena śudhyati |
vidyātapobhyāṃ bhūtātmā buddhir jñānena śudhyati || 5.109 ||
adbhir iti | aṅgāni svedādyupahatāni adbhiḥ śudhyanti | manaś ca kathaṃcid asatsaṃkalpopahataṃ satyābhidhānena śudhyati | pāramārthikaś cātmāparamātmākhyakośakārakṛmivad ātmaiva rāgādimalajālāvabaddho vidyayopaniṣadādyuktātmopāsanābhis tapasā cākhilamalalopī brahmasvarūpa evāvatiṣṭate | tathā buddhir viparītārthagrāhiṇī tenāśuddhāsatī yathā bhūtārthagrāhiṇā vijñānena śudhyati || 5.109 ||
eṣa śaucasya vaḥ proktaḥ śārīrasya vinirṇayaḥ |
nānāvidhānāṃ dravyāṇāṃ śuddheḥ śṛṇuta nirṇayam || 5.110 ||
eṣa iti | eṣa śarīrasaṃbandhinaḥ śaucasya yuṣmākaṃ vicāra uktaḥ | idānīṃ nānāprakārāṇāṃ dravyāṇāṃ śuddheḥ nirṇayaṃ śṛṇuta || 5.110 ||
taijasānāṃ maṇīnāṃ ca sarvasyāśmamayasya ca |
bhasvanādbhir mṛdā caiva śuddhir uktā manīṣibhiḥ || 5.111 ||
taijasānām iti | agnitejasā yāni dravībhavanti tāni taijasāni suvarṇādīni | evaṃ prasiddhes teṣāṃ maṇīnāṃ pāṣāṇamayasya ca sarvasya cakārān mṛdāder api adbhir mṛdbhasmanoś cānyatareṇa lepagandhāpakarṣaṇākhyaikakāryatvād vikalpitena vidvadbhir manvādibhiḥ śuddhir uktā | idaṃ samānajātīyocchiṣṭalepāmedhyadravyasaṃsparśādiṣv alpopaghātaviṣayam | tathā copaghātaviṣaye śuddhyantarāṇi dṛśyante | yathā baudhāyanādibhis taijasānāṃ mūtrapurīṣaśukrāsṛkkuṇapamayair atyantavāsitānām āvartanam | alpakālasaṃsargeṣu parilekhanam, sparśamātropaghāte tu triḥsaptakṛtvaḥ parimārjanāyuktam | āvartanaṃ punaḥkaraṇam, parilekhanaṃ takṣaṇam || 5.111 ||
nirlepaṃ kāñcanaṃ bhāṇḍam adbhir eva viśudhyati |
abjam aśmamayaṃ caiva rājataṃ cānupaskṛtam || 5.112 ||
nirlepam iti | ucchiṣṭādileparahitaṃ pānīyapānādyupahataṃ sauvarṇabhāṇḍam abjaṃ ca rūpyamārakatapūritam adbhir eva kevalābhir viśudhyati, na mṛdbhasmasahitābhiḥ || 5.112 ||
apām agneś ca saṃyogād dharme raupyaṃ ca nirbabhau |
tasmāt tayoḥ svayonyaiva nirṇeko guṇavattaraḥ || 5.113 ||
apām iti | “agnir vai vāruṇānīrakāmayata” ityādi vede śrūyate | tatrāgneḥ suvarṇam indriyaṃ varuṇādīnāṃ rajatam ity evaṃ śrūyate | evaṃ cāgnyāpaḥsaṃyogād yataḥ suvarṇaṃ rūpyaṃ codbhūtam ataḥ tayoḥ svakāraṇenaivodakākhyena tathaikakāraṇakena bhasmanaiva śodhanaṃ praśasyataram evaṃ ca mṛdaḥ kāñcanarajatayor bhasmātiśayavat || 5.113 ||
tāmrāyaḥkāṃsyaraityānāṃ trapuṇaḥ sīsakasya ca |
śaucaṃ yathārhaṃ kartavyaṃ kṣārāmlodakavāribhiḥ || 5.114 ||
tāmrāyaḥkāṃsyaraityānām iti | tāmralohakāṃsyapittalatrapusīsānāṃ bhasmodakalāñjikakādibhir malanirharaṇātikrameṇa śaucaṃ kartavyam | idaṃ ca yatra mṛdbhasmanām uccchiṣṭādimalanirharaṇāsāmarthaṃ tadviṣayam, yathārhavacanāt || 5.114 ||
dravāṇāṃ caiva sarveṣāṃ śuddhir utpavanaṃ smṛtam |
prokṣaṇaṃ saṃhatānāṃ ca dāravāṇāṃ ca takṣaṇam || 5.115 ||
dravāṇām iti | dravāṇāṃ ghṛtādīnāṃ baudhāyanādyabhihitaprasthamātrapramāṇānāṃ śvakākādyucchiṣṭānām amedhyasaṃsargakoṭādyupahatānām amedhyaspṛṣṭadravyaspṛṣṭānāṃ cotplavanam “kuśāgrābhyāṃ pavamānaḥ suvarjana” ity anuvākeneti smṛtyantarokto dadhidugdhaprakṣepaṇaparyantaḥ | samānajātīyadravyasevanaṃ vā smṛtam | saṃhatānāṃ ca śayanādīnām ucchiṣṭādyupahatānāṃ kṣālitaikadeśānām abhyukṣaṇaṃ dārumayānāṃ cocchiṣṭalepādyupahatānāṃ takṣaṇam || 5.115 ||
mārjanaṃ yajñapātrāṇāṃ pāṇinā yajñakarmaṇi |
camasānāṃ grahāṇāṃ ca śuddhiḥ prakṣālaṇena tu || 5.116 ||
mārjanam iti | yajñapātrāṇāṃ juhvājyadhānīprabhṛtīnāṃ yajñakriyāyāṃ karmāṅgatayā hastena parimārjanam | camasagrahāṇāṃ ca yajñapātraviśeṣāṇāṃ karmāṅgatayaiva prakṣālanena śuddhiḥ || 5.116 ||
carūṇāṃ sruksruvāṇāṃ ca śuddhir uṣṇena vāriṇā |
sphyaśūrpaśakaṭānāṃ ca musalolūkhalasya ca || 5.117 ||
carūṇām iti | caruśūrpādīnāṃ yajñāṅgabhūtānāṃ bhāṇḍaviśeṣāṇāṃ tadaṅgatayoṣṇodakena śuddhiḥ || 5.117 ||
adbhis tu prokṣaṇaṃ śaucaṃ bahūnāṃ dhānyavāsasām |
prakṣālanena tv alpānām adbhiḥ śaucaṃ vidhīyate || 5.118 ||
adbhir iti | “anekoddhārye dāruśile bhūmisame” iti bodhāyanīyādyanekoddhāryasya dravyāntarasthālpaśaucatvadarśanāt puruṣābhāvād ūrdhvaṃ dhānyavastrāṇāṃ cāṇḍālādir abhyukṣaṇam | tadalpānāṃ punar adbhiḥ prakṣālanaṃ vidhīyate || 5.118 ||
cailavac carmaṇāṃ śuddhir vaidalānāṃ tathaiva ca |
śākamūlaphalānāṃ tu dhānyavac chuddhir iṣyate || 5.119 ||
cailavad iti | spṛśyacarmaṇāṃ vaidalānāṃ ca tarutvagādinirmitānāṃ vastravac chuddhiḥ | śākamūlaphalānāṃ punaḥ pakvāpakvabhedenāvahatānavahataśuddhāśuddhadhānyavac chuddhir iṣyate | cailavad iti siddhe dhānyavad ity ārambhāt || 5.119 ||
kauśeyāvikayor ūṣaiḥ kutapānām ariṣṭakaiḥ |
śrīphalair aṃśupaṭṭānāṃ kṣaumāṇāṃ gaurasarṣapaiḥ || 5.120 ||
kauśeyāvikayor ūṣair iti | kośanivṛttasya paṭṭaviśeṣasyorṇāmayasya ca “soṣarodakagomūtraiḥ” (YDh 1.186) iti yājñavalkyādidarśanād udakasahitair ūṣaiḥ mṛdviśeṣair evaṃ kutapānām (?) ājalomanirmitānāṃ sodakenāriṣṭakacūrṇena | aṃśupaṭṭānāṃ valkalatanukṛtānāṃ paṭṭānāṃ sodakabilvacūrṇena kṣumādikṛtānāṃ sodakena gaurasarṣapacūrṇena śuddhir iṣyate || 5.120 ||
kṣaumavac chaṅkhaśṛṅgāṇām asthidantamayasya ca |
śuddhir vijānatā kāryā gomūtreṇodakena vā || 5.121 ||
kṣaumavad iti | śaṅkhaśṛṅgāspṛśyāsthidantamalānāṃ kṣaumavad gaurasarṣapacūrṇena sodakena gomūtreṇa vā malopaghātaviśeṣajñena śuddhiḥ kāryā || 5.121 ||
prokṣaṇāt tṛṇakāṣṭhaṃ ca palālaṃ caiva śudhyati |
mārjanopāñjanair veśma punaḥpākena mṛnmayam || 5.122 ||
madyair mūtraiḥ purīṣair vā ṣṭhivanaiḥ pūyaśoṇitaiḥ |
saṃspṛṣṭaṃ naiva śudhyeta punaḥpākena mṛnmayam || 5.123 ||
prokṣaṇād iti | mārjanollekhanair veśmeti | tṛṇakāṣṭhaṃ palālaṃ cāṇḍālādisparśanād duṣṭam abhyukṣaṇāc chudhyati | tṛṇapalālasāhacaryād gṛhabhāṇḍavyatiriktam atra kāṣṭhaṃ vijñeyam | tatra tūdakaṃ dāravāṇāṃ cābhyukṣaṇam ityādi gṛhaṃ ca śavodakyāpraveśādispṛṣṭam ityādimalāpakarṣaṇe caṇḍālādipraveśād duṣṭaṃ śavādiduṣṭaṃ sudhāgomayādinopalepanena yan mṛnmayaṃ cocchiṣṭādyupahatadravyādispṛṣṭaṃ punaḥpākena pākaśabdān navatvāpādanenātyantopahatasya punas tyāga eva | yathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ:
madyair mūtrapurīṣair vā ṣṭhīvanaiḥ pūyaśoṇitaiḥ |
saṃspṛṣṭaṃ naiva śudhyeta punaḥpākena mṛnmayam || iti || 5.122 ||
saṃmārjanena dāhena sekenollekhanena ca |
gavāṃ ca parivāsena bhūmiḥ śudhyati pañcabhiḥ || 5.124 ||
saṃmārjanena dāheneti | avakaraśodhanagomadhūpalepanakṣīrodakagomūtrādisekatakṣaṇair ahorātramātraṃ bahugonivāsena cocchiṣṭamūtrapurīṣacaṇḍālaśmaśānādyupaghātaviśeṣāpekṣayā vyastaiḥ samastair vā pañcabhir bhūmiḥ śūdhyati || 5.124 ||
pakṣijagdhaṃ gavāghrātam avadhūtam avakṣutam |
dūṣitaṃ keśakīṭaiś ca mṛtprakṣepeṇa śudhyati || 5.125 ||
pakṣidagdham iti | atyantānupahataṃ śukādipakṣyucchiṣṭaṃ goghrātaṃ avadhūtaṃ kṛtavāsopari avadhūnanaṃ tasyopari (?) kṣuvathukaṃ keśāpahatam amedhyasaṃsarpikṛmyupahataṃ jagdhaśabdād annaṃ mṛprakṣepeṇa śudhyati || 5.125 ||
yāvan nāpaity amedhyāktād gandho lepaś ca tatkṛtaḥ |
tāvan mṛd vāri cādeyaṃ sarvāsu dravyaśuddhiṣu || 5.126 ||
yāvan nāpaity amedhyāktād gandho lepaś ca tatkṛtaḥ iti | purīṣādidagdhād yāvat purīṣādisaṃbandhinau gandhalepau nāpagacchataḥ tāvat sarvadravyaśuddhiṣu mṛd vāri pragrahītavyam ity amedhyāktadravyoktānuktaśuddhiviśeṣaviṣayam idam || 5.126 ||
trīṇi devāḥ pavitrāṇi brāhmaṇānām akalpayan |
adṛṣṭam adbhir nirṇiktaṃ yac ca vācā praśasyate || 5.127 ||
trīṇīti | yasya pramāṇena kenacid apy upaghātasaṃbandho na paricchinno yac ca saṃghātopaghātaśaṅkyaṃ tad adbhir nirṇiktam | tathā hārītaḥ: “yad yan mīmāṃsyaṃ syāt tad adbhiḥ saṃsparśāc chuci bhavati” iti | yac copaghātasaṃdehe sati prakṣālanāsahaṃ “śuddham bhavatv” iti brāhmaṇasya vācā praśasyate | tāni trīṇi pavitrāṇi brāhmaṇānāṃ devāḥ kalpitavantaḥ || 5.127 ||
āpaḥ śuddhā bhūmigatā vaitṛṣṇyaṃ yāsu gor bhavet |
avyāptāś ced amedhyena gandhavarṇarasānvitāḥ || 5.128 ||
āpa iti | yatparimāṇāsv apsu gos tṛṣṇācchedo bhavati tā āpo gandhavarṇarasānvitatvena amedhyena yadi vyāptā na bhavanti tadā bhūmyādigatāḥ śuddhā aprayataprasaṅgāsu cittāśaṅkāyām idaṃ bhūmigrahaṇaṃ nāntarikṣāpāṃ śuddhatvavyāvṛttyartham || 5.128 ||
nityaṃ śuddhaḥ kāruhastaḥ paṇye yac ca prasāritam |
brahmacārigataṃ bhaikṣyaṃ nityaṃ medhyam iti sthitiḥ || 5.129 ||
nityam iti | kāroḥ sūpacārāder devabrāhmaṇādyarthe ‘pi pāke dravyaprayojanāpakṣa-śuddhiviśeṣākaraṇe ‘pi suśuddhyaiva hastaḥ śuddhaḥ, tathā aśauce ‘pi “kārūṇāṃ kārukarmaṇi” iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt | svavyāpāramātre hastaḥ śuddhaḥ hastapradhānaprāyatvāc ca tatkarmaṇāṃ hastagrahaṇam | tathā yad vikretavyam āpaṇabhūmau vitatya sthāpitaṃ tad anekakretṛsāmānyakaraparighaṭitam api “nāpaṇīyam annam aśnīyāt” iti śaṅkhasmaraṇāt | siddhānnavarjaṃ śuci brahmacaryādigataṃ bhaikṣyam anācāntastrīpradānaśuci rathākramaṇādināpi nityaṃ śuddham iti śāstramaryādā || 5.129 ||
nityam āsyaṃ śuci strīṇāṃ śakuniḥ phalapātane |
prasrave ca śucitr vatsaḥ śvā mṛgagrahaṇe śuciḥ || 5.130 ||
nityam iti | “striyaś carati saṃsargam” iti gautamasmaraṇāt tatkālaṃ nityaṃ strīṇām āsyaṃ śuci | tathā kākādyatyantāhatapakṣicañcughātapātiphalaṃ śuci | vatsamukhaṃ ca dohavelāyāṃ kṣīraprasnāvane śuci | śvā ca yadā mṛgādi hantuṃ gṛhṇāti tadā tadvyāpāre śuciḥ || 5.130 ||
svabhir hatasya yan māṃsaṃ śuci tan manur abravīt |
kravyādbhiś ca hatasyānyaiś caṇḍālādyaiś ca dasyubhiḥ || 5.131 ||
ata evam ataḥ śvabhir iti | kravyādbhiś ca hastasyānyaś cāṇḍālādyaiś ca dasyubhiḥ | śvabhir hatasya mṛgāder yan māṃsaṃ śucīty evaṃ manur abyadhāt tathānyair api cāmamāṃsabhakṣyaiḥ śyenādibhiś cāṇḍālapulindādibhiś ca mṛgādivadhajīvibhir hatasyeti || 5.131 ||
ūrdhvaṃ nābher yāni khāni tāni medhyāni sarvaśaḥ |
yāny adhastāny amedhyāni dehāc caiva malāś cyutāḥ || 5.132 ||
ūrdhvam iti | nābher upari yānīndriyāṇi tāni sarvāṇi śucīnīty evaṃ malaśūnyasparśe sati nāśucīni bhavanti (?) | yāni punar nābher adhastāny aśuddhāni tāni nābhyapekṣam adhaḥ chidrāṇāṃ yānīti bahuvacanam | tathā vakṣyamāṇamalāḥ śarīrataḥ svasthānāc cyutā aśuddhāḥ || 5.132 ||
makṣikā vipruṣaś chāyā gaur aśvaḥ sūryaraśmayaḥ |
rajo bhūr vāyur agniś ca sparśe medhyāni nirdiśet || 5.133 ||
makṣikā iti | makṣikā amedhyasparśinyo ‘pi talleparahitāḥ jalakaṇāś ca sparśamātrānumeyāḥ chāyā cāṇḍālādisaṃbandhinī goś ca mukhavarjam | tathā baudhāyanaḥ:
ajāśvā mukhato medhyā gāvo medhyā mukhād ṛte |
mārjāranakulau sparśau śubhāś ca mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ ||
ity aśva ādityaraśmayo rajo vāyuneritaṃ bhūrathyādau cāṇḍālādi spṛṣṭvā gamanamātreṇa vāyvagni cetyādisparśe śucīnīti jānīyāt || 5.133 ||
viṇmūtrotsargaśuddhyarthaṃ mṛdvāri grāhyam arthavat |
daihikānāṃ malānāṃ ca śuddhiṣu dvādaśasv api || 5.134 ||
viṇmūtrotsargaśuddhyartham iti | viṇmūtram utsṛjate yena tasya pādyādeḥ śuddhyarthaṃ gandhalepanirharaṇaprayojanaparyantaṃ mṛdvāri grāhyaṃ grahītavyam, tathā kāyikānāṃ ca vakṣyamāṇamalānāṃ saṃbandhinī śuddhā daśasv api śuddhiṣu lepādyapāsanaparyantaṃ mṛdvāri grāhyam | atra kaścit,
ādadīta mṛdo ‘paś ca ṣaṭsu pūrveṣu śuddhaye |
uttareṣu tu ṣaṭsv adbhiḥ kevalābhir viśudhyati ||
iti smṛtyantaradarśanād uttareṣu karṇamalādiṣu na kadācid api mṛd dātavyeti manyate | tad asat | “dvādaśasv api” ity evam iha codanāt tasmād uttareṣu vikalpaḥ | sa ca deśakālādyapekṣayā vyavasthitavikalpaḥ, yathāha baudhāyanaḥ:
deśaṃ kālaṃ tathātmānaṃ dravyaṃ dravyaprayojanam |
upapattim avasthāṃ ca jñātvā śaucaṃ samācaret || iti |
ato devapitrādyadṛṣṭārthakarmapravṛtta uttareṣv apy ādadīta, anyatra tu neti | ātmā śrotriyatvācārādyatiśayopeto ‘nyathā vā dravyam alpamayā vā bahvyā cāśaucakriyayā prāyaścittārhaṃ dravyaprayojanaṃ dṛṣṭādṛṣṭādyupayogitvenopapattiḥ saṃśodhakadravyasaṃpattir avasthāśarīrāt || 5.134 ||
vasā śukram asṛṅ majjā mūtraviḍ ghrānakarṇaviṭ |
śleṣmāśrudūṣikāsvedo dvādaśaite nṛṇāṃ malāḥ || 5.135 ||
vaseti | vasā kāyasnehaḥ | śukraṃ retaḥ | asṛk rudhiram, majjā śiraḥsthamedo mūtrapurīṣakarṇamalaśleṣmās tv akṣimalasvedā ity ete nṛṇāṃ malāḥ | narādīnām abhakṣyaprāṇīnāṃ malāḥ mūtrapurīṣotsarge sati || 5.135 ||
ekā liṅge gude tisras tathaikatra kare daśa |
ubhayoḥ sapta dātavyā mṛdaḥ śuddhim abhīpsatā || 5.136 ||
eketi | śuddhim icchataikā mṛn mṛdvāry ādeyam arthavad ity udakasahitā liṅge dātavyā | gude ca tisraḥ | tathā ekasmin haste vāme,
dharmavid dakṣiṇaṃ hastam adhaḥ śauce na yojayet |
tathaiva vāmahastena nābher ūrdhvaṃ na śodhayet ||
iti devaladarśanena tenaiva śaucavidhinā daśa dātavyās tatra ubhayor hastayoḥ sapta dātavyāḥ | yat tūktasaṃkhyayā gandhalepakṣayo na bhavati tadā “yāvan nāpaity amedhyāktād” iti vacanāt uktasaṃkhyānatikrameṇāpi tatkṣayaparyantaṃ śaucaṃ kāryam arvāk saṃkhyāpūraṇāl lepādikṣaye ‘pi saṃkhyāvākyārambhasāmarthāt saṃkhyā pūrayitavyaiva || 5.136 ||
etac chaucaṃ gṛhasthānāṃ dviguṇaṃ brahmacāriṇām |
triguṇaṃ syād vanasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ tu caturguṇam || 5.137 ||
etad iti | yad etad “ekā liṅge” ityādi śaucam etad uktaṃ gṛhasthānām eva brahmacāri-vānaprasthabhikṣūṇām etad eva dvitricaturguṇam || 5.137 ||
kṛtvā mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ vā khāny ācānta upaspṛśet |
vedam adhyeṣyamāṇaś ca annam aśnaṃś ca sarvadā || 5.138 ||
kṛtveti | mūtrapurīṣaṃ kṛtvā kṛtapūrvoktaśaucaḥ kṛtānnabhakṣaḥ saṇ śīrṣanyādīndriyachidrāṇi upaspṛśet | ācamanam eva vṛttānurodhāt | evam uktaṃ tathā cottaraśloke viśeṣayiṣyati | vedaṃ cādhyetum icchann annaṃ cāśnan yāvajjīvaṃ ācāmed iti | tac ca dvitīyādhāya ubhayam api “adhyeṣyamāṇas tv ācānto” “nivedya gurave ‘śnīyād ācāmed” ity uktaṃ vratāṅgatvārtham, iha tu śaucāṅgatayocyate || 5.138 ||
trir ācāmed apaḥ pūrvaṃ dviḥ pramṛjyāt tato mukham |
śārīraṃ śaucam icchan hi strī śūdras tu sakṛt sakṛt || 5.139 ||
tathā cāha trir iti | śarīraśuddhim icchan trīn vārān prathamam apa ācāmet tato dvau vārau oṣṭhadeśe mukhaṃ parimṛjyāt | strīśūdraṃ punar ekavāraṃ ācamanārtham apo bhakṣayet
|| 5.139 ||
śūdrāṇāṃ māsikaṃ kāryaṃ vapanaṃ nyāyavartinām |
vaiśyavac chaucakalpaś ca dvijocchiṣṭaṃ ca bhojanam || 5.140 ||
śūdrāṇām iti | śāstramaryādānuvartino dvijātyāśritānāṃ śūdrāṇām āśrayabhūtadvijātīnāṃ māsi māsi muṇḍanaṃ vaiśyavac ca “ekā liṅge” ityādyācamanaparyantaśaucavidhiḥ kāryaḥ, dvijocchiṣṭabhojanaprakṛtatvād ātmasaṃbandhe dātavyam || 5.140 ||
nocchiṣṭaṃ kurvate mukhyā vipruṣo ‘ṅgaṃ patanti yāḥ |
na śmaśrūṇi gatāny āsyaṃ na dantāntaradhiṣṭhitam || 5.141 ||
nocchiṣṭam iti | mukhaniḥsṛtā vipruṣo vyaṅgatvena nipatanti tadocchiṣṭaṃ na janayanti | tasmāt śmaśrūṇy āsyapratiṣṭhāni dantāntaralagnaṃ vānnāvayavādi, dantaśliṣṭaṃ tu dantavad anyatra jihvābhimarśanāt prāk cyuter ity eke | “cyuteṣv āśrāvavad vidyān nirgirann eva tacchuciḥ” ity evaṃ gautamaprakriyā nocchiṣṭaṃ karoti || 5.141 ||
spṛśanti bindavaḥ pādau ya ācāmayataḥ parān |
bhaumikais te samā jñeyā na tair aprayato bhavet || 5.142 ||
spṛśantīti | bhaumikais te samā jñeyā na tair aprayato bhavet | anyeṣām ācamanaṃ dadato ye bindavaḥ pādāv eva spṛśanti na jaṅghādi te tv apahatabhūmyavasthaiḥ śuddhair udakabindubhiḥ tulyā jñeyās tatas tair nācamanārho bhavati || 5.142 ||
ucchiṣṭena tu saṃspṛṣṭo dravyahastaḥ kathaṃcana |
anidhāyaiva tad dravyam ācāntaḥ śucitām iyāt || 5.143 ||
ucchiṣṭeneti | gṛhītadravyaḥ san yady ucchiṣṭena spṛṣṭo bhavati tadā dravyam avasthāpaitat kṛtācamanaḥ śuddhitvaṃ prāpnoti || 5.143 ||
vānto viriktaḥ snātvā tu ghṛtaprāśanam ācaret |
ācāmed eva bhuktvānnaṃ snānaṃ maithuninaḥ smṛtam || 5.144 ||
vānta iti | “kṛtavamano daśaiva tu dviguṇāviraktaḥ” ity āyurvedadarśanād daśavirekān viraktaḥ snātvā ghṛtaprāśanam ācaret | bhuktvānnaṃ punar yadi samantaram eva vamati tad ācāmed eva, na snānaghṛtaprāśane kuryāt | mauthunaṃ ca kṛtavataḥ snānaṃ smṛtam || 5.144 ||
suptvā kṣutvā ca bhuktvā ca niṣṭhīvyoktvānṛtāni ca |
pītvā ‘po ‘dhyeṣyamāṇaś ca ācāmet prayato ‘pi san || 5.145 ||
suptveti | svapnakṣutbhojanaśleṣmanirasanāsatyābhidhānodakapānāni kṛtvādhyayanaṃ ca prāripsan śucir api sann ācāmet | tatra “bhuktvā copaspṛśet samyag” iti dvitīyādhyāye bhuktvācamanam uktaṃ vratāṅgatvārtham iha tu śaucārtham ucyate | tathā vedādhyayanaṃ kāle ‘py adhyeṣamāṇas tv ācānto vedam adhyeṣyamāṇaś ceti dvibhaktam apīhetaravidhivat sārthaṃ nirūpyate || 5.145 ||
eṣa śaucavidhiḥ kṛtsno dravyaśuddhis tathaiva ca |
ukto vaḥ sarvavarṇānāṃ strīṇāṃ dharmān nibodhata || 5.146 ||
eṣa iti | eṣa jananādiśaucavidhiḥ samagro dravyāṇāṃ ca taijasānāṃ śuddhiḥ sakalā sarvavarṇānāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ saṃbandhinī yuṣmākam uktā, adhunā tu strīṇāṃ yo ‘nuṣṭheyā dharmās tāñ chṛṇuta || 5.146 ||
bālayā vā yuvatyā vā vṛddhayā vāpi yoṣitā |
na svātantryeṇa kartavyaṃ kiṃcit kāryaṃ gṛheṣv api || 5.147 ||
bālayeti | bālayauvanavārdhkyāvasthayāpi striyā sūkṣmam api gṛhakāryaṃ svecchayā na kiṃcid api kartavyam || 5.147 ||
bālye pitur vaśe tiṣṭhet pāṇigrāhasya yauvane |
putrāṇāṃ bhartari prete na bhajet strī svatantratām || 5.148 ||
kiṃ tarhi bālya iti | putrāṇāṃ bhartari prete na bhajet strī svatantratām | bālyayauvanabhartṛpramīteṣu pitṛbhartṛputrāṇām āyattā bhavet tadabhāve ‘pi jñātirājāyattā syāt | na tu kadācit svātantryam āśrayet || 5.148 ||
pitrā bhartrā sutair vāpi necched viraham ātmanaḥ |
eṣāṃ hi viraheṇa strī garhye kuryād ubhe kule || 5.149 ||
pitreti | pitṛbhartṛputraiḥ sarvadā sarvair yathāsaṃbhavam ātmanā virahitā na syād yasmād etadviyogenāsatī bhartṛpitṛkule nindite karoti || 5.149 ||
sadā prahṛṣṭayā bhāvyaṃ gṛhakārye ca dakṣayā |
susaṃskṛtopaskarayā vyaye cāmuktahastayā || 5.150 ||
