sadeti | sarvadā bhartari kruddhe ‘pi prasannavadanayā gṛhakarmaṇi caturayā susaṃspṛṣṭakuṇḍakaṭāhādibhāṇḍayā vyaye cānudārayā bhavitavyam || 5.150 ||
yasmai dadyāt pitā tv enāṃ bhrātā vānumate pituḥ |
taṃ śuśrūṣeta jīvantaṃ saṃsthitaṃ ca na laṅghayet || 5.151 ||
yasmai dadyād iti | yasmai pitā pitranumate bhrātā enāṃ dadyāt taṃ jīvantaṃ paricaret, mṛtaṃ ca nātikramet vyabhicāreṇa || 5.151 ||
maṅgalārthaṃ svastyayanaṃ yajñaś cāsāṃ prajāpateḥ |
prayujyate vivāheṣu pradānaṃ svāmyakāraṇam || 5.152 ||
maṅgalārtham iti | yad āsāṃ svastyayanaṃ śāntyādimantravācanaṃ hi prajāpatiyāgaś ca vivāheṣu kriyate tan maṅgalārtham abhīṣṭārthaṃ saṃpattyarthaṃ na tu bhartuḥ svatvāpādanārthaṃ yasmāt prāg vivāhāt yad vāgdānaṃ tad bhartuḥ svāmyajanakaṃ evaṃ ca vāgdānataḥ prabhṛty āsāṃ bhartṛpāratantryaṃ na vivāhāt || 5.152 ||
anṛtāv ṛtukāle ca mantrasaṃskārakṛt patiḥ |
sukhasya nityaṃ dāteha paraloke ca yoṣitaḥ || 5.153 ||
anṛtāv iti | yo vivāhakṛd bhartā “sarvatra vā prītivivarjitam” iti gautamadarśanād ṛtukāle anyadā ca striyā nityam iha loke ca sukhasya dātā tadārādhanenaiva ca svargādilokaprāpteḥ paraloke ca sukhasya dātā || 5.153 ||
viśīlaḥ kāmavṛtto vā guṇair vā parivarjitaḥ |
upacaryaḥ striyā sādhvyā satataṃ devavat patiḥ || 5.154 ||
viśīla iti | durācāro bhāryāntarāsakto vā vidyānuṣṭhānādivarjito vā tathāpi sādhvyā striyā sarvadā deva iva patir ārādhanīyaḥ || 5.154 ||
nāsti strīṇāṃ pṛthagyajño na vrataṃ nāpy upoṣaṇam |
patiṃ śuśrūṣate yena tena svarge mahīyate || 5.155 ||
yathā bhartṛr bhāryāntaram avalambanena yajñakaraṇam asti, naivaṃ bhartāraṃ vinā strīṇāṃ yajño ‘sti, na ca tadanujñātānāṃ kṛcchrādir upavāso vidyate | kiṃ tarhi | bhartṛparicaraṇena svarge mahimānaṃ prāpnomi | tasmāt paralokopayāntaraṃ yasmāc ca bhāvāt || 5.155 ||
pāṇigrāhasya sādhvī strī jīvato vā mṛtasya vā |
patilokam abhīpsantī nācaret kiṃcid apriyam || 5.156 ||
pāṇigrāhasyeti | bhartrā saha dharmācaraṇena svargādilokatvam āptukāmā sādhvī strī bhartur jīvato mṛtasya vā apriyaṃ vyabhicārādi na kiṃcid ācaret, vṛttyasaṃbhave ‘pi ca || 5.156 ||
kāmaṃ tu kṣapayed dehaṃ puṣpamūlaphalaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
na tu nāmāpi gṛhṇīyāt patyau prete parasya tu || 5.157 ||
kāmaṃ tv iti | puṣpamūlaphalaiḥ pavitrair varaṃ dehaṃ kṣapayet na vṛttyarthaṃ vyabhicārabuddhyā parasya nāmāpi uccaret || 5.157 ||
āsītā maraṇāt kṣāntā niyatā brahmacāriṇī |
yo dharma ekapatnīnāṃ kāṃkṣantī tam anuttamam || 5.158 ||
evaṃ ca āsīteti | ekabhartṛkāṇāṃ yo dharmas taṃ sarvadharmotkṛṣṭam abhilaṣantī ā maraṇāt kṣudduḥkhaṃ sahamānānyamanaskā puṃsaḥ prayogaṃ varjayet | na cāpatyārtham api prītimayenāntareṇa tiṣṭhet || 5.158 ||
anekāni sahasrāṇi kumārabrahmacāriṇām |
devaṃ gatāni viprāṇām akṛtvā kulasantatim || 5.159 ||
anekānīti | bālyata eva brahmacāriṇām akṛtadārāṇāṃ vālakhilyānāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ purāṇopavarṇitānāṃ bahusahasrāṇy aṣṭāśītikulavṛddhyartham apatyāny anutpādyaiva svargaṃ gatāni || 5.159 ||
mṛte bhartari sādhvī strī brahmacarye vyavasthitā |
svarge gacchaty aputrāpi yathā te brahmacāriṇaḥ || 5.160 ||
mṛta iti | sādhvī strī mṛte bhartari akṛtapuṃsaṃ prayogā aputrāpi satī svargaṃ prāpnoti | yathā te vālakhilyādyā brahmacāriṇaḥ prāptāḥ, dṛṣṭopakārāpekṣayāpi ca || 5.160 ||
apatyalobhād yā tu strī bhartāram ativartate |
seha nindām avāpnoti paralokāc ca hīyate || 5.161 ||
apatyalobhād iti | yā strī apatyalobhātiśayā bhartāram atikarmya vartate vyabhicarati sā iha loke garhāṃ prāpnoti, bhartrā sahārjitaṃ svargādilokaṃ na prāpnoti | kiṃ cāpatyam api tat tasyā na bhavati || 5.161 ||
nānyotpannā prajāstīha na cānyasya parigrahe |
na dvitīyaś ca sādhvīnāṃ kvacid bhartopadiśyate || 5.162 ||
nānyotpannā iti | bhartṛvyatiriktatayā utpāditā prajāsehaśāstre tasmāt paunarbhavasaṃskārādi yogenāpi na bhartrāntaram āśrayaṇīyam || 5.162 ||
patiṃ hitvāvakṛṣṭaṃ svam utkṛṣṭaṃ yā niṣevate |
nindyaiva sā bhavel loke parapūrveti cocyate || 5.163 ||
patim iti | yā kṣatriyādikā svaṃ patiṃ parityajya utkṛṣṭaṃ brāhmaṇādikam āśrayati sā utkṛṣṭasevanenāpi garhaṇīyaiva loke bhavati | paro ‘nyo bhartā pūrvo yasyāś sā parapūrvety evaṃ ca loke ucyate || 5.163 ||
vyabhicārāt tu bhartuḥ strī loke prāpnoti nindyatām |
śṛgālayoniṃ prāpnoti pāparogaiś ca pīḍyate || 5.164 ||
narāntarasaṃparkāt tu strī ihaloke garhyatāṃ janmāntare ca sṛgālayoniṃ prāpnoti | atyantakrūraiś ca kuṣṭādirogaiḥ saṃpīḍyate || 5.164 ||
patiṃ yā nābhicarati manovāgdehasaṃyatā |
sā bhartṛlokam āpnoti sadbhiḥ sādhvīti cocyate || 5.165 ||
yā manovāgdehasaṃyutā satī tair vāgmanodehaiḥ patiṃ na vyabhicarati sā bhartrā saha arjitān svargādilokān prāpnotīha viśiṣṭaiḥ sādhvīty ucyate | iti vāṅmanasanirāsārthaṃ muktaṃ punarvacanam || 5.165 ||
anena nārī vṛttena manovāgdehasaṃyatā |
ihāgryāṃ kīrtim āpnoti patilokaṃ paratra ca || 5.166 ||
aneneti | manovākkāyasaṃyatayā nāry anena pūrvoktenācāreṇeha loke śreṣṭāṃ kīrtiṃ patilokaṃ ca paraloke prāpnotīty upasaṃhārārtham || 5.166 ||
evaṃvṛttāṃ savarṇāṃ strīṃ dvijātiḥ pūrvamāriṇīm |
dāhayed agnihotreṇa yajñapātraiś ca dharmavit || 5.167 ||
evam iti | dharmajño dvijātir uktācārāṃ samānajātīyāṃ pūrvamṛtastriyaṃ śrautasmārtāgnibhiḥ yajñapātraiś ca sruksruvādibhir dāhayet || 5.167 ||
bhāryāyai pūrvamāriṇyai datvāgnīn antyakarmaṇi |
punardārakriyāṃ kuryāt punarādhānam eva ca || 5.168 ||
bhāryāyā iti | pūrvamṛtāyai bhāryāyai antyeṣṭidāhāv agnīn datvā gārhastyaikāśramanirvāhecchayā putrābhāvād vā prāptaparyantatvādinā vā kenacit kāraṇena vānaprasthādyāśramāntarāśrayeṇa punar vivāhāgniparigrahau kuryāt || 5.168 ||
anena vidhinā nityaṃ pañcayajñān na hāpayet |
dvitīyam āyuṣo bhāgaṃ kṛtadāro gṛhe vaset || 5.169 ||
aneneti | tṛtīyādhyāyoktavidhānena pañcayajñādy ajahan dvitīyam āyurbhāgaṃ kṛtabhāryo gṛhasthāśramam anutiṣṭhet || 5.169 ||
iti śrībhaṭṭamādhavātmajagovindarājaviracitāyāṃ manuṭīkāyāṃ
manvāśayānusāriṇyāṃ
bhakṣyābhakṣyasutakadravyaśuddhistrīdharmanirūpaṇaṃ
nāma pañcamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
ṣaṣṭho ‘dhyāyaḥ
evaṃ gṛhāśrame sthitvā vidhivat snātako dvijaḥ |
vane vaset tu niyato yathāvad vijitendriyaḥ || 6.1 ||
evam iti | kṛtasamāvartano dvija uktaprakriyo yathāśāstraṃ gṛhāśramam anuṣṭhāya tato niyataḥ kṛtaniścayo viśeṣeṇa yatendriyaḥ san yathā vakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇa yathārhati tathā vānaprasthāśramam anutiṣṭhet || 6.1 ||
gṛhasthas tu yadā paśyed valīpalitam ātmanaḥ |
apatyasyaiva cāptyaṃ tadāraṇyaṃ samāśrayet || 6.2 ||
gṛhastha iti | gṛhastho yadātmanas tvakśaithilyaṃ śiraḥpāṇḍuryaṃ pautrāṃś ca paśyet tadā vānaprasthasyārthe araṇyam āśrayet || 6.2 ||
saṃtyajya grāmyam āhāraṃ sarvaṃ caiva paricchadam |
putreṣu bharyāṃ nikṣipya vanaṃ gacchet sahaiva vā || 6.3 ||
saṃtyajyeti | grāmodbhavānnabhakṣaṇaṃ gavāśvādi ca sarvaṃ tyaktvā bhāryā putreṣu samarpya saha vā tayā vanaṃ gacchet || 6.3 ||
agnihotraṃ samādāya gṛhyaṃ cāgniparicchadam |
grāmād araṇyaṃ niḥsṛtya nivasen niyatendriyaḥ || 6.4 ||
agnihotram iti | śrautāgnīn gṛhyaṃ cāgnim agnyupakaraṇaṃ ca sruksruvādi samādāya grāmād bahiḥ niḥsṛtya jitendriyaḥ san nivaset || 6.4 ||
munyannair vividhair medhyaiḥ śākamūlaphalena vā |
etān eva mahāyajñān nirvaped vidhipūrvakam || 6.5 ||
munyannair iti | munyannair aparaiḥ pavitraiḥ śākamūlaphalair vā etān eva pūrvoktān mahāyajñān yathāśāstram anutiṣthet || 6.5 ||
vasīta carma cīraṃ vā sāyaṃ snāyāt prage tathā |
jaṭāś ca bibhṛyān nityaṃ śmaśrulomanakhāni ca || 6.6 |\
“vasīta carma cīraṃ vā mārgaṃ vā cārkṣam eva vā” iti | mṛgādicarma vastrakhaṇḍaṃ vā vṛkṣavalkalaṃ vā ācchadayet | sāyaṃ prātaś ca snāyāt | śmaśrulomanakhāṃś ca nityaṃ dharayet || 6.6 ||
yadbhakṣaḥ syāt tato dadyād baliṃ bhikṣāṃ ca śaktitaḥ |
ammūlaphalabhikṣābhir arcayed āśramāgatam || 6.7 ||
yadbhakṣa iti | yadbhakṣaṃ bhuñjīta tatas tasmāt pūrvoktavat baliṃ bhikṣāṃ ca dadyāt | āśramāgatāṃś ca udakamūlaphalabhikṣādānena pūjayet || 6.7 ||
svādhyāye nityayuktaḥ syād dānto maitraḥ samāhitaḥ |
dātā nityam anādātā sarvabhūtānukampakaḥ || 6.8 ||
svādhyāyaśīlo nityaṃ syād dānto maitrasamāhitaḥ |
tyaktadvandvo ‘niśaṃ dātā sarvabhūtānukampanaḥ ||
vedābhyāsarataḥ śītātapādidvandvasahiṣṇuḥ sarvopakāraśīlasaṃyatamānāḥ syāt || 6.8 ||
vaitānikaṃ ca juhuyād agnihotraṃ yathāvidhi |
darśam asakandayan parva paurṇamāsaṃ ca yogataḥ || 6.9 ||
vaitānikam iti | amāvāsyāpaurṇamasaṃ ca parvayor darśaṃ atyajan śrautam agnihotraṃ yathāśāstraṃ bhāryānikṣepapakṣe ‘pi vacanād dravyalābhārthaṃ ca juhuyāt || 6.9 ||
darśeṣṭyāgrayaṇaṃ caiva cāturmāsyāni cāharet |
turāyaṇaṃ ca kramaśo dākṣasyāyanam eva ca || 6.10 ||
nakṣatreṣṭiṃ tathā darśapaurṇamasāni cāharet |
uttarāyaṇaṃ ca kramaśo dakṣiṇāyanam eva ca ||
nakṣatreṣṭiṃ sūtrakāroktasvarūpabhavam, tasyeṣṭiṃ ca tathā darśapaurṇamāsāni ca uttarāyaṇān dakṣiṇāyaṇān śrautaviśeṣādīn krameṇa kuryāt | yat tu kaiścid uktaṃ sarvam etat smārtaṃ karma darśapaurṇamāsādiśabdaiḥ stutyartham ucyate; śrautasya darśapaurṇamāsādiśrutyā grāmyānnacārupuroṣāśasādhyatvavidhānāt na śaktyā smṛtivrīhyādi nivartata iti — tad asat | ucyate | yato na satyāgatisukhaṃ śabdārthahānaṃ nyāyyaṃ vrīhyādibhir eva kathaṃcid araṇyajātair etāni nivartayiṣyanta iti kim anupapannaṃ svata evottaraśloke “munyannair medhyaiḥ” iti vakṣyati | medho yas tadarhāṇi medhyāni || 6.10 ||
vāsantaśāradair medhyair munyannaiḥ svayam āhṛtaiḥ |
puroṣāśāṃś carūṃś caiva vidhivan nirvapet pṛthak || 6.11 ||
vāsantaśāradair iti | vasantodbhavaiḥ yāgārhaiḥ svayam ānītair munyannaiḥ puroḍāśāṃś carūṃś ca yathāśāstraṃ pratiyogaṃ pratipādayet || 6.11 ||
devatābhyas tu tad buddhvā vanyaṃ medhyataraṃ haviḥ |
śeṣam ātmani yuñjīta lavaṇaṃ ca svayaṃ kṛtam || 6.12 ||
devatābhyaś ceti | tad vanodbhavaṃ saṃpāditahaviḥ atiśayena yāgārhaṃ śāstracoditadevatābhyo hutvā śeṣam ātmany upayojayet | ātmanā ca saṃpāditaṃ lavaṇabhūṣālavādi || 6.12 ||
sthalajaudakaśākāni puṣpamūlaphalāni ca |
medhyavṛkṣodbhavāny adyāt snehāṃś ca phalasaṃbhavān || 6.13 ||
sthalajaudakaśākānīti | sthalodakodbhavāni śākāni araṇyodbhavāni yajñiyavṛkṣōbhavāni ca puṣpamūlaphalāni adyāt | iṅgudā(dyā)diphalodbhavāṃś ca snehānāraṇyatve ‘pi sati || 6.13 ||
varjayen madhu māṃsaṃ ca bhaumāni kavakāni ca |
bhūstṛṇaṃ śigrukaṃ caiva śleṣmātakphalāni ca || 6.14 ||
tyajed āśvayuje māsi munyannaṃ pūrvasaṃcitam |
jīrṇāni caiva vāsāṃsi śākamūlaphalāni ca || 6.15 ||
varjayed iti | mākṣikamāsaṃ bhūjātāni kavakāni chatrākāni na vārkṣāṇi | bhūstṛṇaṃ bhūtṛṇam iti mālavadeśaprasiddham | tatsāhacaryāt tatsadṛśam eva śigrukam iti tṛṇajātīyaṃ vāhīkadeśaprasiddhaṃ śleṣmātakākhyavṛkṣaphalāni vāsāṃsi jīrṇāni āśvayuje māsi tyajet
|| 6.14–15 ||
na phālakṛṣṭam aśnīyād utsṛṣṭam api kenacit |
na grāmajātāny ārto ‘pi mūlāni ca phalāni ca || 6.16 ||
na phālakṛṣṭam iti | na grāmajātāny ārto ‘pi puṣpāṇi ca phalāni ca | āraṇyam api phālakṛṣṭapradeśajātasvāminopekṣitam api nāśnīyāt | tathāraṇyāni akālakṛṣṭāny api kathaṃcit grāme jātāni puṣpamūlaphalāni kṣutpīḍito ‘pi nāśnīyāt || 6.16 ||
agnipakvāśano vā syāt kālapakvabhug eva vā |
aśmakuṭṭo bhaved vāpi dantolūkhaliko ‘pi vā || 6.17 ||
agnipakvāśana iti | agnipakvam āraṇyānnaṃ bhuñjīta kālapakvaṃ vā phalādi tasmāt tathāśmanā vā tad annaṃ nīvārādyavhatyānnam eva bhakṣayet dantolūkhalena dantā evāsya nistūṣīkaraṇe ulūkhalakārye syuḥ || 6.17 ||
sadyaḥprakṣālako vā syān māsasaṃcayiko ‘pi vā |
ṣaṇmāsanicayo vā syāt samānicaya eva vā || 6.18 ||
sadyaḥprakṣālaka iti | ṣaṇmāsanicayo vāpi samānicaya eva vā | āhnikamātram annaṃ vā saṃcinuyāt | māsaparyāptaṃ vā ṣaṇmāsasamarthaṃ vā saṃvatsarapūrakaṃ vā || 6.18 ||
naktaṃ cānnaṃ samaśnīyād divā vāhṛtya śaktitaḥ |
caturthakāliko vā syāt syād vāpy aṣṭamakālikaḥ || 6.19 ||
naktam iti | yathāśaktyānnam āhṛtya pradoṣa eva cāśniyād iti vā caturthakālāśino vā bhavet | “sāyaṃprātar manuṣyāṇām aśanaṃ devanirmitam” ity etat prāpta ity ekasminn ahany upoṣya aparedyuḥ sāyam aśnīyāt aṣṭamakāliko tryaham upoṣya caturthe ‘hani sāyam aśnīyāt || 6.19 ||
cāndrāyaṇavidhanair vā śuklakṛṣṇe ca vartayet |
pakṣāntayor vāpy aśnīyd yavāgūṃ kvathitāṃ sakṛt || 6.20 ||
cānrāyaṇavidhānair iti | śuklakṛṣṇapakṣayoś cāndrāyaṇavidhānair vā vakṣyamāṇaiḥ piṇḍopacayāpacayarūpair varteta | paurṇamāsyamāvāsyayor vā yavapiṣṭādipeyāṃ kvathitām ekavāram aśnīyāt || 6.20 ||
puṣpamūlaphalair vāpi kevalair vartayet sadā |
kālapakvaiḥ svayaṃśīrṇair vaikhānasamate sthitaḥ || 6.21 ||
puṣpamūlaphalair iti | mūlaphalāny eva kālapakvāni nāgnipakvāni svayaṃpakvāni bhakṣayet | vaikhānasākhye vānaprasthaśāstradarśane sthitaḥ taduktam anyad apy anutiṣṭhet || 6.21 ||
bhūmau viparivarteta tiṣṭhed vā prapadair dinam |
sthānāsanābhyāṃ viharet savaneṣūpayann apaḥ || 6.22 ||
bhūmāv iti | bhūmau luṭhan gatāgatāni vā kuryāt | pādāgrabhyāṃ vā dinaṃ tiṣṭhet kiṃcic ca kālasthita eva syāt | kiṃcic copaviṣṭa eva savaneṣu sāyaṃprātar madhyaṃdineṣu ca snānaṃ kuryāt | “prage tathā” ity etena sahāsya vikalpo niyamātiśayāpekṣaḥ || 6.22 ||
grīṣme pañcatapās tu syād varṣāsv abhrāvakāśikaḥ |
ārdravāsās tu hemante kramaśo vardhayaṃs tapaḥ || 6.23 ||
grīṣma iti | śanaiḥ śanais tapovivṛddhyarthaṃ dik catuṣṭayopanihitair upariṣṭāc cādityena grīṣme ātmānaṃ tāpayet | varṣāsu galatsalilaṃ jaladhārāgalitaṃ śirasi dhārayet | hemante cārdravastro bhavet | tryṛtuḥ saṃvatsara ity etaddarśanāvaṣṭaṃbhena sakalasaṃvatsarāpekṣam evaitad uktam || 6.23 ||
upaspṛśaṃs triṣavaṇaṃ pitṝn devāṃś ca tarpayet |
tapaś caraṃś cogrataraṃ śoṣayed deham ātmanaḥ || 6.24 ||
upaspṛśann iti | sāyaṃprātar madhyaṃdineṣūktaṃ triṣavaṇaṃ snānaṃ kurvan devapitṛtarpaṇaṃ kurvann anyad api vaikhānasaśāstroktaṃ tīvraṃ tapaś caraṃś ca śarīraṃ kṣapayet || 6.24 ||
agnīn ātmani vaitānān samāropya yathāvidhi |
anagnir aniketanaḥ syān munir mūlaphalāśanaḥ || 6.25 ||
agnīṃś ceti | ūrdhvaṃ ṣaḍbhyo māsebhyo “anagir aniketaḥ” iti vasiṣṭhadarśanāt tasmāt kālād ūrdhvaṃ śrautān agnīn vaikhānasaśāstravidhānena ātmani bhasmapānādidvāreṇa samāropya tato laukikāgniparṇakuṭīgṛhaśūnyo muniś ca saṃyatavāṅmūlaphalāśana eva syāt || 6.25 ||
aprayatnaḥ sukhārtheṣu brahmacārī dharāśayaḥ |
śaraṇeṣv amamaś caiva vṛkṣamūlaniketanaḥ || 6.26 ||
aprayatna iti | sukhaprayojaneṣu śītātapaparihārādiṣu yatnarahitaḥ strīsaṃprayogarahitaḥ varaṇḍakasthaṇḍilaśāyī āśrameṣu vṛkṣamūleṣu mamedam ity evamātmīyābhimānaśūnyo vṛkṣaniketananivāsī syāt || 6.26 ||
tāpaseṣv eva vipreṣu yātrikaṃ bhaikṣam āharet |
gṛhamedhiṣu cānyeṣu dvijeṣu vanavāsiṣu || 6.27 ||
phalamūlābhāve tu tāpaseṣv iti | vānaprasthebhya eva brāhmaṇebhyaḥ prāṇavṛttimātraprayojanaṃ bhaikṣyam āhared anyebhyaś caiva vanavāsibhyaḥ || 6.27 ||
grāmād āhṛtya vāśnīyād aṣṭau grāsān vane vasan |
pratigṛhya puṭenaiva pāṇinā śakalena vā || 6.28 ||
tadabhāve tu grāmād iti | grāmād ānīya grāmasyāpy annasyāṣṭau grāsān parṇapuṭikayā hastena vā śarāvādikhaṇḍena vā pratigṛhya vānaprastho ‘śnīyāt || 6.28 ||
etāṃś cānyāṃś ca seveta dīkṣā vipro vane vasan |
vividhāś caupaniaṣadīr ātmasaṃsiddhaye śrutīḥ || 6.29 ||
etān iti | vānaprathyam anutiṣṭhan vipra etān anyāṃś caiva vaikhānasaśāstroktān niyamān abhyaset | ātmanaś ca vṛkṣatvaprāptaye nānāprakāra upaniṣaduktāḥ śrutīr arthato ‘bhyaset
|| 6.29 ||
ṛṣibhir brāhmaṇaiś caiva gṛhasthair eva sevitāḥ |
vidyātapovivṛddhyarthaṃ śarīrasya ca śuddhaye || 6.30 ||
yasmād etāḥ ṛṣibhir iti | ṛṣibhiḥ sarvārthadarśibhiḥ aṅgīrasaprabhṛtibhir anyaiś ca brāhmaṇair vānaprasthair eva gṛhasthaiś ca vijñānavivṛddhyartham upaniṣacchrutayaḥ sevitāḥ tapovivṛddhyarthaṃ śarīrasya ca kalmaṣakṣayāya cādareṇa dīkṣāḥ sevitāḥ tasmād etāḥ sevitā iti pūrvavidhiśeṣapurākalpārthavādaḥ || 6.30 ||
aparājitāṃ vāsthāya vrajed diśam ajihmagaḥ |
ā nipātāc charīrasya yukto vāryanilāśanaḥ || 6.31 ||
acikitsyavyādhyudbhave ‘niṣṭasaṃdarśane vā sati aparājitām iti | uttarapūrvāṃ vā diśam āśritya akuṭilayoganiṣṭhāvān vāryambubhakṣaḥ san ā śarīrasaṃnipātād gacchet | na purāyuṣaḥ svakāmī preyād ity etatśrutivirodho na, yataḥ svakāmiśabdād avidhimaraṇam atra niṣidhyate iti avasīyate na śāstracoditam iti || 6.31 ||
āsāṃ maharṣicaryāṇāṃ tyaktvānyatamayā tanum |
vītaśokabhayo vipro brahmaloke mahīyate || 6.32 ||
āsām iti | eṣāṃ pūrvoktānām anuṣṭhānānāṃ madhyād anyatamenānuṣṭḥānena śrīraṃ tyaktvā vigatasaṃtāpo gatabhayaś ca vipro brahmaloke pūjāṃ labhate samyaguktamaraṇākaraṇena vāsau
|| 6.32 ||
vaneṣu ca vihṛtyaivaṃ tṛtīyaṃ bhāgam āyuṣaḥ |
caturtham āyuṣo bhāgaṃ tyaktvā saṅgān parivrajet || 6.33 ||
vaneṣu tu vihṛtyaivam tṛtīyaṃ bhāgam āyuṣaḥ | caturtham āyuṣo bhāgas tyaktasaṅgaḥ parivrajet || evam uktarītyā tṛtīyam āyurbhāgaṃ vānaprasthānuṣṭhānārthaṃ vane uṣitvā tataś caturtham āyurbhāgaṃ pūrvāvasthānād atiśayena rāgādīn tyaktvā caturthāśramam anutiṣṭhet vakṣyamāṇāśramasamuccayapakṣe || 6.33 ||
āśramād āśramaṃ gatvā hutahomo jitendriyaḥ |
bhikṣābalipariśrāntaḥ pravrajan pretya vardhate || 6.34 ||
āśramād iti | pūrvokteṣv āśrameṣu yathākramaṃ pūrvasmāt pūrvasmād anantaraṃ āśramam anuṣṭhāya yathāsaṃbhava āśrameṣu hutahomo jitendriyo bhikṣābalidānaśrāntaḥ san pravrajyām anutiṣṭhan paraloke vardhate | mokṣaprāptyānandamayaṃ brahmety evam ṛddhyatiśayayukto bhavati || 6.34 ||
ṛṇāni trīṇy apākṛtya mano mokṣe niveśayet |
anapākṛtya mokṣaṃ tu sevamāno vrajaty adhaḥ || 6.35 ||
tathāśramasamuccayapakṣāṅgīkaraṇe sati ṛṇāni trīṇy apākṛtya mano mokṣe niyojayed iti | uttaraślokavakṣyamāṇāni trīṇi ṛṇāni saṃśodhya tataḥ pravrajyādvāreṇa mokṣaviṣaye mano niyojayet | tāni punar asaṃśodhya sākṣepo ‘yaṃ pravrajyām anutiṣṭhan narakaṃ vrajati || 6.35 ||
adhītya vidhivad vedān putrāṃś cotpādya dharmataḥ |
iṣṭvā ca śaktito yajñair mano mokṣe niveśayet || 6.36 ||
tāni ṛṇāni darśayati adhītyeti | “tribhir ṛṇair ṛṇavā jāyate manuṣyo yajñena devebhyaḥ prajayā pitṛbhyaḥ svādhyāyena ṛṣibhyaḥ” iti brūyāt | yathāśāstraṃ vedān adhītya purāṃś ca parvagamanānṛturiraṃsāvarjam ṛtukālādigamanadharmeṇotpādya yajñaiś ca jyotiṣṭomādibhir yathāśaktir iṣṭvā tato mokṣopāyapravrajyākhye mano niyojayet || 6.36 ||
anadhītya dvijo vedān anutpādya tathā prajām |
aniṣṭvā caiva yajñaś ca mokṣam icchan vrajaty adhaḥ || 6.37 ||
anadhītya dvijo vedam anutpādya tathā sutam iti | vedādhyayanaprajotpādana-yajñānuṣṭhānāny akṛtvā mokṣam icchan narakaṃ vrajati || 6.37 ||
prājāpatyāṃ nirupyeṣṭiṃ sarvavedasadakṣiṇām |
ātmany agnīn samāropya brāhmaṇaḥ pravrajed gṛhāt || 6.38 ||
prājāpatyām iti | yajurvedapūrvākhyagranthoktāṃ prajāpatidevatākāṃ sarvasvadhanadakṣiṇām iṣṭiṃ kṛtvā taduktavidhinaiva bhasmapānādinā samāropya vānaprathāśramam anantaram akṛtvaiva gṛhasthāśramāntaram eva brāhmaṇaḥ pravrajyām anutiṣṭhed iti | evam āśramāṇāṃ samuccayapakṣo ‘nena darśitaḥ | tricatuḥsamuccayavikalpapakṣāś ca sarva eva śrutinoditāḥ sthitāḥ | tathā ca jābālaśrutau: “brahmacaryaṃ samāpya gṛhī bhavet gṛhī bhūtvā vanī bhavet vanī bhūtvā pravrajed yadi cetarathānukuryāt | brahmacaryād eva pravrajed gṛhād vā vanād vā” ity uktam || 6.38 ||
yo datvā sarvabhūtebhyaḥ pravrajaty abhayaṃ gṛhāt |
tasya tejomayā lokā bhavanti brahmavādinaḥ || 6.39 ||
ya iti | tasya te taijasā lokā bhavanti brahmavādinaḥ | yaḥ sarvabhūtebhyo ‘bhayaṃ datvā gṛhād eva pravrajati tasya brahmasvarūpanirūpaṇārthopaniṣadādiśāstraniṣṭhasya nityaprakāśakā lokā bhavanti || 6.39 ||
yasmād aṇv api bhūtānāṃ dvijān notpadyate bhayam |
tasya dehād vimuktasya bhayaṃ nāsti kutaścana || 6.40 ||
yasmād iti | yasya dvijasya sakāśād bhūtānāṃ sūkṣmam api bhayaṃ notpadyate tasya sāṃpratikaśarīrapātād anantaraṃ kasmāccid api bhayaṃ na bhavati || 6.40 ||
agārād abhiniṣkrāntaḥ pavitropacito muniḥ |
samupoḍheṣu kāmeṣu nirapekṣaḥ parivrajet || 6.41 ||
agārād iti | gṛhān nirgataḥ pavitrair darbhājinadaṇḍakamaṇḍalvādibhir yuktā saṃyatavāk kenacid vopahṛteṣu svādvannādiṣv apagataspṛhaḥ vrajet || 6.41 ||
eka eva caran nityaṃ siddhyartham asahāyavān |
siddham ekasya saṃpaśyan na jahāti na hīyate || 6.42 ||
eka iti | ekasya sato mokṣāvāptir bhavatīti evaṃ jānann eka eva na pravrajitāntareṇa sahāsītāsahāyavān bhṛtyādirahito ‘nyatyāgenety apare | baudhāyanena hi “strīṇāṃ caike” iti strīṇām api pravrajyoktā, evaṃ prakurvan na kiṃcit tyajati, nāpi kena tyajyate, viyogaduḥkhaṃ na tenānubḥūyate, na cānyo ‘nubhāvyate, susaṃbandhātmakābhāvād avighnena mokṣopāyasaṃpattiḥ || 6.42 ||
anagnir aniketaḥ syād grāmam annartham āśrayet |
upekṣako ‘saṃkusuko munir bhāvasamāhitaḥ || 6.43 ||
anagnir iti | aprekṣako ‘saṃkusuko munir bhāvsamāhitaḥ | śāstre ‘gnisamāropaṇasyoktatvāt laukikāgnirahitaḥ agṛhyaka upekṣako vyādhyāder utpatteḥ śarīrasyāpi | yathāha śaṅkhaḥ: “ātmanaḥ pratikāraṃ kuryāt nāpi kārayet” | nānumānyeti (?) | asaṃkusukaḥ saṃyatabuddhiḥ, cetasā ca saṃyataḥ syāt, bhikṣārthaṃ ca grāmaṃ gacchet || 6.43 ||
kapālaṃ vṛkṣamūlāni kucailam asahāyatā |
samatā caiva sarvasminn etan muktasya lakṣaṇam || 6.44 ||
kapālaṃ vṛkṣamūlāni kucailam asahāyavān iti | karpaṭaṃ bhikṣārthaṃ vṛkṣamūlāni āśrayaḥ, kucailaṃ kanthā vāsortham, ekārāmatā saṃgatyāgāya śatrau mitre ca sāmyam ity etat muktisādhanantvān muktasya cihnam || 6.44 ||
nābhinandeta maraṇaṃ nābhinandeta jīvitam |
kālam eva pratīkṣeta nirdeśaṃ bhṛtako yathā || 6.45 ||
nābhinandeteti | maraṇaṃ jīvitaṃ vā nābhilaṣet, api tu bhṛtisaṃśuddhikālaṃ yathā bhṛtako ‘pekṣyate evaṃ svecchāpanipāte taṃ maraṇakālam apekṣate || 6.45 ||
dṛṣṭipūtaṃ nyaset padaṃ vastrapūtaṃ jalaṃ pibet |
satyapūtāṃ vaded vācaṃ manaḥpūtaṃ samācaret || 6.46 ||
dṛṣṭipūtam iti | prāṇyupaghātaparihārārthaṃ cakṣuḥśodhitaṃ padaṃ kṣipet | udakasūkṣmajantuparihārārthaṃ ca vastrasaṃśodhitaṃ jalaṃ pibet | satyapavitrīkṛtāṃ ca vācaṃ vadet | evaṃ ca maunena hāsyavikalpaḥ asatsaṃkalpena parihāreṇa ca manasā pavitrīkṛtātmā sarvadā syāt || 6.46 ||
ativādāṃs titikṣeta nāvamanyeta kaṃcana |
na cemaṃ deham āśritya vairaṃ kurvīta kenacit || 6.47 ||
ativādān iti | sāmarṣavādān kṣameta na kaṃcana paribhavet | na cedaṃ śarīram āśritya vyādhyāyatanabhūtaṃ vidyududdyota iva vinaśvaraṃ etannimittakaṃ svargāpavargaparipanthi kenacid api saha cairaṃ na kuryat || 6.47 ||
krudhyantaṃ na pratikrudhyed ākruṣṭaḥ kuśalaṃ vadet |
saptadvārāvakīrṇāṃ ca na vācam anṛtāṃ vadet || 6.48 ||
krudhyantam iti | saṃjātakrodhāya pratīpaṃ na krudhyeta | adhikṣiptaś cādhikṣeptāram bhadraṃ vadet | dharmo ‘rthaḥ kāmo dharmārthāv arthakāmau dharmakāmārthakāmā samastāś cety etāni yāni vācaḥ saptoccāraṇanimittāni teṣv avakīrṇāṃ vikṣiptāṃ tadviṣayāṃ sarvasya ca bhedasvāstyarūpasyānṛtārthāṃ vācaṃ na vaded api tu mokṣāśritām eva vadet || 6.48 ||
adhyātmaratir āsīno nirapekṣo nirāmiṣaḥ |
ātmanaiva sahāyena sukhārthī vicared iha || 6.49 ||
adhyātmaratir iti | upaniṣadādyadhyātmavṛttiḥ san daṇḍakamaṇḍalvādiṣv apagatāviśeṣāpekṣo niḥspṛho asahāyaḥ saṇ sukhāny asmin saṃsāre vicaran || 6.49 ||
na cotpātanimittābhyāṃ na nakṣatrāṅgavidyayā |
nānuśāsanavādābhyāṃ bhikṣāṃ lipseta karhicit || 6.50 ||
na ceti | bhūkampādyutpātacakṣuḥspandanādinimittaphalakathanena adyāśvinī, hastanakhakaralekālakṣaṇam ity etat nakṣatrāṅgavidyayā ca tathāyaṃ nītimargam icchan nānyaṃ vartitum ity anuśāsanena śāstrārthavadanena kadācid api bhikṣāṃ labdhum icchet || 6.50 ||
na tāpasair brāhmaṇair vā vayobhir api vā śvabhiḥ |
ākīrṇaṃ bhikṣukair vānyair agāram upasaṃvrajet || 6.51 ||
neti | vānaprasthabrāhmaṇapakṣiśvabhir anyair bhikṣāṭanaśīlair vyāptaṃ gṛhaṃ bhikṣārthaṃ na praviśet || 6.51 ||
kḷptakeśanakhaśmaśruḥ pātrī daṇḍī kusumbhavān |
vicaren niyato nityaṃ sarvabhūtāny apīḍayan || 6.52 ||
kḷptakeśanakhaśmaśur iti | lūnakeśanakhaśmaśruḥ pātrī daṇḍakusumbhavā sarvabhūtāny apīḍayan niyato buddhimān sarvadā vicaret || 6.52 ||
ataijasāni pātrāṇi tasya syur nirvraṇāni ca |
teṣām adbhiḥ smṛtaṃ śaucaṃ camasānām ivādhvare || 6.53 ||
ataijasānīti | sauvarṇalohādivarjitāni acchidrāṇi bhikṣābhojanapātrāṇi syur nirvraṇāni ca teṣāṃ ca udakenaiva śuddhir yajñe camasānām iva || 6.53 ||
alābuṃ dārupātraṃ ca mṛnmayaṃ vaidalaṃ tathā |
etāni yatipātrāṇi manuḥ svāyambhuvo ‘bravīt || 6.54 ||
alābuṃ vā dārupātram iti | alābudārumṛttarutvaṅnirmitāni pātrāṇi pravrajitānāṃ svāyaṃbhuvo manur āha || 6.54 ||
ekakālaṃ cared bhaikṣaṃ na prasajjeta vistare |
bhaikṣaprasakto hi yatir viṣeyeṣv api sajjati || 6.55 ||
ekakālam iti | sakṛtprāṇayātrāmātrārtham ekavāram eva bhaikṣaṃ caret | nobhayakālam api bhaikṣacaraṇavistaraprasaṅgaṃ na kuryāt | yasmād bhaikṣāśanātiśayena śakto yatir annamadodrekāt viṣayāntareṣv api sajjati || 6.55 ||
vidhūme sannamusale vyaṅgāre bhuktavajjane |
vṛtte śarāvasaṃpāte bhikṣāṃ nityaṃ yatiś caret || 6.56 ||
vidhūma iti | vigatadhūme kāle nivṛttamusalāvahananeṣu pākajvālāsūpaśāntāsu gṛhasthaparyanteṣu janeṣu kṛtabhojaneṣu ucchiṣṭaśarāveṣu tyakteṣu sarvadā yatir bhikṣāṃ caret
|| 5.56 ||
alābhe na viṣādī syāl lābhe caiva na harṣayet |
prāṇayātrikamātraḥ syān mātrāsaṅgād vinirgataḥ || 6.57 ||
“na rāgī na viṣādī syāl lābhaś cainaṃ na harṣayet” iti | lābhālābhayor harṣaviṣadau na bhajeta | prāṇāpyāyanamātram annaṃ bhuñjīta | daṇḍakamaṇḍalvādimātrāyām idaṃ śodhanaṃ nety evaṃ saṅgaṃ na kuryāt || 6.57 ||
abhipūjitalābhāṃs tu jugupsetaiva sarvaśaḥ |
abhipūjitalābhaiś ca yatir mukto ‘pi badhyate || 6.58 ||
“atipūjitalābhāt tu bhikṣāṃ yatnena varjayet | atipūjitalobhais tu yatir mukto ‘pi badhyat” || pūjāpūrvakabhaikṣyalābhāt sarvadā nindyed ataś ca varjayet | yasmāt pūjitalābhasahotpattyā yatir mokṣamāṇo ‘pi saṃsāraṃ prāpnoti || 6.58 ||
alpānnābhyavahāreṇa rahaḥsthānāsanena ca |
hriyamāṇāni viṣayair indriyāṇi nivartayet || 6.59 ||
alpānnābhyavahāreṇeti | viṣayai rūpādibhir upabhogārtham indriyāṇi alpānnabhojanena ekāntadeśa upaveśanena ca hriyamāṇāni viṣayendriyāṇi nivartayet || 6.59 ||
indriyāṇāṃ nirodhena rāgadveṣakṣayeṇa ca |
ahiṃsayā ca bhūtānām amṛtatvāya kalpate || 6.60 ||
yasmāt indriyāṇām iti | indriyajayarāgadveṣādinaḥ ahiṃsāvarjanair mokṣāya yogyo bhavati
|| 6.60 ||
avekṣeta gatīr nṝṇāṃ karmadoṣasamudbhavāḥ |
niraye caiva patanaṃ yātanāś ca yamakṣaye || 6.61 ||
adhunendriyajaypāyatayā saṃsāraniṣedham upadiśati avekṣeteti | śiṣṭākaraṇapratiṣiddhasevanenotpannapāpasamudbhavāḥ kṣetrajñānāṃ sarpakṛkalāsakṛmyādiśarīraprāptīḥ yamagṛhe 'pi narakapatanaṃ yātanāś ca niśitatarakarapatravidārātmakāḥ śāstradṛṣṭyā vilokayet || 6.61 ||
viprayogaṃ priyaś caiva saṃyogaṃ ca tathāpriyaiḥ |
jarayā cābhibhavanaṃ vyādhibhiś copapīḍanam || 6.62 ||
viprayogam iti | iṣṭaviyogāniṣṭasaṃyogajarajābhibhavavyādhyupapīḍanāni karmadoṣodbhavāni paryālocayet || 6.62 ||
dehād utkramaṇaṃ cāsmāt punar garbhe ca saṃbhavam |
yonikoṭisahasreṣu sṛtīś cāsyāntarātmanaḥ || 6.63 ||
dehād iti | asya kṣetrajñasya asmāc charīrād duḥsahasandhibandhanighaṭṭanapūrvabahulatara-tamaḥpraveśakarkaśam utkramaṇaṃ punaś ca śītoṣṇatiktakaṭukādijananāny aśanapānādiduḥkhabahulagarbha utpattiṃ sṛgālādinikṛṣṭajātyantayonigamanāni ca karmadoṣasamudbhavāny avekṣeta || 6.63 ||
adharmaprabhavaṃ caiva duḥkhayogaṃ śarīriṇām |
dharmārthaprabhavaṃ caiva sukhasaṃyogam akṣayam || 6.64 ||
adharmaprabhavam iti | śarīravatāṃ kṣetrajñānām adharmakāraṇakaṃ duḥkhasaṃbandhaṃ dharmārthanimittakaṃ ca kṣemaṃ sukhasaṃbandham avekṣeta | ata itthaṃ paryālocyendriyajaye mokṣopāyabhūte yateta || 6.64 ||
sūkṣmatāṃ cānvavekṣeta yogena paramātmanaḥ |
deheṣu caivopapattim uttameṣv adhameṣu ca || 6.65 ||
sūkṣmatām iti | paramātmanaś cittavṛttisthairyeṇa sūkṣmatām avekṣeta | ya tṣo ‘ntarhṛdaye ‘ṇīyān iti śruter yadi vā śarīrādisthūlapadārthavyatiriktatvāvalambanenoparacitasūkṣmatva-vyapadeśe sati sūkṣmatām avekṣeta | tathotkṛṣṭāpakṛṣṭaśarīreṣu śubhāśubhaphalopabhogārtham adhiṣṭhātṛtvam asvāvekṣeta || 6.65 ||
bhūṣito ‘pi cared dharmaṃ yatra tatrāśrame rataḥ |
samaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu na liṅgaṃ dharmakāraṇam || 6.66 ||
“bhūṣito ‘pi cared dharmaṃ yatra tatrāśrame vasan” iti | yasmin kasmiṃścid āśrame sthitaḥ sarvabhūteṣu samaḥ san āśramaliṅgaparityāgena tadviruddhaveṣālaṅkārayukto ‘pi tadāśramoktaṃ yamasamūham anutiṣṭhet | yasmān na daṇḍakamaṇḍalvādidhāraṇaṃ dharmasya kāraṇam api tu yamaniyamānuṣṭhānaṃ yamaniyamānuṣṭhānakarmaprādhānyakhyāpanārtham etalliṅganindāvacanaṃ na liṅgaparityāgārthaṃ vihitatvāt || 6.66 ||
phalaṃ katakavṛkṣasya yady apy ambuprasādakam |
na nāmagrahaṇād eva tasya vāri prasīdati || 6.67 ||
atra dṛṣṭāntam āha phalam iti | katakavṛkṣaphalaṃ yady api kaluṣodakaprasannatotpādakaṃ tathāpi tatsaṃbandhināmoccāraṇamātreṇaiva udakaṃ prasannaṃ na bhavati, api tu phalaprakṣepakriyām apekṣate, evaṃ na liṅgadhāraṇamātreṇaiva dharmonnaty api tu anuṣṭhānam apekṣate || 6.67 ||
saṃrakṣaṇārthaṃ jantūnāṃ rātrāv ahani vā sadā |
śarīrasyātyaye caiva samīkṣya vasudhāṃ caret || 6.68 ||
saṃrakṣaṇārtham iti | śarīrapīḍāyām api yasmād etaṃ samīkṣya vasudhāṃ cared iti, tasya ca prāyaścittam aprakaraṇe ‘py ādarārtham || 6.68 ||
ahnā rātryā ca yāñ jantūn hinasty ajñānato yatiḥ |
teṣāṃ snātvā viśuddhyarthaṃ prāṇāyāmān ṣaḍ ācaret || 6.69 ||
ahneti | yān acintitān prāṇino ‘hani rātrau vā yatir hanti tadvadhpāpahirharaṇārthaṃ snātvā,
savyāhṛtiṃ sapraṇavāṃ gāyatrīṃ śirasā saha |
triḥ paṭhed āyataprāṇaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ sa ucyate ||
iti vasiṣṭhakṛtalakṣaṇāt prāṇāyāmān ṣaṭ kuryāt | asya ca prāyaścittaprakarṣeṇotkarṣa ādarārthaḥ | asiṃcitavṛtte ‘pi vyatikrame pāpaṃ bhavati asmād ajñānata iti vacanād avasīyate || 6.69 ||
prāṇāyāmā brāhmaṇasya trayo ‘pi vidhivat kṛtāḥ |
vyāhṛtipraṇavair yuktā vijñeyaṃ paramaṃ tapaḥ || 6.70 ||
yasmāt prāṇāyāmā iti | prāṇāyāmā bhūr bhuvaḥ svar ity etābhir vyāhṛtibhir oṃkāreṇa ca yuktāḥ kumbhakapūrakarecakavidhānena kṛtā prakṛṣṭaṃ tapo brāhmaṇasya boddhavyam | tatra bāhyābhyantaravāyoḥ koṣṭhapraveśaniṣkramaṇanirodhakumbhakocchvāsyavāyor anavaratotsargo recaka iti || 6.70 ||
dahyante dhmāyamānānāṃ dhātūnāṃ hi yathā malāḥ |
tathendriyāṇāṃ dahyante doṣāḥ prāṇasya nigrahāt || 6.71 ||
dahyanta iti | dhātūnāṃ suvarṇādyutpattimṛdādyagnimadhye bhasmādinā dhmāyamānānāṃ yathā dravyāṇi dahyante evam indriyāṇāṃ viṣayapravṛttyā ye rāgādayo doṣā udbhavanti te prāṇanirgraheṇa viṣayān prāṇanigrahaṇaviṣayānām abhidhyānaṃ tato rāgādivyāvṛttiḥ ity etad anenocyate || 6.71 ||
prāṇāyāmair dahed doṣān dhāraṇābhiś ca kilbiṣam |
pratyāhāreṇa saṃsargān dhyānenānīśvarān guṇān || 6.72 ||
evaṃ ca sati prāṇāyāmair iti | anantaroktanītyā prāṇāyāmai rāgādīn dahet | deśabandhaś cittasya dhāraṇetiyogādilakṣaṇādivittasyāvyāpṛṣṭatāvasthasya apratiṣṭāsya hṛdayādideśadhāraṇātmikābhiḥ kilbiṣaṃ dahet | pāpam uddhartuṃ rakṣet | pratyāhāreṇa ca manaso viṣayebhyaḥ pratyākarṣaṇena saṃsargān indriyavṣayasaṃparkākhān viṣayopalabdhihetūn uddhartuṃ rakṣet | tatpratyaikatādhyānam iti yogaśāstroktalakṣaṇena vijātīyapratyān anuviddhātmasākṣatkāraṇarūpeṇa ca dhyānena dharmo jñānaṃ nirāgam aiśvaryaṃ sāttvikam etadrūpaṃ nityam asmād viparyastaṃ ity etat sāṃkhyoktena dharmājñānādyair vairāgānaiśvaryān guṇān dahet || 6.72 ||
uccāvaceṣu bhūteṣu durjñeyām akṛtātmabhiḥ |
dhyānayogena saṃpaśyed gatim asyāntarātmanaḥ || 6.73 ||
uccāvaceṣv iti | asya kṣetrajñasyotkṛṣṭāpakṛṣṭeṣu brahmādistambaparyanteṣu bhūteṣu prāptim asaṃskṛtātmabhir aśāstrāt tair evāharet (?) tathāvad dvijāyatīyād anena hetunā yaṃ saṃsārān pratipadyata iti || 6.73 ||
samyagdarśanasaṃpannaḥ karmabhir na nibadhyate |
darśanena vihīnas tu saṃsāraṃ pratipadyate || 6.74 ||
yasmāt samyag iti | ātmaviṣayasamyagjñānopetaḥ karmabhis tailapakvabījavad atmajñāne sati kāryajananāsamarthair na nibadhyate saṃsāre nopapadyate ātmajñānaśūnyaḥ punaḥ saṃsāram āvartate || 6.74 ||
ahiṃsayendriyāsaṅgair vaidikaiś caiva karmabhiḥ |
tapasaś caraṇaiś cograiḥ sādhayantīha tat padam || 6.75 ||
ahiṃsayeti | aśāstritahiṃsāvartanenendriyāṇāṃ ca viṣayāsaktiparihāreṇa vaidikaiś ca nityaiḥ karmabhiḥ tīrthaiś ca kṛcchracāndrāyaṇānuṣṭhānair iha saṃsāre ca brahmaprāptilakṣaṇaṃ padaṃ sādhayantīha ślokadvayena jñānakarmasamuccayaphalam āha || 6.75 ||
asthisthūṇaṃ snāyubaddhaṃ māṃsaśoṇitalepanam |
carmāvanaddhaṃ durgandhi pūrṇaṃ mūtrapurīṣayoḥ || 6.76 ||
jarāśokasamāviṣṭaṃ rogāyatanam āturam |
rajasvalam anityaṃ ca bhūtāvāsam imaṃ tyajet || 6.77 ||
idānīṃ apavargasādhanabhūtavairāgyodbhavaviṣayakāyasvarūpam āha asthisthūṇaṃ snāyubaddham iti | jarāśokasamāviṣṭam iti | pṛthivyādibhūtavikāramāṃsādimayaṃ śarīram idaṃ gṛhakaṃ tyajet | rūpakabhaṅgyā cāsya gṛhatvaṃ darśayati | asthīni sthūṇā iva yasya tadavaṣṭabdhatvāt snāyubhir baddhaṃ māṃsarudhirābhyām upaliptaṃ carmacchāditaṃ mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ pūrṇam ata eva durgandhijaropetābhyām ākrāntaṃ vyādhīnāṃ āśrayabhūtam āturaṃ kṣutpipāsādikātaraṃ bāhulyena rājasaṃ naśvaram iti || 6.76–77 ||
nadīkūlaṃ yathā vṛkṣo vṛkṣaṃ vā śakunir yathā |
tathā tyajann imaṃ dehaṃ kṛcchrād grāhād vimucyate || 6.78 ||
tyaktur vairūpyād dṛṣṭāntadvayaṃ darśayitum āha nadīkūlam iti | yas tāvad ātmajñānābhyāsaniṣṭho na cātmatattvataḥ karmakṣayāt paratantraṃ śarīrapātaṃ nadīkūlavṛkṣavad apekṣeta yaḥ punar adhigataviviktātmajyotiḥ sa buddhipūrvakam api śakunibhir iva vṛkṣam utkrāntisamavalambanena dehaṃ tyajet | evam idaṃ śarīraṃ tyajan duḥkhamūlāj jalacaraprāṇibhedād iva saṃsārād vimucyate | ātmajñānābhyāsaniṣṭhasya saty apy antarā kathaṃcit śarīrapāte śarīrāntareṇāvaśiṣṭātmajñānaparipūraṇe sati apavargo ‘vaśyaṃ bhavatīti yogavidāṃ darśanam atas taṃ praty api tathā tyajann imaṃ dehaṃ kṛcchrād grāhād vimucyate ity etat ghaṭate || 6.78 ||
priyeṣu sveṣu sukṛtam apriyeṣu ca duṣkṛtam |
visṛjya dhyānayogena brahmābhyeti sanātanam || 6.79 ||
priyeṣv iti | svadharmāvirodhipriyeṣu kenacit krteṣu na prāgjanmārjitasukṛtam antareṇa kasyacit ghaṭate | tenāyaṃ puruṣo mama priyakartā api tv ātmakrtasukṛtam ity evaṃ dhyānāpayāsenātmīyam eva sukṛtaṃ kartṛtvenāropya evam apriyeṣv api kenacit kṛteṣv ātmīyam eva prāgjanmārjitaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ kāraṇatvena prakalpya tatsaṃpādayitāraṃ puruṣaṃ prati rāgadveṣābhāvān nityaṃ brahmābhyeti tadbhāvam upagacchati || 6.79 ||
yadā bhāvena bhavati sarvabhāveṣu niḥspṛhaḥ |
tadā sukham avāpnoti pretya ceha ca śāśvatam || 6.80 ||
yadeti | yadā pāramārthikena cetodharmeṇa sarvavastuṣu vigatābhilāṣo bhavati tadā dehapātād ūrdhvam ihaloke śatamakhaṃ pramītaś cāpavargasukhaṃ nityaṃ prāpnoti || 6.80 ||
anena vidhinā sarvāṃs tyaktvā saṅgāñ chanaiḥ śanaiḥ |
sarvadvandvavinirmukto brahmaṇy evāvatiṣthate || 6.81 ||
aneneti | anena vidhinā saṃsārasvarūpanirūpaṇātmakena śanaiḥ śanaiḥ sarvān viṣayasaṃparkāṃs tyaktvā tataḥ sarvadvandvaiḥ kṣutsauhityādibhir vinirmuktaḥ siddharūpe brahmaṇy avatiṣṭhate || 6.81 ||
dhyānikaṃ sarvam evaitad yad etad abhiśabditam |
na hy anadhyātmavit kaścit kriyāphalam upāśnute || 6.82 ||
dhyānikam iti | yad etac chāstram uktam etad dhyānikaṃ dhyāne na sati paramātmacintane sati utkṛṣṭaphalaṃ bhavati yasmād ātmasvarūpavid yo na bhavati so ‘nuṣṭhānaphalam aparipūrṇaṃ prāpnoti yathā rahasyabrāhmaṇaṃ “yo vā etad akṣaraṃ gārgya viditvāsmiṃl loke juhoti yajate tapas tapyate bahūni varṣasahasrāṇy antavad evāsya bhavati” iti || 6.82 ||
adhiyajñaṃ brahma japed ādhidaivikam eva ca |
ādhyātmikaṃ ca satataṃ vedāntābhihitaṃ ca yat || 6.83 ||
adhiyajñam iti | yajñam adhikṛtya pravṛttaṃ brāhmaṇākhyaṃ vedaṃ devatām adhikrtya pravṛttaṃ devatāprakāśanaparam agnir mūrdhetyādikaṃ ātmānaṃ cādhikṛtya pravṛttaṃ puruṣasūkādikam upaniṣatsu coktaṃ sarvakālaṃ japet || 6.83 ||
idaṃ śaraṇam ājñānām idam eva vijānatām |
idam anvicchatāṃ svargam idam ānantyam icchatām || 6.84 ||
idam iti | śāstrārthānabhijñānāṃ brahmaiva vedākhyaṃ śaraṇagatiḥ ity utkarṣāpakarṣaṇaprāptaparihārahetutvā | evam idam eva jñānavatāṃ svargāpavargam icchatām iti
|| 6.84 ||
anena kramayogena parivrajati yo dvijaḥ |
sa vidhūyeha pāpmānaṃ paraṃ brahmādhigacchati || 6.85 ||
aneneti | uktenāśramasaṃbandhena yo dvijaḥ pravrjyām āśrayati ihaiva loke kalmaṣaṃ nirdahya kṣayāt paraṃ brahmarūpaṃ saṃpadyate || 6.85 ||
eṣa dharmo ‘nuśiṣṭo vo yatīnāṃ niyatātmanām |
vedasaṃnyāsikānāṃ tu karmayogaṃ nibodhata || 6.86 ||
eṣa iti | eṣo ‘nyatarokto yatātmanāṃ saṃbandhī dharmo yuṣmākam uktaḥ | idānīṃ vedoditāgnihotrādikarmaparityāginām etān aike mahāyajñān ityādiprakāreṇa jñānabalād agnihotrādikarmasaṃpādināṃ karmabandhaṃ śṛṇuta | na cāgnihotrādīnāṃ śruticoditānāṃ smṛtyā kathaṃ tyāga iti vācyam ātmajñānaniṣṭhaṃ prati yāvad vai puruṣo bhāṣata ityādi caturthādhyāyadarśitaśrutiprakāreṇāsyāpy anuṣṭhānasya śrautatvād yady api gṛhasthaviśeṣa eva vedasaṃnyāsikas tathāpi pravrajyāvaikalpikatvaṃ saṃnyāsikatvasya jñāpayituṃ pravrajitadharmasamanantaram abhidhānam evaṃ ca gṛhasthavedatvena vedasaṃnyāsikasya cāturāśramyāntarbhāvād āśramāṇāṃ catustridvisamuccayavikalpabādhapakṣāt darśayituṃ tāvad iṣṭam āha || 6.86 ||
brahmacārī gṛhasthaś ca vānaprastho yatis tathā |
ete gṛhasthaprabhavāś catvāraḥ pṛthag āśramāḥ || 6.87 ||
brahmacārīti | ya ete brahmacaryādaya āśramiṇaḥ pṛthag uktā ete gṛhasthajanyāḥ | evaṃ ca gṛhasthavyatiriktāśramibhiḥ śāstravyatikrameṇa jñātānāṃ nāsti hastacaryādyāśramādhikāraḥ
|| 6.87 ||
sarve ‘pi kramaśas tv ete yathāśāstraṃ niṣevitāḥ |
yathoktakāriṇaṃ vipraṃ nayanti paramāṃ gatim || 6.88 ||
sarve ‘pīti | sarve ‘pi catvāro ‘py apiśabdāt trayo dvāv eka eva ity etad uktaśāstrānatikrameṇānuṣṭhitāḥ santo yathoktānuṣṭhātāraṃ viprabrahmaprāptilakṣaṇāṃ gatiṃ prāpayanti | samuccayavikapapakṣāṇāṃ brahmacaryaṃ parisakta ityādiśrutimūlaṃ darśitam | bādhāpakṣe ‘pi jarāmayaṃ ca etat satraṃ yad agnihomaṃ juhoti yad darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ yajata ityādiśrutimūlatvam | evaṃ ca samuccayavikalpabādhāpakṣāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ śrutimūlatvād yathārucy anyatamapakṣānuṣṭhānaṃ naiṣṭhikatvasyāpi śrutimūlatvaṃ darśitam | evaṃ ca yat kaiścin naiṣṭhikatvādīnāṃ smārtatvād gārhasthyena śrautena bādho gārhastham apy anadhikṛtam andhādiviṣayatā cety uktam, tad asat – sarveṣāṃ pratyakṣaśrutimūlatvasya darśitatvāt || 6.88 ||
sarveṣām api caiteṣāṃ vedaśrutividhānataḥ |
gṛhastha ucyate śreṣṭhaḥ sa trīn etān bibharti hi || 6.89 ||
sarveṣām iti | sarveṣām eva brahmacāryādīnāṃ madhyāt gṛhasthabāhulyena sākṣād vedavākyaiḥ karmavidhānād gṛhasthaḥ śreṣṭhī iti manvādibhir ucyate | sa ca yasmāt brahmacāritāpasayatīn bhikṣādinābhipoṣayati | yathoktam “jñānenānnena cānvaham” gṛhasthair eva dhāryante iti tasmād asau śreṣṭhaḥ || 6.89 ||
yathā nadīnadāḥ sarve sāgare yānti saṃsthitim |
tathaivāśramiṇaḥ sarve gṛhasthe yānti saṃsthitim || 6.90 ||
yatheti | yathā gaṅgāśoṣādyāḥ sarve nadīnadāḥ samudre ‘vasthānaṃ prāpnuvanti | evaṃ sarva eva brahmacāryādaya āśramiṇo annādilābhārthaṃ gṛhasthaviṣaye ‘vasthitiṃ prāpnuvanti || 6.90 ||
caturbhir api caivaitair nityam āśramibhir dvijaiḥ |
daśalakṣaṇako dharmaḥ sevitavyaḥ prayatnataḥ || 6.91 ||
etaiś caturbhir brahmacāryādibhiḥ āśramibhir dvijair vakṣyamāṇadaśakāraṇiko dharmo yatnenābhyasanīyaḥ || 6.91 ||
dhṛtiḥ kṣamā damo ‘steyaṃ śaucam indriyanigrahaḥ |
dhīr vidyā satyam akrodho daśakaṃ darmalakṣaṇam || 6.92 ||
dhṛtir iti | saṃtoṣo ‘parādhasahanaṃ śītātapādidvandvasahiṣṇutā paradravyāgrahaṇaṃ mṛdvāryādiśuddhir jitendriyatānupahatā buddhir ātmajñānaṃ yathopalabdhābhidhānaṃ krodhānutpattir ity etat daśaparimāṇaṃ dharmalakṣaṇaṃ sarvam etad iha śāstravikṣiptam uktam api prāyaśa etāvanmātratvād dharmasyeha punar ucyate | saṃkṣepato saṃmohenāvabodhārtham || 6.92 ||
daśa lakṣaṇāni dharmasya ye viprāḥ samadhīyate |
adhītya cānuvartante te yānti paramāṃ gatim || 6.93 ||
daśa lakṣaṇānīti | ye brāhmaṇāḥ etāni daśa lakṣaṇāni paṭhanti paṭhitvā cānutiṣṭhanti te utkṛṣṭaṃ sthānaṃ brahmalokaṃ vā jñāne sati mokṣaṃ prāpnuvanti || 6.93 ||
daśalakṣaṇakaṃ dharmam anutiṣṭhan samāhitaḥ |
vedāntaṃ vidhivac chrutvā saṃnyaset anṛṇo dvijaḥ || 6.94 ||
daśalakṣaṇikam iti | evaṃ daśalākṣaṇikam uktaṃ dharmaṃ saṃyatātmanā ācarann upaniṣadaḥ samyak jñātvā “tribhir ṛṇai ṛṇavāṃ” nityādyupākṛtavarṇatrayo dvija uktaṃ vedasaṃnyāsitvam anutiṣṭhet || 6.94 ||
saṃnyasya sarvakarmāṇi karmadoṣān apānudan |
niyato vedaṃ abhyasya putraiśvarye sukhaṃ vaset || 6.95 ||
saṃnyasyet | sarvāṇy agnihotrādikarmāṇi parityajya “jñānenaivāpare viprāḥ” ity evamādiprakāreṇa jñāvabalāt tāny anuthiṣṭhann abuddhipūrvāṃ saṃcitapāpāny uttaraśloke vakṣyamāṇatvāt saṃnyāsenaiva saṃharan gṛhītaniścayo vedaṃ granthato ‘rthataś cābhyasan putragatasamṛddhyupetena putropakṛtagrāsācchādanaviṛttitvāt sukhaṃ vaset || 6.95 ||
evaṃ saṃnyasya karmāṇi svakāryaparamo ‘spṛhaḥ |
saṃnyāsenāpahatyainaḥ prāpnoti paramāṃ gatim || 6.96 ||
evam iti | evam uktanītyā karmāṇi agnihotrādīni parityajyātmajñānākhyasvakāryapradhāno vigatābhilāṣakaṃ saṃnyāsenāsaṃcetitotpannaṃ pāpaṃ nirhṛtya brahmalokaṃ jñāne vā sati mokṣaṃ prāpnoti || 6.96 ||
eṣa vo ‘bhihito dharmo brāhmaṇasya caturvidhaḥ |
puṇyo ‘kṣayaphalaḥ pretya rājñāṃ dharmaṃ nibodhata || 6.97 ||
eṣa iti | eṣa brahmacārigṛhasthavānaprasthapravrajitānuṣṭheyarūpaś catuḥprakāro brāhmaṇasaṃbandhī puṇyahetutvāt puṇyo brahmalokāpavargaphalatvāt paraloke ‘kṣayaphalo dharmo yuṣmākam uktaḥ | evaṃ cāsmād brāhmaṇagrahaṇāt “brāhmaṇaḥ pravrajed gṛhāt” iti cāsmād brāhmaṇāḥ pravrajantīti śruter brāhmaṇasyaiva pravrajyādhikāro na dvijātimātrasyety avasīyate | idānīṃ rājasaṃbandhinaṃ dharmaṃ śṛṇuta || 6.97 ||
iti śrībhaṭṭamādhavātmajagovindarājaviracitāyāṃ
manuṭīkāyāṃ yatidharme ṣaṣṭho ‘dhyāyaḥ || 6 ||
saptamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
rājadharmāṇ pravakṣyāmi yathāvṛtto bhaven nṛpaḥ |
saṃbhavaś ca yathā tasya siddhiś ca paramā yathā || 7.1 ||
rājadharmān iti | nṛpatinā yathācāreṇa bhavitavyam, vīkṣya dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthān kartavyān kathayiṣyāmi | tathā yena prakāreṇa rājña utpattir yathā ca prakṛṣṭā dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthaphalasaṃpattis tad vakṣyāmi || 7.1 ||
brāhmaṃ prāptena saṃskāraṃ kṣatriyeṇa yathāvidhi |
sarvasyāsya yathānyāyaṃ kartavyaṃ parirakṣaṇam || 7.2 ||
brāhmam iti | vedādhigamārtham upanayanārthaṃ snānaparyantaṃ yathāśāstraṃ saṃskāraṃ prāptavatā kṣatriyeṇa sarvasyāsya jagataḥ śāstramaryādān atikrameṇa niyamato rakṣaṇaṃ kartavyam, vṛttyarthatve ‘pi “tapaḥ kṣatrasya rakṣaṇam” iti adṛṣṭārthatvenāpi rakṣaṇopadeśāt
|| 7.2 ||
arājake hi loke ‘smin sarvato vidrute bhayāt |
rakṣārtham asya sarvasya rājānam asṛjat prabhuḥ || 7.3 ||
arājaka iti | yasmāt pūrvam avidyamānarājake asmin jagati bhayena samantāt pracalati sati asya jagato rakṣārthaṃ hiraṇyagarbho rājānaṃ sṛṣṭavān, tasmāt tena rakṣaṇaṃ kartavyam || 7.3 ||
indrānilayamārkāṇām agneś ca varuṇasya ca |
candravitteśayoś caiva mātrā nirhṛtya śāśvatīḥ || 7.4 ||
katham asṛjad ity āha indrānilayamārkāṇām iti | indravāyuyamādityāgnivaruṇacandra-vaiśravaṇānāṃ saṃbandhino ‘ṃśān sārabhūtān niṣkṛṣya rājānam asṛjat || 7.4 ||
yasmād eṣāṃ surendrāṇāṃ mātrābhyo nirmito nṛpaḥ |
tasmād abhibhavaty eṣa sarvbhūtāni tejasā || 7.5 ||
yasmād iti | teṣām indrādīnāṃ dedeśvarāṇāṃ saṃbandhibhyaḥ aṃśebhyo yasmān nṛpatiḥ kṛtaḥ, tasmād eṣa prāṇino vīryeṇādharīkaroti || 7.5 ||
tapaty ādityavac caiṣa cakṣūṃṣi ca manāṃsi ca |
na cainaṃ bhuvi śaknoti kaścid apy abhivīkṣitum || 7.6 ||
tapatīti | eṣa ca rājā paśyatāṃ svavīryeṇa cakṣūṃṣi manāṃsi ca ādityavat saṃtāpayati, na cainaṃ rājānaṃ vīryeṇātiśayayogāt kaścid api pṛthivyām ābhimukhyena vīkṣituṃ śaknoti | evaṃ cendrāṃśāṃśodbhāvatvād iti kathanārtham || 7.6 ||
so ‘gnir bhavati vāyuś ca so ‘rkaṃ somaḥ sa dharmarāṭ |
sa kuberaḥ sa varuṇaḥ mahendraḥ prabhāvataḥ || 7.7 ||
sa iti | “pratāpayuktas tejasvī” ityādivakṣyamāṇāt | ādityatulyavyāpārakaraṇād asāv alaukikaśaktiyogena tāsu kriyāsu rājāgnivāyvādityacandrayamavaiśravaṇavaruṇendratulyo bhavati || 7.7 ||
bālo ‘pi nāvamantavyo manuṣya iti bhūmipaḥ |
mahatī devatā hy eṣā nararūpeṇa tiṣṭhati || 7.8 ||
bālo ‘pīti | bhūpatir bālo ‘pi manuṣyabuddhyā nāvajñeyo yasmād devāṃśodbhavatvān mahaty eṣā devatā manuṣyarūpeṇāste || 7.8 ||
ekam eva dahaty agnir naraṃ durupasarpiṇam |
kulaṃ dahati rājāgniḥ sapasudravyasaṃcayam || 7.9 ||
ekam iti | agnir anupāyasevitaṃ manuṣyam ekam eva dahati | rājāgniḥ punaḥ paśudhanasamūhasahitam aparādhisaṃbandhibandhuvargam api nāśayati | tasmād asau navamantavyo rājāgnir iva vināśakatvān na ca snehātiśayātidarśanena taṃ pratyāśvasanīyaṃ yasmāt || 7.9 ||
kāryaṃ so ‘vekṣya śaktiṃ ca deśakālau ca tattvataḥ |
kurute dharmasiddhyarthaṃ viśvarūpaṃ punaḥ punaḥ || 7.10 ||
kāryam iti | sa rājā prayojanaṃ paryālocyātmaśaktiṃ deśakālau ca snehavairakaraṇocitaiḥ samyag avekṣya śāstravyavasthāsiddhyarthaṃ mitraśatrumadhyasthatayā muhur muhur nānāviśvarūpaṃ kurute || 7.10 ||
yasya prasāde padmā śrīr vijayaś ca parākrame |
mṛtyuś ca vasati krodhe sarvatejomayo hi saḥ || 7.11 ||
yasyeti | yasmād asāv agnyādyaṃśodbhavatvād agnyādisarvatejoyuktas tasmāt tadgatāyāṃ prasannatāyāṃ mahatī śrīr vasati | evaṃ ca śrīkāmenāsau ārādhanīyo vīrye tadā || 7.11 ||
taṃ yas tu dveṣṭi saṃmohāt sa vinaśyaty asaṃśayam |
tasya hy āśu vināśāya rājā prakurute manaḥ || 7.12 ||
tam iti | yathoktam upadeśam aparigaṇayya paunaḥpunyena yatas tan na dviṣyād anyathārthāparādhaṃ ca yena | anyas tu dviṣyamāṇaḥ kadācid dharmaparīkṣā śaktyā vā saheta | rājñaḥ punaḥ sahamānasya svatantravirodhaḥ | yato ‘śaktaś cāsau nigrahaṇaṃ kartum iti | ataḥ sadānuvartitavyaḥ nirupadhaiḥ sadbhiḥ || 7.12 ||
tasmād dharmaṃ yam iṣṭeṣu sa vyavasyen narādhipaḥ |
aniṣṭaṃ cāpy aniṣṭeṣu taṃ dharmaṃ na vicālayet || 7.13 ||
yata evaṃ tasmād dharmam iṣṭeṣu vartayet taṃ narādhipa iti | tasmān nareśvara ātmavallabheṣu śāstraviruddhāyāṃ dṛṣṭārthāyāṃ vyavasthām iṣṭānām eṣāṃ vartitavyam iti niścinuyāt | evam apriyeṣv api yac chāstrāviruddhaṃ bahiṣkaraṇīyādīṃś caitāṃ vyavasthāṃ nātikramet || 7.13 ||
tasyārthaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ goptāraṃ dharmam ātmajam |
brahmatejomayaṃ daṇḍam asṛjat pūrvam īśvaraḥ || 7.14 ||
tadartham iti | rājñaḥ prajāpālanasya prayojanasiddhyarthaṃ sarvaprāṇināṃ ca rakṣitāraṃ caurādyupadravasaṃrakṣaṇena ca samyak karmaghaṭanād [??] dharmaṃ svaśarīrāc codbhūtaṃ brahmaṇasya yat tejas tena nirmitaṃ damanād daṇḍaṃ pūrvaṃ prajāpatiḥ sṛṣṭavāṃs taṃ vinā rājatvānupapatteḥ || 7.14 ||
tasya sarvāṇi bhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca |
bhayād bhogāya kalpante svadharmān na calanti ca || 7.15 ||
tasyeti | “antaḥsaṃjñā bhavanty ete” iti sthāvarāṇām api caitanyasyoktatvāt chedanadidaṇḍabhayena vṛkṣādisthāvarāṇy api phalapuṣpādidvāreṇopabhogārthaṃ saṃpadyante niyatakālaṃ puṣpādidānavyavasthāṃ natikrāmanti kiṃ punar jaṅgamāni || 7.16 ||
taṃ deśakālau śaktiṃ ca vidyāṃ cāvekṣya tattvataḥ |
yathārhataḥ saṃpraṇayen nareṣv anyāyavartiṣu || 7.16 ||
tasmāt tam iti | taṃ daṇḍaṃ grāmyāraṇyādidivānaktādyaparādhakatvād rajakatvasya gatadeśakālau vicārya aparādhisaṃbandhi ca śarīradhanasāmarthaṃ vedādhyayanādi samyag avekṣya anyāyakāriṣu nareṣu aparādhānurūpaṃ kurvīta ity amātyādiviṣayam idaṃ vyavahāriṇā prabhavantam “anubandhaṃ parijñāya” [8.126] iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt || 7.16 ||
sa rājā puruṣo daṇḍaḥ sa netā śāsitā ca saḥ |
caturṇām āśramāṇāṃ ca dharmasya pratibhūḥ smṛtaḥ || 7.17 ||
sa iti | daṇḍahetutatra (hetutas tatra) drohatvasya sa daṇḍa eva rājā sa evam anenaiva nyāyena sa eva puruṣo ‘parādhyānetā sa eva ca netā daṇḍanāyakaḥ senāpatyādiḥ sa eva cānuśāsitā dharmādhikṛto rājasthānīyaḥ sa eva caturṇām api brahmacary(ādy)āśramāṇāṃ dharmānuṣṭhāne pratibhūr ivānusthāpakatvāt smṛtaḥ || 7.17 ||
daṇḍaḥ śāsti prajāḥ sarvā daṇḍa evābhirakṣati |
daṇḍaḥ supteṣu jāgarti daṇḍaṃ dharmaṃ vidur budhāḥ || 7.18 ||
yata evaṃ tasmād daṇḍa iti | daṇḍabhayād eva rājādiśāsanaṃ prajānāṃ nivartate | ato daṇda eva sarvāḥ prajāḥ kartavyā(diṣu) iti tu sthāpayati na rājā śāstraṃ vā daṇḍa eva cāsādhubhyaḥ sādhūn rakṣati supteṣu cāpi rakṣayitṛṣu daṇḍa eva jāgarti tadbhayād eva caurādīnām apravṛtter daṇḍa(m) eva dharmaṃ vadvāṃso manyante aihikāmutrikadaṇḍabhayād dharmapravṛtteḥ || 7.18 ||
samīkṣya sa dhṛtaḥ samyak sarvā rañjayati prajāḥ |
asamīkṣya praṇītas tu vināśayati sarvataḥ || 7.19 ||
samīkṣyeti | sa daṇḍo deśakālādi vicārya samyak lobhādiparityāgena kṛtaḥ sarvāḥ prajāḥ sānurāgāḥ karoti | avicārya punaḥ kṛto dṛṣṭādṛṣṭebhyaḥ kartāraṃ nāśayati || 7.19 ||
yadi na praṇayed rājā daṇḍaṃ daṇḍyeṣv atandritaḥ |
śūle matsyān ivāpakṣyan durbalān balavattarāḥ || 7.20 ||
yadīti | yadi rājānalaso bhūtvā daṇḍārheṣu daṇḍaṃ na kuryāt yathā śūlyā matsyā niṣādādibhir nibhayaiḥ śūle pacyante evaṃ balātiśayayuktā durbalān paceyuḥ || 7.21 ||
adyāt kākaḥ puroḍāśaṃ śvāvalihyād dhavis tathā |
svāmyaṃ ca na syāt kasmiṃścit pravartetādharottaram || 7.21 ||
adyād iti | yadi daṇḍo nābhaviṣyat tadā devebhyaḥ dattam atyantāpasadaḥ kāko ‘pi puroḍāśākhyaṃ havir ātsyat tathātyantādhama(ḥ) śvāpi yavāgvādi havir alekṣyat, na ca kasyacit kasmiṃścit svāmitvaṃ bhavet | brāhmaṇakṣatriyādīñ cotkarṣāpakarṣavaiparītyaṃ pravarteta
|| 7.21 ||
sarvo daṇḍajito loko durlabho hi śucir naraḥ |
daṇḍasya hi bhayāt sarvaṃ jagad bhogāya kalpate || 7.22 ||
sarva iti | sarvaḥ kaścil loko daṇḍena sanmārge sthāpyate tasmān nisargaśuddho naraḥ kṛcchreṇa labhyate | tathā sarvam idaṃ jagad daṇḍabhayād avaśyakartavyopakārāya pravartate
|| 7.22 ||
devadānavagandharvā rakṣāṃsi patagoragāḥ |
te ‘pi bhogāya kalpante daṇḍenaiva nipīḍitāḥ || 7.23 ||
devadānavarakṣāṃsi gardharvā iti | devādayaḥ prathamādhyāyoktabhedā pataṅgā pakṣiṇa uragāḥ sarpā ye te 'pi sakalajagadutpattyanuśāsanakāraṇarūpeśvarasaṃbandhibhayapīḍitāḥ santo varṣādānādyupakāraṃ ca dānavādayo na kurvanti | tathā cādhvaryavaśrutiḥ:
bhayād asyāgnis tapati bhayāt tapati sūryaḥ |
bhayād indraś ca vāyuś ca mṛtyur dhāvati pañcamaḥ || iti || 7.23 ||
duṣyeyuḥ sarvavarṇāś c bhidyeran sarvasetavaḥ |
sarvalokaprakopaś ca bhaved daṇḍasya vibhamāt || 7.24 ||
duṣyeyur iti | daṇḍasyākaraṇād anyāyena ca karaṇāt sarve brāhmaṇādayo varṇā itaretarstrīgamanena saṃkīryeran sarvāś (sarve) ca dharmārthakāmamokṣārthāḥ śāstramaryādā utsīdeyuḥ | evaṃ cāgnau pāstetyādi parasparopakārāya pravartate || 7.24 ||
yatra śyāmo lohitākṣo daṇḍaś carati pāpahā |
prajās tatra na muhyanti netā cet sādhu paśyati || 7.25 ||
yatreti | yatra rāṣṭtre śyāmavarṇo hohitanetraḥ itthaṃbhūtaḥ śāstrapramāṇakādhiṣṭhātṛ-devatārūpo daṇḍaḥ pāpakṛnnāśayitā vicarati tatra prajā na vyākulībhavanti | yadi daṇḍapraṇayanakartā rājādiḥ samyag vicārayati || 7.25 ||
tasyāhuḥ saṃpraṇetāraṃ rājānaṃ satyavādinam |
samīkṣykāriṇaṃ prājñaṃ dharmakāmārthakovidam || 7.26 ||
tasyāhur iti | tasya daṇḍasya kartāraṃ kṣatriyam abhiṣekādiyuktaṃ yathopalabdhābhidhāyinaṃ vimṛśyakāriṇam ūhāpohasamarthaṃ prājñopetaṃ dharmārthakāmānām itaretarānuṣṭhānakuśalaṃ manvādayaḥ āhuḥ || 7.26 ||
taṃ rājā praṇyan samyak trivargeṇābhivardhate |
kāmātmā viṣamaḥ kṣudro daṇḍenaiva nihanyate || 7.27 ||
tam iti | taṃ daṇḍaṃ rājā samyak kurvan dharmārthakāmair vṛddhim eti | yaḥ punaḥ rāgapradhānaḥ krodhanaḥ chalī dveṣī rājā na samyakkṛtena daṇḍenaivātyantakopād adharmād vā vinaśyati || 7.27 ||
daṇḍo hi sumahat tejo durdharaś cākṛtātmabhiḥ |
dharmād vicalitaṃ hanti nṛpam eva sabāndhavam || 7.28 ||
daṇda iti | yato yat tat sumahat tejaḥ sa daṇḍaḥ sahajād eva vinayaśūnyātmabhiś ca duḥkhena kartuṃ śakyate ‘nyāyavartinaṃ rājānam eva putrādi bāndhavasahitaṃ vināśayati || 7.28 ||
tato durgaṃ ca rāṣṭraṃ ca lokaṃ ca sacarācaram |
antarikṣagatāṃś caiva munīn devāṃś ca pīḍayet || 7.29 ||
tata iti | sabandhunṛpatināśānantaraṃ sa daṇḍo vakṣyamāṇaṃ dhanvadurgādidurgaṃ rāṣṭraṃ ca deśaṃ pṛthivīlokaṃ jaṅgamasthāvarasahitam itaḥ pradānajīvino devā ity atas tadbhāvād antarikṣagatān ṛṣīṃś ca devāṃś ca pīḍayet || 7.29 ||
so ‘sahāyena mūrkhena lubdhenākṛtabuddhinā |
na śakyo nyāyato netuṃ saktena viṣayeṣu ca || 7.30 ||
so ‘sahāyena mūrkheneti | sa daṇḍo ‘vidyamānamantryādisahāyena mūrkheṇa lobhavatā śāstrasaṃskāraś cākṛtabuddhinā (śāstrasaṃskārarahitenākṛtabuddhinā) dyūtādiviṣayasaktena rājñā śāstramaryādayā kartum aśakyaḥ || 7.30 ||
śucinā satyasandhena yathāśāstrānusāriṇā |
praṇetuṃ śakyate daṇḍaḥ susahāyena dhīmatā || 7.31 ||
śucineti | arthādiśaucayuktena jitendriyatvāt satyapratijñena śāstrāntatikramavartinā sahāyena prajñāvatey evaṃ pūrvoktadoṣapratipakṣabhūtaguṇayuktena rājñā samyak daṇḍaḥ kartuṃ śakyate || 7.31 ||
svarāṣṭre nyāyavṛttiḥ syād bhṛśadaṇḍaś ca śatruṣu |
suhṛtsv ajihmaḥ snigdeṣu brāhmaṇeṣu kṣamānvitaḥ || 7.32 ||
svarāṣṭre nyāyavṛttir iti | ātmarāṣṭre śāstramaryādayā varteta | śatruviṣaye punas tīkṣṇadaṇḍo bhavet | snigdheṣu ca mitreṣu kāryamitreṣu akuṭilaḥ syāt | brāhmaṇeṣu buddhipūrvaṃ cālpaṃ tadaparādhaṃ kṛtavatsu kṣamāyuktaḥ syāt || 7.32 ||
evaṃvṛttasya nṛpateḥ śiloñchenāpi jīvataḥ |
vistīryate yaśo loke tailabindur ivāṃbhasi || 7.33 ||
evam iti | evamācārasya rājño ‘yaṃ tatkṣīṇakośasyāpi jale tailabindusekād vā loke khyātiḥ vistaram eti || 7.33 ||
astu viparītasya nṛpater ajitātmanaḥ |
saṃkṣipyate yaśo loke ghṛtabindur ivāmbhasi || 7.34 ||
ata iti | etasmād ācārād viparītād ācārato rājño jitendriyasya ghṛtabindhur ivodake khyātiḥ saṃkocam upayāti || 7.34 ||
sve sve dharme niviṣṭānāṃ sarveṣām anjpūrvaśaḥ |
varṇānām āśramāṇāṃ ca rājā sṛṣṭo ‘bhirakṣitā || 7.35 ||
sve sva iti | krameṇa svadharmaratānāṃ sarveṣāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ varṇānāṃ brahmacaryādīnāṃ cāśramāṇāṃ brahmaṇā rājā rakṣitā sṛṣṭaḥ | uttaravivakṣārtham uktavān || 7.35 ||
tena yad yat sabhṛtyena kartavyaṃ rakṣatā prajāḥ |
tat tad vo ‘haṃ pravakṣyāmi yathāvad anupūrvaśaḥ || 7.36 ||
teneti | tena rājñā prajāṃ pālayatā amātyasahitena yat kartavyaṃ tat kṛtsnaṃ krameṇa yuṣmākam ahaṃ kathayiṣyāmi || 7.36 ||
brāhmaṇān paryupāsīta prātar utthāya pārthivaḥ |
traividyavṛddhān viduṣas tiṣṭhet teṣāṃ ca śāsane || 7.37 ||
brāhmaṇān iti | ahar ahaḥ prātar utthāya brāhmaṇān ṛgyajuḥsāmākhyavidyātrayagranthārtha-pāragān viduṣo vakṣyamāṇān vīkṣikīdaṇḍanītyābhijñān seveta tadājñāṃ cānutiṣṭhet || 7.37 ||
vṛddhāṃś ca nityaṃ seveta viprān vedavidaḥ śucīn |
vṛddhasevī hi satataṃ rakṣobhir api pūjyate || 7.38 ||
vṛddhān iti | tāṃś ca viprān vayovṛddhān vedajñānārthādiśuddhān viśvasanīyatvotpādanārtham api nityaṃ seveta | yasmād vṛddhasevī yaḥ so ‘tyantāviśvāsibhī rākṣasair api pūjyate, teṣām api viśvasanīyo bhavati, kiṃ punar manuṣyāṇām || 7.38 ||
tebhyo ‘dhigacched vinayaṃ vinītātmāpi nityaśaḥ |
vinītātmā hi nṛpatir na vinaśyati karhicit || 7.39 ||
tebhya iti | yady apy aśakyaśāstrābhijñatayā sahajaprajñayā vātivinīto bhavati, tathāpy atiśayārthaṃ tebhyo brāhmaṇebhyo nītimārgaṃ nityaṃ śikṣeta, yasmān nītipradhāno rājā na kadācid api naśyet || 7.39 ||
bahavo ‘vinayān naṣṭā rājānaḥ saparicchadāḥ |
vanasthā api rājyāni vinayāt pratipedire || 7.40 ||
bahava iti | bahavo rājāno hastyaśvakośādipracchadayuktā api avinītatayā naṣṭāḥ, anye punar vanavāsino ‘paricchadā api vinayatvena rājyāni prāptavantaḥ || 7.40 ||
veno vinaṣṭo ‘vinayān nahuṣaś caiva pārthivaḥ |
sudāḥ paijavanaś caiva sumukho nimir eva ca || 7.41 ||
tathā ca vena iti | veno nahuṣaś cāpi pijavanasya ca putraḥ sudānāmamukhau nimiścāvinayān naṣṭāḥ || 7.41 ||
pṛthus to vinayād rājyaṃ prāptavān manur eva ca |
kuberaś ca dhanaiśvaryaṃ brāhmaṇyaṃ caiva gādhijaḥ || 7.42 ||
pṛthur iti | pṛthumanū vinītatayā rājyaṃ prāptavantau | vaiśravaṇaś ca vinaye sati dhanaiśvaryaṃ prāptavān | gādhiputraś ca viśvāmitraḥ kṣatriyaḥ san tenaiva śarīreṇāttyantaduṣprāpaṃ brāhmaṇyaṃ vinayayogāt prāptavān | tasmāt vṛddhebhyo vinayam adhigacchet || 7.42 ||
traividyebhyas trayīṃ vidyāṃ daṇḍanītiṃ ca śāśvatīm |
ānvīkṣiṅīṃ cātmavidyāṃ vārtārambhāṃś ca lokataḥ || 7.43 ||
traividyebhya iti | ṛgyajuḥsāmākhyavidyātrayādhyāyibhya ṛgyajuḥsāmākhyāṃ trayīvidyām abhyaset, na tu śikṣayet | samāvṛttasya rājyādhikārāt turyāvasthāyām evādhigacchet | daṇḍanītiṃ cārthaśāstram arthayogakṣemopayoginīṃ śāśvatīm anādipāraṃparyāgatāṃ tebhya eva śikṣayeta | tathā tarkavidyāṃ cādhyātmikāṃ sāṃkhyādividyāṃ paramaṇḍaladūtoktipaṅktyādyupayoginīm abhyudayavyasanakāleṣu ca harṣaviṣādānutpādakāriṇīṃ śikṣeta tathā kṣatriyaḥ kṛṣivāṇijādipaśupālanādivārtāṃ tannimittārambhān pravṛttīḥ dhanopacayahetūn lokatas tadvidhebhyaḥ karṣakādibhyaḥ śikṣet || 7.43 ||
indriyāṇāṃ jaye yogaṃ samātiṣṭhed divāniśam |
jitendriyo hi śaknoti vaśe sthāpayituṃ prajāḥ || 7.44 ||
indriyāṇām iti | cakṣurādīnām indriyāṇāṃ viṣayaśaktiparihāre | nityakālam abhiyogaṃ kuryāt | sa jitendriyaḥ prajāḥ vaśīkartuṃ śaknoti || 7.44 ||
daśa kāmasamutthāni tathāṣṭau krodhajāni ca |
vyasanāni durantāni prayatnena vivarjayet || 7.45 ||
daśeti | daśa kāmodbhavāni vakṣyamāṇāni nityavyaanāni, krodhajāni cāṣṭau duḥkhāvasānāni yatnataḥ pariharet || 7.45 ||
kāmajeṣu prasakto hi vyasaneṣu mahīpatiḥ |
viyujyate ‘rthadharmābhyāṃ krodhajeṣv ātmanaiva tu || 7.46 ||
kāmajeṣu vyasaneṣu prasakto mahīpatir arthadharmābhyāṃ viyuyate | krodhajeṣu vyasaneṣu prasakto mahīpatir ātmanaiva viyujyate || 7.46 ||
mṛgayākṣo divāsvapnaḥ parivādaḥ striyo madaḥ |
tauryatrikaṃ vṛthāṭhyā ca kāmajo daśako gaṇaḥ || 7.47 ||
mṛgayeti | ākheṭakārthaṃ mṛgavadhākṣādikrīḍāsakalam api vighnahetubhūto divāsvapnaḥ paradoṣavacanaṃ striyo mado madyapānakṛtaḥ tūryatriko nṛtyagītavāditrāṇi, animittaś cānugamanam ity eṣa daśaparimāṇo vyasanavargo ‘bhilāṣaviśeṣāj jāyate || 7.47 ||
paiśunyaṃ sāhasaṃ droha īrṣyāsūyārthadūṣaṇam |
vāgdaṇḍajaṃ ca pāruṣyaṃ krodhajo ‘pi gaṇo ‘ṣṭakaḥ || 7.48 ||
paiśunyam iti | paiśunyam avijñātadoṣāviṣkaraṇam | sāhasaṃ sādhor nikṛṣṭakarmaviniyogo bandhanaṃ vā | drohaś chadmanā vadhaḥ | īrṣyā parasyātmanā saha guṇasāmānyākṣamaṇam | asūyā paraguṇastavāmarṣaṇam | arthadūṣaṇaṃ prāptānām arthānām adānam apahāro vā | vākpāruṣyam ākrośādi | daṇḍapāruṣyaṃ tāḍanādi | ity aṣṭaparimāṇo vyasanasaṃghaḥ krodhāj jāyate || 7.48 ||
dvayor apy etayor mūlaṃ yaṃ sarve kavayo viduḥ |
taṃ yatnena jayel lobhaṃ tajjāv etāv ubhau gaṇau || 7.49 ||
dvayor iti | dvayor apy etayoḥ kāmakordhavyasanavargayoḥ svayaṃ kāraṇabhūtaṃ sarve smṛtyupanibandhagranthakārā manyante | taṃ lobhaṃ yatnataḥ pariharet | yasmād etat samudbhavam etad gaṇadvayaṃ sarveṣāṃ lobho mūlam, kvacid dhanalipsayā kvacic charīrasukhalobhena pravṛtteḥ || 7.49 ||
pānam akṣāḥ striyaś caiva mṛgayā ca yathākramam |
etat kaṣṭatamaṃ vidyāc catuṣkaṃ kāmaje gaṇe || 7.50 ||
pānam iti | madaparyantaṃ pānam akṣakriḍā strīsaṃprayogo mṛgayā cety etat kramapaṭhitaṃ kāmajagaṇaṃ madhvādhīneṣu vyasaneṣu catuṣkam atiśayena duḥkhahetuṃ bahutaraduḥkhahetutvāj jānīyāt || 7.50 ||
daṇḍasya pātanaṃ caiva vākpāruṣyārthadūṣaṇe |
krodhaje ‘pi gaṇe vidyāt kaṣṭam etat trikaṃ sadā || 7.51 ||
daṇḍasyeti | vākpāruṣyadaṇḍapāruṣyārthaduṣaṇātmakaṃ trikaṃ krodhajavyasanaganamadhyād doṣabhūyastve sati sarvadā doṣahetuṃ budhyeta || 7.51 ||
saptakasyāsya vargasya sarvatraivānuṣaṅgiṇaḥ |
pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ gurutaraṃ vidyād vyasanam ātmavān || 7.52 ||
saptakasyāsyeti | asya pānadaṇḍapātanādeḥ kāmakrodhodbhavasya saptaparimāṇagatasya vyasanagaṇasya sarvasminn eva rājamaṇḍale prāyeṇāvasthitasya yad yat pūrvaṃ paṭhitaṃ vyasanaṃ tat tat parasmād duṣṭataraṃ praśastātmā jānīyāt | dyūtāt pānaṃ duṣṭataram, tatra hi saṃjñāpraṇāśādayo nītiśāstroktā doṣāḥ, dyūteṣu pākṣiko dhanaprāptir api bhavati | strīvyasanāt dyūtaṃ duṣṭataram, tatra hi vairodbhādayo nītiśāstroktā doṣāḥ, strīvyasane punar apatyotpādaguṇayogotpattiḥ | mṛgayāyāṃ punar vyāyāmādinā guṇayogo ‘pi bhavati | evaṃ kāmajacatuṣkasya pūrvaṃ pūrvam pāpīyaḥ | krodhajānām api vākpāruṣyād daṇḍāpāruṣyaṃ duṣṭam, aṅgacchedādinā aśakyapratikāratvāt | vākpāruṣye punar dānasaṃmānādinā asti pratisandhanam | arthadūṣaṇād vākpāruṣyaṃ duṣṭataram, aruntudākrośasantāpopaśamanasya duḥsahatvāt | arthadūṣaṇaṃ punaḥ pracuratarārthadānena parihartuṃ pāryate | evaṃ krodhajatrikasyāpi pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ pāpīyaḥ, ataś ca yad yad duṣṭataraṃ tat tad durvyasanaṃ pariharet || 7.52 ||
vyasanasya ca mṛtyoś ca vyasanaṃ kaṣṭam ucyate |
vyasany adho ‘dho vrajati svar yāty avyasanī mṛtaḥ || 7.53 ||
vayasanasyeti | mṛtyvyasanayor madhye vyasanaṃ duṣṭataraṃ yasmād vyasanī yāvad dīrghakālaṃ jīvati tāvat tāvac chāstrārthātikramādhikyād adhikataraṃ narakaṃ gacchati | avyasanī punar mṛtaḥ śāstrānuṣṭhānāt svargaṃ vrajati || 7.53 ||
maulāñ chāstravidaḥ śūrāṃl labdhalakṣān kulodgatān |
sacivān sapta cāṣṭau vā kurvīta suparīkṣitān || 7.54 ||
maulān iti | pitrādyanvayāyātān snehātiśayenāvyabhicārārthaṃ dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthaśāstrajñān dṛṣṭādṛṣṭamaryādānullaṅghanārthaṃ vikrāntān śarīrānapekṣaṃ prabhuvṛttyarthaṃ punaḥ kṛtarājakāryān asaṃmohena karmasaṃpattyartham | kulīnān kulābhimānenāvyabhicārārtham | dharmārthakāmamayaviṣaye cāraprayogādidvāreṇa kṛtaparīkṣān śuddhān sapta cāṣṭau vāmātyān sahāyabhūtān mantrādau kurvīta || 7.54 ||
api yat sukaraṃ karma tad apy ekena duṣkaram |
viśeṣato ‘sahāyena kiṃ nu rājyaṃ mahodayam || 7.55 ||
yasmād apīti | viśeṣato ‘sahāyena kim u rājyaṃ mahodayam | yad api karma kartuṃ śakyate, tad apy ekasya duḥsādhyaṃ bhavati, viśeṣeṇa rājyaṃ yan mahāphalaṃ tat katham asahāyena kartuṃ śakyate || 7.55 ||
taiḥ sārdhaṃ cintayen nityaṃ sāmānyaṃ saṃdhivigraham |
sthānaṃ samudayaṃ suptiṃ labdhapraśamanāni ca || 7.56 ||
tair iti | taiḥ sacivaiḥ saha sarvadā yac cātirahasyaṃ saṃdhivigrahādivakṣyamāṇaṃ tad vicārayet | tathā tiṣṭhaty aneneti sthānaṃ daṇḍakośapurarāṣṭrākhyaṃ cintayet | tatra daṇḍo hastyaśvarathapadātyātmakas tasya poṣaṇādi cintyam | kośo ‘rthanicayas tasya vyayādi cintyam | purasya tasyāyudhasaṃpannam iti vakṣyati | rāṣṭraṃ deśas tasya ca rāṣṭriyajanapaśvādiraṇākṣamatvādi cintyam | tathā samudyanty utpadyante ‘smād arthā iti samudayaḥ, kṛṣyādidhānyahiraṇyādi sthānaṃ (=utpattisthānam) tac cintayet | guptiṃ rakṣām ātmagatām, suparīkṣitam annādyam adyāt | “parīkṣitāḥ striyaś cainam” (7.219) ityādinā vakṣyati rāṣṭragatāṃ ca “rāṣṭrasya saṃgrahe nityam” (7.212) ityādinā abhidhāsyati, labdhapraśamanāni ca “jitvā saṃpūjayed devān” (7.201) iti vakṣyati || 7.56 ||
teṣāṃ svaṃ svam abhiprāyam upalabhya pṛthak pṛthak |
samastānāṃ ca kāryeṣu vidadhyād dhitam ātmanaḥ || 7.57 ||
teṣām iti | teṣāṃ sacivānāṃ rahaḥ pragalbhatarasaṃbhavāt pṛthak pṛthak svaṃ svaṃ mataṃ kāryeṣu vijñāya parṣatpragalbhasaṃbhavāc ca samastānām api yugapad abhiprāyaṃ buddhvā yad ātmānaṃ hitaṃ tat kuryāt || 7.57 ||
sarveṣāṃ tu viśiṣṭena brāhmaṇena vipaścitā |
mantrayet paramaṃ mantraṃ rājā ṣāḍguṇyasaṃyutam || 7.58 ||
sareṣām iti | eṣām eva sarveṣāṃ madhyād anyatamena guṇātiśayavatā atyantaviduṣā brāhmaṇena saha sandhivigrahādi vakṣyamāṇaguṇaṣaṭkayuktaṃ prakṣṛṣṭamantraṃ rājā vicāreyate || 7.58 ||
nityaṃ tasmin samāśvastaḥ sarvakāryāṇi nikṣipet |
tena sārdhaṃ viniścitya tataḥ karma samārabhet || 7.59 ||
nityam iti | tasmin brāhmaṇe samyak nirviśaṅkaḥ saṃjātaviśvāso bhūtvā sarvadā sarvakāryāṇi samarthayet (samarpayet?) | yady api cātmanā kuryāt tad api tena saha niścayīkṛtya tato ‘nutiṣṭhet || 7.59 ||
anyān api prakurvīta śucīn prājānavasthitān |
samyagarthasamāhartṝn amātyān suparīkṣitān || 7.60 ||
anyān iti | anyān api bhṛtyān arthādiśuddhān prajñāvataḥ sthirānucārādidvāreṇa kṛtārthādiśuddhiparīkṣān ākarasthānebhyaḥ samyag arthotpādanaśīlān arthādhigatān kuryāt
|| 7.60 ||
nivartetāsya yāvadbhir itikartavyatā nṛbhiḥ |
tāvato ‘tandritān dakṣān prakurvīta vicakṣaṇān || 7.61 ||
nivarteteti | asya rājñaḥ karmajātaṃ yat kartavyaṃ tad yat saṃkhyākair manuṣyaiḥ saṃpadyate tatsaṃkhyān analasāṃś caturāṃs tatkarmajñān kurvīta || 7.61 ||
teṣām artha niyuñjīta śūrān dakṣān kulodgatān |
śucīn ākarakarmānte bhīrūn antarniveśane || 7.62 ||
teṣām iti | teṣāṃ madhye śūrān kośe viniyuñjīta svākareṣu suvarṇādyutpattisthāneṣu ikṣudhānyādipīḍanādisthāneṣu arthaśaucopetān kurvīta | antarniveśane ca bhojanāntaḥpurādau bhīrūn viniyojayet || 7.62 ||
dūtaṃ caiva prakurvīta sarvaśāstraviśāradam |
iṅgitākāraceṣṭajñaṃ śuciṃ dakṣaṃ kulodgatam || 7.63 ||
dūtam iti | dūtaṃ ca dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthaśāstrārthapāragam iti tasyābhiprāyasūcakasya vākkāyavyāpāraviśeṣasyākārasya cābhiprāyasūcakasya śarīravikārasya ceṣṭāyāś ca kavacāyudhabhojanātmikāyā vijñātāram arthastrīśaucādiyuktaṃ kulīnaṃ kurvīta || 7.63 ||
anuraktaḥ śucir dakṣaḥ smṛtimān deśakālavit |
vapuṣmān vītabhīr vāgmī dūto rājñaḥ praśasyate || 7.64 ||
yasmāt anurakta iti | anurāgavān arthastrīśaucayuktabhedyarthaparihārārthaṃ caturaḥ kālānatikramārthaṃ smaraṇavān, samagrasaṃdeśārthaṃ deśakālajñaḥ, tadanurūpe vyavahārārthaṃ svākṛtipriyadarśanenādeyavākyatatvārthaṃ caturo yo vāgmī puruṣasaṃdeśakathanārthaṃ saṃskṛtavicitrābhidhāyī uktipratyuktisāmarthyārtham ity evaṃvidho dūto rājñaḥ prakarṣeṇeṣyate || 7.64 ||
amātye daṇḍa āyatto daṇḍe vainayikī kriyā |
nṛpatau kośarāṣṭre ca dūte saṃdhiviparyayau || 7.65 ||
amātya iti | senāpatau hastyaśvarathapadātyātmake daṇḍa āyattaḥ, tad icchayā tasyopacayaḥ daṇḍe ca hastyādike nītyartho vyāpāra āyattaḥ, tadabhāve nītyābhāvāt rājani cārthāgāradeśāv āyattau, tadicchayā vṛddhikṣayotpatteḥ, dūte ca saṃdhivigrahāv āyattau tadicchayā tatpravṛtteḥ
|| 7.65 ||
dūta eva hi saṃdhatte bhinatty eva ca saṃhatān |
dūtas tat kurute karma bhidyante yena mānavāḥ || 7.66 ||
dūta iti | dūtas tat kurute karma bhidyante yena mānavāḥ | yasmād dūta eva bhinnānāṃ saṃdhyutpādane samarthaḥ, saṃhatānāṃ ca bhedane tathā pararāṣṭre vyavaharati yena saṃhatā bhidyante tasmād dūte saṃdhiviparyayāv āyattau cety etan niyamārtham āha || 7.66 ||
sa vidyād asya kṛtyeṣu nigūḍheṅgitaceṣṭitaiḥ |
ākāram iṅgitaṃ ceṣṭāṃ bhṛtyeṣu ca cikīrṣitam || 7.67 ||
sa vidyād iti | sa sūto ‘prakāśābhir ākāraceṣṭābhir ātmīyābhiḥ parasya rājño mukhavikāśavaivarṇyādyākāraṃ harṣaviṣādasūcitam iṅgitasvedavepayuromāñcādyātmakaṃ pañcasaṃdhivigrahābhiprāyasūcakaṃ ceṣṭāṃ ca vāhanāyudhasaṃskārodāsīnātmikām utsāhasūcanīṃ jānīyāt | abhilaṣitaṃ tu nopekṣitam iti jānīyāt, kṛtyeṣu ca kruddhalubdhabhītāpamāniteṣu tadbhṛtyeṣu kartum iṣṭasaṃgrahaṇam || 7.67 ||
buddhvā ca sarvaṃ tattvena pararājacikīrṣitam |
tathā prayatnam ātiṣṭhed yathātmānaṃ na pīḍayet || 7.68 ||
buddhveti | pararājasaṃbandhi karum iṣṭam aśeṣaṃ paramārthato jñātvā tathā dūto yateta yathā svāmināśadvāreṇa tasyātmanāśo na bhavati || 7.68 ||
jāṅgalaṃ sasyasaṃpannam āryaprāyam anāvilam |
ramyam ānatasāmantaṃ svājīvyaṃ deśam āvaset || 7.69 ||
jāṅgalaṃ sasyasaṃpannam āryaprāyam anākulam iti |
alpodakatṛṇo yas tu pravātaḥ pracurātapaḥ |
jñeyaḥ sa jāṅgalo deśaḥ ||
iti tathāvidhaṃ deśaṃ pracuradhānyādikaṃ bahudhārmikajanaṃ rogamaśakādyupadravānākulaṃ phalakusumodyānādiyutaṃ ca cāṭavikādisāmantanivāsinaṃ karṣakādīnāṃ sukhopajīvyam āśritya rājā nivāsaṃ kuryāt || 7.69 ||
dhanvadurgaṃ mahīdurgam abdurgaṃ vārkṣam eva vā |
nṛdurgaṃ giridurgaṃ vā samāśritya vaset puram || 7.70 ||
dhanvadurgam iti | dhanvadurgam antarudakaṃ bāhyataḥ samantāt pañcayojanamātramarubhūmyupetam | mahīdurgaṃ dṛḍhaṃ vapropanihitaṃ vistārād dviguṇocchrāyadvādaśahastādhikocchritopari yuddhārthagavākṣādiprākāropetam | ambudurgaṃ bāhyato ‘gādhānāśrāvakodakaparivṛtam | vṛkṣadurgaṃ bahiḥsamantāt tato yojanamātraṃ vyāpyāsti saṃhatamahāvṛkṣopetam | manuṣyadurgaṃ samantato ‘vasthāpitahastyaśvapadātyupetam, āyudhāvikrāntapuruṣam | giridurgaṃ parvatapṛṣṭham atidurāroham atisaṃkaṭair vā mārgānugatam | antarnadīprasravaṇādi udakayuktam ity evam eteṣu durgeṣu madhyād anyatamaṃ durgam āśritya puraṃ kuryāt || 7.70 ||
sarveṇa tu prayatnena giridurgaṃ samāśrayet |
eṣāṃ hi bāhuguṇyena giridurgaṃ viśiṣyate || 7.71 ||
sarveṇa tu prayatneneti | yat eṣāṃ durgāṇāṃ madhyād guṇabhūyastvena giridurgaḥ praśasyate iti giridurgaṃ sarvaprayatnena samāśrayet || 7.71 ||
trīṇy ādyāny āśritās tv eṣāṃ mṛgagartāśrayāpsaraḥ |
trīṇy uttarāṇi kramaśaḥ plavaṅgamanarāmarāḥ || 7.72 ||
trīṇy ādyāny āśritās tv eṣāṃ mṛgagartāśrayā pureti | madhyādyāni trīṇi dhanvamahyabdurgāṇi mṛgādaya āśritāḥ | tatra dhanvadurgaṃ mṛgair āśritam | mahīdurgaṃ bilāsādair nakulādibhiḥ | ambudurgam udakacāribhiḥ kūrmādibhiḥ | uttarāṇi trīṇi vṛkṣanṛgiridurgāṇi krameṇa plagaṅgamādaya āśritāḥ | tatra vṛkṣadurgaṃ vānarair āśritam | nṛdurgaṃ manuṣyaiḥ | giridurgaṃ devaiḥ || 7.72 ||
yathā durgāśritān etān nopahiṃsanti śatravaḥ |
tathārayo na hiṃsanti nṛpaṃ durgasamāśritam || 7.73 ||
yatheti | yathaitān mṛgādīn durgavartino vyādhādayaḥ śastravaḥ na hiṃsanti aśaktatvāt, evaṃ durgāśritaṃ rājānaṃ na śatrava iti || 7.73 ||
ekaḥ śataṃ yodhayati prākārastho dhanurdharaḥ |
śataṃ daśasahasrāṇi tadmād durgaṃ vidhīyate || 7.74 ||
eka iti | yasmād eko dhānuṣkaḥ prākārasthaḥ parakīyānāṃ śataṃ yoddhuṃ śaknoti tasmād durgaṃ kartavyatayopadiśyate || 7.74 ||
tat syād āyudhasaṃpannaṃ dhanadhānyena vāhanaiḥ |
brāhmaṇaiḥ śilpibhir yantrair yavasenaudakena ca || 7.75 ||
tasmād iti | tad durgaṃ khaḍgādyāyudhadhanadhānyāśvādivāhanabrāhmaṇatkṣādiśilpayantra-ghāsodakasamṛddhaṃ kāryam || 7.75 ||
tasya madhye suparyāptaṃ kārayed gṛham ātmanaḥ |
guptaṃ sarvartukaṃ śubhraṃ jalavṛkṣasamanvitam || 7.76 ||
tasyeti | tasya durgasya madhye suṣṭhu paryāptaṃ pṛthag āyudhānāṃ śālādyupetaṃ prākāraparikhādirakṣitaṃ sarvartukaṃ phalapuṣpādiyogena sarve ṛtavo yatra sudhādhavalitaṃ dīrghakādijalayuktaṃ vṛkṣāśritaṃ gṛham ātmanaḥ kārayet || 7.76 ||
tad adhyāsyodvahed bhāryāṃ savarṇāṃ lakṣaṇānvitām |
kule mahati saṃbhūtāṃ hṛdyāṃ rūpaguṇānvitām || 7.77 ||
tad iti | tad gṛham adhiṣṭhāya samānavarṇāṃ praśastalekhātilakādilakṣaṇopetāṃ mahākulīnāṃ manoramāṃ svākṛtim ācārādisaṃpannāṃ bhāryām upayacchet || 7.77 ||
purohitaṃ ca kurvīta vṛṇuyād eva cartvijaḥ |
te ‘sya gṛhyāṇi karmāṇi kuryur vaitānikāni ca || 7.78 ||
purohitam iti | purohitaṃ cātharvaṇavidhijñaṃ kurvīta | ṛtvijaś ca sūtrakāroktaprakriyayā vṛṇuyāt | te cāsya rājño gṛhyoktāni śrautāni karmāṇi kuryuḥ || 7.78 ||
yajeta rājā kratubhir vividhair āptadakṣiṇaiḥ |
dharmārthaṃ caiva viprebhyo dadyād bhogān dhanāni ca || 7.79 ||
yajeteti | rājāśvamedhapuṇḍarīkādīn nānāprakārān paryāptadakṣiṇān yajñān kuryāt | brāhmaṇebhyaḥ svīyān gṛhaśayyādīn bhogān dhanāni ca suvarṇādīni dharmārthaṃ dadyād itīdam adhikaṃ rājño dānayāgam avaśyakartavyam ucyate || 7.79 ||
sāṃvatsarikam āptaiś ca rāṣṭrād āhārayed balim |
syāc cāmnāyaparo loke varteta pitṛvan nṛṣu || 7.80 ||
sāṃvatsarikam iti | āptair hitakāribhir uktaguṇaiḥ amātyair vārṣikaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ dhānyādikaṃ rāṣṭrād ānāyayet | śāstrapradhānaś ca loke karaśulkādiṣu syāt rāṣṭrīyeṣu pitṛvat snehādinā varteta || 7.80 ||
adhyakṣān vividhān kuryāt tatra tatra vipaścitaḥ |
te ‘sya sarvāṇy avekṣeran nṛṇāṃ kāryāṇi kurvatām || 7.81 ||
adhyakṣān iti | tasya kāryāṇy avekṣeran nṛṇāṃ kāṛyāṇi kurvatām | tatra tatra hastyaśvāyudhārthādisthāneṣu adhikṛtān vipaścitaḥ tatra tatkarmakuśalān vividhān pṛthak pṛthak kuryāt | na cāsyākṣateṣu teṣu hastyaśvādisthāneṣu tadaupayikāni kāryāṇi kurvatāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ sarvāṇi kāryāṇi samyakkaraṇārtham avekṣeran || 7.81 ||
āvṛttānāṃ gurukulād viprāṇāṃ pūjako bhavet |
nṛpāṇām akṣayo hy eṣa nidhir brāhmo ‘bhidhīyate || 7.82 ||
āvṛttānām iti | nṛpāṇām akṣayo hy eṣa nidhir brāhmo vidhīyate | yato gurukulanivṛttānāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ niyamato dhanadānena pūjāṃ ca kuryāt, yasmāt so ‘yaṃ nidhir iva nidhiḥ brāhmaṇeṣu nihito dhanādiṣu vinaśvaro rājñāṃ śāstreṇopadiśyate | brāhmaṇākhyo janmāntare ‘pi sunihito bhavati || 7.82 ||
na taṃ stenā na cāmitrā haranti na ca naśyati |
tasmād rājñā nidhātavyo brāhmaṇeṣv akṣayo nidhiḥ || 7.83 ||
neti | taṃ brāhmaṃ nidhiṃ na caurā nāpi śatravo haranti | anyanidhivan nāpi mṛtsaṃyogādinā naśyati | tasmād yo ‘yam akṣayo nidhir brāhmaṇākhyanidhir janmāntare uttamāntaphalāvāptaye rājñā dhanādir brāhmaṇeṣu nidhātavyaḥ || 7.83 ||
na skandate na cyavate na vinaśyati karhicit |
variṣṭham agnihotrebhyo brāhmaṇasya mukhe hutam || 7.84 ||
na skandata iti | agnihotrādyāhuteḥ kila sravaṇaṃ calam nāśākhyā doṣā bhavanti | ataś ca karmavaiguṇyaṃ yat punar etad brāhmaṇamukhāgnau hutam, brāhmaṇasya dattaṃ tan na sravati na calati nāpi dāhādinā kadācid vinaśyati | tasmād etad doṣāsaṃbhavād agnihotrādibhyaḥ śreṣṭham iti prakṛtadānastutiparam etat || 7.84 ||
samam abrāhmaṇe dānaṃ dviguṇaṃ brāhmaṇabruve |
ācārye śatasāhasram anantaṃ vedapārage || 7.85 ||
samam iti | sahasraguṇam ācārye anantaṃ vedapārage | brāhmaṇavyatiriktaviṣaye upakārakaraṇarūpeṇa yad dānaṃ na dānapratigraharūpeṇa tatra tadabhāvāt tatsamaṃ yasya deyadravyasya yat phalaṃ śrutaṃ tat tato nātyutkṛṣṭam iti prāpyate | evaṃ jātimātropajīvini brāhmaṇe pūrvāpekṣayā dviguṇaṃ phalam evaṃ prakrāntādhyayane lakṣaguṇaṃ phalam, samastaśākhādhyāyini anantaphalam || 7.85 ||
pātrasya hi viśeṣeṇa śraddadhānatayaiva ca |
alpaṃ vā bahu vā pretya dānasya phalam aśnute || 7.86 ||
pātrasya hi viśeṣeṇa śraddadhānas tathaiva ca | alpaṃ vā bahu vā pretya dānasyāvāpyate phalam | adhyayanaṃ vijñānatapoyuktatayā pātratāratamyam apekṣya śraddhātāratamyaṃ cānyaṃ vā bahu cāparaloke vā dānasya phalaṃ prāpyate || 7.86 ||
samottamādhamai rājā tv āhūtaḥ pālayan prajāḥ |
na nivarteta saṃgrāmāt kṣātraṃ dharmam anusmaran || 7.87 ||
samottamādhamair iti | samādhikahīnabalaiḥ rājabhir yuddhārtham āhūtaḥ prajārakṣaṇodyataḥ kṣatriyeṇāhūtenāvaśyaṃ yoddhavyam ity evaṃ kṣātradharmaṃ saṃbhāvayan saṃgrāmān na nivarteta || 7.87 ||
saṃgrāmeṣv anivartitvaṃ prajānāṃ caiva pālanam |
śuśrūṣā brāhmaṇānāṃ ca rājñāṃ śreyaskaraṃ param || 7.88 ||
saṃgrāmeṣv iti | yuddhebhyo ‘parāṅmukhatvam, prajānāṃ pālanam, brāhmaṇānāṃ paricaryety etad rājñāṃ prakṛṣṭaṃ svargādisādhanam || 7.88 ||
āhaveṣu mitho ‘nyonyaṃ jighāṃsanto mahīkṣitaḥ |
yudhyamānāḥ paraṃ śaktyā svargaṃ yānty aparāṅmukhāḥ || 7.89 ||
tathā cāhaveṣv iti | mahīpālāḥ mithaḥ spardhamānāḥ saṃgrāmeṣv itaretaraṃ hantukāmāḥ prakṛṣṭayā śaktyā saṃmukhaṃ yudhyamānāḥ svargaṃ gacchanti || 7.89 ||
na kūṭair āyudhair hanyād yudhyamāno raṇe ripūn |
na karṇibhir nāpi digdhair nāgnijvalitatejanaiḥ || 7.90 ||
na kūṭair iti | na karṇikair nāpi dagdhair nāgnijvalitatejanaiḥ | saṃgrāme yuddhaṃ kurvan kūṭaśastrādibhiḥ śatrūn hanyamāno ‘pi kṛtakarṇākāraphalakair nāpi viṣaliptair na cāgnijvalitaphalakaiḥ || 7.90 ||
na ca hanyāt sthalārūḍhaṃ na klībaṃ na kṛtāñjalim |
na muktakeśaṃ nāsīnaṃ na tavāsmīti vādinam || 7.91 ||
na ceti | yuddhabhūmiṃ tyaktvā sthalam ārūḍhaṃ napuṃsakaṃ baddhāñjaliṃ, muktakeśopaviṣṭaṃ tvadīyo ‘ham ity evaṃ vaktāraṃ na hanyāt || 7.91 ||
na suptaṃ na visannāhaṃ na nagnaṃ na nirāyudham |
nāyudhyamānaṃ paśyantaṃ na pareṇa samāgatam || 7.92 ||
na suptam iti | suptaṃ vigatasannāham aparidhānān āyudhapradhanaprekṣakān anyaiś ca yudhyamānān na hanyāt || 7.92 ||
nāyudhavyasanaprāptaṃ nārtaṃ nātiparikṣatam |
na bhītaṃ na parāvṛttaṃ satāṃ dharmam anusmaran || 7.93 ||
nāyudhavyasanaprāptam iti | bhagnāyudhaṃ hataputrādikaṃ prahāropetaṃ bhītaṃ saṃgrāmapratinivṛttaṃ śiṣṭācāraṃ smaran na hanyāt || 7.93 ||
yas tu bhītaḥ parāvṛttaḥ saṃgrāme hanyate paraiḥ |
bhartur yad duṣkrtaṃ kiṃcit tat sarvaṃ pratipadyate || 7.94 ||
yas tv iti | yo bhītaḥ parāṅmukhībhūtaḥ saṃgrāme śatrubhir hanyate ‘pi yadi tathāpi prabhor yat kiṃcit pāpaṃ tat sarvaṃ prāpnoti | pāpaṃ tasya bhavati hy etāvad atra vivakṣitam | anyaṃ vā kathayati apāpe prabhau pāpābhāvaprasaṅgena ca prabhor eva tat prāyaścittaṃ tenāsau duṣkṛtāt pramucyate | bhartṛgrahaṇād amātyasyāpi parāvartanādidoṣa eva, na prakṛtasyaiva rājñaḥ || 7.94 ||
yac cāsya sukṛtaṃ kiṃcid amutrārtham upārjitam |
bhartā tat sarvam ādatte parāvṛttahatasya tu || 7.95 ||
yad iti | asya ca parāṅmukhahatasya yat kiṃcit paralokārthaṃ sukṛtam arjitam asti tat sarvaṃ prabhor bhavati || 7.95 ||
rathāśvaṃ hastinaṃ chatraṃ dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ paśūn striyaḥ |
sarvadravyāṇi kupyaṃ ca yo yaj jayati tasya tat || 7.96 ||
rathāśvam iti | rathāśvahastidhvajahiraṇyadhānyapaśudāsyātmakaṃ sarvāṇi guḍalavaṇādīni kupyaṃ tāmralohādi yo yat parān avajitya harati tasyaiva tat bhavati na rājñaḥ || 7.96 ||
rājñaś ca dadyur uddhāram ity eṣā vaidikī śrutiḥ |
rājñā ca sarvayodhebhyo dātavyam apṛthagjitam || 7.97 ||
rājñe ceti | yady api yo yaj jayati tasya tad yuktam | tathā ca yad yat tatra śreyas tat tat rājñe bhṛtyā dadyuḥ | “māhendram uddhāram uddhāram uddharan vṛtraṃ hatvā” ity evamādikātra vaidikī śrutiḥ | yac cāvibhāgena jitaṃ tad rājñā sarvayodhebhyo dātavyam || 7.97 ||
eṣo ‘nupaskṛtaḥ prokto yodhadharmaḥ sanātanaḥ |
asmād dharmān na cyaveta kṣatriyo ghnan raṇe ripūn || 7.98 ||
eṣa iti | eṣo ‘vigarhito ‘nādipāramparyāyāto yodhadharma uktaḥ | tataḥ saṃgrāme śatrūn hiṃsan kṣatriya etaṃ dharmaṃ na parityajet || 7.98 ||
alabdhaṃ caiva lipseta labdhaṃ rakṣet prayatnataḥ |
rakṣitaṃ vardhayec caiva vṛddhaṃ pātreṣu nikṣipet || 7.99 ||
alabdham iti | ajitaṃ bhūhiraṇyasuvarṇādi jetuṃ yatnaṃ kuryāt | jitaṃ ca yatnato rakṣet | rakṣitaṃ ca vṛddhiṃ nayet | evam asya mahādhanatvāt svaparaparibhavo na bhavati | vardhitaṃ ca pātrebhyo dadyāt | tathā sati paralokābhyudayo ‘py asya bhavati || 7.99 ||
etac caturvidhaṃ vidyāt puruṣārthaprayojanam |
asya nityam anuṣṭhānaṃ samyak kuryād atandritaḥ || 7.100 ||
etad iti | etadarjanarakṣaṇavardhanadānātmakaṃ catuḥprakāraṃ puruṣakārasya prayojanaṃ jānīyāt | ato ‘syānalasaḥ sarvadānuṣṭhāne yatnaṃ kuryat || 7.100 ||
alabdham icched daṇḍena labdhaṃ rakṣed avekṣayā |
rakṣitaṃ vardhayed vṛddhyā vṛddhaṃ pātreṣu nikṣipet || 7.101 ||
alabdham iti | anarjitaṃ hastyaśvarathapadātyātmakena daṇḍena jetum icchet | jitaṃ ca śāstradṛṣṭena pratyavekṣaṇena rakṣet | rakṣitaṃ ca vṛddhyarthair upāyaiḥ sthalapadādibhir vardhayet | vardhitaṃ ca yad adhikaṃ tat paralokārthaṃ pātrebhyo dadyāt || 7.101 ||
nityam utdyatadaṇḍaḥ syān nityaṃ vivṛtapauruṣaḥ |
nityaṃ saṃvṛtasaṃvāryo nityaṃ chidrānusāry areḥ || 7.102 ||
nityam iti | hastyaśvarathapadātyātmako daṇḍo nityam udyataḥ kṛtayuddhādiśiṣyābhyāso yasya sa tathā syāt | nityaṃ ca vivṛtaṃ prakāśīkṛtaṃ sīmāṭavyādideśodyatāyudhaṃ sannaddhapuruṣamāraṇena pauruṣaṃ parākramo yasya sa tathā syāt | nityaṃ saṃvṛtaṃ guptaṃ saṃvāryaṃ gopanīyamantrakṛtyotthānāni yasya sa tathā syāt | sarvadā ca śatror vyasanāpekṣaṇakṛtyadūṣaṇādinā chidrānveṣaṇaparaḥ syāt || 7.102 ||
nityam udyatadaṇḍasya kṛtsnam udvijate jagat |
tasmāt sarvāṇi bhūtāni daṇḍenaiva prasādhayet || 7.103 ||
nityam udyatadaṇḍasyeti | yasmān nityodyatadaṇḍāt sarvaṃ jagad udvijate tasmāt sarvāṇi bhūtāni daṇḍenaiva vaśīkuryāt || 7.103 ||
amāyayaiva varteta na kathaṃcana māyayā |
budhyetāriprayuktāṃ ca māyāṃ nityaṃ susaṃvṛtaḥ || 7.104 ||
budhyetāriprayuktāṃ ca nityaṃ māyāṃ susaṃvṛtaḥ | yāvac chatrūṇāṃ prakṛtyupajāpādichadmanā kṛtaṃ tāvac chadmanaiva vyavahārān na tu kadācit pūrvaṃ chadma samācaret | suvyaktapakṣarakṣaś ca bhūtvā śatrukṛtaṃ prakṛtibhedādichadmaṃ sarvadā cārādibhir avekṣeta || 7.104 ||
nāsya chidraṃ paro vidyād vidyāc chidraṃ parasya ca |
gūhet kūrma ivāṅgāni rakṣed vivaram ātmanaḥ || 7.105 ||
nāsyeti | tathā yateta yathāsya chidraṃ prakṛtibhedādi śatrur na jānīte, śatroḥ punar asau prakṛtibhedādi yatnato jānīyāt | tathā kūrma ivāvayavānupraveśena prakṛtyādīni rājyāṅgāni saṃgrāmādinā rakṣet | kathaṃcic ca prakṛtibhedādi yad ātmanaḥ chidram upajātaṃ tatra yatnataḥ samādhānaṃ kuryāt || 7.105 ||
bakavac cintayed arthān siṃhavac ca parākramet |
vṛkavac cāvalumpeta śaśavac ca viniṣpatet || 7.106 ||
bakavac cintayed arthān śaśavac ca viniṣpatet | vṛkavac cāvalumpeta siṃhavac ca parākramet | yathā bako durgāśrayam aticaran svabhāvam api matsyamāṃsagrahaṇād anirviṇṇaikāgrāntaḥkaraṇatayā cintayaty evam atigupte ca ripau tadrāṣṭrasaṃbandhādīn arthān cintayet | yathā śabaragocaratām upagato ‘pi śaśo ‘ticalakuṭilagatitayā deśāntaram upasaraty evaṃ sakalasāmantaprakopāticalaripumadhyavartī vā kathaṃcid arisaṃmoham ādhāya tataḥ pradeśād guṇavadrājāntarasaṃśrayārthaṃ upasarpet | yathā ca vṛkaḥ kṛtarakṣāṇ api paśūṃs tadgrahaṇābhiyogena kathaṃcid anavadhāne pāne (prāpte?) hinasty evaṃ durgādyavasthitam api paraṃ kathaṃcid api, tatpramādam āsādyāvalumpeta | yathā ca girivaraśikharākāram api kariṇam utsāhaśaktiyogāt siṃho nihantum ākrāmaty evam alpabalo ‘pi balavattarābhibhūtasaṃśrayādyupāyāntarābhāve sakalabalāvaṣṭambhena parihantum ākramet || 7.106 ||
evaṃ vijayamānasya ye ‘sya syuḥ paripanthinaḥ |
tān ānayed vaśaṃ sarvān sāmādibhir upakramaiḥ || 7.107 ||
evam iti | evam uktarītyā jetuṃ pravṛttasya rājño ye vijayapratikūlāḥ syuḥ tān sarvān sāmabhedadānadaṇḍair upāyair vaśīkuryāt || 7.107 ||
yadi te tu na tiṣṭheyur upāyaiḥ parthamaiḥ tribhiḥ |
daṇḍenaiva prasahyaitāṃś chanakair vaśam ānayet || 7.108 ||
yadīti | te ca paripanthino yady ādyaiḥ sāmadānabhedair upāyaiḥ vaśaṃ nāyānti tadā daṇḍenaiva deśopamaryādādinā haṭhād eva śanakair yādṛśo daṇḍakramas tena vaśaṃ nayet || 7.108 ||
sāmādīnām upāyānāṃ caturṇām api paṇḍitāḥ |
sāmadaṇḍau praśaṃsanti nityaṃ rāṣṭrābhivṛddhaye || 7.109 ||
sāmādīnām iti | sāmādīnāṃ caturṇām apy upāyānāṃ madhyāt sāmadaṇḍau rāṣṭravivṛddhyarthaṃ praśastau nityaṃ vidvāṃso manyante | sāmni kṣayavyayāsānām abhāvād daṇḍe ca yuddhādyanaryotpāde ca siddhyatiśayāt || 7.109 ||
yathoddharati nirdātā kakṣaṃ dhānyam ca rakṣati |
tathā rakṣen nṛpo rāṣṭraṃ hanyāc ca paripanthinaḥ || 7.110 ||
yathoddharatīti | yathā kṣetrasya kakṣasyotpāṭayitā kakṣaṃ nāśayati dhānyaṃ ca rakṣati, evaṃ nṛpo ‘nukūlān rāṣṭriyān rakṣet pratikūlāṃś ca hanyāt || 7.110 ||
mohād rājā svarāṣṭraṃ yaḥ karśayaty anavekṣayā |
so ‘cirād bhraśyate rājyāj jīvitāc ca sabāndhavaḥ || 7.111 ||
mohād iti | yo rājā hitāhitāvivekena prajāḥ pīḍayati sa kṣipraṃ prakṛtilopādinādharmabhūyastvena rājyāj jīvitāc ca bandhusahito naśyaty acirāt || 7.111 ||
śarīrakarṣaṇāt prāṇāḥ kṣīyante prāṇināṃ yathā |
tathā rājñām api prāṇāḥ kṣīyante rāṣṭrakarṣaṇāt || 7.112 ||
śarīrakarṣaṇād iti | yathā prāṇabhṛtāṃ dīrghādhvagamanādinā prāṇāḥ kṣīyante eva tathā rājñām api rāṣṭropapīḍanāt prakṛtikope sati prāṇāḥ vināśam upayānti || 7.112 ||
rāṣṭrasya saṃgrahe nityaṃ vidhānam idam ācaret |
susaṃgṛhītarāṣṭro hi pārthivaḥ sukham edhate || 7.113 ||
evaṃ caitad doṣaparijihīrṣayāha rāṣṭrasyeti | rāṣṭrasaṃrakṣaṇe sarvadā vakṣyamāṇam upāyaṃ kuryāt | yasmāt susaṃrakṣitarāṣṭro rājā yatnena vardhate || 7.113 ||
dvayos trayāṇāṃ pañcānāṃ madhye gulmam adhiṣṭhitam |
tathā grāmaśatānāṃ ca kuryād rāṣṭrasya saṃgraham || 7.114 ||
dvayor iti | dvayor grāmayor madhe trayāṇāṃ pañcānāṃ bahūnāṃ vā grāmaśatānāṃ deśakālādyapekṣayā ātta(āpta)puruṣādhiṣṭhitaṃ rāṣṭrasya saṃgrahaṃ rakṣābhūtaṃ sthānakaṃ kuryāt grāmadoṣādyavabodhārthaṃ karādigrahaṇārthaṃ ca || 7.114 ||
grāmasyādhipatiṃ kuryād daśagrāmapatiṃ tathā |
viṃśatīśaṃ śateśaṃ ca sahasrapatim eva ca || 7.115 ||
grāmasyeti | grāmadaśagrāmādhyakṣaṃ kuryāt || 7.115 ||
grāmadoṣān samutpannān grāmikaḥ śanakaiḥ svayam |
śaṃsed grāmadaśeśāya daśeśo viṃśatīśine || 7.116 ||
viṃśatīśas tu tat sarvaṃ śateśāya nivedayet |
śaṃsed grāmaśateśas tu sahasrapataye svayam || 7.117 ||
grāmadośān iti | viṃśatīśa iti | grāme cauryādidoṣān utpannān grāmādhipatir ājñāṃ pratisaṃdhānāśakto ‘nākulatayā svayaṃ daśagrāmādhipataye kathayet | daśādhipatir viṃśatisvāmine, viṃśatīśaḥ śateśāya, śateśo sahasreśāya || 7.116–117 ||
yāni rājapradeyāni pratyahaṃ grāmavāsibhiḥ |
annapānendhanādīni grāmikas tāny avāpnuyāt || 7.118 ||
