yānīti | yāni grāmavāsibhir annapānādīni sarvāṇi svāmine dātavyāni tāni grāmādhipatir gṛhṇīyāt || 7.118 ||
daśī kulaṃ tu bhuñjīta viṃśī pañcakulāni ca |
grāmaṃ grāmaśatādhyakṣaḥ sahasrādhipatiḥ puram || 7.119 ||
daśīti |
aṣṭāgavaṃ dharmahalaṃ ṣaḍgavaṃ jīvikārthinām |
caturgavaṃ gṛhasthānāṃ dvigavaṃ brahmaghātinām ||
iti hārītaḥ | ṣaḍgavaṃ madhyamaṃ halam, taddviguṇaṃ kulaṃ manyante | tena madhyamena haladvayena yāvatī bhūr vāhyate tatphalaṃ daśādhipatiḥ bhṛtyarthaṃ bhuñjīta | evaṃ viṃśatyadhipatiḥ pañcakulāni śatādhipatir madhyamaṃ grāmam, sahasrādhipatir madhyamaṃ puram || 7.119 ||
teṣāṃ grāmyāṇi kāryāṇi pṛthakkāryāṇi caiva hi |
rājño ‘nyaḥ sacivaḥ snigdhas tāni paśyed atandritaḥ || 7.120 ||
teṣām iti | evaṃ grāmādhipatiprabhṛtīnāṃ yāni grāmanivāsakāryāṇi, tadaśkyapratisaṃdhānāni yāni ca teṣāṃ saṃbandhīni kṛtākṛtāni vānyāny api rājñā niyukto ‘nalaso bhūtvāvekṣeta || 7.120 ||
nagare nagare caikaṃ kuryāt sarvārthacintakam |
uccaiḥsthānaṃ ghorarūpaṃ nakṣatrāṇām iva graham || 7.121 ||
nagara iti | pratinagaram ekaikam uccaiḥsthānaṃ mahākulīnaṃ ghorarūpaṃ chātrālaṅkārahastyaśvādibalasāmagryā bhayānakākāraṃ madhye graham iva bhāskarākāraṃ nagarādhipatiṃ kuryāt || 7.121 ||
sa tān anuparikrāmet sarvān eva sadā svayam |
teṣāṃ vṛttaṃ pariṇayet samyag rāṣṭreṣu taccaraiḥ || 7.122 ||
sa iti | sa nāgarika ekagrāmādhipatyādīn balapūraṇāya sati prayojane sarvadā svayaṃ paścāt yātāt | teṣāṃ nāgarikaparyantānāṃ rāṣṭreṣu yadācaritaṃ tadviṣayaniyuktaiḥ cārair yathā tad rājā āgamayet || 7.122 ||
rājño hi rakṣādhikṛtāḥ parasvādāyinaḥ śaṭhāḥ |
bhṛtyā bhavanti prāyeṇa tebhyo rakṣed imāḥ prajāḥ || 7.123 ||
rājña iti | yasmād rājño bhṛtyā rakṣādhikṛtāṃ bhṛtyās te bāhulyena parasvagrahaṇaśīlā vañcakāś ca bhavanti, tasmāt tebhya ātmīyāḥ prajāḥ rājā rakṣet || 7.123 ||
ye kāryikebhyo ‘rtham eva gṛhṇīyuḥ pāpacetasaḥ |
teṣāṃ sarvasvam ādāya rājā kuryāt pravāsanam || 7.124 ||
ya iti | ye rājādhikṛtāḥ kāryikebhyaḥ parasvādāyirūpeṇa pāpabuddhayo ‘rthaṃ gṛhṇīyuḥ teṣāṃ sarvasvam apahṛtya deśān nirvāsanaṃ rājā kuryāt || 7.124 ||
rājakarmasu yuktānāṃ strīṇāṃ preṣyajanasya ca |
pratyahaṃ kalpayed vṛttiṃ sthānakarmānurūpataḥ || 7.125 ||
rājakarmasv iti | rājopayogikarmasu yuktānāṃ dāsyādistrīṇāṃ dāsavargasya cotkṛṣṭahīnasthānānurūpeṇaiva karmānurūpeṇa pratyahaṃ vṛttiṃ dadyāt || 7.125 ||
paṇo deyo ‘vakṛṣṭasya ṣaḍ utkṛṣṭasya vetanam |
ṣāṇmāsikas tathācchādo dhānyadroṇas tu māsikaḥ || 7.126 ||
tām eva darśayati paṇo deyo ‘vakṛṣṭasya ṣad utkṛṣṭasya bhaktakam iti | paṇo vakṣyamāṇaḥ, sa jaghanyabhṛtakasya bhaktārthaṃ pratyahaṃ dātavyaḥ | ṣāṇmasikaś cācchādyo vastrayugalaṃ māsi dhānyadroṇo dvādaśottarāṇi pañcapalaśatāni deyaḥ | utkṛṣṭasya tu ṣaṭpaṇā bhaktārthaṃ deyāḥ | anayaiva kalpanayā ṣāṇmāsikaṃ vastrayugaṣaṭkaṃ deyam | anayaiva ca diśā madhyamasya paṇatrayaṃ ṣāṇmāsikaṃ vastrayugatrayam, māsikaṃ ca dhānyadroṇatrayaṃ deyam iti || 7.126 ||
krayavikrayam adhvānaṃ bhaktaṃ ca saparivyayam |
yogakṣemaṃ ca saṃprekṣya vaṇijo dāpayet karān || 7.127 ||
krayavikrayam iti | kiyatā krītam, kiyatā ca vikrīyate, kiyaddūrād ānītam, kim asya vaṇijo deśāntrarād āgacchato bhaktasūpādyarthaṃ gatam, kiṃ cāsyāraṇyādau caurādiyogakṣemārthaṃ gatam ity etad apekṣya vaṇijaḥ karān dāpayet || 7.127 ||
yathā phalena yujyeta rājā kartā ca karmaṇām |
tathāvekṣya nṛpo rāṣṭre kalpayet satataṃ karān || 7.128 ||
yatheti | yathā rājño vaṇikkarṣaṇādeś ca karmakartuḥ phalasaṃbandho bhavati tathā paryālocya rājā sarvadā rāṣṭre karān kalpayet || 7.128 ||
yathālpālpam adanty ādyaṃ vāryokovatsaṣaṭpadāḥ |
tathālpālpo grahītavyo rāṣṭrād rājñābdikaḥ karaḥ || 7.129 ||
yathālpālpam iti | atra dṛṣṭāntam āha – yathā jalaukovatsabhramarāḥ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ stokaṃ stokam adanīyaṃ asṛkkṣīramakarandam adanty evaṃ mūlam acchindatā rājñā sāṃvatsariko karo ‘lpo ‘lpo grahītavyaḥ || 7.129 ||
pañcāśadbhāga ādeyo rājñā paśuhiraṇyayoḥ |
dhānyānām aṣṭamo bhāgaḥ ṣaṣṭho dvādaś eva vā || 7.130 ||
pañcāśadbhāga iti | mūlādhikayoḥ paśuhiraṇyayoḥ pañcāśattamo bhāgo rājñā grāhyaḥ | evaṃ dhānyānāṃ ṣaṣṭho ‘ṣṭamo dvādaśo vā karṣakakleśāpekṣayā grāhyaḥ || 7.130 ||
ādadītātha ṣaḍbhāgaṃ drumāṃsamadhusarpiṣām |
gandhauṣadhirasānāṃ ca puṣpamūlaphalasya ca || 7.131 ||
patraśākatṛṇānāṃ ca carmaṇāṃ vaidalasya ca |
mṛnmayānāṃ ca bhāṇḍānāṃ sarvasyāśmamayasy aca || 7.132 ||
ādadīteti | tṛṇānāṃ caiva ṣaḍbhāgaṃ carmaṇāṃ vaiṇavasya ca | iti | vṛkṣamāṃsamākṣikaghṛtakarpūrādigandhānāṃ guḍūcyādyauṣadhīnāṃ lavaṇādirasānāṃ puṣpamūlaphalapatraśākatṛṇacarmaveṇumayamṛnmayāśmamayānāṃ ṣaḍbhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyāt || 7.131–132 ||
mriyamāṇo ‘py ādadīta na rājā śrotriyāt karam |
na ca kṣudhāsya saṃsīdec chrotriyo viṣaye vasan || 7.133 ||
mriyamāṇa iti | na ca gacched viṣādaṃ ca śrotriyo viṣaye vasan | atyantakṣīṇakośo ‘pi rājā vedādhyāyino brāhmaṇān karaṃ na gṛhṇīyāt | na ca tadīye deśe vasan śrotriyo viṣādaṃ gacchet
|| 7.133 ||
yasya rājñas tu viṣaye śrotriyaḥ sīdati kṣudhā |
tasyāpi tatkṣudhā rāṣṭram acireṇaiva sīdati || 7.134 ||
yasmāt yaasyeti | yasya rājño deśe śrotriyaḥ kṣudhāvasanno bhavati, tasya rāṣṭram api acirāt kṣudhāvasādam eti || 7.134 ||
śrutavṛtte viditvāsya vṛttiṃ dharmyāṃ prakalpayet |
saṃrakṣet sarvataś cainaṃ pitā putram ivaurasam || 7.135 ||
yata evam ataḥ śrutavṛtte iti | bhayebhyaś ca tathā rakṣet pitā putram ivaurasam | jñānānuṣṭhāne ‘sya jñātvā tadanurūpāṃ dharmād anapetāṃ sa jīvikāṃ kalpayet, caurapāradārikādibhyo bhayebhyaś ca rakṣaṇīyas tathā yathā pitā putraṃ rakṣati || 7.135 ||
saṃrakṣyamāṇo rājñā yaṃ kurute dharmam anvaham |
tenāyur vardhate rājño draviṇaṃ rāṣṭram eva ca || 7.136 ||
saṃrakṣyamāṇa iti | sa ca brāhmaṇo rājñā rakṣyamāṇo dharmam anvahaṃ kurute, tena rājña āyur vardhate, rāṣṭrāṇi vardhante || 7.137 ||
yat kiṃcid api varṣasya dāpayet karasaṃjñitam |
vyavahāreṇa jīvantaṃ rājā rāṣṭre pṛthagjanam || 7.137 ||
yat kiṃcid iti | tadrāṣṭre krayavikrayādijīvitam āpūpikādikaṃ nikṛṣṭajanaṃ yat kiṃcit svalpam api karākhyaṃ varṣe rājā dāpayet || 7.137 ||
kārukān śilpinaś caiva śūdrāṃś cātmopajīvinaḥ |
ekaikaṃ kārayet karma māsi māsi mahīpatiḥ || 7.138 ||
kārukān iti | kārūn sūpakārādīn śilpinaś carmakārādīn śūdrāṃś ca kāyakleśajīvino bhāriprabhṛtīn māsi māsi ekaikam ahorātraṃ karma kārayet || 7.138 ||
nocchindyād ātmano mūlaṃ pareṣāṃ cātitṛṣṇayā |
ucchindan hy ātmano mūlam ātmānaṃ tāṃś ca pīḍayet || 7.139 ||
nocchindyād iti | ucchindann ātmano mūlam iti | rājā atisnehena karādyagrahaṇe sati ātmanaḥ kośakṣayād rājyavināśākhyaṃ mūlacchedaṃ na kuryāt | tathātilobhavān āsāṃ prajānām api na ca mūlacchedaṃ kuryāt | tasmād ātmamūlacchedena rājā karākramaṇe sati ātmānaṃ prajāś ca pīḍayet || 7.139 ||
tīkṣṇaś caiva mṛduś ca syāt kāryaṃ vīkṣya mahīpatiḥ |
tīkṣṇaś caiva mṛduś caiva rājā bhavati saṃmataḥ || 7.140 ||
na tīkṣṇo na mṛduś ca syāt kāryaṃ vīkṣya mahīpatir iti | kāryavaśāt tīkṣṇo mṛdur vā rājā bhavet | naikāntataḥ kāryataḥ tīkṣṇamārdavāvalambī rājā prajānām abhimato bhavati || 7.140 ||
amātyamukhyaṃ dharmajñaṃ prājñaṃ dāntaṃ kulodgatam |
sthāpayed āsane tasmin khinnaḥ kāryekṣaṇe nṛṇām || 7.141 ||
amātyamukhyam iti | bhṛtyaviṣaye kāryadarśane rājā khinnaḥ san pradhānāmātyaṃ dharmajñaṃ prājñaṃ yatendriyaṃ kulīnaṃ tasmin kāryadarśanasthāne viniyuñjīta || 7.141 ||
evaṃ sarvaṃ vidhāyedam itikartavyam ātmanaḥ |
yuktaś caivāpramattaś ca parirakṣed imāḥ prajāḥ || 7.142 ||
evam iti | evam uktarītyā sarvam ātmanaḥ kāryajātaṃ saṃpādya yatnavān buddhyāskhalann ātmīyāḥ prajāḥ parirakṣet || 7.143 ||
vikrośantyo yasya rāṣṭrād hriyante dasyubhiḥ prajāḥ |
saṃpaśyataḥ sabhṛtyasya mṛtaḥ sa na tu jīvati || 7.143 ||
vikrośanya iti | yasya rājñaḥ sabhṛtyasya paśyata eva rāṣṭrād ākrandantyaḥ prajāḥ śatruprabhṛtibhir apahriyante svajīvitakāryābhāvāt mṛta eva, na tu jīvati || 7.143 ||
kṣatriyasya paro dharmaḥ prajānām eva pālanam |
nirdiṣṭaphalabhoktā hi rājā dharmeṇa yujyate || 7.144 ||
kṣatriyasyet | kṣatriyasya dharmāntarebhyaḥ prajāpālanam eva prakṛṣṭo dharmo yasmād rakṣaṇam iti phalabhoktā rājā dharme(ṇa) saṃbadhyate || 7.144 ||
utthāya paścime yāme kṛtaśaucaḥ samāhitaḥ |
hutāgnir brāhmaṇāṃś cārcya praviśet sa śubhāṃ sabhām || 7.145 ||
utthāyeti | muhūrtamātrāvaśiṣṭāyāṃ rātrāv utthāya kṛtāvaśyakaśaucaḥ saṃyatamanāḥ kṛtāgnihotro brāhmaṇān pūjayitvā vāstulakṣaṇopetāṃ sabhām amātyādidharśanaveśma praviśet || 7.145 ||
tatra sthitaḥ prajāḥ sarvāḥ pratinandya visarjayet |
visṛjya ca prajāḥ sarvā mantrayet saha mantribhiḥ || 7.146 ||
tatreti | tasyāṃ sabhāyāṃ sthito darśanārthaṃ sabhāyātāḥ sarvāḥ prajāḥ vākcakṣurādinā paritoṣya visarjayet | tāḥ visarjya mantribhiḥ saha saṃdhivigrahādīn cintayet || 7.146 ||
giripṛṣṭhaṃ samāruhya prāsādaṃ vā rahogataḥ |
araṇye niḥśalāke vā mantrayed avibhāvitaḥ || 7.147 ||
giripṛṣṭham iti | parvatapṛṣṭham āruhya dhavalagṛhaṃ vā viviktadeśastho ‘raṇye vā tṛṇādirahite mantrabhedakabhayādivarjite pañcāṅgamantrakarmaṇām ārambhopāyaḥ – puruṣadravyasaṃpat, deśakālavibhāgo, vinipātapratīkāraḥ, kāryasiddhiś cety evaṃ cintayet || 7.147 ||
yasya mantraṃ na jānanti samāgamya pṛthagjanāḥ |
sa kṛtsnāṃ pṛthivīṃ bhuṅkte kośahīno ‘pi pārthivaḥ || 7.148 ||
yasyeti | yasya rājño mantrivyatiriktāḥ janāḥ saṃmīlyābhyūhya mantraṃ na jānanti sa kṣīṇakośo ‘pi samagrāṃ pṛthivīṃ bhunakti || 7.148 ||
jaḍamūkāndhabadhirāṃs tairyagyonān vayotigān |
strīmlecchavyādhitavyaṅgān mantrakāle ‘pasārayet || 7.149 ||
jaḍamūkāndhabadhirān iti | buddhivākcakṣuḥśrotravikalān tathā gomṛgaśukān tathā vṛddhastrīmlecchāturān aṅgahīnān ca mantrakāle apasārayet || 7.149 ||
bhindanty avamatā mantraṃ tairyagyonās tathaiva ca |
striyaś caiva viśeṣeṇa tasmāt tatrādṛto bhavet || 7.150 ||
bhindantīti | jaḍatvādidoṣayogena sāvamānā jaḍādayo mantrabhedaṃ kurvanti | tathā kṣitiparivartādiśravaṇā tiryagyonā api mantraṃ bhindanti | viśeṣataś ca striyo ‘pi cāpalātiśayātvāt tasmāt tadapāsane yatnavān syāt || 7.150 ||
madhyaṃdine ‘rdharātre vā viśrānto vigataklamaḥ |
cintayed dharmakāmārthān sārdhaṃ tair eka eva vā || 7.151 ||
madhyaṃdina iti | vigatacittakhedo ‘pagataśarīraklamaś ca madhyāhne ‘rdharātre vā mantribhiḥ sahaiko vā dharmakāmārthān “kathaṃ mayā dharmaḥ kāmo vārtho vā saṃsevyaḥ” iti cintayet || 7.151 ||
parasparaviruddhānāṃ teṣāṃ ca samupārjanam |
kanyānāṃ saṃpradānaṃ ca kumārāṇāṃ ca rakṣaṇam || 7.152 ||
parasparaviruddhānām iti | teṣāṃ ca dharmakāmārthānāṃ parasparavirodhāvirbhāve sati avirodhārjanopāyaṃ cintayet | duhitṝṇāṃ dānaṃ svakāryasaṃsiddhiphalaṃ vicārayet | kumārāṇāṃ ca putrāṇām avinayanivartanaṃ nīcopasaṃgrahaphalaṃ cintayet || 7.152 ||
dūtasaṃpreṣaṇaṃ caiva kāryaśeṣaṃ tathaiva ca |
antaḥpurapracāraṃ ca praṇidhīnāṃ ca ceṣṭitam || 7.153 ||
dūtasaṃpreṣaṇam iti | dūtānāṃ ca nisṛṣṭārthaparimitārthalekhahāritvādinā pararāṣṭrasaṃpreṣaṇaṃ cintayet | tathārabdhakāryāvaśeṣasaṃpādanāya cintayet | strīṇāṃ cāticapalaviṣamācārādhiṣṭhitatvād ātmanā ātmasaṃrakṣaṇārtham avyabhicārāya cāntaḥpuraceṣṭitaṃ cintayet | carāṇāṃ carāntaraiś ceṣṭitatam āgamayet || 7.153 ||
kṛtsnaṃ cāṣṭavidhaṃ karma pañcavargaṃ ca tattvataḥ |
anurāgāparāgau ca pracāraṃ maṇḍalasya ca || 7.154 ||
kṛtsnam iti | aṣṭavidhaṃ karma uśanasoktam |
ādāne ca visarge ca tathā praiṣaniṣedhayoḥ |
pañcame cārthavacane vyavahārasya cekṣaṇe ||
daṇḍaśuddhau sadā yuktas tenāṣṭagatiko nṛpaḥ |
aṣṭakarmā divaṃ yāti rājā śakrābhipūjitaḥ ||
tatrādānaṃ karādīnāṃ bhṛtyebhyo dhanavisargaḥ | praiṣo yo duṣṭatyāgo ‘dhikṛtānāṃ kāmacāraniṣedho varṇāśramiṇāṃ svakarmasaṃdehe ‘rthavadanam, prajānāṃ dhanādivipratipattau vyavahārekṣaṇam, parājitānāṃ daṇḍanaṃ pramādaskhalite prāyaścittaṃ śuddhiḥ ity etad aṣṭavidhaṃ karma cintayet | tathā kārpaṭikodāsthitagṛhapativaidehikatāpasa-vyañjanātmakaṃ pañcaparimāṇaṃ cāravargaṃ yathāvac cintayet | tatra ca paramarmavidṛṣṭachātraḥ kārpaṭikaḥ vyañjanas taṃ vṛttyarthaṃ matvā arthamānābhyāṃ svīkṛtya anyat pūrvavat, rahasi rājā brūyāt taṃ ca paśyasi tat tadānīm evam api vaktavyam iti pravrajyārūḍhapatitaudāsthavyañjanas taṃ prajāśaucayuktaṃ vṛttyarthinaṃ jñātvā rahasi rājā taṃ brūyāt | tvaṃ ca sarvānnotpādanayogyāyāṃ bhuñjyantevāsikarma kuru tataś ca bhūmer atmano ‘nyeṣāṃ ca tatprayuktānāṃ pravrajitānāṃ asmadarthavyavahāriṇāṃ yatheṣṭam āhārcchādanādi dehīti brūyāt karṣakakṣīṇavṛttiḥ prajñāśaucopeto gṛhapativyañjanas taṃ pūrvavad uktvā yathoktāyāṃ bhūmau kṛṣiṃ kārayet | śiṣṭaṃ pūrvavat | muṇḍo jaṭilo vā vṛttikāmas tāpasavyañjanaḥ pūrvavad uktvā uktapūrvavad bhūmau vaṇikkarma kārayet | muṇḍo jaṭilo vā vṛttikāmas tāpasavyañjanaḥ pūrvavad rājājñayā nagarasamīpe atibahumuṇḍajaṭilāntara-kapaṭaśiṣṭapaṇopetas tāpasyaṃ kuryāt | māsadvimāsāntaritaṃ prakāśam igudabadarādimuṣṭim aśnīyāt | rahasi tu yatheṣṭam āhāraṃ rājārpitaṃ bhuñjīta | loke cāsyātītānāgatajñaṃ tadantevāsinaḥ khyāpayeyuḥ | tathāyaṃ lokānāṃ lābhapūjācauropadravādi vyādiśet | tad rājyaprayuktāḥ chadmacāriṇaḥ satyābhidhāyitvajananārthaṃ saṃpādayeyuḥ | evaṃ sakalajanopasañjātāsvā(śvā)samayā sarva eva kāryākāryaṃ saṃsiddhivijijñāsavas tam āgatya pṛcchanti | vārtāntarāṇy ārirādhayiṣayā vartayantīty evaṃ pañcavargaṃ tattvataś cintayet | evaṃ pañcavargaṃ prakalpya tatas tenaiva vargadvāreṇa pararājasyātmīyāṃs tāṃś ca mantryādīnām anurāgāparāgau jñātvā tad anucintayet | vakṣyamāṇasya rājamaṇḍalasya pracāraṃ kaḥ sandhyarthīty evamādikaṃ buddhvā tadanuguṇaṃ cintayet || 7.154 ||
madhyamasya pracāraṃ ca vijigīṣoś ca ceṣṭitam |
udāsīnapracāraṃ ca śatroś caiva prayatnataḥ || 7.155 ||
madhyamasyeti | arivijigīṣayor bhūmyantaraṃ saṃhatahor anutsāhasamartho vigrahe vā saṃhatayoḥ samamadhyamas tasya pracāraṃ cintayet | tathā prajñotsāhaprakṛtiguṇasaṃpanno vijigīṣus tasya ceṣṭitaṃ cintayat kim asau cikīrṣati | tathārivijigīṣumadhyamāyāṃ yaḥ saṃhatānām anugrahasamartho nigraha(he) cāsaṃhatānāṃ sa udāsīnas tatpracāraṃ cintayet | śatroś ca saha kṛtrimabhūmyanantarasya trividhasyāpi pūrvebhyaḥ prayatnena pracāraṃ cintayet || 7.155 ||
etāḥ prakṛtayo mūlaṃ maṇḍalasya samāsataḥ |
aṣṭau cānyāḥ samākhyātā dvādaśaiva tu tāḥ smṛtāḥ || 7.156 ||
etā iti | etā madhyamādyās catasraḥ prakṛtayaḥ saṃkṣepeṇa maṇḍalasya mūlam anyāsāṃ prakṛtīnām ābhyaḥ prasūter etā mūlam ity ucyante | anyāś cāṣṭau samākhyātāḥ | tad yathā – agrato bhūtānāṃ mitram amitraṃ mitramitram arimitraṃ ceti | evam anyāś catasro bhavanti | paścāc ca pārṣṇigrāha ākranda pārṣṇigrāhāsāra ākrandāsāra iti catasra evam aṣṭau mūlaprakṛtibhiś catasṛbhiḥ saha dvādaśaprakṛtayaḥ smṛtāḥ || 7.156 ||
amātyarāṣṭradurgārthadaṇḍākhyāḥ pañca cāparāḥ |
pratyekaṃ kathitā hy etāḥ saṃkṣepeṇa dvisaptatiḥ || 7.157 ||
amātyarāṣṭradurārthadaṇḍākhyāḥ iti | āsāṃ catasṝṇāṃ mūlaprakṛtīnāṃ ca śākhāprakṛtīnām uktānām ekaikasyāḥ prakṛter amātyadeśadurgakośadaṇḍākhyāḥ pañca dravyaprakṛtayo bhavanti | evaṃ ca mūlaprakṛtayaś catasraḥ, śākhāprakṛtayo ‘ṣṭau, dravyaprakṛtayaḥ ṣaṣṭir ity evaṃ saṃkṣepato dvisaptatiḥ kathitāḥ || 7.157 ||
anantaram ariṃ vidyād arisevinam eva ca |
arer anantaraṃ mitram udāsīnaṃ tayoḥ param || 7.158 ||
anantaram ariṃ vidyād arisevinam eva ceti | vijigīṣoḥ samantan maṇḍalībhūtānāṃ bhūmyanantarān arīn jānīyāt | tathā tatsevinaṃ cārim eva vidyād areḥ punar antarān vijigīṣoḥ bhūmyekantarā mitraprakṛtiṃ vidyāt | tayoś cārimitrayoḥ parām udāsīnaprakṛtiṃ vidyāt | tāsām evāgre paścāt bhedenāripārṣṇgrādivyapadeśabhedaḥ || 7.158 ||
tān sarvān abhisaṃdadhyāt sāmādibhir upakramaiḥ |
vyastaiś caiva samastaiś ca pauruṣeṇa nayena ca || 7.159 ||
tān sarvān abhisaṃdhyāyād iti | tān sarvān prakṛtīn sāmadānabhedadaṇḍākhyair yathāsaṃbhavaṃ vyastaiḥ samastair vā pauruṣeṇa vā daṇḍenaiva kevalena nayena vā sāmnaiva vā kevalena vaśīkuryāt || 7.159 ||
saṃdhiṃ ca vigrahaṃ caiva yānam āsanam eva ca |
dvidhībhāvaṃ saṃśrayaṃ ca ṣaḍ guṇāṃś cintayet sadā || 7.160 ||
saṃdhim iti | dvaidhībhāvaṃ saṃśrayaṃ ca ṣāḍguṇyaṃ cintayet sadā | tatrobhayānugrahakṛtyaparībandhaḥ saṃdhiḥ, apakāro vigrahaḥ abhyudaye saty upari gamanaṃ yānam, upekaṇam āsanam | ekena saha saṃdhir anyena saha vigraho dvaidhībhāvaḥ | param ātmārpaṇaṃ saṃśrayam ity etān ṣaḍguṇān upakārakān sarvadā cintayet | ṣaḍguṇāśrayaṇe nātmano ‘bhyudayaḥ parasyāpacayo bhavati tam eva guṇam adhitiṣṭhet || 7.160 ||
āsanaṃ caiva yānaṃ ca saṃdhiṃ vigraham eva ca |
kāryaṃ vīkṣya prayuñjīta dvaidhaṃ saṃśrayam eva ca || 7.161 ||
āsanaṃ caiva yānaṃ ca saṃdhiṃ vigraham eva ceti | ātmābhyudayaparahānyādikaṃ kāryaṃ vīkṣya saṃdhāyāsanaṃ vigṛhya vā yānaṃ dvaidhaṃ vā saṃśrayaṃ vānutiṣṭheta, tatra tadātvāyatikālayoḥ (?) pareṇa saha sāmyāśaṅkāyāṃ saṃdhiṃ krtvāsīta | yadā tu tadāvasāmye ‘py ātmā prakṛtīnāṃ na vicalitatvaṃ paraprakṛtīnāṃ copajāpasahatvaṃ manyeta tadā vigraham āśrityāsīta | yadā punaḥ parasmād abhyutthito bhavati tathā kṛtātmarāṣṭrasaṃdhāno vigṛhya yāyād vigṛhya yānahetvabhāve pārṣṇigrāheṇa saha saṃdhāya yāyāt tathā kāryāpekṣayā ekena saha saṃdhim apareṇa saha vigraham ity evaṃ dvaidhaṃ kuryāt | yadā tu durbalaḥ san balavatākṣiptas tadā tadviśiṣṭabalam anyam āśrayet || 7.161 ||
saṃdhiṃ tu dvividhaṃ vidyād rājā vigraham eva ca |
ubhe yānāsane caive dvividhaḥ saṃśrayaḥ smṛtaḥ || 7.162 ||
saṃdhim iti | saṃdhivigrahādīn guṇān sarvān eva dviprakārān rājā jānīyāt ity uttaraṃ vipakṣārtham || 7.163 ||
samānayānakarmā ca viparītas tathaiva ca |
tadātvāyatisaṃyuktaḥ saṃdhir jñeyo dvilakṣaṇaḥ || 7.163 ||
samānayānakarmeti | sāṃpratikaphalayogenāgāmiphalāvaṣṭambhena rājāntareṇa yātrānyaṃ prati yānakarma kriyate sa samānayānakarmā saṃdhir yaḥ tv anayā hy aham anuyāsyāmīti sāmpratikaḥ | āgāmiphalāpekṣayaiva kriyate so ‘samānayānaṃ karmasaṃdhir ity evaṃ dviprakāraḥ saṃdhir vijñeyaḥ || 7.163 ||
svayaṃkṛtaś ca kāryārtham akāle kāla eva vā |
mitrasya caivāpakṛte dvividho vigrahaḥ smṛtaḥ || 7.164 ||
svayaṃkṛta iti | mitreṇa caivāpakrte dvividho vigrahaḥ smṛtaḥ | vakṣyamāṇamārgaśīrṣādikālād anyatra tasminn eva vā kāle kāryāpekṣayā svayaṃkṛta ity eko vigrahaḥ | yaś ca parasya śatruḥ tadvijigīṣor mitraṃ tenāpakāre kriyamāṇe vyasanini śatrau dvitīya ity evaṃ dvividho vigrahaḥ smṛtaḥ || 7.164 ||
ekākinaś cātyayike kārye prāpte yadṛcchayā |
saṃhatya ca mitreṇa dvividhaṃ yānam ucyate || 7.165 ||
ekākina iti | parasya vyasanādau kṣiprapratīkāre ‘kasmāt kārye prāpte samarthasyaikākino yānasamarthasya tu mitrasahitasyety evaṃ yānam ucyate dvividham || 7.165 ||
kṣīṇasya caiva karmaśo daivāt pūrvakṛtena vā |
mitrasya cānurodhena dvividhaṃ smṛtam āsanam || 7.166 ||
kṣīṇasyeti | prāgjanmārjitaṃ karmavaśena aihikena pūrvapramādena śanaiḥ śanaiḥ kṣīṇabhūmyādikasya sarvasamṛddhasyāpi vā mitrānurodhena tatkāryarakṣārtham ity evaṃ smṛtam dvividham āsanam || 7.166 ||
balasya svāminaś caiva sthitiḥ kāryārthasiddhaye |
dvividhaṃ kīrtyate dvaidhaṃ ṣāḍguṇyaguṇavedibhiḥ || 7.167 ||
balasyeti | kāryasiddhyarthaṃ balasya hastyādeḥ senāpatyādyadhiṣṭhitasyāvasthānam ekatra kāryam | rājñaś ca balaikadeśayuktasya durgāvasthānam ity evaṃ dviprakāraṃ saṃdhyādiguṇaṣaṭkopakārajñaiḥ dvaidhaṃ kīrtyate  | ekena saṃdhir anyena saha vigrahaḥ, pratyekarūpatayāvasthānam ity evaṃ dvaidhasyāpi punar dvaividhyam || 7.167 ||
arthssaṃpādanārthaṃ ca pīḍyamānasya śatrubhiḥ |
sādhuṣu vyapadeśārthaṃ dvividhaḥ saṃśrayaḥ smṛtaḥ || 7.168 ||
arthasaṃpādanārtham iti | śatrubhir abhibhūyamānasya abhayanivṛttyākhyaḥ prayojanasaṃpādanārtham asaty api vā tadānīm abhibhava āgāmyabhibhavāśaṅkayāmukasṃśrito ‘yam ity evaṃ vyapadeśārthaṃ sādhunṛpasaṃśrayanam ity evaṃ dvividhaḥ saṃśayaḥ smṛtaḥ || 7.168 ||
yadāvagacched āyatyām ādhikyaṃ dhruvam ātmanaḥ |
tadātve cālpikāṃ pīḍāṃ tadā saṃdhiṃ samāśrayet || 7.169 ||
yadāvagacched āyatyām ādhikaṃ dhruvam ātmana iti | yadāgāmini kāla ātmano niścitam ādhikaṃ manyeta, sāṃpratikaś cālpa upakṣayas tadāpakṣayam aṅgīkṛtyādhisaṃdhim evāśrayet || 7.169 ||
yadā prahṛṣṭā manyeta sarvās tu prakṛtīr bhṛśam |
atyucchritaṃ tathātmānaṃ tadā kurvīta vigraham || 7.170 ||
yadeti | yadāmātyādikāḥ sarvāḥ prakṛtīḥ dānamānādinātyantaṃ parituṣṭāḥ manyeta, ātmānaṃ ca prabhūtamantrotsāhaśakatyadinocchritaṃ tadā vigraham adhyavasyet || 7.170 ||
yadā manyeta bhāvena hṛṣṭaṃ puṣṭaṃ balaṃ svakam |
parasya viparītaṃ ca tadā yāyād ripuṃ prati || 7.171 ||
yadāmanyeteti | yadā svakam amātyādisainyaṃ parituṣṭaṃ dhanādinā puṣṭaṃ paramarthena jānīyāt, śatroś ca viparītaṃ tadā tasyopari yāyāt || 7.171 ||
yadā tu syāt parikṣīṇo vāhanena balena ca |
tadāsīta prayatnena śanakaiḥ sāntvayann arīn || 7.172 ||
yadā tv iti | yadā punar hastyādinā bhṛyaiś ca parikṣīṇaḥ syāt tadā śanaiḥ sāmopapradānādinā śatrūn sāntvayan yatnenātyucchrayād āsanam āśrayet || 7.172 ||
manyetāriṃ yadā rājā sarvathā balavattaram |
tadā dvidhā balaṃ kṛtvā sādhayet karyam ātmanaḥ || 7.173 ||
manyeteti | sarvaprakāreṇa yadātiśayena balinam aśakyasādhanaṃ śatruṃ manyeta tadā balaikadeśasahito durgo ‘pāśrayo ‘pareṇa balaikadeśena tadupaghātam ācaran evaṃ dvidhā balaṃ kṛtvā mitrasaṃgrahād ātmanaḥ kāryaṃ sādhayet || 7.173 ||
yadā parabalānāṃ tu gamanīyatamo bhavet |
tadā tu saṃśrayet kṣipraṃ dhārmikaṃ balinaṃ nṛpam || 7.174 ||
yadeti | yadā śatrubalānāṃ prakṛtikopādinātiśayena yātavyo bhavati, tadā kṣipram eva dhārmikaṃ paritrāṇasamarthaṃ nṛpam āśrayet || 7.174 ||
nigrahaṃ prakṛtīnāṃ ca kuryād yo ‘ribalasya ca |
upaseveta taṃ nityaṃ sarvayatnair guruṃ yathā || 7.175 ||
nigraham iti | yatsaṃbandhidoṣeṇāsya gamanīyatvam āyātaṃ tāsāṃ prakṛtīnāṃ śatrubalasya ca yenāsau balibhūtas tayor ubhayor api sa vijitaḥ san yo nigrahaṃ kuryāt, taṃ sarvabalair guruvan nityaṃ seveta || 7.175 ||
yadi tatrāpi saṃpaśyed doṣaṃ saṃśrayakāritam |
suyuddham eva tatrāpi nirviśaṅkaḥ samācaret || 7.176 ||
yadīti | suyuddham eva tatrāpi nirvikalpaḥ samācaret | agatikā hi gatiḥ saṃśrayo nāma, tatrāpi ca saṃśrayakṛtaṃ doṣaṃ yadi paśyet tadā nirvikalpo bhūtvā śobhanaṃ yuddham eva tasmin kāle samācaret, durbalenāpi balavataḥ parājayadarśanāt nihatasya ca svargaprāpteḥ || 7.176 ||
sarvopāyais tathā kuryān nītijñaḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ |
yathāsyābhyadhikā na syur mitrodāsīnaśatravaḥ || 7.177 ||
sarvopāyair iti | nītikuśalo rājā sarvaiḥ sāmādibhir upāyaiḥ tathā yateta yathāsya mitrodāsīnaśatrumadhyamā adhikā na bhavanti | ādhikye hi teṣāṃ sutarāṃ kṣobhyo bhavati, dhanagarveṇa mitrasya śātravāpatteḥ || 7.177 ||
āyatiṃ sarvakāryāṇāṃ tadātvaṃ ca vicārayet |
atītānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ guṇadoṣau ca tattvataḥ || 7.178 ||
āyatim iti | sarveṣāṃ svalpānām api kāryāṇām āgāmini kāle guṇadoṣā ity evaṃ vicārayet vartamānakāle ca kṣiprasaṃpādanārthaṃ sarveṣāṃ kāṛyāṇāṃ guṇadoṣau kim eṣāṃ kṛtaṃ vicāritaṃ kiṃ vāvaśiṣṭam ity evaṃ yāvad vicārayet || 7.178 ||
āyatyāṃ guṇadoṣajñas tadātve kṣipraniścayaḥ |
atīte kāryaśeṣajñaḥ śatrubhir nābhibhūyate || 7.179 ||
āyatām iti | yaḥ kāryāṇām āgāmikāladoṣajño guṇavatkāryam ārabhate, doṣavat pariharati yaś ca vartamānakāle kṣipram evāvadhāryaṃ kāryaṃ karoti, na dīrghasūtro kārye yaḥ kāryaśeṣajño bhavati sa tatkāryasamāptau tat phalaṃ labhate | ata evaṃvidhakālatrayasāvadhānatvān na kadācit śatrubhir abhibhūyate || 7.179 ||
yathainaṃ nābhisaṃdadhyur mitrodāsīnaśatravaḥ |
tathā sarvaṃ saṃvidadhyād eṣa sāmāsiko nayaḥ || 7.180 ||
yatheti | yathainaṃ rājānaṃ mitrādaya uktā na vañcayeyuḥ tathā sarvaṃ savidhānaṃ kuryāt | ity evaṃ sāṃkṣepiko nayo nītiḥ || 7.180 ||
yadā tu yānam ātiṣṭhed arirāṣṭraṃ prati prabhuḥ |
tadānena vidhānena yāyād aripuraṃ śanaiḥ || 7.181 ||
yadeti || yadā tv arirāṣṭraṃ prati yātrām ārabheta śaktaḥ saṃs tadānena vakṣyamāṇena prakāreṇa śatrudeśe ‘tvaramāṇo gacchet || 7.181 ||
mārgaśīrṣe śubhe māsi yāyād yātrāṃ mahīpatiḥ |
phālgunaṃ vātha caitraṃ vā māsau prati yathābalam || 7.182 ||
mārgaśīrṣa iti | yaś caturaṅgabalaprāyo mahīpatiḥ sa mārgaśīrṣe māsi śubhe pracuratarayavasaṃ dhānyarathamārdopete tacchubhe dīrghakālam agrato vāsannasasyapratyāsanne yātrāṃ kuryāt | yaḥ punar aśvabalaprāyo vāsantasasyaprāyaṃ deśaṃ yāti, adīrghakāla eva yasya yātrā sa phālgune caitre vā evaṃ svabalayogyakālānatikrameṇa yāyāt || 7.182 ||
anyeṣv api tu kāleṣu yadā paśyed dhruvaṃ jayam |
tadā yāyād vigṛhyaiva vyasane cotthite ripoḥ || 7.183 ||
anyeṣv iti | uktakālavyatirikteṣv api kāleṣu yadātmano niścitaṃ jayam āśaṅketa tadā hastibalaprāyo ‘śvabalaprāyo varṣāsv aśvabalaprāyo hastibalaprāyo grīṣme tadā vigṛhyaiva yātrāṃ kuryāt | śatroś ca prakṛtikopādau vyasane upapanne uktakālavyatirikte ‘pi kāle yāyāt || 7.183 ||
kṛtvā vidhānaṃ mūle tu yātrikaṃ ca yathāvidhi |
upagṛhyāspadaṃ caiva cārān samyag vidhāya ca || 7.183 ||
saṃśodhya trividhaṃ mārgaṃ ṣaḍvidhaṃ ca balaṃ svakam |
sāṃparāyikakalpena yāyād aripuraṃ śanaiḥ || 7.184 ||
kṛtveti | saṃśodhyeti | pārṣṇigrāhasaṃdhānajanapadadurgasaṃrakṣaṇakruddhādivargopadeśam anādikaṃ mūlam ātmadurgarāṣṭrarūp saṃvidhānaṃ kṛtvā upayogivāhanāyudhacarmamantrādividhānaṃ yathāśāstraṃ kṛtvā paramaṇḍalagatasya yenāspadam avasthānaṃ bhavati tad upagṛhya tadīyān bhṛtyapakṣān ātmasātkṛtya parān svakārpaṭikādīn paramaṇḍalavārtāṃ ca yodhārthaṃ samyag avasthāpya jāṅgalānūpāṭavikabhedena trividhaṃ panthānaṃ mārgarodhakagulmādicchedanimnonnatādisamīkaraṇodapānakhananādinā saṃśodhya, tathā hastyaśvarathapadātisenāpatikarmakarātmikaṃ ṣaḍvidhaṃ balaṃ svakam, yathopayogam āhārauṣadhasatkārapūraṇādinā saṃśodhya sāṅgrāmikavidhinā śatrudeśam atvarayā vrajet ||
7.184–185 ||
śatrusevini mitre ca gūḍhe yuktataro bhavet |
gatapratyāgate caiva sa hi kaṣṭataro ripuḥ || 7.186 ||
śatrusevinīti | gatapratyāgate caive tau hi kaṣṭatarau ripū | yasmin mitraṃ gūḍhaṃ kṛtvā śatruṃ sevate, yaś ca bhṛtyādiḥ pūrvaṃ virāgād gataḥ paścād āgatas tayoḥ sāvadhāno bhavet yasmāt tāv atiśayena ripū kaṣṭatarau || 7.186 ||
daṇḍavyūhena tan mārgaṃ yāyāt tu śakaṭena vā |
varāhamakarābhyāṃ vā sūcyā vā garuḍena vā || 7.187 ||
daṇḍavyūheneti | daṇḍākṛtivyūharacanā vyūhaḥ, evaṃ śakaṭādivyūho ‘pi | tatrāgre ca balādhyakṣo, madhye rājā, paścāt senāpatiḥ, pārśvayor hastinaḥ, tatsamīpe aśvāḥ, tataḥ padātayaḥ, evaṃ kṛtaracano dīrghaḥ sarvataḥ samavinyāso daṇḍavyūhas tena taṃ yātavyaṃ mārgaṃ sarvato bhaye sati yāyāt | sūcyākārāgraḥ paścāt pṛthuḥ śakaṭavyūhas tena pārśvato bhaye sati vrajet | varāhaviparyayeṇa makaravyūhas tenāgre paścādbhāge bhaye sati gacchet | pipīlikāpaṅktivat agrapaścādbhāgenāsaṃhatarūpatayā yatra sainikāvasthānaṃ sa dīrghapracāraḥ puruṣamukhaḥ sūcīvyūhas tenāgrato bhaye sati yāyāt || 7.187 ||
yataś ca bhayam āśaṅket tato vistārayed balam |
padmena caiva vyūhena niviśeta sadā svayam || 7.188 ||
yata iti | yasyā diśaḥ śatrukṛtaṃ bhayam āśaṅyeta tasyām eva balaṃ vistārayet | tam antato vistṛtaparimaṇḍalo madhyopaviṣṭo vijigīṣuḥ padmavyūhena purān nirgatyaivaṃ sarvadā svayaṃ susthātavyam || 7.188 ||
senāpatibalādhyakṣau sarvadikṣu niveśayet |
yataś ca bhayam āśaṅket prācīṃ tāṃ kalpayed diśam || 7.189 ||
senāpatibalādhyakṣāv iti | hastyaśvarathapadātyātmakasyāṅgadaśakasyaikaḥ patiḥ kāryaḥ, sa pattika ucyate | pattikadaśakasyaikaḥ patiḥ sa senāpatiḥ | taddaśakasyaikaḥ sa nāyakaḥ | sa eva ca balādhyakṣaḥ, tau sarvāsu dikṣv anekapradhānatve sati saṃgharṣayuddhārthaṃ niyojayet | yasyāṃ ca diśo bhayam āśaṅket tām agre diśaṃ kuryāt || 7.189 ||
gulmāṃś ca sthāpayed āptān kṛtasaṃjñān samantataḥ |
sthāne yuddhe ca kuśalān abhīrūn avikāriṇaḥ || 7.190 ||
gulmān iti | gulmān sthānakavyāptapuruṣādhiṣṭhitān sthānāpasaraṇayuddhādyathakṛtaśaṅkha-bherīpaṭahādisaṅketān vanāvasthānapraṇītān nirbhayān avyabhicārān samantāt sthāpayet
|| 7.190 ||
saṃhatān yodhayed alpān kāmaṃ vistārayed bahūn |
sūcyā vajreṇa caivaitān vyūhena vyūhya yodhayet || 7.191 ||
saṃhatān iti | alpān yodhān saṃhatān kṛtvā yodhayed bahūn punar yathecchaṃ vistārayet | sūcyā pūrvoktayā vajrākhyena vā vyūhena tridhāvasthitabalena racayitvā yodhanaṃ yodhayet
|| 7.191 ||
syandanāśvaiḥ same yudhyed anūpe naudvipais tathā |
vṛkṣagulāvṛte cāpair asicarmāyudhaiḥ sthale || 7.192 ||
syandanāśvair iti | samabhūbhāge rathāśvair yoddhavyam, tatra rathasāmarthyāt, tathānugatodake nauhastibhir vṛkṣagulmavyāpte dhanurbhiḥ pāṣāṇakaṇṭakagartādirahite khaḍgaphalakākhyair āyudhaiḥ || 7.192 ||
kurukṣetrāṃś ca matsyāṃś ca pañcālān śūrasenajān |
dīrghāṃl ladhūṃś caiva narān agrānīkeṣu yojayet || 7.193 ||
kaurukṣetrān iti | kurukṣetrabhavān matsyān virāṭadeśavāsinaḥ pāñcālān kānyakubjān bhṛtyān śūrasenajān pṛthuśarīrān prāyeṇa pṛthuśarīrabalaśauryābhimānayogāt senāgreṣu yojayet, tathānyān api dīrghān laghumanuṣyāṃś ca || 7.193 ||
praharṣayed balaṃ vyḥya tāṃś ca samyak parīkṣayet |
ceṣṭāś caiva vijānīyād arīn yodhayatām api || 7.194 ||
praharṣayed iti | ceṣṭāṃ caiva vijānīyād arīṇāṃ yudhyatām iti | balaṃ racayitvā jaye dharmārthau parājaye svargānṛṣye ity evamādinā yuddhārthaṃ protsāhayet | tāṃś ca yodhān samīkṣya parīkṣayet | ka evam uktvā hṛṣyanti kupyanti ceti tathā yodhānām ariṣv ayudhyamānānām api sopadhānayuddhādiceṣṭāḥ vijānīyāt || 7.194 ||
uparudhyārim āsīta rāṣṭraṃ cāsyopapīḍayet |
dūṣayec cāsya satataṃ yavasānnodakendhanam || 7.195 ||
uparudhyeti | durgāpāśritam api yudhyamānam ariṃ durgaveṣṭanadvāreṇāveṣṭyāsīta | asya ca rātrau durgād bahirdeśaṃ luṇṭhanādinopapīḍayet | ghāsānnodakendhanāni vāsya sarvadāpaddravyasaṃmiśraṇādinā dūṣayet || 7.195 ||
bhindyāc caiva taḍāgāni prākāraparikhās tathā |
samavaskandayec cainaṃ rātrau vitrāsayet tathā || 7.196 ||
bhindyād iti | durgopasthajīvyāni taḍāgādīni prabhedena nāśayet | tathā durgaprākārān bhindyāt tatparikhāś ca pūrayet | chidrakaraṇena vā nirudakāḥ kuryāt | evaṃ ca śatrum aśaṅkitam eva samyag avaskandayet, tasyopari nipated rātrau vā divā vā śivāsitādibhiḥ trāsayet | idānīṃ ca muhur muhuḥ || 7.196 ||
upajapyān upajaped budhyetaiva ca tatkṛtam |
yukte ca daive yudhyeta jayaprepsur apetabhīḥ || 7.197 ||
upajapyān iti | tadanvayino rājyārthinaḥ kruddhān bhṛtyāṃś copajāpārhān upajapet | tatkṛtaṃ ca yat tatkṛtyaṃ yat taj jānīyāt grahasaumyādinānukūle daive sati vijayepsur apagatabhayo yudhyeta || 7.197 ||
sāmnā dānena bhedena samastair atha vā pṛthak |
vijetuṃ prayatetārīn na yuddhena kadācana || 7.198 ||
sāmneti | vijetuṃ prayatetārīn na yuddhena kadācana | sāmnā prītyā varadarśanena hitakathanādyātmakena dānena ca hiraṇyādīnāṃ bhedanena ca tatprakṛtīnāṃ tadanvayināṃ ca viśleṣaṇenaivaṃ tair vyastaiḥ samastair vā yathopayogaṃ vijetum arīn prayateta | na punaḥ kadācid yuddhena || 7.198 ||
anityo vijayo yasmād dṛśyate yudhyamānayoḥ |
parājayaś ca saṃgrāme tasmād yuddhaṃ vivarjayet || 7.199 ||
anitya iti | yudhyamānayor yato balavadabalasthānāpekṣāyām evāniyamena jayaparājayau dṛśyete | tasmād upāyāntarasaṃbhave sati yuddhaṃ vivarjayet || 7.199 ||
trayānām apy upāyānāṃ pūrvoktānām asaṃbhave |
tathā yudhyeta saṃpanno vijayeta ripūn yathā || 7.200 ||
trayāṇām apy upāyānāṃ pūrvoktānāṃ parikṣaye | tato yudhyeta saṃyatta iti | sāmādināṃ pūrvoktānām upāyānām asādhakatve sati jayaparājayasaṃdehe tathā sāvadhāno yudhyeta yathā śatrūn vijayet | yadi no jayet tadā svarganiḥsaṃdigdhe punaḥ parājaye niḥsaraṇaṃ sādhayed yathā vakṣyati ‘ātmānaṃ sarvato rakṣed’ iti || 7.200 ||
jitvā saṃpūjayed devān brāhmaṇāṃś caiva dhārmikān |
pradadyāt parihārāṃś ca khyāpayed abhayāni ca || 7.201 ||
jitveti | pararāṣṭraṃ jitvā tatratyān devān dharmapradhānān brāhmaṇāṃś ca dānamānādibhiḥ pūjayet | tathā śrotriyādigatāvaśyadāneṣu mayaitad anujñātam ity evaṃ parihārān dadyāt tathā svāmibhaktyāsmākaṃ yair apakṛtaṃ teṣāṃ mayā kṣāntam idānīm āśvastāḥ santaḥ svavyāpāram anutiṣṭhantv evam abhayāni khyāpayet || 7.201 ||
sarveṣāṃ tu viditvaiṣāṃ samāsena cikīrṣitam |
sthāpayet tatra tadvaṃśyaṃ kuryāc ca samayakriyām || 7.202 ||
sarveṣām iti | teṣāṃ parakīyānāṃ sarveṣāṃ saṃkṣepato ‘bhilaṣitaṃ jñātvā tasmin jitarāṣṭre jitarājānvayinam abhiṣecayet | rājye idaṃ tvayā kāryam idaṃ nety evaṃ tasya tadamātyādīnāṃ paribhāṣaṇaṃ ca kuryāt || 7.202 ||
pramāṇāni ca kurvīta teṣāṃ dharmyān yathoditān |
ratnaiś ca pūjayed enaṃ pradhānapuruṣaiḥ saha || 7.203 ||
pramāṇānīti | teṣāṃ parakīyānāṃ ye dharmād anapetā deśadharmatayā śāstreṇābhyupagatā ācārās tān pramāṇīkuryāt | evaṃ cābhiṣiktaṃ mantryādibhiḥ saha ratnādidānena pūjayed yatnāt || 7.203 ||
ādānam apriyakaraṃ dānaṃ ca priyakārakam |
abhīpsitānām arthānāṃ kāle yuktaṃ praśasyate || 7.204 ||
ādānam iti | ye abhilaṣitānām arthānām eva haraṇam apriyakaraṃ dānaṃ ca teṣāṃ priyajanakaṃ ity utsargaṃ tathā kālavaśena dānam api vā praśasyate tasmāt tasmin kāle enaṃ pūjayet || 7.204 ||
sarvaṃ karmedam āyattaṃ vidhāne daivamānuṣe |
tayor daivam acintyaṃ tu mānuṣe vidyate kriyā || 7.205 ||
sarvam iti | yat kiṃcit saṃpādyaṃ tat prāgjanmaśubhāśubhaṃ ubhe karmaṇī tad ihalokakṛte ca manuṣyavyāpāra āyattaṃ tatra tayor madhye daivaṃ cintayitum aśakyam | mānuṣe punaḥ paryālocanaṃ bhavaty etad dvāreṇa kāryasiddhyarthaṃ yatitavyaṃ nirīhena bhāvyam || 7.205 ||
saha vāpi vrajed yuktaḥ saṃdhiṃ kṛtvā prayatnataḥ |
mitraṃ hiraṇyaṃ bhūmiṃ vā saṃpaśyaṃs trividhaṃ phalam || 7.206 ||
saha vāpi vrajed yas tv iti | evaṃ yātavyena saha yuddhaṃ kāryaṃ yadi vā sa eva mitraṃ hiraṇyaṃ vā tena datte bhūmyekadeśo vā tyakta ity evaṃ yātrāphalaṃ matvā tena saha saṃdhiṃ kṛtvā yatnavān vrajet || 7.206 ||
pārṣṇigrāhaṃ ca saṃprekṣya tathākrandaṃ ca maṇḍale |
mitrād athāpy amitrād vā yātrāphalam avāpnuyāt || 7.207 ||
pārṣṇigrāham iti | vijigīṣor ariṃ prati niryātasya yo deśākramaṇādy ācarati sa pārṣṇigrāhas tathā kurvataḥ pārṣṇigrāhasya yo ‘ṃkuśasthānīyaḥ sa ākrandas tam apekṣya yātavyān maitrībhūtād amitrād vā phalaṃ gṛhṇīyāt tāvad anavekṣya gṛhṇan kadācit tadgatena doṣeṇa yujyate || 7.207 ||
hiraṇyabhūmisaṃprāptyā pārthivo na tathaidhate |
yathā mitraṃ dhruvaṃ labdhvā kṛśam apy āyatikṣamam || 7.208 ||
hiraṇyabhūmisaṃprāptyeti | hiraṇyabhūmilābhena rājā tathā na vardhate yathā saṃprati kṛśam apy āgāmikālalabdhavṛddhyādyupetaṃ sthiraṃ mitraṃ prāpya vardhate || 7.208 ||
dharmajñaṃ ca kṛtajñaṃ ca tuṣṭakṛtim eva ca |
anuraktaṃ sthirārambhaṃ laghumitraṃ praśasyate || 7.209 ||
dharmajñam iti | dharmajñam upakārasya dakṣam anuraktāmātyaṃ sānurāgaṃ sthirakāryaṃ dakṣaṃ yat tasmin mitram atiśayeneṣyate || 7.209 ||
prājñaṃ kulīnaṃ śūraṃ ca dakṣaṃ dātāram eva ca |
kṛtajñaṃ dhṛtimantaṃ ca kaṣṭam āhur ariṃ budhāḥ || 7.210 ||
prājñam iti | prājñaṃ mahākulaṃ vikrāntam utsāhayuktam udāram | upakārasya rakṣitāram arthānarthayor ekarūpaṃ śatruṃ durucchedaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ āhuḥ | atas tathāvidhaṃ yatnataḥ saṃdadhyāt || 7.210 ||
āryatā puruṣajñānaṃ śauryaṃ karuṇaveditā |
sthaulalakṣyaṃ ca satatam udāsīnaguṇodayaḥ || 7.211 ||
āryateti  | sādhutvaṃ puruṣaviśeṣajñatā śauryaṃ dayālutvaṃ sarvadā ca sthūlalakṣam artheṣu sūkṣmadarśitvam iṣyate | sthūlalakṣaṃ satatam udāsīnaguṇasāmagryam ata evaṃvidham udāsīnam āśritya uktalakṣaṇenāpy ariṇā saha yoddhavyam || 7.211 ||
kṣemyāṃ sasyapradāṃ nityaṃ paśuvṛddhikarīm api |
parityajen nṛpo bhūmim ātmārtham avicārayan || 7.212 ||
kṣemyām iti | parityajen nṛpo bhūmim ātmārtham avadhārayan | rakṣākṣamām api nadīmmātṛkatayā sarvadā satyapradām api bahutṛṇapatrādiyogāt paśuvṛddhikarīm api bhūmim ātmarakṣārtham avalambamāno rājānanyagatikaḥ parityajet || 7.212 ||
āpadarthe dhanaṃ rakṣed dārān rakṣed dhanair api |
ātmānaṃ satataṃ rakṣed dārair api dhanair api || 7.213 ||
yataḥ sakalapuruṣaviṣayo ‘yaṃ dharmaḥ smaryate āpadartha iti | apannivṛttyarthaṃ dhanaṃ rakṣaṇīyam | dhanaparityāgenāpi dārāḥ saṃrakṣaṇīyāḥ | ātmā punar dhanadārādinā sarvaparityāgenāpi saṃrakṣyaḥ | yat tu rājavyatirekeṇānyasya dārārtham ātmatyāgo nyāyya ity āhus tad asat | sarvata ātmānaṃ gopāyed iti aśāstritamaraṇasya niṣiddhatvān na ca sahasaiva dhanadāraparityāgaḥ kāryaḥ || 7.213 ||
saha sarvāḥ samutpannāḥ prasamīkṣyāpado bhṛśam |
saṃyuktāṃś ca viyuktāṃś ca sarvopāyān sṛjed budhāḥ || 7.214 ||
yasmāt saheti | prakṛtikopakoṣakṣayamitravyasanādikā yugapat sarvā āpado ‘tyartham utpannā jñātvā na moham upeyād api tu vyastān samastān vā sāmādīn upāyān sarvān vā prājñaḥ prayuñjīta sarvathā || 7.214 ||
upetāram upeyaṃ ca sarvopāyāṃś ca kṛtsnaśaḥ |
etat trayaṃ samāśritya prayatetārthasiddhaye || 7.215 ||
upetāram iti | etat trayaṃ samāśritya prayatetātmasiddhaye | upetātmā upeyaṃ ca prāptavyaṃ sarve ca sāmādaya upāyās te ca paripūrṇā iti tat trayasāmarthye sati ātmasiddhyarthaṃ yatetey upsaṃhāraḥ || 7.215 ||
evaṃ sarvam idaṃ rājā saha saṃmantrya mantribhiḥ |
vyāyamyāplutya madhyāhne bhoktum antaḥpuraṃ viśet || 7.216 ||
evam iti | evam uktanītyā sarvaṃ rājā mantribhiḥ saha vicārya tad anu dhanurādinā vyāyāmaṃ kṛtvā snātvā ca madhyāhne kāle antaḥpuraṃ bhoktuṃ praviśet || 7.216 ||
tatrātmabhūtaiḥ kalajñair ahāryaiḥ paricārakaiḥ |
suparīkṣitam annādyam adyān mantrair viṣāpahaiḥ || 7.217 ||
tatreti | tatrāntaḥpura ātmatulyair bhojanakālavedibhir abhedyaiḥ sūpakārādibhir yat kṛtam annādyaṃ suṣṭhu ca parīkṣitam agniprakṣepacakorādidarśanena agnir hi saviṣānnaprakṣepe sati catacaṭāyate cakorasyākṣiṇī rakte bhavata ity evmādinā parīkṣitaṃ viṣāpahāraiś ca mantrair japtam adyāt || 7.217 ||
viṣaghnair udakaiś cāsya sarvadravyāṇi śodhayet |
viṣaghnāni ca ratnāni niyato dhārayet sadā || 7.218 ||
viṣaghnair iti | viṣaghnair auṣadhaprayogaiḥ sarvadravyāni bhogabhojyādīni lakṣayet | viṣaghnāni ca ratnāni garuḍodgīrṇanāgamaṇiprabhṛtīni yatnavān sarvadā dhārayet || 7.218 ||
parīkṣitāḥ striyaś cainaṃ vyajanodakadhūpanaiḥ |
veṣābharaṇasaṃśuddhāḥ spṛśeyuḥ susamāhitāḥ || 7.219 ||
parīkṣitā iti | striyaś ca dāsyo dharmārthakāmobhayaviṣaye cāraprayogādidvāreṇa kṛtaparīkṣāḥ gūḍāyudhagrahaṇaviṣaliptābharaṇadhāraṇāśaṅkayā saṃśuddhaveṣālaṅkaraṇāḥ ananyamanaskāś cāmarasnānādyudakadhūpair enaṃ rājānaṃ paricareyuḥ || 7.219 ||
evaṃ prayatnaṃ kurvīta yānaśayyāsanāśane |
snāne prasādhane caiva sarvālaṃkārakeṣu ca || 7.220 ||
evam iti | evaṃvidhaparīkṣādiprayatnaṃ vāhanaśayanāsanabhojanasnānānulepaneṣu sarvālaṃkārārtheṣu kurvīta || 7.220 ||
bhuktavān viharec caiva strībhir antaḥpure saha |
vihṛtya tu yathākālaṃ punaḥ kāryāṇi cintayet || 7.221 ||
bhuktavān iti | kṛtabhojanaḥ san tasminn evāntaḥpure bhāryābhiḥ saha krīḍet | kālātikrameṇa ca saptame divasasya bhāge tatra vihṛtyāṣṭame bhāge punaḥ kāryāṇi cintayet || 7.221 ||
alaṃkṛtaś ca saṃpaśyed āyudhīyaṃ punar janam |
vāhanāni ca sarvāṇi śastrāṇy ābharaṇāni ca || 7.222 ||
alaṃkṛta iti | alaṃkrtaś ca sann āyudhajīvinaṃ punar janaṃ vāhanāni cāśvādīni sarvāṇi khaḍgādīni ca śastrāṇy alaṃkaraṇāni ca saṃpaśyet || 7.222 ||
saṃdhyāṃ copāsya śṛṇuyād antarveśmani śastrabhṛt |
rahasyākhyāyināṃ caiva praṇidhīnāṃ ca ceṣṭitam || 7.223 ||
saṃdhyām iti | tataḥ saṃdhyopāsanaṃ kṛtvā gṛhābhyantare bhṛtaśastro rahasyābhidhāyināṃ svavyāpāraṃ śṛṇuyāt || 7.223 ||
gatvā kakṣāntaraṃ tv anyat samanujñāpya taṃ janam |
praviśed bhojanārthaṃ ca strīvṛto ‘ntaḥpuraṃ punaḥ || 7.224 ||
gatveti | tasmāt pradeśād anyad vimuktaṃ pradeśāntaraṃ gatvā cārajanaṃ saṃprekṣya paricārikādibhiḥ strīvṛtaḥ san punar bhoktum antaḥpuraṃ praviśet || 7.224 ||
tatra bhuktvā punaḥ kiṃcit tūryaghoṣaiḥ praharṣitaḥ |
saṃviśet tu yathākālam uttiṣṭhec ca gataklamaḥ || 7.225 ||
tatra bhuktvā punaḥ kiṃcit tūryaghoṣaiḥ praharṣibhiḥ | saṃviśec ca yathākālam uttiṣṭhed vigataklamaḥ | tatrāntaḥpure kiṃcin nātitṛptaṃ vāditraśabdaiḥ śrutisukhair yukte bhuktvā kālānatikrameṇa gatārdharātrapraharāyāṃ rātrau supyāt | suptvā ca paścime yāme uttiṣṭhet || 7.225 ||
etad vidhānam ātiṣṭhed arogaḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ |
asvasthaḥ sarvam etat tu bhṛtyeṣu viniyojayet || 7.226 ||
etad iti | arogo rājaitad anutkrāntaṃ prajārakṣaṇāi svayam anutiṣṭhet | asvasthaḥ punaḥ sarvam etad amātyeṣu samarpayet || 7.226 ||
iti śrībhaṭṭamādhavātmajagovindarājaviracitāyāṃ
manuṭīkāyāṃ manvānusāriṇyāṃ rājadharmo nāma saptamo ‘dhyāyaḥ || 6 ||
aṣṭamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
vyavahārān didṛkṣus tu brāhmaṇaiḥ saha pārthivaḥ |
mantrajñair mantribhiś caiva vinītaḥ praviśet sabhām || 8.1 ||
vyavahārān nṛpaḥ paśyed iti | arthipratyarthinor ṛṇādānādiviṣayāṇītaretaraviruddhāvaharaṇarūpāṇi kāryāṇi draṣṭum icchan rājā vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇair mantribhiś coktapañcāṅgamantrajñaiḥ saha cāpalādirahito vakṣyamāṇasabhāṃ praviśet || 8.1 ||
tatrāsīnaḥ sthito vāpi pāṇim udyamya dakṣiṇam |
vinītaveṣābharaṇaḥ paśyet kāryāṇi kāryiṇām || 8.2 ||
tatreti | tasyāṃ sabhāyāṃ gurukāryāpekṣayopaviṣṭo ‘nyathotthito dakṣiṇabāhuṃ vāsasa uddhṛtya anuddhataveṣālaṃkāraḥ kāryārthināṃ vyavahāraṃ niyamato vicārayet || 8.2 ||
pratyahaṃ deśadṛṣṭaiś ca śāstradṛṣṭaiś ca hetubhiḥ |
aṣṭādaśasu mārgeṣu nibaddhāni pṛthak pṛthak || 8.3 ||
pratyaham iti | tāṃś ca vyavahārān aṣṭādaśavyavahārasthāne ṛṇādānādiṣu vakṣyamāṇeṣu pṛthak pṛthak vyavasthitān pratiniyatadeśavyavasthāvaṇikkarṣakādigatayā śāstrāviruddhayā śāstragatayā ca sākṣiśapathānumānādyātmakayā pratyaham ālokyam eva || 8.3 ||
teṣām ādyam ṛṇādānaṃ nikṣepo ‘svāmivikrayaḥ |
saṃbhūya ca samutthānaṃ dattasyānapakarma ca || 8.4 ||
vetanasyaiva cādānaṃ saṃvidaś ca vyatikramaḥ |
krayavikrayānuśayo vivādaḥ svāmipālayoḥ || 8.5 ||
sīmāvivādadharmaś ca pāruṣye daṇḍavācike |
steyaṃ ca sāhasaṃ caiva strīsaṃgrahaṇam eva ca || 8.6 ||
strīpuṃdharmo vibhāgaś ca dyūtam āhvaya eva ca |
padāny aṣṭādaśaitāni vyavahārasthitāv iha || 8.7 ||
teṣām iti | vetanasyeti | sīmāvivādadharma iti | strīpuṃdharma iti | teṣām aṣṭādaśānām ādāv adharmagṛhītasya ṛṇasyādānam iha vicāryate | tataḥ krameṇa nikṣepo ‘svāminā ca yaḥ kriyate vikrayādiḥ | ekībhūya dhanārjanehā | yāgādyarthaṃ dattasya pratiśrutasya vā yāgādyakaraṇe satyapradānam | bhṛter adānam | grāmādikṛtavyavasthāvyatikramaḥ | kraye vikraye vā sati paścattāpaḥ | svāmipālayoḥ vipratipattiḥ | grāmādisīmāvivādavyavasthā | vākpāruṣyaṃ tāḍanādi | cauryam | pradhaṣyāpaharaṇādi sāsaham | striyaś ca parasparaṃ ca saṃparkaṃ rāmakām strīsahitasya | puṃso bhartur vyavasthā | paitṛkādidhanavibhāgo ‘kṣādikrīḍāvyavasthāpanam | paśupakṣyādiyodhanam ity evam aṣṭādaśaitāni vyavahārotpattau loke sthānāni || 8.4–7 ||
eṣu sthāneṣu bhūyiṣṭhaṃ vivādaṃ caratāṃ nṛṇām |
dharmaṃ śāśvatam āśritya kuryāt kāryavinirṇayam || 8.8 ||
eṣv iti | eṣv aṣṭādaśasu sthāneṣu vyavasthāneṣu bāhulyena manuṣyāṇāṃ vivādaṃ kurvatām anādipāramparyāyānāṃ vyavasthām āśritya vyavahāranirṇayaṃ kuryāt | bhūyiṣṭhavacanād anyāny api santi | yathoktaṃ nāradena:
teṣām eva prabhedo ‘nyaḥ śatam aṣṭotaraṃ smṛtam || 8.8 ||
yadā svayaṃ na kuryāt tu nṛpatiḥ kāryadarśanam |
tadā niyuñjyād vidvāṃsaṃ brāḥmaṇaṃ kāryadarśane || 8.9 ||
yadeti | yadā kāryāntaravyākulatayātmanā vyavahārekṣaṇam rājā na kuryāt tadā kāryadarśanārthaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ niyuñjīta || 8.9 ||
so ‘sya kāryāṇi saṃpaśyet sabhyair eva tribhir vṛtaḥ |
sabhām eva praviśyāgryām āsīnaḥ sthita eva vā || 8.10 ||
sa iti | sa brāhmaṇaḥ sabhāyāṃ sādhubhir dhārmikair brāhmaṇaiḥ yuktas tām eva rājasabhāṃ śreṣṭhāṃ praviśyopaviṣtaḥ sthito vā asya rājño draṣṭavyān vyavahārān saṃpaśyet || 8.10 ||
yasmin deśe niṣīdanti viprā vedavidas trayaḥ |
rājñaś cādhikrto vidvān brahmaṇas tāṃ sabhāṃ viduḥ || 8.11 ||
yasminn iti | yasmin sthāne ṛgyajuḥsāmapāragāḥ trayo ‘pi brāhmaṇā rājādhikṛtaś ca vidvān avatiṣṭhate hiraṇyagarbhām iva tāṃ sabhāṃ manyante || 8.11 ||
dharmo viddhas tv adharmeṇa sabhāṃ yatropatiṣṭhate |
śalyaṃ cāsya na kṛntanti viddhās tatra sabhāsadaḥ || 8.12 ||
yasmin draṣṭari dharmaśāstravyavasthāś cādharmeṇa vyavasthāyāpīḍitaḥ sabhām upatiṣṭhate yatrāvasthānaṃ kurute sabhyāś ca brāhmaṇās tasya dharmasya śalyam iva śalyam adharmākhyaṃ noddharanti tatra tenādharmaśalyena sabhyā viddhā bhavanti || 8.12 ||
sabhāṃ vā na praveṣṭavyam vaktavyaṃ vā samañjasam |
abruvan vibruvan vāpi naro bhavati kilbiṣī || 8.13 ||
sabhāṃ vā na praveṣṭavyeti | vyavahāradarśanārthaṃ sabhā na praveṣṭavyā | praviṣṭaś cet tadā satyaṃ vaktavyam | atha vā tūṣṇīm āsīno viruddhaṃ vā bruvan sabhya eva enasvī bhavati || 8.13 ||
yatra dharmo hy adharmeṇa satyaṃ yatrānṛtena ca |
hanyate prekṣamāṇānāṃ hatās tara sabhāsadaḥ || 8.14 ||
yatreti  | yasyāṃ sabhāyāṃ sabhāvasthitānāṃ prāḍvivākavādīnāṃ prekṣamāṇānāṃ vādinā prativādinā vā dharmo bādhyate | yatra ca sākṣibhiḥ satyam anṛtena bādhyate tatra te prāḍvivākādayaḥ sabhāsadaḥ pāpena hatā bhavanti | tasmān na cāpi prativādinā sākṣikṛto vyatikramo rakṣaṇīyaḥ || 8.14 ||
dharma eva hato hanti dharmo rakṣati rakṣitaḥ |
tasmād dharmo na hantavyo mā no dharmo hato vadhīt || 8.15 ||
dharma iti | tasmād dharma eva vyatikrānto dṛṣṭādṛṣṭābhyāṃ nāśayati | nārthipratyarthyādi | sa eva cānatikrāntatayā rakṣati tasmād dharmo nātikramaṇīyaḥ māsmākaṃ tvatsahitānāṃ dharmo vyatikrānto dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthaṃ nāśaṃ kārṣīd iti | sabhyair asatpravṛttaḥ prāḍvivākaḥ saṃbodhanīyaḥ || 8.15 ||
vṛṣo hi bhagavān dharmas tasya yaḥ kurute hy alam |
vṛṣalaṃ taṃ vidur devās tasmād dharmaṃ na lopayet || 8.16 ||
vṛṣa iti | tasmāt sakalakāmavarṇānāṃ vṛṣaśabdena dharma ucyate | tasya vyāharaṇaṃ kurute | taṃ devā vṛṣalaṃ manyante na jātivṛṣalaṃ tasmād dharmaṃ nocchindyād iti dharmavyatikramaparihārārthaṃ vṛṣalavacanam || 8.16 ||
eka eva suhṛd dharmo nidhane ‘py anuyāti yaḥ |
śarīreṇa samaṃ nāśaṃ sarvam anyad dhi gacchati || 8.17 ||
eka iti | tasmād anyat sarvaṃ bharyāputramitrādi śarīreṇaiva saha nāśaṃ gacchati na dharmavat trāṇārthaṃ kiṃcid api paścād anveti tasmāt suhṛd dharmo na hātavyaḥ || 8.17 ||
pādo ‘dharmasya kartāraṃ pādaḥ sākṣiṇam ṛcchati |
pādaḥ sabhāsadaḥ sarvān pādo rājānam ṛcchati || 8.18 ||
pāda iti | tad vyavahārato ‘dharmasaṃbandhī caturtho bhāgo ‘rthinaṃ pratyarthinaṃ vādisaṃpravṛttaṃ gacchaty anyaś caturthabhāgo ‘satyavādinaṃ sākṣiṇaṃ cānyaḥ paraḥ sarvān sabhāsthitān prekṣamāṇān parityajya rājānaṃ prayātīti sarveṣām adharmasaṃbandho bhavatīty etatpratipādanaparam etat || 8.18 ||
rājā bhavaty anenās tu mucyante ca sabhāsadaḥ |
eno gacchati kartāraṃ nindārho yatra nidyate || 8.19 ||
rājeti | yatra punaḥ sabhāyām asadvāditvād arthī pratyarthī vā samyagdarśanena kutsyate tatra rājāpāpo bhavati sabhāsadaś ca pāpena na saṃbadhyante cārthādikartāram eva gacchati || 8.19 ||
jātimātropajīvī vā kāmaṃ syād brāhmaṇabruvaḥ |
dharmapravaktā nṛpater na tu śūdraḥ kathaṃcana || 8.20 ||
jātimātropajīvīti | kevalaṃ brāhmaṇajātimātraṃ yasya tena brāhmaṇadharmānusāreṇa sa tu brāhmaṇo ‘sya rājño vidvadbrāhmaṇābhāve vyavasthāne dharmasya pravaktādhikṛtaḥ syāt na kadācit śūdra iti | tadā niyuñjyād vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇam ity anenaiva siddhe śūdrapratiṣedhe punarvacanaṃ kṣatriyavaiśyāniyojanāc chūdraniyojanasya niṣedhaḥ || 8.20 ||
yasya śūdras tu kurute rājño dharmavivecanam |
tasya sīdati tad rāṣṭraṃ paṅke gaur iva paśyataḥ || 8.21 ||
yasmāt yasyeti | yasya rājñaḥ śūdro dharmavicāraṃ kurute tasya paśyata eva kardama iva gauḥ tad rāṣṭram avasīdati || 8.21 ||
tad rāṣṭraṃ śūdrabhūyiṣṭhaṃ nāstikākrāntam advijam |
nivaśyaty āśu tat kṛtsnaṃ durbhikṣavyādhipīḍitam || 8.22 ||
yad iti | yad rāṣṭraṃ śūdrabahulaṃ paralokāyatikākrāntaṃ avidyamānadvijaṃ tat sarvaṃ durbhikṣavyādhipīḍitaṃ bhūtvā vinaśyati | agnau prāstety etad abhāvāc chāntyarthayāgādyabhāvāc ca || 8.22 ||
dharmāsanam adhiṣṭhāya saṃvītāṅgaḥ samāhitaḥ |
praṇamya lokapālebhyaḥ kāryadarśanam ācaret || 8.23 ||
dharmāsanam iti | praṇamya lokapālebhyaḥ kāryadarśanam ācaret | dharmadarśanārtham āsane upaviśyācchāditadeho ‘nanyamanā lokapālān namaskṛtya tato vyavahārekṣaṇaṃ rājā caret || 8.23 ||
arthānarthāv ubhau buddhvā dharmādharmau ca kevalau |
varṇakrameṇa sarvāṇi paśyet kāryāṇi kāryiṇām || 8.24 ||
arthānarthāv  iti | prakṛtisaṃprasādakopātmakān tathāvidhān lokagatān ālocya paralokārthaṃ ca dharmādharmāv eva kevalāv ālokya yathā dharmahāniḥ adharmānarthotpādaś ca na bhavati tathā sarvān vyavahārān paśyet | saṃnipāte ca varṇānāṃ brāhmaṇādikrameṇa paśyet || 8.24 ||
bāhyair vibhāvayel liṅgair bhāvam antargataṃ nṛṇām |
svaravarṇeṅgitākāraiś cakṣuṣā ceṣṭitena ca || 8.25 ||
bāhyair iti | gadgadatvādicakṣuḥsvarapāṇḍurādivarṇavepathuromāñcādīṅgitavivarṇatvādy-ākārādhonirīkṣaṇādicaturgatahastaniṣpeṣādiviśeṣaceṣṭākārair bāhyaliṅgair avaśyagamakena svabhāvair gūḍham abhiprāyaṃ sadasadrūpaṃ vyavahāriṇāṃ niścayaṃ anubhūya || 8.25 ||
ākārair iṅgitair gatyā ceṣṭayā bhāṣitena ca |
netravaktravikāraiś ca gṛhyate ‘ntargataṃ manaḥ || 8.26 ||
yasmāt loke ‘pi ākārair iti | netravaktravikāraiś ca jñāyate ‘ntargataṃ manaḥ | ākārādibhiḥ pūrvoktaiḥ gatyā ca skalanarūpayā nigūḍhasadrūpaṃ jñāyate || 8.26 ||
bāladāyādikaṃ rikthaṃ tāvad rājānupālayet |
yāvat sa syāt samāvṛtto yāvac cātītaśaiśavaḥ || 8.27 ||
bāladāyādikam iti | anāthabālasvāmikaṃ dhanaṃ tāvad rājā rakṣet | yāvad asau gurukulān nivṛtto yāvad vā nivṛttabālabhāvaḥ syād iti bhāvaḥ pitṛvyādibhyaḥ svāmitvopanyāsādivyājenāpajihīrṣadbhyo rakṣitārtham idam asmin vyavahāra ucyate | sāmānyaviṣayasya rakṣaṇasya rājñaḥ sarvaviṣayatvāt || 8.27 ||
vaśāputrāsu caivaṃ syād rakṣaṇaṃ niṣkulāsu ca |
pativratāsu ca strīṣu vidhavāsv āturāsu ca || 8.28 ||
vandhyāputrāsv iti | yathā bāladhanasya rājño rakṣaṇaṃ syād evaṃ saty api bhartari vandhyāsu strīṣv aprajāsu niṣkulāsu ca kanyakāsu mṛtaproṣitabhartṛkāsu sādhvīṣu vyādhitāsu ca rakṣaṇaṃ syād atra gobalivardanyāyenānekaśabdopādānam || 8.28 ||
jīvantīnāṃ tu tāsāṃ ye tad dhareyuḥ svabāndhavāḥ |
tāñ chiṣyāc cauradaṇḍena dhārmikaḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ || 8.29 ||
jīvantīnām iti | bhāvisvāmyād asmād adhīnatvādivyājena ye bāndhavās tāsāṃ jīvantīnāṃ dhanaṃ hareyuḥ || 8.29 ||
pranaṣṭasvāmikaṃ rikthaṃ rājā tryabdaṃ nidhāpayet |
arvāk tryabdād dharet svāmī pareṇa nṛpatir haret || 8.30 ||
pranaṣṭasvāmikam iti | ajñāpamānasvāmikaṃ dhanaṃ rājā kasya kiṃ pranaṣṭam ity evaṃ paṭahādinākhyāya dvārādau varṣatrayaṃ sthāpayet | varṣatrayād arvāk svāmy āyāto gṛhṇīyād ūrdhvaṃ punar nṛpatir gṛhṇīyāt | yāvat svāmy āgacchati tāvad apaharet | evaṃ parako yasya dharmato varṣaśatair api svāpahārasyānyāyyatvāt pranaṣṭa ityāvi cārvāk || 8.30 ||
mamedam iti yo brūyāt so ‘nuyojyo yathāvidhi |
saṃvādya rūpasaṃkhyādīn svāmī tad dravyam arhati || 8.31 ||
mameti | matsaṃbandhy etad dhanam iti yo brūyāt sa kiṃ sāraṃ kiṃ saṃkhyākaṃ kva praṇaṣṭam ity evamādividhānena paryanuyoktavyaḥ | tato yadi rūpasaṃkhyādīn satyān bravīti tad dhanaṃ grahītum arhati || 8.31 ||
avedayāno naṣṭasya deśaṃ kālaṃ ca tattvataḥ |
varṇaṃ rūpaṃ pramāṇaṃ ca tatsamaṃ daṇḍam arhati || 8.32 ||
avedayāna iti | naṣṭadravyasya deśakālau yathāsaṃbhavaṃ ca varṇaśulkādikam ākāraṃ kaṇṭakatvādikaṃ pramāṇaṃ ca yathāvad ajānataḥ tattulyaṃ daṇḍam iti || 8.32 ||
ādadītātha ṣaḍbhāgaṃ pranaṣṭādhigatān nṛpaḥ |
daśamaṃ dvādaśaṃ vāpi satāṃ dharmam anusmaran || 8.33 ||
saṃkhyādisaṃvāde punaḥ ādadīteti | yad etat pranaṣṭaṃ rājñā prāptaṃ tataḥ svāmigatasaṃkhyādisaṃvādānantaraṃ rakṣaṇakleśāpekṣayā ṣaḍbhāgaṃ daśamaṃ dvādaśaṃ vā sādhūnām ayaṃ dharma ity evaṃ rājā gṛhṇīyāt śiṣṭaṃ punaḥ svāmine utsṛjet || 8.33 ||
pranaṣṭādhigataṃ dravyaṃ tiṣṭhed yuktair adhiṣṭhitam |
yāṃs tatra caurān gṛhṇīyāt tān rājebhena ghātayet || 8.34 ||
pranaṣṭādhigatam iti | yad dravyaṃ kasyacit pranaṣṭaṃ sad rājapuruṣair labdhaṃ tad rakṣayet | niyuktaṃ rakṣitaṃ kṛtvā rājā sthāpyaṃ tasmiṃś ca dravye yān caurān rājā gṛhṇīyāt tān śatād abhyadhike vadha iti darśanāt suvarṇaśatamūlyādicaurye sati hastinā ghātayet || 8.34 ||
mamāyam iti yo brūyān nidhiṃ satyena mānavaḥ |
tasyādadīta ṣaḍbhāgaṃ rājā dvādaśam eva vā || 8.35 ||
mamāyam iti | tatsaṃbandhīdaṃ nidhānam ity evaṃ nidhiṃ anyena vā tathye sati tayorūpasaṃkhyādipūrvapratijñāsaṃvādena manuṣyo brūyāt tasya tato nidhānād deśakālāvarṇādyapekṣayā ṣaḍbhāgaṃ dvādaśam eva vā rājā gṛhṇīyāt śiṣṭaṃ tasyotsṛjet || 8.35 ||
anṛtaṃ tu vadan daṇḍyaḥ svavittasyāṃśam aṣṭamam |
tasyaiva vā nidhānasya saṃkhyālpīyasīṃ kalām || 8.36 ||
anṛtam iti | aptapunaḥ (?) satyaṃ bruvan svadhanasyāṣṭamaṃ bhāgaṃ daṇḍyāḥ | yad vā tasyaiva nidhānasyātyantālpaṃ bhāgaṃ viganayya sa viṣādaṃ na gacchati vinayaṃ ca gṛhṇīyāt taṃ daṇḍyo guṇavadaguṇāpekṣayā vikalpaḥ || 8.36 ||
vidvāṃs tu brāhmaṇo dṛṣṭvā pūrvopanihitaṃ nidhim |
aśeṣato ‘py ādadīta sarvasyādhipatir hi saḥ || 8.37 ||
vidvān iti | śāstravid punar brāhmaṇaḥ pitrādisthāpitaṃ nidhiṃ dṛṣṭvā rājño bhāgaṃ na dadyāt | aśeṣaṃ gṛhṇīyād yasmāt sarvasya dhanajātasya brāhmaṇaḥ prabhuḥ ity aśeṣagrahaṇārthavādaḥ | evaṃ ca mamāyam iti | ayam avidvadbrāhmaṇaviṣayaḥ kṣatriyādiviṣayaś ca || 8.37 ||
yaṃ tu paśyen nidhiṃ rājā purāṇaṃ nihitaṃ kṣitau |
tasmād dvijebhyo datārdham ardhaṃ kośe praveśayet || 8.38 ||
yam iti | yaṃ punaḥ cirantanasvāmikaṃ bhūmiprakṣiptaṃ nidhiṃ labheta tasmād ardhaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo datvā ardham ātmano ‘rthāgāre praveśayet || 8.38
nidhīnāṃ tu purāṇānāṃ dhātūnām eva ca kṣitau |
ardhabhāg rakṣaṇād rājā bhūmer adhipatir hi saḥ || 8.39 ||
nidhīnām iti | cirantanānāṃ nidhīnām asvāmikānāṃ anyenāpi labdhānāṃ bhūmigatānāṃ ca suvarṇānāṃ ardhaharo rājā yasmād rakṣaṇād asau bhūmisvāmīti || 8.39 ||
dātvayaṃ sarvavarnebhyo rājñā caurair hṛtaṃ dhanam |
rājā tad upayuñjanaś caurasyāpnoti kilbiṣam || 8.40 ||
dātavyam iti | yac caurair hṛtaṃ lokānāṃ dhanaṃ tad rājñā caurebhyaś cāhṛtaṃ dhanaṃ tebhyo deyam | yasmād rājā svīkuryāt caurasya yāvat pāpaṃ tāvat prāpnoti || 8.40 ||
jātijānapadān dharmān śreṇīdharmāṃś ca dharmavit |
samīkṣya kuladharmāṃś ca svadharmaṃ pratipādayet || 8.41 ||
jātidharmān jānapadān iti | jātidharmān adhyayanādīn jānapadāṃś ca niyatadeśavyavahārān aviruddhān evaṃ vaṇikkarṣaṇādigatān śreṇīdharmān kuladharmāṃś ca pratiniyatakulasthitān jñātvā yasya yaḥ svadharmas taṃ tathā tat sthāpayet | ataś ca tadviruddhavyavahāriṣu vādinaṃ nigṛhṇīyāt || 8.41 ||
svāni karmāṇi kurvāṇā dūre santo ‘pi mānavāḥ |
priyā bhavanti lokasya sve sve karmaṇy avasthitāḥ || 8.42 ||
yasmāt svānīti | jātikuladeśadharmāṇy anutiṣṭhantaḥ santo ‘pi niṣkarṣasnehāvidūre ‘pi vartamānā manuṣyāḥ sve sve karmany avasthitā parakarmāṇi paralokasya priyā bhavanti || 8.42 ||
notpādayet svayaṃ kāryaṃ rājā nānyasya kasyacid |
na ca prāpitam anyena grased arthaṃ kathaṃcana || 8.43 ||
evaṃ dhanaprasaṅgena prasaktānuprasaktikayābhidhāyādhunā prākṛtam āha | notpādayet svayaṃ kāryaṃ rājā nānyasya kasyacid iti | yady api paśyet karyāṇi kāryiṇām ity uktaṃ bhavati tathāpi tadākṣepāntareṇa ṛṇādānādirājakartavyasāhasādhirakṣaṇavarjiteṣu rājā svayam upajāpena vyavahārān notpādayet | nāpi rājādhikṛtaḥ svayam utpādayet tathā pratyarthinām api tadā veditavyaṃ kāryārthaṃ kenāpi prakāreṇa rāgadveṣādinā nopekṣeta || 8.43 ||
yathā nayaty asṛkpātair mṛgasya mṛgayuḥ padam |
nayet tathānumānena dharmasya nṛpatiḥ padam || 8.44 ||
yatheti | yathā mṛgasya viddhasya sato dṛṣṭinaṣṭasya padaṃ vyādho rudhirasrāvabindudarśanena nayati tathā rājānumānapramāṇena dharmasya gatiṃ nayet | vidvadbhiḥ dharmapradhānair dvijātibhir yac chāstrāviruddham āsevitaṃ tad deśakulajātīnām atra rūpaṃ kalpayet | vādivādyāśayagrahaṇāya yatnādiśayaṃ kuryāt ity evamartham || 8.44 ||
satyam arthaṃ ca saṃpaśyed ātmānam atha sākṣiṇam |
deśaṃ rūpaṃ ca kālaṃ ca vyavahāravidhau sthitaḥ || 8.45 ||
satyam iti | vyavahārakarmapravṛtto rājā satyaṃ draṣṭādraṣṭahetutayā saṃpaśyet | arthaṃ ca sārāsāratvena ātmānaṃ ca sadasatkarmaṇā, deśaṃ ca samanantaraṃ rājāntaraṃ vihāraṃ kālaṃ ca jīvitasaṃbandham atikrāntabalaṃ rūpaṃ cedaṃ vyādhijarābhojyaprāyaṃ lobhādināskhalanārthaṃ saṃpaśyet || 8.45 ||
sadbhir ācaritaṃ yat syād dhārmikaiś ca dvijātibhiḥ |
tad deśakulajātīnām aviruddhaṃ prakalpayet || 8.46 ||
sadbhir iti | etac ca viruddhavyavahāreṣu vādinaṃ gṛhṇīyād iha ca sadbhir ācaritam ity upādānād idaṃ sadācāraviṣayaṃ jātijānapadādy etat punaḥ sadcāravyatiriktadharmaviṣayam || 846 ||
adhamaṇārthasiddhyartham uttamarṇena coditaḥ |
dāpayed dhikasyārtham adhamarṇād vibhāvitam || 8.47 ||
evaṃ sakalavyavahārasādhāraṇaparibhāṣātmakam uktvādhunā ṛṇādānādy adhikṛtyāha adhamarṇārthsiddhyartham iti | dāpayed dhanikasyārtham adhamarṇavibhāvitam | adhamarṇena dhanagrāhiṇā gṛhītaṃ tatprāptyarthaṃ dhanasvāminā rājñā jñāpitaḥ san vakṣyamāṇalekhyādinā pratipāditaṃ dhanaṃ dhanasvāmino ‘dhamarṇaṃ dāpayet ity uttaraprapañcasyaivāyaṃ sāmānyanirdeśaḥ || 8.47 ||
yair yair upāyair arthe svaṃ prāpnuyād uttamarṇikaḥ |
tais tair upāyaiḥ saṃgṛhya dāpayed adhamarṇikam || 8.48 ||
yair yair iti | uttamarṇo yair yair upāyaiḥ svadhanaṃ labhate tais tair upāyaiḥ adhamarṇaṃ nigṛhya tam arthaṃ dāpayet || 8.48 ||
dharmeṇa vyavahāreṇa chalenācaritena ca |
prayuktaṃ sādhayed arthaṃ pañcamena balena ca || 8.49 ||
kaiḥ punas tair ity ata āha dharmeṇeti | yad adhamarṇasya prayuktaṃ dhanaṃ tad dharmeṇa nītyā vyavahāreṇa vakṣyamāṇena chalenopādhipūrvakaṃ pratidravyagrahaṇenācaritenābhojanādinā balena gṛhāvarodhādinā paṇcamenottamarṇaṃ sādhayet || 8.49 ||
yaḥ svayaṃ sādhayed artham uttamarṇo ‘dhamarṇikāt |
na sa rājñābhiyoktavyaḥ svakaṃ saṃsādhayan dhanam || 8.50 ||
svayaṃ ya iti | uttamarṇo vyavahāram antareṇa dharmādibhir upāyair vā naraiḥ svaṃ rājanirapekṣam adhamarṇāt dhanaṃ sādhayet | sa ātmadhanaṃ sādhayan madanapekṣaṃ bandhanādikṛtavān asīti evaṃ rājā na kṣeptavyaḥ || 8.50 ||
arthe ‘pavyayamānaṃ tu karaṇena vibhāvitam |
dāpayed dhanikasyārthaṃ daṇḍaleśaṃ c śaktitaḥ || 8.51 ||
arthe ‘pavyayamānaṃ tu karaṇena vibhāvayed iti | nāham asmai dhārayiṣyāmīty evaṃ dhane vipratipadyamānam adhamarṇaṃ karaṇenālikhyasākṣipathātmakena pratipāditam uttamarṇasya dhanaṃ dāpayet || 8.51 ||
apahnave ‘dhamarṇasya dehīty uktasya saṃsadi |
abhiyokādiśed deśyaṃ kāraṇaṃ vānyad uddiśet || 8.52 ||
apahnava iti | abhiyoktā diśed deśyaṃ karaṇaṃ vānyam uddiśet | adhamarṇasya dehīty uttmarṇasya dhanam ity evaṃ sabhāyāṃ prāḍvivākenoktaś ca na dhārayāmīty evam apalāpe saty ākṣepakrodhagrahaṇadeśaṃ tatratryasākṣyupanyāsādyapahnavatvāt karaṇaṃ śaṃset || 8.52 ||
adeśaṃ yaś ca diśet nirdiśyāpahnute ca yaḥ |
yaś cādharottarān arthān vigītān nāvabudhyate || 8.53 ||
apadiśyāpadeśyaṃ ca punar yas tv apadhāvati |
samyakpraṇihitaṃ cārthaṃ pṛṣṭaḥ san nābhinandati || 8.54 ||
asaṃbhāṣye sākṣibhiś ca deśe saṃbhāṣate mithaḥ |
nirucyamānaṃ praśnaṃ ca necched yaś cāpi niṣpatet || 8.55 ||
brūhīty uktaś ca na brūyād uktaṃ ca na vibhāvayet |
na ca pūrvāparaṃ vidyāt tasmād arthāt sa hīyate || 8.56 ||
adeśaṃ yaś ca diśati nirdiśyāpahnavīti ceti | apadiśyeti | asaṃbhāṣyeti | brūhīti | abhiyoktā diśed ity uktaṃ tatra yo ‘rthī tathāvidhaṃ deśam apahnavādikaṃ nirdiśati yatra dānagrahaṇe na saṃbhāvyete adhamarṇasya vā tadānīṃ tatrāpasthānaṃ saṃbhavatīti yaś cādeśādikaṃ nirdiśya na tan mayā nirdiṣṭam ity evaṃ paścād apajānīte yo vā pūrvaṃ paścād uktān arthān parasparaviruddhān nāvagacchati yaś cānena mama hastāt suvarṇaṃ gṛhītam ity evaṃ nirdeṣṭavyaṃ nirdiśya etat patreṇa gṛhītam ity evamādinā punar apasarati yo vā pratijñākālopanyastam arthaṃ kasmāt punas tathā rātryādau dānagrahaṇaṃ kṛtam ity evamādirūpeṇa pūrvapakṣavistārakāle prāḍvivākena samyak pṛṣṭaḥ sann antardhatte yaś ca saṃbhāṣaṇānarhe upahārādau deśe sākṣibhiḥ parasparaṃ bhāṣate yaś ca sākṣipraśnaṃ kriyamāṇaṃ no gacchati vā sabhāntaḥ prativādisaṃnihitahaṭhād utplutya gacchati yathāsmin pūrvapakṣe kiṃ brūhīty ukta uttaraṃ na brūyāt yo vā pratijñātaṃ sākṣibhir na vibhāvayet pūrvaṃ vā sākṣigataṃ na jānīyāt svagatasvayaṃ cādharottarāṇi vyaktatvāt sa tasmād vyavahārād dhīyate || 8.53–56 ||
sākṣiṇaḥ santi mety uktvā diśety ukto diśen na yaḥ |
dharmasthaḥ kāraṇair etair hīnaṃ tam iti nirdiśet || 8.57 ||
sākṣiṇa iti | sākṣiṇo ‘tra mama vidyante ity uktvā brūhi tān ity ukto yasmān na brūyāt taṃ pūrvoktair ebhiḥ parājayakāraṇaiḥ dharmanirṇayapravṛttaḥ prādvivāko jito ‘ym iti kathayet
|| 8.57 ||
abhiyoktā na ced brūyād vadhyo daṇḍyaś ca dharmataḥ |
na cet tripakṣāt prabrūyād dharmaṃ prati parājitaḥ || 8.58 ||
abhiyokteti | yo ‘rthī sadya eva pūrvapakṣaṃ na brūyāt tadā dharmavyatikramatāratamyāpekṣayā vadhārho daṇḍanīyaś ca syāt | ākṣiptaḥ punaḥ pakṣatraye yadi na brūyāt tadā dharmaviṣaye parājito ‘nyāyyapravṛttatvenādhārmikatvāt || 8.58 ||
yo yāvan nihnuvītārthaṃ mithyā yāvati vā vadet |
tau nṛpeṇa hy adharmajñau dāpyau taddviguṇaṃ damam || 8.59 ||
ya iti | yaḥ pratyarthī yatparimāṇaṃ dhanam apajānīte yatparimāṇaṃ vā dhanam arthī mithyā kathayati adharmapravṛttau tasmād apahnutān mithyoktāc ca dhanāt dviguṇaṃ daṇḍārthaṃ dāpanīyaḥ | adharmajñāv iti vacanād idam atyantābhiniveśādipravṛttaviṣayam | abhiniveśe tv apahnave taddviguṇam api daśakaṃ śataṃ vakṣyati || 8.59 ||
pṛṣṭo ‘pavyayamānas tu kṛtāvastho dhanaiṣiṇā |
tryavaraiḥ sākṣibhir bhāvyo nṛpabrāhmaṇasaṃnidhau || 8.60 ||
pṛṣṭa iti | pratyarthī dhanārthinottamarṇena kṛtarājapuruṣakarṣaṇādiparibhavaḥ prāḍvivākena pṛṣṭo yadāvadhārayāmīty evam apahnavāno bhavati tadā rājādhikṛtabrāhmaṇasamakṣaṃ yady atyantanyūnais tataḥ tribhiḥ sākṣibhiḥ tatra dhanārthinā bhāvanīyaḥ || 8.60 ||
yādṛśā dhanibhiḥ kāryā vyavahāreṣu sākṣiṇaḥ |
tādṛśān saṃpravakṣyāmi yathā vācyam ṛtaṃ ca taiḥ || 8.61 ||
yā iti | yathāvidhāḥ sākṣiṇa ṛṇādānādivyavahāreṣu uttamarṇādibhiḥ kartavyāḥ tathāvidhān kathayiṣyāmi yathā ca taiḥ satyaṃ kartavyaṃ tam api prakāram abhidhāsyāmi || 8.61 ||
gṛhiṇaḥ putriṇo maulāḥ kṣatraviṭśūdrayonayaḥ |
arthyuktāḥ sākṣyaṃ arhanti na ye kecid anāpadi || 8.62 ||
gṛhiṇa iti | gṛhasthāḥ putravantaḥ taddeśābhijanāḥ kṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrajātīyā arthinirdiṣṭāḥ santaḥ sākṣitvayogyā bhavanti | te ‘pi gṛhādiparibhavabhayān nānyathā bruvate | nanu ye kecid ṛṇādānād anapakṣe vyavahāre vāgdaṇḍapāruṣyādau punaḥ sākṣepa uktagṛhādivyatiriktā api bhavanti || 8.62 ||
āptāḥ sarveṣu varṇeṣu kāryāḥ kāryeṣu sākṣiṇaḥ |
sarvadharmavido ‘lubdhā viparītāṃs tu varjayet || 8.63 ||
āptā iti | sarvavarṇānāṃ vyavahāreṣv asaṃvādinaḥ sarvadharmaśāśtrajñā lobharahitāḥ sākṣinaḥ kāryāḥ | etadviparītān punar varjayed ity arthaḥ | siddhasya tu śabdābhidhānaṃ dāṇḍārtham
|| 8.63 ||
nārthasaṃbandhino nāptā na sahāyā na vairiṇaḥ |
na dṛṣṭadoṣāḥ kartavyā na vyādhyārtā na dūṣitāḥ || 8.64 ||
neti | sākṣiṇo bhayalobharāgadveṣasmṛtibhraṃśādinānyāyābhidhānāśaṅkāyāṃ na kāryāḥ | ṛṇādharmarṇasaṃbandhimitravairakṛtakauṭasākṣivyādhimūrcchitamahāvyādhikā dūṣitā na kāryāḥ || 8.64 ||
na sākṣī nṛpatiḥ kāryo na kārukakuśīlavau |
na śrotriyo na liṅgastho na saṅgebhyo vinirgataḥ || 8.65 ||
na sākṣīti | rājānantatvād vyavahāradṛṣṭatvāc ca śāstradharmeṇa ca praṣṭum aśakyatvān na sākṣī karyaḥ | sūpakāranaṭādayaś ca vedasaṃnyāsino gṛhasthā ete dharmaparā na kāryāḥ naṭādayaś ca prāyeṇārthaparāḥ svakarmagrastāś ca vyagrāś ca te na kāryāḥ | śrotriyabrahmacāritāpasabhikṣavaś ca liṅgasthāḥ saṅgavarjitā na kāryāḥ | śrotriyagrahaṇād adhyayanānuṣṭhānarahitasya brāhmaṇasya na niṣedhaḥ || 8.65 ||
nādhyadhīno na vaktavyo na dasyur na vikarmakṛt |
na vṛddho na śuśur naiko nāntyo na vikalendriyaḥ || 8.66 ||
nādhyadhīna iti | adhyadhīno garbhadāso ‘tyantaparatvān na kāryaḥ | na vaktavyasarvakarmadoṣād garhaṇīyaḥ yaḥ kutsāditarata eva vacanādidasyur bhṛtakasatyudāsa iti karmāṇīti nairuktāt so ‘pi pāratantryān na kāryaḥ | taskarasya tathābhidhāsyamānatvāt pratiṣiddhakarmakṛdanāsvāsāt na vṛddho ‘tikrāntavyavahārakatvāt |  na śiśuḥ avyavahārakatvāt | eko ‘pi na nāśapravāsāśaṅkayā tasya tryavarair iti niṣedhasiddhau kasyāṃcid avasthāyāṃ dvayor abhyanujñānārthavacanam | antyaś cāṇḍālādiḥ dharmānabhijñatvāt | vikalendriya upalabdhivaikalyāt || 8.66 ||
nārto na matto nonmatto na kṣuttṛṣṇopapīḍitaḥ |
na śramārto na kāmārto na kruddho nāpi taskaraḥ || 8.67 ||
nārta iti | ārto bandhuvināśādinā matto madyādinā unmatto bhūtādyāviṣṭaḥ kṣudhā pipāsayā cākrāntaḥ śrānto ‘dhvagamanādikhinnaḥ kāmākrānta atyantakrodhabuddhivaikalyāt na kāryāś caurā adhārmikatvāt || 8.67 ||
strīṇāṃ sākṣyaṃ striyaḥ kuryur dvijānāṃ sadṛśā dvijāḥ |
śūdrāś ca santaḥ śūdrāṇām antyānām antyayonayaḥ || 8.68 ||
strīṇām iti | strīṇāṃ striyaḥ sākṣiṇo bhavantīti anyāgocararahasyaprāyaḥ strīkāryaviṣayam idam | tatra puṃsaḥ sākṣitve ko yasmān na gocara iti nityābhidhānāśaṅkā syāt | anyatra ṛṇādānādivyavahāre tāsāṃ pūrvoktā eva sākṣiṇo bhavantīty eva dvijānām ity evamādāv api draṣṭavyam | dvijānāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ samānajātiguṇā dvijāḥ śūdrāṇāṃ sādhavaḥ śūdrāḥ cāṇḍālādīnāṃ tajjātīyā eva bhavanti || 8.68 ||
anubhāvī tu yaḥ kaścit kuryāt sākṣyaṃ vivādinām |
antarveśmany araṇye vā śarīrasyāpi cātyaye || 8.69 ||
anubhāvīti | yena gṛhābhyantare ratnādicaurakṛtopadrave śarīropaghāte cātatāyyādibhiḥ kriyamāṇe ṛṇādānādisvasthakāryavaśāc coktasākṣisampādanāya śakte yaḥ kaścid upalabdhavān sa vādināṃ sākṣī bhavati || 8.69 ||
striyāpy asaṃbhave kāryaṃ bālena sthavireṇa vā |
śiṣyeṇa bandhunā vāpi dāsena bhṛtakena vā || 8.70 ||
striyeti | antarveśmādau manuṣyābhāve sati strībālavṛddhaśiṣyabandhudāsakarmakarā api sākṣiṇo bhavanti || 8.70 ||
bālavṛddhāturāṇāṃ ca sākṣyeṣu vadatāṃ mṛṣā |
jānīyād asthirāṃ vācam utsiktamanasāṃ tathā || 8.71 ||
nanv asthirabuddhitvādikāraṇāvagame stryādīnāṃ sākṣyaṃ niṣiddham ato ‘trāpi kathaṃ tadvacanān niścaya ity ata āha bālavṛddhāturāṇām iti | bālavṛddhavyādhitānām aplutamanasāṃ ca mattonmattādīnām anṛtaṃ vadatām asthirā vāg bhavati atas tān sākṣye anumānena “bāhyair vibhāvayel liṅgaiḥ” iti jānīyāt || 8.71 ||
sāhaseṣu ca sarveṣu steyasaṃgrahaṇeṣu ca |
vāgdaṇḍayoś ca pāruṣye na parīkṣeta sākṣiṇaḥ || 8.72 ||
sahaseṣv iti | vastrapāṭanāgnidānādisarveṣu sāhaseṣu cauryastrīsaṃgrahaṇavāgdaṇḍapāruṣyeṣu ca gṛhasthaputradhanādinā sākṣiṇo na parīkṣeta | bālārthasaṃbandhādīnāṃ punar anumānaparīkṣā na niṣidyate | tathā sati nanv aniścayabhāvaḥ bālavṛddhāturānāṃ cetyādeś coktatvāt || 8.72 ||
bahutvaṃ parigṛhṇīyāt sākṣidvaidhe narādhipaḥ |
sameṣu tu guṇotkṛṣṭān guṇidvaidhe dvijottamān || 8.73 ||
yatrārthipratyarthinaḥ sākṣiṇas tatra bahutvam iti | sākṣiṇaḥ sarveṣu adhigatānāṃ parasparaviruddhabhāve sati bahubhiḥ yad uktaṃ tad evaṃ nirṇayārthatvena prāḍvivāko gṛhṇīyāt | sameṣu tūbhayor uktavatsu guṇavataḥ pramāṇī kuryāt | guṇavatāṃ vipratipattāv ubhayatra guṇavatsu brāhmaṇān pramāṇī kuryāt || 8.73 ||
samakṣadarśanāt sākṣyaṃ śravaṇāc caiva sidhyati |
tatra satyaṃ bruvan sākṣī dharmārthābhyāṃ na hīyate || 8.74 ||
samakṣadarśanād iti | cakṣurgrāhye sākṣād darśanāt śrotragrāhye ca sākṣāt sākṣyaṃ siddhyati tatra sākṣī satyam abhivadan dharmārthābhyāṃ na mucyate satyavacanadharmotpādād daṇḍābhāvaḥ || 8.74 ||
sākṣī dṛṣṭaśrutād anyad vibruvann āryasaṃsadi |
avāṅ narakam abhyeti pretya svarvāc ca hīyate || 8.75 ||
sākṣīti | sākṣī dṛṣṭaśrutaviparītaṃ brāhmaṇasabhāyāṃ viruddhaṃ vadann adhomukho narakaṃ vrajati | paraloke dharmāntarajanakāt svargādeś tatpāpapratibandhe sati pratihīyate || 8.75 ||
yatrānibaddho ‘pīkṣeta śruṇuyād vāpi kiṃcana |
puṣṭas tatrāpi tad brūyād yathādṛṣṭaṃ yathāśrutam || 8.76 ||
yatreti | tvaṃ me sākṣī syā ity evaṃ nibaddho ‘pi yatra kiṃcid ṛṇādānādi paśyed vākpāruṣyādi śṛṇuyāt tatrāpi pṛṣṭaḥ san sākṣī na hy arthopalabdhaṃ kathayet || 8.76 ||
eko lubdhas tv asākṣī syād bahavyaḥ śucyo ‘pi na striyaḥ |
strībuddher asthiratvāt tu doṣaiś cānye ‘pi ye vṛtāḥ || 8.77 ||
eko ‘ubdhas tv asākṣīti | lobhavān ekaḥ sākṣī na bhavaty evaṃ cālubdho ‘tyantaguaṇavān kayāṃcid avasthāyām eko bhavati | striyaś ca sthiracāpalyāt bahavyo ‘pi arthaśaucayuktā api sākṣiṇyo na bhavanty eva ṛṇādānādau svasthe vyavahāre ātyantike tūktaṃ striyo ‘py asaṃbhave kāryam ity evam anyato ‘pi ye yaiḥ aśucyādibhir doṣaiḥ prāptās te ‘pi svasthavyavahāre sākṣiṇo na bhaveyuḥ || 8.77 ||
svabhāvenaiva yad brūyus tad grāhyaṃ vyāvahārikam |
ato yad anyad vibrūyur dharmārthaṃ tad apārthakam || 8.78 ||
svabhāveneti | asākṣiṇaḥ svābhāvikaṃ satyarūpaṃ brūyuḥ | tadvyavahāranirṇayārthatvābhāvikāt punaḥ yad anyakāraṇārthaṃ viruddhaṃ brūyus tan niṣprayojanam ataś ca na grāhyam || 8.78 ||
sabhāntaḥ sākṣiṇaḥ prāptān arthipratyarthisaṃnidhau |
prāḍvivāko ‘nuyuñjīta vidhinānena sāntvayan || 8.79 ||
sabhāntar iti | sabhāmadhye sākṣiṇaḥ saṃprāptān arthipratyarthisamakṣaṃ rājādhikṛto brāhmaṇaḥ priyaṃ kurvan vakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇa pṛcchet || 8.79 ||
yad dvayor anayor vettha kārye ‘smiṃś ceṣṭitaṃ mithaḥ |
tad brūta sarvaṃ satyena yuṣmākaṃ hy atra sākṣitā || 8.80 ||
yad iti | asamyak vastu hy anayor arthipratyarthinor yat parasparaṃ vṛttaṃ jānīdhve yat sarvaṃ satyena kathaya | tad yuṣmākam atra sākṣāt draṣṭavyam || 8.80 ||
satyaṃ sākṣye bruvan sākṣī lokān āpnoty aninditān |
iha cānuttamāṃ kīrtiṃ vāg eṣā brahmapūjitā || 8.81 ||
satyam iti | sākṣyakarmaṇi satyaṃ kathayan sarvotkṛṭān brahmalokādīn ihaloke cāpy utkṛṣṭāṃ khyātiṃ prāpnoti | yasmād eṣā satyātmikā vāk prajāpatinā pūjitā || 8.81 ||
sākṣye ‘nṛtaṃ vadan pāśair badhyate vāruṇair bhṛśam |
vivaśaḥ śatam ājātīs tasmāt sākṣyaṃ vaded ṛtam || 8.82 ||
sākṣya iti | yasmāt sākṣyakarmaṇi sākṣy asatyaṃ kathayan varuṇasaṃbandhibhiḥ pāśaiḥ sarparajjubhiḥ paravaśaḥ san janmaśataṃ bādhyate ‘tyarthaṃ tasmāt sākṣye satyam eva vadet || 8.82 ||
satyena pūyate sākṣī dharmaḥ satyena vardhate |
tasmāt satyaṃ hi vaktavyaṃ sarvavarṇeṣu sākṣibhiḥ || 8.83 ||
satyeneti | yasmāt satyenānyasmād api pāpakarmaṇaḥ sākṣī mucyate | dharmaś cāsya satyābhidhānena vṛddhim eti | tasmāt sarvavarṇaviṣaye sākṣibhiḥ satyaṃ vaktavyam || 8.83 ||
ātmaiva hy ātmanaḥ sākṣī gatir ātmā tathātmanaḥ |
māvamaṃsthāḥ svam ātmānaṃ nṛṇāṃ sākṣiṇam uttamam || 8.84 ||
atmeti | yasmāt śubhāśubhakarmapravṛttasyātmanaḥ cātmaiva sākṣī tathātmaivātmanaḥ śaraṇaṃ tasmād enaṃ svam ātmānaṃ narāṇāṃ madhyād uttaraṃ sākṣiṇam asatyābhidhānena bhāvajñāsīḥ kena mamaitad dṛṣṭamity evaṃ buddhiṃ kṛtvā, yata ātmā tavātra sarvasākṣy uttamaḥ sākṣī vidyate ekasyaivātmanaḥ karmakartavyāpadeśo ‘satyanivṛttyarthaḥ || 8.84 ||
manyante vai pāpakṛto na kaścit paśyatīti naḥ |
tāṃs tu devāḥ prapaśyanti svasyaivāntarapūruṣaḥ || 8.85 ||
mayante ‘mīti | tāṃs tu devāḥ prapaśyanti svasyaivāntarapūruṣaḥ | asmān na kaścit paśyatīty evaṃ pāpavṛttāvabudhyante na punar vakṣyamāṇā devāḥ paśyanti svaś cāntrātmā paśyati || 8.85 ||
dyaur bhūmir āpo hṛdayaṃ candrārkāgniyamānilāḥ |
rātriḥ saṃdhye ca dharmaś ca vṛttajñāḥ sarvadehinām || 8.86 ||
dyair iti | dyuloke pṛthivyudakacandramādityāgnivāyurātrisaṃdhyādharmāṇām itihāsādidṛṣṭyā vigrahavad rūpasadbhāvā ete hṛdayāyatanaś cātmā yamaś ca sarvaśarīriṇām ācaritaṃ jānanti
|| 8.86 ||
devabrāhmaṇasāṃnidhye sākṣyaṃ pṛcched ṛtaṃ dvijān |
udaṅmukhān prāṅmukhān vā pūrvāhṇe vai śuciḥ śucīn || 8.87 ||
devabrāhmaṇasāṃnidhya iti | ālekhyādivinyastoktadevasaṃnidhāne brāhmaṇānāṃ samakṣaṃ sabhyānāṃ yatnakaraṇena satyarūpatām āpannaṃ sākṣyaṃ dvijātīn prayatān uttarābhimukhān pūrvābhimukhān vā prayataḥ san prāḍvivākaḥ pūrvāhṇakāle tu pṛcchet || 8.87 ||
brūhīti brāhmaṇaṃ pṛcchet satyaṃ brūhīti pārthivam |
gobījakāñcanair vaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ sarvais tu pātakaiḥ || 8.88 ||
brūhīti | gobījakāñcanair vaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ sarvais tu pātakaiḥ | brūhīty evaṃ śabdam uccārya brāhmaṇaṃ pṛcchet | satyam ity evam abhidhāya kṣatriyam | gobījasuvarṇāpahāre yat pātakaṃ tavānṛtavadane syād ity evaṃ vaiśyam | śūdraṃ vakṣyamāṇaiḥ pāpapratipādakaiḥ śabdaiḥ pṛcchet || 8.88 ||
brahmaghno ye smṛtā lokā ye ca strībālaghātinaḥ |
mitradruhaḥ kṛtaghnasya te te syur bruvato mṛṣā || 8.89 ||
brahmaghnānāṃ smṛtā lokā ye ca strībālaghātinām iti | brāhmaṇastrībālaghātināṃ ye narakādilokā ṛṣibhiḥ smṛtā ye cātrāpakāriṇāṃ ye ca kṛthāpakāriṇaḥ te tava ca mithāvadato bhaveyuḥ || 8.89 ||
janmaprabhṛti yat kiṃcit puṇyaṃ bhadra tvayā kṛtam |
tat te sarvaṃ śuno gacchet yadi brūyās tvam anyathā || 8.90 ||
janmaprabhṛtīti | he kalyāṇa bāla janmaprabhṛti yat kiṃcit puṇyaṃ kṛtaṃ tat sarvaṃ tava saṃbandhi śunaḥ phaladaṃ brūyāt yadi tvam asatyaṃ kathayase || 8.90 ||
eko ‘ham asmīty ātmānaṃ yas tvaṃ kalyāṇa manyase |
nityaṃ sthitas te hṛdy eṣa puṇyapāpekṣitā muniḥ || 8.91 ||
eka iti | he bhadra eka evāhaṃ bhavān śarīramātrātmaka eva aham ity evaṃrūpam ātmānaṃ yas tvaṃ budhyase tan maivaṃ maṃsthāḥ | yasmād eva puṇyapāpānāṃ draṣṭā munisvarūpato ‘tyantaśuddharūpas tava hṛdaye nityam avasthitaḥ || 8.91 ||
yamo vaivasvato devo yas tavaiṣa hṛdi sthitaḥ |
tena ced avivādas te mā gaṅgāṃ mā kurūn gamaḥ || 8.92 ||
yama iti | sarvabhūtamayaḥ savitā vivasvat putro yamākhyo devo ‘yaṃ lokataḥ karṇagocarībhūtatvāt tava hṛdaye parisphurati tena saha yavitavādharmakāritvābhāvād vivādo nāsti tadā pāpanirharaṇārthaṃ mā gaṅgāṃ kurukṣetraṃ yāsīḥ || 9.92 ||
nagno muṇḍaḥ kapālī ca bhikṣārthī kṣutpipāsitaḥ |
andhaḥ śatrugṛhaṃ gacced yaḥ sākṣyam anṛtaṃ vadet || 8.93 ||
nagna iti | andhaḥ śatrugṛhaṃ gacched iti | yaḥ sākṣyam anṛtaṃ brūyāt sa nagnaḥ kṛtakeśamuṇḍanaḥ paribhāvād andhaḥ kṣuttṛṣṇārtaḥ karpaṭeṇa yukto bhikṣārthaṃ śatrugṛhaṃ gacched iti sākṣimātraviṣayatvenaitad ucyate na śūdrapraśnārthaṃ madhyapuruṣanirdeśād ete śapathā babhūvuḥ || 8.93 ||
avākśirās tamasy andhe kilbiṣī narakaṃ vrajet |
yaḥ praśnaṃ vitathaṃ brūyāt pṛṣṭaḥ san dharmaniścaye || 8.94 ||
avāg iti | yo dharmaniścayanimittaṃ pṛṣṭaḥ san asatyaṃ praśnaṃ brūyāt sa pāpī adhomukho ‘tisāndre tamasi narake patet || 8.94 ||
andho matsyān ivāśnāti sa naraḥ kaṇṭakaiḥ saha |
yo bhāṣate ‘rthavaikalyam apratyakṣaṃ sabhāṃ gataḥ || 8.95 ||
andha iti | yaḥ sabhāṃ prāptaḥ tat tv asya vipralabdhaṃ mukhalekhabhrāntyā cādṛṣṭaṃ bhāṣate so ‘ndha iva sakaṇṭakān matsyān bhakṣayati saḥ | sukhahetor vartamāno duḥkham evātiśayaṃ tad upārjayati || 8.95 ||
yasya vidvān hi vadataḥ kṣetrajño nātiśaṅkate |
tasmin na devāḥ śreyāṃsaṃ loke ‘nyaṃ puruṣaṃ viduḥ || 8.96 ||
yasyeti | yasya vadataḥ śubhāśubhaṃ satyaṃ vadati utānṛtam ity evaṃ sāśaṅko na bhavaty api niścitam evārthaṃ satyaṃ vaktīty evaṃ nirviśaṅkas tasmāt puruṣād anyaṃ praśasyataraṃ puruṣaṃ devā na manyanta ity arthaḥ || 8.96 ||
yāvato bāndhavān yasmin hanti sākṣye ‘nṛtaṃ vadan |
tāvataḥ saṃkhyayā tasmin śṛṇu saumyānupūrvaśaḥ || 8.97 ||
yāvanta iti | yasmin paśvādinimitte sākṣye ‘nṛtaṃ bruvan yatsaṃkhyākān mitrādibāndhavān narakeṇa yojayati tasmin sākṣye tatsaṃkhyākān krameṇa saṃkhyayā mayocyamānān śṛṇuta ity ataś ca narakapātanimittācaraṇas tasyāpi narakaṃ bhavati ity uktaṃ bhavati, tatra cānṛtavādibāndhavāḥ svārjitakarmacarān narakam upayanti | akṛtābhyāgamakṛtavipraṇāśacodyāpattir yathā viṣādinā kvacit kiṃcid vyāpādayati tatra prāgjanmārjitakarmavaśato ‘sau tathā mriyate yathā yam api taṃ nimittabhāvam upagacchan phale cātra nirālambanārthavādamātraparyavasāyitā yuktā apramādābhidhā iti || 8.97 ||
pañca paśvanṛte hanti daśa hanti gavānṛte |
śatam aśvānṛte hanti sahasraṃ puruṣānṛte || 8.98 ||
pañceti | paśuviṣaye ‘nṛte pañcabāndhavān narakeṇa yojayati daśa goviṣaye śatam aśvaviṣaye sahasraṃ puruṣaviṣaye || 8.98 ||
hanti jātān ajātāṃś ca hiraṇyārthe ‘nṛtaṃ vadan |
sarvaṃ bhūmyanṛte hanti mā sma bhūmyanṛtaṃ vadīḥ || 8.99 ||
hantīti | hiraṇyaviṣaye ‘nṛtaṃ vadan jātān pitrādīn anujātāṃś ca putrādīn ātmānṛtatvān narake yajayati | bhūmiviṣaye cānṛtaṃ vadan sarvaprāṇijātaṃ yojayati, tasmād bhūmiviṣaye ‘nṛtaṃ na vadet || 8.99 ||
apsu bhūmivad ity āhuḥ strīṇāṃ bhoge ca maithune |
abjeṣu caiva ratneṣu sarveṣv aśmamayeṣu ca || 8.100 ||
apsv iti | udakaviṣaye ‘nṛte strīṇāṃ bhogeṣu viṣaye abjeṣu padmarāgādiṣu pāṣāṇamayeṣu ca sarvavaiḍūryadiṣu anṛte bhūmivad doṣaṃ āhuḥ sarvaṃ hantīti || 8.100 ||
etān doṣān avekṣya tvaṃ sarvān anṛtabhāṣaṇe |
yathāśrutaṃ yathādṛṣṭaṃ sarvam evāñjasā vada || 8.101 ||
etān iti | etānsarvān anṛtabhāṣaṇe doṣān jñātvā dṛṣṭaśrutānatikrameṇa sarvam etat tattvato brūhi || 8.101 ||
gorakṣakān vāṇijakāṃs tathā kārukuśīlavān |
preṣyān vārddhuṣikāṃś caiva viprān śūdravad ācaret || 8.102 ||
gorakṣakān iti | gorakṣakavāṇijyasūpakārādikārukarmadāsyapratiṣiddhajīvino brāhmaṇān sākṣipraśne śūdravat pṛcchet || 8.102 ||
tad vadan dharmato ‘rtheṣu jājann apy anyathā naraḥ |
na svargāc cyavate lokād daivīṃ vācaṃ vadanti tām || 8.103 ||
tad vadann iti | tad etat sākṣyam anyathā jānann api manuṣyo dharmeṇa dayayā hetubhūtayopādhirahito vyavahāreṣv anyathā bruvan na svargāt lokāt bhraṣyati yasmād etannimittaviśeṣāṇām anṛtābhidhānam etāṃ daivasaṃbandhinīṃ vācaṃ manvādayo vadanti | anye tu prāḍvivākaviṣayām idaṃ vyācakṣate prāḍvivāko gorakṣakādiṣu brāhmaṇeṣu sacchūdrapraśnaṃ kurvan na dharmahāniṃ prāpnoti || 8.103 ||
śūdraviṭkṣatraviprāṇāṃ yatrartokau bhaved vadhaḥ |
tatra vaktavyam anṛtaṃ tad dhi satyād viśiṣyate || 8.104 ||
kva punas tad anṛtaṃ vaktavyam ity ata āha śūdraviṭkṣatraviprāṇāṃ yatra caiva bhaved vadha iti | yatra tatprāṇasaṃrakṣaṇenedaṃ cātyantadhārmikapramādaskhalitapuruṣaviṣayaṃ na tv atyantādhārmikaviṣayam, tathā ca gotamo nānṛtavacane doṣo jīvanaṃ vet tadadhīnaṃ na tu pāyīyaso jīvanam ityādiviṣaye pramāṇāntareṇa rājā nirṇayaṃ kuryāt na sākṣibhiḥ na pramāṇāntarān na caivānumānādinātyantaṃ sākṣipariṅṣāṃ kuryāt | na jātu brāhmaṇaṃ hanyād iti vacanāt kathaṃ brāhmaṇasya vadha āpated ity āśaṅkanīyaṃ kṛtadaṇḍatvād rājñaḥ kathāṃ saṃbhavād vyavahāro ‘nyatrāpi caivaṃvidhe viṣaye ‘nṛtasyeṣyamāṇatvāt vadhasyāmaṅgalyatvāc chūdrādikrameṇa nirdeśaḥ || 8.104 ||
vāgdaivatyaiś ca carubhir yajetaṃs te sarasvatīm |
anṛtasyainasas tasya kurvāṇā niṣkṛtiṃ parām || 8.105 ||
vāgdaivatyair iti | vāg vai sarasvatīti śruter vāksarasvatyor aikyād vāgdevatākaiś carubhiḥ sarasvatīṃ te sākṣiṇo ‘nṛtābhidhāyinas tasyāsatyasya yat pāpaṃ tasya prakṛṣṭāṃ saṃsiddhiṃ kurvāṇā yajeran | atraike sākṣiṇaḥ api kapiñjalavac carutrayaṃ āhus tad asad devaś ced varṣe bahavo brāhmaṇā yajeran iti | ity etat prāyaścittaṃ kartṛbahutvāpekṣayā carubhir ity bahuvacanaṃ vrātyastomavad eṣāṃ saṃpādya yāgās cotpāditāḥ | atra prāyaścittasya prakaraṇotkarṣo lādhavā tatra hi kriyamāṇe viṭkṣatriyaviṣayān ṛtābhidhāne vṛddha etat kartavyaṃ syāt || 8.105 ||
kūṣmāṇḍair vāpi juhuyād ghṛtam agnau yathāvidhi |
ud ity ṛcā vā vāruṇyā tryṛcenābdaivatena vā || 8.106 ||
kūṣmāṇḍair iti | yad devā devaheḍanam ity ebhir mantradevatākair ghṛam agnau paristaraṇādihomavidhinā juhuyāt | ud uttamaṃ varuṇadevatākayā ṛcā āpo hi ṣṭety anenābdevatākena juhuyāt || 8.106 ||
tripakṣād bruvan sākṣyam ṛṇādiṣu naro ‘gadaḥ |
tad ṛṇaṃ prāpnuyāt sarvaṃ daśabandhaṃ ca sarvataḥ || 8.107 ||
tripakṣān iti | avyādhitaḥ sākṣī ṛṇādānādivyavahāreṣu pakṣatrayaṃ yāvat yadi sākṣyaṃ na dadyāt tadā tadvivādāspadībhūtasarvam ṛṇam apy uttamarṇasya dadyāt | tasmāc ca ṛṇāt sarvasmād daśamaṃ bhāgaṃ rājño daṇḍaṃ dadyāt || 8.107 ||
yasya dṛśyeta saptāhād uktavākyasya sākṣiṇaḥ |
rogo ‘gnir jñātimaraṇam ṛṇaṃ dāpyo damaṃ ca saḥ || 8.108 ||
yasyeti | yasya sākṣiṇa uktasākṣyasya saptāhasya madhyān nimittāntaraṃ vināpi vyādhyagnibandhudāhamaraṇānām anyatamam utpadyate sadaiva pratipāditamithyābhāvāt tādṛśadaṇḍaṃ dāpyaḥ ṛṇaṃ ca || 8.108 ||
asākṣikeṣu tv artheṣu mitho vivadamānayoḥ |
avindaṃs tattvataḥ satyaṃ śapathenāpi lambhayet || 8.109 ||
asākṣikeṣv iti | avidyamānasākṣike parasparaṃ vivadamānayoḥ vādinos tattvena satyam alabhamānaḥ prāḍvivākaḥ śapathena vakṣyamāṇena satyam uddharet || 8.109 ||
maharṣibhiś ca devaiś ca kāryārthaṃ śapathāḥ kṛtāḥ |
vasiṣṭhaś cāpi śapathaṃ śepe praijavane nṛpe || 8.110 ||
maharṣibhir iti | maharṣibhir agastyādibhir viśastainyād devaiś candrādibhiḥ saṃdigdhakāryanirṇayārthaṃ śapathā vṛtāḥ | vasiṣṭho ‘pi anena putraśataṃ bhakṣitam ity evaṃ viśvāmitrākṛṣṭaḥ pijavanāpatyai sudāse rājani patiśuddhyarthaṃ sapathaṃ cakāra | na vṛthā śapathaṃ cakāra || 8.110 ||
na vṛthā śapathaṃ kuryāt svalpe ‘py arthe naro budhaḥ |
vṛthā hi śapathaṃ kurvan pretya ceha ca naśyati || 8.111 ||
neti | svalpe ‘pi kārye vidvān mithyāśapathaṃ na kuryāt | mithyāśapathaṃ kurvan ihalokaparalokayor akīrtiḥ, narakāvāptyā ca naśyati || 8.111 ||
kāminīṣu vivāheṣu gavāṃ bhakṣye tathendhane |
brāhmaṇābhyupapattau ca śapathe nāsti pātakam || 8.112 ||
kāminīṣv iti | bahubhāryasya nānyām ahaṃ kāmaye tvaṃ me prāṇeśvarīty evaṃ vidhiviṣaye na ca tvayānyā voḍhavyā ity evaṃvidhe gavārthaṃ ghāsāpahāre vā saṃbhavād upāyāntara evaṃ śāstritāgnihomārthaṃ carvapahāre brāhmaṇarakṣāyāṃ ca dhanadānādiviṣayāyāṃ mithyāśapathe pātakaṃ na bhavati || 8.112 ||
satyena śāpayed vipraṃ kṣatriyaṃ vāhanāyudhaiḥ |
gobījakāñcanair vaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ sarvais tu pātakaiḥ || 8.113 ||
satyeneti | brāhmaṇaṃ vādinaṃ satyaśabdoccāraṇena śāpayet | kṣatriyaṃ ca vāhanāyudhaṃ niṣphalaṃ syād ity evam | vaiśyaṃ ca gobījakāñcanādīni te niṣphalāni syur ity evam | śūdraṃ sarvāṇi te pātakāni syur ity evaṃ śāpayet || 8.113 ||
agniṃ vāhārayed enam apsu cainaṃ nimajjayet |
putradārasya vāpy enaṃ śirāṃsi sparśayet pṛthak || 8.114 ||
kāryagurutvāpekṣayā ca agnim iti | agnivarṇasuvarṇapiṇḍaṃ śūdram aśvatthapatrāvṛtahastaṃ yājñavalkyādyuktyetikartavyatayā saptapadāni bhāvayet | apsu vā gṛhītvāntarjalaṃ viśed ity evamāditadetadyuktyetikartavyatayaiva nimajjayed atha vā putrāṇāṃ dārāṇāṃ ca etasya śalya ity evaṃ pṛthag ekaikasya śirāṃsi sparśayet || 8.114 ||
yam iddho na dahaty agnir āpo nonmajjayanti ca |
na cārttim ṛcchati kṣipraṃ sa jñeyaḥ śapathe śuciḥ || 8.115 ||
yam iti | dīpto ‘gnir yaṃ na dahati āpaś ca noplāvayanti na ca samanantaram eva nirnimittāṃ mūrcchāvasthāṃ prāpnoti sa śapateṣu vṛddho jñeyaḥ || 8.115 ||
vatsasya hy abhiśastasya purā bhrātrā yavīyasā |
nāgnir dadāha romāni satyena jagataḥ spṛśaḥ || 8.116 ||
ataḥ purākṛtārthavādam āha vatsasyeti | yasmāt pūrvasmin kāle ca samāpte ṛṣe brāhmaṇo ‘si śūdrāpura ity evaṃ vaimātreyeṇa kanīyasā bhrātrākruṣṭasya naitad evam iti śapathārtham agnipraviṣṭasya agniḥ sarvasya jagataḥ sadasatkartavyeṣu cārabhūtaḥ satyena hetubhūtena lomamātram api na dagdhavān || 8.116 ||
yasmin yasmin vivāde tu kauṭasākṣyaṃ kṛtaṃ bhavet |
tat tat kāryaṃ nivarteta kṛtaṃ cāpy akṛtaṃ bhavet || 8.117 ||
yasminn iti | yasmin yasmin vyavahāre sākṣibhiḥ anṛtam uktaṃ syāt tat tat kāryam apy akṛtaṃ eva prāḍvivāko nivartayet | yad api kṛtadaṇḍaparyantaṃ tad api punaḥ prīkṣayet || 8.117 ||
lobhān mohād bhayān maitrāt kāmāt krodhāt tathaiva ca |
ajñānād bālabhāvāc ca sakṣyaṃ vitatham ucyate || 8.118 ||
lobhād iti | lobhaviparītajñānabhayamaitrīkāmakrodhād ajñānamaurkhyaiḥ bālabhāvāt sākṣyam asatyam ucyate iti daṇḍavaicitryārtho ‘yam upanyāsaḥ || 8.118 ||
eṣām anyatame sthāne yaḥ sakṣyam anṛtaṃ vadet |
tasya daṇḍaviśeṣāṃs tu pravakṣyāmy anupūrvaśaḥ || 8.119 ||
eṣām iti | eṣāṃ lobhādīnām anyatamasmin nimitte sati yo ‘satyaṃ sākṣyaṃ brūyāt tasya daṇḍaviśeṣāt krameṇa vakṣyāmi || 8.119 ||
lobhāt sahasraṃ saṇḍyas tu mohāt pūrvaṃ tu sāhasam |
bhayād dvau madhyamau daṇḍau maitrāt pūrvaṃ caturguṇam || 8.120 ||
lobhād iti | lobhenānṛtābhidhāne sati paṇānāṃ vakṣyamāṇānāṃ sahasraṃ daṇḍyaḥ | mohena prathamasāhasaṃ vakṣyamāṇam | bhayena vakṣyamāṇasāhasau dvau | maitryāt prathamasāhasaṃ caturguṇam || 8.120 ||
kāmād daśaguṇaṃ pūrvaṃ krodhāt tu triguṇaṃ param |
ajñānād dve śate pūrṇe bāliśyāc chatam eva tu || 8.121 ||
