kāmād iti | kāmenānṛtaṃ vadan prathamasāhasaṃ daśaguṇaṃ daṇḍyaḥ | krodhenottamasāhasaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ triguṇam | ajñānena dve śate paṇe saṃpūrṇe maarkhyeṇa śatam eva ca || 8.121 ||
etān āhuḥ kauṭasākṣye proktān daṇḍān manīṣibhiḥ |
dharmasyāvyabhicārārtham adharmaniyamāya ca || 8.122 ||
etān iti | śāstravyavasthāyā aparilopārtham avyavasthāyāś cotpattivāraṇārtham etān kūṭasākṣiviṣaye cirantanair vidvadbhiḥ proktān daṇḍān manvādayaḥ prāhuḥ | etac ca sakṛtadhikaraṇe || 8.122 ||
kauṭasākṣyaṃ tu kurvāṇāṃs trīn varṇān dhārmiko nṛpaḥ |
pravāsayed daṇḍayitvā brāhmaṇaṃ tu vivāsayet || 8.123 ||
kūṭasākṣyam iti | kṣatriyādīn trīn varṇān kṛtakūṭasākṣyān pūrvoktena daṇḍena daṇḍayitvā dharmapradhāno rājā rāṣṭrān nirvāsayet | brāhmaṇaṃ punar daṇḍayitvā nagnīkuryāt || 8.123 ||
daśa sthānāni daṇḍasya manuḥ svāyaṃbhuvo ‘bravīt |
triṣu varṇeṣu yāni syur akṣato brāhmaṇo vrajet || 8.124 ||
daśeti  | hairaṇyagarbho manur daśadaṇḍasyāśramayān uktavān | ye kṣatriyādivarṇatrayaviṣaye bhavanti brāhmaṇaṃ punar mahaty api aparādhe akṣataśarīraṃ deśān nirvāsayet || 8.124 ||
upastham udaraṃ jihvā hastau pādau ca pañcamam |
cakṣur nāsā ca karṇau ca dhanaṃ dehas tathaiva ca || 8.125 ||
upastham iti | liṅgād etāni daṇdasthānāni tatas tadaṅgāparādhe aparādha bhūyiṣṭhāpekṣayā tat tad aṅgāparādhatānādikartavyam | alpāparādhe tu dhanadaṇḍa iti || 8.125 ||
anubandhaṃ parijñāya deśakālau ca tattvataḥ |
sārāparādhau cālokya daṇḍaṃ daṇḍyeṣu pātayet || 8.126 ||
anubandham iti | punaḥ punar icchāto ‘parādhakāraṇam avalokya grāmāraṇyādi cāparādhasthānam divārātryādikaṃ cāparādhakālaṃ samyag avekṣya sāraṃ cāparādhino dhanaśarīrādi sāmarthyam aparādhaṃ ca gurulaghubhāvenālokya daṇḍanīyeṣu daṇḍaṃ kuryāt itīdam uktānuktadaṇḍaśeṣabhūtam || 8.126 ||
adharmadaṇḍanaṃ loke yaśoghnaṃ kīrtināśanam |
asvargyaṃ ca paratrāpi tasmāt tat parivarjayet || 8.127 ||
adharmadaṇḍanam iti | yasmād anubandhādy anapekṣya daṇḍanam iha loke yaśaso jīvanakhyāter nāśam anṛtasya ca paraloke samanantarakapātāt karmāntarārjitasvargapratibandhakaṃ tasmāt tat pariharet || 8.127 ||
adaṇḍyān daṇḍayan rājā daṇḍyāṃś caivāpy adaṇḍayan |
ayaśo mahad āpnoti narakaṃ caiva gaccheti || 8.128 ||
adaṇḍyān iti | rājā daṇḍānarhān daṇḍayan daṇḍārhaś cotsñya mahad ayaśaḥ prāpnoti narakṃ ca gacchati || 8.128 ||
vāgdaṇḍaṃ prathamaṃ kuryād dhigdaṇḍaṃ tadanantaram |
tṛtīyaṃ dhanadaṇḍaṃ tu vadhadaṇḍam ataḥ param || 8.129 ||
vāgdaṇḍam iti | na sādhu kṛtavān asi naivaṃ bhūyaḥ kārṣīḥ ity evaṃ vāgnir bhartsanaṃ karmabhir adharmāparādhe guṇavataḥ kuryād iti | yadi tu tathā nopaśāmyati tadā dhigjālmatvām abhibhavati steyaṃ bhūyād ity evamādi tasya kāryaṃ tato ‘pi yadi tanmārgān na nivartate tadā dhanadāṇḍaṃ tṛtīyaṃ tasya kuryāt | evam api cen nāvatiṣṭhate ataḥ paraṃ vadhatāḍanāṅgacchedanādirūpaṃ tasya kuryāt || 8.129 ||
vadhenāpi yadā tv etān nigrahītuṃ na śaknuyat |
tadaiṣu sarvam apy etat prayuñjīta catuṣṭayam || 8.130 ||
vadheneti | yadā punas tānāṅgacchedanādināpi etān vaśīkartuṃ na śaknuyāt tadaiṣv aparādhiṣu etad dhigdaṇḍādi catuṣṭayam api sarvaṃ kuryat || 8.130 ||
lokasṃkhyāvyavahārthaṃ yāḥ saṃjñāḥ prathitā bhuvi |
tāmrarūpyasuvarṇānāṃ tāḥ pravakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ || 8.131 ||
lokasaṃvyavahārārtham iti | tāmrarūpyasuvarṇānāṃ paṇādisaṃjñā krayavikrayalokavyavahārārthaṃ pṛthivyāṃ viśeṣeṇābhidhāsyāmy upayogārthaṃ daṇḍādeḥ || 8.131 ||
jālāntaragate bhānau yat sūkṣmaṃ dṛśyate rajaḥ |
prathamaṃ tat pramāṇānāṃ trasareṇuṃ pracakṣate || 8.132 ||
trasareṇavo ‘ṣṭau vijñeyā likṣaikā parimāṇataḥ |
tā rājasarṣapas tisras te trayo gaurasarṣapaḥ || 8.133 ||
sarṣapāḥ ṣaṭ yavo madhyas triyavaṃ tv ekakṛṣṇalam |
pañcakṛṣṇaliko māṣas te suvarṇas tu ṣoḍaśa || 8.134 ||
palaṃ suvarṇāś catvāraḥ palāni dharaṇaṃ daśa |
dve kṛṣṇale samadhṛte vijñeyo rūpyamāṣakaḥ || 8.135 ||
te ṣoḍaś syād dharaṇaṃ purāṇaś caiva rājataḥ |
kārṣāpaṇas tu vijñeyas tāmrikaḥ kārṣikaḥ paṇaḥ || 8.136 ||
dharaṇāni daś vijñeyaḥ śatamānas tu rājataḥ |
catuḥsauvarṇiko niṣko vijñeyas tu pramāṇataḥ || 8.137 ||
jālāntaragata iti | trasareṇava iti | sarṣapā iti | palam iti | ta iti | dharaṇānīti | gavākṣādipraviṣṭābhir ādityaraśmiṣu yad rajo dṛśyate tat trasareṇuḥ | te ‘ṣṭau likṣaikā pramāṇena vijñeyā tāḥ likṣās tisro rājasarṣapaḥ | te trayaḥ gaurasarṣapaḥ | te ṣaṭ yavo madhyamaḥ na sthūlo nāpi sūkṣmaḥ tribhir yavaiḥ kṛṣṇalam | pañcabhiḥ kṛṣṇalaiḥ māṣaḥ | ṣoḍaśa māṣāḥ suvarṇaḥ | catvāraḥ suvarṇāḥ palam | daśapaṇā dharaṇam, kṛṣṇaladvayaṃ tulayā dhṛtaṃ rūpyamāṣako bodhavyaḥ | ṣoḍaśarūpyamayamāṣakāḥ rūpyaṃ dharaṇaṃ purāṇaṃ vocyate | rājato rūpyasambandhī karṣapramāṇaḥ tāmramayaḥ kārṣāpaṇaḥ iti vijñeyaḥ daśarūpyadharaṇāni rūpyaśatamāno boddhavyaḥ caturbhiḥ suvarṇair niṣkaḥ pramāṇena vijñeyaḥ || 8.132–137 ||
paṇānāṃ dve śate sārdhe prathamaḥ sāhasaḥ smṛtaḥ |
madhyamaḥ pañca vijñeyaḥ sahasraṃ tv eva cottamaḥ || 8.138 ||
paṇānām iti | sārdhe dve śate paṇānāṃ prathamaḥ sāhaso manvādibhiḥ mṛtaḥ, pañcapaṇaśatāni madhyamo vijñeyaḥ, sahasraṃ tv evaṃ cottamaḥ tataḥ sahasra eva || 8.138 ||
ṛṇaṃ deye pratijñāte pañcakaṃ śatam arhati |
apahnave taddviguṇaṃ tan manor anuśāsanam || 8.139 ||
ṛṇa iti | deyaṃ mayottamarṇasya ṛṇam ity evam adhamarṇena sabhāyām ukte saty asau adhamarṇaḥ paṇaśatāt pañcapaṇā ity evaṃ daṇḍam arhati | na kiṃcid asmai dārayāmīty evam apahnute sati daśakaśataṃ daṇḍam arhati | etan manusmṛtaṃ daṇḍam || 8.139 ||
vasiṣṭhavihitāṃ vṛddhiṃ sṛjed vittavivarddhinīm |
aśītibhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyān māsād vārdhuṣikaḥ śate || 8.140 ||
vasiṣṭhavihitām iti | vasiṣṭhoktāṃ vṛddhiṃ dharmatvād dhanavṛddhikarīṃ vṛddhijīvy utpādayet | sākalyata āha aśītibhāgam iti | śate prayukte māsād gatāt tasmāc chatād aśītibhāgaṃ vṛddhyarthaṃ gṛhṇīyāt || 8.140 ||
dvikaṃ śataṃ vā gṛhṇīyāt satāṃ dharmam anusmaran |
dvikaṃ śataṃ vā hi gṛhṇāno na bhavaty arthakilbiṣī || 8.141 ||
dvikam iti | paṇaśatād vā māsena dvau paṇau pūrvavṛddhyā ca jīvan sādhūnām arthadharmaḥ nādharma iti manyamāno gṛhṇīyāt | yasmāt dvikaṃ śataṃ gṛhṇan parasvādānapāpayukto na bhavati || 8.141 ||
dvikaṃ trikaṃ catuṣkaṃ ca pañcakaṃ ca śataṃ samam |
māsasya vṛddhiṃ gṛhṇīyād varṇānām anupūrvaśaḥ || 8.142 ||
dvikam iti | brāhmaṇādivarṇānāṃ krameṇa dvikatrikacatuṣkapañcaśataṃ samam anyūnādhikaṃ māsasya saṃbandhivṛddhiṃ gṛhṇīyāt || 8.142 ||
na tv evādhau sopakāre kausīdīṃ vṛddhim āpnuyāt |
na cādheḥ kālasaṃrodhān nisargo ‘sti na vikrayaḥ || 8.143 ||
na tv iti | bhojanārthabandhe datte dhanaprayogābhāvānantaroktā yā vṛddhis tām uttarmarṇo na labhate | na ca vṛddhidattasya bandhasya dviguṇībhūta dhanakālād ūrdhvam avyavasthānād anyatra bandha vetanatvenārtham asti nāpi vikrayo ‘pi dviguṇībhūtadhanakālād ūrdhvaṃ samokṣaṇād ādhir bhujyata eva anyathā dhanaprāptyabhāvāt || 8.143 ||
na bhoktavyā balād ādhir bhuñjāno vṛddhim utsṛjet |
mūlyena toṣayec cainam ādhisteno ‘nyathā bhavet || 8.144 ||
na bhoktavya iti | mā bhuṅkṣveti svāmivacanam avagaṇayan yadlābhād dhanaṃ na bhuñjīta tathā bhuñjāno vṛddhiṃ nāpnuyāt yadi bhogena bandhaḥ kṣayaṃ yātas tadā akṣatabandhasaṃbandhimūlyena bandhasvāminaṃ toṣayed anyathā bandhacauraḥ syāt || 8.144 ||
ādhiś copanidhiś cobhau na kālātyayam arhataḥ |
avahāryau bhavetāṃ tau dīrghakālam avasthitau || 8.145 ||
ādhir iti | bandhaḥ prītibhogyaś ca padārtha etau dvau kālātikramaṇaṃ nārhato ‘pi tu prāpte kāle svāminā pratyāharaṇīyau yasmāt tasmāt tau dīrghakālam anyahastāvasthitau ādhitvopanidhitvāt sākṣyādyabhāvena hārayituṃ śakyāv iti suhṛdupadeśarūpatayaivedaṃ na kadācid api hriyate ādhiḥsīmety apahārasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt || 8.145 ||
saṃprītyā bhujyamānāni na naśyanti kadācana |
dhenur uṣṭro vahann aśvo yaś ca damyaḥ prayujyate || 8.146 ||
saṃprītyeti | dogdhrī gaur uṣṭro vahaṃś cāśva ete prītyā anyonyaṃ bhujyamānā na kadācid api svāmino ‘pahāryante | aśvādidamanārthaṃ damanasya dīyate saṃprītyā bhujyamānānīti sāmānyopakramaviśeṣābhidhānam || 8.146 ||
yat kiṃcid daśavarṣāṇi saṃnidhau prekṣate dhanī |
bhujyamānaṃ parais tūṣṇīṃ na sa tal labdhum arhati || 8.147 ||
yad iti | yat kiṃcid dhanajātaṃ samakṣam evānyair daśavarṣāṇi bhujyamānaṃ dhanasvāmī tūṣṇīṃ pratīkṣate kim etan mā maivaṃ bhuṅkṣveti na pratibadhnāti nāsau tat punar nyāyena svīkartum arhati || 8.147 ||
ajaḍaś ced apogaṇḍo viṣaye cāsya bhujyate |
bhagnaṃ tad vyavahāreṇa bhoktā tad dhanam arhati || 8.148 ||
ajaḍa iti | nidhānasvāmī yadi buddhivikalo na bhavati bālo vā, asya ca tasminn eva deśe vyavasthitasyānyena tadīyaṃ bhujyate tad dhanaṃ vyavahāreṇa bhagnaṃ svāminas tatra vivādo na bhavati | yato bhoktaiva tad dhanam arhati | vyavahāreṇa bhagnam iti vacanād dharmeṇa tatsvāmina eva sarvadā svam evaṃ cāyaṃ pūrvaśeṣaślokaḥ || 8.148 ||
ādhiḥ sīmā bāladhanaṃ nikṣepopanidhiḥ striyaḥ |
rājasvaṃ śrotriyasvaṃ ca na bhogena praṇaśyati || 8.149 ||
ādhir iti | bandhaḥ kṣetragrāmādimaryādā bāladhanaṃ nikṣepaḥ | prītibhogyaṃ dāsyo rājaśrotriyadanādi uktena daśavarṣabhogyena svāmino nāpahārayituṃ cāsya daśavarṣaṃ bhogyadravyāpavādatvād bandhādīnāṃ cālpakālabhogyatvena nehānanupraveśāśaṅkāsthānaṃ prītyā bhujyamānānīti pṛthag vacanam || 8.149 ||
yaḥ svāminānanujñātam ādhiṃ bhuṅkte vicakṣaṇaḥ |
tenārdhavṛddhir bhoktavyā tasya bhogasya niṣkṛtiḥ || 8.150 ||
ya iti | yo vṛddhidattaṃ dhanaṃ bhogenāvinaśyarūpaṃ svāminānanujñāto mūrkho rahasi bhuṅkte balabhogena bhoktavyo balāvadhir ityuktāntena tasya śuddhyartham ardhavṛddhis tyaktavyā || 8.150 ||
kusīdavṛddhir dvaiguṇyaṃ nātyeti sakṛdāhitā |
dhānye sade lave vāhye nātikrāmati pañcatām || 8.151 ||
kusīdavṛddhir iti | yā vṛddhyarthaprayuktadhanasaṃbandhinī vṛddhir atibhūyasyakāle gate sakṛdadhamarṇād ānītā śanaiḥ śanaiḥ māsaṃ pratyakṣaṃ vāsau dvaiguṇyaṃ nātikrāmati mūladviguṇaiva bhavati na tato ‘dhikā labhyate dhānye punar vṛddhiprayukte sahe vā vṛkṣaphale lave vā ūrṇamaye vāhanīye ca balīvardādau prayukte bhūyasy api kālagate sakṛd adhamarṇād āhṛtā satī mūladhānyādinā saha pañcaguṇatāṃ nātikrāmati || 8.151 ||
kṛtānusārād adhikā vyatiriktā na siddhyati |
kusīdapatham āhus taṃ pañcakaṃ śatam arhati || 8.152 ||
kṛtānusārād iti | gṛhītṛnṛgatam arthitāviśeṣam uttamarṇena vijñāya uktavṛddhir adhikā vṛddhiḥ kṛtā satīyataḥ śāstrabāhyā sā ca no siddhyati | pratiṣiddhavṛddhimārgaiḥ kilainaṃ śiṣṭāḥ prāhuḥ | yadi paraṃ śūdraviṣaye yad uktaṃ pañcakaṃ śataṃ tad asāv uttamarṇo dvijāter ādātum arhati | śūdraviṭkṣatriyadivjāter apy ādatum arhati || 8.152 ||
nātisāṃvatsarīṃ vṛddhiṃ na cādṛṣṭāṃ vinirharet |
cakravṛddhiḥ kālavṛddhiḥ kāritā kāyikā ca yā || 8.153 ||
nātisaṃvatsarīm iti | yadi dvayor vā trayāṇāṃ varṣāṇāṃ rūpakān na gṛhṇāti tato dadāti | yātu saṃvatsaram atikrāntā vṛddhis tāṃ na gṛhṇīyāt | cakravat gantryādiyānaprayoge sati yā vṛddhiḥ sā cakravṛddhiḥ | sā pūrvoktavṛddher adhikāpi syād ity evaṃ nānujñātā sā cāryasyābhimatā prathamānirdeśāṇ na vinihared ity etat saṃbandhābhāvāc cakravṛddhisamārūḍha iti ca vakṣyamāṇatvād ata eva ceha vṛddher vṛddhiś cakravṛddhir ity evamādikā cakravṛddhiśabdenocyate | api tu granthāśrite vā kālikā pratimāsaṃ tu kāliketi smaraṇān māsi grāhyāḥ | kāritecchā kṛtā parasparāpekṣayā vaṇik prabhṛtīnāṃ syād anyeṣāṃ punaḥ kṛtānusārād adhikety uktam | kāyikā kāyakarmasaṃkhyāvācyā syāt || 8.153 ||
ṛṇaṃ dātum aśakto yaḥ kartum icchet punaḥ kriyām |
sa datvā nirjitāṃ vṛddhiṃ karaṇaṃ parivartayet || 8.154 ||
ṛṇam iti | yo ‘dharmarṇo dhanadānāsāmarthyāt ānītaṃ syād iti kriyāṃ kartum icchet ānītāṃ vṛddhiṃ datvā karaṇaṃ lekhyādi punaḥ kuryāt || 8.154 ||
adarśayitvā tatraiva hiraṇyaṃ parivartayet |
yāvatī saṃbhaved vṛddhis tāvatīṃ dātum arhati || 8.155 ||
adarśayitveti | daurgatyād vṛddhihiraṇyam apy adatvā tatraiva punaḥ kriyamāṇe lekhyādau tad āropayet || 8.155 ||
cakravṛddhiṃ samārūḍho deśakālavyavasthitaḥ |
atikrāman deśakālau na tatphalam avāpnuyāt || 8.156 ||
cakravṛddhim iti | bhāṭakapūrvakaṃ gantryādidānena gantryādivṛddhim āśritya uttamarṇo deśakālavyavasthitasya gatadeśakālaviśeṣas tau deśakālau deyādeyaparipūrayaṃs tadgantrīṃ vahanalābhaṃ na prāpnoti || 8.156 ||
samudrayānakuśalā deśakālārthadarśinaḥ|
sthāpayanti tu yāṃ vṛddhiṃ sā tatrādhigamaṃ prati || 8.157 ||
samudrayānakuśalā iti | vāryadhvayānakuśalā asmin deśe kāle ‘sminn arthe hiraṇyādika uhyamāne ayaṃ lābha ity evamādivetano vaṇikprabhṛtayo yāṃ vṛddhiṃ tathāvidhaviṣaye nirūpayanti sā tatra niścayārthaṃ vijñeyā || 8.157 ||
yo yasya pratibhūs tiṣṭhed darśanāyeha mānavaḥ |
adarśayan sa taṃ tasya yateta svadhanād ṛṇam || 8.158 ||
ya iti | adarśayan mataṃ tasya yateta svadhanād ṛṇam | pratibhūḥ dānakāle maya ayaṃ darśanīya ity evaṃ dhanaṃ prayogakāle ‘nyadā ca yo yasyādharmarṇasyottamarṇapratibhūtvena tiṣṭhet | sa tam adharmarṇaṃ tasmin kāle uttamarṇasyādarśayan svadhanā ṛṇadānaṃ yateta || 8.158 ||
prātibhāvyaṃ vṛthā dānam ākṣikaṃ saurikaṃ ca yat |
daṇḍaśulkāvaśeṣaṃ ca na putro dātum arhati || 8.159 ||
prātibhāvyam iti | pratibhuvā yad deyaṃ vṛthā dānaṃ pāritoṣikādi yat pitrā deyatvenāṅgīkṛtaṃ dyūtasamāhvyābhyāṃ yaj jitaṃ pitrā surāpānādi yad dhvajinaḥ surāmūlyaṃ daṇḍaśeṣaṃ śulkaśeṣaṃ ca yat pitṛsaṃbandhi pitari mṛte putro dātuṃ nārhati || 8.159 ||
darśanaprātibhāvye tu vidhiḥ syāt pūrvacoditaḥ |
dānapratibhuvi prete dāyādān api dāpayet || 8.160 ||
darśanaprātibhāvya iti | yo yaṃ prātibhāvyaṃ na putro dātum arhatīti puroktavidhiḥ sa darśanapratibhūkarmaṇi pitṛkṛte vijñeyo dānarthaṃ punaḥ yaḥ pratibhūḥ sthitaḥ tasmin mṛte rikthabhājo ‘pi dāpayet kum uta putrān || 8.160 ||
adātari punar dātā vijñātaprakṛtāv ṛṇam |
paścāt pratibhuvi prete parīpset kena hetunā || 8.161 ||
adātarīti | tadgrahaṇapūrvakatve ‘yaṃ pratibhūḥ sthita ity evaṃ vijñātaprātibhāvyakaraṇe punardarśanatvapratyayapratibhuvi prete saty anantaraṃ dhanaprayoktā kena hetunā pratibhūputrād dhanaṃ prāptum icchet kiṃ tasya prāptihetur vidyate uta neti yato ‘sau dānapratibhūputro na bhavaty ata prāptyāśaṅkā vidyate | yatas tu dhanagrahaṇe pūrvakaṃ tatpitādarśanapratibhūr vā sthito ‘sti tatputrāt prāptyāśaṅkā || 8.161 ||
nirādiṣṭadhanaś cet tu pratibhūḥ syād alaṃdhanaḥ |
svadhanād eva tad dadyān nirādiṣṭa iti sthitiḥ || 8.162 ||
evaṃ saṃdeham upanyasyedam āha nirādiṣṭadhana iti | asau darśanapratibhūḥ pratyayapratibhūr vā yadi nisṛṣṭadhano bhavati | bhavpratibhūr idaṃ te viśvāsārthaṃ dhanam ity evaṃ prāptadhanaś ca yāvaty asau pratibhūḥ sthitaḥ tāvat pramāṇaṃ tasy ahaste dattaṃ tadā svadhanād eva tad dhanaṃ nirādiṣṭaputraḥ prakṛtam uttamarṇāya dadyād iti śāstramaryādā || 8.162 ||
mattonmattārtādhyadhīnair bālena sthavireṇa vā |
asaṃbaddhakṛtaś caiva vyavahāro na siddhyati || 8.163 ||
mattonmattārtānydhīnair iti | kṣībonnmattavyādhyādyupahatacittagarbhadāsabālātivṛddhaiḥ asaṃbaddhena pitṛbhrātṛniyuktādivyatirekeṇa yo vyavahāraḥ ṛṇadānādi kṛtaḥ na siddhyati || 8.163 ||
satyā na bhāṣā bhavati yady api syāt pratiṣṭhitā |
bahiś ced bhāṣyate dharmān niyatād vyāvahārikāt || 8.164 ||
bhāṣā na satyā bhavatīti | mayā idaṃ tavānuṣṭheyam ityādikā paribhāṣā lekhyādyupanibandhane yady api sthirīkṛtā bhavati tathāpi yady anādipāraṃparyāyātā lokaśāstravyavahāravartino dharmād bahir bhāsate | tadviruddhā nirūpyate tadā satyā na bhavati na tadīyārthānuṣṭheyam || 8.164 ||
yogādhamanavikrītaṃ yogadānapratigraham |
yatra vāpy upadhiṃ paśyet tat sarvaṃ vinivartayet || 8.165 ||
yogādhamanavikrītam iti | upādhinā ye bandhavikrayadānapratigrahāḥ kriyante na grahaṇādi tān rājā vinivartayet kṛtān akṛtān kuryāt | tathānyatrāpi nikṣepādau yatra chadmārthaṃ karaṇaṃ jānīyān na vastuto nikṣepādi kṛtaṃ vinivartayet || 8.165 ||
grahītā yadi naṣṭaḥ syāt kuṭumbārthe kṛto vyayaḥ |
dātavyaṃ bāndhavais tat syāt pravibhaktair api svataḥ || 8.166 ||
grahīteti | avibhakto vibhakto vā bhrātrādisādhāraṇakuṭumbavyayārthaṃ vibhaktabhātṛkuṭumbavyayārthaṃ ca ṛṇaṃ gṛhītvā kuṭumbavyayakṛt mṛto bādhito vā tadā tad ṛṇaṃ avibhaktair vibhaktair vā ṛkthibhiḥ svadhanād dātavyam || 8.166 ||
kuṭumbārthe ‘dhyadhīno ‘pi vyavahāraṃ yam ācaret |
svadeśe vā videśe vā taṃ jyāyān na vicālayet || 8.167 ||
kuṭumbārtha iti | kuṭumbārthe dhanino ‘pi vyavahāraṃ samācaran svadeśe videśe vā gataṃ santaṃ āgacchantaṃ prīkṣayet || 8.167 ||
balād dattaṃ balād bhuktaṃ balād yac cāpi lekhitam |
sarvān balakṛtān arthān akṛtān manur abravīt || 8.168 ||
balād iti | balād dattaṃ kutsitakanyādi, balād bhuktaṃ kṣetrādi balāc ca lekhitaṃ patrādīty evamādīn sarvān balakṛtān arthān nivartanīyān manur āha || 8.168 ||
trayaḥ parārthe kliśyanti sākṣiṇaḥ pratibhūḥ kulam |
catvāras tūpacīyante vipra āḍhyo vaṇiṅ nṛpaḥ || 8.169 ||
traya iti | sākṣiṇaḥ pratibhuvo dharmadraṣṭāro trayaḥ kliśyante kleśam anubhavanty ete | ha haṭhād ete sākṣyādikārayitavyāḥ | catvāraḥ punaḥ brāhmaṇottmarṇavaṇigrājānaḥ parārthadānaphalotpādanodyame dhanārpanavikrayavyavahāreṇa upakurvāṇā dhanopacayaṃ prāpnuvanti | tasmān naiva haṭhād anāgrahaṇakrayavyavahārakaraṇeṣu dātradhamarṇake vyavahāre pravartanīyāḥ || 8.169 ||
anādeyaṃ nādadīta parikṣīṇo ‘pi pārthivaḥ |
na cādeyaṃ samṛddho ‘pi sūkṣmam apy artham utsṛjet || 8.170 ||
anādeyam iti | kṣīṇakośo ‘pi rājā agrāhyaṃ na gṛhṇīyāt | samṛddho ‘pi alpam api grāhyam arthaṃ na parityajet || 8.170 ||
anādeyasya cādānād ādeyasya ca varjanāt |
daurbalyaṃ khyāpyate rājñaḥ sa pretyeha ca naśyati || 8.171 ||
anādeyasyeti | agrāhyagrahaṇāt grāhyaparityāgāc cāśaktān asaddaṇḍe yojayati | śaktānāṃ punaḥ prakṛtam apy upekṣate bhīrutayety evaṃ paurair daurbalyaṃ rājñaḥ prakāśyate sa cādeyādāne asvadharmotpādaprakṛtikopābhyām ādeyavarjanaena ca kṣīṇakośatayā rājyavināśād ihalokaparalokayor vinaśyati || 8.171 ||
svādānād varṇasaṃsargāt tv abalānāṃ ca rakṣaṇāt |
balaṃ saṃjāyate rājñaḥ sa pretyeha ca vardhate || 8.172 ||
svādānād iti | balān khyāpyate rājā sa pretyeha ca vardhate | nyāyyadhanagrahaṇād varṇānāṃ samānajātīyair varṇaiḥ strīṣu ca saṃbandhād durbalānāṃ ca balavadbhyo rakṣaṇād balavān iti rājā loke kathyate | ataś cāsāv iha(para)lokayor vardhate || 8.172 ||
tasmād yama iva svāmī svaṃ hitvā priyāpriye |
varteta yāmyayā vṛtyā jitakrodho jitendriyaḥ || 8.173 ||
yataḥ tasmād iti | tasmād yama iva rājā vaśīkṛtakrodhendriya ātmanaś ca priyāpriye parihṛtya yamasaṃbandhinyā ceṣṭayā ripumitrasāmyatvena vartate (varteta) || 8.173 ||
yas tv adharmeṇa kāryāṇi mohāt kuryān narādhipaḥ |
acirāt taṃ durātmānaṃ vaśe kurvanti śatravaḥ || 8.174 ||
ya iti | yaḥ punar nṛpo lobhādivyāmūḍhatayādharmeṇa vyavahāradharśanādīni kāryāṇi kurute, tam adharmaprakṛtatvāt kṣipram eva śatravaś cāsādayanti || 8.174 ||
kāmakrodhau tu saṃyamya yo ‘rthān dharmeṇa paśyati |
prajās tam anuvartante samudram iva sindhavaḥ || 8.175 ||
kāmakrodhāv iti | yaś ca dveṣaparityāgena rājā dharmeṇa kāryāṇi nivārayati taṃ nadya iva samudraṃ prajāḥ saṃbhajante tan manasaś ca bhavanti || 8.175 ||
yaḥ sādhayantaṃ chandena vedayed dhanikaṃ nṛpe |
sa rājñā taccaturbhāgaṃ dāpyas tasya ca tad dhanam || 8.176 ||
ya iti | sa rājñarṇacaturbhāgaṃ daṇḍārthaṃ tasya tad dhanam | yo ‘dhamarṇo rājavallabhābhimānī sāmādinā sādhayantam uttamarṇaṃ nṛpe nivedayet, sa rājñā ṛṇacaturbhāgaṃ daṇḍārthaṃ tasya tad dhanaṃ dāpanīyaḥ || 8.176 ||
karmaṇāpi samaṃ kuryād dhanikāyādhamarṇikaḥ |
samo ‘vakṛṣṭajātis tu dadyāc chreyāṃs tu tac chanaiḥ || 8.177 ||
karmaṇeti | samahīnajātiś cādharmarṇo dhanābhāve sati svajātyanurūpakarma kārayitavyo ‘pi tu śanaiḥ tad dhanaṃ yathāsaṃbhavaṃ dadyāt || 8.177 ||
anena vidhinā rājā mitho vivadatāṃ nṛṇām |
sākṣipratyayasiddhāni kāryāṇi samatāṃ nayet || 8.178 ||
aneneti | parasparaṃ vivadamānayor arthipratyarthinor anenoktaprakāreṇa rājā sākṣinirṇītāni anumānaśapathādipratyayanirṇītāni kāryāṇy arthiprathyarthivipratipattirodhena samīkuryāt || 8.178 ||
kulaje vṛttasaṃpanne dharmañje satyavādini |
mahāpakṣe dhaninyārye nikṣepaṃ nikṣiped budhaḥ || 8.179 ||
kulaja iti | mahājane ācāravartini dharmajñe satyavādini bahusuhṛtsujanake dhanavati ṛjuprakṛtau manuṣye vyabhicārābhāvān nikṣepaṃ chādayet (sthāpayet) iti suhṛdupadeśo ‘yaṃ dṛṣṭārthaḥ || 8.179 ||
yo yathā nikṣiped dhaste yam arthaṃ yasya mānavaḥ |
sa tathaiva grahītavyo yathā dāyas tathā grahaḥ || 8.180 ||
ya iti | yo manuṣyo yena prakāreṇa samudraṃ vā sasākṣikam asākṣikaṃ vā yad arthaṃ suvarṇādikaṃ yasya haste nikṣipet so ‘rthas tena nikṣeptrā tenaiva prakāreṇa gṛhītavyaḥ yasmād yenaiva prakāreṇa samarpaṇaṃ tenaiva grahaṇaṃ nyāyyam evam anyathākaraṇe daṇḍyaḥ || 8.180 ||
yo nikṣepaṃ yācyamāno nikṣeptur na prayacchati |
sa yācyaḥ prāḍvivākena tannikṣeptur asaṃnidhau || 8.181 ||
sa yadi pratipadyeta yathānyastaṃ yathākṛtam |
na tatra vidyate kiṃcid yat parair abhiyujyate || 8.182 ||
teṣāṃ na dadyād yadi tu tad dhiraṇyaṃ yathāvidhi |
sa nigṛhyaobhayaṃ dāpya iti dharmasya dhāraṇā || 8.183 ||
sākṣyabhāve praṇidhibhir vayorūpasamanvitaiḥ |
apadeśaiś ca saṃnyasya hiraṇyaṃ tasya tattvataḥ || 8.184 ||
yo nikṣepam iti | sa iti | teṣām iti | sākṣyabhāva iti | dehi me nikṣiptaṃ hiraṇyādi ity evaṃ yaḥ prārthyamānaḥ sākṣyabhāvān nikṣeptur na dadāti tasya nikṣeptrarthitvena prāḍvivākena tasmin nikṣeptary asaṃnihite kaścic cārapuruṣaiḥ ruciraprāyaiḥ saumyākṛtibhir nṛpopadravavyājābhidhāyibhiḥ hiraṇyaṃ tattvena yathā tasya śaṅkā notpadyate tathā nikṣepayitvā, tato ‘sau kālāntare prāḍvivākena pūrvanikṣeptur asaṃnidhau tena taccāranikṣeptṛhiraṇyaṃ tair eva puruṣair dehy asmākaṃ nikṣepahiraṇyaṃ ity evaṃ yācitavyaḥ | sa ca nikṣepadhārī yathānyastaṃ samudram amudraṃ vā yathākṛtaṃ ca kaṭasūtrādyākāreṇa yadi tathaiva pratipādyate samastair gṛhītaṃ tad iti tadā yat parair nikṣeptṛbhiḥ prāḍvivākavedibhiḥ asya haste asmākaṃ vidyata ity ākṣipyate tat tatra kiṃcid vijñeyam | yadi punas teṣāṃ cārapuruṣāṇāṃ tat kṣiptaṃ hiraṇyaṃ yathānyastaṃ na dadyāt tadā dvāv api nikṣepau jñāpakacārasaṃbandhināv avaṣṭabhya dāpanīyaḥ syād ity evaṃrūpo dharmaniścayaḥ | ity evam etad ślokacatuṣṭayam || 8.181–184 ||
nikṣepopanidhī nityaṃ na deyau pratyanantare |
naśyato vinipāte vāvinipāte tv anāśitau || 8.185 ||
nikṣepopanidhīti | naśyato vinipāte vāvinipāte tv anāśitau | nikṣepaprītibhogyau nikṣeptrupanidhātroḥ jīvatos tadardhenāha putrabhātrādau na kadācid deyau | yatas tasya putrāder vināśe tau nikṣepopanidhī hy arthasvāmini yācamāne kiṃ vaktavyaṃ putrādeḥ punar avināśe kadācid avināśau syātām || 8.185 ||
svayam eva tu yau dadyān mṛtasya pratyanantare |
na sa rājñābhiyoktavyo na nikṣeptuś ca bandhubhiḥ || 8.186 ||
svayam iti | nikṣeptuḥ mṛtasya yo ‘jñātaṃ nikṣiptaṃ svayam eva taddhanārhe putrādau dadyāt, na rājñā nikṣeptuḥ putrādibhir bandhubhiḥ (na) niyoktavyaḥ || 8.186 ||
acchalenaiva cānvicchet tam arthaṃ prītipūrvakam |
vicārya tasya vā vṛttaṃ sāmnaiva parisādhayet || 8.187 ||
athāsya tasyopari kiṃcid bhrāntiḥ syāt tadā acchaleneti | vicārya tasya deyaṃ sāmnaiva paritoṣayan | tad vastu dhanāntarasadbhāvalakṣaṇavākchalādiparihāreṇaiva prītipūrveṇa vakragatidivyādidānena niścinuyāt tasya vā nikṣepadhāriṇaḥ śīlam avikṣya dhārmiko ‘yam iti jñātvā sāmādiprayogeṇaiva pratiniścinuyāt tad daṇḍādinā divyair vā || 8.187 ||
nikṣepeṣv eṣu sarveṣu vidhiḥ syāt parisādhane |
samudre nāpnuyāt kiṃcid ydi tasmān na saṃharet || 8.188 ||
nikṣepeṣv iti | sarveṣu nikṣepeṣu pratyāyane sākṣyabhāva ityādividhiḥ syāt mudrite punaḥ pratyarthine nikṣepadhārī na kiṃcid doṣajātaṃ prāpnuyād yadi tasmād adhaḥ śravaṇapratimudrādinā na kiṃcid apaharet || 8.188 ||
caurair hṛtaṃ jalenoḍham agninā dagdham eva vā |
na dadyād yadi tasmāt sa na saṃharati kiṃcana || 8.189 ||
caurair iti | caurair muṣitam udakena deśāntaraṃ prāpitam agninā ca dagdhaṃ nikṣepadhārī na dadyāt | yadi tattvato nikṣepāt kiṃcin nāpaharati || 8.189 ||
nikṣepasyāpahartāram anikṣeptāram eva ca |
sarvair upāyair anvicchec chapathaiś caiva vaidikaiḥ || 8.190 ||
nikṣepasyet | nikṣepasyāpahartāraṃ munibhis tattvadarśibhiḥ sarvair upāyaiḥ sāmādibhir vaidikaiś ca śapathair agniharaṇādibhiḥ rājā niścinuyāt | anikṣiptāram iti bruvan niyoktur api iti jñāpayati || 8.190 ||
yo nikṣepaṃ nārpayati yaś cānikṣipya yācate |
tāv ubhau cauravac chāsyau dāpyau vā tatsamaṃ damam || 8.191 ||
nikṣepam iti | yo nikṣepaṃ na tyajati yaś cānikṣipyaivārthayate tau dvau mahaty aparādhe kṛtāparādhe cauravad uttamasāhasādikaṃ daṇḍyau | svalpe vā tasya nikṣepasya samaṃ daṇḍyau
|| 8.191 ||
nikṣepasyāpahartāraṃ tatsamaṃ dāpayed damam |
tathopanidhihartāram aviśeṣeṇa pārthivaḥ || 8.192 ||
nikṣepasyeti | nikṣepāpahāriṇaṃ tatsamaṃ daṇḍayed iti pūrvasiddhasya punarvacanaṃ prathamāparādhe mahaty api punaś cauravad ity evamartham | ye tu brāhmaṇasyāṅgavicchedādicauradaṇḍanivṛttyartham idaṃ punarvacanam ityādi tad asat | na jātu brāhmaṇaṃ hanyād ityāder vakṣyamāṇatvāt tathā prītibhogyadravyāpahāriṇam aviśeṣeṇa jātimātraviśeṣeṇa tat sarvam eva dāpayet || 8.192 ||
upadhābhiś ca yaḥ kaścit paradravyaṃ haren naraḥ |
sasahāyaḥ sa hantavyaḥ prakāśaṃ vividhair vadhaiḥ || 8.193 ||
upadhābhis tu yat kiṃcid iti | yo naraḥ paradravyaṃ haret sasahāyaḥ karacaraṇachedādibhiḥ nānāprakāraiḥ vadhopāyaiḥ śakalaṃ kṛtvā rājamārgādau rājñā hantavyaḥ || 8.193 ||
nikṣepo yaḥ kṛto yena yāvāṃś ca kulasaṃnidhau |
tāvān eva sa vijñeyo vibruvan daṇḍam arhati || 8.194 ||
niṣepa iti | yajjātīyaṃ dravyaṃ dravyaparimāṇaṃ ca atha sākṣisamakṣaṃ nikṣiptaṃ dravyaṃ tatparimāṇam eva sākṣipratyayena boddhavyaṃ na pramāṇāntareṇa tatra parīkṣā kāryā | tatra ca nikṣeptā vitathaṃ bruvan viṣṇūktaṃ daṇḍam arhati || 8.194 ||
mithā dāyaḥ kṛto yena gṛhīto mitha eva vā |
mitha eva pradātavyo yathā dāyas tathā grahaḥ || 8.195 ||
mitha iti | parasparaṃ. rahasi yena nikṣepo kṛto nikṣepitaḥ nikṣepadhāriṇāpi rahasy eva gṛhītaḥ sa nikṣepo rahasy eva pratyarpaṇīyaḥ | na pratyarthena sākṣyādyapekṣaṃ (kṣyaṃ) yasmād yena prakāreṇa dānaṃ tenaiva pratyarpaṇam itīva nikṣepadhāriniyamārthaṃ pradātavya iti yo yathā nikṣiped vastu itīdaṃ nikṣeptṛniyamārthaṃ gṛhītavyaśravanādhikye sati yad asya ślokapratividhiviṣayatvaṃ vyākhyātam, sāmānyaviṣayaṃ tāvat vyākhyānaṃ vā keṣāṃcit tacchiṣṭaiḥ parīkṣyam || 8.195 ||
nikṣiptasya dhanasyaivaṃ prītyopanihitasya ca |
rājā vinirṇayaṃ kuryād akṣiṇvan nyāsadhāriṇam || 8.196 ||
nikṣiptasyeti | nikṣepadhāriṇam apīḍayan nikṣiptasya dravyasya prītyā ca sāmpratikopabhogārtham arpitasyānena prakāreṇa nirṇayaṃ kuryāt || 8.196 ||
vikrīṇīte parasya svaṃ yo ‘svāmī svāmyasaṃmataḥ |
na taṃ nayeta sākṣyaṃ tu stenam astenamāninam || 8.197 ||
vikrīṇīta iti | yo ‘svāmī svāminānanujñātaḥ parakīyaṃ dravyaṃ vikrīṇīte tam acauram ātmānaṃ śrutacauraṃ prāḍvivākaḥ sākṣikaṃ na kārayed iti sākṣikarmaniṣedhadvāreṇa tasyāpakāritvapratipādanaṃ kriyāntareṣv apy ayogyatvapratipādanārtham || 8.197 ||
avahāryo bhavec caiva sānvayaḥ ṣaṭśataṃ damam |
niranvayo ‘napasaraḥ prāptaḥ syāc caurakilbiṣam || 8.198 ||
avahārya iti | eṣa ca parasvavikrayī yadi svāminā putrādirūpatvenānugato bhavati ṣaṭpaṇaśatāni daṇḍārtham ānayitavyaḥ | yadi punaḥ svāmino nānumato bhavati avidyamānāpasaraś ca yadi svāmisaṃbandhaputrādeḥ sakāśāt kāryādinā tad dravyaṃ tasya na saṃśritraṃ bhavati tadā caurasaṃbandhipāpaṃ prāpnoti | pāpasahacarāpadeśe ‘pi daṇḍatideśaḥ || 8.198 ||
asvāminā kṛto yas tu dāyo vikraya eva vā |
akṛtaḥ sa tu vijñeyo vyavahāre yathā sthitiḥ || 8.199 ||
asvāmineti | asvāminā adattaṃ vikrītaṃ vā tad akṛtam eva boddhavyam ity evaṃvidhā vyavahāramaryādā || 8.199 ||
saṃbhogo dṛśyate yatra na dṛśyetāgamaḥ kvacit |
āgamaḥ kāraṇaṃ tatra na saṃbhoga iti sthitiḥ || 8.200 ||
saṃbhoga iti | yasmin vastuni bhogo dṛśyate āgamaḥ punaḥ kriyādiḥ na kvacid asti tatrāgamaḥ kāraṇaṃ na saṃbhogo ‘taś ca śuddhāgamasya daśavarṣabhogenāpi kāryasiddhiḥ || 8.200 ||
vikrayād yo dhanaṃ kiṃcid gṛhṇīyāt kulasaṃnidhau |
krayeṇa sa viśuddhaṃ hi nyāyato labhate dhanam || 8.201 ||
vikreyād iti | āpaṇabhūmau (yat) prayaccheta tad vyavahartṛpuruṣasamakṣaṃ boddhavyam | kiṃcin maulyena gṛhṇīyāt sa yasmāt prakāśocitaḥ mūlyakulasaṃnidhyāpaṇasthānānuṣṭhitena nyāyakrameṇa viśuddhaṃ tadīyaṃ dhanaṃ mūlyamānādhikaṃ hastān na labhate || 8.201 ||
atha mūlam anāhāryaṃ prakāśakrayaśodhitaḥ |
adaṇḍyo mucyate rājñā nāṣṭiko labhate dhanam || 8.202 ||
atha mūlam anāhāryaṃ prakāśakrayaśodhitam iti | yadi tena kiṃcid deśāṇtaraṃ gatvā krītadravyamūlyam āhartum aśakyaṃ tadā pūrvaślokanyāyena prakāśakrayaśodhitaṃ kṛtvā kretā daṇḍata eva rājñā draṣṭavyaḥ | svāmī ca tad dravyaṃ yad asvāminā vikrītaṃ tat kretṛhastāl labhate || 8.202 ||
nānyad anyena saṃsṛṣṭarūpaṃ vikrayam arhati |
na sāvadyaṃ na ca nyūnaṃ na dūre na tirohitam || 8.203 ||
neti | na sāvadyam iti | kuṅkumādidravyaṃ kusumbhādinā vyājabuddhyā miśrīkṛtaṃ vikretavyam | na cāptakarāntaḥkleśādidoṣopetaṃ yat tulādināpi vyavasthitaṃ na ca rāgādinā sthagitarūpaṃ evaṃ cātrāsvāmivikrayadaṇḍavat syāt || 8.203 ||
anyāṃ ced darśayitvānyā voḍhuḥ kanyā pradīyate |
ubhe te ekaśulkena vahed ity abravīn manuḥ || 8.204 ||
anyām iti | śulkadeyāṃ kanyāṃ śulkavṛddhyecchākāle niravadyāṃ darśayitvā yadi sāvadyā varasya dīyate tadā dve api kanye tena ekenaiva śulkadānena haret | iti vikrayasādharmyeṇāsyehābhidhānam || 8.204 ||
nonmattāyā na kuṣṭinyā na ca yā spṛṣṭamaithunā |
pūrvaṃ doṣān abhikhyāpya pradātā daṇḍam arhati || 8.205 ||
neti | unmattāyāḥ kanyāyās tathā kuṣṭhavatyā yā cānubhūtamaithunā tasyāḥ brāhmyādivivāhādāv api dānāt pūrvam unmattādīn doṣān varāya kanyāṃ datvā khyāpayan daṇḍam arhati | daṇḍaṃ ca doṣavatīm iti vakṣyate || 8.205 ||
ṛtvig yadi vṛto yajñe svakarma parihāpayet |
tasya karmānurūpeṇa deyo ‘ṃśaḥ saha kartṛbhiḥ || 8.206 ||
saṃbhūya ca samutthānam āha ṛtvig iti | pradhānaṛtvik homādiśrautakaraṇena kṛtakaraṇo jyotiṣṭomādau yadi vyādhinā svaṃ karma parityajet tadā tasya tad anuyācitamitrāvaruṇādibhiḥ tasya kṛtakarmānusāreṇa dakṣiṇā dātavyāḥ || 8.206 ||
dakṣiṇāsu ca dattāsu svakarma parihāpayan |
kṛtsnam eva labhetāṃśam anyenaiva ca kārayet || 8.207 ||
dakṣiṇāsv iti | dakṣiṇāsu ca mādhyandine savane dakṣiṇānayantīty evamādidakṣiṇākāle dattāsu daivāt svasādhyaṃ karma parityajan kṛtsnam eva dakṣiṇākarmaśeṣaṃ cānyenāsau prakṛta ṛtvik kārayet || 8.207 ||
yasmin karmaṇi yās tu syur uktā pratyaṅgadakṣiṇāḥ |
sa eva tā ādadīta bhajeran sarva eva vā || 8.208 ||
yasminn iti | yasmin karmaṇi ādānāgnau aṅgam aṅgaṃ prati yāḥ dakṣiṇāś ca uditā syuḥ yathārabdham adhvaryum ityādau pradhānadakṣiṇāś ca gāvo deyāḥ ādinā kiyat uddeśena śrūyante sa eva śrutatvāt svīkuryāt | atha parṣadvārikāvat pratigrahamātrāvyāpārāt sarvaṃ eva uditaḥ iti saṃdehe upanyāsaḥ || 8.208 ||
rathaṃ hareta cādhvaryur brahmādhāne ca vājinam |
hotā vāpi hared aśvam udgātā vāpy anaḥ kraye || 8.209 ||
atra śrutatvāt tādarthyapakṣam āśritya nirṇayam āha ratham iti | adhvaryorathodeyatvena śrutovasatām eva svīkuryāt tathādhāne brahmā aśvo deya iti tasya śruteḥ ādadīta hotāpy aśvam ātmasāt kuryāt uddhātāpi somakraye yac chakaṭaṃ tad gṛhṇīyāt || 8.209 ||
sarveṣām ardhino mukhyās tadardhenārdhino ‘pare |
tṛtīyinas tṛtīyāṃśāś caturthāṃśāś ca pādinaḥ || 8.210 ||
adhunā pradhānadakṣiṇāvidhim āha sarveṣāṃ iti | taṃ śatena dīkṣayatīti śrūyate | tatra sarveṣām ṛtvijāṃ madhyāṃśe mukhyā ṛtvijo hotradhvaryurbrahmodgātāras te ardhaharā aṣṭācatvāriṃśadgobhāja evaṃ tadardhādisaṃkhyābhidhānaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ samañjasam bhavati | kātyāyanaś ca dvādaśadvādaśādyebhyaḥ ity āha | tasyā aṣṭācatvāriṃśato ‘rdhena caturviṃśatyāś ca pare dvitīyebhya ity āha | tṛtīyasthāninīcchāvākāna śabda agnīn pratihartāro ‘ṣṭācatvāriṃśattṛtīyāṃśaṣoḍaśagobhājaḥ | kātyānanaś catasracatasras tṛtīyebhya iti āha, pādinaś caturthavyavasthāḥ pādasubrahamaṇyāgrāvaṣṭunotāre aṣṭācatvāriṃśaś caturthāṃśadvādaśagobhājaḥ | kātyāyanaś ca tisras tisra itarebhya ity ata āha || 8.210 ||
saṃbhūya svāni karmāṇi kurvadbhir iha mānavaiḥ |
anena vidhiyogena kartavyāṃśaprakalpanā || 8.211 ||
saṃbhūyeti | ekībhūya gṛhanirvartanādīni svakāryāṇi sthapativardhakisūtradhārādibhiḥ manuṣyaiḥ saṃpādayadbhir anekam uktavidhānāśrayeṇa vijñānavyāpārādyapekṣayā bhāgakalpanā kāryā || 8.211 ||
dharmārthaṃ yena dattaṃ syāt kasmaicid yācate dhanam |
paścāc ca na tathā tat syān na deyaṃ tasya tad bhavet || 8.212 ||
dharmārtham iti | yean yāgādyarthaṃ bhikṣamāṇāya kasmaicid dhanaṃ dattaṃ pratijñātaṃ ca syāt paścāt taddhanasamau yāgārthaṃ yadi na yuñjīta tadāsya pratiśrutaṃ na deyam, dattaṃ vā pratyāharaṇīyam || 8.212 ||
yadi saṃsādhayet tat tu darpāl lobhena vā punaḥ |
rājñā dāpyaḥ suvarṇaṃ syāt tasya steyasya niṣkṛtiḥ || 8.213 ||
yad iti | yadi tatpratiśrutaṃ dhanasamau pratigṛhītāhaṃkārāl lobhād vā mārge svakṛtaṃ vā dātuṃ na tyajet tadā tasya cauryapāpasya saṃśuddhyarthaṃ rājñā daṇḍanīyo bhavet | haṭhād api daṇḍitasya daṇḍena pāpanirharaṇaṃ bhavatīty asmān niṣkṛtivacanād avasīyate || 8.213 ||
dattasyaiṣoditā dharmyā yathāvad anapakriyā |
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi vetanasyānapakriyām || 8.214 ||
dattasyeti | etad asya pratipādanaṃ dharmād anapetaṃ tattvaṃ tad uktam ato ‘nantaram anyatarad api pratipādanaṃ kathayiṣyāmi || 8.214 ||
bhṛto ‘nārto na kuryād yo darpāt karma yathoditam |
sa daṇḍyaḥ kṛṣṇalāny aṣṭau na deyaṃ cāsya vetanam || 8.215 ||
bhṛta iti | yo bhṛtaḥ parakrīto vyādhyādyapīḍito yathānirūpitaṃ karma ahaṅkārān na kuryāt sa kurvāṇa kriyoktasvarūpe suvarṇādikṛṣṇalān aṣṭau daṇḍyo bhṛtiś cāsya karmabhāgād api na deyā || 8.215 ||
ārtas tu kuryāt svasthāh san yathābhāṣitam āditaḥ |
sa dīrghasyāpi kālasya tal labhetaiva vetanam || 8.216 ||
ārta iti | yadā punaḥ pīḍādinā vā karma na karoti tadā svasthaḥ san pūrvaṃ yādṛk nirūpitaṃ tādṛk kuryāt tāṃ ca vṛttiṃ sakalād api kālāl labhetaiva || 8.216 ||
yathoktam ārtaḥ sustho vā yas tat karma na kārayet |
na tasya vetanaṃ deyam alponasyāpi karmaṇaḥ || 8.217 ||
eṣa dharmo ‘khilenokto vetanādānakarmaṇaḥ |
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi dharmaṃ samayabhedinām || 8.218 ||
yathoktam iti | eṣa dharma iti | eṣā vyavasthā vetanadānākhyasya karmaṇo niḥśeṣata uktā | anantaraṃ saṃvidvyatikramakāriṇāṃ daṇḍādivyavasthāṃ vakṣyāmi || 8.218 ||
yo grāmadeśasaṃghānāṃ kṛtvā satyena saṃvidam |
visaṃvaden naro lobhāt taṃ rāṣṭrād vipravāsayet || 8.219 ||
ya iti | grāmyāṇāṃ deśavāsināṃ vaṇigādīnāṃ madhyād yo nara idam asmābhiḥ kartavyaṃ parihāryaṃ cety evaṃ saṃketaṃ niruddhena rūpeṇa kṛtvā vacalet taṃ rāṣṭrād rājā nirvāsayet || 8.219 ||
nigṛhya dāpayec cainaṃ samayavyabhicāriṇam |
catuḥsuvarṇān ṣaṇṇiṣkāṃś chatamānaṃ ca rājatam || 8.220 ||
nigṛhyeti | ya evaṃ saṃvidvyatikramet catuḥsuvarṇakārṣikāniṣkān uktarūpān na tu sārdhaśataṃ suvarṇānāṃ niṣkān āhur iti sāstrāntaroktān rājataṃ ca śatamānam uktam || 8.220 ||
evaṃ daṇḍavidhiṃ kuryād dhārmikaḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ |
grāmajātisamūheṣu samayavyabhicāriṇam || 8.221 ||
evaṃ daṇḍavidhim iti | dharmapradhāno rājā grāmeṣu brāhmaṇādiṣu vyatikramiṇaṃ etad daṇḍavidhānam anutiṣṭhet || 8.221 ||
krītvā vikrīya vā kiṃcid yasyehānuśayo bhavet |
so ‘ntardaśāhāt tad dravyaṃ dadyāc caivādadīta vā || 8.222 ||
krīvety | vikrīya kiṃcid dravyam acirasvabhāvaṃ sthirārthaṃ vikrīya vā yasya lobhāt paścāttāpo bhavati na sādhu mayā kṛtam iti daśāhamadhye pratyarthaṃ yad vikrītaṃ tad gṛhṇīyāt || 8.222 ||
pareṇa tu daśāhasya na dadyān nāpi dāpayet |
ādadāno dadac caiva rājā daṇḍyo śatāni ṣaṭ || 8.223 ||
pareṇeti | daśāhottarakālaṃ krītaṃ ca na dāpayet || 8.223 ||
yas tu doṣavatīṃ kanyām anākhyāya prayacchati |
tasya kuryān nṛpo daṇḍaṃ svayaṃ ṣaṇṇavatiṃ paṇān || 8.224 ||
akanyeti tu yaḥ kanyāṃ brūyād dveṣeṇa mānavaḥ |
sa śataṃ prāpnuyād daṇḍaṃ tasyā doṣam adarśayan || 8.225 ||
yas tv iti || akanyeti | neyaṃ kanyā kṣatayonir iyam ity evaṃ yo manuṣyo dveṣeṇa kanyāyāḥ kuryāt sa tasyāḥ taṃ doṣam avibhāvayan paṇaśataṃ daṇḍaṃ prāpnuyāt | uktasyāpy atikramakāritvād daṇḍaḥ || 8.225 ||
pāṇigrahaṇikā mantrāḥ kanyāsv eva pratiṣṭhitāḥ |
nākanyāsu kvacin nṛṇāṃ luptadharmakriyā hi tāḥ || 8.226 ||
yasmāt prāṇigrahaṇikā iti | aryamaṇaṃ nu deyaṃ kanyā agnimayakṣatety evamādayo vaivāhikā manuṣyāṇāṃ mantrāḥ || 8.226 ||
pāṇigrahaṇikā mantrā niyataṃ dāralakṣaṇam |
teṣāṃ niṣṭhā tu vijñeyā vidvadbhiḥ saptame pade || 8.227 ||
pāṇigrahaṇikā mantrā niyatam iti | vaivāhikā mantrā niścitaṃ bhāryātve nimittam, taiḥ prayuktaiḥ bhāryā bhavati, teṣāṃ punar mantrāṇāṃ sakha saptapadī bhaveti mantreṇa kanyāyā saptame pade datte sati śāstrajñaiḥ samāptir boddhavyaiva ca saptamāt padād arvāg akanyakantvaparijñāne ‘nuśaye sati tyajen nordhvaṃ kānīnasahoḍhagūḍhotpannānāṃ śāstre voḍhuḥ putradarśanāt || 8.227 ||
yasmin yasmin kṛte yasyehānuśayo bhavet |
tam anena vidhānena dharmye pathi niveśayet || 8.228 ||
yasminn iti | na kevalaṃ krayavikrayaḥ evaṃ yāvad yasmint saṃvidvedanādāv api kārye kṛte yasya loke paścāttāpaḥ syāt taṃ putradānādikavidhinā dharmād anapete mārge nṛpaḥ sthāpayet || 8.228 ||
paśuṣu svāmināṃ caiva pālānāṃ ca vyatikrame |
vivādaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi yathāvad dharmatattvataḥ || 8.229 ||
paśuṣv iti | paśuvivādadoṣair utpannaiḥ svāmināṃ tatpālānāṃ ca vivādam aśeṣaaṃ kṛtvā tāttvikavyavasthāṃ vakṣyati || 8.229 ||
divā vaktavyatā pāle rātrau svāmini tadgṛhe |
yogakṣeme ‘nyathā cet tu pālo vaktavyatām iyāt || 8.230 ||
diveti | pālakanyastānāṃ paśūnāṃ divā doṣa utpanne pālasya garhaṇīyatā rātrau punaḥ pālaprathyarthināṃ svāmino doṣo yai punā rātrāv api pālahastagatā bhavanti tatra doṣa ucyate | tadā pāla eva garhatāṃ prāpnuyād ayogakṣeme yogakṣemaśabdaprakāśavat || 8.230 ||
gopaḥ kṣirabhṛto yas tu sa duhyād daśato varām |
gosvāmyānumate bhṛtyaḥ sā syāt pāle ‘bhṛte bhṛtiḥ || 8.231 ||
gopa iti | yo gopālākhyo bhṛtyaḥ kṣīrabhṛto na bhaktādibhṛtaḥ sa svāmyanujñayā daśabhyo gobhya ekāṃ śreṣṭhām ātmabhṛtyarthaṃ duhīta | sā bhaktādinā bhṛte pāle bhṛyā syād eva caikakṣīrād anena daśa gāḥ pālayet || 8.231 ||
naṣṭaṃ vinaṣṭaṃ kṛmibhiḥ śvahataṃ viṣame mṛtam |
hīnaṃ puruṣakāreṇa pradadyāt pāla eva tu || 8.232 ||
naṣṭam iti | pālasaṃbandhirakṣaṇād yaḥ sākṣāt puruṣavyāpāreṇa śūnyaṃ santaṃ naṣṭaṃ draṣṭigocarātītaṃ kṛmibhiḥ bhakṣitaṃ śvabhrādimṛtaṃ pāla eva svāminaṃ dadyāt || 8.232 ||
vighuṣya tu hṛtaṃ caurair na pālo dātum arhati |
yadi deśe ca kāle ca svāminaḥ svaya śaṃsati || 8.233 ||
vighuṣyeti | cauraiḥ punaḥ paṭahādyāghuṣya hṛtaṃ pālo dātuṃ nārhati | yadi tasminn eva deśe haraṇaṃ kālānantaraṃ evātmīyasya svāminaḥ kathayate (kathyate) || 8.233 ||
karṇau carma ca vālāṃś ca bastiṃ snāyuṃ ca rocanām |
paśuṣu svāmināṃ dadyāt mṛteṣv aṅgāṃś ca darśayet || 8.234 ||
karṇau ca lomāṃś ceti | paśusvāṣu dadyāt tu mṛteṣv aṅgāṃś ca darśayet | svayaṃmṛteṣu paśuṣu karṇacarmalomabastyākhyamūtrādhārāṅgaviśeṣasnāyugorocanā upakārakatvāt svāmine dadyāt | cihnāni vadatākarṇatvagādīni pratyabhijñānaṃ darśayet || 9.234 ||
ajāvike tu saṃruddhe vṛkaiḥ pāle tv anāyati |
yāṃ prasahya vṛko hanyāt pāle tat kilbiṣaṃ bhavet || 8.235 ||
ajāvikeṣv iti | ajaiḍake śṛgālādibhiḥ ākrānte bhavati tadā pāle ‘nāgacchati yān abhibhūya vṛko hanyāt sa pālasya doṣaḥ syāt | bahutvād vṛkākṣepāsaṃbhave saty ajādigrahaṇam || 8.235 ||
tāsāṃ ced avaruddhānāṃ carantīnāṃ mitho vane |
yām utplutya vṛko hantān na pālas tatra kilbiṣī || 8.236 ||
tāsām iti | yadi tāsām ajāvikānāṃ pālenaiva śvānugataś cāvaruddhagamanānāṃ saṃhatībhūya vane carantīnāṃ madhyāt kaṃścit kutascit vanāder utpatya śṛgālādi hanyāt na tatra pālo doṣabhāk || 8.236 ||
dhanuḥśataṃ parīhāro grāmasya syāt samantataḥ |
śamyāpātās trayo vāpi triguṇo nagarasya tu || 8.237 ||
dhanuḥśatam iti | caturhastaṃ dhanuḥ śamyā yaṣṭiḥ tasyāḥ pātaḥ śamyāprakṣepaḥ grāmasamīpe sarvāsu dikṣu catvāri hastaśatāni trīn vā yaṣṭiprakṣepān yāvat sasyavapanaparihāraḥ paśupracārārthaṃ kāryo nagarasamīpe punar ayaṃ triguṇaḥ kāryaḥ || 8.237 ||
tatrāparivṛtaṃ dhānyaṃ vihasyuḥ paśavo yadi |
na tatra praṇayed daṇḍaṃ nṛpatiḥ paśurakṣiṇām || 8.238 ||
tatreti | tasmin vyavahārasthāne yadi kenacid dhānyam upyeta adattavṛttike ced yadi paśavo bhakṣayeyuḥ tadā tatra paśupālānāṃ rājā daṇḍaṃ na kuryāt || 8.238 ||
vṛtiṃ tatra prakurvīta yām uṣṭro na vilokayet |
chidraṃ ca vārayet sarvaṃ śvasūkaramukhānugam || 8.239 ||
vṛtim iti | tasmin parihārasthāne kṣetre vṛtiṃ kaṇṭakādimayī prākārākṛtiṃ tathāvidhām ūrjitāṃ kuryāt vāmaparapārśvataḥ yathā uṣṭro nāvalokayet | tasyāṃ ca yāvat kiṃcic chidraṃ śvasūkaramukhapraveśakṣamam api tat sarvaṃ sthagayeyuḥ || 8.239 ||
pathi kṣetre ‘parivṛte grāmāntīye ‘tha vā punaḥ |
sapālaḥ śatadaṇḍārho vipālān vārayet paśūn || 8.240 ||
pathīti | yadi punar mārgasamīpavartini grāmasamīpe gate ‘kṛtavṛtau sapālaḥ paśupālaprakṣipto na rakṣati tadā paṇaśataṃ daṇḍyaḥ paśor daṇḍāsaṃbhavāt pāla eva daṇḍyo vipālaṃ punar bhakṣaṇe pravṛttaṃ kṣetrasvāmī vārayet || 8.240 ||
kṣetreṣv anyeṣu tu paśuḥ sapādaṃ paṇam arhati |
sarvatra tu sado deyaḥ kṣetrikasyeti dhāraṇā || 8.241 ||
kṣetreṣv iti | sarvatra tv asitaṃ jñeyam iti | pathigrāmāntavyatirikteṣu kṣetreṣu paśur bhakṣayan sapādaṃ paṇadaṇḍaṃ arhati, arthāt pāla eva sarvasmiṃś ca kṣetre svāminaḥ paśubhakṣitaṃ phalaṃ pālena paśusvāminā vā yathāparādhaṃ dātavyam iti niścayaḥ || 8.241 ||
anirdaśāhāṃ gāṃ sūtāṃ vṛṣān devapaśūṃs tathā |
sapālān vā vipālān vā na daṇḍyān manur abravīt || 8.242 ||
anirdaśāhām iti | gāṃ prasūtām anirdaśāhāṃ tathā grahaṇadattachāgavṛṣabhādi pratimāsaṃ bandhipaśūn yavān rakṣāyuktān śūnyān vā sasyabhakṣaṇe ‘daṇḍārhān manur āha | na pālasya daṇḍo svāmina ity arthaḥ | paripālakopekṣānāṃ devaprakṛtīnāṃ paripālāya paśusaṃbandhiśaṅkāte ato vipāladevapaśuḥ sasyabhakṣakasya daṇḍa iti na codanīyaḥ || 8.242 ||
kṣetrikasyātyaye daṇḍo bhāgād daśaguṇo bhavet |
tato ‘rdhadaṇḍo bhṛtyānām ajñānāt kṣetrikasya tu || 8.243 ||
kṣetrikasyet | kṣetrasvāminaḥ ātmapaśusasyabhakṣaṇakālavapanasayāpahārādau aparādhe yāvato rājabhāgasya tena hāniḥ kṛtā tato daśaguṇo daṇḍaḥ syāt | bhṛtyānāṃ punaḥ kṣetrasvāmyavidita evaṃ kurvatāṃ daśaguṇād ardhadaṇḍaḥ syāt | tadaujñānāt tu tasyaiva daśaguṇo daṇḍa iti kṣetrāparādhe ‘pīḍamantram || 8.243 ||
etad vidhānam ātiṣṭhed dhārmikaḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ |
svāmināṃ ca paśūnāṃ ca pālānāṃ ca vyatikrame || 8.244 ||
etad iti | paśubhiḥ sasyabhakṣaṇena svāmināṃ pālānāṃ cāparādhe devapaśvādisasyabhakṣaṇe dharmapradhāno rājā etat pūrvoktaṃ kartavyam anutiṣṭhet || 8.244 ||
sīmāṃ prati samutpanne vivāde grāmayor dvayoḥ |
jyeṣṭhe māsi nayet sīmāṃ suprakāśeṣu setuṣu || 8.245 ||
sīmām iti | dvayor grāmamaryādāviṣayāyāṃ vipratipattau utpannāyāṃ jyeṣṭhe māsi grīṣme tāpasaṃśuṣkatṛṇatvāt prakaṭībhūteṣu sīmāliṅgeṣu rājā sīmāṃ niścinuyāt | sīmāparijñāne sarvadā loke janānāṃ bhrāntiṃ dṛṣṭvoktāni gṛhyāni vakṣyamāṇāni sīmā cihnāni kārayet || 8.245 ||
sīmāvṛkṣāṃś ca kurvīta nyagrodhāśvatthakiṃśukān |
śālmalīn sālatālāṃś ca kṣīriṇaś caiva pādapān || 8.246 ||
sīmāvṛkṣān iti |sīmāliṅgabhūtān nyagrodhādīn vṛkṣaviśeṣān kṣīriṇaś ca vṛkṣān udumbarādīn cirasthān kārayet || 8.246 ||
gulmān veṇūṃś ca vividhān śamīvallīsthalāni ca |
śarān kubjakagulmāṃś ca tathā simā na naśyati || 8.247 ||
gulmān iti | aprakāṇḍāḥ smṛtā iti kāṇḍābhidhānāt gulmān vṛkṣān vividhān veṇūṃś ca veṇukīcakaveṇvādīn nānāprakārān śamyākhyān mallikādivicitrān uptān bhūbhāgān śarān kubjakasaṃbandhino gulmān aciropabhogatvena pṛthak viniṣṭatvāt || 8.247 ||
taḍāgāny udapānāni vāpyaḥ prasravaṇāni ca |
sīmāsaṃdhiṣu kāryāṇi devatāyatanāni ca || 8.248 ||
taḍāgānīti | taḍāgakūpapuṣkariṇyudakapraṇītadevagṛhāṇi sīmāyāṃ grāmadvayasaṃdhisthāneṣūdakābhyarthitvena janasaṃsarge sati sākṣisaṃbhāvanārthe kartavyāni || 8.248 ||
upacchannāni cānyāni sīmāliṅgāni kārayet |
sīmājñāne nṛṇāṃ vīkṣya nityaṃ loke viparyayam || 8.249 ||
upacchannānīti | sīmāparijñāne sarvadā loke janānāṃ bhrāntiṃ dṛṣṭvā aduṣṭāni guhyāni vakṣyamāṇāni sīmācihnāni kārayet || 8.249 ||
aśmano ‘sthīni govālāṃs tuṣān bhasmakapālikāḥ |
karīṣam iṣṭakāṅgārāṃś charkarā vālukās tathā || 8.250 ||
yāni caivaṃprakārāṇi kālād bhūmir na bhakṣayet |
tāni saṃdhiṣu sīmāyā aprakāśāni kārayet || 8.251 ||
aśmana iti | yānīti | pāṣāṇāsthigovālatuṣabhasmakharparikāśuṣkagomayapakveṣṭakāṅgāra-dṛṣatkaṇikā sikatā anyāny apy evaṃvidhāni khadirasārikākālāñjanādīn tāni grāmasaṃdhiṣu pracchannāni sthāpayet || 8.250–251 ||
etair liṅgair nayet sīmāṃ rājā vivadamānayoḥ |
pūrvabhuktyā ca satatam udakasyāgamena ca || 8.252 ||
etair iti | vivadamānayor grāmayoḥ śūnyayor aśūnyayor vā etair uktaiś cihnair nṛpaḥ sīmāṃ niścinuyāt | aśūnyatve punar api pūrvabhuktyā ca śūnyatve ādhiḥ sīmety uktatvād udakapravāhe cānyakāraṇakena grāmayor yat saṃniveśakālavṛttena śūnyatā vasateḥ || 8.252 ||
yadi saṃśaya eva syāl liṅgānām api darśane |
sākṣipratyaya eva syāt sīmāvādaviniścayaḥ || 8.253 ||
yadīti | liṅgadarśane ‘pi īṣad ubhayatra sīmāṃ prati saṃśaya eva bhavet tadā sākṣipratyayaḥ sīmāvivādanirṇayaḥ syāt || 8.253 ||
grāmeyakakulānāṃ ca samakṣaṃ sīmni sākṣiṇaḥ |
praṣṭavyāḥ sīmaliṅgāni tayoś caiva vivādinoḥ || 8.254 ||
grāmeyakakulānām iti | grāmajanasamūhānāṃ grāmeṇa niyuktayoś ca vivādinoḥ pratyakṣasīmācihnāni sākṣiṇaḥ praṣṭavyāḥ || 8.254 ||
te pṛṣṭās tu yathā brūyuḥ samastāḥ sīmni niścayam |
nibadhnīyāt tathā sīmāṃ sarvāṃs tāṃś caiva nāmataḥ || 8.255 ||
te pṛṣṭās tu yathā brūyuḥ samastāḥ sīmni niścayam iti | te sākṣiṇaḥ pṛṣṭāḥ santo yena prakāreṇa samastān advaidhaṃ sīmāviṣayaṃ niścitaṃ brūyuḥ tena prakāreṇa smaraṇārthaṃ patrake sīmāṃ lekhya tāṃś ca sarvān eva sākṣiṇo nāmavibhāgato likhet || 8.255 ||
śirobhis te gṛhītvā vā sragviṇā raktavāsasaḥ |
sukṛtaiḥ śāpitāḥ svaiḥ svair nayeyus te samañjasam || 8.256 ||
śirobhir iti | te sākṣiṇaḥ kṛtamālyaraktavāsaso mūrdhani mṛlloṣṭhaṃ gṛhītvā yad asmākaṃ sukṛtaṃ tan niṣphalaṃ syād ity evamadibhiḥ sukṛtaiḥ śāpitāḥ samastā sīmāṃ satyād anapetāṃ kṛtvā niścinuyuḥ || 8.256 ||
yathoktena nayantas te pūyante satyasākṣiṇaḥ |
viparītaṃ nayantas tu dāpyāḥ syur dviśataṃ damam || 8.257 ||
yathokteneti | te satyapradhānāḥ sākṣiṇaḥ śāstroktena rūpeṇa niścayantaḥ satyābhidhānaniyamāḥ sarve pavitrā bhavanti | asatyaṃ punar niścayantaḥ pratyekaṃ paṇaśatadvayaṃ daṇḍaṃ dāpyāḥ syuḥ || 8.257 ||
sākṣyabhāve tu catvāro grāmasīmāntavāsinaḥ |
sīmāvinirṇayaṃ kuryuḥ prayatā rājasaṃnidhau || 8.258 ||
sākṣyabhāve tu catvāro grāmyāḥ sīmāntavāsinaḥ iti | tadgrāmadvayasya vivādībhūtasya sākṣiṇo ‘bhāve catasṛṣu dikṣu sīmāntavāsinaḥ catvāro grāmyāḥ sākṣidharmeṇa rājasamakṣaṃ sīmānirṇayaṃ kuryuḥ || 8.258 ||
sāmantānāṃ abhāve tu maulānāṃ sīmni sākṣiṇām |
imān apy anuyuñjīta puruṣān vanagocarān || 8.259 ||
sāmantānāṃ abhāve tu maulānāṃ sīmasākṣiṇām | itarān api yuñjīta puruṣān vanagocarān || grāmasaṃniveśakālād ārabhya tadgrāmābhijanānāṃ sīmāntavāsināṃ sīmāsākṣiṇām abhāve itarān api vakṣyamāṇān vanam abhyavahāriṇaḥ puruṣān pṛcchet || 8.259 ||
vyādhāṃś chākunikān gopān kaivartān mūlakhānakān |
vyālagrāhān uñchavṛttīn anyāṃś ca vanacāriṇaḥ || 8.260 ||
vyādhān iti | lubdhakān pakṣivadhajīvino gopālān taḍāgādikhananvṛttīn vṛkṣādimūlotpādakān sarpagrāhiṇo śiloñchajīvino ‘nyāṃśca phalapuṣpendhanādyarthaṃ vanavyavahāriṇaḥ pṛcched etair ihakāryārthaṃ grāmamadhyaṃ te sakṛd vanaṃ vrajanti || 8.260 ||
te pṛṣṭās tu yathā brūyuḥ sīmāsaṃdhiṣu lakṣaṇam |
tat tathā sthāpayed rājā dharmeṇa grāmayor dvayoḥ || 8.261 ||
ta iti | te vyādhadayaḥ pṛṣṭāḥ santaḥ sīmāyāḥ saṃdhir yathādeśaṃ yena prakāreṇa jñāpakaṃ kuryuḥ tat tena rūpeṇa pakṣapātaparihāreṇa rājā dvayor grāmayoḥ maryādāṃ sthāpayeta
|| 8.261 ||
kṣetrakūpataḍāgānām ārāmasya gṛhasya ca |
sāmantapratyayo jñeyaḥ sīmāsetuvinirṇayaḥ || 8.262 ||
sāmantāś cen mṛṣā brūyuḥ setau vivadatāṃ nṛṇām |
sarve pṛthak pṛthag daṇḍyā rājñā madhyamasāhasam || 8.263 ||
kṣetrakūpataḍāgānām iti | sāmantā iti | sīmānimittaṃ vivadamānānāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ yadi sīmāntevāsinaḥ mithyā brūyuḥ tadā rājñā te sarve pratyekaṃ madhyamasāhasaṃ daṇḍyāḥ | evaṃ ca sāmantarūpāṇāṃ dve śate damaḥ pūrvokto vijñeyaḥ || 8.262–263 ||
gṛhaṃ taḍāgam ārāmaṃ kṣetraṃ vā bhīṣayā haran |
śatāni pañca daṇḍyaḥ syād ajñānād dviśato damaḥ || 8.264 ||
gṛham iti | gṛhataḍāgodyānānm anyatamamāraṇabandhanabhayakathanapūrvakaṃ haran pañcaśatapaṇāni daṇḍanīyaḥ syād atra bhrāntyā tu harato dviśato daṇḍyaḥ || 8.264 ||
sīmāyām aviṣahyāyāṃ svayaṃ rājaiva dharmavit |
pradiśed bhūmim eteṣām upakārād iti sthitiḥ || 8.265 ||
sīmāyām iti | sīmāyāṃ liṅgasākṣyādyabhāvāt paricchetum aśakyāyāṃ rājā dharmajño ‘pakṣapātena grāmadvayasya madhyavartinīṃ bhūmiṃ yeṣām eva grāmīṇānāṃ tayā bhūmyā upakāro bhavati teṣām eveti maryādā || 8.265 ||
eṣo ‘khilenābhihito dharmaḥ sīmāvinirṇaye |
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi vākpāruṣyavinirṇayam || 8.266 ||
eṣa iti | eṣo ‘nantarokto dharmād anapetaḥ sīmāvinirṇayo niḥśeṣeṇoktaḥ | anantaraṃ paruṣabhāṣaṇaviṣayanirṇayaṃ prakarṣeṇa vakṣyāmi daṇḍapāruṣyato vākpāruṣyasya prāyeṇāsahatvam | prathamaṃ vākpāruṣyavicāro ‘nukramaṇyāṃ punaḥ pāruṣye daṇḍaḥ vācika iti vṛttānurodhād daṇḍaśabdasya pūrvābhidhānam || 8.266 ||
śataṃ brāhmaṇam ākruśya kṣatriyo daṇḍam arhati |
vaiśyo ‘py ardhaśataṃ dve vā śūdras tu vadham arhati || 8.267 ||
śatam iti | vādeṣv iti vakṣyamāṇatvān mātṛbhaginyāsyaślīlapatanīyavarjaṃ puruṣaṃ brāhmaṇam ākruśya paṇaśataṃ kṣatriyo vaiśyo ‘dhyardhaśataṃ dve vā śūdras tu vadham arhati | punar ākrośaviśeṣāpekṣayā tāḍanajihvākartanādyam arhati || 8.267 ||
pañcāśad brāhmaṇo daṇḍyaḥ kṣatriyasyābhimarśane |
vaiśye syād ardhapañcāśac chūdre dvādaśako damaḥ || 8.268 ||
pañcāśad iti | brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrāṇām uktādyapekṣā  pañcāśat pañcaviṃśatiḥ viṃśatiḥ cārdhadvāśapaṇān yathākramaṃ brāhmaṇo daṇḍyaḥ || 8.268 ||
samavarṇe dvijātīnāṃ dvādaśaiva vyatikrame |
vādeṣv avacanīyeṣu tad eva dviguṇaṃ bhavet || 8.269 ||
samavarṇa iti | dvijātīnāṃ samajātiviṣaye uktarūpe ākrośe kṛte vyatikrame sati dvādaśaiva paṇān daṇḍyaḥ avacanīyeṣu vādeṣu mātṛbhaginyādyaślīlarūpeṣu śataṃ brāhmaṇam ākruśyetyādi yad uktaṃ tad eva daṇḍaṃ dviguṇaṃ daṇḍād bhavati || 8.269 ||
ekajātir dvijātīṃs tu vācā dāruṇayā kṣipan |
jihvāyāḥ prāpnuyāc chedaṃ jaghanyaprabhavo hi saḥ || 8.270 ||
ekajātir iti | śūdro dvijātīn ghātakādyabhiyoginyā vācā ākruśya jihvācchedaṃ prāpnuyāt yad amī pādākhyanikṛṣṭa iti || 8.270 ||
nāmajātigrahaṃ tv eṣām abhidroheṇa kurvataḥ |
nikṣepyo ‘yomayaḥ śaṅkur jvalann āsye daśāṅgulḥ || 8.271 ||
nāmajātigraham iti | eṣāṃ dvijātīnām ākrośabuddhyā nāmoccārya jātyucchāraṇaṃ ca śūdrasya kṛtavataḥ āyasakīlo ‘gninā dīpyamāno daśāṅgulapramāṇa āsye prakṣeptavyaḥ || 8.271 ||
dharmopadeśaṃ darpeṇa viprāṇām asya kurvataḥ |
taptam āsecayet tailaṃ vaktre śrotre ca pārthivaḥ || 8.272 ||
dharmopadeśam iti | chadmanā kṛtaścid dharmaleśaṃ buddhvā ayaṃ te svadharma ity evam ahaṅkāreṇa brāhmaṇānāṃ śūdrasyopadiśataḥ āsye karṇayoś ca jvalat tailaṃ rājā prakṣepayet
|| 8.272 ||
śrutaṃ deśaṃ ca jātiṃ ca karma śārīram eva ca |
vitathena bruvan darpād dāpyaḥ syād dviśataṃ damam || 8.273 ||
śrutam iti | na tvayaitac chrutaṃ na bhavān etad deśābhijāto na taveyaṃ jātiḥ na tavopanayanādiśarīrasaṃskārakaṃ karma kṛtam ity evam anṛtam evvāmārṣīt samānajātiṃ bruvan dviśataṃ daṇḍaṃ dāpyaḥ syān na śūdraḥ prakṛto dvijātīnāṃ bruvan daṇḍalāghavāt || 8.273 ||
kāṇaṃ vāpy atha vā khañjam anyaṃ vāpi tathāvidham |
tathyenāpi bruvan dāpyo daṇḍaṃ kārṣāpaṇāvaram || 8.274 ||
kāṇam iti | kāṇaṃ pādavikalaṃ vā anyam api vā tathārūpam aṅgavikalam andhādikaṃ kāṇādiśabdena satyenāpi bruvan yadi sarvanikṛṣṭaṃ kārṣāpaṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ dāpyaḥ || 8.274 ||
mātaraṃ pitaraṃ jāyāṃ bhrātaraṃ tanayaṃ gurum |
ākṣārayañ chataṃ dāpyaḥ panthānaṃ cādadad guroḥ || 8.275 ||
mātaram iti | kṣāritākṣāritau sadbhir abhiśatāv udāhṛtāv iti trikāṇḍadarśanāt mātṛpitṛbhāryābhrātṛputragurūṇāṃ mahāpātakābhiśāpam utpādayan guroś ca panthānam adadac chataṃ daṇḍyaḥ | abhyāsānabhyāsākṣāraṇena vātra mātṛbhāryādīnāṃ daṇḍasya viṣamasīmākaraṇaṃ pariharaṇīyam || 8.275 ||
brāhmaṇakṣatriyābhyāṃ tu daṇḍaḥ kāryo vijānatā |
brāhmane sāhasaḥ pūrvaḥ kṣatriye tv eva madhyamaḥ || 8.276 ||
brāhmaṇakṣatriyābhyām iti | brāhmaṇakṣatriyābhyām itaretaraṃ patanīyākrośe kṛte sati tayoḥ śāstrābhijanadeśajātyādināyaṃ daṇḍaḥ kartavyaḥ | kṣatriyākrośe sati brāhmaṇe prathamasāhsaḥ kartavyaḥ | brāhmaṇākrośe nipuṇaḥ kṣatriye madhyamasāhasaḥ || 8.276 ||
viṭśūdrayor evam eva svajātiṃ prati tattvataḥ |
chedavarjaṃ praṇayanaṃ daṇḍasyeti viniścayaḥ || 8.277 ||
viṭśūdrayor iti | vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ parasparajātiṃ prati patanīyākrośe brāhmaṇakṣatriyavat | prathamamadhyamasāhasātmakaṃ jihvācchedavarjaṃ yathāvad daṇḍasya karaṇam | ekaṃ ca brāhmaṇakṣatriyākrośe | evaṃ śūdrasya jihvācchedanam avatiṣṭhate || 8.277 ||
eṣa daṇḍavidhiḥ prokto vākpāruṣyasya tattvataḥ |
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi daṇḍapāruṣyanirṇayam || 8.278 ||
eṣa iti | eṣo ‘nantaro vāksaṃbandhinaḥ pāruṣyasya daṇḍaprakāro yaḥ prāgukto ‘smād anantarahastakāṣṭhaśastrādisaṃbandhinaḥ pāruṣyasya duḥkhotpādanahetos tāḍanahiṃśanāder daṇḍanirṇayaṃ vakṣyāmi || 8.278 ||
yena kenacid aṅgena hiṃsyāc cec chreṣṭam antyajaḥ |
chettavyaṃ tat tad evāsya tan manor anuśāsanam || 8.279 ||
yeneti | antyajaḥ śūdro yena kenacid dhastapādādinā vā na sākṣād daṇḍādivyavahitena vā dvijam eva praharet tat tad aṅgam evāsya chedanīyam ity evaṃ amnusaṃbandhinī upadeśo manugrahaṇam ādarārtham asyaivotttaraṃ ca || 8.279 ||
pāṇim udyamya daṇḍaṃ vā pāṇicchedanam arhati |
pādena praharan kopāt pādacchedanam arhati || 8.280 ||
pāṇim udyamyeti | hastaṃ daṇḍaṃ vāvagūrya hastacchedanayogyo bhavati | pādena krodhena praharan pādacchedanārho bhavati || 8.280 ||
sahāsanam abhiprepsur utkṛṣṭasyāpakṛṣṭajaḥ |
kaṭyāṃ kṛtāṅko nirvāsyaḥ sphicaṃ vāsyāvakartayet || 8.281 ||
sahāsanam iti | brāhmaṇena sahaikasminn āsane nikṛṣṭajanmā śūdra upaviśan kaṭyāṃ kṛtataptāyaḥsthiracihno deśān nirvāsanīyaḥ | sphijākhyaṃ vā śroṣyadhokartanaṃ kuryāt || 8.281 ||
avaniṣṭhīvato darpād dvāv oṣṭhau chedayen nṛpaḥ |
avamūtrayato meḍhram avaśardhayato gudam || 8.282 ||
avaniṣṭhīvanād iti | niṣṭhīvanena śleṣmaṇāṃ marṣeṇa śleṣmāṇaṃ vamayan śūdrasya dvāv apy oṣṭhau chedayed evaṃ mūtreṇāvamānayato liṅgam adhamāṅgadhvanināvamānayataḥ pāyuṃ chedayet || 8.282 ||
keśeṣu gṛhṇato hasto chedayed avicārayan |
pādayor dāḍhikāyāṃ ca grīvāyāṃ vṛṣaṇeṣu ca || 8.283 ||
keśeṣv iti | keśapādaśmaśrugrīvāvṛṣaṇānāṃ cānyatamasmād brāhmaṇaṃ śūdrasya hastābhyām ākarṣayato hastāvilambamāno rājā chedayet || 8.283 ||
tvagbhedakaḥ śataṃ daṇḍyo lohitasya ca darśakaḥ |
māṃsabhettā tu ṣaṇṇiṣkān pravāsyas tv asthibhedakaḥ || 8.284 ||
tvagbhedaka iti | carmamātrabhedakṛt samānajātiḥ na tu śūdro brāhmaṇasya daṇḍalāghavāt paṇaśataṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ | tathā rudhirotpādī śatam eva daṇḍyo māṃsabhedakṛt ṣaḍ niṣkān daṇḍyo ‘sthibhedī deśān nirvāsyaḥ || 8.284 ||
vanaspatīnāṃ sarveṣām upabhogo yathā yathā |
tathā tathā damaḥ kāryo hiṃsāyām iti dhāraṇā || 8.285 ||
vanaspatīnāṃ sarveṣām upabhogo yathā yatheti | chāyādānātmakena nikṛṣṭa upabhogaḥ vanaspativṛkṣāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ yena yena prakāreṇa upabhogaḥ kusumādānarūpeṇa madhyamaḥ phalādānamāyatotkṛṣṭaḥ tadapekṣayā chedane daṇḍaḥ kāryaḥ | chāyāmātropabhogini tadādau svalpo daṇḍaḥ puṣpopbhogini mallikādāu madhyamaḥ phalopabhogini āmrādāv utkṛṣṭaḥ
|| 8.285 ||
manuṣyāṇāṃ paśūnāṃ ca duḥkhāya prahṛte sati |
yathā yathā mahad duḥkhaṃ daṇḍaṃ kuryāt tathā tathā || 8.286 ||
manuṣyānām iti | manuṣyāṇāṃ paśūnāṃ ca pīḍotpādanārthaṃ prahāre datte sati yathā yathā mahatī pīḍā tvagbhedādāv api marmabhedanādinā bhavati tathā tathā tvagbhedakarī śaraṃ daṇḍya ity evamādito ‘dhikam api daṇḍaṃ kuryāt || 8.286 ||
aṅgāvapīḍanāyāṃ ca prāṇaśoṇitayos tathā |
samutthānavyayaṃ dāpyaḥ sarvadaṇḍam athāpi vā || 8.287 ||
aṅgāvapīḍanāyāṃ ca prāṇaśoṇitayos tathā | samutthānavyayaṃ dāpyaḥ sarvadaṇḍam athāpi vā | aṅgenodarabāhvādīnāṃ yena vastrādibandhanādinā pīḍanāni kṛtāni tathā prāṇasya vāyor yena nirodhādinā pīḍanāni kṛtāni rudhirasyāpi yena dṛḍhamuṣṭirajjvādyākarṣaṇena bahiḥsravaṇavarjitam api pīḍanaṃ kṛtaṃ saśarīrasya prāg rūpāpattyutpādakam auṣadhādivyayaṃ tasya rājñā dāpanīyaḥ | ātmīyaś ca daṇḍam asau samutthānavyayaṃ na gṛhṇāti tadā tad vyayayaṃ daṇḍaṃ cobhayam api daṇḍārthaṃ rājñā dāpanīyaḥ || 8.287 ||
dravyāṇi hiṃsyād yo yasya jñānato ‘jñānato ‘pi vā |
sa tasyotpādayet tuṣṭiṃ rājñe dadyāc ca tatsamam || 8.288 ||
dravyāṇīti | carmacārmikādi vakṣyamāṇam atiriktāni kaṭakāṅgadādīni dravyāṇi yasya saṃbandhīni yo haṭhāt pramādād vā nāśayet sa tasya pratisaṃskārādinā tuṣṭim utpādayed rājñe vināśitadravyasamānaṃ daṇḍaṃ dadyāt || 8.288 ||
carmacārmikabhāṇḍeṣu kāṣṭhaloṣṭhamayeṣu ca |
mūlyāt pañcaguṇo daṇḍaḥ puṣpamūlaphaleṣu ca || 8.289 ||
carmacārmikabhāṇdeṣv iti | carmeṣu carmakāṣṭhamṛṇmayeṣu bhāṇḍeṣu puṣpamūlaphaleṣu ca parakīyeṣu ca nāsti teṣu tanmūlyāt pañcaguṇo daṇḍo rājñādeyaḥ tuṣṭyutpattiś ca svāminaḥ kāryāḥ || 8.289 ||
yānasya caiva yātuś ca yānasvāmina eva ca |
daśātivartanāny āhuḥ śeṣe daṇḍo vidhīyate || 8.290 ||
yānasyeti | yānasya gantryāder yātuś ca sārathyāder yānasvāminaḥ yatsaṃbandhiyānaṃ teṣāṃ chinnanāsāsyatvādīni vakṣyamāṇāni nimittādīni ativartanāni daṇḍaṃ cātikramya vartante | teṣu satsu yānena prāṇihiṃsādravyavināśayor api kṛtayor yānasvāmyādīnāṃ sārathye ity evaṃ sārathyādisaṃbandhivāgdāne sati yānena prāṇihiṃsādravyavināśayoḥ sārathyādeḥ daṇḍo na bhavatīti manvādaya āhus tan na, nimitavyatirekeṣu punar daṇḍaḥ kriyate || 8.290 ||
yatra nāsti doṣas tāni tāvad āha |
chinnanāsye bhagnayuge tiryakpratimukhāgate |
akṣabhaṅge ca yānasya cakrabhaṅge tathaiva ca || 8.291 ||
chedane caiva yantrāṇāṃ yoktraraśmyor tathaiva ca |
ākrandane cāpy apaihīti na daṇḍaṃ manur abravīt || 8.292 ||
chinnanāsya iti | chedana iti | chinnanāsikārajjau balīvarde bhagne yugmākhyakāṣṭhe gantryādau bhūmiviṣamādinā tiraścīnaṃ vā gataṃ pratīcīnaṃ vā tathā yānasya gantyādeṣv akṣamapraviśya kīlakādi tasya bhaṅge yantrāṇāṃ ca carmabandhānāṃ chedane yoktrākhyapaśugrīvākāṣṭharajjuchedane ‘pasarāpasarety evaṃ sārathyādisaṃbandhini cāhvāne sati yānena prāṇihiṃsādravyavināśayoḥ sārathyāder daṇḍo na bhavati iti manur āha || 8.291–92 ||
yatrāpavartate yugyaṃ vaiguṇyāt prājakasya tu |
tatra svāmī bhaved daṇḍyo hiṃsāyāṃ dviśataṃ damam || 8.293 ||
yatrāpavartate yugmam iti | yatra punaḥ sārather akauśalyāt yānam anyathā vrajati tatra hiṃsāyām akuśalasārathikaraṇādyān asvāmī dviśataṃ daṇḍaṃ dāpyaḥ | sāratheś ca manuṣyamāraṇe saty api avilambitatvaṃ kṣipram ity eva sārathir vakṣyamāṇo bhavati | evaṃ ca dviśatagrahasyottaraśloke ca śatagrahaṇasya vivakṣitatvam āhus tad asad apramādābhidhāyitatvād ṛṣeḥ || 8.293 ||
prājakaś ced bhaved āptaḥ prājako daṇḍam arhati |
yugyasthāḥ prājake ‘nāpte sarve daṇḍyāḥ śataṃ śatam || 8.294 ||
prājaka iti | yadi punaḥ sārathiḥ kuśalas tadā sārathir eva manuṣyamāraṇe ityādi vakṣyamāṇam arhati daṇḍaṃ na svāmī dviśatam akuśale sārathau svāmyatiriktā anye ‘pi yānārūḍhāḥ akuśalasārathiyānārohaṇāt sarve śataṃ śataṃ daṇḍyāḥ | sārathis tu manuṣyamāraṇa ityādisthita eva || 8.294 ||
sa cet tu pathi saṃruddhaḥ paśubhir vā rathena vā |
pramāpayet prāṇabhṛtas tatra daṇḍo ‘vicāritaḥ || 8.295 ||
sa iti | sa prājakaḥ saṃmukhāgatayānāntarād avaruddhair balīvardādibhiḥ saṃruddhayāno rathāntareṇa va bhūmivaiṣamyāt pratyag apasarpiṇā akuśalatvāt prāṇino rathapramāpayet tatra daṇḍo manvādibhir vicāritaḥ || 8.295 ||
manuṣyamāraṇe kṣipraṃ cauravat kilbiṣaṃ bhavet |
prāṇabhṛtsu mahatsv arthaṃ gogamoṣṭrahayādiṣu || 8.296 ||
manuṣyamāraṇa iti | prājakasyākauśalena manuṣyamāraṇe saty api vilambatvam eva coravat tasya uttamasāhaso na tu vadhādicoradaṇḍaḥ | prāṇabhṛtsu mahatsv ardhadarśanān mahatsu ca prāṇiṣu prabhāvato gavādiṣu pramāṇataḥ syāt | (māriteṣu ahimahiṣeṣu) uttamasya sāhasapañcaśatāni daṇḍo bhavet || 8.296 ||
kṣudrakāṇāṃ paśūnāṃ tu hiṃsāyāṃ dviśato damaḥ |
pañcāśat tu bhaved daṇḍaḥ śubheṣu mṛgapakṣiṣu || 8.297 ||
kṣudrakāṇām iti | ajāvikānāṃ vakṣyamāṇatvād apacitapramāṇānāṃ vatsakiśorādīnāṃ paśūnāṃ hiṃsāyām dviśato daṇdaḥ kāryaḥ | punar mṛgapakṣiṣu varāhādiṣu hiṃsāyāṃ pañcāśatpaṇo daṇḍo bhavet || 8.298 ||
gardabhājāvikānāṃ tu daṇḍaḥ syāt pāñcamāṣikaḥ |
māṣikas tu bhaved daṇḍaḥ śvasūkaranipātane || 8.298 ||
gardabhājāvikānām iti | kharacchāgāviviṣayavadhe rūpyamāṣakaparimāṇo daṇḍaḥ syād uttarottaram apacitaṃ daṇḍābhidhānadarśanād ahairaṇyaṃ māṣagrahaṇaṃ pūrvadaṇḍānūnatvān nāpi tāmrikasya karaṇam atyantaladhutvodayaṃ śvaśūkaramāraṇe ‘pi rūpyamāṣakaparimāṇo daṇḍaḥ syāt || 8.298 ||
bhāryā putraś ca dāsaś ca preṣyo bhrāta ca sodaraḥ |
prāptāparādhās tāḍyāḥ syū rajjvā veṇudalena vā || 8.299 ||
bhāryā śiṣyaś ca dāsaś ca putro bhrātā ca sodara iti | bhāryādayaḥ kṛtāparādhā rajjvā veṇutvacā vā daṇḍanīyāḥ syur iti hiṃsādaṇḍoparādhārthaḥ | sādhane daṇḍyāḥ syuḥ || 8.299 ||
pṛṣṭhatas tu śarīrasya nottamāṅge kathaṃcana |
ato ‘nyathā tu praharan prāptaḥ syāc caurakilbiṣam || 8.300 ||
pṛṣṭhata iti | te punaḥ śarīrasya pṛṣṭhadeśe tāḍanīyāḥ na tu kadācitd uttmāṅge na vakṣasi uktaprakāravyatirekeṇa tāḍayitvā daṇḍarūpeṇa vāgdaṇḍarūpa cauradaṇdaṃ prāpnuyāt || 8.300 ||
eṣo ‘khilenābhihito daṇḍapāruṣyanirṇayaḥ |
stenasyātaḥ pravakṣyāmi vidhiṃ daṇḍavinirṇaye || 8.301 ||
eṣa iti | anantarokto vāgdaṇḍaḥ pāruṣyasya nirṇayo niḥśeṣeṇoktaḥ | ata ūrdhvaṃ cauradaṇḍasya vyavasthāyā vidhānaṃ vakṣyāmi || 8.301 ||
paramaṃ yatnam ātiṣṭhet stenānāṃ nigrahe nṛpaḥ |
stenānāṃ nigrahād asya yaśo rāṣṭraṃ ca vardhate || 8.302 ||
paramam iti | cauraniyame prakṛṣṭam abhiyogaṃ rājā kuryāt yasmāt tannigrahāt rājñaḥ prajāḥ nirupdravatayā ca deśo janadhanabāhulyena vṛddhim eti || 8.302 ||
abhayasya hi yo dātā sa pūjyaḥ satataṃ nṛpaḥ |
sattraṃ hi vardhate tasya sadaivābhyadakṣiṇam || 8.303 ||
abhayasyeti | caurapāpasya nigraheṇa sādhūnām abhayaṃ dadāti sa sadā sarvasya pūjyo bhavati | yasmāt sattram ṛtuviśeṣavat stenanigrahaḥ saṃpadyate | stenanigrahākhyaṃ tasyānyasatravilakṣaṇam atiśayena saṃpadyate | anyat kila satraṃ niyatakālaṃ bhavati idaṃ sadaiva bhavati cādakṣiṇaṃ cānyat satram | idaṃ punar abhayaṃ dakṣiṇā yasya tad abhayadakṣiṇam || 8.303 ||
sarvato dharmaṣaḍbhāgo rājño bhavati rakṣataḥ |
adharmād api ṣaḍbhāgo bhavaty asya hy arakṣataḥ || 8.304 ||
sarvata iti | sarvato bhṛtipradānavyatiriktāc ca śrotriyādīnāṃ sādhūnāṃ sakāśād dharmaṣaḍbhāgo rājñaḥ prajārakṣaṇād bhavati | arakṣataś cādharmād api loke cauryabhāgāt ṣaḍbhāgo ‘sya bhavati tasmād yatnataḥ stenanigraheṇa prajāsaṃrakṣaṇaṃ kuryāt | na ca vṛttiparikrītatvād rājño dharmaprāptir ayuktā vṛttiparikrayavad dharmabhāgaparikrayasyāpi śāstrīyatvāt || 8.304 ||
yad adhīte yad yajate yad dadāti yad arcati |
tasya ṣaḍbhāgabhāg rājā samyag bhavati rakṣaṇāt || 8.305 ||
yad adhīta iti | yaḥ kaścid adhyayanayajanayājanadevatārcanādi yat phalaṃ tasya phalasya rājñā samyak prajāpālanāt ṣaḍbhāgaḥ prāpyate || 8.305 ||
rakṣan dharmeṇa bhūtāni rājā vadhyāṃś ca ghātayan |
yajate ‘har ahar yajñaiḥ sahasraśatadakṣiṇaiḥ || 8.306 ||
rakṣan dharmeṇeti | bhūtāni sthāvarajaṅgamāni śāstravyavasthayā rājā rakṣan vadhyān stenādīn yathāśāstraṃ ghātayan lakṣadakṣiṇānāṃ yajñānāṃ saṃbandhiphalaṃ pratyaham arjayati | tatra mahāprayāsakarmasaṃbandhiphalaṃ katham alpaprayāsāt karmaṇaḥ prāpyate tathā sati ko nāma prayāseṣu varteteti codanīyam | phalopabhogakālālpabhūyastve ca viśeṣasaṃbhavāt | evam anyatrāpi draṣṭavyam || 8.306 ||
yo ‘rakṣan balim ādatte karaṃ śulkaṃ ca pārthivaḥ |
pratibhāgaṃ ca daṇḍaṃ ca sa sadyo narakaṃ vrajet || 8.307 ||
yo ‘rakṣann iti | bhūtibhogaṃ ceti | yo rājā prajāsaṃrakṣaṇam akurvan dhānyādeḥ ṣaḍbhāgādikaṃ karaṃ gulmadāsyādikaṃ sthalapathādijīvibhyo gatāgatibhyo bhūter bhogaṃ phalaṃ phalakusumādyupāyena śulkaṃ daṇḍaṃ vyavahārādau gṛhṇāt | sa āyuḥkṣayeṇa satyam eva narakaṃ yāti || 8.307 ||
arakṣitāram attāraṃ baliṣaḍbhāgahāriṇam |
tam āhuḥ sarvalokasya samagramalahārakam || 8.308 ||
arakṣitāram iti | yo rājā na rakṣati anyac ca dānyādiṣaḍbhāgaṃ balirūpaṃ gṛhṇāti taṃ sakalalokasaṃbandhisarvapāpasvīkāriṇaṃ manvādyā āhuḥ || 8.308 ||
anavekṣitamaryādaṃ nāstikaṃ vipralumpakam |
arakṣitāram attāraṃ nṛpaṃ vidyād adhogatim || 8.309 ||
anapekṣitamaryādam iti | atikrāntaśāstrācāramaryādaṃ paralokābhāvabuddhim asaddaṇḍādidhanacchednaṃ karabaliyāder ca rājānaṃ narakagāminaṃ jānīyāt || 8.309 ||
adhārmikaṃ tribhir nyāyair nigṛhṇīyāt prayatnataḥ |
nirodhanena bandhena vividhena vadhena ca || 8.310 ||
adhārmikam iti | adhārmikaṃ cauram aparādhāpekṣayā tribhir niyamanaprakāraiḥ prayatnena niyacchet | bandhanāgārasthāpananigaḍādibandhanakaracaraṇakartanādi nānāprakārahiṃsanair iti || 8.310 ||
nigraheṇa hi pāpānāṃ sādhūnāṃ saṃgraheṇa ca |
dvijātaya ivejyābhiḥ pūyante satataṃ nṛpāḥ || 8.311 ||
nigraheṇeti | yasmāt pāpakarmaṇo nigraheṇa sādhvācārāṇāṃ cānugraheṇa rājāno dvijātaya iva mahāyajñādibhiḥ sarvakālaṃ pavitrī bhavanti tasmād adhārmikaṃ nigṛhṇīyāt || 8.311 ||
kṣantavyaṃ prabhuṇā nityaṃ kṣipatāṃ kāryiṇāṃ nṛṇām |
bālavṛddhāturāṇāṃ ca kurvatā hitam ātmane || 8.312 ||
kṣantavyam iti | bālavṛddhāturāṇāṃ ca kurvatāṃ hitam ātmane | kāryavatāṃ kṣipatāṃ stenahatabāndavādīnāṃ santāpenākrośatāṃ bālavṛddhavyādhitānāṃ cākrośatāṃ ātmane vakṣyamāṇam upakāram icchatāṃ pārthivena sarvadā kṣamaṇīyam || 8.312 ||
yaḥ kṣipto marṣayaty ārtais tena svarge mahīyate |
yas tv aiśvaryān na kṣamate narakaṃ tena gacchati || 8.313 ||
yat kṣipto marṣayatīti | saṃtaptair ākruṣṭo yat teṣāṃ kṣamate tena svargaloke mahīyate mahimānaṃ bhajate yat punaḥ prabhutvdarpān na sahate tena narakaṃ vrajati || 8.313 ||
rājā stenena gantavyo muktakeśena dhīmatā |
ācakṣāṇena tat steyam evaṃkarmāsmi śādhi mām || 8.314 ||
skandhenādāya muśalaṃ laguḍaṃ vāpi khādiram |
śaktiṃ cobhayatas tīkṣṇām āyasaṃ daṇḍam eva vā || 8.315 ||
rājeti | skandheneti | suvarṇasteyakṛd vipra ityādinā prāyaścittaprakaraṇe vakṣyamāṇas tena kartavyaḥ anena ślokadvayena asmin daṇḍaprakaraṇe ‘nūdyate suvarṇastenaṃ prati daṇḍākhyarājakartavyasyopadeśārthaḥ | brāhmaṇasuvarṇacaureṇa muktakeśenādarād vegagatinā brāhmaṇasuvarṇam idaṃ mayāpahṛtam ity evaṃ tatsteyaṃ khyāpayatā muśalākhyenāyudhaṃ khadiramayaṃ vā laguḍaṃ śaktyākhyaṃ cāyudhaṃ tīkṣṇobhayaprāntam ayomayaṃ vā daṇḍaṃ skandhena gṛhītvā rājasamīpaṃ gantavyam | gatvā ca brāhmaṇasuvarṇāpahārī cāham anena muśalādinā māṃ jahīty evaṃ rājño vaktavyam || 8.314–315 ||
śāsanād vā vimokṣād vā stenaḥ steyād vimucyate |
aśāsitvā tu taṃ rājā stenasyāpnoti kilbiṣam || 8.316 ||
śāsanād iti | praśādhi tveti | rājasaṃbandhihananena parityāgena vā sa cauraṃs tasmāt pāpāt vimucyate | taṃ punaḥ stenaṃ na jātu brāhmaṇaṃ hanyāt sarvapāpeṣv api sthitam iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt brāhmaṇatarjanahantā rājā tasya saṃbandhi yat pāpaṃ prāpnoti tasmād asau rājñā hantavyaḥ | ity evaṃparam etat sarvam ihābhidhānam || 8.316 ||
annāde bhrūṇahā mārṣṭi patyau bhāryāpacāriṇī |
gurau śiṣyaś ca yājyaś ca steno rājani kilbiṣam || 8.317 ||
yasmād annāda iti | yo brāhmhā sa tatsaṃbandhi annam atti tadviṣayam ātmīyaṃ pāpaṃ śodhayati pāpaṃ tasya bhavatīty evam etad evātra vivakṣitam | na tu bhrūṇapāpaprāyaścittadhiyā | tathaivaṃ bhāryā vyabhicāriṇī upapatiṃ kṣamamāṇe bhartari pāpaṃ taṃ saṃśleṣayati | śiṣyaś ca yamaniyamānanuṣṭhānajanyaṃ pāpaṃ gurau sahamāne arpayati | yājyaś ca yāge ‘nyathā pracaran yājake kṣamamāṇe prakṣipati | stenaś ca rājani sahamāne saty asati tasmād rājñā vaktavyaḥ || 8.317 ||
rājabhir dhṛtadaṇḍās tu kṛtvā pāpāni mānavāḥ |
nirmalāḥ svargam āyānti santaḥ sukṛtino yathā || 8.318 ||
rājabhir iti | te samuddhṛtāḥ pāpāni kṛtvā pūrvarjanaṃ tathā kratuvarān kṛtvā svargam apāpāḥ santaḥ śiṣṭā iva śubhakāriṇo vrajanti | ye tatpāpanimittaṃ rājani kṛtadaṇḍā bhavanti iha pāpānīti bahuvacanāt hiraṇyastenaviṣaya eva daṇḍyaḥ na kāryaniṣkṛtiḥ api tu sakalainaḥ svaviṣaye vyavasīyate na ca svayam āgataviṣayaiva daṇḍena pāpaniṣkṛtiḥ na haṭhadaṇḍitaviṣaye mantavyam | haṭhadaṇḍitasyāpi yadi saṃsādhayet taccityādiniṣkṛter darśitatvād yāmyayātanābhir vā kathaṃ paravaśasya niṣkṛtiḥ syāt || 8.318 ||
yas tu rajjuṃ ghaṭaṃ kūpād dhared bhindyāc ca yaḥ prapām |
sa daṇḍaṃ prāpnuyān māṣaṃ tac ca tasmin samāharet || 8.319 ||
ya iti | yaḥ kūpād rajjughaṭam apaharet yo vā udakadānagṛhaṃ vināśayet [vidāreyet] sarūpyaṃ suvarṇaṃ vā māṣaṃ sa rājñā sakaleśādyapekṣayā daṇḍaṃ prāpnuyāt | tatra ca rajjvādi tasmin kūpe pratyānayet || 8.319 ||
dhānyaṃ daśabhyaḥ kumbhebhyo harato ‘bhyadhikaṃ vadhaḥ |
śeṣe ‘py ekādaśaguṇaṃ dāpyas tasya ca tad dhanam || 8.320 ||
dhānyam iti | dvipalaṃ śataṃ droṇaṃ viṃśatidroṇā ekakumbhaḥ | daśakumbhebhyo ‘dhikaṃ dhānyaṃ harato hartṛkriyamāṇaguṇāpekṣayā subhikṣadurbhikṣādyapekṣayā ca tāḍanāṅgacchedanādyātmako vadhadaṇḍaḥ kartavyo rājñā daśakumbhaṃ punar yāvad apahṛtam ekādaśaguṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ dāpyaḥ | tasya ca tad dhanaṃ dāpyaḥ | vakṣyamāṇaṃ ca sarvatra svāmino dāpyaḥ || 8.320 ||
tathā dharimameyānāṃ śatād abhyadhike vadhaḥ |
suvarṇarajatādīnām uttamānāṃ ca vāsasām || 8.321 ||
tatheti | suvarṇarajatādīnāṃ mahārghāṇāṃ vāsasām | na kevalaṃ dhānye yathā pūrvokto yāvat tulāchedyānām api suvarṇarajatapravālādīnām utkṛṣṭānāṃ ca vāsasāṃ cāntaḥpaṭṭādīnāṃ etac chatād abhyadike ‘pahṛte vadhaḥ kartavyaḥ | viṣamasamīkaraṇaṃ cātra deśakālāpahṛtadravyaṃ svāmijātiguṇādyapekṣayā pariharaṇīyam | anyathā mahārghālapārghayoḥ tulyadaṇḍatā syād evaṃ draṣṭavyam || 8.321 ||
pañcāśatas tv abhyadhike hastacchedanam iṣyate |
śeṣe tv ekādaśaguṇaṃ mūlyād daṇḍaṃ prakalpayet || 8.322 ||
pañcāśata iti | eṣāṃ pūrvanirdiṣṭānāṃ pañcāśata ūrdhvaṃ śatasya yāvad apahṛte caitat hastacchedanaṃ manvādibhir ucyate | pañcāśataḥ pañcāśataḥ yāvad apahṛte tanmūlyād ekādaśaguṇaṃ rājā daṇḍaṃ kuryāt || 8.322 ||
puruṣāṇāṃ kulīnānāṃ nārīṇāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
mukhyānāṃ caiva ratnānāṃ haraṇr vadham arhati || 8.323 ||
puruṣāṇām iti | mahākulānāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ viśeṣeṇa ca strīṇāṃ mahākulotpannānāṃ pradhānānāṃ maṇīnāṃ vajravaiḍuryādīnāṃ haraṇe deśakālahartṛsvāmijātyādyapekṣayā tāḍanāṅgacchedanatmakaṃ vadham arhati || 8.323 ||
mahāpaśūnāṃ haraṇe śastrāṇām auṣadhasya ca |
kālam āsādya kāryaṃ ca daṇḍaṃ rājā prakalpayet || 8.324 ||
mahāpaśūnām iti | hastyaśvagomahiṣādīnāṃ mahatāṃ paśūnāṃ haraṇe khaḍgādīnāṃ kalyāṇaghṛtādeś ca kālavigrahadurbhikṣātmakaṃ kāryaṃ cāpahāraprayojanaṃ dyutavairarajjvavasādādirūpaṃ paryālocya rājanyūnādhikaṃ daṇḍaṃ prakalpayet | anubandhaṃ parijñāya naitallāghavārtham api sādarārtham ucyate śastrauṣadhahastādyapahāre vigrahādau sthūlānarthotpādanāt || 8.324 ||
goṣu brāhmaṇasaṃsthāsu nāsikāyāś ca bhedane |
paśūnāṃ haraṇe caiva sadyaḥ kāryo ‘rdhapādikaḥ || 8.325 ||
goṣu brāhmaṇasaṃsthāsu nāsikāyāś ca bhedana iti | brāhmaṇasaṃbandhīnāṃ gavām apaharaṇe, evaṃ vandhyāyāś ca goḥ vāhanādhyarthaṃ nāsikābhedane paśūnāṃ caiḍakādīnāṃ daṇḍabhūyastād yāgādyarthānām apaharaṇe samanantaram eva pādārdhacchedayukto daṇḍaḥ kāryaḥ || 8.325 ||
sūtrakārpāsakiṇvānāṃ gomayasya guḍasya ca |
dadhnaḥ kṣīrasya takrasya pānīyasya tṛṇasya ca || 8.326 ||
veṇuvaidalabhāṇḍānāṃ lavaṇānāṃ tathaiva ca |
mṛṇmayānāṃ ca haraṇe mṛdo bhasmana eva ca || 8.327 ||
matsyānāṃ pakṣiṇāṃ caiva tailasya ca ghṛtasya ca |
māṃsasya madhunaś caiva yac cānyat paśusaṃbhavam || 8.328 ||
anyeṣāṃ caivamādīnām adyānām odanasya ca |
pakvānnānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ tanmūlyād dviguṇo damaḥ || 8.329 ||
sūtrakārpāsakiṇvānām iti | veṇuvaidalabhāṇḍānām iti | ūrṇādisūtrasya kiṇvākhyasya ca turādravyasya takrasya gorasasya vaidalānāṃ ca veṇvāditvaṅnirmitānāṃ bhāṇḍānāṃ lavaṇānāṃ saindhavabiḍalavaṇādīnāṃ madhunaḥ mākṣikasya yad apy alpatarapaśuprabhavaṃ śṛṅgadantādi tasya tathānyeṣām apy evaṃvidhānāṃ ca sāradravyāṇāṃ manaḥśilasindūrādīnāṃ odanasya ca bhaktasaktvādeḥ pakvānnānāṃ cāpūpamodakānāṃ sarveṣāṃ ca kārpāsādīnāṃ ca prasiddhānāṃ haraṇān mūlyād dviguṇo daṇḍaḥ kāryaḥ || 8.326–329 ||
puṣpeṣu harite dhānye gulmavallīnageṣu ca |
anyeṣv aparipūteṣu daṇḍaḥ syāt pañcakṛṣṇalaḥ || 8.330 ||
puṣpeṣv iti | puṣpeṣu nīlīkṣetreṣu dhānyeṣu gulmalatāvṛkṣeṣu puruṣabhāraprāyeṣu vakṣyamāṇāc chlokāt parivapanasaṃbhavāc ca dhānyeṣu khalavāsitāpahṛteṣu deśakālāpekṣayā suvarṇasya rūpyasya vā pāñcakṛṣṇalaparimāṇo daṇḍaḥ syāt || 8.330 ||
paripūteṣu dhānyeṣu śākamūlaphaleṣu ca |
niranvaye śataṃ daṇḍaḥ sānvaye ‘rdhaśataṃ damaḥ || 8.331 ||
paripūteṣv iti | apāsiteṣu śeṣeṣu śākādiṣu vāpahṛteṣu svāminā saha saṃgrāme ‘pahāre paṇaśataṃ daṇḍaḥ | saṃgateṣu punaḥ pañcāśataṃ daṇḍaḥ || 8.331 ||
syāt sāhasaṃ tv anvayavat prasabhaṃ karma yat kṛtam |
niranvayaṃ bhavet steyaṃ hṛtāpavyayate ca yat || 8.332 ||
syāt sāhasam iti | yat karmadhānyāpahārādidravyasvāmisamakṣam eva prasahya kṛtaṃ tat sāhasaṃ syād atas tatra steyadaṇḍo na kāryaḥ ity evamarthasteyaprakārasyopadeśaḥ | yat punaḥ parokṣaṃ kṛtaṃ tat steyaṃ bhavet | yady api ca hṛtvāpahnute tad api steyam eva || 8.332 ||
yaś caitāny upakḷptāni dravyāṇi stenayen naraḥ |
tasmād yaṃ daṇḍayed rājā yaś cāgniṃ corayed gṛhāt || 8.333 ||
yaś caitānīti | etāni sūtrādīni dravyāṇi upabhogārtham udyamitāni upabhogārtham udyamitāni yo manuṣtaś corayet yaś cāgniṃ laukikam api corayet taṃ prathamasāhasaṃ rājā daṇḍayet | agneś ca mūlyavyavahārāprasiddhes tanmūlyāt dviguṇo dama ity etadasaṃbhave sate sūtrādibhyaḥ pṛthag grahaṇam || 8.333 |||
yena yena yathāṅgena steno nṛṣu viceṣṭate |
tat tad eva haret tasya pratyādeśāya pārthivaḥ || 8.334 ||
yena yeneti | yac ca yena hastapādādikāṅgena yena prakāreṇa granthicchedaniḥśreṇyārohaṇādinā cauro manuṣyeṣu viruddhaṃ dhanāpahārādi kartum īhate tat tad evāṅgaṃ tasyābhyāsapravṛttau satyāṃ tadaparādhacchedanāya rājā chedayet || 8.334 ||
pitācāryaḥ suhṛn mātā bhāryā putraḥ purohitaḥ |
nādaṇḍyo nāma rājño ‘sti yaḥ svadharme na tiṣṭhati || 8.335 ||
pitācārya iti | pitrācāryamitramātṛbhāryāputrapurohitānām madhyādyaḥ svadharme na pravarteta sa nādaṇḍanīyo nāma rājño vartate, api tu so ‘pi daṇḍanīyaḥ | pitṛmātrādīnāṃ gaurvasnehābhyāṃ adaṇḍaśaṅkāyām idaṃ vacanam || 8.335 ||
kārṣāpaṇaṃ bhaved daṇḍyo yatrānyaḥ prākṛto janaḥ |
tatra rājā bhaved daṇḍyaḥ sahasram iti dhāraṇā || 8.336 ||
kārṣāpaṇam iti | yasminn aparādhe rājavyatirikto janaḥ paṇaṃ daṇḍanīyo bhavet tasminn aparādhe rājā paṇasahasraṃ daṇḍanīya iti niścayaḥ | apsu praveśya taṃ daṇḍam ityādi vakṣyamāṇena rājātmako daṇḍaḥ prakṣeptavyo brāhmaṇāya vā deyaḥ || 8.336 ||
aṣṭāpādya tu śūdrasya steye bhavati kilbiṣam |
ṣoḍaśaiva tu vaiśyasya dvātriṃśat kṣatriyasa ca || 8.337 ||
brāhmaṇasya catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ pūrṇaṃ vāpi śataṃ bhavet |
dviguṇā vā catuḥṣaṣṭis taddoṣaguṇavid dhi saḥ || 8.338 ||
aṣṭāpādym iti | brāhmaṇasyeti | yasmin steye yo daṇḍa uktaḥ sa guṇdoṣajñasya śūdrasyāṣṭabhir āpadyate guṇato ‘ṣṭaguṇaḥ kāryaḥ | ṣoḍaśaguṇo viduṣo vaiśyasya dvātriṃśadguṇaḥ śāstroktasya kṣatriyasya catuḥṣaṣṭiguṇo brāḥmaṇasya dvicatuḥṣaṣṭiguṇo vā pūrṇaṃ vā śataṃ guṇātiśayāpekṣayā yasmād asau brāhmaṇaḥ steyaguṇadoṣajñaḥ || 8.337–338 ||
vānaspatyaṃ mūlaphalaṃ dārvagnyarthaṃ tathaiva ca |
tṛṇaṃ ca gobhyo grāsārtham asteyaṃ manur abravīt || 8.339 ||
vānaspatyam iti | “puṣpāṇi phalāni aparivṛttānām” iti gautamasmaraṇād aparivṛttavanaspatyādisaṃbandhimūlaphalaṃ gavāgnisāhacaryaṃ dṛṣṭārthaṃ śāstritāgnyarthaṃ ca dāru gatyantarābhāve cātra gogrāsārthaṃ tṛṇaṃ parakīyam api asteyaṃ manur āha ataś cātra daṇḍādyabhāvaḥ || 8.339 ||
yo ‘dattādāyino hastāl lipseta brāhmaṇo dhanam |
yājanādhyāpanenāpi yathā stenas tathaiva saḥ || 8.340 ||
ya iti | adattagrāhiṇaś caurasya brāhmaṇo yājanādhyāpanapratigrahair api parakīyaṃ dhanam aṇv api gṛhṇāti so ‘pi cauro vijñeyaḥ ataś cāsau cauravad daṇḍyaḥ || 8.340 ||
dvijo ‘dhvagaḥ kṣīṇavṛttir dvāv ikṣū dve ca mūlake |
ādadānaḥ parakṣetrān na daṇḍaṃ dātum arhati || 8.341 ||
dvija iti | dvijātiḥ pathikaḥ kṣīṇapātheyaḥ dvāv ikṣū dve ca mūlake tu trapusīsādi parakṣetrāt gṛhṇan daṇḍaṃ dātuṃ yogyo na bhavati || 8.341 ||
asaṃditānāṃ saṃdātā saṃditānāṃ ca mokṣakaḥ |
dāsāśvarathahartā ca prāptaḥ syāc caurakilbiṣam || 8.342 ||
asaṃditānām iti | parakīyānām aśvādīnām abaddhānāṃ darpeṇa bandhayitā baddhānāṃ vā mocayitā rathānāṃ ca mandurādau corayitā yo vā dāsāśvarathāpahārī sa cauradaṇḍaṃ prāpnuyāt
|| 8.342 ||
anena vidhinā rājā kurvāṇaḥ stenanigraham |
yaśo ‘smin prāpnuyāl loke pretya cānuttamaṃ sukham || 8.343 ||
aneneti | anenoktavidhānena cauraniyamanaṃ kurvan nṛpa ihaloke yaśolābhaṃ paratra cottamaṃ sukhaṃ prāpnuyāt || 8.343 ||
aindraṃ sthānam abhiprepsur yaśaś cākṣayam avyayam |
nopekṣeta kṣaṇam api rājā sāhasikaṃ naram || 8.344 ||
idānīṃsāhasam āha | aindram iti | sarvādhipatyalakṣaṇaṃ padaṃ cāvināśanam anapacayaṃ khyātiṃ cābhimukhyenecchan rājā sāhasena balavaśenāpi agnidāhavastrapāṭanādikāriṇaṃ manuṣyaṃ kṣaṇam api nopekṣeta || 8.344 ||
vāgduṣṭāt taskarāc caiva daṇḍenaiva ca hiṃsataḥ |
sāhasasya naraḥ kartā vijñeyaḥ pāpakṛttamaḥ || 8.345 ||
vāgduṣṭād iti | vākpāruṣyakṛc cauradaṇḍakāribhyaḥ sakāśāt sāhasakṛn manuṣyo ‘tiśayena pāpakṛd boddhavyaḥ || 8.345 ||
sāhase vartamānaṃ tu yo marṣayati pārthivaḥ |
sa vināśaṃ vrajaty āśu vidveṣaṃ cādhigacchati || 8.346 ||
sāhasa iti | sāhase pravṛttasya yaḥ kṣamate so ‘timātraṃ prasaṅgāt sāhasakāribhiḥ kṣipram eva vināśyate dveṣyatāṃ ca loke prāpnoti || 8.346 ||
na mitrakāraṇād rājā vipulād vā dhanāgamāt |
samutsṛjet sāhasikān sarvabhūtabhayāvahān || 8.347 ||
neti | mitroktyā vā mahatyā vā dhanaprāptyā sāhasikān sarvabhūtabhayajanakān rājā na parityajet || 8.347 ||
śāstraṃ dvijātibhir grāhyaṃ dharmo yatroparudhyate |
dvijātīnāṃ ca varṇānāṃ viplave kālakārite || 8.348 ||
ātmanaś ca paritrāṇe dakṣiṇānāṃ ca saṅgare |
strīviprābhyupapattau ca ghnan dharmeṇa na duṣyati || 8.349 ||
śāstram iti | ātamna iti | brāhmaṇādibhiś ca khaḍgādyāyudhaṃ grahītavyam | yasmin kāle varṇāśramiṇāṃ caurādibhiḥ dharmaṃ kartuṃ na dīyate tatra tathā dvijānāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ rājābhāvaparacakrākṣepakārādibhiḥ kālajanite varṇānāṃ saṃkare tathā ātmanaś ca śarīradāyādirakṣāyāṃ dakṣiṇāsaṃbandhini saṅgare apahāranimitte saṃgrāme strībrāhmaṇarakṣāyāṃ ca dharmeṇa kūṭayuddhena tena śastreṇānanyagatikaḥ parān hiṃsan na pratyavaiti evaṃ cātra sāhasadaṇḍo na kāryaḥ || 8.348–349 ||
guruṃ vā bālavṛddhau vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā bahuśrutam |
ātatāyinam āyāntaṃ hanyād evāvicārayan || 8.350 ||
gurum iti |
agnido garadaś caiva śastrapāṇir dhanāpahaḥ |
kṣetradārāpahārī ca ṣaḍ ete ātatāyinaḥ ||
iti vasiṣṭhādyuktaṃ gurubālavṛddhabrāhmaṇānām apy anyatamam ātatāyinam āyāntaṃ hananādivyāpārābhimukhībhūtam avilambamāno hanyād eva. kim utānyam api pūrvārthavādakāmāya syāt | ācāryaṃ ca pravaktāram ityādīnām apakāriṇām api gurvādīnāṃ hiṃsāyāvininditvāt ?? || 8.350 ||
nātatāyivadhe doṣo hantur bhavati kaścana |
prakāśaṃ vāprakāśaṃ vā manyus taṃ manyum ṛcchati || 8.351 ||
neti | prakāśaṃ yuddhenāprakāśaṃ cābhicaraṇe cātatāyimāraṇe hantur na kaścid api adhamadaṇḍaprāyaścittākhyadoṣo bhavati | yasmād dhantṛkṛto manyuḥ krodhābhimānī devatā taṃ manuṃ hanyamānagataṃ krodhaṃ nivartayatīti pūrvārdhavādaḥ kāmaḥ kāmāya syād  itivat | sāhase cāparādhāpekṣayā prathamamadhyamottamasākṣādaṅgacchedanirvāsanādayo daṇḍāḥ kāryāḥ || 8.351 ||
paradārābhimarśeṣu pravṛttān nṝn mahīpatiḥ |
udvejanakarair daṇḍaiś cihnayitvā pravāsayet || 8.352 ||
strīsaṃgrahaṇam idānīm āha | paradārābhimarśeṣv iti | udvejanakarair daṇḍaiś cihnayitvā pravāsayet | paradārasaṃbhogeṣu bahuṣu pravṛttān manuṣyān pīḍākarair daṇḍair nāsauṣṭhakartanādibhir aṅkayitvā rājā deśād nirvāsayet || 8.352 ||
tatsamuttho hi lokasya jāyate varṇasaṃkaraḥ |
yena mūlaharo ‘dharmaḥ sarvanāśāya kalpate || 8.353 ||
tatsamuttha iti | yasmāt paradārasaṃgamanasamudbhūto lokasya varṇasaṃkara utpadyate yena varṇasaṃkareṇa yāgādyadhikṛtayajamānābhāvād agnau prāstety etadabhāve sati avṛṣṭyākhyajagadvināśamūladharmo mūlacchedena nivāśāyotpadyate || 8.353 ||
parasya patnyā puruṣaḥ saṃbhāṣāṃ yojayan rahaḥ |
pūrvam ākṣārito doṣaiḥ prāpnuyāt pūrvasāhasam || 8.354 ||
parasya patnyā puruṣaḥ saṃbhāṣāṃ yojayet saheti | paradāragamanapūrvadoṣaiḥ pūrvam utpannābhiśāpaḥ parabhāryayā saha saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ kuryāt saḥ | prathamasāhasaṃ daṇḍaṃ prāpnuyāt || 8.354 ||
yas tv anākṣāritaḥ pūrvam abhibhāṣeta kāraṇāt |
na doṣaṃ prāpnuyāt kiṃcin na hi tasya vyatikramaḥ || 8.355 ||
ya iti | yaḥ punaḥ pūrvābhiśāpahitakāraṇena kenacij janasamakṣam abhibhāṣaṇaṃ kuryāt sa na daṇḍaṃ prāpnuyāt yasmān na kaścid aparādho ‘sti || 8.355 ||
parastriyaṃ yo ‘bhivadet tīrthe ‘raṇye vane ‘pi vā |
nadīnāṃ vāpi saṃbhede sa saṃgrahaṇam āpnuyāt || 8.356 ||
parastriyam iti | udakottīryamāṇe grāmād bahir nirjane pradeśe vṛkṣagulmalatākīrṇe vā deśe nadīnāṃ vā saṃgame yaḥ parastriyam anākṣārito ‘pi pūrvakāraṇād iti saṃbhāṣeta sa vakṣyamāṇaṃ doṣaṃ prāpnuyāt | samyag gṛhyate jñāyate ‘syāḥ śrayaṃ saṃbandha iti yena tatsaṃgrahaṇam || 8.356 ||
upacārakriyākeliḥ sparśo bhūṣaṇavāsasām |
saha khaṭvāsanaṃ caiva sarvaṃ saṃgrahaṇaṃ smṛtam || 8.357 ||
upakārakriyā kelir iti | saha khaṭvāsanam iti | aṅgānulepanādyupacārakaraṇaṃ krīḍā alaṃkaraṇavāsasāṃ sparśanam | ekakhaṭvāsanaṃ ekayānagamanam ity etat sarvaṃ saṃgrahaṇaṃ manvādibhiḥ smṛtam || 8.357 ||
striyaṃ spṛśed adeśe yaḥ spṛṣṭo vā marṣayet tayā |
parasparasyānumate sarvaṃ saṃgrahaṇaṃ smṛtam || 8.358 ||
striyam iti | yo ‘deśe stayor aṅge vā nirjane pradeśe vā spṛśet tayā vā apradeśe śroṇyādau nirjane vā deśe spṛṣṭas tūṣṇīm āsīta tasyaitad anyonyasyāṅgīkaraṇe sati saṃgrahaṇaṃ manvādibhiḥ smṛtam | atra yady api saṃgamo na jātaḥ tadā saṃgrahaṇamātre mukhopagamadaṇḍād ūnadaṇḍakalpanā kāryā yato ‘nubandhaṃ parijñāyeti uktam || 8.358 ||
abrāhmaṇaḥ saṃgrahaṇe prāṇāntaṃ daṇḍam arhati |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ dārā rakṣyatamāḥ smṛtā || 8.359 ||
abrāhmaṇa iti | caturṇām api varṇānāṃ dārā rakṣyatamā smṛtāḥ | daṇḍabhūyastvād abrāhmaṇaśūdro brāhmaṇyām anicchantyāṃ saṃgrahaṇe daṇḍam arhati | caturṇām api varṇānāṃ dhanaputrādīnāṃ madhyād dārā atiśayena rakṣaṇīyāḥ | ata utkṛṣṭasaṃgrahanād api varṇaiḥ bhāryā saṃrakṣaṇīyā || 8.359 ||
bhikṣukā bandinaś caiva dīkṣitāḥ kāravas tathā |
saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ saha strībhiḥ kuryur aprativāritāḥ || 8.360 ||
bhikṣukā iti | bhikṣājīvinaḥ stāvakāḥ yajñārthaṃ kṛtadīkṣāḥ sūpakārādayo bhikṣādisvarūpārthaṃ gṛhe strībhiḥ saha saṃbhāṣaṇam anivāritā eva kuryuḥ | teṣāṃ nivāraṇā nāsty eva saṃgrahaṇābhāvāt || 8.360 ||
na saṃbhāṣāṃ saha strībhiḥ pratiṣiddhaḥ samācaret |
niṣiddho bhāṣamāṇas tu suvarṇaṃ daṇḍam arhati || 8.361 ||
na saṃbhāṣaṃ saha strībhir iti | svāminā niṣiddhaḥ san strībhiḥ sambhāṣaṇaṃ na kuryat | niṣiddhaḥ saṃbhāṣamāṇas tu rājñaḥ suvarṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ dadāti || 8.361 ||
naiṣa cāraṇadāreṣu vidhir nātmopajīviṣu |
sajjanayanti hi te nārīr nigūḍhāś cārayanti ca || 8.362 ||
naiṣa iti | “parastriyaṃ yo ‘bhivadet” ityādikaḥ saṃbhāṣaṇaniṣedhavidhiḥ naṭagāyanādidāreṣu veśajīvidāreṣu ca nāsti yataś cāraṇā ātmopajīvinaś ca svabhāryāḥ parapuruṣe saṃśleṣayanti pracchannāś ca bhūtvā naitat kilāsmābhiḥ śatam iti kṛtvā veśakarmaṇi sūcayanty atas taddhāraṇāṃ veśyāprakāratvāt nāsti saṃbhāṣaṇe niṣedhaḥ || 8.362 ||
kiṃcid eva tu dāpyaḥ syāt saṃbhāṣāṃ tābhir ācaran |
