preṣyāsu caikabhaktāsu rahaḥ pravrajitāsu ca || 8.363 ||
kiṃcid eva tu dāpyaḥ syāt saṃbhāṣāṃ tābhir ācaran | cāraṇa ātmopajīvistrībhiḥ raho ‘prakāśaṃ saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ kurvan kiṃcid eva svalpaṃ daṇḍaṃ dāpanīyaḥ | dāsībhir avaruddhābhir bauddhābhir vratacāriṇībhiś ca saha saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ kurvan kāṃcid daṇḍamātrāṃ dāpyaḥ syāt || 8.363 ||
yo ‘kāmāṃ dūṣayet kanyāṃ sa sadyo vadham arhati |
sakāmāṃ dūṣayaṃs tulyo na vadhaṃ prāpnuyān naraḥ || 8.364 ||
yo ‘kāmāṃ dūṣayet kanyāṃ sa sadyo vadham arhatīti | yas tulyajātīyo ‘nicchantīṃ kanyāṃ dūṣayati gacchati sa tatkṣaṇād eva brāhmaṇavarjaṃ liṅgacchedādikaṃ vadham arhati | sakāmāṃ dūṣayaṃs tulyo na vadhaṃ prāpnuyāt || 8.364 ||
kanyāṃ bhajantīm utkṛṣṭaṃ na kiṃcid api dāpayet |
jaghanyaṃ sevamānaṃ tu saṃyatāṃ vāsayed gṛhe || 8.365 ||
kanyām iti | utkṛṣṭaṃ puruṣaṃ kanyāṃ sevamānāṃ na kiṃcid api daṇḍaṃ rājā dāpayet | hīnajātiṃ punaḥ sevamānāṃ rakṣitā pitṛgṛhe sthāpayet | yāvad anivṛttābhilāṣā syād vṛttihīnajñātisaṃprayogād yāvajjīvaṃ sthāpayet || 8.365 ||
uttamāṃ sevamānas tu jaghanyo daṇḍam arhati |
śulkaṃ dāpyaḥ sevamānaḥ samām icchet pitā yadi || 8.366 ||
uttamām iti | śulkaṃ dāpyaḥ sevamānaḥ samām icchet pitā yadi | utkṛṣṭajātīyāṃ kanyām icchantīṃ vā hīnajātir gacchan jātyapekṣayāṅgacchedamāraṇātmakaṃ vadham arhati | samānajātīyā punar icchantīṃ gacchan yadi pitā icchati tadā pituḥ śulkam anurūpam āsuravad dadyān na tu daṇḍyo ‘tha pitā necchati tadā rājñā śulkaparimāṇaṃ dāpyaḥ || 8.366 ||
abhiṣahya tu yaḥ kanyāṃ kuryād darpeṇa mānavaḥ |
tasyāśu kartyā aṅgulyo daṇḍaṃ cārhati ṣaṭśatam || 8.367 ||
abhiṣahyeti | manuṣyaḥ prashya haṭhād ahaṅkāreṇa samānāṃ samānajātīyāṃ tāṃ gamanavarjaṃ aṅguliprakṣepamātreṇaiva nāśayet tasya kṣipram evāṅgulidvayaṃ kartanīyam, daṇḍaṃ ṣaṭśatam asau dāpanīyaḥ || 8.367 ||
sakāmāṃ dūṣayaṃs tulyo nāṅgulicchedam āpnuyāt |
dviśataṃ tu damaṃ dāpyaḥ prasaṅgavinivṛttaye || 8.368 ||
sakāmām iti | samānajātir icchantīṃ kanyām aṅgulimātraprakṣepeṇa nāśayan aṅgulicchedaṃ na prāpnuyāt kiṃ tu paunaḥpunyena eṣaḥ prasaṅgān nāvarteta tadarthaṃ śatamayaṃ daṇḍaṃ dāpyaḥ || 8.368 ||
kanyaiva kanyāṃ yā kuryāt tasyāḥ syād dviśato damaḥ |
śulkaṃ ca dviguṇaṃ dadyāc chiphāś caivāpnuyāt daśa || 8.369 ||
kanyeti | śulkaṃ ca triguṇaṃ dadyāt śiphāś caivāpnuyād daśa | yā kanyaiva kanyām aparāṃm aṅguliprakṣepeṇa nāśayet tasyā dviśato daṇḍaḥ syāt | śulkaṃ vāsau kanyāpitur dadyāt |śiphāprahārāṃś ca daśa prāpnuyāt || 8.369 ||
yā tu kanyāṃ prakuryāt strī sā sadyo mauṇḍyam arhati |
aṅgulyor eva vā chedaṃ khareṇodvahanaṃ tathā || 8.370 ||
yeti | yā punaḥ strī kanyāṃ nāśayet sā tatkṣaṇād eva śiromuṇḍanasānubandhāpekṣayā aṅgulyor eva chedanam arhati | tathā khareṇa rājamārge vahanam arhati idaṃ cātra pūrvābhyāṃ vikalpitābhyāṃ samanvitam || 8.370 ||
bhartāraṃ laṅghayed yā tu strī jñātiguṇadarpitā |
tāṃ śvabhiḥ kḥadayed rājā saṃsthāne bahusaṃsthite || 8.371 ||
bhartāram iti | yā strī balavadāḍhyapitrādibāndhavadarpeṇa rūpavaidagdhyād guṇagaṇadarvitā pūrveṇa ca bhartāraṃ puruṣāntarakaraṇenollaṅghayet tāṃ rājā bahujanākīrṇe pradeśe śvabhir bhakṣayet || 8.371 ||
pumāṃsaṃ dāhayet pāpaṃ śayane tapta āyase |
abhyādadhyuś ca kāṣṭhāni tatra dahyeta pāpakrt || 8.372 ||
taṃ darpitājāraṃ puruṣaṃ dāhayed iti | taṃ pāpakāriṇaṃ puruṣam ayomaye śayane agnijvalite rājā dāhayet | kāṣṭhāni nikṣipeyuḥ yāvad asau pāpakṛd dagdhaḥ syāt || 8.372 ||
saṃvatsarābhiśastasya duṣṭasya dviguṇo damaḥ |
vrātyayā saha saṃvāse cāṇḍālyā tāvad eva tu || 8.373 ||
saṃvatsarābhiśastasyeti | parastrīgamanadoṣeṇa puṃso daṇḍitasya punaḥ saṃvatsare ‘tīte tasyām evābhiśaptasya tasya yathopadeśasya dviguṇo daṇḍaḥ kartavyaḥ daṇḍaḥ kalpate | iha cāṇḍālyā saha nirdeśāt sahasraṃ tv antyajastriyaḥ iti cāṇḍālīgamanatulya eva sa punaḥ saṃvatsare ‘tīte tām eva vrātyāṃ gacchato dviguṇo daṇḍaḥ kāryaḥ || 8.373 ||
śūdro guptam aguptaṃ vā dvaijātaṃ varṇam āvasan |
aguptam aṅgasarvasvī guptaṃ sarveṇa hīyate || 8.374 ||
śūdra iti | śūdro yadi bhartrā rakṣitām arakṣitāṃ punargacched ā strīrakṣitā gacchan liṅgacchedasarvasvāpahārābhyāṃ yojanīyaḥ | arakṣitāṃ punar gacchan śarīradhanadaṇḍaḥ kartavyaḥ | kāryāvaruddhāpekṣayā daṇḍasya gurulaghubhāvaḥ kalpanīyaḥ || 8.374 ||
vaiśyaḥ sarvasvadaṇḍaḥ syāt saṃvatsaranirodhataḥ |
sahasraṃ kṣatriyo daṇḍyo mauṇḍyaṃ mūtreṇa cārhati || 8. 375 ||
vaiśya iti | vaiśyo guptabrāhmaṇīgamane saṃvatsarabandhane ca sthāpyaḥ sarvasvaṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ | kṣatriyāgamane vaiśyasya vaiśyaś cet kaṣatriyāyāṃ guptāyām iti vakṣyati | kṣatriyo guptabrāhmaṇīgamane sahasraṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ | śiromuṇḍanaṃ kharamūtreṇāsya kāryam || 8.375 ||
brāhmaṇīṃ yady aguptāṃ tu gacchetāṃ vaiśyapārthivau |
vaiśyaṃ pañcaśataṃ kuryāt kṣatriyaṃ tu sahasriṇam || 8.376 ||
brāhmaṇīm iti | arakṣitāṃ punar brāhmaṇīṃ yadi vaiśyakṣatriyau gacchetāṃ tadā vaiśyaḥ pañcaśataṃ daṇḍyaḥ | kṣatriyaḥ punā rakṣām akṛtatvād vaiśyād adhikadaṇḍaḥ sahasradaṇḍopetaḥ kāryaḥ || 8.376 ||
ubhāv api tu tāv eva brāhmaṇyā guptayā saha |
viplutau śūdravad daṇḍyau dagdhavyau vā kaṭāgninā || 8.377 ||
ubhāv iti | tāv eva dvāv api kṣatriyavaiśyau rakṣitayā brāhmaṇyā saha kṛtamaithunau śūdravad gupte sarveṇa hīyata iti daṇḍyau vāgninā vā sarvathā dagdhavyāv iti cātyantadaṇḍyau śrotriyadāraguṇavad brāhmaṇīviṣayaṃ daṇḍagurutvāt | vaiśyaḥ sarvasvadaṇḍyaḥ syāt sahasraṃ kṣatriyas tathā || 8.377 ||
sahasraṃ brāhmaṇo daṇḍyo guptāṃ viprāṃ balād vrajan |
śatāni pañca daṇḍyaḥ syād icchantyā saha saṃgataḥ || 8.378 ||
sahasram iti | brāhmaṇo rakṣitāṃ brāhmaṇīṃ haṭhāt gacchan sahasraṃ daṇḍyaḥ | icchantyā tu punaḥ saha maithune pañcaśatāni daṇḍanīyaḥ || 8.378 ||
mauṇḍyaṃ prāṇāntako daṇḍo brāhmaṇasya vidhīyate |
itareṣāṃ tu varṇānāṃ daṇḍaḥ prāṇāntako bhavet || 8.379 ||
mauṇḍyam iti | brāhmaṇasya vadhadaṇḍasthāne śiromuṇḍane śāstreṇopadiśyate | kṣatriyādīnāṃ punar ukteṣu vadhadaṇḍo bhavati || 8.379 ||
na jātu brāhmaṇaṃ hanyāt sarvapāpeṣv api sthitam |
rāṣṭrād enaṃ bahiḥ kuryāt samagradhanam akṣatam || 8.380 ||
na jātv iti | sarvapāpakāriṇam api brāhmaṇaṃ kadācid api na hanyāt | api sarvadhanasaṃyuktam akṣataśarīraṃ rāṣṭrān nirvāsayet || 8.380 ||
na brāhmaṇavadhād bhūyān adharmo vidyate bhuvi |
tasmād asya vadhaṃ rājā manasāpi na cintayet || 8.381 ||
na brāhmaṇavadhād iti | brāhmaṇavadhād api adhiko ‘nyaḥ pṛthivyām adharmo nāsti | brāhmaṇaṃ sarvapāpakāriṇam api rājā manasāpi na hanyāt || 8.381 ||
vaiśyaś cet kṣatriyāṃ guptāṃ vaiśyāṃ vā kṣatriyo vrajet |
yo brāhmaṇyām aguptāyāṃ tāv ubhau daṇḍam arhati || 8.382 ||
vaiśya iti | vaiśyo yadi rakṣitāyāṃ kṣatriyāyāṃ gacchet kṣatriyo vā vaiśyāṃ tadā yo brāhmaṇyām arakṣitāyāṃ gamane kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ daṇḍo vaiśyaṃ pañcaśataṃ kuryāt kṣatriyaṃ tu sahasram iti daṇḍaṃ tau kṣatriyavaiśyau vārhataḥ | ataś ca vaiśyasya kṣatriyāgamane pañcaśatāni kṣatriyasya vaiśyādhigamane rakṣādhikṛtatvād adhikaguṇadaṇḍasahasram iti || 8.382 ||
sahasraṃ brāhmaṇo daṇḍaṃ dāpyo gupte tu te vrajan |
śūdrāyāḥ kṣatriyaviśoḥ sāhasro vai bhaved damaḥ || 8.383 ||
sahasram iti | kṣatriyavaiśye rakṣite brāhmaṇo gacchan sahasraṃ daṇḍyaḥ | kṣatriyavaiśyau rakṣitāṃ śūdrāṃ gacchataḥ sahasraparimāṇo daṇḍaḥ syāt || 8.383 ||
kṣatriyāyām aguptāyāṃ vaiśye pañcaśataṃ damaḥ |
mūtreṇa mauṇḍyam ṛcchet tu kṣatriyo daṇḍam eva vā || 8.384 ||
agupte kṣatriyāvaiśye śūdrāṃ vā brāhmaṇo vrajan |
śatāni pañca daṇḍyaḥ syāt sahasraṃ tv antyajastriyam || 8.385 ||
kṣatriyām iti | agupte iti | vaiśyāṃ kṣatriyāṃ śūdrāṃ rakṣitāṃ brāhmano vrajan pañcaśatāni daṇḍyaḥ syāt | cāṇḍālādistriyaṃ gacchan punaḥ sahasraṃ daṇḍyaḥ || 8.384–385 ||
yasya stenaḥ pure nāsti nānyastrīgo na duṣtavāk |
na sāhasikadaṇḍaghno sa rājā śakralokabhāk || 8.386 ||
yasyeti | yasya rājño rāṣṭre caurapāradārikavākpāruṣyakṛddaṇḍapāruṣyāgnidāhādisāhasakāriṇaḥ na santi sa rājā svargaṃ vrajet || 8.386 ||
eteṣāṃ nigraho rājñaḥ pañcānāṃ viṣaye svake |
sāmrājyakṛt sajātyeṣu loke caiva yaśaskaraḥ || 8.387 ||
eteṣām iti | eteṣāṃ stenādīnāṃ svarāṣṭre rājño nigrahaḥ samajātīyeṣu madhye rājatvasya kārako loke ca khyāter utpādakaḥ || 8.387 ||
ṛtvijaṃ yas tyajed yājyo yājyaṃ cartvik tyajed yadi |
śaktaṃ karmaṇy aduṣṭaṃ ca tayor daṇḍaḥ śataṃ śatam || 8.388 ||
ṛtvijam iti | yo yājya ṛtvijaṃ pāraṃparyāgataṃ karmānuṣṭhānasamartham atijāpādidoṣarahitam aduṣṭaṃ yājyaṃ tyajati tayoḥ śataṃ śataṃ daṇḍaḥ kārya iti prasaṅgād etad āha || 8.388 ||
na mātā na pitā na strī na putras tyāgam arhati |
tyajann apatitān etān rājñā daṇḍyaḥ śatāni ṣaṭ || 8.389 ||
na māteti | mātṛpitṛbhāryāputratyāgaṃ bharaṇaśuśrūṣādykaraṇātmakaṃ nārhati | ata etān patitān api parityajan anyonyaparityāge rājñā ṣaṭśatāni daṇḍyaḥ || 8.389 ||
āśrameṣu dvijātīnāṃ kārye vivadatāṃ mithaḥ |
na vibrūyān nṛpo dharmaṃ cikīrṣan hitam ātmanaḥ || 8.390 ||
āśrameṣv iti | dvijānāṃ gṛhasthānām āśramaviṣaye śāstrārtho ‘yaṃ nety evaṃ parasparaṃ vivadamānānāṃ rājā ātmane dṛṣṭādṛṣṭahitaṃ kartum icchan nāyaṃ śāstrārtha iti mahāsanaviśeṣeṇa brūyāt || 8.390 ||
yathārham etān abhyarcya brāhmaṇaiḥ saha pārthivaḥ |
sāntvena praśamayyādau svadharmaṃ pratipādayet || 8.391 ||
kiṃ tarhi yathārham iti | yādṛśīṃ yaḥ pūjām arhati taṃ tathā pūjayitvā anyair brāhmaṇaiḥ saha rājā prathamaśāstrād apagatakopāt kṛtvā tatas teṣāṃ yaḥ svadharmaḥ taṃ bodhayet || 8.391 ||
prativeśyānuveśyau ca kalyāṇe viṃśatidvije |
arhāv abhojayan vipro daṇḍam arhati māṣakam || 8.392 ||
prātiveśyānuveśyāv iti | yatrānye viṃśtimātrādhikā bhojanīyās tasminn utsave veśmāgrāt paścān nivāsinau yogyāv abhojayan brāhmaṇādir uttaratra hiraṇyagrahaṇād iha māṣakaṃ daṇḍam arhati || 8.392 ||
śrotriyaḥ śrotriyaṃ sādhuṃ bhūtikṛtyeṣv abhojayan |
tadannaṃ dviguṇaṃ dāpyo hairaṇyaṃ caiva māṣakam || 8.393 ||
andho jaḍaḥ pīṭhasarpī saptatyā sthaviraś ca yaḥ |
śrotriyeṣūpakurvaṃś ca na dāpyāḥ kenacit karam || 8.394 ||
śrotriya iti | andha iti | andhabadhirapaṅgavaḥ saptativarṣāt prabhṛti ca vṛddhaḥ śrotriyāṇāṃ ca śuśrūṣādinopakārakaraṇe kenacid api kṣoṇakośenāpi rājñā dānyaṣaḍbhāgaṃ gulmādānādikaṃ rājadeyam na dāpanīyaḥ || 8.393–394 ||
śrotriyaṃ vyādhitārtau ca bālavṛddhāv akiṃcanam |
mahākulīnam āryaṃ ca rājā saṃpūjayet sadā || 8.395 ||
śrotriyaṃ vyādhinārtaṃ ceti | adhyayanānuṣṭhānavantaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ rogiṇaṃ priyaviyogākrāntaṃ bālavṛddhadaridramahākulīnotpannārjavopetān rājā dānamānapriyakaraṇena sarvadā pūjayet || 8.395 ||
śālmalīphalake ślakṣṇe nenijyān nejakaḥ śanaiḥ |
na ca vāsāṃsi vāsobhir nirharen na ca vāsayet || 8.396 ||
śālmalīphalaka iti | śālmalyādivṛkṣasaṃbandhini phalake aparuṣe vāsodhāvakaḥ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ vāsāṃsi kṣālayet | na ca parakīyair vastaiḥ saṃveṣṭya vastrādi bahiḥ na cānyasyācchādanartham dadyāt | yady evaṃ kuryāt tadāsau daṇḍyaḥ syāt || 8.396 ||
tantuvāyo daśapalaṃ dadyād ekapalādhikam |
ato ‘nyathā vartamāno dāpyo dvādaśakaṃ damam || 8.397 ||
tantuvāya iti | tantuvāyinā chede daśapalādisūtrasya gṛhītvā piṣṭakhalyānupraveśyād ekādaśapalaṃ vāso dadyāt | ato yac chiṣṭaṃ tat tad dvādaśaguṇaṃ daṇḍyaḥ || 8.397 ||
śulkasthāneṣu kuśalāḥ sarvapaṇyavicakṣaṇāḥ |
kuryur ardhaṃ yathāpaṇyaṃ tato viṃśaṃ nṛpo haret || 8.398 ||
śulkasthāneṣv iti | āpaṇādivyavahartṛbhyo rājagrāhyaṃ śulkaṃ ca sthāpyāpaṇasthalapathajalapathādiṣu niyataṃ labdhum aśakyāḥ paṇyānāṃ ca sārāsārādijñās te paṇe paṇe ‘nurūpam ardhaṃ kuryus tato lābhadhanād viṃśatibhāgaṃ śulkaṃ rājā dāpayet || 8.398 ||
rājñaḥ prakhyātabhāṇḍāni pratiṣiddhāni yāni ca |
tāni nirharato lobhāt sarvahāraṃ haren nṛpaḥ || 8.399 ||
rājña iti | yāni rājñas taddeśodbhavāni manimuktādīni yāni ca rājñā pratiṣiddhāni tathā durbhikṣe dhānyaṃ deśāntare na neyam iti tāni lobhād deśāṇtaraṃ nayataḥ sarvaharaṇaṃ kuryāt || 8.399 ||
śulkasthānaṃ pariharann akāle krayavikrayī |
mithyāvādī ca saṃkhyāne dāpyo ‘ṣṭaguṇam atyayam || 8.400 ||
śulkasthānam iti | uktaparijihīrṣayā ya utpathena gacchaty akāle vā krayavikrayaśulkam eva yathārthaṃ cātmadravyaṃ svalpāṃ saṃkhyāṃ vakti sa rājadeyaṃ yat tadaṣṭaguṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ dāpyaḥ || 8.400 ||
āgamaṃ nirgamaṃ sthānaṃ tathā vṛddhikṣayāv ubhau |
vicārya sarvapaṇyānāṃ kārayet krayavikrayau || 8.401 ||
āgamam iti | kiyato dūrād ānītam etad iti deśāntarīyadravyasya sthānam āgamanaṃ vicārya kiyaddūraṃ nīyate svadeśodbhavasya nirgamam avekṣya kiyatkālo vāṇijyasya vikītam etad āsitam iti evaṃ ca sthānam ālokya tathā kiyān tasya vaṇijo lābho ‘napāyo vā bhaktācchādanakarmakarādim ata iti matvā sarvapaṇyānāṃ yathā viṇijā kartṝṇāṃ ca pīḍanaṃ na bhavati tathā krayavikaryau kārayet || 8.401 ||
pañcarātre pañcarātre pakṣe pakṣe ‘tha vā gate |
kurvīta caiṣāṃ pratyakṣam arghasaṃsthāpanaṃ nṛpaḥ || 8.402 ||
pañcarātra iti | āgamopayogāder aniyatatvād eṣāṃ paṇyānām asthirārghāṇāṃ pañcarātre pañcarātre tat tat sthiraprāyārghāṇāṃ ca pakṣe pakṣe gate mūlyasthāpanam ātmano rājñā (rājā?) pratyakṣī kuryāt || 8.402 ||
tulā mānaṃ pratīmānaṃ sarvaṃ ca syāt sulakṣitam |
ṣaṭsu ṣaṭsu ca māseṣu punar eva parīkṣayet || 8.403 ||
tulāmānam iti | tulāṃ tathā mānaṃ kāṣṭhādimayaṃ droṇakuḍapātapratīmānaṃ suvarṇādiparicchedakavartakādi tad etat sarvaṃ rājacināṅkitaṃ kāryam | ṣaṭsu ṣaṭsu māseṣu punaḥ punaḥ tat sarvaṃ āptapuruṣaiḥ parīkṣayet || 8.403 ||
paṇaṃ yānaṃ tare dāpyaṃ paurūṣo ‘rdhapaṇaṃ tare |
pāde paśuś ca yoṣic ca pādārdhaṃ riktakaḥ pumān || 8.404 ||
paṇam iti | bhāṇḍapūrṇāni yāni tānīti vakṣyati ato riktaṃ gantryāditaramūlyaṃ paṇaṃ rājā (rājñā?) dāpyam | evaṃ puruṣavāhyo bhāro ‘rdhapaṇaṃ dāpyaḥ | gavādiś ca paśuḥ strī ca paṇacaturbhāgaṃ dāpanīyo bhāṇḍarahito manuṣyaḥ paṇāṣṭabhāgaṃ dāpanīyaḥ || 8.404 ||
bhāṇḍapūrṇāni yānāni tāryaṃ dāpyāni sārataḥ |
riktabhāṇḍāni yat kiṃcit pumāṃsaś cāparicchadāḥ || 8.405 ||
bhāṇḍapūrṇānīti | pratyagūrṇāni gantryādīni dravyagatotkarṣāpakarṣagurulaghubhāvāpekṣayā taramūlyaṃ dāpyāni pumāṃsaś ca paricchadārhā vaṇikprabhṛtayo ‘pi aparicchadā āyātā yat kiṃcid dāpanīyāḥ | anyasya puṃsaḥ padārthaṃ riktakaḥ pumān ity uktam, atra niyamasya kartum aśakyatvāt | etāvad evopadeśārthaṃ evaṃ dravyarahitāni mañjūṣādīni bhāṇḍāni yat kiṃcit svayaṃ dāpyāni || 8.405 ||
dīrghādhvani yathādeśaṃ yathākālaṃ taro bhavet |
nadītīreṣu tad vidyāt samudre nāsti lakṣaṇam || 8.406 ||
dīrghādhvanīti | pārāvārataraṇārthaṃ pūrvam uktaṃ dūrādhvani punaḥ nadīmārgeṇa nāvādinā yātavyaskhalanādyupalādyupetadūradeśāpekṣayā grīṣmahemantādikālāpekṣayā taramūlyaṃ kalpayitavyam etac ca nadītīreṣu boddhavyaṃ samudre punar vātavaśataḥ pātālādivahanāt āyatatvābhāve sati taramūlyaviśeṣajñāpakam | nadī ca yojanādikaṃ nāsty ato ‘trocitam eva śulkaṃ grāhyam || 8.406 ||
garbhiṇī tu dvimāsādis tathā pravrajito muniḥ |
brāhmaṇā liṅginaś caiva na dāpyās tārikaṃ tare || 8.407 ||
garbhiṇīti | gṛhītagarbhā strī māsadvayād ūrdhvaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ taramūlyaṃ te na dāpyāḥ
|| 8.407 ||
yan nāvi kiṃcid dāśānāṃ viśīryetāparādhataḥ |
tad dāśair eva dātavyaṃ samāgamya svato ‘ṃśataḥ || 8.408 ||
yad iti | nāvārūḍhaiḥ pradeyaṃ tatsamāgamya svato ‘ṃśataḥ | yat kiṃcid dravyaṃ nāvikāparādhena vipadyeta yan nāvārūḍheḥ saṃbhūya svāṃśād dātavyam || 8.408 ||
eṣa nauyāninām ukto vyavahārasya nirṇayaḥ |
dāśāparādhatas toye daivike nāsti nigrahaḥ || 8.409 ||
eṣa iti | nāvikāparādhena yad uktaṃ tat sarvaṃ nāvikair dātavyam | ity eṣa nauyāyināṃ vyavahāranirṇaya ukto daivike punar doṣe utpātavātanaubhaṅgādau sa ca na nāvikānāṃ daṇḍabandhanādivigraho vidyate || 8.409 ||
vāṇijyaṃ kārayed vaiśyaṃ kusīdaṃ kṛṣim eva ca |
paśūnāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ caiva dāsyaṃ śūdraṃ dvijanmanām || 8.410 ||
vāṇijyam iti | vāṇijyadhanaprayogakṛṣipaśurakṣaṇāni dṛṣyatvād vaiśyam, śūdraṃ ca dvijanmanāṃ dāsyaṃ niyamena rājā kārayet akurvāṇau rājñā daṇḍyau ihopadeśāt || 8.410 ||
kṣatriyaṃ caiva vaiśyaṃ ca brahmaṇo vṛttikarśitau |
bibhṛyād ānṛśaṃsyena svāni karmāṇi kārayan || 8.411 ||
kṣatriyam iti | kṣatriyavaiśyau vṛttyabhāve pīḍitau brāhmaṇaḥ kārayet svāni karmāṇi rakṣaṇakṛṣyādīni kārayan grāsācchādanādinā bibhṛyād evaṃ ca (na kurvāṇo) mahādhano brāhmaṇas tāvad vidhivat rājñā tāḍanīyam iti prakaraṇam || 8.411 ||
dāsyaṃ tu kārayaṃl lobhād brāhmaṇaḥ saṃskṛtān dvijān |
anicchataḥ prābhavatyād rājñā daṇḍyaḥ śatāni ṣaṭ || 8.412 ||
yo dāsyam iti | brāhmaṇa upanītān dvijān dāsyakarmaṇi laulyāt tadā tat karma pādadhāvanādi kārayan rājñā ṣaṭpaṇaśatāni daṇḍyaḥ || 8.412 ||
śūdraṃ kārayed dāsyaṃ krītam akrītam eva vā |
dāsyāyaiva hi sṛṣṭo ‘sau brāhmaṇasya svayaṃbhuvā || 8.413 ||
śūdram iti | śūdraṃ punar bhaktādinā bhṛtam abhṛtaṃ vā dāsyaṃ kārayet | yasmād asau brāhmaṇadāsā(-dāsyā)rtham eva prajāpatinā sṛṣṭaḥ || 8.413 ||
na svāminā nisṛṣṭo ‘pi śūdro dāsyād vimucyate |
nisargajaṃ hi tat tasya kas tasmāt tad apohati || 8.414 ||
na svāmineti | yasyāsau svajātivihitatvāt dāsatvam āpannas tena vimukto ‘pi śūdro dāsyān na mucyate | tasmād dāsyaṃ hi śūdrasya sahajaṃ śūdratvaṃ vadataḥ kaḥ śūdrāt taddāsyam apanetuṃ dṛṣṭārtham api dṛśyate | na dāsyaṃ kartavyam ity evaṃ param etad anyathā vartamānadāsayonigaṇitam || 8.414 ||
dhvajāhṛto bhaktadāso gṛhajaḥ krītadatrimau |
paitriko daṇḍadāsaś ca saptaite dāsayonayaḥ || 8.415 ||
dhvajāhṛta iti | saṃgrāme svāminā kośāj jito vetanabhṛto dāsīputro ‘nyasmāt krīto ‘nyena dattaḥ pitrādiprabandhāyāto nirdhanatayā daṇḍaśuddhyabhāve sati śuddhyarthaṃ dāsyam upagata iti satadhvajāḥ saptatattvādīni dāsatvakāraṇāni || 8.415 ||
bhāryā putraś ca dāsaś ca traya evādhanāḥ smṛtāḥ |
yat te samadhigacchanti yasya te tasya taddhanam || 8.416 ||
bhāryeti | ato ‘pi nirdhana eva manvādibhiḥ smṛtaḥ yasmād tad vittaṃ te ‘rjayanti yasya te bhāryādayas tatsaṃbandhi taddhanam iti | tatpāratantryādi vā yasyaitad ucyate || 8.416 ||
viśrabdhaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ śūdrād dravyopādānam ācaret |
na hi tasyāsti kiṃcit svaṃ bhartṛhāryadhano hi saḥ || 8.417 ||
viśrabdham iti | brāhmaṇo nirvicikitsam eva prākṛtād dāsaśūdratvena ayuktaḥ | tasmāt tasya dāsaśūdrasya na kiṃcit svaṃ vidyate yato ‘sya svāmino dhanam apahriyate evaṃ ca yadi viśrabdhaṃ dravyaṃ dāsād brāhmaṇo gṛhṇan na daṇḍanīya eva ity evaṃ sarvam ity etad ucyate
|| 8.418 ||
vaiśyaśūdrau prayatnena svāni karmāṇi kārayet |
tau hi cyutau svakarmabhyaḥ kṣobhayetām idaṃ jagat || 8.418 ||
vaiśyaśūdrāv iti | vaiśyaśūdrau kṛṣidāsyādīni svakārmāṇi rājā kārayet | yasmād vaiśyaḥ kṛṣivāṇijyādi śūdro dāsyādy akurvan dharmakṣayād idaṃ jagad ākulī kuryat | śūdraś ca dāsyam akurvan anatim abhijñāya kadācid dvaijāte karmaṇi pravartamāno durbhiṣanarakādyupadravakṛt syāt || 8.418 ||
ahany ahany avekṣeta karmāntān vāhanāni ca |
āyavyayau ca niyatāv ākarān kośam eva ca || 8.419 ||
ahanīti | pratyahaṃ karmāntān kṛṣiśulkādisthānāni dhanasaṃcayaṃ yogakṣemārthaṃ prati jāgṛyād iti vyavahāradarśanāśaktasyāpi rājadharmāparityāgārthaṃ punar vacanam || 8.419 ||
evaṃ sarvān imān rājā vyavahārān samāpayan |
vyapohya kilbiṣaṃ sarvaṃ prapnoti paramāṃ gatim || 8.420 ||
evam iti | vyapohya kalmaṣaṃ sarvaṃ prāpnoti paramāṃ gatim | evam uktanītyā etān ṛṇādānādīn vyavahārānirṇayenāntaṃ nayan rājā apakṣapātavyavahāradarśanena śāstradānasāmarthyāt pramādakṛtasaṃcitapāpam apanudya brahmalokaṃ prāpnoti iti samastavyavahārāsamāptāv api pradhānavivādasamāptābhiprāyeṇedaṃ phalakathanam || 8.420 ||
iti śrībhaṭṭamādhavātmajagovindarājaviricitāyāṃ manvāśayānusāriṇyāṃ
sarvavyavahāranirṇayo nāmāṣṭamo ‘dhyāyaḥ ||
navamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
puruṣasya striyāś caiva dharmye vartmani tiṣṭhatoḥ |
saṃyoge viprayoge ca dharmān vakṣyāmi śāśvatān || 9.1 ||
puruṣasyeti | strīpuṃsayor dampatyor anyonyāvyabhicāralakṣaṇe mārge vartamānayoḥ saṃyuktayor viyuktayoś ca nityān dharmān vakṣyāmi | jāyāpatyor itaretaravipratipattau kathaṃcit rājanivedane saty anayā vyavasthayā rājñā daṇḍādināpi mārgasthāpanaṃ karaṇīyam | akaraṇād rājño doṣaḥ | ity etadarthaṃ vyavahāramadhye rājadharmeṣu strīpuṃsayoḥ dharmā ucyante || 9.1 ||
asvatantrāḥ striyaḥ kāryāḥ puruṣaiḥ svair divāniśam |
viṣayeṣu ca sajjantyaḥ saṃsthāpyā ātmano vaśe || 9.2 ||
asvatantrā iti | bhartrādibhiḥ striyaḥ sarvadā paratantrāḥ kāryāś ca, niṣiddhe ‘pi ca śabdādiṣu viṣayeṣv atisaktiṃ bhajamānās tato ‘pāsyātmavartinyaḥ karyāḥ || 9.2 ||
pitā rakṣati kaumāre bhartā rakṣati yauvane |
rakṣanti sthavire putrā na strī svātantryam arhati || 9.3 ||
piteti | prāg vivāhāt pitā striyaṃ rakṣati | tad anu bhartā, tadabhāve putrāḥ, ata ebhir na kadācit svatantrāḥ kāryāḥ | bhartṛputrayauvanavārdhakyagrahaṇaṃ vyavasthārtham api tv aucityāt prāptānuvādo ‘yam || 9.3 ||
kāle ‘dātā pitā vācyo vācyāś cānupayan patiḥ |
mṛte bhartari putras tu vācyo mātur arakṣitā || 9.4 ||
kāla iti | “pradānaṃ pārg ṛtor” iti gautamasmaraṇam | asmin kāle pitā kanyād adan garhyaḥ | ṛtukāle ‘gacchan bhartā nindyaḥ | patau punar mṛte mātaram arakṣan putro garhaṇīyaḥ || 9.4 ||
sūkṣmebhyo ‘pi prasaṅgebhyaḥ striyo rakṣyā viśeṣataḥ |
dvayor hi kulayoḥ śokam āvaheyur arakṣitāḥ || 9.5 ||
sūkṣmebhya iti | alpebhyo ‘pi duḥsamparkebhyo duḥśīlatvasampādibhyo ‘tiśayena rakṣaṇīyāḥ | kim uta mahadbhyo yasmād arakṣitā sadyo ‘bhicārotpattyā pitṛbhartṛkulayor dvayor api santāpaṃ kuryuḥ || 9.5 ||
imaṃ hi sarvavarṇānāṃ paśyanto dharmam uttamam |
yatante rakṣituṃ bhāryāṃ bhartāro durbalā api || 9.6 ||
imam iti | yasmāt sarveṣāṃ brāhmaṇādivarṇānāṃ bhāryārakṣaṇaṃ vakṣyamāṇaślokanītyā sarvadharmebhya utkṛṣṭam ato bhartāro durbalā ap bhāyāṃ rakṣituṃ yatnaṃ kuryuḥ | yataḥ “striyo rakṣyāḥ” (9.5) iti pūrvam eva (uktam?) || 9.6 ||
svāṃ prasūtiṃ caritraṃ ca kulam ātmānam eva ca |
svaṃ ca dharmaṃ prayatnena jāyāṃ rakṣan hi rakṣati || 9.7 ||
svām iti | bhyāyāṃ yatnato rakṣan svāṃ santatiṃ saṅkarānutpādanena rakṣati | bhāryāsaṃrakṣaṇe ca tiṣṭhan samācāraṃ ca rakṣati | pitṛpitāmahādyanvayam ātmānaṃ cāsaṃkīrṇāpady aurdhvadaihikakaraṇeṣu rakṣati | svaṃ dharmaṃ viśuddhanā(bhā)ryasyādhānādyadhikārād rakṣati || 9.7 ||
patir bhāryāṃ sampraviśya garbho bhūtveha jāyate |
jāyāyās tad dhi jāyātvaṃ yad asyāṃ jāyate punaḥ || 9.8 ||
patir iti | bhartā bhāryāyām saṃpraviśya garbhatām āpadyeha jāyate | evaṃ cāpy akṣaravarṇasāmānyāni brūyād iti tena hetunā jāyāyā jāyātvaṃ punar asau putrarūpeṇaivaṃ cāsau rakṣaṇiyety etadarthaṃ nāmanirvacanam || 9.8 ||
yādṛśaṃ bhajate hi strī sutaṃ sūte tathāvidham |
tasmāt prajāviśuddhyarthaṃ striyaṃ rakṣet prayatnataḥ || 9.9 ||
yādṛśam iti | yasmād yathāvidhaṃ puruṣaṃ yathācoditaṃ bhartāraṃ śāstraniṣiddhaṃ vā strī sevate tādṛśaṃ śāstroktam eva tathokṛṣṭaṃ niṣiddhasevanena ca nikṛṣṭaṃ putraṃ janayati | tasmād apatyaśuddhyarthaṃ bhāryāṃ yatnato rakṣet || 9.9 ||
na kaścid yoṣitaḥ śaktaḥ prasahya parirakṣitum |
etair upāyayogais tu śakyās tāḥ parirakṣitum || 9.10 ||
evaṃ rakṣaṇiyam upadiṣṭe kathaṃ rakṣyā ity ata āha na kaścid iti | na kaścid balād anta[ni]rodhādinā striyo rakṣituṃ (pārayati?) anayor manovyabhicāratve ‘rakṣyatvād vakṣyamāṇai rakṣaṇopayayogais tu śakyās tāḥ parirakṣitum || 9.10 ||
arthasya saṃgrahe caināṃ vyaye caiva niyojayet |
śauce dharma ‘nnapaktyāṃ ca pāriṇāhyasya vekṣaṇe || 9.11 ||
tān upāyān āha arthasyeti | dhanāvasthānaviniyogayos tathā dravyaśarīraśuddhau śauce dharme ca śayanāsanādiṣu pratijāgaraṇe evaṃ ca tadvyāpṛtamanā śrāntā svapiti saṃyogaṃ na smarati
|| 9.11 ||
arakṣitā gṛhe ruddhāḥ puruṣair āptakāribhiḥ |
ātmānam ātmanā yās tu rakṣeyus tāḥ surakṣitāḥ || 9.12 ||
arakṣitā iti | tā api arakṣitā eva manovyabhicārasaṃbhavād yāḥ punar ātmanaivātmānaṃ saṃyamāśrayeṇa rakṣata eva suṣṭhumanā (samā?)cāreṇa saṃyamādhāna āsāṃ yatnaḥ kāryaḥ | iti pradhānasaṃrakṣaṇopāyopadeśaḥ || 9.12 ||
pānaṃ durjanasaṃsargaḥ patyā ca viraho ‘ṭanam |
svapno ‘nyagehavāsaś ca nārīsaṃdūṣaṇāni ṣaṭ || 9.13 ||
pānam iti | madyapānadurjanasaṃsargo ‘satpuruṣeṇa sahādhivāsaḥ, bhartṛviyogaḥ, itaragṛhe vāsaḥ sarvathaiva nārīṇām ityetāni ṣaṭ dūṣaṇāni sādhuprakṛter api nāryāḥ vyabhicārākhyadoṣajananāni, tasmād ebhya etāḥ parirakṣaṇīyāḥ || 9.13 ||
naitā rūpaṃ parīkṣante nāsāṃ vayasi saṃsthitiḥ|
surūpaṃ vā virūpaṃ vā punām ity eva bhuñjate || 9.14 ||
naitā iti | naitā surūpakurūpavicāraṃ kurvanti | na hy āsāṃ yauvanavān puruṣaś ca priyo bhavati | api tu surūpaṃ vā virūpaṃ vā pumān ity evāvataiva tāḥ naram upabhuñjante | api ca puṃmātradarśaṇena dharyacalanāyāsāṃ tāḥ puṃścalyaḥ || 9.14 ||
pauṃścalyāc calacittāc ca naiḥsnehyāc ca svabhāvataḥ |
rakṣitāḥ yatnato ‘pīha bhartṛṣv etā vikurvate || 9.15 ||
pauṃścalyād iti | pūmścalyāḥ calacittatvāt svabhāvenaiva snehābhāvāt etāḥ yatnena rakṣitā api (a) satyoktyabhicārāśrayeṇa bhartṛṣu vikriyāṃ gacchanti || 9.15 ||
evaṃ svabhāvaṃ jñātvāsāṃ prajāpatinisargajam |
paramaṃ yatnam ātiṣṭhet puruṣo rakṣaṇaṃ prati || 9.16 ||
evam iti | evaṃ ślokadvayaṃ dauḥśīlyāc calacittatvoktarūpam āsāṃ svabhāvaṃ hiraṇyagarbhasṛṣṭikāle janitaṃ jñātvā rakṣāyāṃ yatnena (yatnaḥ?) puruṣeṇa kāryam (kāryāḥ?)
|| 9.16 ||
śayyāsanam alaṃkāraṃ kāmaṃ krodham anārjavam |
drohabhāvaṃ kucaryāṃ ca strībhyo manur akalpayan || 9.17 ||
śayyāsanam alaṃkāraṃ kāmaṃ krodham anārjavam iti | svāpopaveśanālṅkaraṇamaṇiṃ kāmakrodhalobhāpathātitvakutsitācāratvāni strībhyo manuḥ sargādau kalpitavān | tato āsāṃ rakṣākaraṇe yatnaḥ kāryaḥ || 9.17 ||
nāsti strīṇāṃ kriyā mantrair iti dharme vyavasthitaḥ |
nirindriyā hy amantrāś ca striyo ‘nṛtam iti sthitiḥ || 9.18 ||
nāsti strīṇāṃ kriyā mantrair iti dharme vyavasthitaḥ iti | mantrair jātakarmādisaṃskārakriyā strīṇāṃ nāstīti śāstramaryādā | evaṃ mantravaj jātakarmādisaṃskārābhāvāt svabhāvataś ca striyo nirvīryā adhyayanābhāvāc cāmantrāś cāmantritve sati dharmajñatvābhāva(vāda?)śauce ‘pi pravarteran | tatparvṛttau ca bhayādyabhāvātvād aśubhāḥ striyaḥiti śāstramaryādā || 9.18 ||
tathā ca śrutayo bahvyo nigītā nigameṣv api |
svālakṣaṇyaparīkṣārthaṃ tāsāṃ śṛṇuta niṣkṛtīḥ || 9.19 ||
tatheti | niścitam eva vyabhicārātmakatve jñāpikā ‘na caitad vidmo brāhmaṇo(-ṇāḥ) vā” ity evamādyā bahvyāḥ śrutato vedeṣv iti paṭhitāḥ | tāsāṃ ca śrutīnāṃ madhyāt prāyaścittarūpāṃ śrutiṃ śṛṇuta || 9.19 ||
yan me mātā pralulubhe vicaranty apativratā |
tan me retaḥ pitā vṛṅktām ity asyaitan nidarśaṇanm || 9.20 ||
yan ma iti | mama mātā aptyaparāyaṇā yathānyapuruṣaḥ saṃjātalobhābhūt parapuruṣasaṃkalpaduṣṭaṃ mātṛrajo mama pitā bodhayitvānyasya vyabhicārātmakatvasyaitat liṅgam iti kāraṇāvasthito ‘yam mantro ‘yaṃ ca cāturmāsyeṣṭyaṣṭakāyāṃ ca pādyānumantraṇe viniyuktaprasaṅgān mantraprayojanam āhuḥ || 9. 20 ||
dhyāyaty aniṣṭaṃ yat kiṃcit pāṇigrāhasya cetasā |
tasyaiṣa vyabhicārsya nihnavaḥ samyag ucyate || 9.21 ||
dhyāyatīti | bhartur anabhipretaṃ yat kiṃcit puruṣagamanaṃ manasā cintayati tasya manovyabhicārasyānena samyag buddhir ucyate | na vākkāyaviṣayo ‘tas tasyāṃ tadvyabhicārasvabhāvatvād etā yatnato rakṣyā | māteti śravaṇāt putrasyaivedaṃ prāyaścittaṃ na mātuḥ || 9.21 ||
yādṛgguṇena bhartrā strī saṃpūjyeta yathāvidhi |
tādṛgguṇā sā bhavati samudreṇeva nimnagā || 9.22 ||
yādṛgguṇeneti | yathārūpeṇa sādhunāsādhunā vā strībhiḥ saha vidhinā saṃyogaṃ (-go?) bhavet tathārūpeṇa tatsaṃparkāt sādhvācārā duḥśīlā vā bhavati | samudreṇa saṃyujyamānā gaṅgāpi tathā svādūdakāpi kṣārodakā saṃpadyata ity ātmasaṃyamākhyāṃ strīrakṣaṇopāyāntaropadeśārtham idam || 9.22 ||
akṣamālā vasiṣṭhena saṃyuktādhamayonijā |
sāraṅgī mandapālena jagāmābhyarhaṇīyatām || 9.23 ||
ata dṛṣṭāntam āha akṣamāleti | śārṅgeti ca | akṣamālākhyā caṇḍālī pariṇītā vasiṣṭhena cāṭavikā śārṅgī ca mandapālākhyena ūḍhā satī pūyatāṃ prāptā || 9.23 ||
etāś cānyāś ca loke ‘sminn apakṛṣṭaprasūtayaḥ |
utkarṣaṃ yoṣitaḥ prāptāḥ svaiḥ svair bhartṛguṇaiḥ śubhaiḥ || 9.24 ||
etā iti | utkarṣaṃ yoṣitaḥ prāptāḥ svaiḥ svair bhartṛguṇaiḥ śubhaiḥ | etāḥ prakṛtatve darśanārtham anayor matvā etā iti bahuvacanam āhuḥ, api tās tathānyādhamakulādyā nikṛṣṭapatayo nāryaḥ svabhartur asmiṃl loke utkṛṣṭatāṃ prāptāḥ | ato bhāryāsaṃrakṣaṇārthaṃ mayātmasaṃyama āśrayaṇīyaḥ || 9.24 ||
eṣoditā lokayātrā nityaṃ strīpuṃsayoḥ śubhā |
pretyeha ca sukhodarkān prajādharmān nibodhata || 9.25 ||
eṣa iti | eṣa lokācāraṃ sarvo vaḥ śubho dampatīnirṇaya uktaḥ, idānīṃ sukhahetūn kṛpaṇo paṇyasutajīvinoś cobhayor ity evaṃvidhān prajādharmān śṛṇuta || 9.25 ||
prajanārthaṃ mahābhāgāḥ pūjārhā gṛhadīptayaḥ |
striyaḥ śriyaś ca geheṣu na viśeṣo ‘sti kaścana || 9.26 ||
prajanārtham iti | evam eṣāṃ doṣavaty api śakyapratīkārtavād ataḥ striyo mālyavastrālaṅkaraṇādidānena saṃpādanārhāḥ | etāś ca gṛhaśobhābhūtāḥ striyaḥ śriyaś ca gṛheṣu tulyarūpāḥ niḥśrīkaṃ gṛhaṃ na rājate strīṃ vinā gṛhaṃ ca | evaṃ mahādhanam api prajānimittatvāt strīṇām iyaṃ ślokatraye ‘pi bhūtiḥ || 9.26 ||
utpādanam apatyasya jātasya paripālanam |
pratyahaṃ lokayātrāyāḥ pratyakṣaṃ strī nibandhanam || 9.27 ||
utpādanam iti | prītyarthaṃ lokayātrāyāḥ pratyahaṃ strī nibandhanam | apatyotpādanaṃ jātasya ca pālanam mitrabandhubhojanādeś ca lokavyavahārasya ekaikaguṇaḥ || 9.27 ||
apatyaṃ dharmakāryāṇi śuśrūṣā ratir uttamā |
dārādhīnas tathā svargaḥ pitṝṇām ātmanaś ca ha || 9.28 ||
apatyam iti | dārādhīnas tathā svargaḥ pitṝṇām ātamaḥ sadā | prajotpādanam agnihotrasādhanaśarīraśuśrūṣāratikrīḍāpitṝṇām ātmanaś cāpatyajananādinā svarga ity etat sadā bhāryāyattam || 9.28 ||
patiṃ yā nābhicarati manovāgdehasaṃyatā |
sā bhartṛlokān āpnoti sadbhiḥ sādhvīti cocyate || 9.29 ||
patim iti | yā manovāgdehasaṃyatā satī manovāgdehaiḥ patiṃ na vyabhicarati sā bhartrārjitān svargalokān prāpnoti sā sādhvīti cocyate || 9.29 ||
vyabhicārāt tu bhartuḥ strī loke prāpnoti nidyatām |
śṛgālayoniṃ cāpnoti pāparogaiś ca pīḍyate || 9.30 ||
vyabhicārād iti | durjanasaṃsargāl loke strī garhyatām, janmāntare śṛgālayoniṃ cāpnoti | antaḥkaraś ca kuṭhārai rogādibhiḥ pīḍyate | mahāprayojanatayā strīṇām utsekotpattyāśaṅkāyāṃ tannivāraṇārtham iha punar uktam || 9.30 ||
putraṃ pratyuktaṃ sadbhiḥ pūrvajaiś ca maharṣibhiḥ |
viśvajanyam imaṃ puṇyam upanyāsaṃ nibodhata || 9.31 ||
putam iti | punar adhikṛtya śiṣṭair manvādibhiḥ pūrvajātaiś cānyair maharṣibhiḥ saṃvakṣyamāṇaṃ sarvajanahitaṃ kalyāṇakaraṃ paraṃ śṛṇuta || 9.31 ||
bhartuḥ putraṃ vijānanti śrutidvaidhaṃ tu bhartari |
āhur utpādakaṃ kecid apare kṣetriṇaṃ viduḥ || 9.32 ||
bhartur iti | yo bhartā sākṣād utpāditaputra iti taṃ yo na manyate nātra vipratipattiṃ [-tiḥ], yatra punar udvoḍḥā janayitā tatra dviḥprakārā śrutitaḥ | kecid utpādakam avoḍhāram api tena putreṇa putravantam ātmānam āhuḥ | apare tu kṣetrasvāminaṃ voḍhāram anyajanitenāpi putreṇa putravantam āhuḥ || 9.32 ||
kṣetrabhūtā smṛtā nārī bījabhūtaḥ smṛtaḥ pumān |
kṣetrabījasamāyogāt saṃbhavaḥ sarvedehinām || 9.33 ||
kṣetrabhūteti | vrīhyādyutpattisthānatulyā ṛṣibhiḥ smṛtā, manuṣyaś ca vrīhyādibījatulyaḥ smṛto yasmāt kṣetrabījasaṃyogāt sarvaprāṇinām utpattir evaṃ cobhayoḥ kāraṇatvasyātiśiṣṭatvād yuktā vipratipattiḥ – kiṃ yatsaṃbandhi kṣetraṃ tasyāpatyaṃ uta bījinaḥ iti || 9.33 ||
viśiṣṭaṃ kutracid bījaṃ strīyonis tv eva kutracit |
ubhayaṃ tu samaṃ yatra sā prasūtiḥ praśasyate || 9.34 ||
viśīṣṭam iti | kutracid bījaṃ pradhānaṃ yatra “jātā ye tu aniyuktāyām” iti nyāyena śulkopanītā strīkvacit punaḥ kṣetraṃ yatra yat talpajāpramītasyeti nyāyenopanatā yatra punar bījayonyā mātā pitā ca samaṃ janayitārau tad apatyaṃ praśastaṃ bhavati || 9.34 ||
bījasya caiva yonyāś ca bījam utkṛṣṭam ucyate |
sarvabhūtaprasūtir hi bījalakṣaṇalakṣitā || 9.35 ||
tatra prādhānyapakṣe bhāvam āha bījasyeti | bījakṣetrayor madhyāt bījaṃ pradhānam ucyate, yasmāt sarvapadārthānām utpattir bījagatavarṇarūpādicihnenopalakṣitā dṛśyate || 9.35 ||
yādṛśaṃ tūpyate bījaṃ kṣetre kālopapādite |
tādṛg rohati tat tasmin bījaṃ svair vyañjitaṃ guṇaiḥ || 9.36 ||
tathā ca yādṛśam iti | yajjātīyaṃ dravyaṃ vrīhyādi kāle saṅgavādau karṣaṇādinā kṣetre upyate tajjātīyam eva tadbījaṃ svacihair upalakṣitaṃ tasmin kṣetre jāyate || 9.36 ||
iyaṃ bhūmir hi bhūtānāṃ śāśvatī yonir ucyate |
na ca yoniguṇān kāṃścid bījaṃ puṣyati puṣṭiṣu || 9.37 ||
evam anvayadvāreṇa bījaprādhānyaṃ pradarśya vyatirekaguṇena śaṃsayitum āha iyam iti | yasmāt tu gulmalatādīnāṃ bhūtānām eṣā bhūmir yadi yoniḥ kṣetraṃ sarvalokair ucyate na ca bhūmyākhyayonidharmaḥ kāṃścid api mṛnmayatvādīn guṇān gulmādibījam ātmadoṣe svāṅkurakāṇḍavyavasthāsu khalu vartate tasmād yoniguṇān anuvartamānā prakṣepaṃ pradhānam || 9.37 ||
bhūmāv apy ekakedāre kāloptāni kṛṣīvalaiḥ |
nānārūpāṇi jāyante bījānīha svabhāvataḥ || 9.38 ||
kiṃ ca bhūvāv iti | bhūmāv ekasminn api kṣetre karṣakair yogyavāpanakāle kalamamudgādīni vicitrāṇy uptāni bījāni anyarūpāṇi ca bījasvabhāvānuvartamānāj jāyante bhūmāv anekarūpāṇi bhavanti || 9.38 ||
vrīhayaḥ śālayo mudgās tilā māṣās tathā yavāḥ |
yathābījaṃ prarohanti laśunānīkṣavas tathā || 9.39 ||
vrīhaya iti | śūkadhānyeṣu madhyādīyatśūkā vrīhayaḥ ṣaṣṭikādyāni dīrghaśūkāḥ śālayo raktā śālyādayas te tathā mudgādayo bījatvabhāvānnatikramenaiva vicitrā jāyante || 9.39 ||
anyad uptaṃ jātam anyad ity etan nopapadyate |
upyate yad dhi yad bījaṃ tat tad eva prarohati || 9.40 ||
evaṃ ca sati anyad iti | tilādyuptaṃ mudgādi jāyata ity etan na saṃbhavati | yasmād yad eva bījam upyate śālyādāv iva manuṣyeṣv api bījaprādhānyam || 9.40 ||
tat prājñena vinītena jñānavijñānavedinā |
āyuṣkāmena vaptavyaṃ na jātu parayoṣiti || 9.41 ||
idānīṃ kṣetrapādhānyam āha tad iti | tad bījaṃ prajñāvatā nītiyuktena lokaśāstravyavahārajñena īdṛśam anāyuṣyam ity uktatvād āyur icchatā na kadācit parabhāryāyāṃ vaptavyaṃ niṣphalatvāt | tathā ca vakṣyati (9.43) “tathā naśyati tat kṣiptaṃ bījaṃ paraparigrahe” iti || 9.41 ||
atra gāthā vāyugītāḥ kīrtayanti purāvidaḥ |
yato bījaṃ na vaptavyaṃ puṃsā paraparigrahe || 9.42 ||
atreti | asminn arthe vāyuproktā gāthāḥ chandoviśeṣayuktāni vākyāni nītikālajñāḥ kathayanti yathā parabhāryāyāṃ bījaṃ manuṣyeṇa na vaptavyam iti || 9.42 ||
naśyatīṣur yathāviddhaḥ khe viddham anuvidhyataḥ |
tathā naśyati vai kṣipraṃ bījaṃ paraparigrahe || 9.43 ||
atra dṛṣṭāntam āha naśyatīṣur yathā kṣipta iti | yathānyena mṛgaṃ viddhaṃ tasminn eva chidre anyathā paścāc cyutaśaro niṣphalo bhavati, pūrvahantur eva mṛgabhāgatvāt | evaṃ parabhāryāyām uptaṃ kṣipram eva niṣphalaṃ bhavati | garbhagrahaṇasamantaram eva kṣetriṇaḥ phalam ity uktam || 9.43 ||
pṛthor apīmāṃ pṛthivīṃ bhāryāṃ pūrvavido viduḥ |
sthānucchedasya kedāram āhuḥ śalyavato mṛgam || 9.44 ||
pṛthor iti | imām api pṛthivīm anikarājasaṃbandhiparigaṇitatvāt yathā yenaiva sthānum utpāṭhya kṣetraṃ rakṣitaṃ tad dhy upadarśitakṣetram āhuḥ | mṛgam āhur evaṃ pūrvaparigrahītuḥ svāmyāt tasyāivāpatyaṃ bhavati, na janayituḥ || 9.44 ||
etāvān eva puruṣo yaj jāyātmā prajeti ha |
viprāḥ prāhus tathā caitad yo bhartā sā smṛtāṅganā || 9.45 ||
etāvān iti | naikaḥ puruṣo vāpi tu bhāryā śarīram apatyānīty etat pariṇama eva | tathā caivaṃ tasyām utpāditaṃ bhartur evāpatyaṃ bhavati yataś ca dampatyor aikyam ataḥ || 9.45 ||
na niṣkrayavisargābhyāṃ bhartur bhāryā vimucyate |
evaṃ dharmaṃ vijānīmaḥ prāk prajāpatinirmitam || 9.46 ||
neti | evaṃ dharmaṃ vijānīmaḥ prāk prajāpatinirmitam | bandhavikrayādinā tyāgena na ca bhāryātvāt strī bhartur apaity evaṃ prajāpatinā smṛtam | pūrvam eva strī ca ātmasāt kartuṃ na śakyeti || 9.46 ||
sakṛd aṃśo nipatati sakṛt kanyā pradīyate |
sakṛd āha dadānīti trīṇy etāni satāṃ sakṛt || 9.47 ||
sakṛd iti | sakṛd āha dadānīti trīṇy etāni satāṃ sakṛt | bhāgakāle paitṛkādidhanabhāga ekavāram evāpatati na dharmabhāgakalpanāyāṃ punar bhāgī kriyate | kanyāpi sakṛd dātavyā vāgdattāyāḥ kanyāyāḥ paṇyataḥ prāptāyā api na bījino ‘patyaṃ bhavati | tathā kanyāto ‘nyasminn api gavādiṣu etāni trīṇi aṅganipātanādīni sādhūnāṃ sakṛd bhavanti | na punaḥkaranam eṣām asti sakṛd āha dadānīti || 9.47 ||
yathā go’śvoṣṭradāsīṣu mahiṣyajāvikāsu ca |
notpadakaḥ prajābhāgī tathaivānyāṅganāsv api || 9.48 ||
ye ‘kṣetriṇo bījavantaḥ parakṣetrapravāpiṇaḥ |
te vai sasyasya jatasya na labhante phalaṃ kvacit || 9.49 ||
yatheti | ya iti | ye kṣetrasvāmino na bhavanti kevalaṃ bījasvāminaḥ parakīye kṣetre bīje ca patite tasmin kṣetre dhānyāder jātasya yat phalaṃ na kadācid deśe labhanta iti prakṛtasya dṛṣṭāntaḥ|| 9.48–49 ||
yad anyagoṣu vṛṣabho vatsānāṃ janayec chatam |
gominām eva te vatsā moghaṃ skanditam ārṣabham || 9.50 ||
yad iti | svāminām iti | yady anyasaṃbandhinīṣu gostrīṣu parakīyo vṛṣabhaḥ śatam api vatsānāṃ janayet tadā te sarvavatsā gosvāminām eva, taṃ naiko ‘pi bījasvāmino vṛṣabhasaṃbandhīyakaḥ śukro vṛṣabhasvāmino niṣphala eva || 9.50 ||
tathaivākṣetriṇo bījaṃ parakṣetrapravāpiṇaḥ |
kurvanti kṣetriṇām arthaṃ na bījī labhate phalam || 9.51 ||
tatheti | yadvad goṣu tadvad evāvidyamānastrīsvāmyāḥ santo ye parakīyāyāṃ yoṣiti bījaṃ vapanti te kṣetrasvāminām evāpatyalakṣaṇam uktaṃ janayitāpatyākhyaṃ phalaṃ na labhate || 9.51 ||
phalaṃ tv anabhisaṃdhāya kṣetriṇāṃ bījināṃ tathā |
pratyakṣaṃ kṣetriṇām artho bījād yonir garīyasī || 9.52 ||
phalam iti | “yad atrotpadyate tayor ubhayor apatyam” evaṃ kṣetrabījinor yatra saṃbandho nāsti tatra niḥsaṃdigdham eva kṣetriṇo ‘patyaṃ yad uktanītyā bījāt kṣetraṃ balavat || 9.52 ||
kriyābhyupagamāt tv etad bījārthaṃ yat pradīyate |
tasyeha bhāginau dṛṣṭau bījī kṣetrika eva ca || 9.53 ||
kriyābhyupagamād iti | āvayor ubhayor apatyaṃ syād ity evaṃ saṃvidaṅgīkaraṇe punar etat kṣetrasvāminā bījavapanārthaṃ yad bījino phalam || 9.53 ||
oghavātāhṛtaṃ bījaṃ yasya kṣetre prarohati |
kṣetrikasyaiva tad bījaṃ na vaptā labhate phalam || 9.54 ||
oghavātāhṛtam iti | tat kṣepaṃ kṣetrikasyeha na bījī labhate phalam | jalavātābhyām anyadīyakṣetrād ānītaṃ bījaṃ yadīyakṣetre svāminas tad bījaṃ bhavati na tu tat phalaṃ yena bījaṃ nyuptaṃ sa labhate | evaṃ svabhāvāc caivam eṣāṃ dvipūrvam api parastrīgamane kṣetriṇa evāpatyam ity etad anena sūcyate || 9.54 ||
eṣa dharmo gavāśvasya dāsyuṣṭrājāvikasya ca |
vihaṅgamahiṣīṇāṃ ca vijñeyaḥ prasavaṃ prati || 9.55 ||
eṣa iti | go’śvoṣṭraghāgaiḍakapakṣimahiṣāṇāṃ santatiṃ praty eṣā vyavasthā jñeyā | yat kṣetrasvāmina evaṃ gavādisantateḥ svāmyaṃ na ṛṣabhādeḥ, tāḥ saṃvedāt tūbhayor apīti
|| 9.55 ||
etad vaḥ sāraphalgutvaṃ bījayonyoḥ prakīrtitam |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi yoṣitāṃ dharmam āpadi || 9.56 ||
etad iti | etad bījayonyoḥ sāraphalgutvaṃ yuṣmākaṃ kathitam | ato ‘nantaraṃ strīṇāṃ santānābhāve yat kartavyaṃ tad vakṣyāmi || 9.56 ||
bhrātur jyeṣṭhasya bhāryā yā gurupatny anujasya sā |
yavīyasas tu yā bhāryā snuṣā jyeṣṭhasya sā smṛtā || 9.57 ||
bhrātur iti | yavīyasas tu yā bhāryā snuṣā jyeṣṭhasya sā smṛtā | jyeṣṭhabhrātṛpatnī anujasya gurubhāryayā tulyā | kanīyasaḥ patnī punar jyeṣṭhasya snuṣā pracakṣate || 9.57 }}
jyeṣṭho yavīyaso bhāryāṃ yavīyān vāgrajastriyam |
patitau bhavato gatvā niyuktāv apy anāpadi || 9.58 ||
jyeṣṭha iti | jyeṣṭhaḥ kanīyaḥpatnīṃ kanīyān jyeṣṭhabhāryāṃ vakṣyamāṇanītyā guruniyuktām api sāmānyaṃ (?) gatvā patitau bhavataḥ || 9.58 ||
devarād vā sapiṇḍād vā striyā samyaṅ niyuktayā. |
prajepsitādhigantavyā santānasya parikṣaye || 9.59 ||
devarād iti | striyā bhartur abhāve śvaśurādibhir bhartrā vā vyādhitena vā putrotpādanavidhiyuktatvāt niyuktayā bhartṛbhrātus tadabhāve tatsapiṇḍasya vā sakāśād abhīṣṭāḥ putralakṣaṇaprajāḥ putrābhāvāt saṃpādanīyāḥ || 9.59 ||
vidhavāyāṃ niyuktas tu ghṛtākto vāgyato niśi |
ekam utpādayet putraṃ na dvitīyaṃ kathaṃcana || 9.60 ||
vidhavāyām iti | “yas talpajaḥ parmītasya vā” iti darśanād abhartṛkāvyādhitabhāryāviṣaye gurubhir niyukto ghṛtābhyakto maunavāk rātrāv ekaṃ putraṃ janayet, na dvitīyam || 9.60 ||
dvitīyam eke prajanaṃ manyante strīṣu tadvidaḥ |
anirvṛttaṃ niyogārthaṃ paśyanto dharmatas tayoḥ || 9.61 ||
dvitīyam iti | kecit punar ācāryāḥ putravidhijñā “ekaputro ‘putratulyaḥ” ity evam ekaputrotpadane sati dharmeṇa tayor aniṣpannaniyogaprayojanaṃ manyamānāḥ strīṣu dvitīyaputrotpadanaṃ manyante || 9.61 ||
vidhavāyāṃ niyogārthe nirvṛtte tu yathāvidhi |
guruvac ca snuṣāvac ca varteyātāṃ parasparam || 9.62 ||
vidhavāyām iti | vidhavādikāyā niyogaprayojane saṃyogākhye punar yathāśāstrasaṃpanne sati jyeṣṭho bhrātṛbhāryā ca parasparaṃ guruvat snuṣāvac ca varteyātām || 9.62 ||
niyuktau yau vidhiṃ hitvā varteyātāṃ tu kāmataḥ |
tāv ubhau patitau syātāṃ snuṣāgagurutalpagau || 9.63 ||
niyuktāv iti | yau jyeṣṭhakaniṣṭhau niyuktau ghṛtābhyaktatvādividhānaṃ parityajya svecchāto varteyātāṃ tau dvāv api snuṣāgagurutalpagau syātām || 9.63 ||
nānyasmin vidhavā nārī niyoktavyā dvijātibhiḥ |
anyasmin hi niyuñjānā dharmaṃ hanyuḥ sanatanam || 9.64 ||
evaṃ niyogam uktvā dūṣayitum āha “nānyasmin vidhavā nārī niyoktavyā kadācana” iti | vidhavā nārī anyasmin devarādau na niyojanīyā, yasmāt te devarādau niyojayanto ‘nādidharmam ekapatnīvrataṃ pratiṣedhaṃ nāśayeyuḥ || 9.64 ||
nodvāhikeṣu mantreṣu niyogaḥ kīrtyate kvacit |
na vivāhavidhāv uktaṃ vidhavāvedanaṃ punaḥ || 9.65 ||
neti | vivāhaprayojaneṣu mantreṣu “aryamāṇaṃ nu devaṃ” na ca vivāhaśāstre vidhavāyā anyena puṃsā saha punar vivāha uktaḥ || 9.65 ||
ayaṃ dvijair hi vidvadbhiḥ paśudharmo vigarhitaḥ |
manuṣyāṇām api prokto rājyaṃ praśāsati || 9.66 ||
ayam iti | yasmād ayaṃ paśusaṃbandhī vyavahāro ‘tyantakutsito venākhye rājani kurvāṇo ‘nyatvena proktas tasmād āādimanvādibhir eṣa śrutau nāstīti pūrvoktasyāyam hetuḥ || 9.66 ||
sa mahīm akhilāṃ bhuñjan rājarṣipravaraḥ purā |
varṇānāṃ saṃkaraṃ cakre kāmopahatacetasaḥ || 9.67 ||
sa iti | sa venaḥ pūrvaṃ samagrāṃ pṛthivīṃ pālayan tata eva rājarṣiśreṣṭho na tu dhārmikaḥ, tato brāhmaṇānujñayaitad bhrātṛbhāryāgamanalakṣaṇaṃ varṇasaṃkaraṃ vartitavān || 9.67 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti yo mohāt pramītapatikāṃ striyam |
niyojayaty apatyārthaṃ taṃ vigarhanti sādhavaḥ || 9.68 ||
tadāprabhṛtīti | evaṃ cedānīṃtanatvā amuṣyācārasya tasmād venakālāt prabhṛti yo mṛtabhartṛkāṃ striyaṃ samyakśāstravigarhite evaṃ ca vihitapratiṣedhatvān niyogasya kālasūtraniyoktṝṇāṃ nindāśravaṇaṃ strīdharmeṣu || 9.68 ||
yasyā mriyeta kanyāyā vācā satye kṛte patiḥ |
tām anena vidhānena nijo vindeta devaraḥ || 9.69 ||
niyogaprakārakatvāt kanyāgatam idam āha yasyā iti | vāgdattāyāḥ kanyāyāḥ bhartā mriyeta tām anena vidhinā bhartuḥ sodaryo bhrātā udvahet || 9.69 ||
yathāvidhy adhigamyainām śuklavastrāṃ śucivratām |
mitho bhajetāprasavāt sakṛt sakṛd ṛtāv ṛtau || 9.70 ||
yatheti  | sa devaro vivāhavidhinā etāṃ svīkṛtya śuklavastrāṃ vākkāyasaṃyutāṃ rahasy ā garbhagrahaṇāt prabhṛti vācanikatvāt ca saṃskārasya cāsau devarasya bhāryā bhavatīti kṣetrasvāmina eva tad apatyaṃ bhavatīti || 9.70 ||
na datvā kasyacit kanyāṃ punar dadyād vicakṣaṇaḥ |
datvā punaḥ prayacchan hi prāpnoti puruṣānṛtam || 9.71 ||
neti | śāstrajño ‘nyasmai kanyāṃ punar anyasmai na dadyāt || 9.71 ||
[atra dvisaptatitamaślokād ārabhyādhyāyasamāptiparyantam upalabdheṣu pustakeṣu govindarājakṛtaṭīkā na dṛśyate kevalaṃ mūlaślokā eva vartante | atas te ślokā asmin granthe na gṛhītāḥ ]
** Commentary on verses 72–336 is not found in the available manuscripts.
daśamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
adhīyīraṃs trayo varṇāḥ svakarmasthā dvijātayaḥ |
prabrūyād brāhmaṇas tv eṣāṃ netarāv iti niścayaḥ || 10.1 ||
oṃ siddhiḥ || saṃkīrṇānāṃ varṇebhya evotpattir varṇotpattiḥ abhidhānam antareṇa tadutpattikathanasya aśakyatvād varṇotpattyabhidhānasya prasaṅgenotpadanāya dvitīyādhyāyasiddhaṃ traivarṇikapradhānadharmaṃ vedādhyayanam anuvaditum āha adhīyīran iti | brāhmaṇādayas trayo varṇā kuryān na kṣatriyavaiśyāv ity ayaṃ viścaya ity adhyayanānuvādaprasaṅgena brāhmaṇasyādhyāpananiyamam āha ‘prabrūyād brāhmaṇas tv eṣām’ ity anenaiva kṣatriyavaiśyayor adhyāpananivṛttisiddhau svaśabdena niṣedhavacanaṃ niṣiddhācaraṇena prāyaścittaviśeṣārtham || 10.1 ||
sarveṣāṃ brāhmaṇo vidyād vṛttyupāyān yathāvidhi |
prabrūyād itarebhyaś ca svayaṃ caiva tathā bhavet || 10.2 ||
kiṃ ca sarveṣām iti | sarveṣāṃ saṃkaraparyantānām ātmakuṭumbasthityupāyān brāhmaṇo yathāśāstraṃ jānīyāt | tathā tebhyaś copadiśed ātmanā ca vṛttiniyamān anutiṣṭhet || 10.2 ||
vaiśeṣyāt prakṛtiśraiṣṭhyān niyamasya ca dhāraṇāt |
saṃskārasya viśeṣāc ca varṇānāṃ brāhmaṇaḥ prabhuḥ || 10.3 ||
atrārthavādaḥ vaiśeṣyād iti | jātyutkarṣān mukhodbhavatvena ca śreṣṭhakāraṇajanyatvāt snātakasya vratānāṃ ca karaṇāt saṃskārasya copanayanākhyasya bālasyaiva vidhānena viśeṣād varṇānāṃ vṛttivedanatadupadeśayor brāhmaṇa eveśatā || 10.3 ||
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyas trayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ |
caturtha ekajātis tu śūdro nāsti tu pañcamaḥ || 10.4 ||
brāhmaṇa iti | brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyās trayo varṇā dvijā upanayanasya teṣāṃ vihitatvāt | śūdraḥ punaś caturtho varṇaḥ ekavarṇa (ekajātir?) upanayanābhāvāt | pañcamaḥ punar varṇo nāsti antarprabhavānāṃ mātāpitṛjātivyatiriktajātyantaratvād asyetaravad evaṃ cāyam eṣāṃ varṇatvopadeśaḥ śāstre vyavahārārthaḥ | śūdrasyopanayanānupdeśād evaikajātitvasiddhau caturtha ekajātis tu iti idaṃ dharmeṇa vastudharmajñā  ity anenāyaṃ mantraḥ keśavapanādinā saṃskārāntaravad upanayanaṃ nāstīti sūcanārtham || 10.4 ||
sarvavarṇeṣu tulyāsu patnīṣv akṣatayoniṣu |
ānulomyena saṃbhūtā jātyā jñeyās ta eva te || 10.5 ||
sarvavarṣeṣv iti | ānulomyena saṃbhūtā jātyā jñeyās ta eva te | brāhmaṇādicaruṣv api varṇeṣu samānajātīyāsu akṣatayoniṣu yathāśāstrasamūḍhāsu ānulomyena brāhmaṇena brāhmanyāṃ kṣatriyeṇa kṣatriyāyām ity evam anukrameṇa ye jātāḥ te pitror jātitajjātīyā eva boddhavyāḥ | sarvavarṇeṣu ity upādānād varṇatvamātreṇa tulyās tu ity āśaṅkānivṛttyartham ānulomyagrahaṇam | idam eva ca sakalamātāpitṛprabandhasyāpi jātilakṣaṇam anāditvena punaḥ saṃsārasyāsyaiva vyāpakatvāt sarvatra ceha prakaraṇe jātilakṣaṇaprakaraṇaṃ śāstravyavahāraphalajātiparijñānārthaṃ gavāśvādivat brāhmaṇādīnāṃ jātibhedāvabodhakasyākāraviśeṣasyānupalabdheḥ | evaṃ ca patnīgrahaṇād apatnyutpannānāṃ naiva tajjātīyatvam | tathā ca devalaḥ |
dvitīyena tu yaḥ pitrā savarṇāyāṃ prajāyate |
adhareṭa iti khyātaḥ śūdradharmā sajātitaḥ ||
vratahīnā asaṃskāryāḥ savarṇāsv api ye sutāḥ |
utpāditāḥ savarṇena vrātyā iti bahiṣkṛtḥ ||
ity āha | tathā |
savarṇebhyaḥ savarṇāsu jāyante hi svajātayaḥ |
ity etad api yājñavalkyavākyamānavaikavākyatvād vinnāsv eṣa vidhiḥ smṛta iti copasaṃhārāt patnīviṣayam | evaṃ vyāsaḥ |
svoḍhājātā samānāḥ syuḥ saṃkarāḥ syur ato ‘nyathā |
ity āha | baudhāyanaś ca “etān vānyān vivāhān āhur etaiḥ saṃkṛtābhyaḥ utpannās tajjātīyā bhavanti na tv anya” ity āha | bṛhaspatiś ca |
prātilomyena saṃbhūtā niyogena vina ca ye |
sāvitrīpatitā jātān trayaḥ saṃkarajātayaḥ ||
ity āha | anye tu |
satyakāmo jābālo jābālaṃ mātaram āmantrayāṃ cakre | ‘bhavati, brahmacaryaṃ ca vatsyāmi, kiṃgotro ‘ham asmīti’ | sā hainam uvāca, ‘nāham etad veda yadgotras tvam asi tāta, bahv ahaṃ carantī paricāriṇī yauvane tvām ālabhe | sāham etan na veda yadgotras tvam asi | jābālā tu nāmā aham asmi, satyakāmo nāmas tvam asi | sa satyakāma eva jābālo bravītha’ iti | sa ha hāridrumataṃ gautamam etyovāca ‘brahmacaryaṃ bhagavato vatsyāmi, upeyāṃ bhagavantam’ iti | taṃ hovāca ‘kiṃgotro nu somya asīti’ | sa hovāca ‘nāham etad veda bho yadgotro ‘ham asmi | apṛcchaṃ mātaram | sā māṃ pratyabravīt bahv ahaṃ carantī paricāriṇī yauvane tvām ālabhe | sāham etan na veda yadgotras tvam asi | jābālā tu nāmāham asmi, satyakāmo nāmas tvam asi | so ‘haṃ satyakāmo jābālo ‘smi bho’ iti taṃ hovāca | naitad abrāhmaṇo vivaktum arhati | samidhaṃ somya āhara, uta tvā neṣya’
iti jābālaśrutidarśanāt kuṇḍagolakayoś ca śrāddhapratiṣedhadarśanāc ca eṣāṃ yasyāpi pitṛjātīyatvam iti | tac cāpareṇa saha tenāham etad vedeti mātuḥ niścayābhāvān neitad abrāhmaṇo vivaktum arhati iti pitṛto ‘pi tasyotpattisaṃbhavāt tadabhiprāyam etat syāt, yat tu kuṇḍagolakayoḥ śrāddhaniṣedhadarśanaṃ tad uktaṃ śrutibhrameṇa brāhmaṇyāśaṅkāyāṃ parihārārthaṃ syān na tu svabhraṣṭabrāhmaṇyajanakam || 10.5 ||
strīṣv anantarajātāsu dvijair utpāditān sutān |
sadṛśān eva tān āhur mātṛdoṣavigarhitān || 10.6 ||
strīṣv iti | avyavahitavarjaṇātīyāsu bhāryāsu dvijātibhiḥ ye utpāditāḥ putrāḥ yathā brāhmaṇena kṣatriyāyāṃ kṣatriyeṇa vaiśyāyāṃ vaiśyena śūdrāyāṃ tanmātur hīnajātīyatve garhitā, na te eva pitṛsadṛśān tān manvādaya āhuḥ | na tu pitṛjatīyanāmāni ca eṣāṃ mūrdhavasiktamāhiṣyakaraṇākhyāni yājñavalkyādibhir uktāni | vṛttayaś ca eṣāṃ atha uśanasā nirdiṣṭāḥ:
hastyaśvarathaśikṣā astradhāraṇaṃ ca mūrdhāvasikānām, gītanṛtyanakṣatraiḥ jīvanaṃ sasyarakṣā ca mahiṣyāṇām, svajātiśuśrūṣā dhānyādhyakṣatā rājasevā durgāntaḥpurarakṣā ca pāraśavograkaraṇānām |
iti || 10.6 ||
anantarāsu jātānāṃ vidhir eṣa sanātanaḥ |
dvyekāntarāsu jātānāṃ dharmyaṃ vidyād imaṃ vidhim || 10.7 ||
anantarāsv iti | eṣa nityo vidhiḥ anantarajātibhāryotpannānām uktaḥ, ekena dvābhyāṃ vyavahitāḥ tadutpannānāṃ yathā brāhmaṇena vaiśyāyāṃ kṣatriyeṇa śūdrāyāṃ brāhmaṇena ca śūdrāyām imaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ dharmād anapetaṃ nāmavidhānaṃ jānīyāt || 10.7 ||
brāhmaṇād vaiśyakanyāyām ambaṣṭho nāma jāyate |
niṣādaḥ śūdrakanyāyāṃ yaḥ pāraśava ucyate || 10.8 ||
brāhmaṇād iti | “vinnāsv eṣa vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ” ity yājñavalkyadarśanāt brāhmaṇād vaiśyakanyāyām ūḍhāyāṃ ambaṣṭhākho jāyate | śūdrakanyāyām ūḍhāyāṃ niṣada utpadyate | yaḥ pāraśava ity anena saṃjñāntareṇāśāstra ucyate | saṃjñāntarakathanaṃ matsyaghātavṛttipratilomaniṣādabhrāntinirākaraṇārtham || 10.8 ||
kṣatriyāc chūdrakanyāyāṃ krūrācāravihāravān |
kṣatraśūdravapur jantur ugro nāma prajāyate || 10.9 ||
kṣatriyād iti | kṣatriyāt śūdrakanyāyāṃ ūḍhāyāṃ krūraceṣṭāvān pāpasvabhāvaḥ kṣatriyaśūdrotpannadehaḥ prāṇy ugrākho naro jāyate | vṛttiś cāsya pūrvaśloke uśanasoktā darśitā
|| 10.9 ||
viprasya triṣu varṇeṣu nṛpater varṇayor dvayoḥ |
vaiśyasya varṇe caikasmin ṣaḍ ete ‘pasadāḥ smṛtāḥ || 10.10 ||
viprasyeti | brāhmaṇakṣatriyādivarṇatrayastrīṣu kṣatriyasya vaiśyādivarṇadvayastriyoḥ vaiśyasya ca śūdrākhyavarṇastriyām | eta uktās te savarṇaputrakāpekṣayāpasadā putrakāryād avaśīrṇā ity ete apasadāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
kṣatriyād viprakanyāyāṃ sūto bhavati jātitaḥ |
vaiśyān māgadhavaidehau rājaviprāṅganāsutau || 10.11 ||
evam anulomān uktvā pratilomān āha kṣatriyād iti | asmin śloke pūrvārdhaṃ sugamam | uttarārdhaṃ vyākhyātam | atra vivāhāsaṃbhavāt kanyāgrahaṇaṃ strīmātrapradarśanārtham | kṣatriyābrāhmaṇyoḥ yathākramaṃ māgadhavaidehākhyau putrau bhavataḥ | eṣāṃ vṛttayo ‘sminn eva śāstre nirdiṣṭāḥ | anirdiṣṭavṛttīnāṃ punaḥ śāstrāntroktā asmābhir yathāvasaraṃ nirdiśyante
|| 10.11 ||
śūdrād āyogavaḥ kṣattā caṇḍālaś cādhamo nṛṇām |
vaiśyarājanyaviprāsu jāyante varṇasaṃkarāḥ || 10.12 ||
śūdrād iti | vaiśyarājanyaviprāsu jāyante varṇasaṃkarāḥ | śūdrāt dvijātivarṇānāṃ maithunākhyasaṃkare sati vaiśyāyām āyogavaḥ kṣatriyāyāṃ kṣattā brāhmaṇyāṃ cāṇḍālo manuṣyādhamo jāyate || 10.12 ||
ekāntare tv ānulomyād ambaṣṭhograu yathā smṛtau |
kṣattṛvaidehakau tadvat prātilomye ‘pi janmani || 10.13 ||
ekāntara iti | ekavyavahite varṇe varṇānukrameṇa brāhmaṇād vaiśyāyāṃ kṣatriyāt śūdrāyāṃ utpannau ambaṣṭhograu yathā sparśādyarhau tadvat prātilomye janane ‘pi kṣatriyavaidehakau sparśādiyogyau vijñeyau | ekāntarotpannayoś ca sparśādyabhyanujñānād arthāntarotpannānām api sūtamāgadhāyogavānāṃ sparśādiyogyatvaṃ siddhaṃ bhavati | evaṃ caṇḍāla evaiko ‘tra paryudāsyate || 10.13 ||
putrā ye ‘nantarastrījāḥ krameṇoktā dvijanmanām |
tān anantaranāmnas tu mātṛdoṣāt pracakṣate || 10.14 ||
putrā iti | ye dvijātīnām anantaraikāntaradvyantarajātistrīṣu utpannāḥ krameṇoktāḥ putrās tān hīnamātṛkatvāt mātṛjātivyapadeśān ācakṣate ityīdam evaṃ mātṛpitṛvyatiriktajātitvāt dharmās te mātṛjātigatasaṃskārādidharmaprāptyarthaṃ vacanaṃ mātṛdoṣād iti hetūpādānād anantaragrahaṇam ekāntarapradarśanārtham || 10.14 ||
brāhmaṇād ugrakanyāyām āvṛto nāma jāyate |
ābhīro ‘mbaṣṭhakanyāyām āyogavyāṃ tu dhigvaṇaḥ || 10.15 ||
brāhmaṇād iti | kṣatriyeṇa śūdrāyām utpannā ugrī, tasyāṃ brāhmaṇād āvṛto nāmnā jāyate | brāhmaṇena vaiśyāyām utpannā ambaṣṭho, brāhmaṇād ābhīrākhyo jāyate | anayoś ca hastyādisvādhikā āvṛttānāṃ go'jāvirakṣā ābhīrāṇām ity arthe auśanase vṛttir uktā | śūdreṇa vaiśyāyām utpannā āyogavī, tasyāṃ brāhmaṇād ābhīrākhyo jāyate || 10.15 ||
āyogavaś ca kṣattā ca caṇḍālaś cādhamo nṛṇām |
prātilomyena jāyante śūdrād apasadās trayaḥ || 10.16 ||
āyogava iti | āyogavaḥ kṣattā caṇḍālo manuṣyādhama ity ete trayo vyutkrmeṇa vaiśyākṣatriyābrāhmaṇīṣu śūdrād apasadāḥ putrakāryād avagatāḥ jāyante || 10.16 ||
vaiśyān māgadhavaidehau kṣatriyāt sūta eva tu |
pratīpam etate jāyante pare ‘py apasadās trayaḥ || 10.17 ||
jāto niṣādāc chūdrāyāṃ jātyā bhavati pulkasaḥ |
śūdrāj jāto niṣādyāṃ tu sa vai kukkuṭakaḥ smṛtaḥ || 10.18 ||
vaiśyād iti | jāto niṣādāc … kukkuṭakaḥ smṛtaḥ | puruṣajāteḥ pratilomatvān na niṣādo ‘tra pāraśavaḥ, pituḥ pratilome hi yāthā vyāsaḥ |
ugrā māgadhasaṃyogāj jātaḥ kuntalakābhidhaḥ |
sa nāpita iti proktaḥ kṣurakarmavidhānakṛt ||
kṣatriyāj jāyate vaiśyān māgadho vākyajīvanaḥ |
śūdrān niṣādo matsyaghnaḥ kṣatriyāyāṃ vyatikramāt ||
ity evaṃ ya etasmān niṣādāc chūdrāyāṃ jāto jātitaḥ pulkasaḥ sākṣād bhavati | niṣādyāṃ punar evaṃvidhāyāṃ eva yaḥ śūdrāj jātaḥ sa kukkuṭanāmā smṛtaḥ, tasya ca kukkuṭakānāṃ praharakaraṇaṃ cakratomarabhiṇḍimālaprāsāsiparaśulaguḍalohajālikāsattvaghnāti karmāṇīti śaṅkhena vṛttir uktāh || 10.17–18 ||
kṣattur jātas tathogrāyāṃ śvapāka iti kīrtyate |
vaidehakena tva ambaṣṭhyām utpanno veṇa ucyate || 10.19 ||
kṣattur iti | kṣattuḥ śūdrākṣatriyajātāyām utpannaḥ śvapāka ity ucyate | vaidehakena vaiśyato brāhmaṇajātena ambaṣṭyāṃ brāhmaṇena vaiśyājātāyām utpanno vena iti kathyate || 10.19 ||
dvijātayaḥ savarṇāsu janayanty avratāṃs tu yān |
tān sāvitrīparibhraṣṭān vrātyān iti vinirdiśet || 10.20 ||
idānīṃ vrātyajātānām apratilomānām api satāṃ saṃskāratvapratipādanārtham ata ūrdhvaṃ ‘trayo ‘py ete’ ityādy uktaṃ vrātyalakṣaṇam anuvaditum āha dvijātaya iti | dvijātistrīṣu yān putrān utpadayati te cet upanayanākhyavratahīnā bhavanti, tadā tān akṛtopanayanān vrātyā ity anyayā saṃjñayā vyapadiśet || 10.20 ||
vrātyāt tu jāyate viprāt pāpātmā bhūrjakaṇṭakaḥ |
āvantyavāṭadhānau ca puṣpadhaḥ śaikha eva ca || 10.21 ||
vrātyāt tu jāyate viprāt pāpātmā bhūrjakaṇṭakaḥ iti | uktalakṣaṇāt vrātyāt savarṇāsu iti punarāvṛtteḥ brāhmaṇyāṃ pāpavatotpannātvāt duṣṭaśarīraḥ bhṛjjakaṇṭakākhyo jāyate | tathā vāṭadhānapuṣpadhaśaikhākhyā jāyanta iti ekasyaiva deśabhedaprasiddhaitāni nāmāni | evam uttaratrāpi gautamena brāhmaṇād vaiśyāyāṃ jāto bhṛjjakaṇṭaka uktaḥ | sa ca anulomatvād apāpmeti viśeṣaṇam | eṣāṃ ca vṛttayo ‘tha auśanasa uktaḥ: “abhicāramulakarma dviṣatāṃ mantrauṣadhīś ca abhimukhīkaraṇaṃ nāśanaṃ vā bhṛjjakaṇṭakānām āvantyavāṭadhānaiḥ svasainyānāṃ vijitvā caryā vrātyājair anyaiḥ pararāṣṭrāṇāṃ kośamantravṛttajñānaṃ mitrāmitratā ca jñeyeti || 10.21 ||
jhallo mallaś ca rājanyād vrātyān nicchivir eva ca |
naṭaś ca karaṇaś caiva khaso draviḍa eva ca || 10.22 ||
jhallo mallaś ceti | kṣatriyād vrātyāyāṃ savarṇāyāṃ jhallamallanicchavinaṭakaraṇakhasadvraviḍākhyā jāyante |  ity eṣāṃ ca vṛttayaḥ atha uśanasa uktaḥ, cāravṛttitvān naṭakaraṇānām udakāharaṇaṃ prapāveśmadānaṃ ca khasadraviḍānāṃ ca khasadraviḍānāṃ vrātyajaiḥ anyaiḥ ityādipūrvaśloke darśitam || 10.22 ||
vaiśyāt tu jāyate vrātyāt sudhanvācārya eva ca |
kāruṣaś ca vijanmā ca maitraḥ sātvata eva ca || 10.23 ||
vaiśyād iti | vaiśyāt punaḥ vrātyāt sudhanvācāryakāruṣavijanmamaitrasātvatākhyā jāyante | vṛttayaś ca eṣām api auśanasa uktāḥ ācāryasātvataiḥ devapūjanaṃ śiṣṭānāṃ vrātyajair anyair ity ādidarśanam || 10.23 ||
vyabhicāreṇa varṇānām avedāvedanena ca |
svakarmaṇāṃ ca tyāgena jāyante varṇasaṃkarāḥ || 10.24 ||
vyabhicāreṇeti | brāhmaṇādīnāṃ varṇānām itaretarastrīgamane svagotrādyavivāhyāvivāhena upanayanākhyasvakarmatyāgena varṇasaṃkaro nāma ity utpadyate | ato yuktam ity asmin prakaraṇe vrātyajātānām abhidhānam || 10.24 ||
saṃkīrṇayonayo ye tu pratilomānulomajāḥ |
anyonyavyatiṣaktāś ca tān pravakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ || 10.25 ||
saṃkīrṇayonaya iti | ye miśrayonayaḥ pratilomābhyaḥ strībhyaḥ pratilomair anulomaiś ca jāyante anulomābhyaś cānulomaiḥ prailomaiś ca jāyante | evaṃ parasparasaṃbaddhān tān aśeṣeṇa pravakṣyāmi || 10.25 ||
sūto vaidehakaś caiva caṇḍālaś ca narādhamaḥ |
māgadhaḥ kṣattṛjātiś ca tathāyogava eva ca || 10.26 ||
sūta iti | sūtādaya uktalakṣaṇā uttarārtham anūdyam || 10.26 ||
ete ṣaṭ sadṛśān varṇāñ janayanti svayoniṣu |
mātṛjātyāṃ prasūyante pravarāsu ca yoniṣu || 10.27 ||
eta iti | ete ṣaṭ sūtādaya pratilomāḥ svajātīyāsu strīṣu sūtān sūtāyāṃ sūtam eva janayati ity evaṃ samānajātīyān varṇaprabhavatvād varṇān janayanti | anulomāḥ punaḥ mātṛjātīyāyāṃ svajātīyāyāṃ ca sadṛśaṃ janayanti | tad yathā – ambaṣṭho vaiśyāyām ambaṣṭhāyāṃ vā amabaṣṭham eva janayati | saṃkīrṇayonayo ye tu pratilomānulomajā ity anulomānām api prastutatvāt pratilomebhyaś ca mātṛjātīyāyāṃ sadṛśajananāsaṃbhavāt dvitīyārdhaśloko ‘nulomaviṣamatayā vyākhyāyate || 10.27 ||
yathā trayāṇāṃ varṇānāṃ dvayor ātmāsya jāyate |
ānantaryāt svayonyāṃ tu tathā bāhyeṣv api kramāt || 10.28 ||
yatheti | ānantaryāt svayonyāṃ ca tathā bāhyeṣv api kramaḥ | yathā trayāṇāṃ varṇānāṃ kṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrāṇāṃ madhyāt dvayor varṇayoḥ kṣatriyavaiśyayor gamane brāhmaṇasya ānulomyād dvija utpadyate svajātīyāyāṃ ca dvijo jāyate | evaṃ ca bāhyeṣv api vaiśyakṣatriyābhyāṃ kṣatriyābrāhmaṇyor jāte vyutkramo bhavatiti | śūdrajātipratilomāpekṣayā dvijātyutpannapratilomastutiḥ | yat tu ‘dvijatvapratipādakam etad eṣāṃ vacanaṃ upanayanārtham’ ity āhuḥ – tad asat, ‘pratilomās tu dharmahīnāḥ” iti gautamīye pratilomānāṃ saṃskāraniṣedhāt || 10.28 ||
te cāpi bāhyān subahūṃs tato ‘py adhikadūṣitān |
parasparasya dāreṣu janayanti vigarhitān || 10.29 ||
ta iti | te ‘py āyogavādayaḥ ṣaṭ parasparajātīyāsu bhāryāsu pratilomān suṣṭhu bahūn ātmano ‘dhikaduṣṭān ninditān janayanti | tat tathāyogavaḥ kṣatryām ātmano hīnataraṃ janayati, kṣattā āyogavyāṃ hīnataram utpādayati, ity evam anyeṣv api pratilomeṣu draṣṭavyam || 10.29 ||
yathaiva śūdro brāhmaṇyām bāhyaṃ jantuṃ prasūyate |
tathā bāhyataraṃ bāhyaś cāturvarṇye prasūyate || 10.30 ||
yatheti | tathā bāhyataraṃ bāhyād bāhyo varṇaḥ prasūyate | yathā śūdro brāhmaṇyām nikṛṣṭaṃ cāṇḍālākhyaṃ prāṇinaṃ janayati; evaṃ bāhyāt cāṇḍālādeḥ brāhmaṇādiṣu caturṣv api varṇeṣu cāṇḍālādibhyo nikṛṣṭataraṃ jāyate || 10.30 ||
pratikūlaṃ vartamānā bāhyā bāhyatarān punaḥ |
hīnā hīnān prasūyante varṇān pañcadaśaiva tu || 10.31 ||
etad eva prapañcayati pratikūlam iti | cāturvarṇyabāhyāḥ caṇḍālāḥ kṣattāyogavāḥ śūdraprabhāvāḥ trayaḥ cāturvarṇye gaccnata ātmano hīnatarān parasparāpakṣayotkṛṣṭān ātmano saha pañcadaśavarṇaprabhavatvād varṇān saṃpādayanti | tad yathā – caṇḍālaḥ śūdrāyām ātmano hīnataraṃ vaiśyākṣatriyābrāhmaṇījātebhyaḥ utkṛṣṭaṃ janayati | tato ‘py apasadaṃ kṣatriyāyāṃ cāturvarṇye ca caturaś caturo janayete | ity ete śūdraprasavebhyaḥ caṇḍālakṣatrāyogavebhyaḥ [a. 2] dvādaśaprabhedā utpadyante | ātmanā ca cāṇḍālakṣatrāyogavās trayaḥ ity evaṃ śūdraprabhavāḥ pañcadaśa [a. 4] kṣatriyabrāhmaṇaprabhavāś ca pañcadaś bhavanti | evaṃ ṣaṣṭiḥ cāturvarṇyena saha catuḥṣaṣṭispaṣṭaprabhedā bhavanti | te tu parasparasya gamanena anantān janayanti || 10.31 ||
prasādhanopacārajñam adāsaṃ dāsyajīvanam |
sairindhraṃ vāgurāvṛttiṃ sūte dasyur ayogave || 10.32 ||
prasādhanopacārajñam adāsaṃ dāsyajīvinam iti | keśaracanādiprasādhanāṅga-saṃvāhanādyupacārakṣamadāsasamaśūdratvāt prasādhanādidāsakarmajīvanaṃ pāśabandhanena ca mṛgādivadhajīvanaṃ sairandhyākhyaṃ mukhabāhūrupajjānām iti vakṣyamāṇo dasyur āyogavyāṃ śūdrājātāyāṃ janayati | etasya mṛgādihiṃsanam āryāṇāṃ devapitrauṣadhārtham ātmaputradārātyayaprāptaḥ syāt | evam anyeṣām api prāyo vadhajīvināṃ veditavyam || 10.32 ||
maitreyakaṃ tu vaideho mādhūkaṃ saṃprasūyate |
nṝn praśaṃsaty ajasraṃ yo ghaṇṭātāḍo ‘ruṇodaye || 10.33 ||
maitreyakam iti | vaideho vaiśyād brāhmaṇījātaḥ prakṛtāyām āyogavyāṃ maitreyakākhyaṃ madhurabhāṣiṇaṃ janayati yaḥ prātaḥ ghaṇṭām ātāḍya rājaprabhṛtīn anavaratavṛddhyarthaṃ stauti || 10.33 ||
niṣādo mārgavaṃ sūte dāsaṃ naukarmajīvinam |
kaivartam iti yaṃ prāhur āryāvartanivāsinaḥ || 10.34 ||
niṣādo māgadham iti | pratilomādhikārāt pratilomo niṣādo vyāsokto ya uktaḥ sa prakṛtāyām āyogavyāṃ māgadhaṃ dāsāparanāmānaṃ nauvyāpārajīvinaṃ janayati yam āryāvartanivāsināh kaivartaśabdena bruvate || 10.34 ||
mṛtavastrabhṛtsv anāryāsu garhitānnāśanāsu ca |
bhavanty āyogavīṣv ete jātihīnāḥ pṛthak trayaḥ || 10.35 ||
mṛtavastrabhṛtsv anāryāsu iti | ete sairandhramaitreyamāgadhahīnajātīyāḥ trayaḥ śavavastraparidhānāsu krūrāsu ucchiṣṭhādibhakṣyākhyāyogavīṣu pitṛbhedād bhinnajātayo bhavanti || 10.35 ||
kārāvaro niṣādāt tu carmakāraṃ prasūyate |
vaidehikād andhramedau bahirgrāmapratiśrayau || 10.36 ||
kārāvara iti | ‘vaidehyām eva jāyate’ ity asya vakṣyamāṇasyāvadhāraṇasya pūrvatrāpi vaidehyavabodhakatvād ihāśrutāpi vaidehī lakṣyate | niṣādasya pratilomādhikārāt pratiloma uktaḥ, tena niṣādād vaidehyāṃ kārāvarākhyaḥ kārāvarāṇāṃ carmacchedanasīvanam iti paurān saṃdarśanāt carmacchedanasīvanavṛttir jāyate | vaidehikād andhramedākhyau araṇyanivāsinau stutyantaranirdeśāt vaidehyāṃ ca bhinnajātyutpādaḥ syāt na ādyatvāt kārāvaraniṣādajātyoś ca śloke ‘tra saṃnidhānāt kārāvaraniṣādastriyor eva jāyate || 10.36 ||
caṇḍālāt pāṇḍusopākas tvaksāravyavahāravān |
āhiṇḍiko niṣādena vaidehyām eva jāyate || 10.37 ||
cāṇḍālād iti | cāṇḍālāt vaidehyāṃ pāṇḍusopākākhyo veṇuvyavahārajīvī jāyate | sa ca niṣādena vaidehyām eva ahituṇḍikākhyo jāyate | asya bandhanasthāneṣu bāhyasaṃrakṣaṇam āhituṇḍikānām iti auśanase vṛttir uktā || 10.37 ||
caṇḍālena tu sopāko mūlavyasanavṛttimān |
pulkasyāṃ jāyate pāpaḥ sadā sajjanagarhitaḥ || 10.38 ||
vāṇḍālena ceti | pulkasyāṃ jāyate pāpaḥ sadā sajjanagarhitaḥ | caṇḍālena pulkasyāṃ jāto niṣādāt śūdrāyām ity uktāyāṃ sopākākhyaḥ pāpātmā sarvadā sādhubhir nindito rājājñayā vadhyamāraṇavṛttir jāyate || 10.38 ||
niṣādastrī tu caṇḍālāt putram antyāvasāyinam |
śmaśānagocaraṃ sūte bāhyānām api garhitam || 10.39 ||
niṣādastrīti | pratilomaniṣādastrī caṇḍālād atyantāvasāyākhyaṃ putraṃ caṇḍālādibhyo duṣṭatamaṃ śmaśānavāsinaṃ ca janayati || 10.39 ||
saṃkare jātayas tv etāḥ pitṛmātṛpradarśitāḥ |
pracchannā vā prakāśā vā veditavyāḥ svakarmabhiḥ || 10.40 ||
saṃkara iti | varṇasaṃkaraviṣaye etā jātayo asya iyaṃ jananī ayaṃ janako ‘yam evaṃjātīya iti evaṃ pitṛmātṛkathanapūrvakaṃ pradarśitāḥ | etāś ca gūḍhāḥ prakaṭā vā tajjāticoditakarmācaraṇadarśanena boddhavyāḥ || 10.40 ||
sajātijānantarajāḥ ṣaṭ sutā dvijadharmiṇaḥ |
śūdrāṇāṃ tu sadharmāṇaḥ sarve ‘padhvaṃsajāḥ smṛtāḥ || 10.41 ||
svajātijānantarajā iti | dvijātīnāṃ samānajātīyāsu bhāryāsu jātāḥ tathā nānyotpannā brāhmaṇakṣatriyābhyāṃ kṣatriyāvaiśyayor ity ete ṣaṭ sutā dvijadharmāṇaḥ upanetavyāḥ | tān anantaranāmnas tu iti ayaṃ nāmanirdeśo vyavahārārtho na saṃskārārtham iti kasyacid bhrāntiḥ syād ity ata idam eṣām upananayārthaṃ vacanam | ye punar anye saṃkarajāḥ sūtādayaḥ te sarve tulyarūpāḥ teṣām upanayanaṃ nāsti dvijātyutpannānām api || 10.41 ||
tapobījaprabhāvais tu te gacchanti yuge yuge |
utkarṣaṃ cāpakarṣaṃ ca manuṣyeṣv iha janmataḥ || 10.42 ||
tapobījaprabhāvena te gacchanti yuge yuge iti | te svajātijā iti | anantarajāḥ tapaḥprabhāveṇa hetunā jātito gacchanti || 10.42 ||
śanakais tu kriyālopād imāḥ kṣatriyajātayaḥ |
vṛṣalatvaṃ gatā loke brāhmaṇādarśanena ca || 10.43 ||
tathā cāha śanakair iti | imā vakṣyamāṇāḥ kṣatriyajātayaḥ satya upanayanādikriyālopena brāhmaṇānāṃ ca yajanādhyāpanaprāyaścittādyarthaṃ darśnābhāvena śanaiḥ śanaiḥ loke śūdratāṃ prāptāḥ || 10.43 ||
puṇḍrakāś cauḍradraviḍāḥ kāmbojā yavanāḥ śakāḥ |
pāradā pahlavāś cīnāḥ kirātā daradāḥ khaśāḥ || 10.44 ||
pauṇḍrakā iti | pauṇḍrakādayaḥ kṣatriyāḥ santaḥ kriyālopādinā śūdratvam āpannāḥ || 10.44 ||
mukhabāhūrupajjānāṃ yā loke jātayo bahiḥ |
mlecchavācaś cāryavacaḥ sarve te dasyavaḥ smṛtāḥ || 10.45 ||
mukhabāhūrupajjānām iti | brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrāṇāṃ yā bāhyāḥ kriyālopādinā jātayo mlecchabhāṣopetā āryabhāṣāyuktā vā barbarakālādyās te sarve dasyuśabdavācyāḥ smṛtāḥ
|| 10.45 ||
ye dvijānām apasadā ye cāpadhvaṃsajāḥ smṛtāḥ |
te ninditair vartayeyur dvijānām eva karmabhiḥ || 10.46 ||
ya iti | ye dvijānāṃ prātilomyenotpannā ye ca dvijātibhir api śūdrāyām ānulomyena jātās te dvijātyupakārakair eva aninditaiḥ karmabhiḥ jīveyuḥ || 10.46 ||
sūtānām aśvasārathyam ambaṣṭhānāṃ cikitsanam |
vaidehakānāṃ strīkāryaṃ māgadhānāṃ vaṇikpathaḥ || 10.47 ||
sūtānām iti | vaidehakānāṃ strīkāryaṃ māgadhānāṃ vaṇikpathaḥ | sūtānām aśvadamanayojanādikam, ambaṣṭhānāṃ kāyaśalyādicikitsā, vaidehakānām antaḥpurarakṣādiḥ, māgadhānāṃ sthalapathavāripathavāṇijyā | ambaṣṭhaś cātra avadyāyāṃ śūdrajātau baudhāyanadarśanād vijñeyo na tu brāhmaṇena vaiśyāj jātaḥ etacchāstroktā ye dvijānām apasadā ye cāpadhvaṃsajāḥ sutāḥ te ninditaiḥ vartayeyur iti prastutatvāt || 10.47 ||
matsyaghāto niṣādānāṃ taṣṭis tv āyogavasya ca |
medāndhracuñcumadgunām āraṇyapaśuhiṃsanam || 10.48 ||
matsyaghāta iti | medāndhracuñcumadgunām āraṇyapaśuhiṃsanam | āyogavasya takṣaṇaṃ medāndhracuñcumadgūnām āraṇyapaśumāraṇam | māgadhakṣattārau cuñcumadgū | yathāha yamaḥ |
kṣatriyāyāṃ tu vaiśyena cuñcuko nāma jāyate |
śūdreṇa jāyate madguḥ kṣatriyā yatra muhyati ||
kṣatrugrapulkasānāṃ tu bilaukovadhabandhanam |
dhigvaṇānāṃ carmakāryaṃ veṇānāṃ bhāṇḍavādanam || 10.49 ||
kṣatrugrapulkasānām iti | kṣatrādīnāṃ śvāviḍgodhādivadhabandhanam ugraś cātra nikṛṣṭakarmābhidhānād ugrajātiḥ pratilomaḥ kaścid veditavyo nartako ‘nulomas tasya hi dvijātiśuśrūṣādhānādhyakṣatā ityādyauśanasoktā vṛttir darśitā | dhigaṇānāṃ carmakaraṇatadvikrayau, “carmakaraṇaṃ tadvikrayajīvanaṃ dhigvaṇānāṃ ca” iti auśanasadarśanāt | ataḥ kārāvarebhyaḥ eṣāṃ vṛttibhedau veṇānāṃ ca vaṃśam urajādivādyabhāṇḍavādanam || 10.49 ||
caityadrumaśmaśāneṣu śaileṣūpavaneṣu ca |
vaseyur ete vijñātā vartayantaḥ svakarmabhiḥ || 10.50 ||
caityadrumaśmaśāneṣu iti | grāmādisamīpe khyātavṛkṣaś caityas tanmūlaśmaśāna-parvatavanasamīpeṣu ete prakāśāḥ santaḥ svakarmabhir jīvanto vaseyuḥ || 10.50 ||
caṇḍālaśvapacānāṃ tu bahir grāmāt pratiśrayaḥ |
apapātrāś ca kartavyā dhanam eṣāṃ śvagardabham || 10.51 ||
vāsāṃsi mṛtacailāni bhinnabhāṇḍeṣu bhojanam |
kārṣṇāyasam alaṃkāraḥ parivrajyā ca nityaśaḥ || 10.52 ||
caṇḍālaśvapacānāṃ iti | vāsāṃsīti | caṇḍālaśvapākānāṃ grāmād bahir nivāsaḥ syāt | pātrabahiṣkṛtāś ca kartavyāḥ yataḥ tad uktam, lohādipātraṃ saṃskāreṇāpi na śudhyati | dhanaṃ caiṣāṃ śvakharaṃ nānyat, vāsāṃsi ca śavavastrāṇi | khaṇḍaśarāvādiṣu ca bhojanam | āyasaṃ ca alaṃkāraṇam | sarvadā naikatrāvasthānam || 10.51–52 ||
na taiḥ samayam anvicchat puruṣo dharmam ācaran |
vyavahāro mithas teṣāṃ vivāhaḥ sadṛśaiḥ saha || 10.53 ||
na tair iti | dhārmikaḥ puruṣaḥ caṇḍālaśvapacaiḥ sahalokayātrādivyavahārān na kuryāt | teṣāṃ ca dānagrahaṇādivyavahāro vivāhaś ca samānajātīyaiḥ saha parasparaṃ syāt || 10.53 ||
annam eṣāṃ parādhīnaṃ deyaṃ syād bhinnabhājane |
rātrau na vicareyus te grāmeṣu nagareṣu ca || 10.54 ||
annam iti | annaṃ caiṣāṃ parāyattaṃ na tu svataḥ sādhitam | tac ca bhinnapātre eṣāṃ dātavyam | te ca rātrau grāmanagarayor na paryaṭeyuḥ || 10.54 ||
divā careyuḥ kāryārthaṃ cihnitā rājaśāsanaiḥ |
abāndhavaṃ śavaṃ caiva nirhareyur iti sthitiḥ || 10.55 ||
diveti | divā grāmādau rājādiṣṭakṣūrādicihnāṅkitāḥ santaḥ saprayojanaṃ paryaṭeyuḥ | anāthaṃ śavaṃ grāmān nirhareyur iti sādhumaryādā || 10.55 ||
vadhyāṃś ca hanyuḥ satataṃ yathāśāstraṃ nṛpājñayā |
vadhyavāsāṃsi gṛhṇīyuḥ śayyāś cābharaṇāni ca || 10.56 ||
vadhyān iti | rājājñayā ca śāstrānatikrameṇa śuddhavadhaśūlāropaṇādinā sarvadā vadhārhān hanyuḥ tadvastraśayyālaṃkaraṇāni ca gṛhṇīyuḥ || 10.56 ||
varṇāpetam avijñātaṃ naraṃ kaluṣayonijam |
āryarūpam ivānāryaṃ karmabhiḥ svair vibhāvayet || 10.57 ||
varṇāpetam iti | varṇatvād bhraṣṭaṃ manuṣyaṃ saṃkarajātaṃ lokataḥ tathāvidhena ajñātam ata eva cāryasadṛśaṃ vastutaḥ punar anāryaṃ kaluṣayonijam anurūpābhiḥ ceṣṭābhiḥ niścinuyāt, ataś ca niścitya pariharet || 10.57 ||
anāryatā niṣṭhuratā krūratā niṣkriyātmatā |
puruṣaṃ vyañjayantīha loke kaluṣayonijam || 10.58 ||
anāryateti | nairghṛṇyaparuṣabhāṣitvahiṃsratvavihitapratiṣiddhānuṣṭhānaśīlatvāni saṃkarajātaṃ puruṣam asmin loke prakaṭīkurvanti || 10.58 ||
pitryaṃ vā bhajate śīlaṃ mātur vobhayam eva vā |
na kathaṃcana duryoniḥ prakṛtiṃ svāṃ niyacchati || 10.59 ||
yasmāt pitryam iti | tajjanakayoḥ duṣṭasvabhāvatvena akāryakārtivād asau duṣṭayoniḥ pitṛsaṃbandhi duṣṭabhāvatvaṃ sevate mātṛsaṃbandhobhayagataṃ vā, atho na kadācid asau ātmakāraṇaṃ gopāyituṃ śaknoti || 10.59 ||
kula mukhye ‘pi jātasya yasya syād yonisaṃkaraḥ |
sa śrayaty eva tac chīlaṃ naro ‘lpam api vā bahu || 10.60 ||
kula iti | mahākulotpannasyāpi yasya yonisaṃkaraḥ pracchanno bhavati sa janaḥ svapitṛgataṃ svabhāvaṃ īṣad vā bāhulyena sevata eva || 10.60 ||
yatra tv ete paridhvaṃsā jāyante varṇadūṣakāḥ |
rāṣṭriyaiḥ saha tadrāṣṭraṃ kṣipram eva vinaśyati || 10.61 ||
yatreti | yasmin rāṣṭre ete varṇānāṃ dūṣakāḥ saṃkarajā vinipātā jāyante tad rāṣṭraṃ rāṣṭravartijanaiḥ saha śīghram eva nāśam eti | tasmād rājñā varṇasaṃkaro rakṣaṇīyaḥ || 10.61 ||
brāhmaṇārthe gavārthe vā dehatyāgo ‘nupaskṛtaḥ |
strībālābhyupapattau ca bāhyānāṃ siddhikāraṇam || 10.62 ||
brāhmaṇārtha iti | strībālābhyupapattau ca bāhyānāṃ siddhikāraṇam | gobrāhmaṇastrībālaparitrāṇārthaṃ dṛṣṭaprayojanānapekṣaḥ prāṇatyāgaḥ pratilomārthaṃ svargaprāptikāraṇam || 10.62 ||
ahiṃsā satyam asteyaṃ śaucam indriyanigrahaḥ |
etaṃ sāmāsikaṃ dharmaṃ cāturvarṇye ‘bravīn manuḥ || 10.63 ||
ahiṃseti | śrāddhakarmātitheyaṃ ca dānam akrodham ārjavam | prajanaṃ sveṣu dāreṣu tathā caivānasūyatā || hiṃsāvarjanaṃ yathopalabdhābhidhānaṃ parasvagrahaṇaṃ mṛdvāriśuddhir indiryasaṃyama ity etaṃ sāṃkṣepikaṃ dharmaṃ cāturvarṇyānuṣtheyaṃ manur āha | prakaraṇasāmarthyāc ca cāturvarṇyagrahaṇasyāntaraprabhave darśanārthatve saty antaraprabhavaviṣayam apīdaṃ vākyam || 10.63 ||
śūdrāyāṃ brāhmaṇāj jātaḥ śreyasā cet prajāyate |
aśreyān śreyasīṃ jātiṃ gacchaty ā saptamād yugāt || 10.64 ||
idānīṃ “sarvavarṇeṣu tulyāsu” ity etallakṣaṇavyatirekeṇāpi brāhmaṇādi darśayitum āha śūdrāyām iti | śūdrāyāṃ brāhmaṇāj jātaḥ pāraśavākhyo varṇaḥ strīlakṣaṇaḥ | sā yadi strī bāhyeṇoḍhā satī prasūte duhitaraṃ ca janayati sāpy anyena brāhmaṇena ūḍhā satī duhitaram eva janayati, sāpy evam evety evaṃ sa pāraśavākhyo nikṛṣṭe sa bījaprādhānyāt saptame yuge brāhmaṇajātiṃ prāpnoti | saptamo jātaḥ brāhmaṇaḥ saṃpadyate || 10.64 ||
śūdro brāhmaṇatām eti brāhmaṇaś caiti śūdratām |
kṣatriyāj jātam evaṃ tu vidyād vaiśyāt tathaiva ca || 10.65 ||
śūdra iti | evam uktanītyā śūdro brāhmaṇatāṃ prāpnoti | yadi punaḥ brāhmaṇāc chūdrāyāṃ pumān utpadyate sa kevalaśūdrodvāhena tataḥ pumāṃsam eva janayati, so ‘pi kevalaśūdrodvāhena nānyaṃ pumāṃsam eva tadā sa brāhmaṇaḥ saptamāṃ saṃtatiṃ prāptaḥ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ bījaniṣkarṣāt kevalaśūdratāṃ prāpnoti | saptamo jātaḥ śūdraḥ saṃpadyate | evaṃ kṣatriyavaiśyābhyāṃ śūdrāj jātasyotkarṣāpakarṣau jānīyāt | kiṃ tu jātitāratamyāt kṣatriyasya pañcame janmani utkarṣāpakarṣau ca vaiśyasya tṛtīya iti vyacakṣate | evam anenaiva nyāyena brāhmaṇo vaiśyāj jātasya pañcame utkarṣāpakarṣau kṣatriyāj jātasya tṛtīyena vaiśyāj jātasy ca tṛtīya eva || 10.65 ||
anāryāyāṃ samutpanno brāhmaṇāt tu yadṛcchayā |
brāhmaṇyām apy anyāryāt tu śreyastvaṃ kveti ced bhavet || 10.66 ||
anāryāyām iti | yaḥ śūdrāyām anūḍhāyāṃ brāhmaṇād utpanno yaś ca brāhmaṇyām api śūdrāj jātas tayoḥ kasya praśastataratvam iti yadi bhavatāṃ saṃśayaḥ syād iti bhṛgur bījapradhānyenotkarṣam upanyate sati kṣetrabījakāraṇatvāj jagataḥ kṣetraprādhānyenāpy utkarṣaḥ kim iti na syād ity ṛṣīṇāṃ bhrāntim āśaṅkamānas tān āha || 10.66 ||
jāto nāryām anāryāyām āryād āryo bhaved guṇaiḥ |
jāto ‘py anāryād āryāyām anārya iti niścayaḥ || 10.67 ||
atra nirṇayam āha jāta iti | śūdrāyāṃ brāhmaṇāj jātaḥ smṛtyabhyanujñānaiḥ pākayajñādibhir anuṣṭhīyamānaiḥ praśasyo bhavati | śūdrāt punar brāhmaṇyām api jātaḥ pratilomatvena śūdradharmeṣv apy anadhikārāt na praśasya iti niścayo yato yāvad vacanam atra prādhānyam avatiṣthate nānumānataḥ || 10.67 ||
tāv ubhāv apy asaṃskāryāv iti dharmo vyavasthitaḥ |
vaiguṇyāj janmataḥ pūrva uttaraḥ pratilomataḥ || 10.68 ||
tāv iti | tau pāraśavacaṇḍālau dvāv apy anupaneyau ceti śāstramaryādā sthitā | śūdrāj jātatvena jātivaiguṇyāt pāraśavānupaneyacāṇḍālasya prātilomyena utpannatvāt || 10.68 ||
subījaṃ caiva sukṣetre jātaṃ sampadyate yathā |
tathāryāj jāta āryāyāṃ sarvaṃ saṃskāram arhati || 10.69 ||
subījam iti | yathā śobhanaṃ bījaṃ śobhane kṣetre jātaṃ samṛddhaṃ bhavati evaṃ dvijāter dvijātistriyāyām ānulomyena jātaḥ svajātijānantarajaikāntarajarūpaṃ sarvaṃ śrautaṃ smārtaṃ saṃskāram arhati na pāraśavacaṇḍālam iti pūrvoktadārḍhyārtham etat || 10.69 ||
bījam eke praśaṃsanti kṣetram anye manīṣiṇaḥ |
bījakṣetre tathaivānye tatreyaṃ tu vyavasthitiḥ || 10.70 ||
darśanāntarāṇy apāsyaivārthasyopodvahanārtham āha bījam iti | kecid bījaṃ stuvanti, yakṣādyutpannānāṃ ṛṣyaśṛṅgādīnām ṛṣitvadarśanād apare punaḥ sūrayaḥ kṣetraṃ stuvanti, samṛddhidarśanāt tad etasyāāṃ punar vipratipannatvād iyaṃ vakṣyamāṇā vyavasthā vijñeyā
|| 10.70 ||
akṣetre bījam utsṛṣṭam antaraiva vinaśyati |
abījakam api kṣetraṃ kevalaṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ bhavet || 10.71 ||
akṣetra iti | ūṣarapradeśe bījam uptam adatvaiva phalaṃ vinaśyati | śobhanam api na kṣetraṃ bījarahitaṃ śuddhasthaṇḍilam eva syāt sasyānutpādaḥ | tasmād ubhayaṃ pradhānam iti nyāyyam
|| 10.71 ||
yasmād bījaprabhāveṇa tiryagjā ṛṣayo ‘bhavan |
pūjitāś ca praśastāś ca tasmād bījaṃ praśasyate || 10.72 ||
bījaprādhānyapakṣe dṛṣṭāntam āha tasyād iti | yasmād bījamāhatmyena tiryagjātimātṛjā api ṛṣyaśṛṅgādayaḥ saṃpannāḥ pūjiyāś ca pādavandanādibhiḥ pūjābhiḥ praśastāś ca bālāḥ saṃstutāḥ | tasmād bījaṃ kaiścit stūyata iti bījaprādhānyaṃ punar gamayatvena jñāpayati tad bījayonyor madhyād bījotkṛṣṭajātiḥ praśasyata iti || 10.72 ||
anāryam āryakarmāṇam āryaṃ cānāryakamiṇam |
saṃpradhāryābravīd dhātā na samau nāsamāv iti || 10.73 ||
anāryam iti | śūdraṃ dvijātikarmakāriṇaṃ dvijātiṃ ca śūdrakarmakāriṇaṃ prajāpatir vicārya na samau tau nāpy asamāv ity āha | yataḥ śūdro dvijātikarmāṇi na dvijātisamaḥ, tasya dvijatikarmādhkāre sati tata utkarṣābhāvāt niṣiddhācaraṇena ca pratyavāyāt dvijātir api śūdrakarmāṇi ācaran na śūdrasamo niṣiddhasevane jāte ‘pi mahābhāgyān nāpy asamo ‘pi niṣiddhāraṇenobhayoḥ pātitvāt | tasmād yasya vihitaṃ tat tena kartavyam iti saṃkaraparyantavarṇopadeśaḥ || 10.73 ||
brāhmaṇā brahmayonisthā ye svakarmaṇy avasthitāḥ |
te samyag upajīveyuḥ ṣaṭkarmāṇi yathākramam || 10.74 ||
idānīm āpaddharmavivakṣayedam āha brāhmaṇā iti | ye brāhmaṇā brahmaprāptikāraṇa ātmajñānābhyāse vyavasthitāḥ te ṣaṭkarmāṇi samyag anutiṣṭheyuḥ | tāni ca svakarmānuṣṭhānaniratāś ca te ṣaṭkarmāṇi vakṣyamāṇa ātmajñānābhyāse vyavasthitāḥ samyag anutiṣṭheyuḥ || 10.74 ||
adhyāpanam adhyayanaṃ yajanaṃ yājanaṃ tathā |
dānaṃ pratigrahaś caiva ṣaṭ karmāṇy agrajanmanaḥ || 10.75 ||
adhyāpanam iti | adhyāpanādīni ṣaṭ karmāṇi brāhmaṇasyānuṣṭheyāni adhyayanādhyāpanasāṅgasya vedasya pāṭhaśiṣyapravacane yajanayājane yāgārtvijye te ṣaṭ karmāṇi || 10.75 ||
ṣaṇṇāṃ tu karmaṇām asya trīṇi karmāṇi jīvikā |
yājanādhyāpane caiva viśuddhāc ca pratigrahaḥ || 10.76 ||
ṣaṇṇām iti | eṣām adhyāpanādīnāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ karmaṇāṃ madhyād yājanam adhyāpanaṃ praśastānāṃ svakarmasu dvijātīnāṃ brāhmaṇo bhuñjīta | praigṛhṇīyāc ceti gautamadarśanād dhārmikāt dvijāteḥ pratigraha ity etāni trīṇi karmāṇi jīvanārthāni || 10.76 ||
trayo dharmā nivartante brāhmaṇāt kṣatriyaṃ prati |
adhyāpanaṃ yājanaṃ ca tṛtīyaś ca pratigrahaḥ || 10.77 ||
traya iti | brāhmaṇān apekṣya kṣatriyasyādhyāpanayājanapratigrahākhyāni trīṇi vṛttyarthāni karmāṇi nivartante | evaṃ ca saty adhyayanayāgadānāni bhavanti || 10.77 ||
vaiśyaṃ prati tathaivaite nivarterann iti sthitiḥ |
na tau prati hitān dharmān manur āha prajāpatiḥ || 10.78 ||
vaiśyam iti | yathaiva kṣatriyasyādhyāpanayājanapratigrahā nivartante evaṃ vaiśyasyāpīti śāstravyavasthā | yasmān manuḥ prajāpatiḥ kṣatriyavaiśyayor etāni vṛttyarthāni karmāṇi kartavyatvena smṛtavān | evaṃ ca vaiśyasyāpy adhyayanayāgadānāni bhavanti || 10.78 ||
śastrāstrabhṛttvaṃ kṣatrasya vaṇikpaśukṛṣir viśaḥ |
ājīvanārthaṃ dharmas tu dānam adhyayanaṃ yajiḥ || 10.79 ||
śastrāstrabhṛttvam iti | vṛttyarthatvena rakṣaṇārthaṃ khaḍgādyāyudhapraharaṇamantradhāraṇaṃ kṣatriyasya | vāṇijyapaśupālanakarṣaṇāni vaiśyasya jīvanārthāny adṛṣṭārthāḥ punar anayor dānādhyayanayāgā bhavanti || 10.79 ||
vedābhyāso brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyasya ca rakṣaṇam |
vārtākarmaiva vaiśyasya viśiṣṭāni svakarmasu || 10.80 ||
vedābhyāsa iti | vṛttyarthābhyāṃ sahopadeśād adhyayanasya samānatvād vedābhyāso ‘dhyāpanaṃ brāhmaṇasya | lokasaṃrakṣaṇaṃ kṣatriyasya | kṛṣivāṇijyapaśupālyaṃ vaiśyasya ity etāny eṣāṃ vṛttyarthatve ‘pi saty adṛṣṭajanakatvāt svakarmamadhye praśastāni || 10.80 ||
ajīvaṃs tu yathoktena brāhmaṇaḥ svena karmaṇā |
jīvet kṣatriyadharmeṇa sa hy asya pratyanantaraḥ || 10.81 ||
evaṃ naisargikaṃ svadharmam eṣām uktvādhunāpaddharmam āha ajīvann iti | uktarūpeṇa punar adhyāpanādinā svakarmaṇā brāhmaṇakuṭumbasthitinityakarmānuṣṭhānapūrvakam ajīvan kṣatriyakarmaṇā grāmarakṣaṇanagarādinā jīvet, yasmāt so ‘sya saṃnikṛṣṭavarṇanoditatvāt saṃnikṛṣṭā vṛttiḥ || 10.81 ||
ubhābhyām apy ajīvaṃs tu kathaṃ syād iti ced bhavet |
kṛṣigorakṣam āsthāya jīved vaiśyasya jīvikām || 10.82 ||
ubhābhyām iti | brāhmaṇakṣatriyavṛttibhyām apy ajīvan (?) brāhmaṇaḥ kena prakāreṇa varteta iti yadi saṃśayaḥ syāt tadā kṛṣigorakṣaṇe āśritya vaiśyavṛttim anutiṣṭhet | kṛṣigorakṣagrahaṇaṃ vāṇijyā vā api pradarśanārtham | tathā ca vikreyāvikreyāṇi vakṣyati | svayaṃkṛtaṃ cedaṃ brāhmaṇasyāpadi kṛṣyādy ucyate asvayaṃkṛtasyāpadi “ṛtāmṛtābhyāṃ jīved” ityādinābhyanujñānāt || 10.82 ||
vaiśyavṛttyāpi jīvaṃs tu brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo ‘pi vā |
hiṃsāprāyāṃ parādhīnāṃ kṛṣiṃ yatnena varjayet || 10.83 ||
balābalam adhunā kṛṣyādeḥ prāha vaiśyavṛttyeti | brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vā vaiśyavṛttyāpi jīvet | bhūmiṃ bhūmiśayāṃś ca hantīti vakṣyamāṇayā nītyā hiṃsābahulāṃ balīvardādyāyattāṃ kṛṣiṃ yatnataḥ pariharet | ataḥ gorakṣābhāva iyaṃ kāryeti gamyate | kṣatriyo ‘pi cety upādānāt kṣatriyasyāpi svavṛttyabhāve vaiśyavṛttir astīti gamyate || 10.83 ||
kṛṣiṃ sādhv iti manyante sā vṛttiḥ sadvigarhitā |
bhūmiṃ bhūmiśayāṃś caiva hanti kāṣṭham ayomukham || 10.84 ||
kṛṣiṃ sādhv itīti | kṛṣir yuktarūpeti kecin manyante | sā punar jīvikā sādhubhir ninditā | yasmād halakuddālādyayaḥprāntaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ bhūmibilanivāsinaś ca prāṇino hanti || 10.84 ||
idaṃ tu vṛttivaikalyāt tyajato dharmanaipuṇam |
viṭpuṇyam uddhṛtoddhāraṃ vikreyaṃ vittavardhanam || 10.85 ||
idam iti | brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyasya vā svavṛttyā ajīvato dhanābhāvād dharmaṃ prati niṣṇātatvaṃ tyajato vaiśyena yad vikretavyaṃ tadvarjitavakṣyamāṇavarjanīyaṃ dhanavṛddhikaraṃ vikretavyam || 10.85 ||
sarvān rasān apoheta kṛtānnaṃ ca tilaiḥ saha |
aśmano lavaṇaṃ caiva paśyato ye ca mānuṣāḥ || 10.86 ||
tāni varjanīyāny āhuḥ sarvān iti | sarvān khaṇḍādīn rasāṃs tathā siddhānnatilapāṣāṇapaśulavaṇamanuṣyān na krīṇīta | lavaṇasya rasaśabdenaiva grahaṇasiddhau pṛthagvacanaṃ doṣaviśeṣajñāpanārtham | etenānyasyāpi pṛthagāmnātavyākhyātam || 10.86 ||
sarvaṃ ca tāntavaṃ raktaṃ śāṇakṣaumāvikāni ca |
api cet syur araktāni phalamūle tathauṣadhīḥ || 10.87 ||
sarvam iti | sarvatantunirmitaṃ raktaṃ paṭṭabṛhatikādi varjayet kṣaumaurṇātantumayāny alohitāny api yadi bhaveyus tathāpi varjayet | tathā phalamūladravyādyauṣadhīr varjayet
|| 10.87 ||
apaḥ śastraṃ viṣaṃ māṃsaṃ somaṃ gandhāṃś ca sarvaśaḥ |
kṣīraṃ kṣaudraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ tailaṃ madhu guḍaṃ kuśān || 10.88 ||
apa iti | udakāyudhaviṣamāṃsasomakṣīradadhighṛtatailaguḍadarbhās tathā gandhavanti karpūrādīni sarvāṇi kṣaudraṃ ca mākṣikaṃ madhu ca madhūcciṣṭaṃ sikthakaṃ tathā “śastrāsavamadhūcciṣṭeti” yājñavalkyena paṭhitaṃ varjayet || 10.88 ||
āraṇyāṃś ca paśūn sarvān daṃṣṭriṇaś ca vayāṃsi ca |
na nīlīṃ ca lākṣāṃ ca sarvāṃś caikaśaphāṃs tathā || 10.89 ||
āraṇyān iti | āraṇyān paśūn hastyādīn sarvān daṃṣṭriṇaḥ siṃhādīn ekaśaphān aśvādīn tathā pakṣimadyanīlīlākṣān na vikrīṇīta || 10.89 ||
kāmam utpādya kṛṣyāṃ tu svayam eva kṛṣīvalaḥ |
vikrīṇīta tilān śuddhān dharmartham acirasthitān || 10.90 ||
kāmam iti | vikrīṇīta tilān śuddhān dharmartham acirasthitān | karṣakaḥ svayam eva karṣaṇena tilān utpādya dravyāntareṇāmiśrān utpattikāla eva dharmanimittam icchato vikrīṇīteti “kṛtānnaṃ ca tilaiḥ saha” iti pratiṣiddhānām ayaṃ pratiprasavaḥ || 10.90 ||
bhojanābhyañjanād dānād yad anyat kurute tilaiḥ |
kṛmibhūtaḥ śvaviṣṭhāyāṃ pitṛbhiḥ saha majjati || 10.91 ||
bhojanābhyañjanād iti | bhojanābhyañjanadānavyatiriktam anvayād annavikrayādi yadi tilānāṃ kurute tadā pitṛbhiḥ saha kṛmitvaṃ prāptaḥ śvapurīṣe nimajjatīti pūrvavyaktikramaphalakathanam || 10.91 ||
sadyaḥ patati māṃsena lākṣayā lavaṇena ca |
tryaheṇa śūdro bhavati brāhmaṇaḥ kṣīravikrayāt || 10.92 ||
sadya iti | māṃsalākṣālavaṇavikrayo brāhmaṇaḥ tatkṣaṇād eva patito bhavatīti doṣagauravakhyāpanārtham | etat pañcānām eva mahāpātakānāṃ pātityaniyamād etadvikraye vā paṇyānāṃ ca vikraya iti upapātakaprāyaścittasya laghutādarśanāt | etena tryahakṣīravikrayāt śūdratāṃ prāpnoti ity etad api vyākhyātam | tathā vaiśyabhāvagamanaṃ ca vakṣyamāṇam
|| 10.91 ||
itareṣāṃ tu paṇyānāṃ vikrayād iha kāmataḥ |
brāhmaṇaḥ saptarātreṇa vaiśyabhāvaṃ niyacchati || 10.93 ||
itareṣām iti | uktadoṣebhyo māṃsādibhyo ‘nyeṣāṃ pratiṣiddhānāṃ paṇyānām icchāto na tu pramādād anyadravyasaṃpṛktānāṃ saptarātravikrayeṇa brāhmaṇo vaiśyatvaṃ prāpnoti || 10.93 ||
rasā rasair nimātavyā na tv eva lavaṇaṃ rasaiḥ |
kṛtānnaṃ cākṛtānnena tilā dhānyena tatsamāḥ || 10.94 ||
rasā iti | rasā guḍādayo rasair ghṛtādibhiḥ parivartanīyāḥ | lavaṇaṃ punā rasāntareṇa na parivartanīyam | siddhānnam asiddhānnena parivartanīyam | tilā dhānyena prasthaḥ prasthenety evaṃ vinimātavyāḥ || 10.94 ||
jīved etena rājanyaḥ sarveṇāpy anayaṃ gataḥ |
na tv eva jyāyasīṃ vṛttim abhimanyeta karhicit || 10.95 ||
jīved iti | kṣatriya āpadaṃ pāpta etad rasādi sarvaṃ dravyādivikrayeṇāpi jīven na tu kadācid brāhmaṇajīvikām āśrayet || 10.95 ||
yo lobhād adhamo jātyā jīved utkṛṣṭakarmabhiḥ |
taṃ rājā nirdhanaṃ kṛtvā kṣipram eva pravāsayet || 10.96 ||
ya iti | yo nikṛṣṭajātiḥ saṃl lobhād utkṛṣṭajātinoditam akarmabhir jīvitaṃ rājā sarvasvam apahṛtya tadānīm eva deśān nirvāsayet || 10.96 ||
varaṃ svadharmo viguṇaḥ paradharmāt svadhiṣṭhitāt |
paradharmeṇa jīvan hi sadyaḥ patati jātitaḥ || 10.97 ||
varam iti | svakarma viguṇam api kartuṃ nyāyyaṃ na tu parakīyaṃ saṃpūrṇam api | yasmād aparanoditakarmaṇā jīvaṃs tatkṣaṇād eva jātitaḥ patatīti nindātiśayo varjanārtham || 10.97 ||
vaiśyo ‘jīvan svadharmeṇa śūdravṛttyāpi vartayet |
anācarann akāryāṇi nivarteta ca śaktimān || 10.98 ||
