vaiśya iti | vaiśyaḥ svavṛttyā jīvan śūdravṛttyāpi dvijaśuśrūṣayā ucchiṣṭabhojanādīni akāryāṇy akurvan jīven nistīrṇāpatkālaḥ śūdravṛttito nivarteta || 10.98 ||
aśaknuvaṃs tu śuśrūṣāṃ śūdraḥ kartuṃ dvijanmanām |
putravātātyayaṃ prāpto jīvet kārukakarmabhiḥ || 10.99 ||
aśaknuvann iti | śūdro dvijātiśuśrūṣayā ajīvan kṣudavasannātmakalatraḥ sūpakārakarmabhir jīvet || 10.99 ||
yaiḥ karmabhiḥ pracaritaiḥ śuśrūṣyante dvijātayaḥ |
tāni kārukakarmāṇi śilpāni vividhāni ca || 10.100 ||
yair iti | yaiḥ karmabhir anuṣṭhitaiḥ dvijātayaḥ parivaryante tāni kārukakarmāṇi takṣaṇādīni śilpāni ca lākṣādīni nānāprakārāṇi kuryāt || 10.100 ||
vaiśyavṛttim anātiṣṭhan brāhmaṇaḥ sve pathi sthitaḥ |
avṛttikarṣitaḥ sīdann imaṃ dharmaṃ samācaret || 10.101 ||
vaiśyavṛttim iti | brāhmaṇavṛttyabhāvapīḍito ‘vasādaṃ gacchan kṣatriyavaiśyavṛttim anutiṣṭhan svavṛttau vartamāna idaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ vṛttikarmānutiṣṭhet | ataś ca paravṛttito ‘satpratigraho brāhmaṇasya śreyāṃs tad uktam “varaṃ svadharmo viguṇaḥ” iti || 10.101 ||
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyād brāhmaṇas tv anayaṃ gataḥ |
pavitraṃ duṣyatīty etad dharmato nopapadyate || 10.102 ||
sarvata iti | brāhmaṇa āpadgataḥ ninditāninditam ebhyaḥ krameṇa pratigrahaṃ kuryād yasmāt pavitragaṅgādirathodakādi na duṣyaty etac chāstrasthityā nopapadyate | tac ca brāhmaṇa āpady asatpratigraheṇa na pratyavaiti || 10.102 ||
nādhyāpanād yājanād vā garhitād vā pratigrahāt |
doṣo bhavati viprāṇāṃ javlanāmbusamā hi te || 10.103 ||
nādhyāpanāḍ iti | brāhmaṇānām api garhitādhyāpanayājanapratigrahair adharmo na bhavati | tasmāt pavitratvenāgnyudakatulyās te || 10.103 ||
jīvitātyayam āpanno yo ‘nnam atti tatas tataḥ |
ākāśam iva paṅkena na sa pāpena lipyate || 10.104 ||
jīvitātyayam iti | yaḥ prāṇātyayaṃ prāptaḥ pratilomād api annam aśnāti so ‘ntarikṣam iva kardamena pāpena na saṃbadhyate | atra paravṛttyarthavādaḥ || 10.104 ||
ajīgartaḥ sutaṃ hantum upāsarpad bubhukṣitaḥ |
na cālipyata pāpena kṣutpratīkāram ācaran || 10.105 ||
ajīgarta iti | ajīgartākhyaṛṣiḥ bubhukṣitaḥ putraṃ śunaḥśephaṃ hantuṃ pracakrame, na ca tathā kṣutpratīkāraṃ kurvan pāpena liptavān || 10.105 ||
śvamāṃsam icchann ārto ‘ttuṃ dharmādharmavicakṣaṇaḥ |
prāṇānāṃ parirakṣārthaṃ vāmadevo na liptavān || 10.106 ||
śvamāṃsam icchann iti | vāmadevākhyaṛṣiḥ kāryākāryavit kṣudhārtaḥ prāṇaparitrāṇārthaṃ śvamāṃsaṃ bhakṣayitum icchan pāpena na liptavān || 10.106 ||
bhāradvājaḥ kṣudhārtas tu saputro vijane vane |
bahvīr gāḥ pratijagrāha vṛdhos takṣṇo mahātapāḥ || 10.107 ||
bhāradvāja iti | bahvīr gāḥ pratijagrāha vṛdhutakṣṇor mahāmanāḥ | bhāradvājākhyo munir manasvī saputro nirjane vane kṣutpīḍito bṛdhunāmnas takṣṇoḥ bahvīr gāḥ pratigṛhītavān
|| 10.107 ||
kṣudhārtaś cāttum abhyāgād viśvāmitraḥ śvajāghanīm |
caṇḍālahastād ādāya dharmādharmavicakṣaṇaḥ || 10.108 ||
kṣudārta iti | viśvāmitraḥ kāryākāryavit kṣutpiḍitaḥ caṇḍālahastād śvaśroṇīṃ pratigṛhya bhakṣayitum adhyavasitavān || 10.108 ||
pratigrahād yājanād vā yathaivādhyāpanād api |
pratigrahaḥ pratyavaraḥ pretya viprasya garhitaḥ || 10.109 ||
pratigrahād iti | “āpatkaraṇāj japahomair apaityenaḥ” iti vakṣayāṇadarśanāt garhitāyājanādhyāpanapratigrahāṇāṃ madhyāt asatpratiraho nikṛṣṭo brāḥmaṇasya paraloke narakahetuḥ ata āpadi prathamam asadyājanādhyapanayoḥ prativartitavyam | tadabhāve ‘satpratigraha ity evaṃparam etat || 10.109 ||
yājanādhyāpane nityaṃ kriyate saṃskṛtāmanām |
pratigrahas tu kriyate śūdrād apy antyajanmanaḥ || 10.110 ||
atra hetum āha yājanādhyāpana iti | yājanādhyāpane āpady anāpadi ca dvijātīnām eva kriyete | pratigrahaḥ punar nikṛṣṭajāteḥ śūdrād api kriyate, ato ‘sau garhitaḥ || 10.110 ||
japahomair apaity eno yājanādhyāpanaiḥ kṛtam |
pratigrahanimittaṃ tu tyāgena tapasaiva ca || 10.111 ||
kiṃ ca japahomair iti | ato grahaṇād asadyājanādhyāpane yadutpannaṃ pāpaṃ tat prāyaścittaprakaraṇavakṣyamāṇarūpeṇa japahomair nivartate | asatpratigrahajanitaṃ punas taddravyatyāgena “māsaṃ goṣṭhe payaḥ pītvā” ity evamādivakṣyamāṇatapasā copaśāmyati
|| 10.111 ||
śiloñcham apy ādadīta vipro ‘jīvan yatas tataḥ |
pratigrahāc chilaḥ śreyāṃs tato ‘py uñchaḥ praśasyate || 10.112 ||
śiloñcham iti | brāhmaṇaḥ svavṛttyā ajīvann upapātakibhyo ‘pi śiloñchaṃ gṛhṇīyān na tu tatsaṃbhave asatpratigraheṇa vartate | yasmād asatpratigrahāt śiloñccayanaṃ praśastam | tato ‘py ekaikadhānyakaṇoccayanam iti || 10.112 ||
sīdadbhiḥ kupyam icchadbhir dhanaṃ vā pṛthivīpatiḥ |
yācyaḥ syāt snātakair viprair aditsaṃs tyāgam arhati || 10.113 ||
sīdadbhir iti | snātakair brāhmaṇair dhanābhāvāt dharmātmakuṭumbāvasādaṃ gacchadbhir asāradravyaṃ gṛhopayogi śayanāsanāśanādi vā dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ hiraṇyādi vecchadbhiḥ pārthivaḥ kṣatriyo ‘kṣatriyo ‘pi vocchāstravartī yācitavyaḥ syāt | adadatas tasya deśe na vastavyam iti | idaṃ na rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇīyād arājanyaprasūtitaḥ | yo rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇāt lubdhasyocchāstravartinaḥ | ity akṣatriyocchāstravartino ‘rājño ‘nāpadi niṣiddhayor āpady abhyanujñānārtham || 10.113 ||
akṛtaṃ ca kṛtāt kṣetrād gaur ajāvikam eva ca |
hiraṇyaṃ dhānyam annaṃ ca pūrvaṃ pūrvam adoṣavat || 10.114 ||
akṛtam iti | kṛṣṭāt kṣetrāt pratigrahe ‘kṛṣṭam anuptaṃ tathā gochāgaiḍakahiraṇyadhānyānāṃ madhyāt pūrvaṃ pūrvam aduṣṭatamaṃ caiṣāṃ pūrvasyāsaṃbhave paraḥ paraḥ pratigrāhyaḥ ||
10.114 ||
sapta vittāgamā dharmyā dāyo lābhaḥ krayo jayaḥ |
prayogaḥ karmayogaś ca satpratigraha eva ca || 10.115 ||
sapteti | prayogaḥ sarvayogaś ceti | saptadhanāgamā dāyādayo dharmād anapetās tatra dāyo ‘nvayāgataṃ dhanam | lābho nidhiḥ pratīto vā kutaścit | krayaḥ prasiddhaḥ | ete trayaś caturṇām api varṇānāṃ dharmyāḥ | jayo yuddhena kṣatriyasya dharmyaḥ | vṛddhyarthaṃ dhanaprayogaś ca kṛṣivāṇijye ity ete vaiśyasya dharmyāḥ | satpratigraho brāhmaṇasya dharmyaḥ | ity evaṃ ca sati eṣu dharmyavacanād etad bhāve naiṣv anāpaccoditeṣu vṛttikarmasu pravartitavyaṃ tadabhāve bhāve ca vāpady uditeṣu prakṛteṣv ity evamartham ihocyate || 10.115 ||
vidyā śilpaṃ bhṛtiḥ sevā gorakṣyaṃ vipaṇiḥ kṛṣiḥ |
dhṛtir bhaikṣaṃ kusīdaṃ ca daśa jīvanahetavaḥ || 10.116 ||
vidyeti | āpatprakaraṇāj jīvanahetava iti ca nirdeśāt teṣām adhyāpyena yasyānāpadi jīvanaṃ niṣiddhaṃ tena tasyāpady abhyanujñāyate yathā brāhmaṇasya bhṛtakādhyāpakatvena | tad anenāpy abhyanujñāyate | evaṃ śikpādiṣv api vijñeyam | śilpaṃ gandhayuktyādikarmakaraṇaṃ bhṛtiḥ prekṣatvaṃ sevā citrānuvartanaṃ gorakṣā pāśupālyaṃ vipaṇir vaṇijyā kṛṣiḥ svayaṃkṛtābhidhṛtiḥ saṃtoṣaḥ tasmin hi sati svalpakenāpi jīvyate | bhakṣyaṃ snātakasyāpi, kusīdaṃ vṛddhyā dhanaprayogaḥ svayaṃkṛto ‘pi ity ebhir daśabhir āpadi jīvanam || 10.116 ||
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vāpi vṛddhiṃ naiva prayojayet |
kāmaṃ tu khalu dharmārthaṃ dadyāt pāpīyase ‘lpikām || 10.117 ||
brāhmaṇa iti | brāḥmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vā vṛddhyarthaṃ dhanam āpady api na prayuñjīta dharmaṃ tatra prasaṅge punar icchan nikṛṣṭakarmaṇo ‘lpikayā vṛddhyā prayuñjīta || 10.117 ||
caturtham ādadāno ‘pi kṣatriyo bhāgam āpadi |
prajā rakṣan paraṃ śaktyā kilbiṣāt pratimucyate || 10.118 ||
idānīṃ rājña āpaddharmān āha caturtham iti | rājā āpadi dhānyādeś caturtham api bhāgaṃ karārthaṃ gṛhṇan yathānāpadi dhānyānām aṣṭamo bhāga ityādy uktaṃ parayā śaktyā prajā rakṣann adhikakaragrahaṇapāpe na saṃbadhyate || 10.118 ||
svadharmo vijayas tasya nāhave syāt parāṅmukhaḥ |
śastreṇa vaiśyād rakṣitvā dharmyam āhārayed balim || 10.119 ||
kasmāt punar āpady api rakṣaṇam ucyate yasmāt svadharma iti | rājñaḥ śatruparājayaḥ svadharmaḥ prajārakṣaṇapravṛttasya ca yadi kutascid bhayaṃ syāt tadā na yuddhaparāṅmukhaḥ syād evaṃ ca śastreṇa vaiśyān kṣatriyo dasyubhyo rakṣitvā tebhyo dharmād anapetam āptapuruṣair balim āharet || 10.119 ||
dhānye ‘ṣṭamaṃ viśāṃ śulkaṃ viṃśaṃ kārṣāpaṇāvaram |
karmopakaraṇāḥ śūdrāḥ kāravaḥ śilpinas tathā || 10.120 ||
ko ‘sau dharma ity ata āha dhānye ‘ṣṭamam iti | kṛṣijīvināṃ vaiśyānāṃ dhānyaviṣaye upacayato ‘ṣṭamaṃ bhāgaṃ śulkam ahārayed dhiraṇyādīnāṃ kārṣāpaṇāntānāṃ viṃśatitamaṃ caturthatamam || 10.120 ||
śūdras tu vṛttim ākāṅkṣan kṣatram ārādhayed iti |
dhaninaṃ vāpy upārādhya vaiśyaṃ śūdro jijīviṣet || 10.121 ||
śūdraś ceti | śūdro brāhmaṇaśuśrūṣayā ajīvan yadi vṛttyākāṅkṣī syāt tadā kṣatriyaṃ paricaret tadabhāve dhanavantaṃ vaiśyaṃ paricarya jīvitum icchet tadabhāve ‘tyantāpady uktāni kārukarmāṇi kuryāt | evaṃ ca kṣatriyavaiśyārādhanaṃ śūdrasya vṛttyartham eva dharmārthaṃ ca || 10.121 ||
svargārtham ubhayārthaṃ vā viprān ārādhayet tu saḥ |
jātabrāhmaṇaśabdasya sā hy asya kṛtakṛtyatā || 10.122 ||
svarārtham iti | vṛttiprāptyartham atha vā svargavṛttilipsayety evam ubhayārthaṃ brāhmaṇān eva śūdraḥ paricaret | yasmād brāhmaṇāśrito ‘ym ity evam utpannavyapadeśasyātyupadeśatā tayāsau kṛtakṛtyo bhavati || 10.122 ||
viprasevaiva śūdrasya viśiṣṭaṃ karma kīrtyate |
yad ato ‘nyad dhi kurute tad bhavaty asya niṣphalam || 10.123 ||
yata evam ataḥ viprasevaiveti | brāhmaṇaśuśrūṣaiva śūdrasya viśiṣṭaṃ karma karmāntarebhyaḥ śāstra ucyate | yasmād etadvyatiriktaṃ yad asau karma kurute tad asya niṣphalaṃ bhavatīti pūrvārthavādo na tv anyanivṛttyarthaḥ pākayajñādīnāṃ vihitatvāt || 10.123 ||
prakalpyā tasya tair vṛttiḥ svakuṭumbād yathārhataḥ |
śaktiṃ cāvekṣya dākṣyaṃ ca bhṛtyānāṃ ca parigraham || 10.124 ||
prakalpyeti | tasya śuśrūṣoḥ śūdrasya śuśrūṣāsāmartham utsāhaṃ putradārādibhartavyaparimāṇatām apekṣya tair brāhmaṇaiḥ svagṛhād anurūpā jīvikā kalpanīyā
|| 10.124 ||
ucchiṣṭam annaṃ dātavyaṃ jīrṇāni vasanāni ca |
pulākāś caiva dhānyānāṃ jīrṇāś caiva paricchadaḥ || 10.125 ||
kiṃ ca ucciṣṭam iti | asya ca prakṛtasyāśritasya śūdrasya pātrasthaṃ bhuktvāvaśiṣṭaṃ brāhmaṇena deyam | evaṃ ca “na śūdrāya matiṃ dadyān nocchiṣṭam” ity anāśritaśūdraviṣayam iti vyākhyātam | tathā jīrṇavastrāsāradhānyajīrṇaśayyādīny asya deyāni || 10.125 ||
na śūdre pātakaṃ kiṃcin na ca saṃskāram arhati |
nāsyādhikāro dharme ‘sti na dharmāt pratiṣedhanam || 10.126 ||
na śūdra iti | na śūdre pātakaṃ kiṃcid bhavati laśunādybhakṣaṇabhakṣaṇenaitad uktaṃ dvijātīnāṃ bhakṣābhakṣam ity uktatvān na brāhmaṇādibhiḥ “ahiṃsā satyam asteyam” ityādisarvaviṣayatvān na coditatvān na copanayanākhyaṃ saṃskāram asāv arhati garbhāṣṭama ityādikālatraivarṇikakṛtasya coditatvān na cāsyāgnihotrādau śraute dharme ‘dhikāre ‘nyavivāhatvaṃ na ca smārtadharmāc chūdrakartṛkatvād asya nivāraṇam asti vihitatvāt tathā catvāraḥ na cec chūdraḥ patatīti niścayo na caiva saṃskāram ihārahatīti śrutiprayuktaṃ na ca dharmam aśnute na cāsya dharmāt pratiṣedhanaṃ smṛtāv iti | evaṃ cāsyātha dharmasya pretasiddhatvād ayaṃ ślokānuvāda uttarārthaḥ || 10.126 ||
dharmepsavas tu dharmajñāḥ satāṃ dharmam anuṣṭhitāḥ |
mantravarjaṃ na duṣyanti praśaṃsāṃ prāpnuvanti ca || 10.127 ||
tathā ca darśayati dharmepsava iti | ye punaḥ śūdrāḥ svadharmavedino dharmaprāptikāmās traivarṇikānāṃ saṃbandhinam aniṣiddham ācāram āśritās te ‘mantrakam annaprāśanapākayajñādi kurvāṇā na pratyavayanti na tūpanayanaṃ caturtha ekajātis tv ity uktatvāt khyātiṃ ca loke prāpnuvanti || 10.127 ||
yathā yathā hi sadvṛttam ātiṣṭhaty anasūyakaḥ |
tathā tathemaṃ cāmuṃ ca lokaṃ prāpnoty aninditaḥ || 10.128 ||
yatheti  | yasmād anindakaḥ śūdro yathāvad dvijātyācāram aniṣiddham anutiṣṭhati tathā tathā agarhitaḥ sann iha loke kīrtiṃ paraloke svargaṃ prāpnoti || 10.128 ||
śaktenāpi hi śūdreṇa na kāryo dhanasaṃcayaḥ |
śūdro hi dhanam āsādya brāhmaṇān eva bādhate || 10.129 ||
śakteneti | dhanārjanaśaktenāpi hi śūdreṇa na dhanasaṃcayaḥ kartavyaḥ | api tv āśritabrāhmaṇāya yadi niyamato dhanaṃ deyam ity evaṃparam etat | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “yaṃ cārtham āśriyeta bhartavyas tena kṣīṇo ‘pi tena cottaras tadartho ‘sya nicayaḥ syāt” iti | atrārthavādaḥ śūdro ‘rhati, tasmāc chūdro dhanaṃ prāpya śāstrānabhijñatvena dhanotsekā śuśrūṣā || 10.129 ||
ete caturṇāṃ varṇānām apadharmāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
yān samyag anutiṣṭhato vrajanti paramāṃ gatim || 10.130 ||
eta iti | caturṇāṃ varṇānām āpady anuṣṭheyā dharmāḥ ta ete kathitāḥ | yān samyag ācaranto ātmasaṃrakṣaṇe satyātmajñānādyavāptyā brahmaprāptiṃ bhajante || 10.130 ||
eṣa dharmavidhiḥ kṛtsnaś cāturvarṇyasya kīrtitaḥ |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi prāyaścittavidhiṃ śubham || 10.131 ||
eṣa iti | yac caturṇāṃ varṇānāṃ dharmānuṣṭhānaṃ tad etad akhilaṃ kathitam | adhunā ūrdhvaṃ prāyaścitavidhiṃ vakṣyāmi || 10.131 ||
iti śrībhaṭṭamādhavātmajagovindarājaviracitāyāṃ
manuṭīkāyāṃ manvāśayānusāriṇyāṃ pratilomānulomajātakanirūpaṇaṃ
nāma daśamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha ekādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
sāntānikaṃ yakṣyamāṇam adhvagaṃ sārvavedasam |
gurvarthaṃ pitṛmātrarthaṃ svādhyāyārthy upatāpinaḥ || 11.1 ||
na vai tān snātakān vidyād brāhmaṇān dharmabhikṣukān |
niḥsvebhyo deyam etebhyo dānaṃ vidyāviśeṣataḥ || 11.2 ||
yady api dharmaṃ ca varṇānāṃ prāyaścittavidhiṃ tathety anukramaṇyapekṣayātaḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi prāyaścittavidhiṃ śubham iti prāyaścittaprakaraṇam adhunā vaktavyaṃ tathāpy uccāvacadharmaprakaraṇaṃ tāvad anantaram idam etad atikrameṇa prāyaścittaṃ yathā syād ity evam āha sāntānikam iti | na veti | saṃtānaprayojanatvāt sāntāniko vivāhārtho tathā nityaṃ jyotiṣṭomādiyāgaṃ kartum icchan tac ca nivartamānaś ca kṣīṇapātheyaḥ sārvavedaḥ sarvavedakaḥ sarvasvaṃ yena viśvajiti dattaṃ gautamīya eva darśanād vidyāgurupitṛmātrarthaṃ vedagrahaṇakāle cācchādanādyarthaṃ ca bhikṣava upatāpī ca rogī cety etāvannavadharmaśāstrānuvartitvena bhikṣamāṇapravṛttān brāhmaṇān snātakān jānīyād etebhyaś ca nirdhanebhyo vidyānurūpeṇa niyamato dānaṃ kartavyam | ataś cākaraṇād adharmo dravyaviśeṣeṇa caitan niyamasaṃpādanāt taddravyaphalam iti || 11.1–2 ||
etebhyo hi dvijāgrebhyo deyam annaṃ sadakṣiṇam |
itarebhyo bahirvedi kṛtānnaṃ deyam ucyate || 11.3 ||
etebhya iti | etebhyo brāhmaṇebhyo yad deyaṃ tad bhojayitvā dātavyam etadvyatiriktebhyaḥ punaḥ siddham annaṃ yat tad anyatra deyatvenopadiśyate | dhanadāne tv aniyamaḥ || 11.3 ||
sarvaratnāni rājā tu yathārhaṃ pratipādayet |
brāhmaṇān vedaviduṣo yajñārthaṃ caiva dakṣiṇām || 11.4 ||
sarvaratnānīti | rājā punaḥ sarvāṇi maṇimuktādīni kasya yajñopayogīr api dakṣiṇā nityaṃ yajñārthaṃ yathoktebhyaḥ vidyānurūpeṇa vedavidbhyo brāhmaṇebhyo dadyāt || 11.4 ||
kṛtadāro ‘parān dārān bhikṣitvā yo ‘dhigacchati |
ratimātraṃ phalaṃ tasya dravyadātus tu santatiḥ || 11.5 ||
kṛtadāra iti | yaḥ sabhāryaḥ san dharmaprajāsaṃpanne dāre ‘nyān kurvīta anyatarāpāye tu kurvītety āpastambasmaraṇad evaṃvidhanimittam antareṇa paraṃ vivāhaṃ bhikṣitvā karoti tasya manmathamātraṃ phalaṃ dhanadātuḥ punas tadutpannāny apatyāni bhavantīti nindātiśayaḥ | naivaṃdho ‘nyo vivāhaḥ kāryo na tu tadarthaṃ bhikṣaṇīyam | nāpy evaṃvidhāya niyamato dātavyam ity evamarthaḥ || 11.5 ||
yasya traivārṣikaṃ bhaktaṃ paryāptaṃ bhṛtyavṛttaye |
adhikaṃ vāpi vidyeta sa somaṃ pātum arhati || 11.6 ||
yasyeti | yasyāvaśyakartavyabharaṇārthaṃ varṣatrayapūraṇasamarthaṃ tadadhikaṃ ca bhaktādi syāt sa kāmyasomayāgaṃ kartum arhati | nityasya punar yathā kathaṃcid avaśyakartavyatvāt nāyaṃ niṣedhaḥ || 11.6 ||
ataḥ svalpīyasi dravye yaḥ somaṃ pibati dvijaḥ |
sa pītasomapūrvo ‘pi na tasyāpnoti tat phalam || 11.7 ||
tathā ca darśayati ataḥ svalpīyasīti | traivārṣikadhanād alpatare dhane sati yaḥ somayāgaṃ karoti tasya prathamasomayāgo ‘pi nityo ‘vasaṃpanno bhavati kim uta kāmya iti | tasmād ato ‘lpadhanena kāmyayāgo na kartavyaḥ || 11.7 ||
śaktaḥ parajane dātā svajane duḥkhajīvini |
madhvāpāto viṣāsvādaḥ sa dharmapratirūpakaḥ || 11.8 ||
śakta iti | yo dhanaṃ datvā dānasamarthaḥ sajātiṣu daurgatyopeteṣu satsv anyebhyo dadāti tasya tad dānaṃ saṃpati yaśaskaratvāt madhu iva prathamabhakṣaṇayogyo ‘mutra ca narakapātādiviṣavipariṇāmatulyam ataś cāsau dharmapraticchandako na tu dhama iti tasmād tad evaṃ na kartavyam || 11.8 ||
bhṛtyānām uparodhena yat karoty aurdhvadehikam |
tad bhavaty asukhodarkaṃ jīvataś ca mṛtasya ca || 11.9 ||
bhṛtyānām iti | putradārādyavaśyabhartavyopapīḍane yat pāralaukikaṃ dānādi karoti tasya jīvato mṛtasya vāsukhaphalaṃ bhavati || 11.9 ||
yajñaś cet pratiruddhaḥ syād ekenāṅgena yajvanaḥ |
brāhmaṇasya viśeṣeṇa dhārmike sati rājani || 11.10 ||
yo vaiśyaḥ syād bahupaśur hīnakratur asomapaḥ |
kuṭumbāt tasya tad dravyam āhared yajñasiddhaye || 11.11 ||
yajña iti | ya iti | kṣatriyāder yajamānasya yadi yajñejyāyajñasaṃpattau satyām ekenāṅgena vinā na niruddhāh syāt tadā yo vaiśyo bahupaśvādidhanaḥ prāk yajñasiddhyarthaṃ cauryeṇāharet | etac ca dharmapradhāne rājani sati kāryaṃ sa hi śāstram anutiṣṭhantam upekṣate || 11.10–11 ||
āharet trīṇi vā dve vā kāmaṃ śūdrasya veśmanaḥ |
na hi śūdrasya yajñeṣu kaścid asti parigrahaḥ || 11.12 ||
āhared iti | yajñasya dvitrāṅgakavaikalye sati tāni trīṇi vāṅgāni dve cāṅge nirvikalpaḥ | śūdragrahāc cauryeṇāhared yasmāc chūdrasya yajñasaṃbandho manāg api nāsti | yajñārthatvaṃ dhanam | tathā ca yajñāya sṛṣṭāni dhanāni dhātre vismaryate | bhikṣitaśūdradhanasya pratiṣedho bhaviṣyati na yajñārthaṃ dhanaṃ śūdrād iti || 11.12 ||
yo ‘nāhitāgniḥ śatagur ayajñaś ca sahasraguḥ |
tayor api kuṭumbābhyām āhared avicārayan || 11.13 ||
ya iti | yo ‘nāhitāgnir gośataparimāṇadhanaḥ āhitāgnir api vā asomayājī gosahasraparimāṇadhanas tayor api gṛhābhyāṃ prakṛtamadgadvayaṃ trayaṃ vā kṣipram āharet | vaiśyād apaharaṇasyoktatvād brāhmaṇāc ca vakṣyamāṇatvād idaṃ kṣatriyaviṣayam || 11.13 ||
ādānanityāc cādātur ahared aprayacchataḥ |
tathā yaśo ‘sya prathate dharmaś caiva pravardhate || 11.14 ||
ādānanityād iti | pratigrahādinā ādānaṃ dhanagrahaṇaṃ nityaṃ yasyāsāv ādānanityo brāhmaṇas tasmād iṣṭāpūrtadānarahitād yajñāṅgayācñāyāṃ kṛtāyām adadan cauryeṇa hared evaṃ kṛto ‘syāpahartuḥ khyātiḥ prakāśyate dharmaś ca vṛddhim eti || 11.14 ||
tathaiva saptame bhakte bhaktāni ṣaḍ anaśnatā |
aśvastanavidhānena hartavyaṃ hīnakarmaṇaḥ || 11.15 ||
tatheti | sāyaṃprātar bhojanopadeśāt tryaham anubhukte caturthe prātaḥ saptame bhakte yāgādikarmarahitād āhnikamātraparyāptaṃ yathaiva yajñāpratirodhas tathaiva hartavyam
|| 11.15 ||
khalāt kṣetrād agārād vā yato vāpy upalabhyate |
ākhyātavyaṃ tu tat tasmai pṛcchate yadi pṛcchati || 11.16 ||
khalād iti | dhānyādi kṣodanāt kṣetrād vā yato vānyasmād deśād dhīnakarmasaṃbandhena labhyate tato hartavyaṃ yadi vāsau dhanasvāmī pṛcchati tadā tasya pṛcchataḥ tac cauryaṃ sanimittakam || 11.16 ||
brāhmaṇasvaṃ na hartavyaṃ kṣatriyeṇa kadācana |
dasyuniṣkriyayos tu svam ajīvan hartum arhati || 11.17 ||
brāhmaṇasvam iti | ukteṣv api nimitteṣu brāhmaṇadhanaṃ kṣatriyeṇa na hartavyam | arthāc ca vaiśyaśūdrābhyām api na hartavyam | pratiṣiddhakṛdvihitānanuṣṭānayoḥ punar brāhmanayor atyantāpadi kṣatriyo hartum arhati || 11.17 ||
yo ‘sādhubhyo ‘rtham ādāya sādhubhyaḥ saṃprayacchati |
sa kṛtvā plavam ātmānaṃ saṃtārayati tāv ubhau || 11.18 ||
ya iti | yo hīnakarmādibhyaḥ saṃprayacchati sa kṛtvā plavam ātmānam uktebhyo vihiteṣu nimitteṣu uktarūpaṃ yajñāṅgādi dhanaṃ hṛtvā sādhubhya ṛtvigādibhyo dadāti sa yasyāpaharati taṃ dhanasaṃrakṣaṇaduḥkhād yasyaiva dadāti taṃ daurgatyādir ity evaṃ dvāv api tau nau rūpam ātmānaṃ kṛtvā duḥkhān mocayati || 11.18 ||
yad dhanaṃ yajñaśīlānāṃ vedasvaṃ tad vidhur budhāḥ |
ayajvanāṃ tu yad vittam āsurasvaṃ tad ucyate || 11.19 ||
yasmāt dhanaṃ yad yajñaśīlānām iti | yajanasvabhāvānāṃ saṃbandhi yad dravyaṃ tad yāgādiviniyogād devasvaṃ vidvāṃso manyante | yāgaśūnyānāṃ yat punar dravyaṃ tad vikarmaviniyogād dharmābhāvād asurasaṃbandhi | atas tad apahṛtya yāgasaṃpādanena devasvaṃ kartavyam || 11.19 ||
na tasmin dhārayed daṇḍaṃ dhārmikaḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ |
kṣatriyasya hi bāliśyād brāhmaṇaḥ sīdati kṣudhā || 11.20 ||
neti | tasmin nimitteṣu cauryaṃ kuarvāṇe brāhmaṇe dharmapradhāno rājā daṇḍaṃ na kuryāt | yasmād dānamaurkhyāt brāhmaṇaḥ kṣudavasādaṃ prāpnoti || 11.20 ||
tasya bhṛtyajanaṃ jñātvā svakuṭumbān mahīpatiḥ |
śrutaśīle ca vijñāya vṛttiṃ dharmyāṃ prakalpayet || 11.21 ||
tathā cāha tasyeti | tasya brāhmaṇasyāvaśyabharaṇīyaputrādipraimāṇaṃ vijñāyānuṣṭhānabuddhau tadanurūpāṃ jīvikāṃ dharmād anapetāṃ svagṛhād rājā prakalpayet
|| 11.21 ||
kalpayitvāsya vṛttiṃ ca rakṣed enaṃ samantataḥ |
rājā hi dharmaṣaḍbhāgaṃ tasmāt prāpnoti rakṣitāt || 11.22 ||
kalpayitveti | asya ca brāhmaṇasya jīvikāṃ vidhāya śatrucaurādeḥ satataṃ cainaṃ rakṣet | yasmād brāhmaṇarakṣitād rājā tadarjitadharmaṣaḍbhāgaṃ prāpnoti || 11.22 ||
na yajñārthaṃ dhanaṃ śūdrād vipro bhikṣeta karhicit |
yajamāno hi bhikṣitvā caṇḍālaḥ pretya jāyate || 11.23 ||
na yajñārthaṃ dhanaṃ śūdrād vipro bhikṣeta dharmavid iti | dharmajño brāhmaṇo jyotiṣṭomādiyajñasaṃpattyarthe śūdrād dhanaṃ na bhikṣeta | yasmāc chūdrād dhanaṃ bhikṣitvā yo yajate sa janmāntare cāṇḍālo jāyate || 11.23 ||
yajñārtham arthaṃ bhikṣitvā yo na sarvaṃ prayacchati |
sa yāti bhāsatāṃ vipraḥ kākatāṃ vā śataṃ samāḥ || 11.24 ||
yajñārtham iti | yajñasaṃpattyarthaṃ dhanaṃ bhikṣitvā yaḥ sarvaṃ yajñe brāhmaṇebhyo na dadāti sa śataṃ varṣāṇāṃ kākatvaṃ bhāsatvaṃ prāpnoti || 11.24 ||
devasvaṃ brāhmaṇasvaṃ vā lobhenopahinasti yaḥ |
sa pāpātmā pare loke gṛdhrocchiṣṭena jīvati || 11.25 ||
devasvam iti | vṛddhavyavahāraikagocaratvāc chabdārthasaṃbandhasya yasya loke devasvaṃ brāhmaṇadhanaṃ yo lobhād apaharati sa pāpasvabhāvo janmāntare janmasaṃbandhino janmāntare gṛddhrasaṃbandhinocchiṣṭena jīvati || 11.25 ||
iṣṭiṃ vaiśvānarīṃ nityaṃ nirvaped abdaparyaye |
kḷptānāṃ paśusomānāṃ niṣkṛtyartham asaṃbhave || 11.26 ||
iṣṭim iti | saṃvatsarapravṛttau vasante śāstranoditānāṃ paśuyāgasomayāgānām asaṃbhave tadakaraṇadoṣanivarhaṇārthaṃ sarvadā vaiśvānaradevatākām iṣṭiṃ gṛhyādyuktaṃ vaiśvānaryādi nānāpadi yo ‘nuṣṭhānasvarūpaṃ dvijaḥ kuryāt | śrautaprāyaścittena sahāsya vikalpo ‘śvamedhadvādaśavārṣikayor brahmahatyādiprāyaścittayoḥ śrautasmārtayor api || 11.27 ||
āpatkalpena yo dhama kurute ‘nāpadi dvijaḥ |
sa nāpnoti phalaṃ tasya paratreti vicāritam || 11.27 ||
āpatkalpa iti | āpaccoditena vidhinā vaiśvānaryādi nānāpadi yo ‘nuṣṭhānaṃ dvijaḥ kurute tasya paraloke taṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavati iti manvādibhir vicāritam || 11.27 ||
viśvaiś ca devaiḥ sādhyaiś ca brāhmaṇaiś ca maharṣibhiḥ |
āpatsu maraṇād bhītair vidheḥ pratinidhiḥ kṛtaḥ || 11.28 ||
viśvair iti | viśvedevākhair devaiḥ sādhyaiś ca tathā mahārṣibhiḥ brāhmaṇair maraṇād bhītair āpatsu mukhyavidheḥ somāder vaiśvānaryādipratinidhir anuṣṭhito ‘to ‘sau mukhyāsaṃbhave kāryo na tu tatsaṃbhave || 11.28 ||
prabhuḥ prathamakalpasya yo ‘nukalpena vartate |
na sāṃparāyikaṃ tasya durmater vidyate phalam || 11.29 ||
prabhur iti | yo mukhyānuṣṭhānasamarthaḥ sann āpaccoditena pratinidher anuṣṭhānaṃ karoti tasya durbuddheḥ pāralaukikam abhyudayapratyavāyābhāvākhyaṃ na bhavatīty āpatkalpena yo dharma ity anenoktam apy etad upasaṃhārārthaṃ punar ucyate || 11.29 ||
na brāhmaṇo vedayeta kiṃcid rājani dharmavit |
svavīryeṇaiva tān śiṣyān mānavān apakāriṇaḥ || 11.30 ||
neti | dharmajño brāhmaṇo na kiṃcid api aparādhajātaṃ rājñaḥ kathayed api tu śaktyaiva vakṣyamāṇābhicārakaraṇenaiva tān manuṣyān aparāddhān nigṛhṇīyād iti śarīradharmas tan na vyāsāṅgahetuprakṛṣṭāparādhakaraṇe saty abhicaraṇīyābhicāro na doṣāyety evaṃ param etan na vyabhicāro vidhīyate | nāpi rājanivedanaṃ niṣidhyate | kasmāt punar evam ucyate || 11.30 ||
svavīryād rājavīryāc ca svavīryaṃ balavattaram |
tasmāt svenaiva vīryeṇa nigṛhṇīyād arīn dvijaḥ || 11.31 ||
yasmāt svavīryād iti | ysmād rājaśaktitaḥ svaśaktitaḥ prakṛṣṭā svādhīnatvāt tasmād brāhmaṇaḥ śatrūn nigṛhṇīyāt || 11.31 ||
śrutīr atharvāṅgirasīḥ kuryād ity avicārayan |
vākśastraṃ vai brāhmaṇasya tena hanyād arīn dvijaḥ || 11.32 ||
śrutīr iti | atharvāṅgirebhyaḥ proktā abhicāraśrutīr nirvikalpam anutiṣṭhed yasmād abhicāramantroccāraṇātmikā brāhmaṇasya vāg eva kāryakaraṇāc chastraṃ tena brāhmaṇaḥ śatrūn nigṛhṇīyāt || 11.32 ||
kṣatriyo bāhuvīryeṇa tared āpadam ātmanaḥ |
dhanena vaiśyaśūdrau tu japahomair dvijottamaḥ || 11.33 ||
kṣatriya iti | kṣatriyaḥ pauruṣeṇa śatruparābhavalakṣaṇam āpadam atikrāmet | vaiśyaśūdrau tu punaḥ rājñe dhanaṃ datvā vyavahāralekhanena brāhmaṇas tv abhicārātmakair homair ity uktam
|| 11.33 ||
vidhātā śāsitā vaktā maitro brāhmaṇa ucyate |
tasmai nākuśalaṃ brūyān śuṣkāṃ giram īrayet || 11.34 ||
vidhāteti | svakarmānuṣṭhātāram api riktena cauryādivyāpayitācāraḥ sarvabhūtopakārako brāhmaṇa ucyate | tasmād rājan nigṛhyatām ayam ity evam aniṣṭaṃ tasya no brūyān nāpy ākrośavācaṃ vāgdaṇḍarūpāntarasyoccārayet || 11.34 ||
na vai kanyā na yuvatir nālpavidyo na bāliśaḥ |
hotā syād agnihotrasya nārto nāsaṃskṛtas tathā || 11.35 ||
neti | kanyā taruṇā alpādhyayanamūrkhavyādhyādipīḍitānupanītāḥ śrautaṃ sāyaṃprātarhomaṃ na kuryān nāpi kārayitvayā | iti śāstraparijñānā pratinidhirūpenaiṣāṃ homaṃ kārayet tato ‘yaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ kanyādīnāṃ ca || 11.35 ||
narake hi patanty ete juhvataḥ sa ca yasya tat |
tasmād vaitānakusalo hotā syād vedapāragaḥ || 11.36 ||
narakam iti | ete kanyādayo homaṃ kurvāṇā narakaṃ gacchanti | yasya ca agnihotraṃ pratinidhirūpeṇa kurvanti so ‘pi narakaṃ vrajati | tasmāc chrautakarmapravīṇaḥ samastavedādhyāyī hotā kāryaḥ || 11.36 ||
prājāpatyam adatvāśvam agnyādeyasya dakṣiṇām |
anāhitāgnir bhavati brāhmaṇo vibhave sati || 11.37 ||
prājāpatyam iti | ādhāne prajāpatidevatākam aśvam ṛtvigbhyo dakṣiṇāṃ brāhmaṇaḥ saṃpādya datvānāhitāgnir bhavati tasmāc chrautadakṣiṇātirikto ‘śvaḥ samṛddho satyāṃ brāhmaṇenāmnāyena dātavyaḥ || 11.37 ||
puṇyāny anyāni kurvīta śraddadhāno jitendriyaḥ |
na tv alpadakṣiṇair yajñair yajeteha kathaṃcana || 11.38 ||
puṇyānīti | śraddhālur vaśīkṛtendriyo yajñavyatiriktāni puṇyāni kuryān na tu śāstroktadakṣiṇā tebhyo datvā kadācid iha yajñaṃ kuryāt parikrayārthatvād dakṣiṇāyāḥ svalpenāpi parikrayasiddhir ity āśaṅkāyāṃ idaṃ vacanam ||
indriyāṇi yaśaḥ svargam āyuḥ kīrtiṃ prajāḥ paśūn |
hanty alpadakṣiṇo yajñas tasmān nālpadhano yajet || 11.39 ||
indriyāṇīti | cakṣurādīni jīvitaś ca khyātiṃ svargāyuṣī mṛtasya ca khyātim apatyāni paśūṃś cālpadakṣiṇo yajño nāśayati | tasmād alpadakṣiṇādānena yāgaṃ na kuryāt || 11.39 ||
agnihotry apavidhyāgnīn brāhmaṇaḥ kāmakārataḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ caren māsaṃ vīrahatyāsamaṃ hi tat || 11.40 ||
agnihotry apavidhyāgnīn iti | āhitāgnir brāhmaṇa icchāto ‘gnīṃs tyaktvā māsaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt | yasmād yajamānahatyātulyam etad iti | yajñacaryāprasaṅge damaprakaraṇe ‘pi prāyaścittam ādarārthaṃ māsam ity anuvādo ‘nye tu māsam apavidhyeti saṃbandhayanti
|| 11.40 ||
ye śūdrād adhigamyārtham agnihotram upāsate |
ṛtvijas te hi śūdrāṇāṃ brahmavādiṣu garhitāḥ || 11.41 ||
ya iti | ye śūdrād yācitvā ayācitvā vārthaṃ svīkṛtya “vṛṣalāgnyupasevinām” [11.42]  iti vakṣyamāṇaliṅgād ādhānapūrvakam agnihotraṃ kurvanti te paramātmasvarūpanirūpaṇaparaviṣaye ninditāḥ śūdrāṇāṃ yājakāḥ na tu teṣāṃ tataḥ phalaṃ bhavati || 11.41 ||
teṣāṃ satatam ajñānāṃ vṛṣalāgnyupasevinām |
padā mastakam ākramya dātā durgāṇi saṃtaret || 11.42 ||
teṣām iti | teṣāṃ śūdradhanāhitāgniparicāriṇām mūrkhāṇāṃ mūrdhnipādaṃ datvā sa dātā śūdras tena dānena nityaṃ paraloke duḥkhebhya uttarati | na tu tatas teṣāṃ phalam utpadyate || 11.42 ||
akurvan vihitaṃ karma ninditaṃ ca samācaran |
prasajjann indriyārtheṣu prāyaścittīyate naraḥ || 11.43 ||
evam akhilaprakaraṇam uktvādhunā prāyaścittānām adhikāre nirūpaṇārtham āha akurvann iti | prasajjaṃs tv indriyārtheṣu prāyaścittīyate naraḥ | kartavyatvena coditaṃ saṃdhyopāsanādi karma ananutiṣṭhan pratiṣiddhaṃ ca | hiṃsādi kurvan indriyopabhogeṣu cārtheṣu rūpādiṣu viṣayeṣu pratiṣiddheṣu śaktiṃ bhāvayan naro manuṣyamātraṃ prāyaścittam arhati | nanv indriyārtheṣu sarveṣu na prasajjeta kāmataḥ iti pratiṣedhān ninditagrahaṇenaiva prasajjaṃs tv indiryārtheṣu ity etad gatārtham ity ataḥ pṛthak na vaktavyam | ucyate asya snātakavratamadhye pāṭhāt tatra na ca vratānīmāni dhārayed ity evaṃvidhamukhenopakramād vidhisaṃsparśayor yajñiyātmakatvāt pratiṣedhasaṃsparśaḥ yena pṛthagvidhānam | kiṃ cāsaty asmin brāhmaṇāṃś ca snātakavratādhikārād ataḥ prāyaścittīyatā syān netareṣām || 11.43 ||
akāmataḥ kṛte pāpe prāyaścittaṃ vidur budhāḥ |
kāmakārakṛte ‘py āhur eke śrutinidarśanāt || 11.44 ||
akāmata iti | abuddhipūrvakṛtapāpaprāyaścittārthaṃ prajāpatir āhety śrutyarthaḥ || 11.44 ||
akāmataḥ kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ vedābhyāsena śuddhyati |
kāmatas tu kṛtaṃ mohāt prāyaścittaiḥ pṛthagvidhaiḥ || 11.45 ||
akāmata iti | anicchātaḥ kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ śudhyati vedābhyāsena rāgadveṣādivyāmūḍhyā punar icchātaḥ kṛtaṃ nānāprakāraiḥ prāyaścittair iti pūrvoktadārḍhyārtham etad adhikāranirūpaṇaprakaraṇāt na tu viṣayavibhāgārthaṃ brahmahād vijñātaṃ samāhṛtyātaḥ prabhṛti prāyaścittānām upakramyamāṇatvād uttaratra cobhayobhayatrāmnānāt || 11.45 ||
prāyaścittīyatāṃ prāpya daivāt pūrvakṛtena vā |
na saṃsargaṃ vrajet sadbhiḥ prāyaścitte ‘kṛte dvijaḥ || 11.46 ||
prāyaścittīyatām iti | prāgjanmārjitaduṣkṛtakaro naraḥ śyāvadantatvādiśrutena prāyaścittārhatāṃ prāpya pūrvakṛtena na vaitaccharīrakṛtena pramādenāvāpya prāyaścitte kṛte sādhubhiḥ sahādhyayanakriyādi saṃsargaṃ na gacchet || 11.46 ||
iha duścaritaiḥ kecit kecit pūrvakṛtas tathā |
prāpnuvanti durātmāno narā rūpaviparyayam || 11.47 ||
iheti | kecid duṣṭātmāno manuṣyā iha viruddhācaraṇaiḥ kaunakhyādikaṃ rūpaviṣayaṃ prāpnuvanty anye ca prāgjanmārjitair duṣkṛtair iti || 11.47 ||
suvarṇacauraḥ kaunakhyaṃ surāpaḥ śyāvadantatām |
brahmahā kṣayarogitvaṃ dauścarmyaṃ gurutalpagaḥ || 11.48 ||
piśunaḥ pautināsikyaṃ sūcakaḥ pūtivaktratām |
dhānyacauro ‘ṅgahīnatvam ātiraikyaṃ tu miśrakaḥ || 11.49 ||
annahartāmayāvitvaṃ maukyaṃ vāgapahārakaḥ |
vastāpahārakaḥ śvaitryaṃ paṅgutām aśvahārakaḥ || 11.50 ||
evaṃ karmaviśeṣeṇa jāyante sadvigarhitāḥ |
jaḍamūkāṇdhabadhirā vikṛtākṛtayas tathā || 11.51 ||
tathā ca suvarṇacaura iti | piśuna iti | dhānyacauro ‘ṅgahīnatvam ātiraikyaṃ tu miśrakaḥ | annannahareti | evam iti | brāhmaṇaḥ suvarṇasteyaḥ kutsitanakhatvaṃ prāpnoti | niṣiddhasurāpānasurāpaḥ śyāvadantatvam | brahmahā kṣayavyādhiyuktatvam | gurubhāryāgo vikāśendriyatvam | paradoṣābhidhāyī durgandhināsikatvam | asaddoṣāropakāro durgandhamukhatvam | dhānyacauro ‘ṅgahīnatvam | dhānyāder aparadravyamiśrayitātiriktāṅgatvam annacauro ‘jarānnatvam | ananujñāto ‘dhyāyī vāgvikalatvam | vastracauraḥ śvitratvam | aśvacauraḥ pādaviaklyaṃ prāpnotīty evaṃ buddhivākcakṣuḥśrotravikalākṛtarūpāś ca sādhuvigarhitāḥ | prāgjanmārjitamāpagṛhopabhuktaduṣkṛtakarmaśeṣeṇotpdyante || 11.48–51 ||
caritavyam ato nityaṃ prāyaścittaṃ viśuddhaye |
nindyair iha lakṣaṇair yuktā jāyante ‘niṣkṛtainasaḥ || 11.52 ||
yata evaṃ caritavyam iti | yasmād anakṛtapāpā (?) amutra vicitraṃ narakaduḥkham anubhūyādharmaśeṣeṇa kunakhādikutsitacihnayuktā utpadyante tasmāt pāpanirharaṇārthāni prāyaścittāni nanu naimittikamātraparyavasāyīti bhedena bhaumāntarādhikārotpādanārthāni ca na hi karma kṣīyate | phaladānaṃ vineti darśanān na kartavyāni śāstraikagocaratvāt pāpotpattir eva nirharaṇasyāpi || 11.52 ||
brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ steyaṃ gurvaṅganāgamaḥ |
mahānti pātakāny āhuḥ saṃsargaś cāpi taiḥ saha || 11.53 ||
brahmahateti | mahānti pātakāny āhuḥ saṃyogaṃ caiva taiḥ saha | brāhmaṇavadho niṣiddhasurāpānaṃ brāhmaṇasuvarṇasteyaṃ gurubhāryāgamanaṃ etaiś ca saha saṃvatsareṇa patatīti vakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇa saṃsarga ity ete mahāpātakāṇy āhur iti saṃjñānirdeśāl lāghavārtham upapātakādiṣv api vijñeyam || 11.53 ||
anṛtaṃ ca samutkarṣe rājagāmi ca paiśunam |
guroś cālīkanirbandhaḥ samāni brahmahatyayā || 11.54 ||
anṛtam iti | jātisamutkarṣe anṛtaṃ abrāhmaṇaḥ san brāhmaṇo ‘ham iti | rājaviṣaye ca sūcakatvaṃ paramāṇaphalaprāyaṃ rājñaś cittasaṃkṣobhajananaṃ guroś ca mithyābhiśāpotpādanaṃ yathā gautamaḥ “guror anṛtābhiśaṃsanam” iti pātakasamānīty etāni brahmahatyāsamānāni
|| 11.54 ||
brahmojjhatā vedanindā kauṭasākṣyaṃ suhṛdvadhaḥ |
garhitānādyayor jagdhiḥ surāpānasamāni ṣaṭ || 11.55 ||
brahmojjhateti | brahmojjhatā adhītasya vedasyāsacchāstrāśrayeṇāpekṣaṇam | yat tu gurumātṛparityāgaḥ svādhyāyāgnyoḥ sutasya cety upapātakatvaṃ na tv aihika evānyasmin karmaṇy abhiyuktatayā vedatyāge sati veditavyaṃ  vedanindā nāstikyābhiniveśena vedakutsanam | kūṭasākṣitvaṃ yatra vyavahāre ‘rthipratyarthinor vadhārhatopaiti mitrasya cābrāhmaṇasyāpi vadho garhitasya palāṇḍvāder anadanīyasya cāmedhyāder bhakṣaṇam ity etāni ṣaṭ surāpānatulyāni || 11.55 ||
nikṣepasyāpaharaṇaṃ narāśvarajatasya ca |
bhūmivajramaṇīnāṃca rukmasteyasamaṃ smṛtam || 11.56 ||
nikṣepasyeti | niḥkṣepasya brāhmaṇasuvarṇād anyasya saṃbandhinaś ca suvarṇasyāpaharaṇaṃ tathā manuṣyāśvarūpyabhūmihīrakamaṇīnāṃ haraṇaṃ suvarṇasteyatulyam || 11.56 ||
retaḥsekaḥ svayonīṣu kumārīṣv antyajāsu ca |
sakhyuḥ putrasya ca strīṣu gurutalpasamaṃ viduḥ || 11.57 ||
retaḥsekaḥ svayōnyāsv iti | bhaginīkumārīmitrabhāryāsnuṣāsu cāṇḍālādistrīṣu ca gamanaṃ gurubhāryāgamanasamānam āhuḥ | eṣāṃ ca bhedena samīkaraṇaṃ yad yena samīkṛtaṃ tasya tadīyaprāyaścittārtham | yat tu kauṭasākṣyaṃ suhṛdvadhaḥ brahmahatyāprāyaścittātideśaṃ vakṣyati tad vikalpārtham | yaḥ punaḥ guror alīkanirbandhasya brahmahatyāsamīkṛtasya gurutalpaprāyaścittanirdeśaḥ samīkṛtānāṃ nyūnaṃ prāyaścittaṃ bhavatīti jñāpanārthaṃ yathā ca loke ‘pi “rājasamaḥ sacivaḥ” ity ukte sacivasya nyūnataiva pratīyate | atra copadiśikebhya ādeśikebhya ādeśikānāṃ nyūnatvam atideśavidhānaṃ tebhyo ‘pi samīkṛtānām || 11.57 ||
govadho ‘yājyasaṃyājyaṃ pāradāryātmavikrayāḥ |
gurumātṛpitṛtyāgaḥ svādhyāyāgnyoḥ sutasya ca || 11.58 ||
parivittitānuje ‘nūḍhe parivedanam eva ca |
tayor dānaṃ ca kanyāyās tayor eva ca yājanam || 11.59 ||
kanyāyā dūṣaṇaṃ caiva vārdhuṣyaṃ vratalopanam |
taḍāgārāmadārāṇām apatyasya ca vikrayaḥ || 11.60 ||
vrātyatā bāndhavatyāgo bhṛtyādhyāpanam eva ca |
bhṛtāc cādhyayanādānam apaṇyānāṃ ca vikrayaḥ || 11.61 ||
sarvākareṣv adhīkāro mahāyantrapravartanam |
hiṃsauṣadhīnāṃ stryājīvo ‘bhicāro mūlakarma ca || 11.62 ||
indhanārtham aśuṣkāṇāṃ drumāṇām avapātanam |
ātmārthaṃ ca kriyārambho ninditānnādanaṃ tathā || 11.63 ||
anāhitāgnitā steyam ṛṇānām anapakriyā |
asacchāstrādhigamanaṃ kauśīlavyasya ca kriyā || 11.64 ||
dhānyakupyapaśusteyaṃ madyapastrīniṣevaṇam |
strīśūdraviṭkṣatravadho nāstikyaṃ copapātakam || 11.65 ||
govadha iti | parivittiteti | kanyāyā iti | vrātyateti | sarvākareṣv iti | indhanārtham iti | anāhitāginiteti | asacchāstrādhigamanaṃ kauśīlavyasanakriyā || dhānyakupyapaśusteyaṃ madyapastrīniṣevaṇam iti | gohananaṃ jātikarmaduṣṭānāṃ yājanam | paradāragamanam ātmavikrayo mātṛpitṛgurūṇāṃ ca śuśrūṣādyakaraṇaṃ svādhyāyatyāgo vyākhyātāgneś ca smārtasya tyāgaḥ | śrautānām “agnihotry apavidhyāgnīn” ity uktatvāt sutasya saṃskārabharaṇādyupakaraṇam | kanīyasādau vivāhe tyāgāt jyeṣṭhasya yat parivittitvaṃ yathā dārāgnihotrasaṃyogam ityādy uktaṃ yatra jyeṣṭhe ‘kṛtavivāhe kanīyaso vivāhakaraṇaṃ tathā vidhāya kanyādānaṃ tayor eva ca vivāhādi homopadeśanaṃ kanyāyā maithunavarjaṃ liṅgavidāraṇaṃ retaḥseko ‘dhikāro retaḥsekādy ayoniṣu kumārīṣv ity uktam | pratiṣiddhavṛddhijīvanam | brahmacāriṇo maithunam | taḍāgodyānadārāpatyavikrayaḥ | yathākālam upanayanam | yathoktam “ata ūrdhvaṃ trayo ‘py ete yathākālam asaṃskṛtāḥ” iti | bāndhavānāṃ pitṛvyādīnām | anuvṛttipaṇapūrvakam adhyāpanaṃ paṇapūrvādhyāpakādhyayanam | avikreya (vikrayaḥ) suvarṇādyutpattisthāneṣu rājājñādhikāratvam adṛṣṭaprayojanaṃ vinā mahatām udakapravāhapratibandhahetūnāṃ setubandhanādīnāṃ pravartanam | oṣadhīnām aśuṣkānāṃ hiṃsaiva tac ca sa pratyayābhyāsahiṃsāyāṃ prāyaścittagauravākṛṣṭajānām oṣadhīnām ityādi | punar vakṣyamāṇaṃ sakṛd abuddhipūrvaṃ hiṃsāyāṃ prāyaścittalāghavoddeśena stryupajīvanam | śyenādinānabhicaraṇīyasyābhicaraṇaṃ mantrauṣadhādinā vaśīkaraṇam | pākādidṛṣṭaprayojanārtham ārdravṛkṣacchedo na tv aśāstritāgnyarthaṃ ātmārthaṃ ca kriyārambha ityādyaṃ sa kevalaṃ bhuṅkta iti pratiṣedhāt pākaviṣayakriyāmātraviṣayatvena pratiṣedhakalpanaryāṅgair evam āpadyeta | ninditānnasya palāṇḍavādeḥ sakṛd anicchayāśanaṃ buddhipūrvābhyāse punar garhitānadyayor jagdhir ity etac jātaputrādau saty adhikāre nāhitāgnitvam | steyaṃ brāhmaṇasuvarṇād anyat | ṛṇānāṃ cānapakaraṇaṃ tribhir ṛṇair iti yathoktaṃ śrutismṛtiviruddhaśikṣaṇam | naṭagāyanatvādikaraṇam | dhānyasay tāmralohitādeś ca paśūnāṃ ca cauryaṃ brāhmaṇasya pītamadyāyāḥ striya upabhogaḥ strīśūdravaiśyakṣatriyahananam adṛṣṭārthakarmābhāvabuddhir ity etat pratyekam upapātakaṃ bāndhavatyāga ity anenaiva mātrādīnāṃ grahaṇasiddhe pṛthagvacanam ity ārtham evaṃ ca pitṛvyādityāgenāvaśyam etad eva prāyaścittaṃ bhavaty api tu nyūnam āpyate na steyagrahaṇenaiva dhānyādisteyagrahaṇasiddhau pṛthag abhidhānaṃ vyākhyātam | iha yasya sāmānyarūpatayobhayatrābhidhānaṃ yathā hiṃsrauṣadhīnāṃ iti tatra prāyaścittadvayam api tu bhavati vikalpaś ca sapratyayāpratyayābhyāsāpekṣye varṇānāṃ yo yasyāpi ca viśeṣarūpotayobhayatrābhidhānam | yathā kharāśvoṣṭramṛgebhānāṃ kharaṃ hatvaikahāyanam iti | tatrāpy ayam eva nyāyo yatra tv ekasmin ekasyaikatra sāmānyato ‘bhidhānam aparatra viśeṣato yathā yājyasaṃyājyaṃ vrātyānāṃ yājanaṃ kṛtveti tad viśeṣavyatiriktaviśeṣāntarāṇi sāmānyaviśeṣayor yasya punar bhinnakartṛkavākyeṣv ekanāmābhidhānam aparatra viśeṣas tatra tat sāmānyaṃ tadviśeṣaniṣṭam eva vyākaraṇīyam | yathā brahmahatyā sutrāpānaṃ steyaṃ brāhmaṇasuvarṇādiharaṇaṃ mahāpātakam ity evam ekamūlakalpanayā lāghavaṃ syād anyathā sāmānyaviśeṣamūladravyakalpanena gauravam āpadyate || 11.59–65 ||
brāhmaṇasya rujaḥkṛtyaṃ ghrātir aghreyamadyayoḥ |
jaihmyaṃ ca maithunaṃ puṃsi jātibhraṃśakaraṃ smṛtam || 11.66 ||
brāḥmaṇasyeti | brāhmaṇasya pīḍotpādanaṃ palāṇḍvādeḥ madyasya ca ghrāṇaṃ kuṭilatvaṃ puṃsi ca mukhādau mauthunam ity evaṃ pratyekaṃ jātibhraṃśakaraṃ smṛtam || 11.66 ||
kharāśvoṣṭramṛgebhānām ajāvikavadhas tathā |
saṃkarīkaraṇaṃ jñeyaṃ mīnāhimahiṣasya ca || 11.67 ||
kharāśvoṣṭramṛgebhānām iti | kharośvoṣṭramṛgahasticchāgaiḍakamatsyasarpamahiṣāṇāṃ pratyekaṃ vadhaḥ saṃkarīkaraṇaṃ boddhavyam || 11.67 ||
ninditebhyo dhanādānaṃ vāṇijyaṃ śūdrasevanam |
apātrīkaraṇaṃ jñeyam asatyasya ca bhāṣaṇam || 11.68 ||
ninditebhya iti | apratigrāhyebhyaḥ pratigraho vāṇijyaṃ śūdrasya paricaryā avratābhidhānam ity etat pratyekam apātrīkaraṇaṃ vijñeyam || 11.68 ||
kṛmikīṭavayohatyā madyānugatabhojanam |
phalaidhaḥkusumasteyam adhairyaṃ ca malāvaham || 11.69 ||
kṛmikīṭavayohatyeti | kṛmayaḥ śūdrajantavo bhūmiśaraṇās tebhya īṣad upacitāḥ sapakṣāḥ apakṣāś ca kīṭās teṣāṃ pakṣiṇāṃ ca hananaṃ madyena ca saha yat kutascit āvaṃdasārthatayāgatsya bhojanaṃ na tu madyasaṃspṛṣṭasya apaḥ surābhājanasthā madyabhāṇḍasthitā ity asaṃspṛṣṭe ppi madyabhāṇḍasaṃsargamātreṇa sthūlaprāyaścittadarśanāt | phalakāṣṭhapuṣpāṇāṃ vā cauryaṃ svalpe ‘py apacaye ‘tyantavaktavyam ity etat pratyekaṃ malinīkaraṇīyam || 11.69 ||
etāny enāṃsi sarvāṇi yathoktāni pṛthak pṛthak |
yair yair vratair apohyante tāni samyaṅ nibodhata || 11.70 ||
etānīti | brahmahatyādīni pāpāni sarvāṇi yathoktāni yair yaiḥ prāyaścittair nirhīyante tāni vratāni yathātathyaṃ śṛṇuta || 11.70 ||
brahmahā dvādaśasmāḥ kuṭīṃ kṛtvā vane vaset |
bhaikṣāśy ātmaviśuddhyarthaṃ kṛtvā śavaśirodhvajam || 11.71 ||
brahmaheti | yo brāhmaṇaṃ hatavān sa vane tṛṇaparṇādimaṭhikāṃ kṛtvā śavasaṃbandhi ca śiraś cihnaṃ kṛtvā | saptāgārāṇy apūrvāṇi nānyasaṃkalpitāni ca | saṃviśet tāni śanakair vidhūme bhuktavajjane || ekakālaṃ cared bhaikṣyaṃ pibed iti yamasya vidhinā bhaikṣyam utātmanaḥ pāpahirharaṇārtham dvādaśavarṣāṇi vaset || 11.71 ||
lakṣyaṃ śastrabhṛtāṃ vā syād viduṣām icchayātmanaḥ |
prāsyed ātmānam agnau vā samiddhe trir avākśirāḥ || 11.72 ||
lakṣyam iti | dhanuḥ śarādyāyudhadhāriṇāṃ yudhyamānānāṃ prāyaścity ayam ity evaṃ vijānataḥ svecchayā lakṣyabhūto vā tiṣṭhed yāvan mṛto mṛtakalpo vā sa saṃgrāme ca hato lakṣyabhūtaḥ śuddhim āpnuyāt | prahārārto jīvann api viśudhyatīti yājñavalkyadarśanāt | agnau vā pradīpto ‘dhomukhas trīn vārān śarīraṃ prakṣipet tathā prāsyed yathā mriyetety āpastambasmaraṇād evaṃ kṣipet || 11.72 ||
yajeta vāśvamedhena svarjitā gosavena vā |
abhijidviśvajidbhyāṃ vā trivṛtāgniṣṭutāpi vā || 11.73 ||
yajeteti | aśvamedhākhyaṃ vā ṛtaṃ kuryād atra ca sārvabhaumasya rājña āhito ‘gnir adhikāro na tu prāyaścittārtham ādhānaṃ kāryam abhijidviśvajitau prāyaścittadvayaṃ trivṛtā trivṛtstomenāgniṣṭutā trivṛd iti na cocyante stomaḥ stotrasamūhaḥ || 11.73 ||
japan vānyatamaṃ vedaṃ yojanānāṃ śataṃ vrajet |
brahmahatyāpanodāya mitabhuṅ niyatendriyaḥ || 11.74 ||
japann iti | vedānāṃ madhyād vdāv ekaṃ vedaṃ japyaṃ alpahāraḥ saṃyatendriyaḥ brahmahatyānirharaṇāya yojanaśataṃ gacchet || 11.74 ||
sarvasvaṃ vedaviduṣe brāhmaṇāyopapādayet |
dhanaṃ hi jīvanāyālaṃ gṛhaṃ vāsaḥ paricchadam || 11.75 ||
sarvasvam iti | sarvasvaṃ vā vedajñāya brāhmaṇāya dadyād yasmād dhanaṃ jīvanāya samarthaṃ gṛhe vā gṛhopayogi | dhanadhānyādiyuktaṃ yataḥ
gṛhopabhogidhanadhānyādiyuktaṃ yataḥ sarvasvaṃ vā gṛhaṃ saparicchadaṃ dadyāt | jīvanāyālam iti vacanāj jīvanaparyāptaṃ sarvasvaṃ dadyāt nāto ‘rvāk || 11.75 ||
haviṣyabhug vānusaret pratisrotaḥ sarasvatīm |
japed vā niyatāhāras trir vai vedasya saṃhitām || 11.76 ||
haviṣyabhug iti | nīvārādihaviṣyānnabhojī vikhyātaprasravaṇād ārabhya paścimodadheḥ srotaḥ srotaḥ pratisarasvatīm āryaḥ divā parimitabhuk trīn vārān vedasaṃhitām japed evam etāni trayodaśaprāyaścittāni |
jātiśaktiguṇopekṣaṃ sakṛd buddhikṛtaṃ tathā |
anubandhādi vijñānaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vinidiśet |
iti devalasmaraṇāt kartur jātiśaktiguṇānubandhādyapekṣayā hanyamānaguṇāpekṣayā vā vyavasthitaviṣayāṇi kalpanīyāni na tu vikalpāni gurūṇām ānarthakyaprasaṅgāt | sarveṣāṃ sarvatrānadhikārā iha samānajātīya ekanimittopanipātam ekam eva mantreṇa prāyaścittam icchanti tad asat, “enasi ca laghūni gurūṇi ca” iti gautamasmaraṇāt | pūrṇe vānasyanas śūdrahatyāvrataṃ cared iti vedadarśanāt | vidheḥ prāthamikād asmād dvitīye dviguṇaṃ proktaṃ caturthe nāsti mantreṇa niṣkṛtiḥ || 11.76 ||
kṛtavāpano nivased grāmānte govraje ‘pi vā |
āśrame vṛkṣamūle vā gobrāhmaṇahite rataḥ || 11.77 ||
idānīṃ samāpte dvādaśe varṣa ity upasaṃhārād dvādaśavarṣasemaṃ guṇavidhim āha kṛtavāpana iti | lūnakeśaśmaśruromāṅgo brāhmaṇa upakārān kurvan grāmasamīpe goṣṭhapuṇyadeśe vṛkṣamūlānām anyatamasmin pūrvoktavanakuṭīṃ vaikalpiko nivaset || 11.77 ||
brāhmaṇārthe gavārthe vā samyak prāṇān parityajet |
mucyate brahmahatyāyā goptā gor brāhmaṇasya ca || 11.78 ||
gavārtha iti | prakrānte dvādaśe varṣe rājacaurāgnyudakādyākrāntabrāhmaṇaparitrāṇārthaṃ gor voddharaṇārthaṃ nirvikalpaṃ prāṇān parityajan brahmahatyāyā mucyate | brāhmaṇaṃ vā tataḥ paritrāya mṛto ‘pi mucyate || 11.78 ||
tryavaraṃ pratiroddhā vā sarvasvam avajitya vā |
viprasya tannimitte vā prāṇālābhe ‘pi mucyate || 11.79 ||
tryavaram iti | stenādibhir brāhmaṇasarvasve ‘pahriyamāṇe tadupajigīṣārthe yady atyantanyūyas tadā trīn vārān niruddhayuddhe pravartavānenāhṛta sarvo ‘pi mucyate | sarvasvaṃ vā vijitya arvāg api triḥ pravṛtte mucyate | brāhmaṇasya vā prāṇaparityāgāt prakārāntareṇāpi yadi taddhetukaḥ prāṇālābho bhavati tad api mucyate || 11.79 ||
evaṃ dṛḍhavrato nityaṃ brahmacārī samāhitaḥ |
samāpte dvādaśe varṣe brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati || 11.80 ||
evam iti | evam uktarītyā sarvadāvicalaniyamo ‘strīsaṃprayogaḥ saṃyatamanāḥ dvādaśavarṣe samāpte brahmahatyāyāḥ pāpam apanudatīty evaṃ ca brāhmaṇārthe gavārtha ityādi sarvaṃ prakāntadvādaśavārṣikasya veditavyam || 11.80 ||
śiṣṭvā vā bhūmidevānāṃ naradevasamāgame |
svam eno ‘vabhṛthasnāto hayamedhe vimucyate || 11.81 ||
śiṣṭveti | aśvamedhibrāhmaṇānām ṛtvijāṃ rājasamāgame saty ātmīyaṃ brahmahatyāpāpaṃ nivedyāvabhṛthākhyakarmāṅgabhūtavare snāto brahmahatyāpāpād vimucyate | itīdaṃ vaikalpikaprāyaścittmadhānāmnānāt tasya tatrāpi dvādaśavārṣikaśeṣabhūtaṃ tadupasaṃhārottarakālam abhidhānāt | kiṃ tarhy aśvamedhavarjitasakalaprāyaścittaśeṣatāsya nyāyyā | evaṃ copakrāntadvādaśavārṣikādi prāyaścittasyāntarāvabhṛthasnānāpanatau tenaiva śuddhiḥ || 11.81 ||
dharmasya brāhmaṇo mūlam agraṃ rājanya ucyate |
tasmāt samāgame teṣām eno vikhyāpya śudhyati || 11.82 ||
dharmasyeti | yasmād dharmasya brāhmaṇaḥ kāraṇaṃ tadupadeśe sati tasyānuṣṭhānād rājā ca tasya prāntaṃ manvādibhir ucyate | tasmin sati yasmāt tatsamāptes tasmāt brāhmaṇarājñāṃ samāgate ‘śvamedhe pāpaṃ nivedyānabhṛthasnātaḥ śudhyati || 11.82 ||
brāhmaṇaḥ saṃbhavenaiva devānām api daivatam |
pramāṇaṃ caiva lokasya brahmātraiva hi kāraṇam || 11.83 ||
brāhmaṇa iti | brāhmaṇa utpattyaiva kim ut śrutādibhir devānām api pūjyaḥ kim uta manuṣyāṇāṃ lokasya ca pratyakṣavat pramāṇaṃ yad asāv upadiśati tad anuṣṭheyaṃ yasmād brāhmaṇasya prāmāṇye veda eva kāraṇaṃ tasya vedārthopadeśatvāt || 11.83 ||
teṣāṃ vedavido brūyas trayo ‘py enaḥ suniṣkṛtim |
sā teṣāṃ pāvanāya syāt pavitraṃ viduṣāṃ hi vāk || 11.84 ||
yata evam ataḥ teṣām iti | sā teṣāṃ pāvanī yasmāt pavitraṃ viduṣāṃ hi vāk | te brāhmaṇāḥ vedajñās trayo ‘pi kim utādhikāḥ sā pāpakāriṇāṃ saṃśuddhaye syād yasmād viduṣāṃ saṃbandhinī vāk pāvayitry ataś ca prakāśaprāyaścittaniścayaviduṣāṃ parṣadgamanaṃ prāyaścittāṅgatayāvaśyaṃ kāryam | rahasyaprāyaścittaṃ punar etan nāsti rahasyatvavināśaprasaṅgāt || 11.84 ||
ato ‘nyatamam āsthāya vipraḥ samāhitaḥ |
brahmahatyākṛtaṃ pāpaṃ vyapohaty ātmavattayā || 11.85 ||
ata iti | asmāt prāyaścittaṃ maraṇād anyatamaṃ prāyaścittaṃ brāhmaṇādiḥ saṃyatamanāḥ āśritya praśastātmatayā brahmahatyākṛtaṃ pāpam apanudati || 11.85 ||
hatvā garbham avijñātam etad eva vrataṃ caret |
rājanyavaiśyau cejānāv ātreyīm eva ca striyam || 11.86 ||
hatveti | rājanyavaiśyau vejānāv ātreyīm eva ca triyam | prakṛtatvād brāhmaṇagarbhaṃ strīnapuṃsakatvenāvijñātaṃ hatvā kṣatriyavaiśyau vā yāgapravṛttau hatvā “ātreyīṃ rajasvalāṃ ṛtumatīm ātreyīm āhur atra hy eṣyad apatyaṃ bhavati” iti vasiṣṭhasmaraṇāt [VaDh 20.3536], “atrigotrāṃ vā nārīm” iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt, “sā cātreyīṃ ca brāhmaṇīm” iti śaṅkhasmaraṇād, brāhmaṇīṃ tām api cātreyīṃ hatvā vadhe upapātakaṃ yathoktaṃ strīśūdraviṛkṣatriyavadha iti | yad antraśloke kṛtvā strīsuhṛdvadha iti tat |
āhitāgner dvijāgrasya hatvā patnīm aninditām |
brahmahatyāvrataṃ kuryād ātreyīghnas tathaiva ca ||
ity aṅgiraḥsmaraṇād āhitāgnibrāhmaṇapativratābrāhmaṇībhāryāviṣayam || 11.86 ||
uktvā caivānṛtaṃ sākṣye pratirabhya guruṃ tathā |
apahṛtya ca niḥkṣepaṃ kṛtvā ca strīsuhṛdvadham || 11.87 ||
uktveti | yatra sākṣye ‘rthipratyarthino vadhārhatopaiti tatrāsatyam uktvā guroś ca mithyāśāpam utpādya nikṣepaṃ ca brāhmaṇasuvarṇam api vāpahṛtya strīvadhaṃ ca yathā vyākhyātaṃ kṛtvā mitraṃ ca brāhmaṇaṃ nihatya brahmahatyāprāyaścittaṃ kuryāt || 11.87 ||
iyaṃ viśuddhir uditā pramāpyākāmato dvijam |
kāmato brāhmaṇavadhe niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate || 11.88 ||
iyam iti | eṣā niṣkṛtir abuddhipūrvabrāhmaṇavadhe uktā | icchāto brāhmaṇavadhe prāyaścittena saṃśuddhir nopadiśyate | anye tv indro yatīn ityādyuktaṃ śrutiliṅgakāmato ‘pi brāhmaṇavadhe prāyaścittam icchantīdaṃ ca brāhmaṇavadhavarjanādarārthaṃ vyācakṣate || 11.88 ||
surāṃ pītvā dvijo mohād agnivarṇāṃ surāṃ pibet |
tayā sa kāye nirdagdhe mucyate kilibiṣāt tataḥ || 11.89 ||
surām iti | dvijātigandhātiśayavyāmūḍhatayā cecchātaḥ surāṃ paiṣṭīṃ mukhyāṃ pītvā agnisparśāṃ surāṃ pibet | tayā surayā dagdhe sati dvijātiḥ tasmāt pāpād vimucyate || 11.89 ||
gomūtram aginvarṇaṃ vā pibed udakam eva vā |
payo ghṛtaṃ vāmaraṇād gośakṛdrasam eva vā || 11.90 ||
gomūtram iti | gomūtrodakagokṣīraghṛtagomayarasānāṃ vānyatamam agnisparśaṃ maraṇaparyantaṃ pibet || 11.90 ||
kaṇān vā bhakṣayed abdaṃ piṇyākaṃ vā sakṛn niśi |
surāpānāpanuttyarthaṃ vālavāsā jaṭī dhvajī || 11.91 ||
kaṇān iti | atha vā golomādikṛtavāsā jaṭāvān surābhājanacihnas tandulamalaṃ tilamalaṃ caikavāraṃ rātrau saṃvatsaraṃ surāpānapāpanirharaṇārthaṃ bhakṣayed itīdam abuddhipūrvamukhyasurāpāne draṣṭavyaṃ na pūrvavaikalpikaṃ laghutvāt || 11.91 ||
surā vai malam annānāṃ pāpmā ca salam ucyate |
tasmād brāhmaṇarājanyau vaiśyaś ca na surāṃ pibet || 11.92 ||
sureti | yasmāt taṇḍulapiṣṭād api saṃpādyatvāt surānnamalaṃ malaśabdena ca pāpam ucyate | brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyāḥ paiṣṭīṃ surāṃ na pibeyuḥ ity anenaiva pratiṣedhe sati etadatikrame surāṃ pītvā ityādi prāyaścittaṃ surā cānnamalārthavādaḥ lokaprasiddhaś ca paiṣṭam eva mukhyā gauḍīmādhvyos tūpacārāt surāśabdaḥ || 11.92 ||
gauḍī paiṣṭī ca madhvī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā |
yathaivaikā tathā sarvā na pātavyā dvijottamaiḥ || 11.93 ||
gauḍīti | guḍena tatkāraṇenekṣurasena kṛtā ity evaṃ prakārā surā paiṣṭī pūrvavākyaniṣiddhā tadvat sarvā api surā brāhmaṇair na pātavyā | dvijottamagrahaṇād uttaraślokeṣu brāhmaṇagrahaṇāt gauḍīmādhvyoḥ surāyā surātvopacāraḥ || 11.93 ||
yakṣarakṣaḥpiśācānnaṃ madyaṃ māṃsaṃ surāsavam |
tad brāhmaṇena nāttavyaṃ devānām aśnatā haviḥ || 11.94 ||
yakṣarakṣaḥpiśācānnam iti | madyaṃ surāvyatiriktam | māṃsaṃ yad abhakṣyam | surā trividhānyāsavam ariṣṭāpavādi yakṣarakṣaḥpiśācānnam etatsaṃbandhi annam atas tad brāhmaṇena vedoddeśena havir bhakṣayatā nāśayitavyaṃ devānām aśnatā havir iti liṅgāt | puṃsa eva brāhmaṇasy madyapratiṣedho na striya ity ahus tad asat | surālaśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanakāni abhkṣyāṇi syuḥ brāhmaṇī surāpī bhavati naināṃ devāḥ patilokaṃ nayantīhaiva sā durmatiḥ kṣīṇapuṇāpsu jalodbhaveti śuktikā vā |
patilokaṃ na sā yāti brāhmaṇī yā surāṃ pibet |
ihaiva sā śunī gṛdhrī śūkarī cāpi jāyate ||
iti śaṅkhavaśiṣṭhayājñavaklyaiḥ brāhmaṇyā api niṣedhasmaraṇāt || 11.94 ||
amedhye vā paten matto vaidikaṃ vāpy udāharet |
akāryam anyat kuryād vā brāhmaṇo madamohitaḥ || 11.95 ||
amedhya iti | brāhmaṇaḥ kṣīvaḥ sann aśucau vā nipated vedavākyaṃ voccārayed brahmahatyādyakāryaṃ vā madavyāmūḍhaḥ kuryād atas tena madyaṃ na pātavyam ity aparo ‘rthavādaḥ || 11.95 ||
yasya kāyagataṃ brahma madyenāplāvyate sakṛt |
tasya vyapaiti brāhmaṇyaṃ śūdratvaṃ ca sa gacchati || 11.96 ||
yasyeti | tasya vyapaiti brāhmaṇyam avagacchati śūdratām | yasya brāhmaṇasya śarīre saṃskārarūpenāvasthito vedaḥ sakṛd api medyena pūryate tasya brāhmaṇyaṃ vyapagacchati śūdratvaṃ cāsau vrajatīty api pūrvārtha eva || 11.96 ||
eṣā vicitrābhihitā surāpānasya niṣkṛtiḥ |
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi suvarṇasteyaniṣkṛtim || 11.97 ||
eṣeti | surāpānajanitapāpasya nānāprakārā saṃśuddhir uktā tasmāt paraṃ brāhmaṇasuvarṇacauryasya saṃśuddhiṃ vakṣyāmi || 11.97 ||
suvarṇasteyakṛd vipro rājānam abhigamya tu |
svakarma khyāpayan brūyān māṃ bhavān anuśāstv iti || 11.98 ||
suvarṇasteyakṛd iti | “brāhmaṇasuvarṇahārī tu” iti yājñavalkyasmaraṇād brāhmaṇasuvarṇahārī rājānaṃ gatvā suvarṇāpahāraṃ karma prakāśīkurvan nigrahaṃ kurv iti brūyāt || 11.98 ||
gṛhītvā musalaṃ rājā sakṛd bhanyāt tu taṃ svayam |
vadhena śudhyati steno brāhmaṇas tapavaiva tu || 11.99 ||
gṛhītveti | “skandenādāya muśalam” ityādyuktatvāt tenārpitaṃ muśalāi gṛhītvā taṃ stenaṃ na jātu brāhmaṇaṃ hanyād ity uktvā taṃ brāhmaṇavarjitam ekavāraṃ rājātaṃ hanyāt sa ca steno vadhena tatpāpāt pramucyate | tapasā vakṣyamāṇena brāhmaṇo vidhyatikrāntaniṣedhena rājñā hanyate tadā so ‘pi śūdhyaty eva || 11.99 ||
tapasāpanunutsus tu suvarṇasteyajaṃ malam |
cīravāsā dvijo ‘raṇye cared brahmaṇo vratam || 11.100 ||
tapasāpanunutsus tu suvarṇasteyajaṃ malam | cīravāsā dvijo ‘raṇye cared brahmaṇo vratam | tapasā suvarṇasteyotpannaṃ pāpaṃ dvijo hartum icchan vastrakhaṇḍachanno ‘raṇyagrahaṇāt prāthamyāc ca dvādaśavārṣikaṃ brahmahaṇi yad vratam uktaṃ tat kuryāt
|| 11.100 ||
etair vratair apoheta pāpaṃ steyakṛtaṃ dvijaḥ |
gurustrīgamanīyaṃ tu vratair ebhir apānudet || 11.101 ||
etair iti | brāhmanaḥ suvarṇasteyajanitaṃ pāpam ebhir vratair dvijo nirharet | atha tapaso ‘rdhayor uktatvād etair iti bahuvacanam anumanvādyapekṣayānuktam api kalpam iti jñāpanārtham | gurubhāryāgamananimittaṃ punaḥ pāpam ebhir vakṣyamāṇaiḥ prāyaścittaṃ nirharet | guruśabdaḥ ācāryāḥ pitā ca || 11.101 ||
gurutalpy abhibhāṣyainas talpe svapyād ayomaye |
sūrmīṃ jvalantīṃ vāśliṣyen mṛtyunā sa viśudhyati || 11.102 ||
gurutalpīti | gurubhāryāgamanapāpam abhibhāṣya javaladāyase śayanaṃ śayīta | “strīprakṛtiṃ vā kārṣṇāyasīṃ kṛtām” iti saṃvartasmaraṇād ayomayīṃ jvalantīm āliṅged evaṃ maraṇenāsau śudhyati || 11.102 ||
svayaṃ vā śiśnavṛṣaṇāv utkṛtyādhāya cāñjalau |
nairṛtīṃ diśam ātiṣṭhed ā nipātād ajihmagaḥ || 11.103 ||
svayam iti | ātmanaiva vā liṅgavṛṣaṇam utkhāyāñjalau gṛhītvā spaṣṭagatiḥ sann ā śarīrapātād dakṣiṇapaścimāṃ diśaṃ gacchet || 11.103 ||
khaṭvāṅgī cīravāsā vā śmaśrulo vijane vane |
prājāpatyaṃ caret kṛcchram abdam ekaṃ samāhitaḥ || 11.104 ||
khatvāṅgīti | khaṭvaikadeśamṛdvastrakhaṇḍācchanno śmaśrulomāditaḥ saṃyatamanā nirjane vane ekaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ prājāpatyaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ kṛcchraṃ vā kuryāt || 11.104 ||
cāndrāyaṇaṃ vā trīn māsān abhyasen niyatendriyaḥ |
haviṣyeṇa yavāgvā vā gurutalpāpanuttaye || 11.105 ||
cāndrāyaṇam iti | yad vā gurudārāgamanapāpanirharaṇārthaṃ saṃyatendriyo nīvārādinā piṣṭādipayasā vā trīn māsāṃś cāndrāyaṇāny āvartayet | atrāpi jñānājñānamukhyāmukhyagurusāsvasādhvibhāryāgamanādyapkṣayaitāni prāyaścittāni kalpanīyāni | atra gurudārāṇāṃ nāparadāratvaṃ saṃbhavatīty ato na pṛthak dvitīyaparadāraprāyaścittaṃ bhavati | yatra tu nāntarīyakatvāt praveśo nāsti tatrānekanimittasamāveśād anekam api prāyaścittaṃ bhavati | yasya gurubhāryāṃ sagotrāṃ rajasvalāṃ ca samāgatānakalpām asaktām amāvāsyāyāṃ sadivān api jananam asaktaṃ yāvad vāttadasyaktaram (?) ucchiṣṭo gacchan pratinimittaṃ prāyaścittī bhavati || 11.105 ||
etair vratair apoheyur mahāpātakino malam |
upapātakinas tv ebhir nānāvidhair vrataiḥ || 11.106 ||
etair iti | ebhir uktair vrataiḥ brahmahatyādimahāpātakakāriṇaḥ pāpāni nirhareyuḥ | govadhādyupapātakakāriṇaḥ punar vakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇaibhir abhidhāsyamānair anekarūpair nirhareyuḥ || 11.106 ||
upapātakasaṃyukto goghno māsaṃ yavān pibet |
kṛtavāpo vased goṣṭhe carmaṇā tena saṃvṛtaḥ || 11.107 ||
caturthakālam aśnīyād akṣāralavaṇaṃ mitam |
gomūtreṇācaret snānaṃ dvau māsau niyatendriyaḥ || 11.108 ||
divānugacched gās tās tu tiṣṭhann ūrdhvaṃ rajaḥ pibet |
śuśrūṣitvā namaskṛtya rātrau vīrāsano vaset || 11.109 ||
tiṣṭhantīṣv anutiṣṭhet tu vrajantīṣv apy anuvrajet |
āsīnāsu tathāsīno niyato vītamatsaraḥ || 11.110 ||
āturām abhiśastāṃ vā cauravyāghrādibhir bhayaiḥ |
patitāṃ paṅkalagnāṃ vā sarvaprāṇair vimocayet || 11.111 ||
uṣṇe varṣati śīte vā mārute vāti vā bhṛśam |
na kurvītātmanas trāṇaṃ gor akṛtvā tu śaktitaḥ || 11.112 ||
ātmano yadi vānyeṣāṃ gṛhe kṣetre ‘tha vā khale |
bhakṣayantīṃ na kathayet pibantaṃ caiva vatsakam || 11.113 ||
anena vidhinā yas tu goghno gām anugacchati |
sa gohatyākṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tribhir māsair vyapohati || 11.114 ||
upapātakasaṃyukta iti | caturthakālam iti | diveti | tiṣṭhantīṣv iti | ātuarām iti | uṣṇa iti | ātmana iti | aneneti |
goghātī upapātakavān sa māsaṃ yāvat saktuyavāgurūpeṇa yavān pibet | anyan māsadvayaṃ guḍādikṣāravarjitalavaṇam annam ekam ahar ahar bhuktvā dvitīye ‘hni sāyaṃ stokam aśnīyāt | tenaiva ca gocarmaṇācchādito muṇḍitakeśādigomūtreṇa snānaṃ kurvan saṃyatendriyo māsatrayam api gonivāse vaset | yāsāṃ gavāṃ goṣṭhe vaset tāsāṃ divā goṣṭhādūrādigacchantīnāṃ paścād gacched tāsāṃ cānugamanaṃ kurvan sthitaḥ saṃmūrcchanair ūrdhvam ūrdhvam udbhūtaṃ raja āsvadayet tābhir eva saha punar goṣṭham etya kaṇḍuyanādinā tāḥ paricarya praṇamya ca tato rātrāv aniṣiddhopaviṣṭa āsīta | tathā vyādhuyetāṃ cauravyāghrādibhir bhayahetubhir ākrāntāṃ śvabhrādipatitāṃ kardamāvasaktāṃ parayā śaktyā mocayet | tathoṣṇe āditye tapati varṣati maghe vāti vāyau śītair atyarthaṃ vāti gor yathāśakti rakṣām kṛtvātmanas trāṇaṃ kuryāt tathātmano ‘nyeṣāṃ vā gṛhe kṣetre khale ca sasyādi bhakṣayantīṃ gāṃ vatsāṃ ca kṣīraṃ pibantaṃ na brūyāt | anenoktavidhānena yo goghno gām anusarati sa govadhacaritaṃ pāpaṃ tribhir māsair apanudet || 11.107–114 ||
vṛṣabhaikādaśā gāś ca dadyāt sucaritavrataḥ |
avidyamāne sarvasvaṃ vedavidbhyo nivedayet || 11.115 ||
vṛṣabhaikādaśā iti | vṛṣabha ekādaśo yāsāṃ gavāṃ tāḥ cecchāto tatprāyaścitte dadyāt | avidyamāna iyati dhane sarvasvaṃ vedajñebhyo dadyāt || 11.115 ||
etad eva vrataṃ kuryur upapātakino dvijāḥ |
avikīrṇivarjaṃ śuddhyarthaṃ cāndrāyaṇam athāpi vā || 11.116 ||
etad iti | anye ‘py upapātakagaṇaḥ paṭhitavyatikramakāriṇo narā vakṣyamāṇavakīrṇivarjitāḥ pāpanirharaṇārthaṃ etad eva govadhaprāyaścittaṃ kuryuḥ || 11.116 ||
avakīrṇī tu kāṇena gardabhena catuṣpathe |
pākayajñavidhānena yajeta nirṛtiṃ niśi || 11.117 ||
hutvāgnau vidhivad dhomān antataś ca samety ṛcā |
vātendraguruvahnīnāṃ juhuyāt sarpiṣāhutīḥ || 11.118 ||
avakīrṇīti | hutveti | avakīrṇī vakṣyamāṇaḥ kāṇena khareṇa rātrau catuṣpathe pārvaṇatantreṇa nirṛtākhyāṃ devatāṃ yajet | yasmāc ca gardabhād vapādi homān yathārthaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ saṃ mā siñcantv ity anayarcā mārutendrabṛhaspatīty agnīnām atra mantra eṣāṃ sruveṇājyena juhuyāt || 11.117–118 ||
kāmato retasaḥ sekaṃ vratasthasya dvijanmanaḥ |
atikramaṃ vratasyāhur dharmajñā brahmavādinaḥ || 11.119 ||
aprasiddhatvād avakīrṇilakṣaṇam āha kāmata iti | dvijāter brahmacāriṇa icchātaḥ śukrotsargaṃ brahmacaryasya lopakaṃ dharmavido brahmasvarūpanirūpaṇaparāḥ prāhuḥ || 11.119 ||
mārutaṃ puruhūtaṃ ca guruṃ pāvakam eva ca |
caturo vratino ‘bhyeti brāhmaṃ tejo ‘vakīrṇinaḥ || 11.120 ||
mārutam iti | brahmacāriṇo yad adhyayanayamaniyamānuṣṭhānajanitaṃ tejas tad avakīrṇinaḥ sato marudindrabṛhaspatyagnīś catura upasaṃkarme ‘nyatarebhyaḥ ājyāhutīr juhuyād ity ājyāhutividher arthavādaḥ || 11.120 ||
etasminn enasi prāpte vasitvā gardabhājinam |
saptāgārāṃś cared bhakṣaṃ svakarma parikīrtayan || 11.121 ||
etasminn iti | saptāgāraṃ cared bhaikṣyaṃ svakarma parikīrtayan | avakīrṇākhye pāpa utpanne pūrvoktagardabhayāgādi kṛtvā tadgardabhasaṃbandhi carma prāvṛtyākīrṇy asmīty evaṃ svakarma khyāpayan saptagṛhaṃ bhaikṣyaṃ caret || 11.122 ||
tebhyo labdhena bhaikṣeṇa vartayann ekakālikam |
upaspṛśaṃs tiṣavaṇam abdena sa viśudhyati || 11.122 ||
tebhya iti | tadgṛhalabdhena bhaikṣyeṇa ekakālam āhāraṃ kurvan sāyaṃprātarmadhyandineṣu ca snānam ācaran saṃvatsareṇa yo ‘vakīrṇī sa śudhyati || 11.122 ||
jātibhraṃśakaraṃ karma kṛtvānyatamam icchayā |
caret sāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ prājāpatyam anicchayā || 11.123 ||
jātibhraṃśakaram iti | brāhmaṇasya rujaḥ kṛtvetyādi jātibhraṃśakarāṇy uktāni teṣu madhyād anyatamam icchayā kṛtvā vakṣyamāṇaṃ sāntapanaṃ kuryāt | anicchātas tu kṛtvā prājāpatyaṃ vakṣyamāṇam || 11.123 ||
saṃkarāpātrakṛtyāsu māsaṃ śodhanam aindavaḥ |
malinīkaraṇīyeṣu taptaḥ syād yāvakais tryaham || 11.124 ||
saṃkarāpātrakṛtyāsv iti | kharāśvoṣṭretyādi ninditebhyo dhanādānam ityādinā ca saṃkarīkaraṇāpātrīkaraṇyāny uktāni teṣu madhyād anyatamam icchātaḥ kṛtvā anicchayā kṛtaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ śuddhyarthaṃ kuryāt kṛmikīṭavayohatyeti malinīkaraṇīyāny uktāni tanmadhyād anyatamam icchātaḥ kṛtaṃ tryahaṃ yāvakaṃ pibet | yāvakaṃ yavakṛtaṃ peyākṛti taptaṃ pibet
|| 11.124 ||
turīyo brahmahatyāyāḥ kṣatriyasya vadhe smṛtaḥ |
vaiśye ‘ṣṭamāṃśo vṛttasthe śūdre jñeyas tu ṣoḍaśaḥ || 11.125 ||
turīya iti | brahmahatyāvadhād dvādaśavārṣikāc ca caturtho bhāgaḥ kṣatriyavadhe smṛtaḥ | vaiśye ca sādhvācāre ‘ṣṭabhāgaḥ | śūdre hate ṣoḍaśabhāgo boddhavya itīdaṃ sādhvācārakṣatriyaviṭśūdraviṣayaṃ gurutvāt strīśūdraviṭkṣatravadha ity etat puruṣapātakaprāyaścittaṃ jñānājñānabhedena ca cāndrāyaṇavaikalpikam asatkṣatraviṭśūdraviṣayaṃ laghutvāt | etac ca brahmahatyāvratacaturthāṃśād abuddhipūrvavadhe yenedam āha || 11.125 ||
akāmatas tu rājanyaṃ vinipātya dvijottamaḥ |
vṛṣabhaikasahasrā gā dadyāt sucaritavrataḥ || 11.126 ||
akāmata iti | abuddhipūrvaṃ punaḥ kṣatriyaṃ nihatya vṛṣabh ekaḥ sahastre yāsāṃ gavām ātmanaḥ śuddhyarthaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo dadyāt || 11.127 ||
tryabdaṃ cared vā niyato jaṭī brahmahaṇo vratam |
vasan dūratare grāmād vṛkṣamūlaniketanaḥ || 11.127 ||
tryabdam iti | brahmahā dvādaśasamā ityādi varṣatrayaṃ cared itīdam akāmatas tv ity etad vaikalpikam | gosahasrābhāve dānena vadhanirṇekaṃ sarpādīnām aśaknuvann iti darśanāj jaṭī dūratare grāmād vāsaṃ vṛkṣamūlanietana ity sthiro grahaṇād aṅgāntaram ity arthaṃ turīyo brahmahatyāyā ity atra tu sarvaṃ dvādaśavārṣikāṅgājataṃ bhavati || 11.127 ||
etad eva cared abdaṃ prāyaścittaṃ dvijottamaḥ |
pramāpya vaiśyaṃ vṛttasthaṃ dadyād vaikaśataṃ gavām || 11.128 ||
etad iti | brahmahatyāyāṃ yad uktaṃ vrataṃ brahmahā dvādaśasamā ity etad eva kāmādhikārād akāmataḥ sādhvācāraṃ vaiśyaṃ nihatya brāhmaṇādisaṃvatsaraṃ cared vittavān punar ekavṛṣabhādhikagavāṃ śataṃ dadyāt | pūrvottaraślokayor iti vṛṣabhagrahaṇāt || 11.128 ||
etad eva vrataṃ kṛtsnaṃ ṣaṇmāsān śūdrahā caret |
vṛṣabhaikādaśā vāpi dadyād viprāya gāḥ sitāḥ || 11.129 ||
etad iti | etad eva vrataṃ brahmahā dvādaśasamā iti yad uktaṃ tad vrataṃ gavopetaṃ cākāmādhikārād akāmataḥ sādhvācāraṃ śūdraṃ nihatya kuryāt | vittavān punaḥ vṛṣabha ekādaśo yāsāṃ gavāṃ tāḥ śuklā gāḥ brāhmaṇāya dadyāt || 11.129 ||
mārjāranakulau hatvā cāṣaṃ maṇḍūkam eva ca |
śvagodholūkākāṃś ca śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret || 11.130 ||
mārjāranakulāv iti | biḍālanakulacāṣaṃ maṇḍūkaś ca godholūkakākānām ekaikaṃ hatvā śūdravadhavrataṃ strīśūdraviṭkṣatravadha iti upapātakaprāyaścittaṃ govadhavrataṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret | śūdre jñeyas tu ṣoḍaśa ityādyatideśena syāt nyāyyatvāt | yady evaṃ kim iti govadhādi gaṇapāṭha evaitena paṭhitaḥ naivaṃ tatra pāṭhe payaḥ pibet trirātraṃ cety atra punar eṣāṃ grahaṇaṃ kartavyaṃ syāt | yat samucitavadhe etat prāyaścittam uttare payaḥ pānādinā laghunā saha vikalpaḥ syāt nyāyyatvād ity āhus tad asatsamuccayavāciśabdāśravaṇāt jñānādikṛtabhedena ca vyavasthitavikalpasaṃbhavād atiguruṇā laghīyaso jñānādibhedena vyavasthitavikalpo vānyāyya iti | prāsyed ātmānam agnau vā gṛhaṃ vāsaparicchadaṃ brāhmaṇāya dadyād ityevamādiṣu na cāsya samucitaviṣayatvam uttarasya ca pratyekagocaratve payaḥ pibet trirātraṃ vety evaṃ vā śabdaḥ saṃgacchate | tasmāt tatsthaulyāt | prāyaścittasyaicchāto niveśyainaghnataḥ saṃtapasi samarthasyātyantatapasi atyantam aśaktasyeti dhārmikasyedaṃ prāyaścittam | uttaraṃ punaḥ kathaṃcit | pramādasaṃjātavyatikramasyātyantadhārmikasya tapasi atyantaśaktaś ceti na kiṃcid vaiṣamyam
|| 11.130 ||
payaḥ pibet trirātraṃ vā yojanaṃ vādhvano vrajet |
upaspṛśet sravantyāṃ vā sūktaṃ vābdaivataṃ japet || 11.131 ||
paya iti | eṣāṃ mārjārādīnām abuddhipūrve vadhe trirātraṃ kṣīraṃ pibet | tatrāntaratvādināsamarthaḥ prakṛtaṃ trirātraṃ yojanam adhvano gacchet tatrāpy aśaktas trirātraṃ nadyāṃ snāyāt tatrāpy aparyāptaḥ trirātram āpohiṣṭhety etat sūktaṃ japet || 11.131 ||
abhriṃ kārṣṇāyasīṃ dadyāt sarpaṃ hatvā dvijottamaḥ |
palālabhārakaṃ ṣaṇḍhe saisakaṃ caikamāṣakam || 11.132 ||
abhrim iti | palālabhārakaṃ ṣaṇḍhe sīsakaṃ caiva māṣakam || 11.132 ||
ghṛtakumbhaṃ varāhe tu tiladroṇaṃ tu tittirau |
śuke dvihāyanaṃ vatsaṃ krauñcaṃ hatvā trihāyanam || 11.133 ||
ghṛtakumbham iti | śūkare hate ghṛtapūrṇaṃ ghaṭaṃ dadyāt tittirākhye pakṣiṇi hate tilānāṃ palaśatadvayaṃ dadyāc chuke hate dvivarṣaṃ vatsam | krauñcākhyaṃ pakṣiṇaṃ hatvā trivarṣaṃ vatsaṃ dadyāt || 11.133 ||
hatvā haṃsaṃ balākāṃ ca bakaṃ barhiṇam eva ca |
vānaraṃ śyenabhāsau ca sparśayed brāhmaṇāy agām || 11.134 ||
hatveti | haṃsabalākabakamayūravānarapājikabhāsākhyapakṣiṇām anyatamaṃ hatvā brāhmaṇāya gāṃ dadyāt | sparśanaṃ dānam | atrāpi gor mahatvāt samastavadha etat prāyaścittam ity āhuḥ, tad asat krauñcahanane trihāyanavatsadarśanāt || 11.134 ||
vāso dadyād dhayaṃ hatvā pañca nīlān vṛṣān gajam |
ajameṣāv anaḍvāhaṃ kharaṃ hatvaikahāyanam || 11.135 ||
vāsa iti | aśvaṃ hatvā vastraṃ dadyāt | hastinaṃ hatvā pañca nīlān vṛṣān dadyāt | chāgāvikaṃ hatvā vṛṣabhaṃ dadyāt | kharaṃ hatvaikavarṣaṃ vatsaṃ dadyāt || 11.135 ||
kravyādāṃs tu mṛgān hatvā dhenuṃ dadyāt payasvinīm |
akravyādān vatsatarīm uṣṭraṃ hatvā tu kṛṣṇalam || 11.136 ||
kravyādān iti | āmamāṃsabhakṣyān mṛgān vyāghrādīn hatvā pratyagraprasūtāṃ pracurakṣīrāṃ gāṃ dadyāt | amāṃsabhakṣān mṛgān sāraṅgādīn hatvottīrṇavatsabhāvāṃ gāṃ dadyāt | uṣṭraṃ hatvā suvarṇaraktikāṃ dadyāt || 11.136 ||
jīnakārmukabastāvīn pṛthag dadyād viśuddhaye |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ nārīr hatvānavasthitāḥ || 11.137 ||
jīnakārmukabastāvīn iti | brāhmaṇādicāturvarṇyajātīyāḥ striyo naikotkṛṣṭāpakṛṣṭapuruṣagamanena patantībhūtāḥ kathaṃcid anicchāto hatvā carmapūṭadhanucchāgameṣān yathākramaṃ śuddhyarthaṃ dadyāt || 11.137 ||
dānena vadhanirṇekaṃ sarpādīnām aśaknuvan |
ekaikaśaś caret kṛcchraṃ dvijaḥ pāpāpanuttaye || 11.138 ||
dāneneti | puṭādyabbāvād dānena sarpādivadhapāpanirharaṇaṃ kartum asamartho brāhmaṇādipratyekavadhe kṛcchrāṇāṃ madhye prathamaṃ prājāpatyaṃ kṛcchraṃ pāpanirharaṇārthaṃ sarpādayaś ca abhrikārṣṇāyasīṃ dadyād ity ataḥ prabhṛti nirdiṣṭā gṛhyante
|| 11.138 ||
asthimatāṃ tu sattvānāṃ sahasrasya pramāpaṇe |
pūrṇe cānasy anasthnāṃ tu śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret || 11.139 ||
asthyanvitānām iti | anasthisāhacaryād asthimatāṃ prāṇināṃ kṣudrajantūnāṃ kṛkalāsādīnāṃ sahasrasya vadhe śūdravadhavratam aupadeśikaṃ kuryāt | asthirahitānāṃ prāṇīnāṃ kṣudrajantūnāṃ matkuṇādīnāṃ ca śakaṭapūraṇaparimāṇānāṃ vadhād etad eva vrataṃ kuryāt
|| 11.139 ||
kiṃcid eva tu viprāya dadyād asthimatāṃ vadhe |
anasthnāṃ caiva hiṃsāyāṃ prāṇāyāmena śudhyati || 11.140 ||
kiṃcid iti | asthimatāṃ kṣudrajantūnāṃ kṛkalāsādīnāṃ pratyekaṃ vadhe kiṃcid evāsthimatāṃ vadhe paṇo deya iti sumantusmaraṇāt paṇaṃ brāhmaṇasya dadyāt | anasthnāṃ tu pratyekaṃ vadhe prāṇāyāmena |
svyāhṛtiṃ sapraṇavāṃ gāyatrīṃ śirasā saha |
triḥ paṭhed āyataprāṇaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ sa ucyate ||
iti vasiṣṭhoktalakṣaṇena śudhyati || 11.140 ||
phaladānāṃ tu vṛkṣāṇāṃ chedane japyam ṛkśatam |
gulmavallīlatānāṃ ca puṣpitānāṃ ca vīrudhām || 11.141 ||
phaladānam iti | phaladānāṃ vṛkṣāṇām āmrādīnāṃ vīrudhāṃ bahuparṇānāṃ saṃjātapuṣpāṇāṃ chedane pāpapramocane liṅgānām ṛcām śataṃ japanīyam || 11.141 ||
annādyajānāṃ sattvānāṃ rasajānāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ |
phalapuṣpodbhavānāṃ ca ghṛtaprāśo viśodhanam || 11.142 ||
annādyajānām iti | saktvādijātānāṃ prāṇināṃ guḍādyutpannānāṃ ca sarveṣām udumbaramadhukādiphalapuṣpodbhavānāṃ ca vadhe ghṛtaprāśanaṃ śodhanaṃ prāyaścittānāṃ śarīrasaṃtāpanārthatvāt | ghṛtaprāśakṣīrapānādi codanāyamannāntaranivṛttiḥ | śarīraśuddhyarthe punar ghṛtaprāśanena tu gamyecchāyā pretam ityādau na bhojanāntaraṃ nivarteta || 11.142 ||
kṛṣṭajānām oṣadhīnāṃ jātānāṃ ca svayaṃ vane |
vṛthālambhe ‘nugacched gāṃ dinam ekaṃ payovrataḥ || 11.143 ||
kṛṣṭajānām iti | karṣaṇapūrvaṃ jātānām auṣadhīnāṃ ṣaṣṭikādīnāṃ vane ca svayam utpannānāṃ nīvārādīnāṃ niṣprayojane chede kṣīrāhāraḥ sann ekam aho gor anugamanaṃ kuryāt || 11.143 ||
etair vratair apohyaṃ syād eno hiṃsāsamudbhavam |
jñānājñānakṛtaṃ kṛtsnaṃ śṛṇutānādyabhakṣaṇe || 11.144 ||
etair iti | ebhiḥ prāyaścittair hiṃsājanitaṃ pāpam apanodyaṃ syād idānīṃ ca bhakṣaṇe buddhipūrvābuddhipūrvabhakṣaṇajanitam aśeṣapāpaṃ yathāpohyate tathā śṛṇuta || 11.144 ||
ajñānād vāruṇīṃ pītvā saṃskāreṇaiva śudhyati |
matipūrvam anideśyaṃ prāṇāntikam iti sthitiḥ || 11.145 ||
ajñānād iti | mahāpātakakaraṇatāratamyāt tato nedaṃ mukhyasurāviṣayam api tu gauḍīmādhvīviṣayaṃ tenābuddhipūrvāṃ gauḍīṃ mādhvīṃ vā pītvā vapanaṃ mekhalādaṇḍa ity upanayanāṅganiṣedhād upanayanenāmatyāpāne payoghṛtam udakaṃ vāyuḥ pratyahaṃ taptāni sa kṛcchras tato ‘sya saṃskāra iti gautamasmaraṇāt taptakṛcchrasahitena śudhyati | buddhipūrvam api ca gauḍīmādhvyoḥ sakṛt pāne prāṇāntikaṃ na nirdiṣṭavyam iti śāstramaryādā | surāṃ pītvety etasya sarvaprakārasurāviṣayatvāśaṅkāyām iti pūrvam anirdeśyam ity etad uktam || 11.145 ||
apaḥ surābhājanasthā madyabhāṇḍasthitās tathā |
pañcarātraṃ pibet pītvā śaṅkhapuṣpīśṛtaṃ payaḥ || 11.146 ||
apa iti | surābhāṇḍe madyabhāṇḍe sthitā apaḥ surāmadyarasagandhavarjitā śaṅkhapuṣpauṣadhiprakṣepaṇapakvaṃ kṣīraṃ haviṣor eva śṛtaśabdasya sutvā (?) kṣīraṃ nodakaṃ pañcarātraṃ pibet | surāmadyayoḥ sākṣāt pāne prāyaścittabhedadarśanād ihāpi jñānājñānabhedena viṣayasamīkaraṇaṃ pariharaṇīyam || 11.146 ||
spṛṣṭvā datvā ca madirāṃ vidhivat pratigṛhya ca |
śūdrocchiṣṭāś ca pītvāpaḥ kuśavāri pibet tryaham || 11.147 ||
spṛṣṭveti | surāṃ spṛṣṭvā datvā ca svastivācanādipūrvakaṃ ca pratigṛhya śūdrocchiṣṭāḥ sarvāś cāpaḥ pītvā pratigṛhyety upādānād brāhmaṇo darbhakvathitam udakaṃ tryahaṃ pibet || 11.147 ||
brāhmaṇas tu surāpasya gandham āghrāya somapaḥ |
prāṇān apsu trir āyamya ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || 11.148 ||
brāhmaṇa iti | brāhmaṇaḥ punaḥ kṛtasomayāgaḥ surāpasya saṃbandhinaṃ mukhaniḥśvāsaṃ sugandhaṃ ghrātvā udakamadhye prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca kṛtvā viśudhyati
|| 11.148 ||
ajñānāt prāśya viṇmūtraṃ surāsaṃspṛṣṭam eva ca |
punaḥ saṃskāram arhanti trayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ || 11.149 ||
ajñānād iti | viḍvarāhādīnāṃ vakṣyamāṇatvāt abuddhipūrvaṃ manuṣyasaṃbandhimūtraṃ purīṣaṃ vā jagdhvā surāsaṃspṛṣṭaṃ vātirasāsvāde bhuktvā dvijātayas trayo varṇāḥ punarupanayanaṃ mūtrapurīṣakuṇaparetasāṃ prāśane caivam iti gautamasmaraṇāt taptakṛcchrasaṃhitam arhanti | bhakṣaṇe ‘pi ca brāhmaṇam atyācaret kṛcchraretoviṇmūtram eva ca ity uktaṃ tad apy eva gautamīyasaṃskārarahitaṃ draṣṭavyaṃ vacanāc cātra jñānājñānabhakṣaṇe tv atulyam eva prāyaścittam || 11.149 ||
vapanaṃ mekhalā daṇḍo bhaikṣyacaryā vratāni ca |
nivartante dvijātīnāṃ punaḥsaṃskārakarmaṇi || 11.150 ||
vapanam iti | śiromuṇḍanamekhalādaṇḍabhaikṣyacaraṇāni vratāni cāgnikāryaṃ madhumāṃsavarjanādīni prāyaścittārtham upanayane dvijātīnāṃ na bhavanti || 11.150 ||
abhojyānāṃ tu bhuktvānnaṃ strīśūdrocchiṣṭam eva ca |
jagdhvā māṃsam abhakṣyaṃ ca saptarātraṃ yavān pibet || 11.151 ||
abhojyānām iti | abhojyānām aśrotriyahute yajña ityādyuktānām annaṃ bhuktvā śūdrāyāḥ śūdragrahaṇenaivopasaṃgrahanād dvijātistrīṇām apy ucchiṣṭaṃ ca bhuktvā māṃsaṃ cābhakṣyaṃ yatra kravyādaśūkaroṣṭrāṇām ityādi yad vaikalpikaṃ prāyaścittaṃ noktaṃ tad bhutvā saptarātraṃ saktuyavāgūrūpeṇa yavān pibet || 11.151 ||
śuktāni ca kaṣāyāṃś ca pītvā medhyāny api dvijaḥ |
tāvad bhavaty aprayato yāvat tan na vrajaty adhaḥ || 11.152 ||
śuktānīti | yāni prāptasvarasāni kāmaparivāsanadravyāntareṇa saṃsargakālaparivāsābhyāṃ cāmlī bhavanti tāni śuktāni kaṣāyāharītakyādayas tāny apratiṣiddhāny api dvijātiḥ pītvā tāvad aśucir bhavati yāvat tadbhuktādi jīrṇaṃ na bhavati || 11.152 ||
viḍvarāhakharoṣṭrāṇāṃ gomāyoḥ kapikākayoḥ |
prāśya mūtrapurīṣāṇi dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 11.153 ||
viḍvarāhakharoṣṭrāṇām iti | grāmaśūkarakharoṣṭrasṛgālavānarakākānāṃ mūtrapurīṣāṇi dvijātir bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt || 11.153 ||
śuṣkāṇi bhuktvā māṃsāni bhaumāni kavakāni ca |
ajñātaṃ caiva sūnāstham etad eva vrataṃ caret || 11.154 ||
śuṣkāṇīti | vāyvādityādisaṃśoṣitāni māṃsāni vallūrādīni bhuktvā bhūmyudbhavāni ca kavakāni jagdhvā āghātasthānaṃ ca māṃsam abuddhipūrvaṃ bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt || 11.154 ||
kravyādasūkaroṣṭrāṇāṃ kukkuṭānāṃ ca bhakṣaṇe |
narakākakharāṇāṃ ca taptakṛcchraṃ viśodhanam || 11.155 ||
kravyādasūkaroṣṭrāṇām iti | āmamāṃsabhakṣiṇāṃ gṛdhrādīnāṃ grāmasūkaroṣṭragrāmyakukkuṭamanuṣyakākakharāṇāṃ māṃsabhakṣaṇe taptakṛcchraṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ viśodhanam || 11.155 ||
māsikānnaṃ tu yo ‘śnīyād asamāvartako dvijaḥ |
sa trīṇy ahāny upavased ekāhaṃ codake vaset || 11.156 ||
māsikārtham iti | kāmam abhyarthito ‘śnīyād ity amāvāsyāśrāddhabhojanābhyanujñānonmāsikārtham ekoddiṣṭaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇād arvāg yo brāhmaṇo brahmacārī bhuñjīta | sa trirātram upavaset tryahād anantarasmiṃś cāgnyudakamadhye vaset || 11.156 ||
vratacārī tu yo ‘śnīyān madhu māṃsaṃ kathaṃcana |
sa kṛtvā prākṛtaṃ kṛcchraṃ vrataśeṣaṃ samāpayet || 11.157 ||
vratacārīti | yo brahmacārī mākṣikamāṃsaṃ vānicchāta āpadi vāśnīyāt sa prājāpatyaṃ kṛcchraṃ kṛtvā tato brahmacary akrāntasya vratasya yad aśiṣṭaṃ tat samāpayet || 11.157 ||
biḍālakākākhūcchiṣṭaṃ jagdhvā śvanakulasya ca |
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca pibed brahmasuvarcalām || 11.158 ||
biḍālakākākhūcchiṣṭam iti | biḍālakākamūṣakaśvanakulānām ucchiṣṭaṃ keśakīṭasaṃsargaduṣṭaṃ cākṛtam utprakṣepaśuddhim annaṃ bhuktvā brahmasuvarcalāṃ prakvāthya tadrasam avaśeṣād ekāhaṃ pibet || 11.158 ||
abhojyam annaṃ nāttavyam ātmanaḥ śuddhim icchatā |
ajñānabhuktaṃ tūttaryaṃ śodhyaṃ vāpy āśu śodhanaiḥ || 11.159 ||
abhojyam iti | ātmanaḥ śuddhikāmena pratiṣiddham annaṃ haṭhān na bhakṣaṇīyaṃ pramādabhukte punar vamitavyaṃ tadasaṃbhave prāyaścittaiḥ kṣipraṃ śodhanīyam | yat tu vamanaprakṣepe ‘pi laghuprāyaścittaṃ bhavati || 11.159 ||
eṣo ‘nādyādanasyokto vratānāṃ vividho vidhiḥ |
steyadoṣāpahartṝṇāṃ vratānāṃ śrūyatāṃ vidhiḥ || 11.160 ||
eṣa iti | abhakṣyabhakṣaṇe yāni prāyaścittāni teṣām etan nānāprakāraṃ vidhānam uktam | adhunā cauryapāpāpahāriṇāṃ prāyaścittānāṃ vidhānaṃ śrūyatām || 11.160 ||
dhānyānnadhanacauryāṇi kṛtvā kāmād dvijottamaḥ |
svajātīyagṛhād eva kṛcchrābdena viśudhyati || 11.161 ||
dhānyānnadhanacauryāṇīti | brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇagṛhād dhānyarūpāṇi bhaktādyannarūpādi dhanacauryāṇi icchātaḥ kṛtvā na tv ātmīyabhrāntyā nītvā saṃvatsaraṃ prājāpatyakṛcchrācaraṇena śudhyati | iti kāladeśapariṇāmadravyasthānaguṇadaurgatyāpekṣayā idaṃ prāyaścittamahatvāt kalpanīyam evam uttaratrāpi gṛhādau boddhavyam || 11.161 ||
manuṣyāṇāṃ tu haraṇe strīṇāṃ kṣetragṛhasya ca |
kūpavāpījalānāṃ ca śuddhiś cāndrayaṇaṃ smṛtam || 11.162 ||
manuṣyāṇām iti |
dravyāṇām alpasārāṇāṃ steyaṃ kṛtvānyaveśmani |
caret sāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ tan niryāty ātmaśuddhaye || 11.163 ||
dravyāṇām iti | dravyāṇām alpārghāṇām alpaprayojanānām anuktaprāyaścittaviśeṣāṇāṃ trapusīsādīnāṃ paragṛhāc cauryaṃ kṛtvā tad apy akṛtaṃ dravyaṃ dvāmine datvā sāntāpanaṃ kṛcchraṃ vakṣyamāṇātmaśuddhyarthaṃ kuryān niryātyeti śeṣaḥ || 11.163 ||
bhakṣyabhojyāpaharaṇe yānaśayyāsanasya ca |
puṣpamūlaphalānāṃ ca pañcagavyaṃ viśodhanam || 11.164 ||
bhakṣyabhojyāpaharaṇa iti | bhakṣyasya modakāder yānasya gantryādeḥ śayyāyāḥ khaṭvādeḥ āsanasya ca pīṭhādeḥ puṣpāṇāṃ mūlānāṃ cāpaharaṇe gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpir ity etat pañcagavyaṃ viśodhanam || 11.164 ||
tṛṇakāṣṭhadrumāṇāṃ ca śuṣkānnasya guḍasya ca |
celacarmāmiṣāṇāṃ ca trirātraṃ syād abhojanam || 11.165 ||
tṛṇakāṣṭhadrumāṇām iti | tṛṇakāṣṭhavṛkṣaguḍavastracarmamāṃsānāṃ śuṣkānnasya ca taṇḍulāder apaharaṇaṃ trirātram upavāsaḥ syāt || 11.165 ||
maṇimuktāpravālānāṃ tāmrasya rajatasya ca |
ayaḥkāṃsyopalānāṃ ca dvādaśāhaṃ kaṇānnatā || 11.166 ||
maṇimuktāpravālānām iti | maṇimuktāvidrumatāmrarūpyāyaḥkāṃsyapāṣānāṃ cāpaharaṇād dvādaśāhaṃ taṇḍulaphalabhakṣaṇaṃ kāryam | sarvatrātra sakṛdabhyāsadeśakāladravyasvāmidaurgatyādyapekṣayotkṛṣṭadravyāpahāraviṣayam asamīkaraṇaṃ pariharaṇīyam || 11.166 ||
kārpāsakīṭajīrṇānāṃ dviśaphaikakurasya ca |
pakṣigandhauṣadhīnāṃ ca rajjvāś caiva tryahaṃ payaḥ || 11.167 ||
kārpāsakīṭajīrṇānām iti | kārpāsapaṭṭorṇānāṃ dviśaphasyāśvādeḥ pakṣiṇāṃ ca śukādīnāṃ gandhapradhānānāṃ cāgaruprabhṛtīnām auṣadhānāṃ guḍūcyādīnāṃ rajjvāś ca muñjādikṛtāyā apaharaṇe tryahaṃ kṣīrāhāraḥ syāt || 11.167 ||
etair vratair apoheta pāpaṃ steyakṛtaṃ dvijaḥ |
agamyāgamanīyaṃ tu vratair ebhir apānudet || 11.168 ||
etair iti | etair uktaiḥ prāyaścittaiḥ cauryajanitaṃ pāpaṃ dvijātir apanudet | agamyāgamananimittaṃ punar ebhir vakṣyamāṇaiḥ prāyaścittair nirharet || 11.168 ||
gurutalpavrataṃ kuryād retaḥ siktvā svayoniṣu |
sakhyuḥ putrasya ca strīṣu kumārīṣv antyajāsu ca || 11.169 ||
gurutalpavratam iti | bhaginīṣu sodaryāsu ca mitrabhāryāsu snuṣākumārīṣu cāṇḍālādyantyajāsu copagamanaṃ kṛtvā gurudāragamanaprāyaścittaṃ kuryāt | abhyāsādyapekṣayā tatra tatra gurutalpaprāyaścittaṃ vijñeyam | retaḥsekaḥ svayoniṣv iti anenāsya paunaruktyaṃ tatra voktaparihārārtham || 11.169 ||
paitṛṣvaseyīṃ bhaginīṃ svastrīyāṃ mātur eva ca |
matuś ca bhrātur āptasya gatvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 11.170 ||
pitṛṣvasreyīm iti | pitṛṣvasṛmātṛśvasṛduhitaraṃ mātuḥ svabhrātuḥ sodarasya duhitaraṃ gatvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 11.170 ||
etās tisras tu bhāryārthe nopayacchet tu buddhimān |
jñātvenān upeyās tāḥ patati hy upayann adhaḥ || 11.171 ||
etā iti | tisra etāḥ pitṛṣvasrādyāḥ bhāryārthaṃ prājñaiḥ nodvahanīyāḥ | jñātitvād etā noḍhavyāḥ yasmād etā upagacchan narakaṃ vrajati | asapiṇḍā tu yā mātur ity anenaiva niṣedhasiddhau dākṣiṇātyācāradarśanena dārḍhyārthaṃ punarvacanam || 11.171 ||
amānuṣīṣu puruṣa udakyāyām ayoniṣu |
retaḥ siktvā jale caiva kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret || 11.172 ||
amānuṣīṣv anaṅgaṣv iti | amānuṣīṣu vaḍavādyāsu na gavi gautamīye gavi ca talpasama ity atiguruprāyaścittadarśanād anaṅge vāvidyamānastrīpulliṅge napuṃsake rajasvalāyāṃ ca yonitaś cānyatra sthitāyāṃ jale ca retaḥsekaṃ kṛtvā sāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ kuryāt || 11.172 ||
maithunaṃ tu samāsevya puṃsi yoṣiti vā dvijaḥ |
goyāne ‘psu divā caiva savāsāḥ snānam ācaret || 11.173 ||
maithunam iti | puṃsy ūrumadhyakakṣāder maithunaṃ kṛtvā striyāṃ goyukte yāne vā gantryādāv apsu yāne vā nāvādau divā ca kṛtvā sacailasnānaṃ kuryāt || 11.173 ||
caṇḍālāntyastriyo gatvā bhuktvā ca pratigṛhya ca |
pataty ajñānato vipro jñānāt sāmyaṃ tu gacchati || 11.174 ||
cāṇḍālasyeti | cāṇḍālamlecchastriyaṃ gatvā ajñānato bhuktvā ca tebhyaś ca pratigrahaṃ jñānataḥ kṛtvā abhyāse sati patati | ataś ca patitaprāyaścittam asya bhavatīty etat punar etadabhyāsataḥ kṛtvā tattulyatāṃ yāti | ataś cāsya prāyaścittābhāvaḥ || 11.174 ||
vipraduṣṭāṃ striyaṃ bhartā nirundhyād ekaveśmani |
yat puṃsaḥ paradāreṣu tac caināṃ cārayed vratam || 11.175 ||
vipraduṣṭām iti | vipraduṣṭāṃ striyaṃ nirundhya prāyaścittāntaṃ yāvat sthāpayet | yac ca puṃsaḥ samānajātīyaparadāragamane upapātakaprāyaścittaṃ tad evaināṃ kārayet || 11.175 ||
sā cet punaḥ praduṣyet tu sadṛśenopamantritā |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva tad asyāḥ pāvanaṃ smṛtam || 11.176 ||
seti | sā strī sajātīyagamanena sakṛtpraduṣṭā kṛtaprāyaścittā yadi punas tenaiva sajātinā abhyarthitā satī tadgamanaṃ kuryāt tadā prājāpatyaṃ kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ ca tasyāḥ śuddhyarthaṃ dvayam api syāt || 11.176 ||
yat karoty ekarātreṇa vṛṣalīsevanād dvijaḥ |
tad bhakṣyabhug japan nityaṃ tribhir varṣair vyapohati || 11.177 ||
yad iti | “śūdrāṃ śayanam āropya brāhmaṇo yāty adhogatim” | “śūdrāvedī pataty atrer” iti nindātiṣayaśravaṇād akramoḍhāśūdrāsevanena | yad ekarātreṇa pāpam arjayati tad bhaikṣāśī nityaṃ japaṃ kurvan tribhir varṣair apanudati | yat tu cāṇḍāly atra vṛṣalīti vyācakṣate tad asat aprasiddhes tathā cāpastambo “anāryāśayane bibhrat” ity upakramya, “yad ekarātreṇa karoti pāpaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ sevamānaś caturthakāla udakābhyadhāyais tribhir varṣaiḥ tad apahanti pāpam” ity āha || 11.177 ||
eṣā pāpakṛām utā caturṇām api niṣkṛtiḥ |
patitaiḥ saṃprayuktānām imāḥ śṛṇuta niṣkṛtīḥ || 11.178 ||
eṣeti | hiṃsā abhakṣyabhakṣaṇasteyāgamyāgamanakāriṇāṃ caturṇām api mahāpātakinām itareṣāṃ caiṣā saṃśuddhir uktā | idānīṃ patitasaṃyogināṃ cemā vakṣyamāṇāḥ saṃśuddhiḥ śṛṇuta || 11.178 ||
saṃvatsareṇa patati patitena sahācaran |
yājanādhyāpanād yaunān na tu yānāsanāśanāt || 11.179 ||
saṃvatsareṇeti | mahāpātakakāriṇaṃ yājayed yas tena vā yājitas tam adhyāpayaṃs tena vādhyāpitas tasmai kanyāṃ dadaṃs tasmād dattām udvahan saṃvatsareṇa patati | dvijātikarmabhyo hāniḥ patanam iti gautamasmaraṇāt, adṛṣṭārthakāmādhikārād dhīyate | sahagamanabhojanāvasthānāni punaḥ patitena saha kurvan saṃvatsareṇa patati api tu tata ūrdhvaṃ teṣāṃ laghutvāt || 11.179 ||
yo yena patitenaiṣāṃ saṃsargaṃ yāti mānavaḥ |
sa tasyaiva vrataṃ kuryāt tat saṃsargaviśuddhaye || 11.180 ||
ya iti | eṣāṃ brahmahāprabhṛtīnāṃ caturṇāṃ patitānām adhyāpanādi patitena saha pūrvavākyoktaṃ saṃsargaṃ yo manuṣyaḥ karoti sa tasya prāyaścittam uktaṃ brahmahā dvādaśasamā ityādi tad etat saṃparkapāpajanitaṃ kuryāt || 11.181 ||
patitasyodakaṃ kāryaṃ sapiṇḍair bāndhavair bahiḥ |
nindite ‘hani sāyāhne jñātyṛtviggurusaṃnidhau || 11.181 ||
patitasyodakam iti | mahāpātakino jīvataḥ sa eva pretasyaivodakakriyāṃ vakṣyamāṇanītyā sapiṇḍaiḥ samānodakaiḥ sametya jñātyṛtviggurusaṃnidhāne riktāyāṃ navamyādau tithau dinānte kartavyā || 11.181 ||
dāsī ghaṭam apāṃ pūrṇaṃ paryasyet pretavat padā |
ahorātram upāsīrann aśaucaṃ bāndhavaiḥ saha || 11.182 ||
dāsīti | sapiṇḍasamānodakaprayuktā dās¥ udakapūrṇaṃ ghaṭam amum anudakaṃ karomīti nāmagrāham iti gautamasmaraṇād evam uktvā pretasyeva dakṣiṇamukhaṃ pādena prakṣipet tadanu ca te sapiṇḍāḥ samānodakaiḥ saha śaucam āśrayeyuḥ || 11.182 ||
nivarteraṃś ca tasmāt tu saṃbhāṣaṇasahāsane |
dāyādyasya pradānaṃ ca yātrā caiva hi laukikī || 11.183 ||
nivarterann iti | dāyādyasya pradānaṃ ca yātrām eva ca laukikīm | tena ca patitena saha saṃbhāṣaṇasahāvasthāne na kartavye na ca tasmai rithadeyaṃ nāpy utsavādau nimantraṇādi tasya lokavyavahāraḥ kāryaḥ || 11.183 ||
jyeṣṭhatā ca nivarteta jyeṣṭhāvāpyaṃ ca tad vasu |
jyeṣṭhāṃśaṃ prāpnuyāc cāsya yavīyān guṇato ‘dhikaḥ || 11.184 ||
jyeṣṭhateti | jyeṣṭhakāryaṃ pratyutthānādi tasya na kāryaṃ jyeṣṭhalabhyaṃ coddhāradhanaṃ nāsya deyam | etac ca pūrvasiddham eva yavīyasaḥ prāptyartham anūdyate | tasya ca jyeṣṭhasaṃbandhinam uddhārāṃśaṃ tadanujo gaṇādhiko labhate || 11.184 ||
prāyaścitte tu carite pūrṇakumbham apāṃ navam |
tenaiva sārdhaṃ prāsyeyuḥ snātvā puṇye jalāśaye || 11.185 ||
prāyaścitta iti | kṛte punaḥ patitena prāyaścitte udakapūrṇaṃ navaṃ tenaiva prāyaścittena saha pavitre jalādhāre snātvā tena sapiṇḍasamānodakāḥ prakṣipeyuḥ | iha navagrahaṇād dāsīghaṭam ity atropayuktaghaṭagrahaṇaṃ gamyate || 11.185 ||
sa tv apsu taṃ ghaṭaṃ prāsya praviśya bhavanaṃ svakam |
sarvāṇi jñātikāryāṇi yathāpūrvaṃ samācaret || 11.186 ||
sa iti | sa kṛtaprāyaścittaḥ taṃ pūrvoktaṃ ghaṭam udakamadhye kṣiptvā tataḥ svagṛhaṃ praviśya pūrvavat sarvāṇi bandhukāryāṇi kuryāt || 11.186 ||
etam eva vidhiṃ kuryād yoṣitsu patatāsv api |
vastrānnapānaṃ deyaṃ tu vaseyuś ca gṛhāntike || 11.187 ||
etad eva vratam iti | yad eva puṃsaḥ pātityanimittaṃ tad eva strīṇāṃ viśeṣaṃ yājñavalkyaḥ prāha: “nīcābhigamanaṃ garbhapātanaṃ bhartṛhiṃsanam | viśeṣapatanīyāni strīṇām etāny api dhruvam” ity evaṃ strīṣu patitāsu patitasyodakaṃ kāryam ityādi yad uktaṃ kiṃ tu kuryāt kiṃ tu grāsācchādanānnapānādīni gṛhasamīpe ca śīrṇagṛhakādi nivāsārtham āsāṃ deyam || 11.187 ||
enasvibhir anirṇiktair nārthaṃ kiṃcit samācaret |
kṛtanirṇejanāṃś caitān na jugupseta karhicit || 11.188 ||
enasvibhir iti | pāpakāribhir akṛtaprāyaścittaiḥ saha dānapratigrahādikam anarthakaṃ ca nānutiṣṭhet | kṛtaprāyaścittānāṃ caiṣāṃ na kadācid api kiṃcit saṃbhāvayet asyāyaṃ cārthavādaḥ
|| 11.188 ||
bālaghnāṃś ca kṛtaghnāṃś ca viśuddhān api dharmataḥ |
śaraṇāgatahantṝṃś ca strīhantṝṃś ca na saṃvaset || 11.189 ||
bālaghnān iti | bālaṃ yo hatavān yaś ca kṛtam upakārācaraṇena nāśitavān yaś ca trāṇārthinam āgataṃ hatavān striyaṃ ca yo hatavān tān prāyaścittatapasotpannaśuddhikān api sahavāsaṃ pariharet || 11.189 ||
yeṣāṃ dvijānāṃ sāvitrī nānucyeta yathāvidhi |
tāṃś cārayitvā trīn kṛcchrān yathāvidhy upanāyayet || 11.190 ||
