yeṣām iti | yeṣāṃ brāhmaṇādīnām anukalpikakāle ‘py upanayanaṃ yathāśāstraṃ na kṛtaṃ tān prājāpatyakṛcchratrayaṃ kārayitvā yathāśāstram upanāyayet || 11.190 ||
prāyaścittaṃ cikīrṣanti vikarmasthās tu ye dvijāḥ |
brāhmaṇā ca parityaktās teṣām apy etad ādiśet || 11.191 ||
prāyaścittam iti | pratiṣiddhakarmajīvino ye dvijāḥ prāyaścittaṃ kartum icchanti ye vānadhītavedās teṣām api etat kṛcchratrayam upadiśet || 11.191 ||
yad garhitenārjayanti karmaṇā brāhmaṇā dhanam |
tasyotsargeṇa śudhyanti japyena tapasaiva ca || 11.192 ||
yad iti | yad garhitena tapasā karmaṇāsatpratigrahādinā brāhmaṇā dhanam arjayanti tasya dhanasya parityāgena japatapobhyāṃ ca vakṣyamāṇābhyāṃ śudhyanti || 11.192 ||
japitvā trīṇi sāvitryāḥ sahasrāṇi samāhitaḥ |
māsaṃ goṣṭhe payaḥ pītvā mucyate ‘satpratigrahāt || 11.193 ||
japitveti | trīṇi sāvitrīsahasrāṇi yatnavān japitvā sāsaṃ ca kṣīrāhāro goṣṭhanivāsa āsīnaḥ san asatpratigrahajanitaṃ pāpam apaharati || 11.193 ||
upavāsakṛtaṃ taṃ tu govrajāt punar āgatam |
praṇataṃ pratipṛccheyuḥ sāmyaṃ saumyecchasīti kim || 11.194 ||
upavāsakṛtam iti | kevalaṃ kṣīrāhāreṇāśanāntaranivṛttyā tam apavitaśarīaṃ goṣṭhāt pratyāgataṃ prahvībhūtaṃ he sādho kiṃ asmābhiḥ saha sāmānyam icchati punar asatpratigrahea na tvaṃ pravartasa ity evaṃ dharmaṃ brāhmaṇāḥ paripṛccheyuḥ || 11.194 ||
satyam uktvā tu vipreṣu vikired yavasaṃ gavām |
gobhiḥ pravartite tīrthe kuryus tasya parigraham || 11.195 ||
satyam iti | satyaṃ punar asatpratigrahaṃ na kariṣye ity evaṃ brāhmaṇān uktvā gavāṃ ghāsaṃ prakṣipet | tatas tasmin yavasabhakṣaṇapradeśe  gobhiḥ pavitrīkṛtatvāt  tīrtha evāvatārito brāhmaṇā asya saṃvyavahārārtham aṅgīkāraṃ kuryuḥ || 11.195 ||
vrātyānāṃ yājanaṃ kṛtvā pareṣām antyakarma ca |
abhicāram ahīnaṃ ca tribhiḥ kṛcchrair vyapohati || 11.196 ||
vrātyānām iti | vrātyānāṃ bhraṣṭopanayanānām ata ūrdhvaṃ trayo ‘py ete ityādyuktānāṃ vrātyastomādiyajanaṃ kṛtvā pitṛgurvādivyatiriktānāṃ vāntyeṣṭiṃ kṛtvā vākśastraṃvai brāhmaṇasyoktatvāt anabhicāraṇīyasya śyenādikarmabhiḥ caret samaṃ jātamaraṇahīnaṃ ca ṛtuviśeṣaṃ dvirātrādi kṛtvā tribhiḥ kṛcchrair viśudhyati || 11.196 ||
śaraṇāgataṃ parityajya vedaṃ viplāvya ca dvijaḥ |
saṃvatsaraṃ yavāhāras tat pāpam apasedhati || 11.197 ||
śaraṇāgatam iti | śaraṇātam apāpaṃ paritrāṇārthinam āgataṃ prāyaścittārthaṃ vā saṃprāptaṃ śaktaḥ sann aparityajyānadhyāpyaṃ ca vedam adhyāpya dhanārthaṃ vā parīkṣāsthāne paryaṅkayogapūrvakaṃ vedaikadeśam uktvā tajjanitaṃ pāpaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ yavabhakṣo bhūtvā dvijo ‘panudati || 11.197 ||
śvasṛgālakharair daṣṭto grāmyaiḥ kravyādbhir eva ca |
narāśvoṣṭravarāhaiś ca prāṇāyāmena śudhyati || 11.198 ||
śvaśṛgālakharair iti | śvaśṛgālakharamanuṣyāśvoṣṭraśūkarair grāmyaiś cāmamāṃsādair mārjārādibhir daṣṭaḥ prāṇāyāmena śūdhyati || 11.198 ||
ṣaṣṭhānnakālatā māsaṃ saṃhitājapa eva vā |
homāś ca śākalā nityam apāṅktyānāṃ viśodhanam || 11.199 ||
ṣaṣṭhānnakālateti | homāś ca śālakā iti apāṅktyā ye stenapatitaklibā ityādinoktās teṣāṃ ye nāmnātapratipadaprāyaścittās teṣāṃ māsaṃ tryaham abhukte tṛtīye ‘hni sāyaṃ bhojanaṃ vedasaṃhitājapo devakṛtasyainaso ‘vayajanam asītyādibhir yajurbhir aṣṭau kāṣṭhaśakalahomāḥ pratyaham ity evam etat samucitaṃ viśodhanam || 11.199 ||
uṣṭrayānaṃ samāruhya kharayānaṃ tu āmataḥ |
snātvā tu vipro digvāsāḥ prāṇāyāmena śudhyati || 11.200 ||
uṣṭrayānam iti || 11.200 ||
vinādbhir apsu vāpy ārtaḥ śārīraṃ saṃniveṣya ca |
sacailo bahir āplutya gām ālabhya viśudhyati || 11.201 ||
vinādbhir apsu vāpy ārtaḥ śārīraṃ saṃniveṣya tv iti | asaṃnihita udake jalamadhye vāvegārto mūtrapurīṣe vā kṛtvā savāsā bahir grāmān nadyādau snātvā gāṃ ca spṛṣṭvā viśudhyati
|| 11.201 ||
vedoditānāṃ nityānāṃ karmaṇāṃ samatikrame |
snātakavratalope ca prāyaścittam abhojanam || 11.202 ||
vedoditānām iti | vedavihitānāṃ nityānāṃ karmaṇām agnihotrāṇām anāmnātaśrautaviṣayaprāyaścittānāṃ parilope snātakavratānāṃ ca vaiṇavīṃ dhārayed yaṣṭim ityādyuktānām akaraṇe upavāsaḥ prāyaścittam || 11.202 ||
huṅkāraṃ brāhmaṇasyoktvā tvaṅkāraṃ ca garīyasaḥ |
snātvānaśnann ahaḥśeṣam abhivādya prasādayet || 11.203 ||
huṅkāram iti | hum ity evaṃ saroṣaṃ brāhmaṇasyoktvā tvam ity evaṃ ca vittādyadhikasyoktvaiva etat etatkaraṇakālāvaśiṣṭam ahar abhuñjānaḥ snānaṃ kṛtvā taṃ pādopasaṃgrahaṇena apagatakopaṃ kuryāt || 11.203 ||
tāḍayitvā tṛṇenāpi kaṇṭhe vābadhya vāsasā |
vivāde vā vinirjitya praṇipatya prasādayet || 11.204 ||
tāḍayitveti | vivādenāpīti | prākṛtaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ garīyāṃsaṃ ca kopāt tṛṇenāpy āhatya kaṇṭhe ca vastreṇābadhya kalahe ca jitvā praṇipātenāpagatakrodhaṃ kuryāt || 11.204 ||
avagūrya tv abdaśataṃ sahasram abhihatya ca |
jighāṃsayā brāhmaṇasya narakaṃ pratipadyate || 11.205 ||
avagūryeti | brāhmaṇasya hananecchayā daṇḍādy udyamya varṣaśataṃ narakaṃ prāpnoti | daṇḍādinā punaḥ prahṛtya varṣasahasraṃ prāpnoti || 11.206 ||
śoṇitaṃ yāvataḥ pāṃsūn saṃgṛhṇāti mahītale |
tāvanty abdasahasrāṇi tatkartā narake vrajet || 11.206 ||
śoṇitaṃ yāvataḥ pāṃsūn saṃgṛhṇāti dvijanmana iti | brāhmaṇasya prahṛte sati tatsaṃbandhirudhiraṃ yat saṃkhyākān bhūmau rajaḥkaṇān piṇḍīkaroti tatsaṃkhyākāni varṣasahasrāṇi tacchoṇitotpādayitā narakam āste || 11.206 ||
avagūrya caret kṛcchram atiṛcchraṃ nipātane |
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau kurvīta viprasyotpādya śoṇitam || 11.207 ||
avagūryeti | brāhmaṇasya daṇḍādy udyamya prājāpatyaṃ kṛcchraṃ kuryāt | daṇḍādiprahāraṃ datvātikṛcchraṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ caret | rudhiram utpādya kṛcchrātikṛcchrau kuryāt || 11.207 ||
anuktaniṣkṛtīnāṃ tu pāpānām apanuttaye |
śaktiṃ cācekṣya pāpaṃ ca prāyaścittaṃ prakalpayet || 11.208 ||
anuktaniṣkṛtīnām iti | anuktaprāyaścittānāṃ pāpānāṃ pratilomavadhādikṛtānāṃ nirharaṇārthaṃ kartuḥ śarīradhanādisāmarthyam apekṣya pāpaṃ ca jñānājñānasakṛdasakṛdāvṛttyanubandhād-irūpeṇāvekṣya vakṣyamāṇaprāyaścittaṃ prakalpayet || 11.208 ||
yair abhyupāyair enāṃsi mānavo vyapakarṣati |
tān vo ‘bhyupāyān vakṣyāmi devarṣipitṛsevitān || 11.209 ||
yair iti | yaiḥ sādhanair manuṣyaḥ pāpāny apanudati tāni sādhanāni devarṣipitṛbhiḥ pāpāpanuttyarthaṃ sevitāni yuṣmākaṃ vakṣyāmi || 11.209 ||
tryahaṃ prātas tryahaṃ sāyaṃ tryaham adyād ayācitam |
tryahaṃ paraṃ ca nāśnīyāt prājāpatyaṃ caran dvijaḥ || 11.210 ||
tryaham iti | prājāpatyākhyaṃ kṛcchraṃ dvijātiḥ kurvan tryaham ādyaṃ dinādāv aśnīyād aparaṃ tryahaṃ dinānte bhuñjīta | anyaṃ tryaham aprārthitam upanatam adyāt paraṃ tryahān na kiṃcid bhakṣayet | atra “haviṣyāt prātar asau [āśān]” iti [GDh 26.2] gautamasmaraṇād dhaviṣyam aditavyam | grāsasaṃkhyā ca |
sāyaṃ ca dvādaśagrāsāḥ prātaḥ pañcadaśa smṛtāḥ |
ṣaṭtriṃśatir ayācyāḥ syuḥ paraṃ niraśanaṃ smṛtam ||
iti pārāśaroktyā vijñeyam || 11.210 ||
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam |
ekarātropavāsaś ca kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ smṛtam || 11.211 ||
gomūtram iti | gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ godadhi goghṛtadarbhakvathitodakānāṃ pratyaham ekaṃ copavāsaṃ kuryāt | ity evaṃ saptāham ekasminn ahany etāni sarvāṇi pibed ekaṃ copavāsaṃ kuryāt | ity evaṃ tryahaṃ yājñavalkya ubhayadarśanād ity eṣaḥ sāntapanākhyaḥ kṛcchraṃ smṛtaḥ || 11.211 ||
ekaikaṃ grāsam aśnīyāt tryahāṇi trīṇi pūrvavat |
tryahaṃ copavased antyam atikṛcchraṃ caran dvijaḥ || 11.212 ||
ekaikam iti | tryahaṃ copavased iti | atikṛcchākhyakṛcchraṃ dvijātiḥ kurvan prātaḥsāyam ayācitarūpeṇaikaikaṃ grāsaṃ trīṇi tryahāny aśnīyāt | anyac ca tryahaṃ na kiṃcid bhuñjīta
|| 11.212 ||
taptakṛcchraṃ caran vipro jalakṣīraghṛtānilān |
pratitryahaṃ pibed uṣṇān sakṛn snāyī samāhitaḥ || 11.213 ||
taptakṛccham iti | taptakṛcchrākhyakṛcchraṃ brāhmaṇādi kurvan tryaham udakaṃ kṣīraghṛtavāyūn uṣṇān apāṃ pibet |
tripalaṃ tu hy apaḥ pītvā tripalaṃ tu payaḥ pibet |
sarpiṣaḥ palam ekaṃ tu trirātraṃ coṣṇamārutam ||
iti parāśaroktaparimāṇānām ekavāraṃ snānaṃ kurvan saṃyamavān pibet | uṣṇamārutaṃ uṣṇodakaṃ vā || 11.213 ||
yatātmano ‘pramattasya dvādaśāham abhojanam |
parāko nāma kṛcchro ‘yaṃ sarvapāpāpanodanaḥ || 11.214 ||
yatātmāna iti | vigatānnābhilāṣasya saṃyatendriyasya yat dvādaśāham abhojanaṃ tat parākākhyaḥ kṛcchraḥ pāpāpekṣayā sakṛd āvṛttitāratamyena prakāśāprakāśagurulaghusakalapāpānām apanodakaḥ || 11.214 ||
ekaikaṃ hrāsayet piṇḍaṃ kṛṣṇe śukle ca vardhayet |
upaspṛśaṃs triṣavaṇam etac cāndrāyaṇaṃ smṛtam || 11.215 ||
ekaikam iti | sāyaṃprātar madhyāhneṣu snānaṃ kuryāt | paurṇamāsyāṃ pañcadaśagrāsān “grāsapramāṇaṃ vāsyāvikāreṇa” [GDh 27.10) iti gautamoktapramāṇān aśnīyāt | tataḥ kṛṣṇapakṣe pratipatprabhṛti ekaikagrāsāpacayaṃ kuryāt | yāvac ca caturdaśyām ekagrāsam aśnīyāt, amāvāsyāyāṃ copoṣya tataḥ pratipatprabhṛti śuklapakṣa ekaikaṃ grāsaṃ vṛddhiṃ nayed yāvat paurṇamāsyāṃ pañcadaśety etat pipīlikāmadhyaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 11.215 ||
etam eva vidhiṃ kṛtsnam ācared yavamadhyame |
śuklapakṣādiniyataś caraṃś cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam || 11.216 ||
etam eva vidhiṃ kṛtsnam ācarann iti | evam eva piṇḍānāṃ grāsavṛddhiṃ triṣavaṇasnānātmakaṃ vidhānaṃ yavamadhyamākhyacāndrāyaṇe śuklapakṣaṃ āditaḥ kṛtvā kṛtaniyamaḥ etad yavamadhyākhyaṃ cāndrāyaṇavratam anutiṣṭhan kuryāt | pratipady ekaṃ grāsaṃ dvitīyāyāṃ dvau yāvat paurṇamāsyāṃ pañcadaśāśnīyāt tataḥ pratipatprabhṛti ekaikagrāsāpacayaṃ kuryāt yāvaccaturdaśyām ekaḥ paścād amāvāsyāyām upavāsa iti ||
11.216 ||
aṣṭāv aṣṭau samaśnīyāt piṇḍān madhandine sthite |
niyatātmā haviṣyāśī yaticāndrāyaṇaṃ caran || 11.217 ||
aṣṭāv iti | niyatātmā havṣyāsyeti | yaticāndrāyaṇākhyaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ kurvan vigatānnagandho haviṣyasya odanāder aṣṭāv aṣṭau grāsān madhyāhnakāle samaśnīyāt || 11.217 ||
caturaḥ prātar aśnīyāt piṇḍān vipraḥ samāhitaḥ |
caturo ‘stamite sūrye śuśucāndrāyaṇaṃ smṛtam || 11.218 ||
catura iti | śuśucāndrāyaṇākhyaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ brāhmaṇādi kurvaṃś caturo grāsān dinādau caturaś cāditye ‘staṃgate saṃyamitātmā samaśnīyāt || 11.218 ||
yathā kathaṃcit piṇḍānāṃ tisro ‘śītīḥ samāhitaḥ |
māsenāśnan haviṣyasya candrasyaiti salokatām || 11.219 ||
yathā kathaṃcid iti | nivārādi haviṣyānnasaṃbandhināṃ haviṣyāṇāṃ grāsānāṃ ca dve śate catvāriṃśadadhike kadācid ekaṃ kadācid pañca kadācit ṣoḍaśa kadācid upavāsa ity evamādi aniyamane yathā kathaṃcit māsena saṃyamavān aśnaṃś candralokaṃ prāpnoti | ataś cābhyudayārtham apy etat kāryam || 11.219 ||
etad rudrās tathādityā vasavaś cācaran vratam |
sarvākuśalamokṣāya marutaś ca maharṣibhiḥ || 11.220 ||
etad iti | rudrādityavasumarudṛṣaya etac cāndrāyaṇākhyaṃ vrataṃ prakāśāprakāśagurulaghusakalapāpāpanodāya pāpāpekṣayā sakṛdāvṛttitāratamyena kṛtavantaḥ
|| 11.220 ||
mahāvyāhṛtibhir homaḥ kartavyaḥ svayam anvaham |
ahiṃsā satyam akrodham ārjavaṃ ca samācaret || 11.221 ||
mahāvyāhṛtibhir iti | mahāvyāhṛtibhir bhūr bhuvaḥ svar ity etābhiḥ “ājye havir anādeśa” iti sūtrakāravacanāt ājyena svayaṃ pratyahaṃ homaṃ kuryāt | ahiṃsā satyam akrodhākauṭilyāni puruṣārthayoktāny api vratāṅgatayānutiṣṭhet || 11.221 ||
trir ahnas trir niśāyāṃ ca savāsā jalam āviśet |
strīśūdrapatitāṃś caiva nābhibhāṣeta karhicit || 11.222 ||
trir ahnas trir niśāyām iti | ahno rātreś cārambhamadhyāvasāneṣu snānārthaṃ sacailo nadyādau praviśed etac ca pipīlikāmadhyacāndrāyaṇavarjaṃ tatropaspṛśaṃs triṣavaṇam ity uktatvāt strīśūdrapatitaiś ca sahasaṃ bhāṣaṇaṃ na kadācid yāvad vrataṃ kuryāt || 11.222 ||
sthānāsanābhyāṃ vihared aśakto ‘dhaḥ śayīta vā |
brahmacārī vratī ca syād gurudevadvijārcakaḥ || 11.223 ||
sthānāsanābhyām iti | “tiṣṭhed ahani rātrau āsīnaḥ” iti gautamasmaraṇād dviāsthito rātrāv āsīno vartetāsāmarthyād vā sthaṇḍilaśāyī syāt | strīsaṃprayogarahito vratī ca mauñjīdaṇḍakamaṇḍalur agnikāryaṃ ceti śaṅkhadarśanān mekhalādiyukto gurudevabrāhmaṇānāṃ pūjakaḥ syāt || 11.223 ||
sāvitrīṃ ca japen nityaṃ pavitrāṇi ca śaktitaḥ |
sarveṣv eva vrateṣv evaṃ prāyaścittārtham ādṛtaḥ || 11.224 ||
sāvitrīm iti | sāvitrīṃ ca sarvadā rātrāv api japet pavitrāṇi yathāśakti japet | evaṃ kṛcchrādiṣv api sarveṣu yatnavān prāyaścittārthaṃ mahāvyāhṛtibhir homādi sarvaṃ kuryāt | taptakṛcchreṣu ṣaṭsavanaṃ snānam atideśakaṃ na pravartate sakṛt snāyītyauepadeśikavirodhāt || 11.224 ||
etair dvijātayaḥ śodhyā vratair āviṣkṛtainasaḥ |
anāviṣkṛtapāpāṃs tu mantrair homaiś ca śodhayet || 11.225 ||
etair iti | ebhir uktair pāpaiḥ prāyaścittair lokaviditapāpā dvijātayo vakṣyamāṇaparṣadā śodhanīyāḥ | aprakāśavadhaṃ punā rahasyatvavināśaparihārāc chiṣyatvādivyapadeśenāyatān vakṣyamāṇair japahomaiḥ parṣacchodhayet || 11.225 ||
khyāpanenānutāpena tapasādhyayanena ca |
pāpakṛn mucyate pāpāt tathā dānena cāpadi || 11.226 ||
khyāpaneneti | dvitrādividita evaṃ karmāsmīty evam anyeṣām api aviditapāpānāṃ pāpaprakāśanena prakāśāprakāśāṅgabhūtena na tu rahasyāṅgabhūtena rahasyatvahāniprasaṅgād hi kaṣṭaṃ kiṃ mayedaṃ kṛtam ity evaṃ. paścāttāpena ca prakāśāṅgabhūtenaiva tapasā coktarūpeṇa kṛcchrādinā japena ca prakāśāṅgapradhānabhūtenaiva prāyaścittasāmarthyena ca prakrāntatapāḥ dānena pāpakārī pāpān mucyate || 11.226 ||
yathā yathā naro ‘dharmaṃ svayaṃ kṛtvānubhāṣate |
tathā tathā tvacevāhis tenādharmeṇa mucyate || 11.227 ||
yathā yatheti | manuṣyaḥ pāpaṃ kṛtvā tena pāpena svayam eva yathā yathā khyāpayati mayedaṃ duṣṭaṃ karma kṛtam iti nindati tathā tathā taṃ śarīrasthaṃ kṣetrajñaṃ puṇyapāpayoḥ tadāśrayatvāt tena pāpena jīrṇacarmeva sarpas tasmāt pāpāt pramucyate iti khyāpanārthavādaḥ
|| 11.227 ||
yathā yathā manas tasya duṣkṛtaṃ karma garhati |
tathā tathā śarīraṃ tat tenādharmeṇa mucyate || 11.228 ||
yathā yatheti | tathā tathā śarīrāt tat tenādharmeṇa mucyate || 11.228 ||
kṛtvā pāpaṃ hi saṃtapya tasmāt pāpāt pramucyate |
naivaṃ kuryāṃ punar iti nivṛttyā pūyate tu saḥ || 11.229 ||
pāpaṃ kṛtveti | pāpakāriṇo yadi paścāttāpa utpadyate tadā tasmāt pāpāt pramucyate ity uktam | sa ca pāpakārī bhūyaḥ pāpaṃ na kuryām ity evaṃ punaḥ pāpakāriṇo nivṛttisaṃkalpena śuddhyatīti nivṛtte prakāśāprakāśaprāyaścittāṅgabhūtavidhānārtham etat || 11.229 ||
evaṃ saṃcintya manasā pretya karmaphalodayam |
manovāṅmūrttibhir nityaṃ śubhaṃ karma samācaret || 11.230 ||
evam iti | evaṃ śubhāśubhānāṃ karmaṇāṃ paraloka iṣṭaphalotpattiṃ manasā vicārya manovākkāyaiḥ śubham eva karma kuryāt | iṣṭaphaltvān nāśubham akṛtaprāyaścittānām adṛṣṭotpādanatvāt prāyaścittakaraṇasyāpi ca duḥkharūpatvāt || 11.230 ||
ajñānād yadi vā jñānāt kṛtvā karma vigarhitam |
tasmād vimuktam anvicchan dvitīyaṃ na samācaret || 11.231 ||
ajñānād iti | pramādād icchāto vā ninditaṃ karma kṛtvā tatpāpān mokṣam anvicchan paraṃ duṣkṛtaṃ na kuryāt | iti nivṛttyarthavādaḥ || 11.231 ||
yasmin karmaṇy asya kṛte manasaḥ syād alāghavam |
tasmiṃs tāvat tapaḥ kuryād yāvat tuṣṭikaraṃ bhavet || 11.232 ||
yasmin karmaṇy asukṛta iti | yasmin duṣkṛtakarmaṇi kṛte ‘pi śāstritaprāyaścitte mano viciktsyāt tasmiṃs tāvat prāyaścittaṃ tad eva punaḥ kuryād anyad vā surūpaṃ yāvan manaḥ prasādaparaṃ syāt || 11.232 ||
tapomūlam idaṃ sarvaṃ daivamānuṣakaṃ sukham |
tapomadhyaṃ budhaiḥ proktaṃ tapo’ntaṃ vedadarśibhiḥ || 11.233 ||
tapomūlam iti | yad etat sarvadevānāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ sukhaṃ tadādau madhye ‘nte ca tapaḥ kāraṇam iti tapomāhātmyakathanaṃ prāyaścittatapaḥstutyartham || 11.233 ||
brāhmaṇasya tapo jñānaṃ tapaḥ kṣatrasya raṣaṇam |
vaiśyasya tu tapo vārtā tapaḥ śūdrasya sevanam || 11.234 ||
brāhmaṇasyeti | brāhmaṇasya vedārthāvabodhas tapaḥ | kṣatriyasya dharmataḥ prajārakṣaṇaṃ tapaḥ | vaiśyasya kṛṣivāṇijyapāśupālyaṃ tapaḥ | śūdrasya brāhmaṇaparicaryā tapa ity utānām anyeṣāṃ punar ihābhidhānaṃ prāyaścittāṅgatvārtham || 11.234 ||
ṛṣayaḥ saṃyatātmānaḥ phalamūlānilāśanāḥ |
tapasaiva prapaśyanti trailokyaṃ sacarācaram || 11.235 ||
ṛṣaya iti | ṛṣayaḥ vāṅmanaḥkāyopetāḥ phalamūlavāyubhakṣās tapasaiva jaṅgamasthāvarasahitaṃ pṛthvyantarikṣadyurlokātmakaṃ lokatrayam aśeṣam ekadeśasthāḥ santaḥ prakarṣeṇa paśyantīti tapomāhātmyakathanam | prāyaścittatapaḥ stutyartham evam uttaraślokāv api jñeyau || 11.235 ||
auṣadhāny agadā vidyā daivī ca vividhā sthitiḥ |
tapasaiva prasidhyanti tapas teṣāṃ hi sādhanam || 11.236 ||
auṣadhānīti | auṣadhāni vyādhyupaśamanāny agadāni viṣāpahāradravyāṇi vidyā tapasā brahmavijijñāsā ceti śruter ātmajñānarūpāṇi devasaṃbandhinī ca nānārūpā svargādāvasthitir ity etāni tapasaiva prāpyante | tasmāt tapas teṣāṃ prāptinimittam || 11.236 ||
yad dustaraṃ yad durāpaṃ yad durgaṃ yac ca duṣkaram |
sarvaṃ tat tapasā sādhyaṃ tapo hi duratikramam || 11.237 ||
yad iti | yad duḥkhenottiryate grahadausthyāpadādi yac ca duḥkhena prāpyate tenaiva śarīreṇa brāhmaṇādi yac ca duḥkhena gamyate merupṛṣṭhādi yac ca duḥkhena kriyate samudrapānādi etat sarvaṃ tapasā sādhayituṃ śakyaṃ yasmād atiduṣkarakāryakaraṇe tapo ‘pratihataśaktiḥ
|| 11.237 ||
mahāpātakinaś caiva śeṣāś cākāryakāriṇaḥ |
tapasaiva sutaptena mucyante kilbiṣāt tataḥ || 11.238 ||
mahāpātakina iti | brahmahatyādikāriṇo ‘nye copapātakādikartāras tapasaivoktarūpeṇa suṣṭhukṛtena tasmāt pāpāt pramucyante | ityuktasyāpi prāyaścittatapaḥstutyarthaṃ vacanam
|| 11.238 ||
kīṭāś cāhipataṅgāś ca paśavaś ca vayāṃsi ca |
sthāvarāṇi ca bhūtāni divaṃ yānti tapobalāt || 11.239 ||
kīṭā iti | kīṭasarpaśalabhapaśupakṣiṇaḥ sthāvarāṇi ca vṛkṣagulmādīni bhūtāni tapomāhātmyena svargaṃ gacchanti yathetihāseṣu kīṭopākhyānādau darśitam | tapomāhātmyakathanena prāyaścittatapaḥstutir evam uttaraślokā vijñeyāḥ || 11.239 ||
yat kiṃcid enaḥ kurvanti manovākkarmabhir janāḥ |
tat sarvaṃ nirdahanty āśu tapasaiva tapodhanāḥ || 11.240 ||
yat kiṃcid iti | mānasavācikakāyikaṃ yat kiṃcit pāpaṃ puruṣāṇāṃ bhavati ye prāyaścittaṃ vinā teṣāṃ nivartante tac cedam anavaratatapaḥpravṛttānāṃ kathaṃcid utpadyante tathā vinaiva prāyaścittaṃ kṛtenaiva nityapravṛttena tapasā teṣāṃ tat sarvaṃ kṣipraṃ naśyati || 11.240 ||
tapasaiva viśuddhasya brāhmaṇasya divaukasaḥ |
ijyāś ca pratigṛhṇanti kāmān saṃvardhayanti ca || 11.241 ||
tapaseti | prāyaścittatapasā kṣīṇakalmaṣasyaiva brāhmaṇasya puroḍāśādiyāgān devāḥ pratigṛhṇanti abhilaṣitāṃś cārthān puṣṇanti || 11.241 ||
prajāpatir idaṃ śāstraṃ tapasaivāsṛjat prabhuḥ |
tathaiva vedān ṛṣayas tapasā pratipedire || 11.242 ||
prajāpatir iti | hiraṇyagarbhaḥ sakalajanotpattisthitiprabhur api tapaścaraṇapūrvakam evainaṃ grantham akarot | tathā ṛṣayo vasiṣṭhādayaḥ tapasaiva mantradamasaṃpannāḥ || 11.242 ||
yad etat tapaso devā mahābhāgyaṃ pracakṣate |
sarvasyāsya prapaśyantas tapasaḥ puṇyam udbhavam || 11.243 ||
ity evaṃ tapaso devā iti | sarvasyāsya jagato yat śubhaṃ janma tat tapasaḥ sakāśād ity evaṃ devāḥ paśyantaḥ etat tapaso mulam idaṃ sarvam ityādi tapomāhātmyaṃ pravadantīti samastā tapaḥstutiḥ || 11.243 ||
vedābhyāso ‘nvahaṃ śaktyā mahāyajñakriyākṣamā |
nāśayanty āśu pāpāni mahāpātakajāny api || 11.244 ||
vedābhyāsa iti | yathāśakti pratyahaṃ vedādhyayanaṃ pañcamahāyajñānuṣṭhānam aparādhamarṣaṇam ity etāni pātakotpannāni api pāpāni kṣipraṃ nāśayanti kim utānyānīti | nityakarmānuṣṭhānasyāsaṃveditapratanupāpāpakṣapaṇahetutvajñāpanārtham idam || 11.244 ||
yathaidhas tejasā vahniḥ prāptaṃ nirdahati kṣaṇāt |
tathā jñānāgninā pāpaṃ sarvaṃ dahati vedavit || 11.245 ||
yathaidhāṃs tejasā vahniḥ prāptān iti | yathāgniḥ kāṣṭhāni prāpya kṣaṇenaiva tejasā niḥśeṣeṇa dahati tathā jñānāgninā pāpaṃ sarvaṃ dahati vedārthajñaḥ ātmajñānasya pāpāpakṣapaṇahetutvajñāpanārtham etat || 11.245 ||
ity etad enasām uktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ yathāvidhi |
ata ūrdhvaṃ rahasyānāṃ prāyaścittaṃ nibodhata || 11.246 ||
idānīṃ rahasyaprāyaścittāny āha itīti || 11.246 ||
savyāhṛtipraṇavakāḥ prāṇāyāmās tu ṣoḍaśa |
api bhrūṇahaṇaṃ māsāt punanty ahar ahaḥ kṛtāḥ || 11.247 ||
savyāhṛtikā iti | bhūr bhuvaḥ svar mahar janas tapaḥ satyam ity etat samastavyāhṛtyoṃkārasahitāḥ ṣoḍaśaprāṇāyāmāḥ |
savyāhṛtīṃ sapraṇavāṃ gāyatrīṃ śirasā saha |
triḥ paṭhed āyataprāṇaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ sa ucyate ||
iti vasiṣṭhalakṣitāḥ kumbhapūrakarecakavidhānena pratyahaṃ māsaṃ kṛtā brahmahatyāpāpam apy apiśabdāt tatsarvātideśikam apy apanudanti | tatra bāhyābhyantaravāyvoḥ prakoṣṭhapraveśaniṣkramaṇanirodhaḥ kumbhako bāhyena vāyunā koṣṭhaparipūraṇaṃ pūrakaḥ koṣṭhasya vāyor anavaratotsargo recaka iti dvijātīnām eva puṃsāṃ rahasyaprāyaścittādhikāro na tu śūdrastrījapahomaprdhānyāt | rahasyaprāyaścittānām
aviditvā ṛṣicchando daivataṃ yogam eva vā |
yo ‘dhyāpayej japed vāpi pāpīyān jāyate tu saḥ ||
iti vyāsasmaraṇāt prāyaścittaviniyutam arthaṃ devatādi sattvatattvāvabodhamātre vā adhiāro na tv anyatra vidvattopayujyate śrautakarmaparidarśanādiyoge prayogādhikṛto karmāntaragatavijñānopayogo ‘sti || 11.247 ||
kautsaṃ japtvāpa ity etad vāsiṣṭhaṃ ca pratīty ṛcam |
māhitraṃ śuddhvatyaś ca surāpo ‘pi viśudhyati || 11.248 ||
kautsaṃ japtāpa itīti | māhendram iti | kutsena ṛṣiṇā dṛṣṭam apanaḥ śośucad adham ity etat suktaṃ variṣṭhadṛṣṭaṃ ca pratistomebhir ity etasya tṛcaṃ marhā indro ya ojasety etac ca sūktam eto nv indraṃ stavām ety etāś ca śuddhavatya ṛcaḥ prakṛtaṃ māsam ahar ahaḥ ṣoḍaśakṛtvo japitvā surāpo ‘py apiśabdāt tatsāmyādi deśikādhikṛto ‘pi viśudhyati || 11.248 ||
sakṛj japtvāsya vāmīyaṃ śivasaṃkalpam eva ca |
apahṛtya suvarṇaṃ tu kṣaṇād bhavati nirmalaḥ || 11.249 ||
sakṛd iti | brāhmaṇaḥ suvarṇam apahṛtyāsya vāmasyety etat sūktaṃ prakṛtam ahar ahar ekavāraṃ japitvā śivasaṃkalpaṃ ca yaj jāgrato dūram udaiti daivam ity etat japitvā kṣipram eva vipāpo bhavati || 11.249 ||
haviṣyāntīyam abhyasya na tamaṃ ha itīti ca |
japitvā pauruṣaṃ sūktaṃ mucyate gurutalpagaḥ || 11.250 ||
haviṣyāntīyam iti | haviṣyāntam ajaraṃ na tamaṃ ho na duritam iti ca iti me manaḥ sahasraśīrṣā puruṣa ity etāni sūktāni prakṛtaṃ māsaṃ pratyaham abhyāsena japitvā gurudāragas tatpāpam apaharati || 11.250 ||
enasāṃ sthūlasūkṣmāṇāṃ cikīrṣann apanodanam |
avety ṛcaṃ japed abdaṃ yat kiṃcedam itīti vā || 11.251 ||
enasām iti | sthūlānāṃ pāpānāṃ mahāpātakānāṃ nirharaṇaṃ kartum icchan ava te heḷo varuṇa namibhir ity etām ṛcaṃ yat kiṃcedaṃ varuṇadevyai jana ity etāṃ ca saṃvatsaraṃ japet
|| 11.151 ||
pratigṛhyāpratigrāhyaṃ bhutvā cānnaṃ vigarhitam |
japaṃs tarat sa mandīyaṃ pūyate mānavas tryahāt || 11.252 ||
pratigṛhyeti | niṣiddhapratigrahaṃ surādi bhuktvā tarat sa mandī japitvā dvijātir divasatrayeṇa pavitrī bhavati || 11.252 ||
somāraudraṃ tu bahvenāḥ samām abhyasya śudhyati |
sravantyām ācaran snānam aryamṇām iti ca tṛcam || 11.253 ||
somāraudram iti | anena pāpakṛt somā rudrā dhārayethām asuryam ity etat sūktam aryavaṇaṃ varuṇaṃ mitraṃ cety etāṃ ca ṛcaṃ māsam ahar ahar abhyasya nadyāṃ snānaṃ kurvan śudhyati
|| 11.253 ||
abdārtham indram ity etad enasvī saptakaṃ japet |
apraśastaṃ tu kṛtvāpsu māsam āsīta bhaikṣabhuk || 11.254 ||
abdārtham iti | avaśeṣitatvāt sarvapāpānām anyatamakārī indraṃ mitram ity etat saptakaṃ ṣaṇmāsān japet maithunaṃ codakamadhye kṛtvā māsaṃ bhaikṣāśī syāt || 11.255 ||
mantraiḥ śākalahomīyair abdaṃ hutvā ghṛtaṃ dvijaḥ |
sugurv apy apahanty eno japtvā vā nama ity ṛcam || 11.255 ||
mantrair iti | devakṛtasyainaso ‘vayajanam asītyādibhiḥ kāṣṭhaśakalahomaviniyuktair mantraiḥ saṃvatsaraṃ ghṛtahomaṃ kṛtvā nama id ugram ity etāṃ ṛcaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ japitvā mahāpātakam apy apanudati || 11.255 ||
mahāpātakasaṃyukto ‘nugacched gāḥ samāhitaḥ |
abhyasyābdaṃ pāvamānīr bhaikṣāhāro viśudhyati || 11.256 ||
mahāpātakasaṃyukta iti | mahāpātakavān saṃvatsaraṃ bhaikṣyabhuk pāvamānīḥ svādiṣṭhayetyādyā ṛco ‘bhyasya gavām anugamanaṃ ca govadhavratotaprariyayā saṃyātaḥ kurvan viśudhyati || 11.256 ||
araṇye vā trir abhyasya prayato vedasaṃhitām |
mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvaiḥ parākaiḥ śodhitas tribhiḥ || 11.257 ||
araṇya iti | yad vā parākatrayeṇāpanītapāpaḥ san aṭavyāṃ trīn vārān vedasaṃhitāṃ saṃyamavān āvartya sarvāṇi pātakāni mahāpātakopapātakādīny api hanti || 11.257 ||
tryahaṃ tūpavased yuktas trir ahno ‘bhyupayann apaḥ |
mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvais trir japitvāghamarṣaṇam || 11.258 ||
tryaham iti | triṣavaṇaṃ snānaṃ kurvan pratisnānaṃ jalamadhye trīn vārān ṛtaṃ ca satyaṃ cābhīddhād ity etat sūktam japan samāhitas tryaham upavased evaṃ kurvan sarvair mahāpātakādibhiḥ pāpair mucyate || 11.258 ||
yathāśvamedhaḥ kraturāṭ sarvapāpāpanodanaḥ |
tathāghamarṣaṇaṃ sūktaṃ sarvapāpāpanodanaḥ || 11.259 ||
yatheti | yathāśvamedho yajñaśreṣṭhaḥ sarvapāpavināśakṛd evam aghamarṣaṇam api sūktaṃ sakalaṃ kalmaṣanāśanam iti pūrvārthavādaḥ || 11.259 ||
hatvā lokān apīmāṃs trīn aśnnn api yatas tataḥ |
ṛgvedaṃ dhārayan vipro nainaḥ prāpnoti kiṃcan || 11.260 ||
hatveti | lokatrayāntarvarti sakalabhūtajātam api hatvā cāṇḍālāder api sakāśād aśnan ṛgvedam abhyasya na kiṃcid api pāpaṃ prāpnoti | ity ṛgvedābhyāsastutir uttarārtham || 11.260 ||
ṛksaṃhitāṃ trir abhyasya yajuṣāṃ vā samāhitaḥ |
sāmnāṃ vā sarahasyānāṃ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 11.261 ||
ṛksaṃhitām iti | ṛgvedasaṃhitāṃ yajurvedasaṃhitāṃ vā sāmnāṃ vā sāraṇyakādhītasāmasaṃhitānāṃ sāmasāmārcikaṃ trīn vārān saṃyamavān āvartya sarvapāpāny apahanti || 11.261 ||
yathā mahāhradaṃ prāpya kṣiptaṃ loṣṭaṃ vinaśyati |
tathā duścaritaṃ sarvaṃ vede trivṛti majjati || 11.262 ||
yatheti | yathā bṛhatsarasi mṛlloṣṭaṃ nipatyaiva vināśam ety evaṃ sarvaṃ duṣkṛtam ṛgyajuḥsāmātmakatvena tryavayavair vede ‘ntaṃ nīyata iti pūrvārthavādaḥ || 11.262 ||
ṛco yajūṃṣi cādyāni sāmāni vividhāni ca |
eṣa jñeyas trivṛdvedo yo vedainaṃ sa vedavit || 11.263 ||
trivṛttvaṃ vedasya nirūpayitum āha ṛco yajūṃṣi cādyānīti | ṛco yajūṃṣi cāmukhyāni saṃhitāgatāni na tu brāhmaṇam apy adhītāni sāmāni cārcikāstomikādibhedenānekaprakārāṇīty eṣa trivṛdvedo boddhavyo yaś cainaṃ vedam evaṃ trivṛtaṃ veda sa vedavin nānyaḥ || 11.263 ||
ādyaṃ yat tryakṣaraṃ brahma trayī yasmin pratiṣṭhitā |
sa guhyo ‘nyas trivṛdvedo yas taṃ veda sa vedavit || 11.264 ||
aparaṃ trivṛttvaṃ vedasya darśayitum āha ādyam iti | akārokāramakārākṣaratrayasamāhārarūpeṇoṃkārākhyaṃ brahma parabrahmavācakatvāt yasminn oṃkāre ṛgyajuḥsāmātmikātrayyāśritā yathā śaṅkunā sarvāṇi parṇāni saṃvarṇāny evam oṃkāre sarvā vācaḥ saṃvarṇā iti śruteḥ | sa rahasyarūpo paras trivṛdvedo yaś cainaṃ veda ya enaṃ trivṛtaṃ vetti sa vedavit || 11.264 ||
iti śrībhaṭṭamādhavātmajagovindarājaviracitāyāṃ
manvāśayānusāriṇyāṃ manuṭīkāyāṃ caturṇāṃ jātīnām
ācārādhārarūpiṇyāṃ ekādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ ||
atha dvādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
cāturvarṇyasya kṛtsno ‘yam ukto dharmas tvayā ‘nagha |
karmaṇāṃ phalanirvṛttiṃ śaṃsa nas tattvataḥ parām || 12.1 ||
cāturvarṇyasyeti | he apāpa brāhmaṇādivarṇacatuṣṭayasya sāntaraprabhavasyaiṣo ‘khilo dharmas tvayābhihitaḥ | adhunā karmaṇāṃ śubhāśubhānāṃ phalaprāptiṃ janmāntarasaṃbhavānāṃ paramārthenāsmākaṃ brūhīti ṛṣiṃ maharṣayo bhṛgum āhuḥ || 12.1 ||
sa tān uvāca dharmātmā maharṣīn mānavo bhṛguḥ |
asya sarvasya śṛṇuta karmayogasya nirṇayam || 12.2 ||
sa iti | sa manvapatyaṃ dharmapradhāno bhṛguḥ asya sarvasya karmabandhasya phalaniścayaṃ śṇuteti tān maharṣīn āha || 12.2 ||
śubhāśubhaphalaṃ karma manovāgdehasaṃbhavam |
karmajā gatayo nṝṇām uttamādhamamadhyamāḥ || 12.3 ||
śubhāśubhaphalam iti | manovākkāyanimittaṃ saṃkalpābhidhānakriyātmakaṃ yat karma vihitapratiṣiddharūpaṃ sukhaduḥkhaphalaṃ tat karmahetukāś ca devamanuṣyatiryagādibhāvenotkṛṣṭamadhyamāpakṛṣṭāḥ manuṣyāṇāṃ janmāntaraprāptayo bhavanti || 12.3 ||
tasyeha tridhasyāpi tryadhiṣṭhānasya dehinaḥ |
daśalakṣaṇayuktasya mano vidyāt pravartakam || 12.4 ||
tasyeti | tasya śarīrasaṃbandhinaḥ karmaṇaḥ utkṛṣṭamadhyamajaghanyarūpatayā triḥprakārasyāpi manovākkāyāśritasya vakṣyamāṇena daśalakṣaṇenaitasya karaṇe manaḥ pravartayitṛ jānīyān na hi manasā akalpitaṃ kiṃcit kartuṃ vaktuṃvā śakyate || 12.4 ||
paradravyeṣv abhidhyānaṃ manasāniṣṭacintanam |
vitathābhiniveśaś ca trividhaṃ karma mānasam || 12.5 ||
evaṃ daśalakṣaṇatvaṃ karmaṇā darśayitum āha paradravyeṣv iti | kena prakāreṇa paradhanāny apahareyam ity evaṃ manasā cānyasya vināśākāṅkṣā nāsti śāstritaṃ karmaphalam ity evaṃ durgrahaś cety evaṃ triprakāram aśubhaphalaṃ mānasaṃ karma | etadviparītaṃ paradhanāspṛhā dayā śāśtritakarmaphalāstitvabuddhir ity etac chubhaphalaṃ śubhāśubhakarmety ubhayasya prastutatvāt || 12.5 ||
pāruṣyam anṛtaṃ caiva paiśunyaṃ cāpi sarvaśaḥ |
asaṃbaddhapralāpaś ca vāṅmayaṃ syāc caturvidham || 12.6 ||
pāruṣyam iti | apriyābhidhānam asatyabhāṣaṇaṃ sarvatra vaktratvaṃ bāhyasyāsatyasyāpi niṣprayojanam abhidhānam ity evaṃ catuḥprakāram aśubhaphalaṃ vācikaṃ karma syāt tadviparītaṃ priyaṃ satyaṃ hitaṃ śāstrābhyāsa ity etat śubhaphalam || 12.6 ||
adattānām upādānaṃ hiṃsā caivāvidhānataḥ |
paradāropasevā ca śārīraṃ trividhaṃ smṛtam || 12.7 ||
adattānām iti | parasvaharaṇam aśāstritahiṃsā paradāragamanam ity evaṃ triḥprakāram aśubhaphalaṃ śārīram | tadviparītaṃ dānaṃ paritrāṇam indriyasaṃyamaś cety etac chubhaphalam || 12.7 ||
mānasaṃ manasaivāyam upabhuṅkte śubhāśubham |
vācā vācā kṛtaṃ karma kāyenaiva ca kāyikam || 12.8 ||
mānasam iti | manasā yac chubham aśubhaṃ vā karma kṛtaṃ tat phalaṃ karmāntaravaśena rātryāṃ jāto jātas tasyā manodvāreṇa sukhaṃ duḥkharūpaṃ kṣipram upabhuṅkte | evaṃ vācikaṃ śubhāśubhaphalaṃ ca śarīradvāreṇaiva mṛduvyādhitvādinānubhavati || 12.8 ||
śarīrajaiḥ karmadoṣair yāti sthāvaratāṃ naraḥ |
vācikaiḥ pakṣimṛgatāṃ mānasair antyajātitām || 12.9 ||
śarīrajair iti | śvaśūkarakharoṣṭrāṇām ityādinā śārīravācikamānasānāṃ karmaṇāṃ yo ‘bhyantaraprāptiphalatvam api vakṣyata idaṃ sa yadi prāyaśo dharmaṃ sevate | dharmam alpaśaḥ duritabāhulyābhiprāyeṇa vyākhyeyam | bāhulyena śārīrakarmapāpaiḥ kṛtaiḥ sthāvaratvaṃ manuṣyaḥ prāpnoti | bāhulyena vākkṛtaiḥ pakṣitvaṃ mṛgatvam | bāhulyena manaḥkṛtaiś cāṇḍālāditvaṃ prāpnoti || 12.9 ||
vāgdaṇḍo ‘tha manodaṇḍaḥ kāyadaṇḍas tathaiva ca |
yasyaite nihitā vṛddhau tridaṇḍīti sa ucyate || 12.10 ||
vāgdaṇḍa iti | yasya vāṅmanaḥkāyikānām aśubhānāṃ saṃkalpavyāpāraparihāreṇa buddhāv avasthitaṃ sa tridaṇḍī ucyate, na tu daṇḍatrayasādhāraṇamātreṇaiva || 12.10 ||
tridaṇḍam etan nikṣipya sarvabhūteṣu mānavaḥ |
kāmakrodhau susaṃyamya tataḥ siddhiṃ nigacchati || 12.11 ||
tridaṇḍam iti |
vāgdaṇḍo hanti vijñānaṃ manodaṇḍaṃ parāṃ gatim |
karmadaṇḍas tu lokāṃs trīn hanyād aparirakṣataḥ ||
vāgdaṇḍe maunam ātiṣṭhet karmadaṇḍe tv anaśnatām |
mānasasya tu daṇḍasya prāṇāyāmo vidhīyate ||
tridaṇḍaṃ dhārayed yogī śārīraṃ na tu vaiṇavam |
vācikaṃ kāyiaṃ caiva mānasaṃ ca yathāvidhi ||
ity eṣāṃ vāgādīnām aśubhānāṃ sarvabhūtaviṣayaṃ manaḥ saṃyamanaṃ kṛtvā kāmakrodhau cet taddamanārthaṃ manuṣyo ‘pi niyamya śubhaphalāvāptiṃ jātaḥ na tu śarīram eva karmāṇi kurvan na paśyāmaḥ | tac ca naśvaram ataḥ ko ‘sau siddhiṃ prāpnotīty āha || 12.11 ||
yo ‘syātmanaḥ kārayitvā taṃ kṣetrajñaṃ pracakṣate |
yaḥ karoti tu karmāṇi sa bhūtātmocyate budhaiḥ || 12.12 ||
ya iti | yasya lokaprasiddhātmanaḥ śarīrākhyasya yaḥ karmapravartayitā taṃ kṣetrajñaṃ vidvāṃsaḥ kathayanti yaḥ punar eṣa vyāpāraṃ karoti | śarīrākhyaṃ sa pṛthivyādibhūtamayatvāt bhūtātmeti vidvadbhir ucyate || 12.12 ||
jīvasaṃjño ‘ntarātmānyaḥ sahajaḥ sarvadehinām |
yena vedayate sarvaṃ sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ ca janmasu || 12.13 ||
jīvasaṃjña iti | śarīrakṣetrajñavyatirikto ‘ntaḥśarīre ‘nya ātmārthatvād ātmā jīvākhyaḥ sarvakṣetrajñānāṃ sahaja ā mokṣaprāptes te ca teṣām adhiyogatvena kāraṇabhūtena kṣetrajñaḥ pratijanma sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ cānubhavaty etac cātra mano jīvaśabdena nirdiṣṭam | yeneti karaṇavibhaktinirdeśād uttaraśloke ca tāv ubhau manān kṣetrajña eva ceti mahacchabdena pratyakkarmaṇā |
buddhir mano’rthavijñānaṃ vikāraḥ prathamas tathā |
paryāyavācakāḥ śabdā mahataḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||
iti mahātmanaḥ paryāyatvāt || 12.13 ||
tāv ubhau bhūtasaṃpṛktau mahān kṣetrajña eva ca |
uccāvaceṣu bhūteṣu sthitaṃ taṃ vyāpya tiṣṭhataḥ || 12.14 ||
tāv iti | tau dvau manaḥkṣetrajñau pṛthivyāpastejovāyvākāśākhyabhūtasaṃyuktau paramātmānaṃ vakṣyamāṇam utkṛṣṭāvakṛṣṭeṣu vyavasthitaṃ vyāpya tiṣṭhataḥ paramātmā vā sarvasya vyāpyatvāt taṃ vyāpya tiṣṭhata ity ucyate | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
dvāv imau puruṣau loke kṣaraś cākṣara eva ca |
kṣaraḥ sarvāṇi bhūtāni kūṭastho ‘kṣara ucyate ||
uttamaḥ puruṣas tv anyaḥ paramātmety udāhṛtaḥ |
yo lokatrayam āviśya bibharty avyaya īśvaraḥ ||
akṣaraḥ kṣetrajñaḥ kūṭasthaḥ tvaṃ ca tasyā mokṣaprāpteḥ kartṛbhoktṛtvānivṛttyā || 12.14 ||
asaṃkhyā mūrtayas tasya niṣpatanti śarīrataḥ |
uccāvacāni bhūtāni satataṃ ceṣṭayanti yāḥ || 12.15 ||
asaṃkhyā iti | tasya paramātmanaḥ śarīrāt vedāntoktaprakāreṇāvidyāvaśād vāteritād udadher ūrmaya ivānantāḥ kṣetrajñāt prādurbhavanti | ye kṣetrajñād utkṛṣṭāpakṛṣṭāni śarīrāṇi sarvadā karmasu pravartayanti || 12.15 ||
pañcabhya eva bhūtebhyaḥ pretya duṣkṛtināṃ nṛṇām |
śarīraṃ yātanārthīym anyad utpadyate dhruvam || 12.16 ||
pañcabhya iti | śarīraṃ yātanārthīyam anyad utpadyate dṛḍham | duṣkṛtikāriṇāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ pañcabhya eva pṛthivyādibhūtabhāgebhyaḥ mātrākhyebhyo ‘nyaj jarāyujādiśarīravyatiriktaṃ śarīraṃ pīḍāprayojanotpannaṃ dṛḍhaṃ paraloka ucyate || 12.16 ||
tenānubhūya tā yāmīḥ śarīreṇeha yātanāḥ |
tāsv eva bhūtamātrāsu pralīyante vibhāgaśaḥ || 12.17 ||
teneti | tena śarīreṇa tā yamasaṃbandhinyo yātanā duṣkṛtivatānubhūtā na bhavanti | tac charīrārambhakāṇi pṛthivyādīni bhūtāni teṣv eva bhūtabhāgeṣu tanmātrākhyeṣu yathā svaṃ pralīyante || 12.17 ||
so ‘nubhūyāsukhodarkān doṣān viṣayasaṅgajān |
vyapetakalmaṣo ‘bhyeti tāv evobhau mahaujasau || 12.18 ||
sa iti | sa kṣetrajño ‘sukhodayāni pratiśabdasparśarūparasagandhākhyaviṣayopabhogajāni yamaloke duḥkhāny anubhūyānantaram apahatapāpmā tāv eva mahatparamātmānau mahāvīryau dvāv āśrayati || 12.18 ||
tau dharmaṃ paśyantas tasya pāpaṃ cātandritau saha |
yābhyāṃ prāpnoti saṃpṛktaḥ pretyeha ca sukhāsukam || 12.19 ||
tāv iti | tau mahatparamātmānāv eva trastau tasya kṣetrajñasya dharmādharmau saha vicārato yābhyāṃ yukto kṣetrajñaḥ paralokehalokayoḥ sukhaduḥkhaṃ prāpnoti || 12.19 ||
yathācarati dharmaṃ sa prāyaśo ‘dharmam alpaśaḥ |
tair eva cāvṛto bhūtaiḥ svarge sukham upāśnute || 12.20 ||
yathācaratīti | sa kṣetrajño yadi bāhulyena dharmam anutiṣṭhati svalpam adharmaṃ tadā tair eva pṛthivyādibhūtaiḥ uktaḥ pāñcabhautikaśarīraḥ svarge sukham anubhavati || 12.20 ||
yadi tu prāyaśo ‘dharmaṃ sevate dharmam alpaśaḥ |
tair bhūtaiḥ sa parityakto yāmīḥ prāpnoti yātanāḥ || 12.21 ||
yadīti | yadi punar asau kṣetrajño bāhulyenādharmam anutiṣṭhati svalpaṃ dharmaṃ tadā tair aihikaśarīrārambhakair bhūtaiḥ parityaktaḥ pañcabhya eva mātrābhya ityādyuktanītyā kaṭhinaśarīro yāmyā yātanāḥ prāpnoti | etenaitad uktaṃ bhavati | adharmabāhulyena yāmyā yātanā anubhavati | adharmabāhulyena punar dharmaphalaṃ paraloke ‘nubhūyādharmaphalam ihaloke evopabhuṅkta iti || 12.21 ||
yāmīs tā yātanāḥ prāpya sa jīvo vītakalmaṣaḥ |
tāny eva pañcabhūtāni punar apy eti bhāgaśaḥ || 12.22 ||
yāmīr iti | sa kṣetrajñaḥ tā yāmyā yātanās tena krūraśarīreṇānubhūya tato ‘pahatapāpmanānye ye jarāyujādiśarīrārambhakān pṛthivyādibhūtabhāgān punar apy ety aihikaṃ śarīraṃ gṛhṇātīty arthaḥ || 12.22 ||
etā dṛṣṭvāsya jīvasya gatīḥ svenaiva cetasā |
dharmato ‘dharmataś caiva dharme dadhyāt sadā manaḥ || 12.23 ||
etā iti | asya kṣetrajñasyaitā dharmahetukāḥ svargādāv iṣṭāniṣṭaśarīraprāptīḥ svabuddhyā jñātvā dharmānuṣṭhāne jānīyād yair yukto mahātmā imān sarvān sthāvarajaṅgamarūpān padārthān niḥśeṣeṇa vyāpya sthitaḥ sarvagatvāt || 12.23 ||
sattvaṃ rajas tamaś caiva trīn vidyād ātmano guṇān |
yair vyāpyemān sthito bhāvān mahān sarvān aśeṣataḥ || 12.24 ||
sattvam iti | sattvarajastamāṃsi trīṇi vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇāny ātmadharmān jānīyād yair ukto mahātmā imān sarvān sthāvarajaṅgamarūpān padārthān niḥśeṣeṇa vyāpya sthitaḥ sarvagatatvāt
|| 12.24 ||
yo ydaiṣāṃ guṇo dehe sākalyenātiricyate |
sa tadā tadguṇaprāyaṃ taṃ karoti śarīriṇam || 12.25 ||
ya iti | eṣāṃ sattvādīnāṃ madhyād yo guṇaḥ pūrvakarmavaśena yasmin kāle ‘tiśayena śarīre adhiko bhavati sa guṇas tasmin kāle taṃ kṣetrajñaṃ taṃ vaśavartinaṃ karoti | tataś ca tatsārdham evādharma pravartate || 12.25 ||
sattvaṃ jñānaṃ tamo ‘jñānaṃ rāgadveṣau rajaḥ smṛtam |
etad vyāptimad eteṣāṃ sarvabhūtāśritaṃ vapuḥ || 12.26 ||
idānīṃ sattvādīnāṃ lakṣaṇam āha sattvam iti | yathāvad vastusvabhāvo jñānaṃ tat sattvam | etad viparītam ajñānaṃ tat tamaḥ viṣayābhilāṣamāna sa sarve raja ime (?) sa sarvaprāṇyavacchinna eṣāṃ sattvādīnāṃ vyabhicārī svabhāvaḥ || 12.26 ||
tatra yat prītisaṃyuktaṃ kiṃcid ātmani lakṣayet |
praśāntam iva śuddhābhaṃ sattvaṃ tad upadhārayet || 12.27 ||
anyāny apy evaṃlakṣaṇāny āha tatreti | tasminn ātmani vinaiva prītihetunā yat saṃvedanaṃ prītiyuktaṃ astamitakleśam aprakāśarūpam anubhavet tat sattvaṃ jānīyat || 12.27 ||
yat tu duḥkhasamāyuktam aprītikaram ātmanaḥ |
tad rajo ‘pratipaṃ vidyāt satataṃ hartṛ dehinām || 12.28 ||
yad iti | yat punaḥ saṃvedanaṃ duḥkhāt vidvanmana eva sattvavac chuddhaye ātmaprīter ajanakaṃ sarvadā ca śarīriṇaṃ viṣayaspṛhotpādakaṃ tad durnivāratvād rajonavidyamānapratipakṣe rajo jānīyāt || 12.28 ||
yat tu syān mohasaṃyuktam avyaktaṃ viṣayātmakam |
apratarkyam avijñeyaṃ tamas tad upadhārayet || 12.29 ||
yad iti | yat punaḥ sadasadvivekaśūnyam aspaṣṭaviṣayākārāsatsvabhāvam atarkaṇīyasvarūpam antaḥkaraṇābhyāṃ vijñātum aśakyaṃ tat tamo jānīyād evaṃ ca svarūpāvadhāraṇaprayojanaṃ sattvavṛttyavasthitau yatnavān syād iti || 12.29 ||
trayāṇām api caiteṣāṃ guṇānāṃ yaḥ phalodayaḥ |
agryo madhyo jaghanyaś ca taṃ pravakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ || 12.30 ||
trayāṇām iti | eṣāṃ sattvādīnāṃ trayāṇām api yathākramam utkṛṣṭamadhyamanikṛṣṭakāryotpattis tāṃ niḥśeṣeṇa vakṣyāmi || 12.30 ||
vedābhyāsas tapo jñānaṃ śaucam indriyanigrahaḥ |
dharmakriyātmacintā ca sāttvikaṃ guṇalakṣaṇam || 12.31 ||
vedābhyāsa iti | vedābhyāsakṛcchrādyācaraṇaṃ śāstrāvabodho mṛdvāriśaucam indriyasaṃyamo dānādidharmānuṣṭhānaṃ cātmadhyānam ity etat sattvākhyaguṇasaṃbandhikāryam || 12.31 ||
ārambharucitādharyam asatkāryaparigrahaḥ |
viṣayopasevā cājasraṃ rājasaṃ guṇalakṣaṇam || 12.32 ||
ārambharuciteti | phalārthaṃ karmārambhāyāsakṛttā svalpe ‘py anarthe vaiklavyaṃ śāstraviruddhakarmācaraṇam anavarataṃ śabdādiviṣayopabhoga ity etat rajo’bhidhānaguṇasaṃbandhikāryam || 12.32 ||
lobhaḥ svapno ‘ghṛtiḥ krauryaṃ nāstikyaṃ bhinnavṛttitā |
yāciṣṇutā pramādaś ca tāmasaṃ guṇalakṣaṇam || 12.33 ||
lobha iti | arthaparatvaṃ nidrālutā kātaryaṃ nairghṛṇyaṃ dharmādyabhāvabuddhir ācāralopo yācanaśīlatvaṃ dharmādiṣv anavadhānam ity etat tamo’bhidhānaguṇakāryam || 12.33 ||
trayāṇām api caiteṣāṃ guṇānāṃ triṣu tiṣṭhatām |
idaṃ sāmāsikaṃ jñeyaṃ kramaśo guṇalakṣaṇam || 12.34 ||
trayāṇām iti | eṣāṃ sattvādīnāṃ trayāṇām api guṇānāṃ triṣu kāleṣu bhūtabhavyabhaviṣyadvartamāneṣu vartamānānām idaṃ vakṣyamāṇasāpekṣikaṃ krameṇa guṇalakṣaṇaṃ vijñeyam || 12.34 ||
yat karma kṛtvā kurvaṃś ca kariṣyaṃś caiva lajjati |
taj jñeyaṃ viduṣā sarvaṃ tāmasaṃ guṇalakṣaṇam || 12.35 ||
yat karma kṛtvā kurvaṃś ca kariṣyaṃś caiva lajjata iti | yat karmātītavartamānānāgatakālānām anyatamam asmin kāle dvayor vā kālayoḥ sarveṣu kāleṣu kartur lajjāṃ janayati tat sarvaṃ śāstravidā tamaḥkāryatvāt tamo’bhidhānaguṇalakṣaṇaṃ boddhavyam || 12.35 ||
yenāsmin karmaṇā loke khyātim icchanti puṣkalām |
na ca śocaty asaṃpattau tad vijñeyaṃ tu rājasam || 12.36 ||
yeneti | iha loke mahatīṃ kīrtiṃ prāpnuyām ity evam artham eva yat karma karoti na ca paralokārthaṃ na ca tatkarmasaṃpattau duḥkhī bhavati tad rajaḥkāryaṃ vijñeyam || 12.36 ||
yat sarveṇecchati jñātuṃ yan na lajjati cācaran |
yena tuṣyati cātmāsya tat sattvaguṇalakṣaṇam || 12.37 ||
yad iti | yat sarvātmanā jñānaviṣayā icchā bhavati yac ca karma kurvan kālatraye ‘pi na lajjati yena karmaṇācaryamāṇenāsya tuṣṭir jāyate tat sattvākhyasya guṇasya lakṣaṇam || 12.37 ||
tamaso lakṣaṇaṃ kāmo rajasas tv artha ucyate |
sattvasya lakṣaṇaṃ dharmaḥ śraiṣṭhyam eṣāṃ yathottaram || 12.38 ||
tamasa iti | sattvasya lakṣaṇaṃ dharmaḥ śraiṣṭhyam eṣāṃ yathākramam | kāmapradhānatā tamaso lakṣaṇaṃ dharmaniṣṭhatā sattvalakṣaṇam eṣāṃ ca kāmādīnāṃ parasparaśreṣṭhatvam | kāmād arthaḥ śreyān arthamūlatvāt kāmasya tayor alpo ‘pi dharmaḥ pradhānaṃ sattvāt tanmūlāt
|| 12.38 ||
yena yāṃs tu guṇenaiṣāṃ saṃsārān pratipadyate |
tān samāsena vakṣyāmi sarvasyāsya yathākramam || 12.39 ||
yeneti | eṣāṃ sattvādīnāṃ guṇānāṃ madhyād yena guṇena sevyamānena yā gatīḥ puruṣaḥ prāpnoti tāḥ sarvasya jagato gatīḥ saṃkṣepataḥ krameṇa vakṣyāmi || 12.39 ||
devatvaṃ sāttvikā yānti manuṣyatvaṃ ca rājasāḥ |
tiryaktvaṃ tāmasā nityam ity eṣā trividhā gatiḥ || 12.40 ||
devatvam iti | ye sattvavṛttyavasthitās te devatvaṃ prāpnuvanti | ye punā rajovṛttisthās te manuṣyatvaṃ ye tamovṛttisthās te nityaṃ tiryaktvam iti || 12.40 ||
trividhā trividhaiṣā tu vijñeyā gauṇikī gatiḥ |
adhamā madhyamāgryā ca karmavidyā viśeṣataḥ || 12.41 ||
trividheti | yeyaṃ sattvādiguṇanimittā trividhā gatiḥ uktā sā deśakālādibhedena saṃsārahetubhūtakarmabhedāt karmakarmāṅgabhūtadevatādiviśeṣāc cottamamadhyamabhedena punas trividhā boddhavyā || 12.41 ||
sthāvarāḥ kṛmikīṭāś ca matsyāḥ sarpāḥ sakacchapāḥ |
paśavaś ca mṛgāś caiva jaghanyā tāmasī gatiḥ || 12.42 ||
sthāvarā iti | sthāvarā vṛkṣādayaḥ kṛmayaḥ sūkṣmā bhūśaraṇāḥ prāṇinas tebhya eva sthūlāḥ kīṭās tathā matsyasarpakūrmapaśumṛgā ity eṣāṃ tamonimittā nikṛṣṭā gatiḥ || 12.42 ||
hastinaś ca turaṅgāś ca śūdrā mlecchāś ca garhitāḥ |
siṃhā vyāghrā varāhāś ca madhyamā tāmasī gatiḥ || 12.43 ||
hastina iti | hastyaśvaśūdramlecchasiṃhavyāghraśūkara ity etā tamonimittā madhyamā gatiḥ garhitā iti mlecchānāṃ svarūpānuvādaḥ || 12.43 ||
cāraṇāś ca suparṇāś ca puruṣāś caiva dāmbhikāḥ |
rakṣāṃsi ca piśācāś ca tāmasīṣūttamā gatiḥ || 12.44 ||
cāraṇā iti | cāraṇā naṭādayaḥ suparṇo garutmākhyaḥ pakṣiviśeṣo vyājadharmakāriṇaś ca puruṣā rakṣaḥpriśācā ity eṣā tāmaseṣu munuṣyeṣūtkṛṣṭā gatiḥ || 12.44 ||
jhallā mallā naṭāś caiva puruṣāḥ śastravṛttayaḥ |
dyūtapānaprasaktāś ca jaghanyā rājasī gatiḥ || 12.45 ||
jhallā mallā naṭāś caiva puruṣāḥ kuvṛttaya iti | jhallā mallāś ca rājanyād vrātyād iti jhallamallanaṭā uktāḥ śāstrabāhyā ato ‘vineyāś ca puruṣā dyautamadyaśauṇḍāś cety ante rajonimittā nikṛṣṭā gatiḥ || 12.45 ||
rājānaḥ kṣatriyāś caiva rājñāṃ caiva purohitāḥ |
vādayuddhapradhānāś ca madhyamā rājasī gatiḥ || 12.46 ||
rājāna iti | rājāno ‘bhiṣiktāś ca kṣatriyāś cānabhiṣiktā rājapurohitāḥ śāstrārthakalahapriyāḥ priyayuddhaś cety eṣā rājanimittā rājasī gatir madhyamā || 12.46 ||
gandharvā guhyakā yakṣā vibudhānucarāś ca ye |
tathaivāpsarasaḥ sarvā rājasīṣūttamā gatiḥ || 12.47 ||
gandharvā iti | gandharvā guhyakā yakṣā jātiviśeṣā ye devānuyāyino vidyādharādayo ‘psarasaś ca devagaṇikā udakodbhavā anyāś cety eṣāṃ rājaseṣu manuṣyeṣūtkṛṣṭā gatiḥ || 12.47 ||
tāpasā yatayo viprā ye ca vaimānikā gaṇāḥ |
nakṣatrāṇi ca daityāś ca prathamā sāttvikī gatiḥ || 12.48 ||
tāpasā iti | vānaprasthā bhikṣavo brāhmaṇāḥ puṣpakādivimānacāriṇo ye devaviśeṣāḥ nakṣatrāṇi daityākhyā jātiviśeṣāś cety eṣā sattvanimittā nikṛṣṭā gatiḥ tāpasyādikarmāśrayatvāt sāpi sattvādiguṇanimittakam eva iti cet tat tāpasā ity anena darśayati | na tu tāpasajātiyatijātir vā vidyate – santi vaitihāsikadṛṣṭyā mervādivāsino jātiviśeṣā yatiprabhṛtayaḥ | śrūyate ca “indro yatīn sālāvṛkebhyaḥ prāyacchat” iti || 12.48 ||
yajvāna ṛṣayo devā vedā jyotīṃṣi vatsarāḥ |
pitaraś caiva sādhyāś ca dvitīyā sāttvikī gatiḥ || 12.49 ||
yajvāna iti | yāgaśīlā rṣayo devā vedābhimāninaś ca devatāvigrahavatyo yathetihāseṣu brahmasabhāyāṃ varṇyante te jyotīṃṣi dhruvādīni saṃvatsarā itihāsadṛṣṭyā vigrahavantaḥ | somapādayaḥ pitaraḥ sādhyāś ca devaviśeṣā ity eṣā sattvanimittā madhyamā gatiḥ || 12.49 ||
brahmā viśvasṛjo dharmo mahān avyaktam eva ca |
uttamāṃ sāttvikīm etāṃ gatim āhur manīṣiṇaḥ || 12.49 ||
brahmā viśvasṛjo dharmo mahān avyaktam eva ceti | hiraṇyagarbho marīcyādayaḥ prajāpatayo dharmo vigrahavān mahadākhyaprasiddhatattvaviśeṣādhiṣṭhātrī devatā vigrahavaty avyaktasya pradhānasya saṃbandhinīty etāṃ sattvanimittām utkṛṣṭāṃ gatiṃ vidvāṃsa āhuḥ
|| 12.49 ||
eṣa sarvaḥ samuddiṣṭas triḥprakārasya karmaṇaḥ |
trividhas trividhaḥ kṛtsnaḥ saṃsāraḥ sārvabhautikaḥ || 12.50 ||
eṣa iti | eṣa vāṅmanaḥkāyākhyasādhanatrayabhedena trividhasya karmaṇaḥ sattvarajastamoguṇabhedena trirūpaḥ sarvaprāṇigataḥ samagro gativiśeṣaḥ kārtsnyena uktaḥ sārvabhautika ity upādānāt anuktā apy atra gatayaḥ pradarśanārthatvena saṃgṛhītā boddhavyāḥ
|| 12.50 ||
indriyāṇāṃ prasaṅgena dharmasyāsevanena ca |
pāpān saṃyānti saṃsārān avidvāṃso narādhamāḥ || 12.52 ||
indriyāṇām iti | indriyāṇāṃ viṣayaprasaṅgena niṣiddhācaraṇena kutsitāḥ saṃsṛtīmūḍhā akṛtaprāyaścittā manuṣyāpasadaḥ prāpnuvanti || 12.52 ||
yāṃ yāṃ yoniṃ tu jīvo ‘yaṃ yena yeneha karmaṇā |
kramaśo yāti loke ‘smiṃs tat tat sarvaṃ nibodhata || 12.53 ||
ya iti | kṣetrajño yena yeneha karmaṇā kṛtena yāṃ yāṃ yonim asmiṃl loke prāpnoti tat karmaphalaṃ sarvakrameṇa śṛṇuteti niṣiddhācaraṇaphalābhidhānopanyāsārthaḥ ślokaḥ || 12.53 ||
bahūn varṣagaṇān ghorān narakān prāpya tatkṣayāt |
saṃśārān pratipadyante mahāpātakinas tv imān || 12.54 ||
bahūn iti | brahmahatyādimahāpātakakāriṇo bahūn varṣasamūhān bhīṣaṇān narakān prāpya duṣkṛtakṣayād īṣadduṣkṛtaśeṣeṇemā vakṣyamāṇā yonīḥ prāpnuvanti || 12.54 ||
śvasūkarakharoṣṭrāṇāṃ go’jāvimṛgamakṣiṇām |
caṇḍālapukkasānāṃ ca brahmahā yonim ṛcchati || 12.55 ||
śvaśūkarakharoṣṭrāṇām iti | śvaśūkarakharoṣṭragocchāgaiḍakamṛgapakṣināṃ cāṇḍālānāṃ śūdreṇa brāhmaṇījātānāṃ saṃbandhinīṃ jātiṃ brahmahā prāpnoti || 12.55 ||
kṛmikīṭapataṅgānāṃ viḍbhujāṃ caiva pakṣiṇām |
hiṃsrāṇāṃ caiva sattvānāṃ surāpo brāhmaṇo vrajet || 12.56 ||
kṛmikīṭapataṅgānām iti | kṛmikīṭaśalabhānāṃ purīṣabakṣiṇāṃ ca pakṣiṇāṃ hiṃsanaśīlānāṃ ca vyāghrādīnāṃ prāṇināṃ ca saṃbandhinīṃ yoniṃ surāpo brāhmaṇo yāti || 12.56 ||
lūtāhisaraṭānāṃ ca tiraścāṃ cāmbucāriṇām |
hiṃsrāṇāṃ ca piśācānāṃ steno vipraḥ sahasraśaḥ || 12.57 ||
lūtāhisaraṭānām iti | vṛścikasarpakṛkalāsānāṃ jalacāriṇāṃ ceti tiraścāṃ makarādīnāṃ hiṃsanaśīlānāṃ ca piśācānāṃ saṃbandhinīṃ brāhmaṇasuvarṇāpahārī brāhmaṇaḥ prāpnoti
|| 12.57 ||
tṛṇagulmalatānāṃ ca krayādāṃ daṃṣṭriṇām api |
krūrakarmakṛtāṃ caiva śataśo gurutalpagaḥ || 12.58 ||
tṛṇagulmalatānām iti | tṛṇānāṃ dūrvādīnāṃ gulmānām aprakāṇḍānāṃ kupyakādīnām āmamāṃsabhakṣiṇāṃ gṛdhrādīnāṃ daṃṣṭriṇāṃ siṃhādīnāṃ krūrakarmakāriṇāṃ śabarādīnāṃ saṃbandhinīṃ jātiṃ śataṃ vārān gurudārābhigāmī prāpnoti || 12.58 ||
hiṃsrā bhavanti kravyādāḥ kṛmayo ‘medhyabhakṣiṇaḥ |
parasparādinaḥ stenāḥ pretāntyastrīniṣeviṇaḥ || 12.59 ||
hiṃsrā iti | ye prāṇivadhaśīlās te āmamāṃsabhakṣā gṛdhrādayo bhavanti | abhakṣyabhakṣiṇo ye te kṛmayo jāyante | ye mahāpātakavyatiriktāḥ caurās te parasparabhakṣyā matsyādaya utpadyante | ye caṇḍālādistrīgāminas te pretākhyāḥ prāṇiviśeṣā bhavanti || 12.59 ||
saṃyogaṃ patitair gatvā parasyaiva ca yoṣitam |
apahṛtya ca viprasvaṃ bhavati brahmarākṣasaḥ || 12.60 ||
saṃyogam iti | yāvatā kālena patito bhavati tāvantaṃ kālaṃ brahmādibhiś caturbhiḥ saha saṃsargaṃ kṛtvā parastriyaṃ ca gatvā brāhmaṇasvaṃ ca suvarṇād anyad apahṛtyaikena brahmarākṣasākhyo bhūtaviśeṣo jāyate || 12.60 ||
maṇimuktāpravālāni hṛtvā lobhena mānavaḥ |
vividhāni ca ratnāni jāyate hemakartṛṣu || 12.61 ||
maṇimuktāpravālānīti | maṇīn padmarāgādīn muktāvidrumāni ca vividhāni ratnāni vaidūryahīrakādīni lobhenātmīyabhramādinā suvarṇakārayonau jāyate | ity etac ca hemakārākhyaṃ pakṣiṇam āhuḥ || 12.61 ||
dhānyaṃ hṛtvā bhavaty ākhuḥ kāṃsyaṃ haṃso jalaṃ plavaḥ |
madhu daṃśaḥ payaḥ kāo rasaṃ śvā nakulo ghṛtam || 12.62 ||
dhānyam iti | dhānyam apahṛtya mūṣako bhavati | kāṃsyaṃ hṛtvā haṃso jalaṃ sekādyarthe hṛtvā plavaśakaṭabilākhyaḥ pakṣī yat tu sve kākovārīti tatpānārthaṃ stokakasyāpi parātiśayadarśanān mākṣikaṃ hṛtvā daṃśākhyaḥ kīṭo jāyate | kṣīraṃ hṛtvā kāko bhavati | rasam ikṣvāmalakādisaṃbandhinaṃ hṛtvā śvāna utpadyate | guḍalavaṇādīnāṃ pṛthag upādānāt ghṛtaṃ hṛtvā nakulo bhavati || 12.62 ||
māṃsaṃ gṛdhro vapāṃ madgus tailaṃ tailapakaḥ khagaḥ |
cīrīvākas tu lavaṇaṃ balākā śakunir dadhi || 12.63 ||
māṃsam iti | māṃsaṃ hṛtvā gṛdhrākhyaḥ pakṣī bhavati | medo hṛtvā maṅgākhyaḥ pakṣī bhavati | tailaṃ hṛtvā tailapakākhyaḥ pakṣy utpadyate | lavaṇaṃ hṛtvā cīrīvākākhyaḥ uccaiḥsvaraḥ kīṭo bhavati | dadhi hṛtvā balākākhyaḥ pakṣī jāyate || 12.63 ||
kauśeyaṃ tittirir hṛtvā kṣaumaṃ hrtvā tu darduraḥ |
kārpāsatāntavaṃ krauñco godhā gāṃ vāggudo guḍam || 12.64 ||
kauśeyam iti | kauśkīṭavivṛtaṃ paraṃ hṛtvā tittiryākhyaḥ pakṣī jāyate | kṣumāvṛttaṃ cāsau hṛtvā maṇḍūko jāyate | kārpāsaṃ tantumayaṃ paṭṭaṃ hṛtvā krauñcākhyaḥ pakṣy utpadyate | gāṃ hṛtvā godhākhyaḥ prāṇī bhavati | guḍaṃ hṛtvā vāgdomanthākhyaḥ pakṣī bhavati || 12.64 ||
chucchundarīḥ śubhān gandhān patraśākaṃ tu barhiṇaḥ |
śvāvit kṛtānnaṃ vividham kṛtānnaṃ tu śalyakaḥ || 12.65 ||
chucchundarir iti | surabhidravyāṇi karpūrādīni hṛtvā chucchundarī rājaduhitākhyā mūṣikā bhavati | patrātmakaṃ śākaṃ sarṣapādi hṛtvā mayūro jāyate || 12.65 ||
bako bhavati hṛtvāgniṃ gṛhakārī hy upaskaram |
raktāni hṛtvā vāsāṃsi jāyate jīvajīvakaḥ || 12.66 ||
baka iti | agniṃ hṛtvā bakākhyaḥ pakṣī jāyate | gṛhopayogighaṭamuśalādi hṛtvā gṛhakārī ca rūḍhamakṣikākhyaḥ kīṭo bhavati | kusubhādiraktāni vāsāṃsi hṛtvā jīvajīvakākhyaḥ pakṣī bhavati
|| 12.66 ||
vṛko mṛgebhaṃ vyāghro ‘śvaṃ phalamūlaṃ tu markaṭaḥ |
strīm ṛkṣaḥ stokako vāri yānāny uṣṭraḥ paśūn ajaḥ || 12.67 ||
vṛka iti | mṛgahastinaṃ vā hṛtvā vṛkākhyo prāṇi bhavati | aśvaṃ hṛtvā vyāghro bhavati | phalamūle hṛtvā markaṭo jāyate | striyaṃ hṛtvā ṛkṣo vānaraviśeṣo bhavati | pānārtham udakaṃ hrtvā stokakaś cātako ‘tyantaṃ pipāsuḥ pakṣī utpadyate | yānāni gantryādīni hṛtvā uṣṭro bhavati | paśūn hṛtvā chāgo jāyate || 12.67 ||
yad vā tad vā paradravyam apahṛtya balān naraḥ |
avaśyaṃ yāti tiryaktvaṃ jagdhvā caivāhutiṃ haviḥ || 12.68 ||
yad iti | yaḥ kiṃcid asāram api paradhanam icchāto manuṣyo ‘pahṛtya dattāvadānādikaṃ ca puroḍāśādy aśitvā niścittaṃ tiryaktvaṃ prāpnoti || 12.68 ||
striyo ‘py etena kalpena hṛtvā doṣam avāpnuyuḥ |
eteṣām eva jantūnāṃ bhāryātvam upayānti tāḥ || 12.69 ||
striya iti | striyo ‘py anena kalpena rūpeṇecchātaḥ parasvaṃhṛtvā pāpaṃ prāpnuvanti | tena ca pāpenoktajantustrītvaṃ tāḥ prapadyante || 12.69 ||
svebhyaḥ svebhyas tu karmabhyaś cyutā varṇā hy anāpadi |
pāpān saṃsṛtya saṃsārān preṣyatāṃ yānti dasyuṣu || 12.70 ||
evaṃ niṣiddhācaraṇaphalāṇy abhidhāyādhunā vihitākaraṇavipākam āha svebhyaḥ svebhya iti | brāhmaṇādaya āpadaṃ vinā adhyayanādikarmatyāgino vakṣyamāṇāḥ kutsitā yonīḥ prāpya tato janmāntare śatruṣu dāsatvaṃ prāpnuvanti || 12.70 ||
vāntāśy ulkāmukhaḥ preto vipro dharmāt svakāc cyutaḥ |
amedhyakuṇapāśī ca kṣatriyaḥ kūṭapūtnaḥ || 12.71 ||
vāntāśīti | brāhmaṇaḥ bhraṣṭasvakarmāgnipiśāco nāma bhūtaviśeṣo jvālāmukhaḥ chardibhug bhavati | kṣatriyaḥ punaḥ bhraṣṭakarmā pūtanākhyaḥ piśācaviśeṣo gandhaghoṇapurīṣasarvabhuk jāyate || 12.71 ||
maitrākṣijyotikaḥ preto vaiśyo bhavati pūyabhuk |
cailāśakaś ca bhavati śūdro dharmāt svakāc cyutaḥ || 12.72 ||
cailāśa iti | vaiśyo bhraṣṭakarmā maitrākhyo jyotikanāmāśrayārtham api chidranyastadṛṣṭipiśācaviśeṣapūyabhuk janmāntare bhavati | śūdraḥ punaḥ svakarmād bhraṣṭaḥ cailāśakākhyaḥ kīṭaś celā utpadyante tadbhakṣaś ca bhavati || 12.72 ||
yathā yathā niṣevante viṣayān viṣayātmakāḥ |
tathā tathā kuśalatā teṣāṃ teṣūpajāyate || 12.73 ||
yathā yatheti | śabdādiviṣayopabhogavardhakā yathā viṣayopabhogābhyāsaṃ kurvanti tathā tathā viṣayeṣv eva teṣāṃ prāvīṇyam ekarasībhāvo jāyate || 12.73 ||
te ‘bhyāsāt karmaṇāṃ teṣāṃ pāpānām alpabuddhayaḥ |
saṃprāpnuvanti duḥkhāni tāsu tāsv iha yoniṣu || 12.74 ||
tataś ca karmaṇām iti | te ‘lpaprājñā yāvad yāvan niṣiddhaviṣayopabhogābhyāsaṃ kurvanti tāvat tāvad abhyāsatāratamyāpekṣayā tāsu tāsu tiryagādijātiṣu duḥkham anubhavati || 12.74 ||
tāmisrādiṣu cogreṣu narakeṣu vivartanam |
asipatravanādīni bandhacchedanāni ca || 12.75 ||
tāmisrādiṣv iti | tāmisrādiṣu caturthādhyāyokteṣu ghoreṣu pariluṇṭhanaṃ prāpnuvanti | asipatravanādīṃś ca caturthādhyāyoktāṃś ca bandhanacchedenātmakān narakān prāœnuvanti iti pūrvavākyādihottaratra cānuṣajjate || 12.75 ||
vividhāś caiva saṃpīḍāḥ kākolūkaś ca bhakṣaṇam |
karambhavālukātāpān kumbhīpākāṃś ca dāruṇān || 12.76 ||
vividhā iti | nānāprakārāḥ krakapāṭanādikāḥ pīḍāḥ kākolūkaiś ca bhakṣaṇaṃ taptakakarambhasikatābhitāpān sthālīṣu vanāni duḥkhena soḍhuṃ śakyāni prāpnuvanti || 12.76 ||
saṃbhavāṃś ca viyonīṣu duḥkhaprāyāsu nityaśaḥ |
śītātapābhighātāṃś ca vividhāni bhayāni ca || 12.77 ||
saṃbhavān iti | tiryagādiṣu jātiṣu nityaṃ duḥkhabahulāsu janmāni prāpnuvanti | teṣu śītātapopapīḍanāni nānāprakārāṇi bhayāni prāpnuvanti || 12.77 ||
asakṛd garbhavāseṣu vāsaṃ janma ca dāruṇam |
bandhanāni ca kaṣṭāni parapreṣyatvam eva ca || 12.78 ||
asakṛd iti | bandhanāni ca kaṣṭāni pārapreṣyatvam eva ca | bahuśo garbhasthāne samutpattiṃ cātiduḥkhāvahām utapannasya ca galasaṃ phalādiviṣamāṇi bandhanāny anyadāsatvaṃ copagacchanti || 12.78 ||
bandhupriyaviyogāṃś ca saṃvāsaṃ caiva durjanaiḥ |
dravyārjanaṃ ca nāśaṃ ca mitrāmitrasya cārjanam || 12.79 ||
babdhupriyaviyogā iti | bāndhavaiḥ suhṛdbhiḥ saha viyogaṃ durjanaiś ca saha ekatrāvasthānaṃ dhanārjanakleśaṃ dhanavināśaṃ mitrārjanakleśaṃ śatruprādurbhāvaṃ prāpnuvanti || 12.79 ||
jarāṃ caivāpratīkārāṃ vyādhibhiś copapīḍanam |
kleśāṃś ca vividhāṃs tāṃs tān mṛtyum eva ca durjanam || 12.80 ||
jarām iti | jarāṃ cāpratibandhāṃ vyādhibhiś ca pīḍanam anekaprakārāṃś ca kṣutpipāsādinā tāṃś tān upatāpān mṛtyuṃ cānapāsanīyam āsādayanti || 12.80 ||
yādṛśena tu bhāvena yad yat karma niṣevate |
tādṛśena śarīreṇa tat tat phalam upāśnute || 12.81 ||
yādṛśenaiva bhāveneti | yathāvidhena sāttvikena rājasena tāmasena vā cetasā yat karma snānayāgādy anutiṣṭhanti tathāvidhena śarīreṇa sattvabahulena rajobahulena tamobahulena vā tat tat snānādiphalam upabhuṅkte || 12.81 ||
eṣa sarvaḥ samuddiṣṭaḥ karmaṇāṃ vaḥ phalodayaḥ |
naiḥśreyasakaraṃ karma viprasyedaṃ nibodhata || 12.82 ||
eṣa iti | naiḥśreyasaṃ karmavidhiṃ viprasyemaṃ nibodhata | eṣā śubhāśubhānāṃ karmaṇām akhilā utpattir yuṣmākam uktā | idānīṃ brāhmaṇasya svargamokṣādyarthaṃ karmānuṣṭhānam idaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ śṛṇuta || 12.82 ||
vedābhyāsas tapo jñānam indiryāṇāṃ ca saṃyamaḥ |
ahiṃsā gurusevā ca niḥśreyasakaraṃ param || 12.83 ||
vedābhyāsa iti | vedasya granthārthato ‘bhyasanaṃ kṛcchrāditapaḥ ātmajñānam indriyajayo hiṃśāvarjanaṃ gurucaryety etat prakṛṣṭam arthavargasādhanam || 12.83 ||
sarveṣām api caiteṣāṃ śubhānām iha karmaṇām |
kiṃcic chreyaskarataraṃ karmoktaṃ puruṣaṃ prati || 12.84 ||
sarveṣām iti | eṣāṃ vedābhyāsakarmādīnām adhyātmikaṃ karmātiśayena śreyasaḥ sādhanaṃ puruṣasya syād iti vitarkeṇa ṛṣīṇāṃ jijñāsām utpādyottareṇa niścaya iti || 12.84 ||
sarveṣām api caiteṣām ātmajñānaṃ paraṃ smṛtam |
tad dhy agryaṃ sarvavidyānāṃ prāpyate hy amṛtaṃ tataḥ || 12.85 ||
sarveṣām iti | teṣāṃ vedābhyāsādīnāṃ sarveṣām api madhyād upaniṣaduktopāsanābhiḥ paramātmasvarūpāvadhāraṇaṃ prakṛṣṭaṃ smṛtam yasmāt sarvavidyānāṃ tatpradhānaṃ yato mokṣas tasmāt prāpyate || 12.85 ||
ṣaṇṇām eṣāṃ tu pūrveṣāṃ karmaṇāṃ pretya ceha ca |
śreyaskarataraṃ jñeyaṃ sarvadā karma vaidikam || 12.86 ||
ṣaṇṇām eva tu sarveṣāṃ iti | eṣāṃ pūrvoktānāṃ vedābhyāsādīnāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ karmaṇāṃ madhyāt smārtakarmāpekṣayā vaidikaṃ karma sarvam ihalokayor atiśayena svargādisādhanaṃ vijñeyam | ātmajñānasyāmṛtaphaltvād vidhānād vaidikatvāc ca tadapekṣam anyasya vaidikasyedaṃ śreṣṭham ucyate api tu smārtakarmāpekṣam || 12.86 ||
vaidike karmayoge tu sarvāṇy etāny aśeṣataḥ |
antarbhavanti kramaśas tasmiṃs tasmin kriyāvidhau || 12.87 ||
vaidika iti | jyotiṣṭomādivedoktakarmānuṣṭhāne kramenāntarbhavati | tathā ca śrautasmārtādau vedābhyāso ‘ntarbhavati | tapaś ca dīkṣopasatpayovratādau jñānaṃ cātmajñānasya samuccayena vaidikakarmaṇopasargaphalatvād indiryasaṃyamaś ca na māṃsam aśnīyān na maithunam upeyād iti | karmāṅgatvenoktatvād ahiṃsā ca tasmād etāṃ rātriṃ prāṇabhṛtaḥ prāṇān na chindyād iti bhāgatvenābhidhānāt guruśuṣrūṣā ca yathākarmānuṣṭhānakāraṇabhūnaṃ vedam adhigacchatīty evaṃ sarvāṇi vaidikayogena bhavanti || 12.87 ||
sukhābhyudayikaṃ caiva naiḥśreyasikam eva ca |
pravṛttaṃ ca nivṛttaṃ ca dvividhaṃ karma vaidikam || 12.88 ||
sukhābhyudayikam iti | sukhaṃ karma grāmādevāpteḥ yato ‘bhyudite tatsukhābhyudayikaṃ saṃsārapravṛttihetutvākhyam | vaidikaṃ karma tathā niḥśreyaso mokṣas tadarthaṃ karma naiḥśreyasikaṃ saṃsārahetutvān nirvṛttākhyam ity evaṃ karma vaidikaṃ dviprakāraṃ veditavyam || 12.88 ||
iha cāmutra vā kāmyaṃ pravṛttaṃ karma kīrtyate |
niṣkāmaṃ jñānapūrvaṃ tu nivṛttam upadiśyate || 12.89 ||
etad eva vyaktayitum āha iha ceti | svargādyaihikāmutraphalatā kāmatyā yat kriyate tat kāmyam | niṣkāmatayā jñānapūrvakaṃ tu tat saṃsāranivṛttihetutvān nivṛttam ucyate || 12.89 ||
pravṛttaṃ karma saṃsevyaṃ devānāṃ eti sāmyatām |
nivṛttaṃ sevamānas tu bhūtāny atyeti pañca vai || 12.90 ||
pravṛttaṃ karma saṃsevan devānāṃ eti sārṣṭyatām iti | pravṛttakarmābhyāsena devānāṃ samānagatitvaṃ prāpnoti | nivṛttakaramābhyāsena punaḥ śarīrārambhakāṇi bhūtāni atikrāmati mokṣaṃ prāpnotīty arthaḥ || 12.90 ||
sarvabhūteṣu cātmānaṃ sarvabhūtāni cātmani |
samaṃ paśyann ātmayājī svārājyam adhigacchati || 12.91 ||
sarvabhūteṣv iti | teṣ teṣv avaśyakartavyayāgeṣu yady agnyādidevatāntaram iva jyotir ātmaiva tena rūpeṇa tiṣṭhata ity evaṃ manvāna ātmayājī sarveṣu sthāvarajaṅgamātmakeṣu bhūteṣu aham eva vyavasthita ity evaṃ sarvabhūteṣv ātmānaṃ manyamānaḥ sarvāṇi bhūtāni mayy avasthitāni ity evaṃ manvāna ity etat sarvatulyatvena paśūn svātantryaṃ prāpnoti | na śarīrādisaṃbandhi tasya pāratantryaṃ bhavaty api tu tathā jānann ātmajñāne sati mucyate || 12.91 ||
yathoktāny api karmāṇi parihāya dvijottamaḥ |
ātmajñāne śame ca syād vedābhyāse ca yatnavān || 12.92 ||
yathokāny apīti | śāstranoditāny api agnihotrādīni parityjaya yāvad vai puruṣo bhāsate ityādinā caturthādhyāyadarśitaśrutiprakāreṇa tāny anuthiṣṭhann ātmajñānendriyajayavedābhyāseṣu brāhmaṇo yatnaṃ kuryāt || 12.92 ||
etad dhi janmasāphalyaṃ brāhmaṇsya viśeṣataḥ |
prāpyaitat kṛtakṛtyo hi dvijo bhavati nānyathā || 12.93 ||
etad iti | etad vedābhyāsādidvijātīnāṃ janmanaḥ saphalatvotpādkatvāt janmanaḥ śāphalyaṃ viśeṣeṇa brāhmaṇasya yasmād etat saṃpādya dvijātiḥ kṛtakaraṇīyo bhavati na tu karmāntarakaraṇena || 12.93 ||
pitṛdevamanuṣyāṇāṃ vedaś cakṣuḥ sanātanam |
aśakyaṃ cāprameyaṃ ca vedaśāstram iti sthitiḥ || 12.94 ||
pitṛdevamanuṣyāṇām iti | pitṝṇāṃ devatānāṃ ca vayaṃ havyakavyapradātṛtvasya vedāgamyatvād aśakyaṃ ca kartuṃ vedaśāstraṃ tadarthasyātīndriyatvāt pramātuṃ cāśakyaṃ kṛtsnaśāstrasamūhasyādhyetum aśkyatvād iti vyavasthā || 12.94 ||
yā vedabāhyāḥ śrutayo yāś ca kāś ca kudṛṣṭayaḥ |
sarvās tā niṣphalāḥ pretya tamoniṣṭhā hi tāḥ smṛtāḥ || 12.95 ||
yā vedabāhyā iti | yāni vedavirodhāni ity āgamābhāsarūpāṇi śāstrāṇi yāni ca kānicid vinaiva śāsreṇāpavargādihetvābhāsarūpāṇi darśanāni sarvāṇi tāni paraloke niṣphalāni yasmān narakaphalāni tāni manvādibhiḥ smṛtāni || 12.95 ||
utpadyante vinaśyanti yāny ato ‘nyāni kānicit |
tāny arvākkālikatayā niṣphalāny anṛtāni ca || 12.96 ||
utpadyanta iti | vedavyatiriktāni yāni kānicic chāstrāṇi puruṣōdgamatvād utpadyante ata evāśu vinaśyanti tānīdānīṃtanatvān niṣphalāny asatyarūpāṇi ca smṛtīnāṃ vedamūlatvān naitac codyāvatāraḥ || 12.96 ||
cāturvarṇyaṃ trayo lokāś catvāraś cāśramāḥ pṛthak |
bhūtaṃ bhavat bhaviṣyaṃ ca sarvaṃ vedāt prasidhyati || 12.97 ||
cāturvarṇyam iti | yathā gāmahiṣyādīnām itaretarabheda indriyagocaro tac cāturvarṇyaṃ vedād eva prasidhyati tathā vedoktāgnihotrakarmaphalopabhogārthatvāt pṛthivyālokāyeti vedāvedaiḥ prasidhyanty evam āśramā api catvāro brahmacāryādayaḥ parasparavilakṣaṇavaidikagocaratvād vedād eva prasidhyanti | kiṃ ca punaḥ agnau prāstāhutinyāyena yat kiṃcid atītaṃ vartamānaṃ bhaviṣyad vā tat sarvaṃ vedād eva pramāṇaṃ sidhyati || 12.97 ||
śabdaḥ sparśaś ca rūpaṃ ca raso gandhaś ca pañcamaḥ |
vedād eva prasūyante prasūtir guṇakarmataḥ || 12.98 ||
śabda iti | vedād eva prasidhyanti prabhūtaguṇakarmataḥ | ye svargādivipratā upabhogyatvenābhimatāḥ śabdādayo viṣayās te ‘bhimataviṣayopabhogotpattyarthaṃ yad upakārakaṃ karmāgnihotrādikaṃ tadanuṣṭhānadvāreṇa vedād eva prasidhyanti ||
bibharti sarvabhūtāni vedaśāstraṃ sanātanam |
tasmād etat paraṃ manye yaj jantor asya sādhanam || 12.99 ||
bibhartīti | nityaṃ vedaśāstraṃ sarvabhūtāni dhārayati | yathā ca havir agnau hūyate | so ‘gnir ādityaṃ tarpayati | tat sūryo raśmibhir varṣati | tato ‘nnaṃ saṃbhavati | tato ‘rthaṃ vai bhūtānām utpattiḥ sthitaś ceha vijñāyate iti brāhmaṇam | tasmāc chāstraṃ etac chāstraṃ vedasya prakṛṣṭaṃ vedmi prakṛṣṭaṃ yasmād asyādhikārapuruṣasyetat [-aitat?] puruṣārthasādhanam || 12.99 ||
saināpatyaṃ rājyaṃ ca daṇḍanetṛtvam eva ca |
sarvalokādhipatyaṃ ca vedaśāstravid arhati || 12.100 ||
saināpatyam iti | senādhipatitvaṃ rājyaṃ daṇḍanāyakatvaṃ sārvabhaumaiśvaryāni ity evam akhilaṃ dṛṣṭapuruṣārthaṃ vedaśāstrajña evārhatīti vedapraśaṃsā || 12.100 ||
yathā jātabalo vahnir dahaty ārdrān api drumān |
tathā dahati vedajñaḥ karmajaṃ doṣam ātmanaḥ || 12.101 ||
yatheti | yathātipravṛddho ‘gniḥ ārdrān api vṛkṣān dahati evaṃ vedajñānasāmarthyāt śiṣṭākaraṇaniṣiddhasevanajaṃ pāpam ātmano nāśayatīty evaṃ ca na kevalaṃ vedadṛṣṭaprāptihetur yāvad aniṣṭanivṛttir api || 12.101 ||
vedaśāstrārthatattvajño yatra tatrāśrame vasan |
ihaiva loke tiṣṭhan sa brahmabhūyāya kalpate || 12.102 ||
vedaśāstrārthatattvajña iti | yaḥ pāramārthikavedārthajño viṣayadoṣādyabodhe sati rāgādipraṇodāt yasmimś tasmin brahmacaryādyāśrame vyavasthito ‘sminn eva loke tiṣṭhann anenaiva śarīreṇa brahmatvāya saṃpadyate || 12.102 ||
ajñebhyo granthinaḥ śreṣṭhā granthibhyo dhāriṇo varāḥ |
dhāribhyo jñāninaḥ śreṣṭhā jñānibhyo vyavasāyinaḥ || 12.103 ||
ajñebhya iti | ubhayoḥ praśasyatve saty anyatarasya pravarṣaviṣayā yā śreṣṭhaśabdaprayogād īṣadadhyayanāḥ atra ajñā bhaṇyante | na tv atyantamūrkhās tebhyo granthādhyāyinaḥ śreṣṭhās tebhyas tadabhyāsanirastāḥ prakṛṣṭās tebhyo ‘pi tadarthajñās tebhyo ‘py anuṣṭhātāro varāḥ
|| 12.103 ||
tapo vidyā ca viprasya niḥśreyasakaraṃ param |
tapasā kilbiṣaṃ hanti vidyayāmṛtam aśnute || 12.104 ||
tapa iti | kṛcchrāditapa ātmajñānaṃ ca brāhmaṇasya prakṛṣṭaṃ śreyaḥsādhanaṃ yatas tapasā pāpam āhanty ātmajñānena mokṣaṃ prāpnoti || 12.104 ||
pratyakṣaṃ cānumānaṃ ca śāstraṃ ca vividhāgamam |
trayaṃ suviditaṃ kāryaṃ dharmaśuddhim abhīpsatā || 12.105 ||
pratyakṣam iti | dharmasya tattvāvabodham icchatā pratyakṣānumānena śāstraṃ ca vedasmṛtirūpam anekamantrabhedabhinnaṃ suvijñātaṃ kartavyaṃ pratyakṣānumānasvarūpāvabodhe hi karmasādhanabhūtadravyaguṇajātisattvavijñānatamaṃ yujyate || 12.105 ||
ārṣaṃ dharmopadeśaṃ ca vedaśāstrāvirodhinā |
yas tarkeṇānusaṃdhatte sa dharmaṃ veda netaraḥ || 12.106 ||
ārṣam iti | ṛṣibhir dṛṣṭa ārṣo vedo dharmopadeśasmṛtis tau saṃdigdhavyākhyā punaruktāśaṅkāyāṃ vedasmṛtyavirodhena nyāyena tayor vicārayati sa dharmaṃ jānāti na tu tarkānabhijñaḥ || 12.106 ||
naiḥśreyasam idaṃ karma yathoditam aśeṣataḥ |
mānavasyāsya śāstrasya rahasyam upadekṣyate || 12.107 ||
naiḥśryasam iti | tad apavargādyarthaṃ karmaniḥśeṣeṇa yathāvad uktam ata ūrdhvam asya mānavasya śāstraikadeśa ātmajñānopadeśaparatvenopaniṣadrūpo ‘bhidhāsyate || 12.107 ||
anāmnāteṣu dharmeṣu kathaṃ syād iti ced bhavet |
yaṃ śiṣṭā brāhmaṇā brūyuḥ sa dharmaḥ syād aśaṅkitaḥ || 12.108 ||
evaṃ rahasyābhidhānaṃ pratijñāya sakalakarmānabhidhānena pratijñāhānyāśaṅkām āha anāmnāteṣv iti | sagranthakāgranthakatvenobhayarūpatvāt smaraṇasyānupadiṣṭeṣu dharmeṣu upadiṣṭeṣv api cānuktetikartavyatā teṣu kiṃ te kartavyāḥ uta neti kathaṃ vā kartavyā iti yadi saṃśayaḥ syāt tadā yaṃ vakṣyamāṇāḥ śiṣṭāḥ brāhmaṇā brūyuḥ sa niścito dharmaḥ syāt
|| 12.108 ||
dharmeṇādhigato yais tu vedaḥ saparibṛṃhaṇaḥ |
te śiṣṭā brāhmaṇā jñeyāḥ śrutipratyakṣahetavaḥ || 12.109 ||
dharmenādhigata iti | brahmacaryoktetikartavyatayā yair aṅgamīmāṃsādharmaśāstrapurāṇādyupabṛṃhakashito vedo ‘dhītas te brāhmaṇāḥ vedagocarapadārthopadeśakāraṇabhūtāḥ śiṣṭā vijñeyāḥ || 12.109 ||
daśāvarāḥ vā pariṣad yaṃ dharmaṃ parikalpayet |
tryavarā vāpi vṛttasthā taṃ dharmaṃ na vicālayet || 12.110 ||
daśāvareti | yady utpannayūnās tadā daśa vakṣyamāṇavidvāṃso yasyāṃ pariṣadi tadabhāve trayo vā yasyāṃ sādhvācāraṃ dharmaṃ niścinuyāt taddharmatvena pratipādyam || 12.110 ||
traividyo hetukas tarkī nairuto dharmapāṭhakaḥ |
tryaś cāśramiṇaḥ pūrve pariṣat syād daśāvarā || 12.111 ||
traividya iti | vedatrayaśākhātrayādhyetāraḥ śrutismṛtyaviruddhanyāyaśāstravid ūhāpohārthabuddhyupeto nirutajño mānavādidharmādhyetā brahmacārigṛhasthavānaprasthā ity eṣā daśāvarā pariṣat syāt || 12.111 ||
ṛgvedavid yajurvic ca sāmavedavid eva ca |
tryavarā pariṣad jñeyā dharmasaṃśayanirṇaye || 12.112 ||
ṛgvedavid iti | ṛgyajuḥsāmavedānāṃ ye ‘rthajñās tu yasmād dharmasaṃdehavyudāsārthaṃ tyavarā pariṣad boddhavyā || 12.112 ||
eko ‘pi vedavid dharmaṃ yaṃ vyavasyed dvijōttamaḥ |
sa vijñeyaḥ paro dharmo nājñānām udito ‘yutaiḥ || 12.113 ||
tadasaṃbhave ‘pi eka iti | eko ‘pi vedārthajño yaṃ dharmaṃ niścinuyāt sa prakṛṣṭo boddhavyo na vedānabhijñānām ayutair apy uktaḥ | ayutaṃ daśasahasrāṇi || 12.113 ||
avratānām amantrāṇāṃ jātimātropajīvinām |
sahasraśaḥ sametānāṃ pariṣattvaṃ na vidyate || 12.114 ||
avratānām iti | sāvitrādibrahmacārivratarahitānāṃ vedādhyayanaśūnyānāṃ brāhmaṇajātimātropajīvināṃ sahasrāṇām ekībhūtānāṃ pariṣattvaṃ nāsti dharmanirṇayāsāmarthyāt || 12.114 ||
yaṃ vadanti tamobhūtā mūrkhā dharmam atadvidaḥ |
tat pāpaṃ śatadhā bhūtvā tadvaktṝn anugacchati || 12.115 ||
yam iti | ye mūḍhā vivekābhāvāt tamorūpā dharmānabhijñāḥ santaḥ pāpakāriṇo yat prāyaścittam upadiśanti tat prāyaścittanimittabhūtaṃ pāpaṃ śataguṇaṃ bhūtvā teṣāṃ prāyaścittadātṝṇāṃ bhavati || 12.115 ||
etad vo ‘bhihitaṃ sarvaṃ niḥśreyasakaraṃ param |
asmād apracyuto vipraḥ prāpnoti paramāṃ gatim || 12.116 ||
etad iti | etat prakṛṣṭaṃ śreyaso dharmaṃ sarvaṃ yuṣmākam uktam | etad anutiṣṭhan brāhmaṇādiḥ svargāpavargādi prāpnotīti kṛtsnaṃ dharmopasaṃhāram etat || 12.116 ||
evaṃ sa bhagavān devo lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā |
dharmasya paraṃ guhyaṃ mamedaṃ sarvam uktavān || 12.117 ||
evam iti | sa bhagavān aṇimādiguṇayogād devo manur anenaivoktena prakāreṇedaṃ sarvaṃ dharmasya paramārthaṃ lokahitecchayā mamotavān iti bhṛgur maharṣīn āha || 12.117 ||
sarvam ātmani saṃpaśyet sac cāsac ca samāhitaḥ |
sarvaṃ hy ātmani saṃpaśyan nādharme kurute matim || 12.118 ||
idānīṃ yat tad uktaṃ mānavasya śāstrasya rahasyam upadekṣyata iti tat kathayann āha sarvam iti | yat mūrtaṃ pṛthivyādi yac cāmūrtam ākāśādi tat sarvam āmany ekībhūtam ity evam ekāgro bhūtvā paśyed yasmād aham evedaṃ sarvam ity evaṃ bhāvayan rāgadveṣābhāvād adharme mano na kurute || 12.118 ||
ātmaiva devatāḥ sarvāḥ sarvam ātmany avasthitam |
ātmā hi janayaty eṣāṃ karmayogaṃ śarīriṇām || 12.119 ||
ātmeti | indrādyāḥ sarvadevatāḥ paramātmaiva nātmavyatirekeṇa nānyā devatāḥ santi | tathā cendraṃ mitraṃ varuṇam agnim āhur tathodivyaḥ sa suparṇo garutmān | ekaṃ sad viprā bahudhā vadanty agniṃ yamaṃ mātariśvānam āhuḥ ity eva śrūyate | sarvaṃ ca jagat paramātmāśritaṃ yasmād eṣāṃ kṣetrajñānāṃ jagatkāraṇabhūtena śāstreṇāśāstritena karmaṇām ātmaiva saṃbandhaṃ janayati | tathā ca bhagavān vyāsaḥ |
ajño jantur anīśo ‘yam ātmanaḥ sukhaduḥkhayoḥ |
īśvaraḥ prerito gacchet svargaṃ vāśvabhram eva ca ||
ity āha || 12.119 ||
khaṃ saṃniveśayet kheṣu ceṣṭanasparśane ‘nilam |
paktidṛṣṭyoḥ paraṃ tajaḥ snehe ‘po gāṃ ca mūrtiṣu || 12.120 ||
manasīnduṃ diśaḥ śrotre krānte viṣṇuṃ bale haram |
vācy agniṃ mitram utsarge prajane ca prajāpatim || 12.121 ||
adhunā sarvam ātmani saṃpaśced iti yad uktaṃ tatra viśeṣam āha kham iti | manasīti | brāhmam ākāśaṃ śarīragatākāśenaiva sahaikatvena bhāvayed evaṃ ceṣṭāsparśakāraṇabhūtena daihikena vāyunā saha bāhyasya vāyor aikyaṃ koṣṭhyaṃ cākṣuṣatejasā saha prakṛṣṭasya tejaso ‘gnyādityārabdhasyaikyaṃ śārīrābhir adbhiḥ sahāpām aikyaṃ śārīraiś ca kaṭhinair bhāgaiḥ pārthivaiḥ saha bābhyāyāḥ pṛthivyām apy aikyena manasā saha candraṃ śrotreṇa saha viṣṇor vīryeṇa saha bhagavadīśvarasya vāgindriyeṇa agner apānendriyeṇa saha hiraṇyagarbhasyaikyaṃ bhāvayet | evam adhyātmikabhūtādibhiḥ sahābhautikādhidaivikānām aikyaṃ bhāvayet
|| 12.120–121 ||
praśāsitāraṃ sarveṣām aṇīyāṃsam aṇor api |
rukmābhaṃ svapnadhīgamyaṃ vidyāt taṃ puruṣaṃ param || 12.122 ||
praśāsitāram iti | brahmādistambhaparyantānāṃ vyavasthākāriṇaṃ tathā caitasyaivākṣarasya vipraśāsane gārgīgārgyādibrāhmaṇaṃ paramāṇor api sakāśāt sūkṣmataram asyā mūrtatvāt suvarṇavac chuddharūpam visṛtor mitvāpyo ‘śanāyāpipāsais tokamohajarāmṛtyum abhyety ādibrāhmaṇam | suṣuptāvasthāyāṃ vijñānopameyasvarūpaṃ paramātmānaṃ vijānīyād didṛkṣuḥ suṣuptāvasthāyāṃ pratyastamitasukhaduḥkhaviśeṣaṃ kṣetrajñasya rūpam evaṃvidhaṃ sarvadā paramātmana iti svapnasaṃdarśanarahitāvasthā suṣuptam || 12.122 ||
etam eke vadanty agniṃ manum anye prajāpatim |
indram eke pare prāṇam apare brahma śāśvatam || 12.123 ||
etam iti | enaṃ ca paramātmānam agnitvena yājñikā upāsate | tathā ca tam etam agnir ity adhvaryava upāsata iti brāhmaṇaś ca yaḥ punaḥ savata eva ātmano ‘py uccarantīti śraiṣṭhyatvaṃ śruter manvākhyāpratirūpatayopāsatetyādiśruteḥ prāṇam ātmānam etam upāsate ‘nye punar vinaśvaraparamātmarūpatayopāsate evaṃ sarvātmakatvāt paramātmanaḥ kecit kenacid rūpenainam upāsate | tathā ca tasmād enam evaṃ vidmaḥ yair eva tair upāsanta iti brāhmaṇam
|| 12.123 ||
eṣa sarvāṇi bhūtāni pañcabhir vyāpya mūrtibhiḥ |
janmavṛddhikṣayair nityaṃ saṃsārayati cakravat || 12.124 ||
eṣa iti | eṣa paramātmā sarvān prāṇinaḥ pañcabhiḥ pṛthivyādibhūtaiḥ śarīrārambhakaiḥ parigṛhy prāgjanmārjitakarmāpekṣayotpattisthitināśena sakṛd āvartamānaiḥ rathādicakravad ā mokṣān nityaṃ yojayati || 12.124 ||
evaṃ yaḥ sarvabhūteṣu paśyaty ātmānam ātmanā |
sa sarvasamatām etya brahmābhyeti paraṃ padam || 12.125 ||
evam iti | evaṃ sarvabhūteṣu cātmānam ityādinoktena prakāreṇa yaḥ sarvabhūteṣv avasthitam ātmānam ātmanā manyate sa rāgadveṣahāniṃ prāpya paraṃ sthānaṃ brahma prāpnoti mucyata ity arthaḥ || 12.125 ||
ity etan mānavaṃ śāstraṃ bhṛguproktaṃ paṭhan dvijaḥ |
bhavaty ācāravān nityaṃ yatheṣṭāṃ prāpnuyād gatim || 12.126 ||
itīti | iti samāptārtham etan manukṛtaṃ śāstraṃ bhṛguṇā prakarṣeṇoktaṃ dvijātiḥ paṭhan śāstraṃ vidvān vihitānuṣṭāyī pratiṣiddhaṃ varjayaṃś ca bhavaty ata evāmutra bhagavan maheśvarādisāyojyādikām abhimatāṃ prāpnuyād iti || 12.126 ||
iti śrībhaṭṭamādhavātmajaśrīgovindarājaviracitāyāṃ manuṭīkāyāṃ
śrīmanvāśayānusāriṇyāṃ guṇatrayavikarmajātinarakaveda-
praśaṃsāsvarūpanirṇayo dvādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ samāptaḥ ||
